[1,13] yasmin na staḥ karmavipākau yata āstāṃ kleśā yasmai nālam alaṅghyā nikhilānām,

[1,15]  nāvacchinnaḥ kāladṛśā yaḥ kalayantyā lokeśas taṃ kaiṭabhaśatruṃ praṇamāmi.

[1,17] yaḥ sarvavit sarvavibhūtiśaktiḥ vihīnadoṣopahitakriyāphalaḥ,[1,19]  viśvodbhavāntasthitihetur īśo namo^ astu tasmai gurave guror api.

[1,21] athetyādipātañjalayogaśāstra[sūtrabhāṣya]vivaraṇam ārabhyate| tatrānākhyātasambandhaprayojanaṃ na puruṣapravṛttinivṛttibhyāṃ paryāpnotīti, sūtrakārābhiprete puruṣapravṛttinimittabhūte sambandhaprayojane pūrvaṃ prakaṭīkriyete.

[2,1] tatra prayojanaṃ tāvat --- cikitsāśāstre tarccaturvyūhatvapradarśanadvāreṇa vyākhgyātam| tadyathā --- cikitsāśāstraṃ caturvyūhaṃ, rogaḥ, rogahetuḥ, ārogyaṃ, bhaiṣajyam iti|  vidhipratiṣedhaniyamadvāreṇa [ca tat] caturvyūhaviṣayavyākhyānaparam| evam ihāpi "pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair guṇavṛttivirodhāc ca duḥkham eva sarvaṃ vivekinaḥ" ity (YS 2.15) ārabhya caturvyūhatvaṃ śāstrasya pradarśitam| tadyathā --- duḥkhapracuraḥ saṃsāro heyaḥ| tasyāvidyānimitto draṣṭṛ-dṛśyasaṃyogo hetuḥ| vivekakhyātir aviplavā hānopāyaḥ| vivekakhyātau ca satyām avidyānivṛttiḥ, tannivṛttāv ātyantiko draśṭṛdṛśyasaṃyogoparamo hānam, tad eva kaivalyam iti|  ārogyasthānīyakaivalyaprayuktatvād asya tad eva kaivalyaṃ prayojanam||

[2,10] nanu ca heyataddhetū na prastotavye, niṣprayojanatvāt|  hānaprayuktaṃ hi śāstraṃ, tadupāyabhūtā vivekakhyātir eva vaktavyā|  na hi kaṇṭakaviddhacaraṇatalasya tadapanayanaṃ muktvā duḥkhataddhetū codyete| na^ etad evaṃ --- heyatatkāraṇāpekṣatvād^ hānopāyasya|  yāvad idam anenopāyena hātavyaṃ saṃsāracakram asya cāvidyānimitto draṣṭṛ-dṛśyasaṃyogo hetuḥ iti na^ ākhyāyate, tāvan nāvidyāpratipakṣabhūtā vivekakhyātir ākhyātuṃ śakyate|  heyataddhetumato hānopayārthino rogiṇo bhaiṣajyārthitvadarśanāt|  na hi rogaṃ rogahetuṃ cānapekṣya cikitsāśāstram upadiśyate||

[2,18] sambandho^ api vivekakhyāter hānam eva phalaṃ sādhyaṃ hānasyāpi vivekakhyātir eva sādhanam iti sādhyasādhanayor itaretaraniyama^ eva ,na anyasambandhaḥ| tad yathā bhaiṣajyasya^ ārogyam eva phalaṃ ārogyasyāpi bhaiṣajyam eva sādhanam itītaretaraniyamaḥ, sa ca śāstrād iti|  tasmāt sasaṃbandhaprayojanaṃ yogānuśāsanam||

[2,23] nanu ca yadi hānaṃ prayojanaṃ tadupāyaś ca vivekakhyātir iti, tatra vaktavyam 'atha vivekakhyātyanuśāsanam' iti| kim-artham 'atha yogānuśāsanam' iti sūtritam||

[2,26] tadupāyatvād yogasya| upāya eva vaktavyaḥ syāt|  upeyopapradarśane hi^ upeyam upāyaṃ prati sākāṅkṣam eva^ iti^ upāyaḥ sāṅgakalāpaḥ punar api vaktavyaḥ syāt| tasmiṃś cābhihite sarvam eva abhihitam iti||

[3,1] kathaṃ tad upāyatvam? yata^ āha sūtrakāraḥ --- 'yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād aśuddhikṣaye jñānadīptir āvivekakhyāteḥ' iti|  'nirvicāravaiśāradye^ adhyātmaprasādaḥ' 'ṛtaṃbharā tatra prajñā' iti  ca| tathā bhāṣyakāro^ apy āha --- 'yas tv ekāgre cetasi sa bhūtam arthaṃ pradyotayati'^ iti|  bhūtārthāvagatiś ca vivekakhyātiḥ| tasmād yuktaṃ tad upāyayogānuśāsanam eva^ ādau sūtritam iti||

[3,6] nanu ca yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād vivekakhyātiḥ, tatra vaktavyaṃ 'atha yogāṅgānuśāsanam' iti --- na phalenopakramāt| yogāaṅgānuṣṭhānasya hi 
phalaṃ yoga^ iti tadupakramo yuktaḥ||

[3,9] yady evaṃ hānena^ upakramyeta --- na tasya^ upeyatvāt --- upeyam eva hi tat| yogaḥ punaḥ svāṅgānām upeyaś ca, upāyaś ca vivekakhyāter aṇimādiprāpteś ca [ity abhy]arhitataraḥ| phalatadupāyarūpatvāt (phalapayanimittatvāt)||

[3,12] uttarasūtrārthatvāc ca| na hi 'atha yogāṅgānuśāsanam' ity ukte 'yogaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ' iti yuktaṃ vaktum| tadā hi yamaniyamādisūtram eva paṭhitavyaṃ syāt||

[3,15] atha tad eva paṭhitavyam iti cet --- na --- yogopeyāyā/upeyāyā hānopāyabhūtāyā vivekakhyāteḥ, phalasya ca hānasya, sambandha[pra]darśanārthāvāt 'yogaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ' ity asya||

[3,18] (sa) kathaṃ punar etat sambandhapradarśanārtham yāvatā nirbījasamādhilakṣanābhidhānam etat| satyam evaṃ, tathā^ api hānatadupāyasambandam avadyotayad eva sūtraṃ nirbījasamādhilakṣaṇatām āpadyate||

[3,21] na hi nirodhalakṣaṇāt samādher arthāntaraṃ hānam| kiṃ tv etāvatā viśeṣaḥ, nirodhalakṣaṇasamādhau (samādher na) punaḥ pravṛttiḥ, hāne tv ātyantikī nivṛttir iti| samādhyavasthāyāṃ tu hānāviśeṣa eva| tathā ca^āha "tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpe^ avasthānam" [iti]| śvarūpapratiṣṭhā ca citiśaktiḥ kaivalyam" iti svarūpapratiṣṭhatvaṃ(pratiṣṭhaṃ) hi kaivalyam āha| tataś ca nirbījasamādhinā kaivalyam eva śāstrārthapratyayadraḍhimne sākṣātkriyate||

[4,1] tasmād yad ucyate kaiś cit [sa]bījasamādhiḥ kaivalyasādhanam iti tan na| kiṃ tarhi khyātir eva sādhanam avidyānivṛttidvāreṇa| avidyānimitto hi bandhaḥ| tasmād yady api yogānuśāsanaṃ sūtritaṃ, tathā^ api khyātyarthatvād yogasya tad-dvāreṇa khyātihānasaṃbandham api darśayatīti, suṣṭhū-ucyate^ uttarasūtrasaṃbandhārthaṃ ca yogānuśāsanam iti sūtrayitavyam iti|| 

[4,6] yady api phalārthināṃ tat-phalasādhanaṃ pratyutpannākāṅkṣāṇāṃ sādhanopadeśo^ apy asti| tathā^ api na tat-prayojanam, sarvasya 
phaloddeśena pravṛttes tad eva prayojanam iti||

[4,8] yogānuśāsanam iti| yathā śiṣyo^ anuśiṣyate viśiṣṭapravṛttinivṛttiniyamadvāreṇa, tathā viśiṣṭasādhyasādhanatadaṅganiyamamātrasādṛśyād antevāsyanuśāsanavad yogā-anuśāsanam ity ucyate| anuśiṣtṭir anuśāsanaṃ, yogo^ anuśiṣyate^ anena asminn iti vā yogānuśāsanaṃ śāstram||

[4,12] athety ayam adhikārārthaḥ| adhikāra ārambhaḥ prastāvaḥ, artho abhidheyo asya śiṣṭasmṛtipramāṇyāt| nanu ca^ ānantaryārthasya athaśabdasya smarantiśiṣṭāḥ| tathā ca^ āha vṛttād anantarasya 
prakriyā-artho dṛṣṭāḥ iti| na sarvatra^ ārambhārthatvāt| ānantaryasya ca gamyamānatvāat| yathā putra^ ity ukte pitā gamyate, na ca 
putraśabdasyārthaḥ pitā, tathā^ ihāpy ārambha evābhidhīyate, ānantaryaṃ tu pratīyate||

[4,17] ata eva ca 'vṛttād anantarasya prakriyā-arthao dṛṣṭaḥ' ity ucyate| yadi cānantaryārtho^ abhaviṣyat tadā 'vṛttād anantaye^ arthe 
prakriyāyāḥ' ity avadiṣyat| anantarasya^ iti cānantarabhāvina evābhidhānam||

[4,20] api ca^ evaṃ hi smṛtiḥ "kiñcid avyayaṃ vibhaktyarthapradhānaṃ kiñcitkriyā- pradhānam| uccair nīcair ityādi vibhaktyarthapradhānaṃ , hiruk pṛthag ityādi kriyā- pradhānam" [iti]| na ca^ etad vyatirekeṇa avyayānānām artho vidyate| tatrāthaśabdasya^ ānantaryārthatve sati vibhaktyaśravaṇam/vibhaktiśravaṇam ayuktam, sattvapradhānatvāt| ārambhakriyā-arthatve tu na vibhakty(artha)-aśravaṇam ayuktam asattvapradhānatvād iti|| 

[4,25] tasmād athaśabdaḥ prastāvārtha eva^ iti yuktam athety adhikārārtha^ iti| svarūpāvadyotanārtha^ itiśabdaḥ| yathā gaur ity āha^ iti| prasiddho 'py athaśabdārtho^ adhikriyamāṇaviṣaye śiṣyabuddhisamādhānārthaṃ kīrtyate| siddho hy ayaṃ nyāyaḥ śrutau "vyākhyāsyāmi te vyācakṣāṇasya tu nididhyāsasva" iti||

[5,5] kaḥ punar yogo yasyānuśāsanaṃ prastutam iti^ āha yogaḥ samādhir iti| samādhigrahaṇāt 'yuji yoge' iti nivartya, 'yuja samādhau' ity ayaṃ gṛhītaḥ| yogaḥ samādhānam| nanu ca 'yogaḥ samādhiḥ' ity evam antena vyākhyāyate sūtre 'sa ca sārvabhaumaḥ' ityādi bhāṣyam asaṃbandhaṃ dṛśyate| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| iha samādhir ity ukte, samādhīyamānāpekṣatvāt, samādhīyamānānāṃ ca bahutvāt, kimātmā samāḍhīyate? kiṃ vā śarīram, āhosvid indriyāṇīti bahuvidāṃ/bahudhā vipratipatteḥ, svaviśeṣaṇākāṅkṣatvāc ca samādheḥ kasyāyam? kiṃviśeṣaṇakaḥ? ity anuṣakte praśne, idam āha --- sa ca sārvabhaumaś cittasya dharma^ iti| cittasya dharmo na^ ātmādīnām| tac ca cittaṃ svayam eva samādhīyate [samādhā^ atra]ntarapekṣatvāt|| 

[5,14] kāḥ punar bhūmaya^ ity ata^ āha kṣiptaṃ mūḍham ityādayo bhūmaya^ iti| kṣiptam iti karmakartari niṣṭhā| yathā bhinnaḥ kusūlaḥ svayam eva^ iti| kṣiptam aniṣṭaviṣayāsañjanena stimitaṃ| mūḍhaṃ nirvivekam| vikṣiptaṃ nānā-kṣiptam| karmakartary eva| vivekakṣamaṃ ca vikṣiptatvād eva| ekāgraṃ tulyapratyayapravāham| niruddham iti pratyayaśūnyaṃ cittam||

[5,19] nanu ca bhūmiṣu dharmeṣu vivakṣiteṣu, kimarthaṃ kṣiptamityādinā dharmy ucyate ? naiṣa doṣaḥ, dharmiṇā dharma evopadiśyate| dharmāṇāṃ dharmiviṣayatvāt| yathā gotve kiṃ liṅgam iti pṛṣṭe, viṣāṇī kakudmān 
prāntavāladhir iti dharmiṇā dharma evopadiśyate| tasmāt kṣepādayaś cittasya bhūmayo dharmā ityarthaḥ||

[5,23] nanu ca bhūmīnāṃ cittadharmatve samādheś ca, kathaṃ bhūmibhiḥ samādhir ādhāratvena viśeṣyate sārvabhauma^ iti? sāmānyabhūtatvāt samādheḥ| bhūmīnāṃ ca viśeṣatvāt| yathā kṣiptaṃ tiṣṭhati, mūḍhaṃ tiṣṭhati, vikṣiptam ekāgraṃ ceti, kṣiptādibhūmiṣu sthitir anuvartate sāmānyam| sthitiś ca samādhiḥ| tasmāt sāmānyarūpaṃ sarvāsu bhūmiṣu 
prādhānyena vartate^ iti| sarvabhūmipṛthivī^ ity aiśvaryārthīyo 'n-pratyayaḥ| anuśatikāditvād ubhayapadavṛddhih|| 

[5,29] anye punar bāhyādhyātmakān saṃyamaviṣayān bhūmaya^ ity ācakṣate| teṣāṃ 'vikṣipte cetasi'^ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattiḥ| svavacanavirodheś ca katham kṣipyate^ asminn iti kṣiptam, muhyate 'sminn iti mūḍhaṃ , yadā caivaṃ na tadā saṃyamaviṣayatā| tatra kṣepādyasaṃbhavāt ekāgrāvasthāyāṃ hi saṃyamo bhavati||

[6,3] kiṃ cānyat --- kṣipeś ca dhrauvyādyarthābhāvād adhikaraṇe niṣṭhāpratayayābhāvaḥ| kiṃ ca --- niruddhe cetasi saṃyamasyāpi tatrābhāvāt vṛttiśūnyatvāt saṃyamaviṣayābhāvaḥ| na hi nirodhe viṣayaviśeṣe cittaṃ nirudhyate| tadā hi viṣayitvān nivartate| prigaṇanānupapattiś ca| na hi kṣiptādayaḥ pancā eva saṃyamasya viṣayāḥ| teṣām ānantyāt||

[6,8] nanu cānyathā yogaṃ kecid icchanti| tathā ca^ āhuḥ indriyamano^ arthasannikarṣāt sukhaduḥkhe, tadanārambha ātmasthe manasi, [sa]śarīrasya sukhaduḥkhābhāvaḥ prāṇamano-vinigrahāpekṣaḥ saṃyogo [yoga] iti| tad iti prakṛtāpekṣam| yo^ asau sukhaduḥkhayor ātmendriyamono-'arthasannilarṣo hetuḥ, tasya anārambhaḥ tadanutpattiḥ| sa kathaṃ bhavati? ātmasthe manasi, nendriyasthe| saśarīrasyāviśīrṇaśarīrasya| tadā kāraṇābhāvāt kāryābhāva^ iti, sannikarṣābhāve sukhaduḥkhayor apy abhāvaḥ, tasyām avasthāyāṃ yo^ asau vibhor ātmano manasā saṃyogaḥ sa prāṇamano-vinigrahāpekṣaḥ saṃyogaviśeṣo yoga^ iti|| 

[6,16] atra^ ucyate ātmasthe manasīty ayuktam, sarvadā^ ātmasthatvān manasaḥ| indriyādisannikarṣānārambhāpekṣayā^ ātmasthe iti cet, tadanārmbha^ ity eva siddhatvād ātmasthe manasīty anarthakaṃ syāt||

[6,19] kiṃ cānyat, muktasyāpi manasa^ ātmasthatvād indriyasannikarṣābhāvāc ca yogaḥ prāpnoti| tasyāpi hi sarvagatatvāt manasaś ca nityatvāt sarvadā^ ātmasthatvam eva||

[6,21] kiṃ ca^ --- ātmanaḥ pradeśābhāvād ātmastha^ ity ayuktam| na cāpy upacaritātmapradeśasaṃyogaḥ paramārthasya yogasya hetuḥ syāt| upacaritasya mithyātvāt||

[6,23] kiṃ cānyat, mano-arthasannikarṣābhāva^ ity eva sddheḥ(siddhaṃ) indriyagrahaṇam anarthakaṃ syāt| atha indriyair vinā mano-arthasannikarṣābhāvāt tadanārambha^ iti prāptyabhāve pratiṣedho na yukta^ iti cet| naitad evam, vinā-indriyair mano-arthasannikarṣo nāstīty arthāt sannikarṣapratiṣedhaṃ kurvatā viṣayaprāptir indriyadvārā pratipāditā syāt|| 

[6,27] kiṃ cānyat, sarvaprāṇabhājām indriyamano-arthasannikarṣāt sukhaduḥkhapratilambhasya prasiddhatvād ātmanaḥ saṃbandhasya ca nityatvāt sukhaduḥkhābhāvo yoga^ ity eva siddheḥ, tatra [sūtra]śeṣo'narthakaḥ syāt||

[7,5] athāpi syād indriyamano-arthasannikarṣagrahaṇād ṛte sukhaduḥkhābhāva^ ity etāvati sati muktasyāpi sukhaduḥkhābhāvād yogitvaprasaṅga^ iti tannivṛttyarthaṃ sūtraśeṣagrahaṇam iti| tac ca na, muktasya sukhaduḥkhaprāptyabhāvāt, prāptipūrvapratiṣedhasya nyāyyatvāt yasya sukhaduḥkhaprāptiḥ tadviṣayaḥ sukhaduḥkhābhāva^ eva yoga^ iti sūtraśeṣapratyākhyānaṃ yujyate|| 

[7,9] kiṃ cānyat upātte api saśarīragrahaṇe muktād vinivṛttir na śakyate kartum niṣprayojanatvāt| sukhaduḥkhābhāvaś ced yogaḥ vidyamānam api śarīraṃ kāryākaraṇād anarthakaṃ syāt| kāryānapekṣāyāṃ hi muktāmuktayor aviśeṣa^ eva||

[7,12] kiṃ cānyat prāṇamano-vinigrahāpekṣa^ ity api na ghaṭate| vidhārakasya  prayatnasya^ ātmasamavetatvād avidyamāne^ api manasaḥ kriyāvattve^ indriyeṇāsaṃyujya manaḥprāṇa[mano]-nigrahau na kartuṃ 
paryāpyate| indriyasaṃyoge ca manasaḥ tadanārambha^ ity ayuktam| manasaś ca prāṇanigrahe vyāpṛta/vyāvṛtatvād anārambhānupapattiḥ||

[7,16] na cāsatīndriyavāyu-sannikarṣe prāṇanigrahaḥ| na cānigṛhīte 
prāṇe yogaḥ ātmasthavidhārakaprayatnadvārā mano-gatakriyāsaṃbandhahetutvāt prāṇanigrahasya| athāpi syād yogaḥ samādhir iti| tac ca na niṣkriyatvād ātmano nityasamādhānam eva sthitiś ca samādhir ity ucyate| tasmād yuktam abhidhīyate 'sa ca sārvabhaumaś cittasya dharmaḥ' iti||

[7,21] yady evaṃ samādhiś ced yogaḥ sa ca sārvabhaumaś cittadharmaḥ sa ca sarvajanānām ayatnasiddhiḥ kṣiptādyanugatatvāt| tathā ca sati yathā^ eva śvāsapraśvāsādīnāṃ prayatnād ṛte siddhitvāt tatkaraṇam(allakṣaṇam) anarthakam, evaṃ yogānuśāsanam apy anarthakaṃ 
prāptam ity ata āha [tatra] vikṣipte (anavikṣipte) cetasi vikṣepopasarjanībhūtaḥ samādhir na yogapakṣe vartata iti|| kṣiptādyanugatasya yogapakṣa anabhipretatvāt| na hi kṣepādyanugataḥ samādhir bhūtārthāvadyotanādikṣamaḥ, kṣepādipradhānatvāt|   vikṣepaniṣedhenaiva hi pradhānamallanibarhaṇanyāyena kṣiptamūḍhayor api niṣedhaḥ kṛtaḥ| pradhānatā ca vikṣepasamādher adhikārayogyatvāt| vikṣepasthaṃ hi cittam apakṣapātād iṣtaṃ viṣayam upanetuṃ śakyate| na hi viṣayāsañjanādinā kṣiptam iṣtaviṣayaviyogādinā vā mūḍham anyathāupanetuṃ pāryate||

[8,8] yogapakṣa iti| samādhitve saty api kāryākaraṇāt pakṣa ity ucyate| yathā gacchataḥ pratipadaṃ vidyamānāpi sthitiḥ sthitikāryākārān na sthitir ity ucyate| vikṣepopasarjanī-bhūtatvaṃ vikṣepasthaikasamādhivyaktyabhiprāyeṇa| sārvabhauma^ iti tu prādhānyaṃ sāmānyena sarvabhūmiṣu vṛtteḥ||

[8,12] yady upasarjanī-bhāvaḥ samādhipakṣāvṛttau kāraṇam, ihāpy ekāgre^ ekāgratā-upasarjanatvaṃ syāt| tataś ca yogapakṣāvṛttir iti ced ata^ āha yas tv ekāgra^ iti| naikāgratāyāṃ bhūmyupasarjanatvam| kutaḥ? kleśakarmāprabalatvāt| kleśakarmāprabalatve na hi vikṣepādīnām udayaḥ||

[8,16] ekāgratā-bhūmau samādhir yaḥ sa bhūtam arthaṃ yathā-ātmānaṃ 
pradyotayati avagamayati| ayogyarthajñānam ayathā-bhūtatvagandhitam eva^ iti bhūtagrahanam| kṣiṇoti, kṣapayati, pañcaparvaṇo 'vidyādīn kleśān| karmabandhanāni, karmāṇy eva bandhanāni, dharmādharmavimiśrakarmajātāni janmādinibandhanāni, ślathayati, śithilīkaroti, nirodham āmukhīkaroti, abhimukhīkaroti| sa samprajñāta ity ākhyāyate ācāryaiḥ|| 

[8,21] sa ca vitarkānugato vicārānugata ityādi bhāṣyam| samprajñātasya prāthmyāal lakṣaṇābhidhāne prāpte, abhyarhitatvād asamprajñātasyaiva lakṣaṇaṃ yuktam iha vaktum iti, samprajñātam upariṣṭāt pravedayiṣyāma ity ucyate||

[8,24] itaś cāsamprajñātasya lakṣaṇam ihaiva vaktavyaṃ, samprajñātanirapekṣo 'py asamprajñātaḥ para(prakṛṣṭa)vairāgyavirāmapratyayābhyāsābhyāṃ sidhyatīty etat pradarśanārtham| iha samprajñātalakṣaṇābhidhāne taduttarakāle ca asamprajñātalakṣaṇābhdhāne samprajñātāpekṣayaivāsamprajñātasamādhāv adhikāra ity āśaṅkā syāt, tasmād upariṣṭāt pravedayiṣyāma ity āha||1|| 

[9,9] sarvavṛttinirodhe tv asaṃprajñāta iti| tuśabddo 'vadhāraṇā-arthaḥ| tasyaivaṃvidhasya samādher asaṃprajñātasyaiva kevalasya lakṣaṇābhidhitsayā idaṃ sūtraṃ pravavṛte pravṛttaṃ yogaś cittavṛttinirodhaḥ i[tī]ti vaktavyam||

[9,12] nanu ca tasya lakṣaṇam abhidhātum etat sūtraṃ pravṛttaṃ, tathā ca sati yogasya cittavṛttinirodhaḥ iti vaktavyaṃ, sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na yuktam| na hi lakṣyam eva lakṣaṇaṃ syāt| cittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇa^ iti vā vaktavyam| naiṣa doṣaḥ| lakṣye lakṣaṇādhyāsāt| yathā 'yaṃ piṇḍo devadatta^ iti lakṣye lakṣaṇam adhyasyate (pratyasyate)||

[9,17] nanu ca sarvavṛttinirodhe tv asamprajñātaḥ, tasya cedaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ, tataś ca yogaḥ sarvacittavṛttinirodhaḥ iti vaktavyam| na ca uktam| ato 'tivyāpi lakṣaṇam| sarvaśabdāgrahaṇe tāvat kāraṇam ucyate yadi sarvagrahaṇaṃ kriyate, tadā ekadeśanirodhanimittasya saṃprajñātasya yogatvaṃ nivartitaṃ syāt| tan mā^ abhūt iti sarvaśabdāgrahaṇam||

[9,21] astu tarhi saṃprajñātāsaṃprajñātayoḥ sāmānyalakṣaṇaṃ, viśeṣānupādānāt| naiṣa doṣaḥ| yoge prakṛte punar yogagrahaṇāt| viśeṣāvivakṣāyāṃ hi yogagrahaṇaṃ nārthavat syāt||

[9,24] nanu cānuśāsane nyag-bhūtatvād yogasyeha yogagrahaṇe asati cittavṛttinirodha ity ukta anuśāsanārthatā bhavet sūtrasya naivaṃ yogānuśāsanasya prastutatvāt| yogānuśāsanaṃ hi prārabdhaṃ, nāyogānuśāsanaṃ, tallakṣaṇābhidhāne hy atiprasaṅgaḥ, padārthāsaṅgatiś ca| na hi cittavṛttinirodho yogānuśāsanam iti padārthaḥ saṅgacchate||

[9,29] evam api yogagrahaṇāt kuto .asaṃprajñātasyaiva lakṣaṇaṃ, na punaḥ saṃprajñātasya lakṣaṇaṃ syāt yogagrahaṇasyāviśiṣṭatvād iti?

[10,3] ucyate^ avyabhicārāt| vyabhicarati na tat tasya lakṣaṇaṃ nirodhas tv asaṃprajñāte^ api vartata^ iti saṃprajñātaṃ vyabhicarati| na hi viṣāṇitvaṃ gor liṅgaṃ mahiṣyādibhir vyabhicārāt||

[10,8] nanu cāsaṃprajñātam api vyabhicarati saṃprajñātasyāpi heyavṛttinirodhatvāt| satyam evaṃ kiṃ tu na nirodhād anyena lakṣayitum asaṃprajñātaḥ śakyate| nirodha^ eva tu tasya lakṣaṇam anyasyābhāvāt| saṃprajñātasya tv asādhāraṇavitarkādilakṣaṇalakṣyatvāt| yathā sparśalakṣaṇam ity ukte vāyur evāsādhāraṇyāl lakṣyate sparśavattvena atha ca vidyata eva sparśavattvaṃ tejaḥprabhṛtiṣv api||

[10,12] evaṃ ca sati yogagrahaṇam anuvādamātram| ata^ eva ca sarvagrahaṇaṃ na kṛtaṃ sūtre| nirodhagrahaṇenaivāsaṃprajñātalakṣaṇatve siddhe yadi sarvagrahaṇaṃ kriyate tatsaṃprajñātasya yogatāṃ nivārayet| tasmād asaṃprajñātasyaiva^ idaṃ lakṣaṇam| tathā ca^ upasaṃharati bhāṣyakāraḥ na tatra kiṃcit saṃprajñāyate^ ity asaṃprajñātaḥ sa yogaś cittavṛttinirodha iti||

[10,18] cittavṛttitannirodhavyācikhyāsayā cittaṃ hītyādi bhāṣyaṃ kimātmakaṃ punas tac-cittaṃ yasya vṛttinirodhe yoga^ ity ata^ āha cittaṃ hi prakhyāpravṛttisthitiśīlatvāt triguṇam iti| tatra cittaṃ triguṇam iti vyākhyāyate prakhyāpravṛttisthitiśīlatvād iti hetuḥ| prakhyā prakhyānaṃ prakāśanaṃ sa hi sattvaguṇasya dharmaḥ| pravṛttiḥ pravartanaṃ vyāpāraḥ sa hi rajo-dharmaḥ| sthitiḥ sthānaṃ varaṇaṃ pratibandha^ iti sa ca tamo-dharmaḥ| evaṃ śīlā hi sattvādayo guṇāḥ cittaṃ ca prakhyā-ādiśīlaṃ tasmāt trayāṇāṃ guṇānāṃ pariṇāmaś cittaṃ bhavitum arhati.

[10,25] tasya ca prakhyā-ādihetor asiddhaṃ āśaṅkya^ āha prakhyāsvarūpam hi cittasattvam iti| hiśabdaḥ prasiddhāvadyotanārthaḥ| prasiddham hi loke śāstre ca sarvāvabhāsakatvaṃ cittasya| cittam eva sattvaṃ cittasattvaṃ sattvapradhānaguṇapariṇāmatvāt ||

[10,28] atha vā^ anyasaṃsargaviyogābhyām anekavṛtti khyātimātraṃ ca cittaṃ spaṣṭaṃ darśayituṃ śakyata^ iti bravīti prakhyāsvarūpam hi cittasattvam iti||

[11,11] idānīṃ vṛttayo vyākhyāyante| tāsāṃ cānekatvaṃ rajas-tamasor udbhāvibhavanimittaṃ viruddhatvaṃ cety āha rajas-tamobhyāṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ sampradhānābhyāṃ yadā saṃsṛṣṭaṃ tadānīṃ aiśvaryaviṣayapriyaṃ bhavati|| 
prītiḥ rāgaḥ aiśvaryaviṣayarāgayuktā vṛttayo bhavantīty arthaḥ||

[11,15] tat tamasānuviddhaṃ|| tad eva prakhyāsvarūpam cittaṃ yadā guṇabhūtarajaskena^ udbhūtena tamasā^ anuviddhaṃ tadānīm adharmādicatuṣṭayaviṣayā vṛttayaḥ kliṣṭā jāyante|| 

[11,17] tad eva prakṣīṇamohāvaraṇām iti|| nyag-bhūtatamaskaṃ tamaḥ-kṣayād eva jaladhrāpagamād iva (pari) ravibambaṃ sarvataḥ 
pradyotamānaṃ rajomātrayā rajo-leśenānuviddhaṃ yadā tadā dharmādyupagataṃ| dharmādicatuṣṭayaviṣayā vṛttayo^ akliṣṭā bhavanti|| 

[11,20] yadā tad eva rajo[leśa]malāpetaṃ svarūpapratiṣṭhaṃ kevalena svena 
prakhyārūpeṇāvasthitaṃ tadā sattvapuruṣānyathākhyātimātraṃ bhavati| sattvaṃ cittaṃ puruṣo bhaktā tayor anyatā viviktatā tatkhyātiḥ tadavagamaḥ tanmātragrahaṇaṃ kleśādyabhāvapradarśanārtham| dharmameghadhyānopagaṃ bhavati dharmamegho nāma samādhiḥ|| 

[11,24] tad eva prasaṃkhyānabalād rajas-tamasī tiraskṛtya kevalena khyātyātmanā puruṣasvarūpamātradarśanenāvasthānaṃ dhyānaṃ 
prasaṃkhyānam ity ācakṣate dhyāyino yoginaḥ||

[11,26] evaṃ vṛttisvarūpe vyākhyāte tannirodhapradarśanārtham āha citiśaktir ity ādi|| citir eva śaktiḥ citiśaktiḥ| yathā pacyādayah śaktayaḥ śaktimadapekṣāprāpaṇīyajanmāno na svayaṃśaktayaḥ| citiḥ punaḥ svayam eva śaktiḥ nārthāntaram apekṣate| (12,7)tena nityāvasthitatvam| citiśabdasya cinmātrābhidhyāyinaḥ śaktiśabdena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ avikṛtarūpāyā eva citer viṣayitvapradarśanārtham||

[12,7] yasmād evam ato^ apariṇāminī| vyavasthite dharmiṇi dharmāntaratiro-bhāvena dharmāntaraprādur-bhāvaḥ pariṇāmaḥ| na 
pariṇāmituṃ śīlam asyā ity apariṇāminī| ata^ evāpratisaṅkramā pariṇāmina eva cittāder viṣayādau pratisaṅkramadarśanāt||

[12,13] ata^ eva darśitaviṣayā darśito^ antaḥkaraṇena viṣayo^ asyā iti| tenaiva śuddhā| ata^ evānantā deśataḥ kālataś ca| pūrvapūrvam uttarasya^ uttarasya hetutvena draṣṭavyam| vaidharmyadṛṣṭāntaḥ cittam indriyāṇi ca| sattvaguṇātimikā sattvam eva guṇaḥ sattvaguṇaḥ tasya^ iva^ ātmā svarūpam avabhāso yasyāḥ sā^ iyaṃ sattvaguṇāatmakā^ avabhāsasvarūpā^ ity arthaḥ| vṛttyaviśiṣṭavṛttitvād vā sattvaguṇātmikā^ ity ucyate|| 

[12,18] yadi vā khyātyā saṃbandhyate khyātiḥ sattvaguṇātmikā^ iti| ataḥ citiśakter uktalakṣaṇāyā viparītā vilakṣaṇā vivekakhyātiḥ pariṇāmādimatī||

[12,20] yasmāt puruṣād utkṛṣṭāt pariṇāmādiguṇarahitān nikṛṣṭā priṇāmādiguṇayuktā^ ataḥ svarūpadoṣadarśanāt tasyāṃ viraktaṃ aparaktaṃ tām api khyātiṃ ātmano niruṇāddhi|| 

[12,23] tad avasthaṃ nirodhāvasthaṃ saṃskāropagaṃ saṃskāramātrāvaśeṣam| niruddhāsu vṛttiṣu vṛttijanitāḥ saṃskārā evāvaśiṣyante| etasyāṃ nirodhabhūmau yaḥ samādhiḥ sa nirbījaḥ| nirgataṃ bījam atra kleśādibījaṃ sarvam utsannam asminn iti| tasmād asaṃprajñātasamādhilakṣaṇārthaṃm eva sūtram ity upasaṃharati sa yogaś cittavṛttinirodha^ iti||1.2||

[12,25] yogaś cittavṛttinirodha^ ity uktaṃ tatra boddhṛtvena^ eva puruṣasadbhāvādhigamaḥ viṣayabhūtavṛttinirodhe ca viṣayiṇo^ api boddhuḥ puruṣasya nirodhaṃ kaścid āśaṅketā^ api tathā ca sati tatkaivalyaprāptyupāyasyāpi vivekakhyāter anarthakatvaṃ manvīta  (13,10) yogānuśāsanasyāpi tadarthasya niṣphalatvaṃ pratipadyeta tasmāt puruṣasya vṛttinirodhād anirodhaṃ darśayitu-kāmaḥ khyāteś cāpi phalaṃ sākṣād darśayann āha tadavasthe cetasītyādi||

[13,13] tadavasthe---nirodhāvasthe| svaviṣayābhāvāt---svaviṣayaś cittavṛttis tadabhāve[abhāvāt]| buddhibodhātmā buddhiṃ vṛttirūpena pariṇatāṃ budhyata iti buddher boddhā puruṣaḥ| tadbodhanam eva hi puruṣasya svaṃ rūpaṃ, nānyo boddhā, nānyad boddhanam| boddhuś ca bodhanād anyatve sati vikriyātmakatā syāt| tataś ca darśitaviṣayatvaṃ ca na syāt tathā ca karaṇāntarāpekṣitvaṃ puruṣasya prasajyate| tasmād bodhanaṃ boddhṛtvaṃ copacaritaṃ tadvṛttisārūpyeṇa| tathā ca vakṣyati draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ iti| tasmād āha buddhibodhanam evātmā svarūpam yasya sa buddhibodhātmā puruṣaḥ kiṃsvabhāvaḥ kiṃ naśvarasvabhāvaḥ sadbhāve vā kiṃsvabhāvaḥ kathaṃ vā sadbhāva iti|| tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpe^ .avasthānam|| yadā niruddhā vṛttayaḥ svarūpapratiṣṭhā tadānīṃ citiśaktiḥ| svarūpapratiṣṭhety arthaḥ| yathā kaivalye|| tadā sadbhāvaṃ tu na tatsvābhāsaṃ dṛṣyatvāt ity evamādinā vakṣyati| tatra siddhe sadbhāve tata ākṛṣya prāptāśaṅkānivṛttyartham ucyate svarūpapratiṣṭhā^ iti|| 

[13,25] siddhavadvyākhyātam sūtraṃ tatra^ idaṃ prasaktam itaratra na svarūpapratiṣṭhā^ iti| anyathā hi tadā^ iti viśeṣaṇānarthakyaṃ syāt| na ced anyatra svarūpapratiṣṭhā tatrāvasthāntarayogāt pariṇāmitvād adoṣaḥ prāpnotīti tat-parijihīrṣaya^ āha vyutthānacitte tu sati tathā^ api bhavantīti| [anena] anyatrāpi svarūpapratiṣthatvaṃ darśayati||

[14,9] na tathā^ ity anena tadā^ iti viśeṣaṇasyārthavattvaṃ khyāpayati||3||

[14,10] itaras tu tathā^ api bhavanty anena cet svarūpapratiṣṭhatvam ucyate na tathā^ iti ca svarūpapratiṣṭhatvaniṣedhaś ced ekasya vastunas tathābhāvaś cātathā-bhāvaś ca virudhyata^ iti vīkṣāpannaḥ pṛcchati kathaṃ tarhi iti|| itara^ āha vṛttisārūpyam itaratra^ iti|| kasmāt punar vṛttisārūpyam ? darśitaviṣayatvāt || ubhayatra svarūpapratiṣṭhatvāviśeṣe^ api vṛttisārūpyāsārūpyakṛto viśeṣa^ iti, na tathābhāvātathābhāvavirodhaḥ||

[14,15] nanu ca vṛttisārūpyayoge sati^ avasthāntarayogāt pariṇāmitvādidoṣaḥ prāpnoty eva| na| darśitaviṣayatvena parihṛtatvāt| cittavṛttyadhyāropitaṃ hi tat, na svataḥ sphaṭikādyupadhānoparogavat| vyutthāna^ iti|| nirodhād anyatra yaś cittasya kliṣṭādayo vṛttayaḥ tābhir aviśiṣṭā sadṛśī vṛttiḥ svarūpam asya, cittavṛttivyatirekeṇa svarūpavtyatirekeṇa ca puruṣasya vṛttyabhāvāt sadā jñātaviṣayatve pariṇāmānupapatteś ca||

[14,21] tathā ca pūrvācāryasūtram ekam eva darśanaṃ buddhipuruṣayoḥ| kiṃ tad ity āha khyātir eva darśanam| buddhivṛttir eva darśanam| khyāyate puruṣeṇa^ iti ca khyātiḥ khyāte 'nayā ca buddhipuruṣayoḥ svarūpam^ iti khyātiḥ| sā^ evārthākāratayā [arthākāro 'nayā] gṛhyata^ iti karaṇaṃ svena ca khyātirūpeṇa gṛhyata^ iti karma ca bhavati| tathā dṛśyata^ iti darśanaṃ, dṛśyate 'neneti ca [jñāyata^ iti jñānaṃ ] jñāyate 'neneti ca sarvam evamādi drṣṭavyam||

[14,27] yat tu khyātikartṛtve cittasya tad eva jñāyate jānāti ceti (jñānād iti ceti) jñānāntarakalpnā-vaiyarthyaprasaṅga^ iti tasya 'na tatsvābhāsaṃ dṛśyatvāt' ity atraiva parihāraṃ vakṣyāmaḥ||

[15,4] nanu ca yadi cittaparvijṛmbhitam evedaṃ sarvaṃ paruṣaś ced udāste katham asau bhokteti ? na kārakavaicitryāt| kānicid vyāpriyamāṇāni kārakāṇi, kānicid vyāpārād ṛte sannidhimātreṇa kartṛtvaṃ pratipadyante||

[15,7] yathā pacatīty adhiśrayaṇādiṣu vyāpriyamāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ kartety ucyate| sthālī sambhavadhāraṇābhyām avyāpriyamṇaiva pacatīty ucyate| tathā sthālī-madhyagatam ākācam avakācaṃ dadāti, na tasya vyāpāraṃ pratīmaḥ||

[15,10] yathā ca rājā darśanamātreṇa^ eva kartā| sarvāṇi ca kārakāṇi sveṣu vyāpāreṣu kartYṇi| na ca^ ādityaḥ prakāśayan karaṇāntaram apekṣate vyāpriyate vā| na hi prakāśanam avidyamānam utpādayati gamanādivat| prakāśātmatā^ eva hi tasya sadbhāvaḥ tathāpi dṛśimātreṇa ghaṭādīnāṃ prakāśarūpeṇābhivyakteḥ prakāśayatīty ucyate, evam ihāpi dṛśimātreṇa puruṣeṇa dṛśyamānāṃ cittavṛttīnāṃ cidātmanā vyāpyamānatvāt draṣṭā puruṣa itīmam arthaṃ darśayati cittam ayaskāntamaṇikalpam iti||

[15,16] yasyāpy ātmamanas-saṃyogāt jñānam utpadyate tasyāpi jñānena jñeyaṃ vyāpnuvan nātmā jñātety ucyate, na vyāpriyamānaḥ| jñānasyāpi [jñānam api] guṇatve sati niṣkriyatvād ātmana^ evārthaṃ darśayati| nāpi kriyān abhyupagamāt jānātītyādi na^ upapadyate| jñānena hi jñeyaṃ vyāpnivan jānātīty ucyate||

[15,20] kiṃ cānyat ātmamanas-saṃyogād apūrvajñānotpattau saty ātmamanas-taj-jñānaṃ na manasaḥ| ātmā^ eva jñnāti na mana^ iti ko hetur bhavet ? athāpi syāt ātmanaḥ samavāyikāraṇatvād iti| na^ etad evaṃ asiddhatvāt| yathā^ eva jñānam ātmana^ iti sādhyaṃ evam āātmana^ eva samavāyikāraṇattvaṃ na manasa^ iti sādhyam anātmasvarūpatve saty ubahayasaṃyogajatvābhyupagamāt||

[15,25] athāpi syāt jñānāhitasaṃskārasyātma[naḥ]smṛtyekakartṛtvena pratisandhānadarśanād iti na tasyāpi sādhyatvāt| tatrāpi jñānasaṃskārasmṛtipratisandhānādīny ātmana^ eva na manasa^ iti sādhanīyāni| phalena samānāśrayatvād icchādīnām iti cet na tatrāpi tulyatvāt| saṃyogajatve saty ātmanaḥ phalecchādīni na manasa^ iti duḥsādham eva||

[16,4] kiṃ cāsya jñānasyāpi jñeyatvābhyupagamād anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ| na hi jñānam anyena karaṇabhūtena jñānena vijñātuṃ śakyaṃ tad apy anyena tad apy anyenety aniṣṭhaprasaṅgāt||

[16,6] athānavasthāprasaṅgaparihārāya dūram api gatvā kiṃcij jñānam ādyaṃ vā jñānāntareṇa na gṛhyate svayam ātmanā^ eva gṛhyata^ iti tathā jñānavad arthasyāpi svayaṃ grahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ||

[16,8] atha dīpavat svayam avabhāsate cāvabhāsayati ca avabhāsātmakatvāt arthasyānavabhāsātmakatvād adoṣa^ iti cet tathāpi 
prakāśasvarūpeṣv artheṣu vijñānenāgrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ||

[16,11] atha jjānasya^ ātmasamavetatvāvabhāsanarūpābhyāṃ svayaṃ grahaṇaṃ bahiḥprakāśātmakānāṃ tu jñānaviparītadharnatvād adoṣa^ iti cet tac ca na yadi ca^ ātmasamavetatvaṃ prakāśātmakatvaṃ ca jñānasya svayaṃgrahaṇe hetuḥ syāt sarve sarvajjāḥ prāpnuvanti||

[16,14] atha grāhyākāratvam api kāraṇaṃ cet tac ca nānyasya jñānasyānyākāratayā karaṇatvaṃ yathā tathā jñānasyāpi grāhyasyānyākāreṇānyad eva jñānaṃ karaṇaṃ syāt tathā cānavasthā duṣpariharā^ eva||

[16,17] kiṃcānyat na hi kaścit svayaṃgrāhyagrāhakatvaprasādhanāya dṛṣṭānto vidyate pradīpasyāpi cakṣur-ādigrāhyatvāt| na cāpy aṃśadvayam asti jñānasya niravayavatvāt| saty api cāṃśadvaye tayoḥ 
prakāśātmakatvān nānyonyagrāhyagrāhakabhāvaḥ pradīpayor iva dvayoḥ||

[16,21] kiṃ cānyat jñānasya grāhyagrāhakatvābhyupagame saty ātmasadbhāvo api dussampāda^ eva| tasmād arthākārajñānasmṛtipratisandhānaprayotnecchā-ādīnām anātmadharmatvaṃ grāhyatvāt rūpādīnāṃ iva| parārthatvāc ca saṅghāt-āśrayatvaṃ rūpādinidarśanena^ eva| tathā^ āśrayavattvād anityatvāc ca 
prayatrapūrvakatvāc cety evamādibhyo^ anātmadharmatvasiddhiḥ||

[16,25] ayaskānatamaṇikalpam|| ayakāntamaṇir eva| yathā^ ayakāntamaṇir ayasaḥ svātmavyāptiṃ pratyupakaroti sannidhimātraṇa tathā cittam api citsvarūpasyātmanaḥ svātmavyāptiṃ pratyupakaroti dṛśyatvena||

[17,9] nartakīva darśitaviṣayatvād antaḥkaraṇenety uktam tatra darśayitṛtvaṃ cittasya draṣṭṛtvaṃ ca puruṣasya kiṃkṛtam ? svasvāmitvakṛtam| kathaṃ punaḥ svasvāmitvam ? vastu-svabhāvatvāt| katham it ced uktaṃ cittam ayaskāntamaṇikalpam iti|| atha cittavṛttīnāṃ bodhe ko hetuḥ puruṣasya^ iti ? ucyate yasmād evaṃ sannidhimātropakāri cittaṃ dṛśyatvena tasmāc cittavṛttīnāṃ bodhe puruṣasyāyaṃ hetuḥ bhavati yo 'sāv anādisambandaḥ|| 4||

[17,15] tāḥ punar niroddhavyāḥ bahutve api sati cittasya yābhir vṛttibhiḥ vyutthāne sārūpyam uktaṃ puruṣasya| kasmāt punaḥ niroddhavyā ity āha yato vṛttayaḥ kliṣṭākliṣṭāḥ||

[17,18] nanu ca bahutve sati na śakyā niroddhum ity ata^ āha pañcatayya iti| yady api kliṣṭākliṣṭā vṛttayo 'nantāḥ tathāpi pañcatayyaḥ pañcavidhāḥ 
pañcakulā eva| tataś ca 
pañcaprakāravṛttipratipakṣabhūtābhyāsavairāgyaprayogād eva nirodhopapatteḥ pratyavayavanirodhasādhanāprayojakatvād vṛttīnāṃ tad bahutve na nirodhācakyatvaprasaṅgaḥ| kleśahetukāḥ kleśanimittāḥ| avidyādipañcakleśaprayuktaṃ hi cittaṃ vṛttibhir ātmānaṃ punaḥ punar āvedayati| karmācayapracaye karmāṇi kuśalākuśalavimiśrāṇi tāny evāphalapradānāc cherata^ iti karmāśāyaśabdavācyāni teṣāṃ pracaya itaretaraguṇapradhānabhāvena saṃhananaṃ tasmin karmāśayapracaye nimitte^ avidyādikṣetrībhūtāḥ kliṣṭāśrayā ity arthaḥ| satīṣu hi tāsu karmāśayo vipākābhimukhī-bhavati||

[17,27] khyātiviṣāyāḥ khyātyāspadāḥ| guṇānām sattvādīnām adhikāraḥ saṃskārapravṛttiḥ tadvirodhinyaḥ| khyātiviṣayatvād eva tā^ ākliṣṭāḥ apavargahetutvāt||

[18,11] atha kliṣṭapaṅktimadhya(m ānu-)janmāno 'kliṣṭā vṛttayaḥ kiṃ kliṣṭā eva kṣīrakubhaprakṣiptāmbubinduvat ? evam akliṣṭapravāhapatitā api kliṣṭāḥ kim akliṣṭā eva ? kiṃ cātaḥ yadi kliṣṭapravāhagatā akliṣṭāḥ kliṣṭāḥ syuḥ tadā vṛttisaṃskārānuvidhāyinī smṛtiḥ tadanurūpa eva vyavahāraḥ| sa cāpi na sidhyati svarūpavyabhicāre| tasmād āha kliṣṭacchidreṣv apy akliṣṭāḥ akliṣṭā eva bhavanti| akliṣṭacchdreṣu^ api kliṣṭāḥ kliṣṭā eva| yasmād evaṃ tasmāt tathā-jātīyakāḥ saṃskārā vṛttibhir eva kriyante||

[18,18] yady api kleśādayo api saṃskāram ārabhante tathāpi vṛttidvārā^ eva teṣām api saṃskāra^ iti vṛttibhir eva^ ity eva kārārthaḥ| tathā saṃskārair api vṛttayaḥ svānurūpaṃ kriyanta^ ity evaṃ vṛttisaṃskāracakram aniśam anavaratam āvartate| tad evaṃ bhūtaṃ cittaṃ evaṃ hetu-hetumadvṛttisaṃskāradharmakam| avasitāvakāraṃ uvivṛttāvidyāhetu-pravṛttikaṃ hetu-mātrāvasthatvāt| ātmakalpena 
puruṣakalpena khyātimātreṇa^ eva kañcit(kiñcit) kālam avatiṣṭhate 
pārabdhasaṃskāraśeṣānuvṛttyā| pralayaṃ vā gacchati saṃskāṛāvasānād iti|| 5  ||

[18,25] kāḥ punas tāḥ kliṣṭākliṣṭāḥ pañcakārā vṛttaya^ ity āha 
pramāṇaviparyayavikalpanidrāsmṛtaya^ iti||

[18,27] etāvatya^ eva vṛttayaḥ| tatra pratyakṣānumānāgamāḥ pramāṇāni|| 
pramāṇākhyā vṛttis tridhā^ eva bhidyate| tatra pramāṇākhyāyāś cittavṛtteḥ 
prathamo bhedaḥ (19,5) pratyakṣam| atas tal-lakṣaṇam evābhidhīyate tat-pūrvakatvāt bhedadvayasya|| indriyapranāḍikayā|| indriyam iti śrotrādipañcakaṃ na karmendriyaṃ tasya cittavṛttyahetutvāt| kriyārthaṃ hi tat| tasmād indriyaśabdasya sāmānyārthatve api cittavṛtter upavyācikhyāsitatvāc cchrotrādiviṣāyatā^ eva| vṛttyarthatvāc ca śrotrādīnām| indriyam eva pranāḍikā dvāraṃ śabdādyākāravṛttirūpeṇa parigamānasya cittasya| atas tat tena^ indriyadvāreṇa sāmānyaviśeṣātmakabāhyavastvākāratayā pariṇamamānam uparajyate| tasya taduparāgād hetoḥ cittasya yā mudrāpratimudrāvat vṛttiḥ sāmānyaviśeṣātmakavastūparoge api viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānā sā^ eva pratykṣaṃ pramāṇām||

[19,13] atha svaviṣayasāmānyaviśeṣāvabhāsanasāmarthaye śrotrādīnāṃ cittasya ca kiṃkṛtaṃ viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānyam iti ? 19,15 ucyate viśeṣapratibandhatvāt hānopādānādīnāṃ viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānanatety āha [-pradhānanateti]| na sāmānyaṃ nāvadhāryate, guṇabhūtaṃ tu gṛhyata eva| yathā nīlaṃ rūpam iti nīlāvadhāraṇapradhanye saty api rūpameva nīlam iti guṇabhūtam avadhārayati| yathā duḥkhaśabdaḥ sukhaśabdo mūḍhaḥ śabda^ iti| tathā ca viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānā vṛttir iti hy upapadyate||

[19,20] kiṃ ca saṃśayaviparyayanimittatvāc ca sāmānyoplabdheḥ| sāmānye hy upalbdhe bubuhutsitaviśeṣasya saṃśayo jāyate samupalbdhasāmānyasmṛter vā viparyayaḥ samadhigataviśeṣasya tu saṃśayaviparyayau na sthitim upāśnuvāte iti viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānatocyate||

[19,24] yatrāpi sāmānyam evāvadidhārayiṣitaṃ gaur iti vā^ aśva^ iti vā tatrāpy anyataraparityāgenānyatarāvadhāraṇeti viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānatā^ eva| sarvam eva hi vastu vastvantarāpekṣayā sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaś ca||

[19,27] yasya punaḥ sāmānyam avastvadhyāropitam evoṣarāmbuvat nendriyaviṣayam iti tasya pratiniyatasāmānyādhyāropaṇānupapattiḥ| na hy anupalabdhapūrva-(20,1)mukhye saṃbhavaty adhyāropaṇā smṛtyasaṃbhavāt| na kadācid adṛṣṭamukhyodakasya salilādhyāropaṇam ūṣareṣu vidyate| athāsmarann apy adhyāropayet rūpasvalakṣaṇe śabdatvādy apy adhyasyet| athendriyāntaraviṣayatvād rūpasvalakṣaṇe śabdatvādy adhyāso viruddha iti cet nāvastutve saty aviśeṣāt| viśeṣābhyupagame ca vastutvaprasaṅgaḥ| śabdasvalakṣaṇād iva rūpasvalakṣaṇasya||

[20,6] kiṃ cānyat deśakālānyaviśiṣṭapratyakṣā[na]abhyupagame samastalokavyavahāravilopaḥ syāt| na hīdam aham amuṣminn avakāśe kāle cāsminn adrākṣam etasmān nimittād evaṃ-viśeṣaṇam iti ca smṛtyupapattiḥ| na ca pratyakṣa^adṛṣṭeṣu viśeṣyavad viśeṣaṇeṣu smṛtiḥ saṃbhavati na ca kalpanāpoḍhena vyavahāragocarātīten vyavaharamāṇo dṛśyate| tasmāl lokaprasiddhapratyakṣānusaraṇam eva nyāyyam||

[20,11] nanu ca yogināṃ nirvikalpasamādhijaṃ darśanaṃ tasyānevaṃ-lakṣaṇatvāt tathā sukharāgādivijñānasya cānindriyapranāḍī-pūrvatvāt pramāṇāntaratvam abhyupetavyaṃ 
pratyakṣatvaṃ vā vaktavyam| ucyate| pratyakṣasya (na pramāṇasya) 
puruṣapratyayāpekṣatvena parihṛtatvāt| na hy apratyayā vṛttiḥ 
pratyakṣam| caitanyodayahetor eva sapratyayāyāḥ pratyakṣatvam| tathā cāha "phalaṃ tadaviśiṣṭaḥ pauruṣeyaś cittavṛttibodha" iti|| evaṃ ca sati sukharāgādijñānasya kliṣṭākliṣṭarūpasya tadaviśiṣṭapuruṣapratyayaphalāvasānatvāt pratyakṣatā siddhā^ eva||

[21,1] indriyapranāḍikāgrahaṇaṃ laukikapratyakṣodbhavānuvāda^ eva| anyathā hīndriyanirapekṣasya yogīśvarayor jñānasyāpratyakṣatā syāt| asti hi kleśādipratibandhābhāve sarvārthena cittasattvena sarvaviṣayeṇa yugapatsūkṣmavyavahitādigrahaṇam| tathā "tārakaṃ sarvaviṣayaṃ sarvathāviṣayam akramaṃ ceti vivekajaṃ jñānam " iti | tasmān nāvadhāryate^ indriyapranāḍikayaivety||

[21,6] bāhyavastu-uparāgād iti viparyayanivṛttyartham| viśeṣāvadhāraṇapradhānatvena saṃśayanivṛttir iti||

[21,8] tatra keṣāṃ cit phalaṃ tad eva pramāṇam| apareṣāṃ tu pramāṇād anyatra [anyat] pramāṇavyaktyantare [antaram]| yathā ghaṭavijñānasya 
pramāṇāsya hānopādānopekṣā-buddhayaḥ phalam iti guṇadoṣatad ubhaya(vad abhava)śūnyatvaviṣayatvād upādānādibuddhīnāṃ 
pramāṇabhūtaghaṭavijñānād atyantabhedaḥ tataś cānyaviṣayasya 
pramāṇasyānya(prameyasya) viṣayapramāṇa(ṇe )vyaktyantaraṃ(-re) phalam iti||

[21,13] na hi khadiracchedanasya khadiropādānaṃ tat-parityāgo vā phalaṃ bhavituṃ yuktam| viyogasambandhaphalatvāt tyāgopādānayoḥ| na ca
phalasya phalaṃ sambhavati| dvaidhī-bhāvaphalavyavahitatvāc ca||

[21,16] kiṃ ca phalasya cet phalaṃ kriyā-anapekṣāprasaṅgaḥ| phalārthaṃ hi kriyā-apekṣyate| tac cet phalaṃ vināpi kriyayā phalād api sidhyati, ato hy [tato na hy] anekāyasāpekṣitaniṣpattikriyām anutiṣṭhet| sādhanānveṣaṇānupapattiś ca| kriyāyā^ abhāvāt||

[22,4] nāpi kriyā-eva phalaṃ kriyā-anupraramaprasaṅgāt| phalāvasanā api [hi] kriyā| kriyāyāś ca phalatve phalānabhilāṣitvaprasaṅgaḥ| tasyā duḥkhatvāt||

[22,6] tasmānnityā na pramāṇasvarūpam^ eva phalam nāpi tadvyatiriktapramāṇāntaram ity āha phalaṃ tadaviśiṣṭa^ ity ādi| tadaviśiṣṭaḥ tayā pramāṇākhyayā vṛttyā^ aviśiṣṭaḥ tulyaḥ tadākāratvena, 
pauruṣeyaḥ puruṣasyāyaṃ bodha^ iti| puruṣasya hi vṛttyākāratā| tasmānnityā nākriyājaṃ dravyasvarūpamātraṃ phalaṃ| kiṃ tarhi dravyasyaivāvasthāntaraviśeṣa[ḥ ]-kriyājā[jaḥ ]-kriyāparyavasānakālaḥ kriyā-uparamopapatteḥ phalam| phalaniṣpattāv eva hi kriyā-uparama upapadyate| evaṃbhūtasya mukhyaphalatve 
pradhānakriyāviprakṛṣṭāyāḥ[vyavahita-]kriyāyā dravyaṃ dravyasya vā kriyā 
phalam ity anyonyapratyupasthāpakatvena pradhānaphalābhimukhīkaraṇāt 
phalatvopacāro na virotsyate||

[22,14] nanu ca bhavato apy upacritam eva phalam vṛttyaviśiṣṭāyāḥ 
phalatvenābhupagamāt, puruṣasya ca śuddhatvāt, tadākāratayā^ anṛtatvam| na hi sphaṭikamaṇer alaktakopadhānākāratā satyā||

[22,17] bāḍham tathāpi tv etad eva phalaṃ mukhyam, dṛṣṭatvāt| mukhyaphalāntarādarśanāt| sarvasyaiva kriyākārakajātasya etatphalārthatvāt| etadavasānaṃ hi tat sarvam| tathā ca pradarśitam||

[22,20] nanu ca mithyā-bhūtasya eva mukhyaphalatvaṃ vṛttyākārasya, tathā ca tadabhilāṣānupapattiḥ| bāḍham mithyā-bhūta eva vṛttyanugato bhogaḥ| tathā cāha --- "sattvapuruṣayor atyantāsaṃkīrṇayoḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ" iti|| bhāṣyam api "tayoḥ svarūpopalmbhe sati kuto bhoga" iti|| ata eva ca samyagdarśanād ātyantokī nivṛttir upapadyate||

[22,25] yeṣām amithyā phalaṃ teṣāṃ vairāgyakāraṇābhāvāt, samyagdarśanānveṣaṇānupapatter mokṣābhāvaḥ| na ca kriyāpariniṣpādyo mokṣaḥ, anityatvaprasaṅgāt||

[23,4] atha buddhiboddhṛtvaṃ puruṣasya sattvaṃ cāsiddham iti cet āha "pratisaṃvedī boddheḥ puruṣa^ ity upariṣṭāt pravediyiṣyāma" iti||

[23,6] ye ca^api^indriy^arthasannikarṣasya pratyakṣatvam abhyupayanti, na teṣām api samyag-abhyupetaṃ, sannikarṣasya kriyāphalatvāt| sannikarṣaś ca saṃyogaḥ sa ca kriyājas tair iṣyate| yato yad^ upajāyate tat tasya 
pramāṇam| tac ca tasya phalaṃ yad^ upajāyate||

[23,9] phalam api sannikarṣī jñānaṃ prati pramāṇam iti cet tac ca na, jñāpakapramāṇaprastāve kārakapramāṇa^upanyāsasya^ayuktatvāt| sannikarṣī hi jñānasya^utpādako na tasya jñāpakaḥ| prameyādhigamāya hi pratyakṣādīny yas tāni, na jñāna^utpattikāraṇam| tat prakriyāyāma(yānya)prakṛtaṃ 
prakriyeta||

[23,13] atha^api syāt, jñānasya^api prameyatvāt tac ca^apy utpadyamānaṃ 
pramīyata^ iti, tadytpattikāraṇasya sannikarṣasya jñāpakatvam eva^ iti, tac ca na, jñānaṃ jñanena^ eva jñāyeta, teṣāṃ tathā^abhyupagamāt| na ca sannikarṣo jñānam| jñāna^utpādakatvena^abhyupagamāt||

[23,16] atha jñānasya jñāne sannikarṣo nimittatvāt pramāṇam iti ced adṛṣṭa^īśvara^icchā-viṣayanimeṣādīnām api nimittatvāt pramāṇatve kaḥ pradveṣaḥ ? na ca jñānasya jñeyatve^ ātmanaś ca svabhāvacid-rūpatv^anabhyupagame śakyā^ anavasthā parihartum||

[23,19] athātmasamavetamātratayaiva jñānasya jñeyatā, na jñāna^antaram apekṣyate yena^atiprasajyeta^iti cet, asya^apy anyat pratyakṣalakṣaṇaṃ vācyam anindriyasannikarṣādilakṣaṇatvād ātmani jñānasamavāyatvasya| sannikarṣasya ca jñāna^utpattiphalatvāt tadutpattivyavadhāne sati tasya jñānasya na^ānantaryam||

[23,23] tasmāt tad ānantaryeṇāpi na sannikrṣaphalatvaṃ jñānasya| tathā hānādibuddhīnām api rūpādijñānaphalatva^anupapattir uktā||

[23,25] vyabhicārāc ca| yathā^ekasminn aṅganādipiṇḍhaviṣaye jñāne samāne bahūnām utpanne kasyacid upādānabuddhiḥ, anyasya heyabuddhiḥ, kasyacid ubhaya^abhāvaḥ tatra hāna^upādānabuddhir vyabhicarati| anyataratra^ekam eva jñānaṃ tad eva^uhayaṃ vyabhicaraty upekṣakasya| yac ca yad vyabhicarati na tat tasya phalam||

[24,1] kiṃ ca, hāna^upādāna^bhāvamātratvād abhāva^ eva^upekṣā| na ca^abhāvaḥ pramāṇasya vatunaḥ phalaṃ bhavitum arhati| rādadveṣābhyāṃ ca^upādānaparityāgabuddhī| tadabhāvāt kāraṇa^abhāve kārya^abhāva^ iti pramāṇavijñāne saty api hāna^upādānabuddhyabhāva^ eva^upekṣa^iti vyabhicārān na hāna^ādibuddhiphalatvam||

[24,5] kiṃ ca, rāgadveṣatadabhāvānām apy upādānādibuddhiphalatve pramāṇatvaprasaṅgaḥ| pauruṣeyacittavṛttibodhatve tu na kiñcid viruddhyata iti||

[24,7] nanu ca cittavṛtteḥ saṃvedyatvam asiddham| na hi karaṇam eva karma bhavati| na hi dātram eva lūyate| na^eṣa doṣaḥ| kriyākārakayor bhinnadharmatvāt| kriyānivṛttim prati sambhūya kārakāṇi vyāpriyante parasparaṃ viṣayaviṣayibhāvena|  kriyā tu sattāmātreṇa^eva phalahetur iti karaṇatvam asyām upacaryate, na kārakavat| kurvad^ hi kārayed iti kriyā^adhikārakaṃ syān na bhūtaṃ bhavyāya^upadiśyate||

[24,12] kiṃ ca, anavasthāprasaṅgaś ca| kriyā cet karaṇaṃ syāt, tannivṛttyayā ca^anyayā kriyayā bhavitavyam| na hy anyāṃ kriyām antareṇa tasyāḥ kāraṇa<karaṇa>tvaṃ ghaṭate| yā^api ca^anyā sā^api kāraṇaṃ syād anyasyāḥ kriyāyāḥ| sā^apy anyasyā ity anavasthā| tataś ca kriyāsantāna^anuparamāt phala^anabhinivṛttiḥ | tatra^antyā cet kriyā^antareṇa vinā phalahetur bhavet, prathamasya tathātve kaḥ pradveṣaḥ| cittavṛttes tv abhivyañjakatvāt samvedyate 'ty adoṣaḥ||

[24,18] dṛśyate ca^abhivyañjakānāṃ svarūpasaṃvedyatā^eva karaṇatvaṃ pradīpādīnām| tathā cittavṛtter api| cakṣur-ādiṣv abhivyañjakatve api agrahaṇad vyabhicāra^ iti cet, samāna(cetanasamāna) jātīyasaṃkarāt katham||

[24,21] yeṣāṃ tāvad bhautikāni^indriyāṇi, teṣāṃ cakṣuṣas taijasatvāt bāhyena^ālokena tulyajātīyena vyatibhedāt gṛhyamāṇasya^eva^avivekaḥ| yathā pradīpānāṃ bahūnāṃ prabhāvyatibhedāt kasya kā prabhā^iti na vivicyate| tathā śrotrādiṣv api||

[24,24] yeṣām apy abhautikāni, teṣām apy abhivyañjakatvena samānajātīyatā vṛtter abhivyañjakatvād agrahaṇam iti| tan na prāptacaitanya^upagrahatayā 'vibhāvyatvād avyavahitatvāc ca|| sarvagatatve apy ātmanaḥ sūkṣmatvena svaccharatvena ca cittena^avyavadhānāc ca saṃvedyamāna^ eva phalam iti siddham ||

[25,4] yeṣāṃ punaḥ pratyakṣeṇa^asaṃvedyo cittavṛttis teṣāṃ phalagrahaṇapramāṇābhāvaḥ| phale ca gṛgīte jñāna^asiddhatvasya^arthāpattiḥ| na ca phalaṃ gṛhyate pramāṇavyāpāra^anabhyupagamāt| tadabhyupagame ca^anavasthā^prasaṅgaḥ| yat phala^avagrahaṇāya pramāṇam abhyupagamyate tasya^apy anyat phalaṃ tat pramāṇasya^apy anyad iti na kvacid avatiṣṭheta||

[25,8] atha na pramāṇavyāpāras tasya tadā pramāṇa^abhāvāt 
phalasya^asattvaṃ prasaktam| yatra hi sadupalambhakāni pramāṇāni na vyāpāraṃ gacchanti, tan nāstīty abhyupagacchāmaḥ(teṣām)||

[25,11] athāgṛhyamāṇam api tad eva jñānaṃ phalasya^apīti cet, na, abhivyañjakadharmavyatikramāt| na hy abhivyañjakaṃ gṛhyamāṇam abhivyañjayad dṛṣṭam| na hy ulkā giriśikhare api nijaṃ dharmaṃ atikramati||

[25,14] athābhivyañjayad eva phalaṃ praty api pramāṇam iti cet siddhaṃ tarhi^īpsitam asmākam| rāgādīnāṃ vṛttiviśeṣaṇatvena grahaṇa^upapatteḥ| rakto 'haṃ dviṣṭo 'ham iti yady ahaṃ-pratyayaviśeṣaṇaṃ, tad api vṛttibhāva^abhāvitvena^ eva grahaṇād vṛttiviśeṣaṇānāṃ rāgādīnāṃ grahaṇam upapadyate| ā(tenā)nyathā rūpādivan mānāntarāpekṣatvaprasaṅgāt| tataś cānavastheti||

[25,19] tasmāt pramāṇaphalānabhijñānam abhimānamātram eva kevalaṃ 
paṇḍitaṃ manyānām ity upekṣaṇīyam iti||

[25,21] anumānam atha^ucyate^ anumeyasya tulyajātīyeṣv anuvṛtta^ iti| anumeyasya [jijñāsita]dharmaviśiṣṭasya| tad dharmavattayā tulyajātīyeṣv anuvṛttaḥ sadbhāvamātreṇa, [bhinnajātīyebhyaḥ] tadviruddhadharmakebhyo vyāvṛttaḥ teṣv asan ity arthaḥ| sambandha^ iti| kaḥ sambandhaḥ ? kayor vā| na hi sambandhināv anāpekṣya sambandhaḥ sambandhatāmiyāt, yad^ asau 
prasiddhavac ca^ucyate ya^ iti||

[25,26] nanu cānumānasya prakṛtatvāt, liṅgaliṅginoḥ sambandhaḥ 
prasiddha^ eva nāgamakatvāt| na kevalaḥ sambandho gamakaḥ| ucyate liṅgasya^ eva sambadho vyāvṛttyanuvṛttyoḥ| kaḥ punar asau ? liṅgam eva| na hi liṅgād anyad anuvṛttaṃ vyāvṛttaṃ ca| tac ca gamakam| nanu liṅgam api na^ eva gamakaṃ 26,5 liṅgisambandhānapekṣaṃ yadi ca^anapekṣitaliṅgisambandhaṃ gamakaṃ, yadvṛttibhūmijayakrameṇa^ ubhayākṣepapūrvako kiñcid gamakaṃ syāt, tasmāt liṅgaliṅgisambandho vaktavyaḥ||

[26,7] na^ uktatvāt|| anumeyasya tulyajātīyeṣv anuvṛttaḥ ity ādinaiva^ uktatvāt| na hy anumeyena^asaṃbaddhaṃ liṅginā tat sadṛśajātīyeṣv anuvartate, bhinnajātīyebhyo vā nivartate| tasmāt ya^ iti ca prasiddham eva saṃbandham anuvadati||

[26,10] tadviṣayā evam anvayavyatirekavośiṣṭasambandiviṣayā vṛttiḥ| sāmānya^avadhāraṇapradhānā liṅgasāmānya^avadhāraṇapradhānā^ anumānam||

[26,12] yathā deśāntaraprāpter gatimac candratārakaṃ caitravad iti|| yady api lakṣaṇa^abhidhānamātraṇa tad abhivyaktih, tatjā^apy udāharaṇapratyudāharaṇapradarśanād atitarām ity udāharaṇapratipādanam| tatra gatimac candratārakam iti candratārakasya gatimattvam anumeyam| tatsumanajātīyeṣv anuvṛttā deśāntaraprāptir gatimatsu caitrādiṣu, bhinnajātīyebhyaś ca^agatibhyo vindhyādibhyo vyāvṛttā^ity avyabhicarantī| nityaṃ gatimatā sambandhāt||

[26,19] bhavatu tāvad aviruddhānāṃ nityasambandhād deśāntaraprāptyādīnāṃ gamyagamakatā, vadhyaghātakādīnāṃ tu sambandh^abhāve katham ity ucyate gamyagamakabhāvaḥ (bhāvvena) ?

[26,21] tatrāpi nityasambandhād adoṣaḥ| na hy atrāpi prāptilakṣaṇaḥ sambandho 'abhipreyate| sāmānyatodṛṣṭā(nu)bhāvaprasaṅgāt| na hi candratārakādiṣu deśāntaraprāpteḥ prāptilakṣāṇasambandhaḥ pratyakṣeṇa gṛhyate| agṛhyamāṇaś ced gamakaḥ sarvaṃ sarvasya gamakaṃ syāt| na hi kiṃcit kenacid asambaddham, ākāśādīnāṃ sarvatra bhāvāt||

[26,25] tasmād ganyagamakabuddhyutpattihetur eva sambandho anumānavisaye| tathā ca badhyaghātakayor apy anyatara(rā)bhāvadarśane 'nyatarabhāvo gamyata^ ity anumānam eva| yasya^apy ucite deśe abhāvadarśanād anyatra bhāvo gamyate| yathā ghātakadarśanena badhyābhāvo 'numīyate| caitrasya jīvato gṛhe^ abhāva(gṛhyabhāva)darśane bahirbhavaś ca^iti||

[27,1] nanu cābhāvārthāpattī^ ete, nānumāne, (na) lakṣaṇāsaṅgateṅ gavādivat||

[27,2] na yathā^ uktalakṣānopapatteḥ badhyaghātakayor virodhalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ| yatra yatra ghātakabhāvas tatra badhyā-bhāva^ eva^ity avinā-bhāvaḥ| na hi badhyasya^abhāvaṃ ghātaka(kā)bhāvo vyabhicarati| badhyasya vā bhāvo ghātakā(ka)bhāvam, agnibhāvam iva dhūmabhāvaḥ||

[27,6] kathaṃ punar avagamyate ghātakabhāvadarśanad eva tad-gocare badhyasya^abhāvabuddhir iti ? na punaḥ sadupalambhakapramāṇānāṃ ghātaka iva badhye vyāpārābhāvād iti||

[27,8] ucyate ghātakabhāve vyāpṛ(pyāvṛ)tapramāṇasya^ eva badhyā-bhāvabuddhiupapattiḥ, dhūmabhāve vyāpṛ(vṛ)tatapramāṇasya badhyaviśeṣābhāvabuddhyutpattiḥ, dhūmaviśeṣavyāpṛ(vṛ)tapramāṇasyeva^agniviśeṣabuddhir iti||

[27,12] abhāvapramāṇavādinas tu sarvatra pramāṇābhāvasya^aviśiṣṭatvād virodhiviśeṣadarśanānuvidhānaṃ virodhyantarābhāvadarśanasya^anupapannaṃ, liṅgaviśeṣa^anuvudhānam iva liṅgiviśeṣadarśanasya| na hi sadupalambhakapramāṇavyāpārābhāvasya kaścid viśeṣaḥ yena viśeṣabuddhis tad viśeṣam anuvidhīyate||

[27,16] atha jajñāsite viśeṣe sadupalambhakapramāṇa^abhāvād iti cet tan nāsataḥ smṛti^icchādisahabhāva^apekṣanupapatteḥ| na hy asat sahāya^apekṣaya kāryaṃ nirvartayad dṛṣṭaṃ śaśa-_Viṣāṇādi| dṛṣṭam iha^ iti cet na vipratipattisthānatve dṛṣṭāntābhāvāt| tulyam iti cet na ghaṭādeḥ sato 'pekṣādarśanāt||

[27,20] na cāpi nirvyāpārasyāvastutvād abhāvasya pramaṇabhāvo yoktaḥ| na hi nirastavyāpārakaṃ kiṃcit kāryārambhakaṃ dṛśyate loke| tasmād vyāpārarūpapramāṇajanitā prameyābhāvabuddhiḥ prameyaviṣayatvat ghaṭādibuddhivat| tathā ca vastutvāt pravṛttinivṛttihetutvāt| vipakṣaḥ śaśaviṣāṇādiḥ| tathā^ abhāva[pramā]pramāṇā(ṇa)bhāvajanitā na bhavati, yathā 'bhihitahetu-dṛṣṭāntadarśanāt(-nena)||

[27,25] ghātakadarśanaṃ badhya^abhāvabuddher liṅgaṃ tad bhāvitvād agnyādibuddhinimittadhūmādibuddhivat saṃśayanivartakatvāc ca| badhyā-bhāvabuddhir vā liṅgajanitā niścayarūpatvād agnibuddhivad iti||

[28,1] iha gaṭo nāstīti kila nānumānaṃ liṅgaliṅgisambandhāgrahaṇāt| na hi yathā^ agnyādibuddheer dhūmadiliṅga^anveṣaṇaṃ tathā iha nāsti ghaṭa^ ity atra liṅgānveṣaṇam asti| prāg eva liṅgānveṣaṇāt buddhyutpatteḥ||

[28,4] na^ eṣa doṣaḥ ihapratyaya^ākārasya^ eva liṅgatvāt| na hi prathamam eva liṅgabuddhu-upajanane sati liṅgānveṣaṇaṃ bhavati| iha^ityākāra^uparañjitā buddhir liṅgam umuparañjakavyatiriktaghaṭādyabhāvabudder ghātakabuddhivad eva| virodhalakṣaṇa^ eva ca^atra^api sambandhaḥ||

[28,8] yatra punar aviruddhāny uparañjakāni tatra^astitvabuddheḥ etena^ eva^arthāpatter anumānatvaṃ vyākhgyātam avinābhāvena sambandhena| tatra^api jīvato hi devadattasya gṛhābhāve bahir-bhāvaḥ, yadā gṛhe bhāvaḥ na tadā bahir-bhāva^ ity anyatareṇānumānam | sākṣāt dṛśyamānabahir-bhāva^abhyantarābhāvatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntaḥ| vṛttyuparañjaka^anuparañjakatvena hy abhāvagrahaṇasya^abhihito hetuḥ||

[28,13] agnyādāv api dhanādiśaktigrahaṇam anumānam eva| kāryanirvartakayor avinā-bhāvasambandhasya^ātmani dṛṣṭatvād bhujyādau| dṛśyate hi pratyagātmani śakto 'ham idaṃ kāryaṃ kartum, aśakto 'ham ity ātmavad ātmaviśeṣaṇasya śakter grahaṇam| śaktinirvartyam eva dahanādikāryam api^ity anumānasiddhiḥ||

[28,17] nanu cābhāvasya pratyakṣatām upagāyanti kecit| yathā kilādarśakena pradīpādinā sati gṛhyamāṇe vastuni na tad avayavini yad upalbhyate tan na^asti^ity evaṃ sataḥ prakarśakaṃ pramāṇam asad api 
prakāśayati| tathā ca^udāharanti^ anaṅkitaṃ vāsa^ ānayety praviśya bhāṇḍāgāram aṅkitānaṅkitavāsas sannidhāv aṅkitavāso-grahaṇena^ eva 
pratyakṣeṇāṅkitābhāvaviśiṣṭaṃ vāsa^ upalabhyanayati| tasmāt pratyakṣa^ evābhāvo lakṣaṇāntara^anupapatter iti||

[28,23] tan na buddhyākāreṇārthāvadhāraṇānṛtatvaprasaṅgāt| yadākārā hi buddhis tasya pratyakṣa^avadhriyamāṇātmatābhyupagamyate| yathā nīlaṃ vastu pītam ity atra^api viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasambandhapratyayo mithyā 
prāpnoti| tadyathā^iha ghaṭo nāsti^itīhapradeśasya ghaṭābhāvena^asati sambandhe sambandhapratyayah, na hi ghaṭābhāvasya^ihapradeśasya cā[prāpta]prāptilakṣaṇaḥ samavāyābhidhānovā sambandhas tair abhyyathā^iha devadatta^ iti| tasmād abhāva(ve)pratyayavā(yā)n nīlaṃ vastv iti viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasambandhapratyayo api sambandhapratyayatvān mithyā prasajyeta||

[29,4] yathā ca^asty api^ihadeśaghaṭa^abhāvasambandhe sabandhapratītis tathā^eva^anīle api vastuny asaty eva nīlatvasambandge nīlatvasambandhapratyayaḥ prāpnoti, tathā sambandhiṣv api| atha^asty eva sambandhaḥ dviṣṭhatā viruddhyeta sambandhasya| abhāvasya^avastutva^abhyupagamāt||

[29,7] atha^apy ekaśataṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthā iti yāvat ṣaṣthyarthāḥ sambandha iṣyeta, tadā dvividha^ eva sambandhaḥ samavāyākhyaḥ samyogākhyaś ca^iti na yujyate||

[29,9] athai(tathai)kaśatasya^api ṣaṣṭhyarthānāṃ ubhayāntaḥpātitvam ucyeta nābhāve 'anyatarasya^apy asambhavāt||

[29,11] kiñcā(ntva)nyat| iha nabhasi kusumaṃ na^asti^iti sambandhino nabhaso apy apratyakṣatvāt gaganakusuma^abhāvasya^apratyakṣatvaṃ 
prāpnoti| atha^apratyakṣatvam eva^iti ced āpatitaṃ gaganakusuma^abhāvavad apratyakṣatvam iha bhū-pradeśe ghaṭa^abhāvasya^api| viśeṣo vaktavyaḥ||

[29,14] atha vyomno 'pratyakṣatvād iti tan na puṣpasya patato nabhasy astitvadarśanād vyoma^apratyakṣatvam ahetuḥ| yathā^ eva nipatatkusumasadbhāve vyoma^apratyakṣatvam ahetus tathā sadbhāvaviruddhagrahaṇe apy ahetuḥ syāt| atha^ākāśāpratyakṣatvād eva^iti cet, asambandham api yat kiṃcid vyavahitāpratyakṣatvādi so api hetuḥ syāt| atha nabhaḥ-kusuma^abhāvaḥ pratyakṣa^ iti cet indriya^arthasannikarṣa^anarthakyaṃ sarvatra prasajyate| tathā ca saty asannikarṣṭa^upalabdheḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvajñatvaṃ prasaktam||

[29,20] atha^anumeyaṃ tatreti cet nabhaḥ kusuma^abhāvavad iha ghaṭābhāvasya^aviśeṣād anumānikatva(kīya)m eva siddham| evaṃ ca saty abhāvapramāṇavādino api vyavahitaviprakṛṣṭodakādāv analādy^abhāvasya^anumeyatvād iha ghaṭābhāvo api tatsāmānyād anumānagrāhya^ eva^ iti niścetuṃ yuktam||

[29,24] vindhyāś cāprāpter agatir iti ca prayogāntaram| agatir vindhya iti pratijñānam| aprāpter iti hetuḥ caitravad iti kākalocanavad ubhayatra sambadhyate| gatimac candratārakam ity anena sādharmyeṇa, vindhyāś cāaprāpter ity anena vaidharmyeṇa sambandhaḥ evaṃ ca saty anvayavyatirekā(ka)darśanakṛto na doṣaḥ||

[30,7] atha vaitasmin prayoge vyatirekaviparyayadarśanena doṣābhāva^ ity upanyastam| lakṣaṇasya ca lakṣyaviśeṣeṇa^ūhyatvād adoṣaḥ|| 

[30,9] āptaḥ parānujighṛkṣāprayukto doṣarahito dṛṣṭānumita^arthaḥ| tena dṛṣṭo vā anumito vārthaḥ paratra śrotṛviśeṣe svabodhasaṅkrāntaye svādhigamasaṃkramaṇāya śabdena śabda^ iti vākyaṃ tato viśiṣṭārthapratipatteḥ| tasmāt tadupadiṣṭād vākyāt| [tadarthaviṣayā] vākyārthaviṣayā yā cittavṛttiḥ sā^ anumānavad eva sāmānyāvadhāraṇapradhānā||

[30,14] ittham āptyādidharmagrahaṇapūrvakā śrotur āgamaḥ na vaktuḥ| dṛṣṭānumita^artho hi vaktā tasya laiṅgakam aindriyakaṃ vā taj-jñānam| śrotur eva tu śravaṇapūrvakaṃ niścitaṃ vijñānaṃ laiṅgikaindriyakavyatiriktam| sa^ āgamaḥ [na] pratyakṣam indriyā[viṣaya]viṣayatvāt yathā-abhihitaliṅgaliṅgisambandhanirapekṣatvāc ca nānumānam| na hi svargāpūrvadevatādiśabdā(bde ')arthānvitaṃ jñānam āsāditaliṅgaliṅgisambandhapurassaram upajāyate| phalaṃ tu sarvatra 
pauruṣeyo vṛttibodha^ eva||

[30,20] yasya^ āgamasya vaktā^ āgama^ākhyavṛttyutpattinimitte śabdoccāraṇāt āgamasya prayogopanyāsena pratipāditaṃ phalaṃ tu pūrvavad eva| yadā^apy eka^ eva prayogas ta(thā)dā^ api sādhyasādhana^abhāvamātrasya vivakṣitatvān na viparītavyatirekadarśanād ity ucyate||

[30,23] aśraddheya^arthavaktrabhihitatvena^āgama^ eva viśiṣyate| katham asāv aśraddheya^artho vaktā^ity ata^ āha na dṛṣṭo 'numito vārthaḥ tena vaktā| śiṣṭaleka^prasiddha^anyathā pratipādanāt buddhārhata^ādiḥ| na hi rāgādidoṣānupahatāntaḥkaraṇaḥ prasiddham anyathā nirvarṇayati| sa āgamaḥ plavate^ ābhāsī-bhavati||

[30,27] yady api pratyakṣa^anumānaviṣaya^ābhāsadarśanaṃ svaśabdena na kṛtaṃ tathā apy āgama^ābhāsa^upadarśanena^ eva pūrvatrāpi yathābhihitalakṣaṇaviparītatvena pratyakṣānumānābhāsādi draṣṭavyam arthād uktam||

[31,1] mūlavaktari tu^ ādyavaktarīśvare tann nimitto ya^ āgamaḥ sa viplavanimittābhāvānnirviplavaḥ syāt| yathā-artha(ya)vaktṛvat||

[31,3] upamānaṃ (mānaṃ) śabdapūrvakaṃ tadāgama^antaḥpātitvān na 
pramṇāntaram| yathā gaur iva gavaya^ iti vanecarabhihatād eva vākyād avadhṛtasādṛśya punar vane gavayadarśane api 
pūrvaśrutavākya^upasthāpitasādṛśyasmaraṇamātram eva nādhikam| sādṛśyaṃ tu vākyād eva pūrvapratipannam||

[31,7] na^api saṃjñāsaṃjñisambandhapratipattita^ eva mānaṃ tasyā^ api tadvākyād evāvagatatvāt| na hi gaur ity etāvatānavagatagavayanāmakasya vanagatagavayadarśino api gavayo 'yam iti saṃjñāsaṃjñisambandhāvabodho bhavati||

[31,10] tasmād gaur iva gavaya^ iti gavayaśabdo api tadvākye 'pekṣitavyaḥ| tathā ca sati pūrvaśrutād eva sarvaṃ pratītam| athāpi syād ayaṃ(dasya)gavaya^ iti na pūrvabuddhiviṣayam iti tad api tatrabhavatām api paricodanaparihāram ava[pa]śyatām api 
pīḍitakaivale[kaule]yakavṛttikoṇo'vasthānam iva lakṣyate||

[31,14] tathā hi deśakālasannikarṣaviprakarṣādibhedenanekopamānabhedaḥ prasajyeta| tathāstv iti cet tat-pūrvadhiyā^ eātatvam antar-nihitasamastaviśeṣam eva hi vā neyau pūrvam upapāditam| na hi gaur ity ukte deśakāla^ādiviśeṣābhāve na go-śabdād arthapratipattiḥ||

[31,17] kiṃ cānyat na hi saṃjñāsaṃjñisambandhapratipattir upamānaṃ yena hy apamīyate tadupamānaṃ sādṛśyajñānaṃ hi tat| na hi nāmināmasambandhapratipattyā kiṃcid upamīyate^ uttarakālatvāt| avadhṛtasādṛśyasya hi paścād ā(sya)yaṃ gavaya^ ity evaṃ so api nipatati||

[31,21] evam tarhi syād aśabdapūrvakaṃ pramāṇāntaraṃ yathā gavayadarśanād etat sadṛśo gaur iti grāmīṇasya buddhiḥ tad api na 
prameyābhāvāt| sati hi prameye sadupalmbhakatva^abhimatasya pramāṇasya vyāpāraḥ na hi go-gavayayor vyāvṛttam anuvṛttaṃ ca vastutaḥ sādṛśyaṃ nāmaikam asti||

[31,25] nanu ca sādṛśyabuddhir asty eva nāsau nirālambanā bhavitum arhati| satyam evaṃ tatra^ eva vicāraṇā kim asau vanayūthādipratyayavat ? atha ghaṭādibuddhivad iti| kim cātaḥ yadi vanapratyayavad bhavet tadā yathā^ eva viśiṣṭasanniveśānugatā mahīruhā eva^ ātmavyatirekeṇāvidyamānavanapratyayahetavas tathā^ eva go-gavayāvayavā eva dvayadarśanasmṛtyapekṣayā svātmavyatirekeṇāsataḥ sādṛśyasya 
pratītau kāraṇam iti prāptam avastutvam||

[32,1] atha punar ghaṭādipratyayavad vastutvaṃ pramāṇatvaṃ ca tadā syād iti||

[32,2] tatra kīdṛśaṃ vastv iti vicāryate 32,3  kiṃ go-gavayayor anuvṛttam ekaṃ sādṛśyaṃ gotvagavayatvābhyām anyat ? te^ eva vānyonyaviśiṣṭe yadi vā tābhyāṃ bhinne go-gavayādhāre dve sādṛśye parasparānupekḍe, sāpekṣe vā ? 32,6  kiṃ vyaktir eva sānyatarajātiviśiṣṭā vā kiṃ jātiḥ sācānyataravyaktiviśiṣṭā vyaktidvayaviśiṣṭā vā sambandho vaiteṣāṃ yathā-vikalpayogam ity ekaikānyonyaviśiṣṭaviśeṣaṇatvādinā bhūyāṃso 'nye api vikalpā udbhāvayitavyāḥ| tatra yady ekaṃ sādṛśyam ubhayagataṃ tadā na^upameyatvam ekatvāt yathā gaur iva gaur iti||

[32,10] atha tenānyataravyaktir upamīyate na^ etad api svātmagatatvāt gaur iva gotvena| atha tadvatyā vyaktyā vyaktyantaram upamīyate na^ etad api na hi gotvvaṃ khaṇḍamuṇḍa^upameyatvakāraṇam| atha dharmāntarāpekṣayā sādṛśyaṃ tadbuddheḥ kāraṇam iti naṣṭaṃ tarhi sādṛśyaṃ sa^ evāstu dharmaḥ tadbuddheḥ kiṃ te sādṛśyahatakena kalpitena||

[32,14] kiṃ ca sa cāpy apekṣyamāṇo dharmaḥ kim ekaḥ sādṛśyavad ubhayatrātha bhinna^ ity evam ādivikalpāḥ syur ity asambhavaḥ pūrvavad eva| atha bhinnam\ṃ sādṛśyam ucyate tac ca na bhedād eva na hi bhavati gaur ivāśva^ iti| yathaivaikaikatvād aghaṭamānatā tathā bahubhir apekṣyamāṇaiḥ| etena jātivyaktisambandhādayaḥ pūrvavikalpitāḥ 
pratyuktāḥ||

[32,19] atha buddhyutpattyanyathānupapattyā sādṛśyakalpanā saty evaṃ vanavivarapūrvadakṣiṇottarādibuddhiyogānyathānubhavāt [nyathānupapattyā] vastutvaṃ kiṃ na kalpyate| syād eṣā tava buddhir vṛkṣādaya^ eva tatra tadbuddhijanmahetava^ iti| yady evaṃ mithyāpratyayo gavayena sadṛśī gaur iti vanabuddhivat| atha ye vyāvṛttāḥ ka iha^upamārthaḥ gaur ivāśva^ iti| atha^ubhaye api te 'vayavā vyāvṛttanuvṛttātmānas tadbudhdihetavaḥ tathāpi vanapratyayavad eva 
prāptam||

[32,25] tasmāt parikṣyamāṇaṃ nātivimardanakṣamam iti ta evāvayavā ubhayatra^upalbhyamānā bhūyāṃsaḥ sādṛśyabuddheḥ kāraṇaṃ vanabuddher iva 
pādapāḥ [iti] na^upamānaṃ nāma prmāṇāntaram iti siddham||

[32,27] nāpi premeyadvitvāt pramāṇadvitvakalpanam| kutaḥ pramāṇavaśād arthapratīteḥ| na hy arthavaśāt pramāṇakalpanā| yadi cārthavaśāt 
pramāṇakalpanā syāt adhunā 33,4       tan na śakyaṃ jānānām asarvajñatvāt caityavandanāt svargo bhavati^ity atra caityavandanasya svargahetutvabuddheḥ pratyakṣanumāna^aviṣayatvān mithyātvaṃ syāt| tad arthaṃ vā pramāṇāntarakalpanam||

[33,7] atha sā buddhir anmānaphalam iti cet pramāṇād anyatra 
phalaprasaṅgaḥ evaṃ canirmokṣaḥ(ka)raṇatrayādivākyārthaviṣyabuddhir apīti||

[33,9] nāpi cātra pramāṇalakṣaṇanirūpaṇatātparyaṃ 
prasiddhapramāṇādivṛttinirodhābhyupāya^abhipradarśanaparatvāt| ata eva na sūtrakāraḥ sūtrayāñcakāra| bhāsyakāreṇa tu kiñcit 
prasiddhānuvādamātram avalambitam iti tad anusareṇena kim api jalpitam iti||6-7||

[33,13] pramāṇākhyāṃ vṛttim vyākhyāya viparyayābhidhānaṃ vṛttim idānīm upavyācikhyāsamāna^ āha viparyayo mithyā-jñānam iti||

[33,15] nanu nirodhasya prakṛtatvāt tasya ca hānatvād dhānasya ca heyāpekṣatvād dheyasya ca^avidyamūlatvād avidyā ca viparyya^ iti sa^ eva 
prādhānyena niroddhvayaḥ| tat-prabhavatvād dheyasya| kāraṇanirodhe hi kāryam arthād eva niruddhaṃ syāt| tasmād viparyayaḥ prādhānyāt pramāṇāt 
pūrvaṃ vyākhyātavaya||

[33,19] satyam evam asyeva prāthamyaṃ viparyayasya nirodhanīyatayā tathāpi guṇadoṣajñāna^apekṣatvān nirodhaṃ prati puruṣapravṛtteḥ| pramāṇam eva hi guṇadoṣajñānam| na hy apremāṇena viparyayasya duṣṭatvaṃ gamyate nirodhatat-phalasādhanāni vā jñāyante| tasmāt pramāṇavṛtter eva 
prāthamyaṃ yuktam| jñāyete ca guṇadoṣau pramṇena^ iti tad anantaraṃ viparyaya^abhidhānam| vṛttyantara^apekṣayā tu viparyayasya prāthamyam| tan-mūlatvāc ca vṛttyantarāṇāṃ tannirodhapūrvam evānyanirodhopapatteḥ||

[33,25] viparyayo mithyā-jñanam iti paryāyakathanam| atad-rūpapratiṣṭham iti lakṣaṇoktiḥ| tasya rūpaṃ tad-rūpaṃ yad^ ābhāsaṃ vijñānaṃ tasya vijñānasya ca sā pratiṣṭhā^ ālambanam| agṛhītaviśeṣasya sāmānyamātrabuddhir upalabdhapūrvānyavastu-smṛtyupasaṃhitā niścayākāreṇopasaṃhitā niścayākāreṇopājāyamānā viparyayaḥ||

[34,6] saṃśayo hy ubhayor idaṃ sāmānyam ity evaṃ gṛhītasāmānyasya viśeṣam eva bubhutsamānasya jāyata^ iti smṛtāv evāntarbhavati| yathā sthāṇuḥ kiṃ vā 
purusa^ iti gṛhītapūrvasya^ eva viśeṣasya smarataḥ saṃśayo jāyante, na tathā viparyayaḥ| anyatarasmaraṇamātreṇa^ evāvasitatvāt| tad yathā sthāṇau purastād avasthite pariṇāhārohaṇādisāmānyamātropalambhena samupajātapuruṣas,araṃasya puruṣa^ evāyam iti niśtirā buddhir utpadyate||

[34,12] sā yady apīndriyamārgeṇopalabhyamānavastu-sāmānyapradhānā, tathāpi smaryamāṇātadvastupratiṣṭhatvān na pramāṇam| smṛtyavasānā hi sā yataḥ pramāṇena prabādhyate^ ā(ā)bhūtārtha(arthādi)viṣayatvāt| (ity ataḥ 
pratiṣṭhatvād ity etat) abhūto hi smaryamāṇo 'rthas tadā| yady evaṃ smṛtir eva prāpnoti na viśiṣṭatvāt| upalabhyamānaṃ vastu nimittīkṛtyānyatra smṛtir jāyate yathānubhūtavastu-viṣayā| na viparyayas tathā| sannihitasāmānyamātre deśakālādiviśeṣānugate vastuni smaraṇaparyavasānatve saty apy ayam iti viśeṣarūpeṇa viparyayapratyayo jāyate| tasmād evaṃ na pramāṇadharmo nāpi smṛtir dharma^ iti vṛttyantaratvam||

[34,20] tatrāmramāṇasya pramāṇena bādhasaṃdarśanārtham udāharaṇaṃ darśayati tad yathā dvicandradarśanam iti|| yathā kācakāmalādyupahatadarśanasyābhūtārthaviṣayatvād dhi< dvicandradarśanasya sadviṣayeṇa bhūtārthaviṣayeṇa^ ekacandradarśanena dvitīyacandrābhāvopapattijñānasmṛtyanugṛhītena bādhanam||

[34.24] nanu ca dvicandram iti dvipūlī paṇcapūlītivat dvigusamāsatvāt dvigur ekavacanam iti pratibandhe tadbhāvāt akārāntottarapado dviguḥ striyām iṣṭaḥ iti dvigor akārāntāt (iti) dvicandraśabdād īkāraḥ prāpnoti naiṣa doṣaḥ pātrādipratiṣedhāt| tatra ca^ ādiśabdāsya 
prakāravacanatvāt| prakārasya ca śiṣṭaprayogānusāritvāt, (35,10) dvicandre api pātrādiṣu draṣṭavyaḥ| dvau candrau yasmin darśana^ iti bahuvrīhiḥ| ekacandraśabdasyādvigutvād eva caodyābhāvaḥ| sā ca 
pañcaparvā^ avidyāḥ sā viparyayavṛttir avidyā paṇca parvaṇi^ asyā iti 35,13     kāni punas tānīty āhāvidyā-asmitā^ ityādi| ata^ eva avidyā-ādayaḥ kleśāḥ svasaṇjñabhiḥ svābhiḥ śāstrāntaraprasiddhābhis tama^ ādisaṃjñabhiḥ viśiṣṭāḥ vittamalaprasaṅgenābhidhāyiṣyante dvitīyapāde||1.8||

[35,16] idānīṃ vikalpaṃ darśayati śabdajñānānupātī vastuśūnyo vikalpaḥ|| śabdasya jñānaṃ śabdajñānam vācyavācakaniyamena sādhvasādhuśabdajñānaṃ tad anupatituṃ śīlam asya^ iti śabdajñānānupātī| vastuśūnya^ iti|| yathāvācyārthaśūnyāḥ yasya śabdasya jñānam anupatati tasya śabdasya yathābhūtābhidheyena śūnya^ iti yāvat| yathā-bhūtārthavyatirekeṇa hi vikalpanaṃ vikalpaḥ||

[35,21] tasya hi śabdajñānānupātitvād āgamāntaḥpātitvaṃ prāptam ata^ āha sa na pramāṇopārohīti|| na pramāṇāntaḥpātī vastuśūnyatvāt| na hy āgamasya śabdajñānānupātitve api vastuśūnyatā| yataḥ śābdārth-viṣayā vṛttir āgamaḥ vikalpas tu vastuśūnyatvān na pramāṇam||

[35,25] kiñ ca vaktṛśrotroś ca tulyo vikalpaḥ| dvayor api śabdād uccaritād vikalpeaiva hi buddhir jāyate| na tathā^ āgame| śrotur eva hy āgamaḥ| vaktā tu dṛṣṭānumitārthaḥ|| 

35,27   kiñ ca nirvitarkasamāpannasya vikalpo nirvartate nāgamaḥ| tasmānnityā na pramāṇopārohī||

[36,6] yady evaṃ vastuśūnyatvād viparyayāntaḥpātitvaṃ prāptam ata^ āha na viparyayopārohīti|| kutaḥ śabdajñānānupātitvāt| na hi vastuśūnyatve api viparyayaḥ śabdajñānam apekṣate| yataḥ "viparyayo mithyājñānam atad-rūpapratiṣṭham" ity uktam| pramānapariddhedyapratyayaṃ pratyariguṇabhūtatvāc ca vikalpasya| na hi yathā sarveṣāṃ viparyayo virodhād bādhyate tathā vikalpaḥ| pramāṇapratyayaguṇabhāvād eva||

[36,12] tasmād vastuśūnyatvagrahaṇena śabdajñānānupātitvagrahaṇena ca parasparasaṃbandhena pramāṇaviparyayābhyāṃ pramāṇaviparyayagandhamātrānuliptam api bhinnaṃ vikalpākhyaṃ vṛttyantaraṃ darśitam| tadvadgandhānulepanād eva tad ānantaryeṇa vikalpasyārambhaḥ| nāpi smṛtitvam| smṛteḥ kevalānubhūtāspadatvāt||

[36,16] atha na tv īdṛśī cittavṛttiḥ prasiddhā| na hi svabhāvaṃ kiṃcid vṛttinimittam iti ced āha vastuśūnyatve api śabdajñānamāhātmya[mātra]nibandhano vyavahāro dṛśyata iti| mātragrahaṇaṃ śabdajñānavyatiriktakāraṇābhāvakhyāpanārtham | kva dṛśyata iti ced āha tadyathā caitanyaṃ puruṣasya svarūpam iti| citiśaktir apariṇāminīty uktatvād caitanyam eva puruṣaḥ tad eva ca svarūpam iti vyatirekābhāvād vyatirekopanibandhano vyapadeśo na syāt puruṣasya caitanyam iti| tathā cemam evārtham upaśritya śabdavidā vārttikakāreṇoktam siddhaṃ tu vyapadeśivadbhāvād iti| tathaivehāpi yathā sati vyatireke caitrasya gaur iti tadvad eva caitanyaṃ puruṣasya iti| na hi yathā gor anyaś caitro vyapadeśahetuḥ tathā citer arthāntaram puruṣo 'sti yena vyapadiśyeta| nāpi citiḥ puruṣārthāntaram vyapadiśyeta| tathā api vyatirekasaṃbandhaviṣaye bhavati caitanyaṃ puruṣasya svarūpam iti vyapadeśe vṛttiḥ||

[37,7] pratiṣiddhavastudharmeti| vastuno dharmo vastudharmaḥ| pratiṣiddho vastudharmo 'sya^ iti pratiṣiddhavastudharmā| dharmād anic kevalāt itivacanāt samāsāntaś cintyasamādhiḥ| vastuśabdena sattvādiguṇā ucyante teṣāṃ dharmāḥ pratiṣiddhā^ asya^ iti||

[37,12] na hi pratiṣiddhāḥ sattvādidharmāḥ pratiṣiddhatvād evāsya 
puruṣasya sambandhino bhavanti| yena te puruṣeṇāsya^ iti vyapadeśyeran| tad abhāvamātram eva tu tatrārthaḥ| tathāpi yathā citrā gāvo 'sya^ iti vyapadeśe buddhiḥ tathā pratiṣiddhā vastudharmā^ asya^ iti vyapadeśe buddhiḥ||

[37,16] nirgatā kriyā^ asmād ity atrāpinirgatir apāyaḥ| apāyaś ca sambandhapūrvaḥ| yathā caitrān nirgatā kriyā| na ca^ evaṃ puruṣe kriyāyā asambhavān nirgatih puruṣāt sambhavati| tathā api niṣkriyaḥ puruṣa^ iti vyapadeśe bhavati vṛttiḥ||

[37,19] tathā 'nutpattidharmā puruṣa^ iti|| na vidyate^ utpattidharmo 'sya^ iti pūrvavad eva| atra svayam evārtham ācaṣṭe bhāṣyakāraḥ utpattidharmasyābhāva(jñāna)mātram ava(matra)gamyate| yathā nirguṇo nirañjano niṣkala^ ity evam ādīny apy udāharaṇāni| ātmaviṣayānekodāharaṇapradarśanam ātmatattvanirdhāraṇārthatvāt||

[37,23] tiṣṭhati bāṇa iti lokaprasiddhodāharaṇapratipādanam aprasiddhyāśaṅkānivṛttyartham| tiṣṭhatīti gatinivṛttir gatyabhāvaḥ| na cābhāvasya kālatrayayogo bhavati| nāpi bāṇasya kriyānirapekṣasya dravyasya| nāpi cābhāvena gatinivṛttyā bāṇasya vastunaḥ sambandhaḥ| tiṣṭhati bāṇaḥ [sthāsyati sthitaḥ ] ity evaṃ trīṇy udāharaṇāni||

[38,12] gatinivṛttau dhātvartha^ iti|| nanu gatinivṛttir eva dhātvarthaḥ tatra kathaṃ gatinivṛttāv ity ādhāraḥ ata^ evodāharaṇāntaram etat| asati vyatireke^ ādhārādheyabhāvasyābhāvāt| asti ca gatinivṛttau dhātvartha^ iti vyapadeśe śabdasāmarthyasamutthāpitā buddhir ādhārādheya[bhāva]sambandhāv almbinī| tasmād itthaṃ jātīyaṃ vikalpa viṣayam anekam udāharaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam||1.9||

[38,17] nidrākhyāṃ vṛttim idānīm ācaṣṭe abhāvapratyayālambanā vṛttir nidrā| jāgradviṣayatvāt prāg uktasya vṛtti trayasya tadupamardena ca nidrāsamudbhava^ iti tad anantaraṃ nidrākhyā vṛttir vyākhyāyate||

[38,20] abhāva^ iti jāgradviṣayābhāvaḥ na tv atyantābhāva^ eva| tatra 
pratyayānupapatteḥ| abhāve pratyayaḥ abhāvapratyayaḥ| sa^ eva^ ālambanaṃ yasyāḥ sā vṛttir abhāvapratyayālambanā| sā nidrā suṣūptāvasthā||

[38,23] nanu ca svapnāvasthā '_Pi nidrā^ eva na^ eṣa doṣaḥ svapnanidrājñānālambanaṃ vā iti sūtrakāreṇa bhedena^ upadiṣṭatvāt iha suṣuptāvasthā^ eva nidrābhipretā||

[38,25] kiṃ cābhāvapratyayālambanatvāc ca suṣuptam eva| na hi svapnasyābhāvapratyayālambanatā smṛtitvāt| smṛteś cānubhūtaviṣayatvāt| tathā bhāṣyakāraḥ svapne bhāvitasmartavyā iti smṛtitvaṃ darśayati||

[39,4] kiñ ca svapnasya cittavṛttitā svasaṃvedyeti nāśaṅkā^ eva^ upapadyate| tathā ca cittavṛttitām āśaṅkyāha sā tu[ca sam]prabodhe 
pratyavamarśād ity ādi| tasmāt pratyayaviśeṣo nidrā| na hy asati 
pratyayaviśeṣe parāmarśo yaktaḥ| asti cāyaṃ prabuddhasya pratyavamarśaḥ sukham aham asvāpsam ityādiḥ| pratyavamarśo nāma kim apy anvabhūvam iti 
pratyayālocanā nānubhavābhāve sā bhavet| nāpi tadviṣayāḥ smṛtayo yuktāḥ| nidrāyā^ apratyayatve prajñāṃ viśāradīkaroti manaḥ manasaḥ kartṛtvaṃ satyānaṃ stimitam akarmaṇyam gurugurūṇīti prakāre guṇavacanasya^ iti dvir vacanaṃ guruprakārāṇi^ alasaṃ muṣitam iva mallenāpahṛtam iva^ etāni pratyayānubhavakāryāṇi na syuḥ| kāś cana smṛtayaḥ kānicit kāryāṇi kecana pratyavamarśāḥ| tad etat trayam api bhāṣyakāropanyastaṃ pratyayaviśeṣatva^ evopapadyate||

[39,14] kiñca garbhagṛhādiṣu cādṛṣṭabāhyanimittānām asati viśeṣe ciram ahaṃ suptavān kṣiptam iti ca parāmarśo 'nyathā na yujyate||

[39,16] nanu ca nātra smṛtayaḥ smṛtīnām anvabhavam ity evaṃ viśiṣṭatvāt| na hi suṣuptād utthitoviśeṣaṃ smarati^ atrocyate bālasya^ iha janmany ananubhūtastanyasambandhasya janmānantaram eva tasyābhilāṣo dṛśyate| na ca [sa] svābhāvikaḥ| abālānāṃ smṛtipūrvapravṛttidarśanāt| tathā^ ihāpi| tasmāt pratyayapūrvakaḥ suṣuptotthitasya gātragauravādi kāryaṃ duḥkhādy anubhavapūrvaṃ gātrādiviṣayagauravādikāryatvāt 
prasiddhaduḥkhānubhavagārtagātragauravavad iti pratyayaviśeṣo nidreti siddham||1.10||

[39,23] smṛtim idānīṃ vyācaṣṭe^ anubhūtaviṣayāsampramoṣaḥ smṛtiḥ| smṛteḥ 
pramāṇādisarvavṛttikāryatvād ante niveśaḥ| anubhūtaś cāsau viṣayaś cānubhūtaviṣayaḥ| yo^ asāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ anubhūtaḥ sa^ eva viṣayaḥ na punar yo viṣayaḥ sa^ evānubhūta^ iti tathā ca smṛter aśabdāditvāt smṛtiviṣayāyāḥ smṛter asmṛtitvaṃ prāpnoti^iti| iṣyate ca smṛtivisayā api smṛtiḥ svasaṃvedyatvāt anubhūtavisayasyāsaṃpramoṣo 'napaharaṇam atiro-bhāvaḥ| 
pārokṣye api sādṛśyādinimittāpekṣayā anubhūtasya viṣayasya dṛśyamānavad avabhāsaḥ smṛtiḥ||

[40,7] tatra pratyayasmaraṇayor visayākārāvabhāsāviśeṣāt kiṅkṛtas tayor bheda^ iti tad vivekaṃ pradarśāyitukāma^ āha kiṃ pratyayasya cittaṃ smaratītyādi||

[40,9] yady apy anubhūtānubhūyamānaviṣayatvena vivekaḥ tathāpi sādṛśyanimittāṃ bhrāntiṃ tayoḥ kāryakāraṇatvapradarśanāya nirvaratayati| etad eva ca vaināśikānāṃ bahir arthānupapatter buddhikāraṇam| kiṃ 
pratyayasya smaratīti karmaṇy ubhayatra ṣaṣṭhī| cittaṃ smaratīti 
puruṣakartṛtvaṃ nivārayati smṛtipratyayayor ekakartṛtvapradarśanārtham| na hi puruṣasya pratyayakartṛtvaṃ smṛtikartṛtvaṃ vā| dṛśyatvāt pratyayānām| dṛśyānāṃ ca svātmātiriktadṛśyatvaṃ ghaṭādivad iti vakṣyāmaḥ||

[40,15] kiñcātaḥ yadi viṣayasya smarati pratyayasyāpi viṣayaviṣayatvād anarthāntaraṃ smṛtiḥ pratyayāt| atha pratyayasya smarati tadā tv idam ayuktam anubhūtaviṣayāsampramoṣa^ iti| tadā hy anubhavāsampramoṣa^ iti syāt tatrāha grāhyoparatktaḥ grāhyo viṣaya^ ālambanam tena^ uparaktaḥ lākṣādyuparaktas phaṭikavat| grāhyagrahaṇobhayākāranirbhāsaḥ grāhyasya ca ghatādeḥ pṛthubudghnādyākāreṇa svena cāvabhāsarūpeṇopajāyamānatvād ubhayanirbhāsaḥ||

[40,21] sa ca pratyaya utpadya vinivartamānaḥ svāśraye pratyayini saṃskāram ādadhāti| sa ca saṃskāraḥ kāraṇānuvidhāyitvād ubhayā(pāta)kāra eva| svavyañjakāñjana iti saṃskārādhyinaḥ pratyayasya hetubhūtaṃ yat karma dattaphalaṃ tattulyajātīyaṃ yatkarmāntaraṃ phalapradānāyābhimukhībhavati tat svavyañjakam| tathā ca vakṣyati tatas tadvipākānuguṇānām evābhivyaktir vāsanānam iti tatkarma pratyupanītaṃ vā sādṛśyapraṇidhānādi| svena vyañjakenābhivyajyata iti svavyañjakāñjanaḥ| grāhyagrahaṇātmikāṃ smṛtiṃ janayati| anyathā tadvyañjakasya karmaṇo vipākānupapattiḥ||

[41,10] tatra grahaṇākārapūrvā buddhiḥ grahaṇam [svī]karaṇaṃ [svīkaraṇam eva pūrvaṃ prathamam utpdyamānasya pratyayasya| na hi viṣayaviśeṣam apekṣate pūrvam iṣṭāniṣṭagrahaṇāt nirbhagnaghaṭapradīpavinirgataprabhā-jālavat||

[41,13] grāhyākārapūrvā smṛtiḥ anubhūtāpekṣatvāt | yadi ca grhaṇapūrvikā smṛtiḥ pratyayavad abhaviṣyat anubhūtaṃ nāpaikṣiṣyata| apekṣate tu| tasmād grāhyo viṣayaḥ pūrvo 'syā na grahaṇam| tathāpi tūbhayātmikā^ eva| smṛtisaṃskārayor ekarūpatvāt/ nāpy anyatarapratyākhyānena sambhavati| tathā cānubhūtaviṣayāsampramoṣa^ ity upapadyate| smṛtipratyayayor api [amāna]viṣayatvāt grahaṇatvasya ca sarvatra sambhavāt| tasmāt pakṣadvaye apy abhihitadoṣo na dṛśyata^ iti||

[41,19] sā ca dvayī| katham hāvitasmartavyā bhāvitaṃ paraniṣpannaṃ asakṛdbhāvitatvāt| yathā dravyāntareṇāsakṛdbhāvitaṃ tailādi| asakṛdbhāvitatvād eva praṇidhānādiprayatnāntaraṃ nāpekṣate| bhāvitaṃ smartavyaṃ praṇidhānādiyatnāntarā[na]pekṣayā yasyāḥ sā svapne bhāvitasmartavyā| jā[gṛ]grat samaye tv apekṣate 
praṇidhānādiyatnāntaram iti tadviparītā||

[41,24] sarvāś ca pramāṇādivṛttipañcatayānubhavād udbhavanti| sarvāś ca^ etāḥ sukhādyā(rasattvādyā)tmikāḥ| tadātmakaś ca saṃsāraḥ| sukhaduḥkhamohāś ca kleśeṣu vyākhyātāḥ sūtrakāreṇa| sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ vivekinaḥ iti hi vakṣyati| taddhānaṃ ceha vivakṣitam| (42,14) tasmāt sukhaduḥkhamohātmakatvāt niroddhavyā vṛttayaḥ| kiñca^ āsāṃ nirodhe samprajñāto vā samādhir bhavaty asamprajñāto vā ||1.11||

[42,16] atha^ āsāṃ nirodhe ka^ upāya^ iti||

[42,17] upāyaṃ pravedayati^ abhyāsavairāgyābhyāṃ tannirodha^ iti|| abhyāsavairāgye vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇe| tābhyāṃ yathā^ uktalakṣaṇānāṃ [vṛttīnāṃ] nirodhaḥ viroghāt| nirodha^ upaśamaḥ| abhyāsavairāgyayor viṣayavivekapradarśanārtham etadbhāṣyam cittanadītyādi| cittena nady eva saṃsāriṇaḥ satataṃ viṣaye pariṇīyante saṃsāraprāg bhārā saṃsārāvasānā samudrāvasāneva| khilīkriyate pratibadhyata^ iti| uddhāṭyate pravartyate| uddhāṭaḥ kriyata^ ity uddhāṭyate| anyathā mitvāt ghaṭādayo mitaḥ iti (hatu) mitāṃ hnasvaḥ iti syāt| evaṃ ubhayatantra^ ity arthaḥ||1.12||[42,24] tatrābhyāsasvarūpam pratipādayiṣyann āha tatra sthitau yatno 'bhyāsaḥ| tatra tasmin nirodhe sā ca nimittasaptamī| nirodhanimittā cittasya yā sthitiḥ yatnasya phalabhūtā tasyā nimitto[ttabhūto] yo yatnaḥ so 'bhyāsaḥ| tathā ca^ āha (43,13) avṛttikasya 
praśānta paṅkakara(ṅga)ñjasyevābhyāsaḥ praśāntavāhitā prasannarūpata ya 
pariṇāmo niruddhavṛttikasya cittasya| yatno vīryam abhyutsāha^ iti 
paryāyāḥ| sthitisaṃpipādayiṣayā yamaniyamādiyogasādhanānuṣṭhānam abhyāsa^ iti||1.13|| [43,16] sa tu kathaṃ sthirībhavatīty ata^ āha dīrghakālanairantaryasevitaḥ dīrghakāla tv avarjito vā [nairantaryavarjito vā na]dṛḍhabhūmir bhavaty abhyāsaḥ| tasmād ubhayor api (viśeṣya)viśeṣaṇatvam eva| saṃskārāsevita^ iti tv abhyāsa^ eva viśeṣyate| dṛḍhabhūmir ity asya vyākhyānaṃ vyutthānasaṃskāreṇeti| vyutthānajena saṃskāreṇa drāg iti sahasā nābhibhūyate||1.14||

[43,21] vairāgyapratipādanārtham āha dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayāvitṛṣṇasya vaśīkārasaṃjñā vairāgyam viṣayaśabdaḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbandhyate vitṛṣṇaya iti ca drṣṭaviṣaya_Vitṛṣṇasya^ ānuśravikaviṣayavitṛṣṇasya ceti||

[44,9] dṛṣṭaś cāsau viṣayaś ca tasmin|| kaḥ punar asau striyo 'nnaṃ 
pānam aiśvaryam iti vyaktyapekṣayā| śabda^ādīnām ānantye api rāgaprabalatvam eteṣv atitarām iti stryādigrahaṇaṃ prādhānyāt| atra hi 
prabalataro rāgaḥ sa prayatnatareṇa niṣeddhavyaḥ| evam ānuśravikaviṣayaḥ| ānuśraviko nāma^ āgamika^ iha sa ca svargādiprāptyānubhavanīyaḥ 
prakṛtilayagatabhogyo vaidehyāprāptāv upabhogyaś ca||

[44,14] nanu ca vaitṛṣṇyam eva vairāgyam tathā rāgas tṛṣṇā garddhaḥ iti hi vakṣyati| nāvasthā-viśeṣasya vivakṣitatvāt| tathā ca yatamānasaṃjñā vyatikrāntasaṃjñā ekendriyasamjñā vaśīkārasaṃjñeti catasṛṣṇām āsāṃ sāmānyasaṃjñā vairāgyam iti| evaṃ sati dṛṣṭanuśravikaviṣayavitṛṣṇasya^ ity etad uktaṃ bhavati^ abigatapūrvāvasthātrayasya caturthīṃ yā^ avasthā sā tv iha vairāgyam iti vivakṣitaṃ| tasmād iha vaitṛṣṇyād anyavairāgyam abhipretam| tathā ca darśayati viṣyadoṣadarśinaḥ doṣadarśanasya viṣayanicṛttihetutvāt| tad guṇadarśanaṃ hi rāgakāraṇam prasaṃkhyānabalāt tad-doṣadarśanābhyāsabalāt| anābhogātmikā viṣayavyatikarātmikā| divyādivyayoḥ samprayogo api heyopādeyaśūnyā rañjanāvamuktasyeva sphuṭikasya viṣayarāgavimuktasya cittasyāvasthānam| vaśīkārasaṃjñā vaśīkartuṃ śakyante 'syām avasthāyāṃ sarve gauṇāḥ padārthāḥ vaśīkartavyatvena saṃjñāyante vaśīkṛtāni ca tasyām avasthāyām indriyāṇi saṃjñāyante vaśīkaraṇaṃ vā saṃjñāyate 'syām iti||1.15||

[44,26] tac ca dvividham paraṃ cāparaṃ ca viṣayanimittabhedena| aparaṃ vyākhyātam| adhunā param ucyate tat paraṃ puruṣakhyāter guṇavaitṛṣṇyam| tad iti vairāgyaṃ parāmṛśyate| (45,10) param iti pūrvoktavairāgyād uttarakālabhāvīti| yadi vā tasya viṣayanimittāmhuām utkṛṣṭanimittaviṣayatvāt kaivalyasya vā pratyāsannataratvāt param utkṛṣṭam||

[45,12] kiṃ nimittam asya^ ity āha puruṣakhyāter nimittāt| kiṃ-viṣayam ity āha guṇavaitṛṣṇyam iti kuṇeṣu sattvādiṣu vaitṛṣṇyam| dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayadoṣadarśīti| pūrvavairāgyanimittaviṣayaparāmarśanam asya viṣayanimittabhedavivekārtham| puruṣadarśanābhyāsāt nimittāt| tacchuddhipravivekāpyāyitabuddhiḥ| tad iti puruṣadarśanaṃ parāmṛśyate| tasya śuddhiḥ tacchuddhiḥ nirṇiktakleśādimalatvam| atha vā tasya 
puruṣasya śuddhis tacchuddhiḥ| tacchuddhes tadālambanadarśanaṃ 
pravivicyate| tat-pravivekenāpy āyitā buddhir asya yoginaḥ||

[45,19] guṇebhya^ iti viṣayapratipādanam| pūrvas tu dṛṣṭānuśravikaviṣayebhyo (api) viraktaḥ eṣa tatkāraṇebhyo guṇebhya^ eva| vyaktāvyaktadharmakebhyaḥ mahadādisaṃsthitā vyaktadharmakāḥ| 
pradhānāvasthitā^ avyaktadharmakāḥ tebhyo virakta^ iti tat dvayaṃ vairāgyaṃ 
pūrvam idaṃ ca tatra yad^ uttaraṃ puruṣadarśananimittaṃ guṇaviṣayaṃ tajjñānaprasādamātram| puruṣadarśanasya^ eva paraḥ praviveka^ iti yāvat||

[45,24] puruṣadarśī hi heyopādeyaśūnyacitto bhavati| tad āha prasṃkhyāne apy akusīd asya sarvathā vivekakhyāter dharmameghaḥ samādhiḥ iti||

[45,26] yasya^ udaye yasya vairāgyasyodaye| pratyuditakhyātiḥ 
pratyutpannadarśanaḥ | evaṃ manyate prāptaṃ prāpaṇīyam ity ādi^ etasya hi nāntarīyakaṃ kaivalyaṃ tataḥ (46,4)paraṃ sādhanānapekṣatvāt| vairāgyasya jñānaprasādamātratve jñānavairāgyayor ananyatvāt tadviparītayoś ca rāgājñānayor anarthāntaratvam eva siddham| evaṃ sati rāgo apy avidyāyā evāvasthāntaram iti pañcaparvā^ avidyety upapannam bhavati||

[46,7] nanu ca^ evaṃ buddher aṣṭaguṇatvaṃ bādhyeta| ṣaḍ-guṇatvaṃ hi dhyānāvasthābhedābhyupagamāt| jñānatve api vairāgyasya 
prasannataratvābhyupagamaḥ| rāgo apy ajñānasyāvasthāntaraviśeṣaḥ||

[46,10] nanu ca^ evaṃ sati jñānājñānayor anekāvasthābhedād aṣṭaguṇatvaṃ 
punar api na prāpnoti| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ phalavattvapratyanīkatvaniyamāt| jñānāvasthācatuṣṭayasya phalavattvena bhedaniyamaḥ| tathā ajñānāvasthācatuṣṭayapratyanīkatvenādharmādicatuṣṭayaniyama^ ity aṣṭaguṇatvasiddhiḥ dharmādipratidvandvina^ eva hi guṇatvena niyamyante nājñānabhedāḥ sarva^ eva||1.16||

[46,15] uktaṃ purastāt samprajñātaḥ samādhir vitarkādy anugata^ ity upariṣṭāt pravedayiṣyāmaḥ iti| so 'vasaraprāpta^ idānīṃ lakṣyate| upāyadvayena niruddhavṛtteḥ vairāgyadvataṃ parāparatvena vyākhgyātam| abhyāsasyāpi dvitvaṃ sāmarthyād avasīyate| katham viśiṣṭam asamprajñātasamādher abhyāsaṃ sūtreṇa^ eva vakṣyati virāmapratyayābhyāsapūrvaḥ iti| tena sālambanaviṣayaḥ pūrvābhyāsa^ iti niścīyate| vakṣyati ca tatpratiṣedhārtham ekatattvābhyāsaḥ iti||

[46,21] bhāṣyakāro apy avadīt vairāgyeṇa viṣayasrotaḥ khilīkriyate| vivekadarśanābhyāsena kalyāṇasrota^ uddhaṭyate^ iti nāvocata vṛttisvarūpatiraskāram eva samprajñātasamādheḥ| vṛttinirodhaprabhavatvam aniṣṭaviṣayapravṛttyuparamottarakālabhāvitvāpekṣayā vṛttiviṣayaviśeṣānugata^ eva hi saḥ||

[46,25] tathā ca dvitīyasūtre samprajñātasya nirodho na lakṣaṇam iti vyākhyātam| ataḥ pūrvokt_Bhyāsavairāgyopāyadvayena niruddhavṛtteś cittasya katham ucyate kathaṃ (47,7) lakṣaṇam ucyata^ iti yāvat| vitarkavicārānandāsmitārūpānugamāt samprajñātaḥ| anugamaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbandhanīyaḥ| vitarkānugamād vicārānugamād ānandānugamād asmitārūpānugamād iti| rūpaśabdo mātrārtho vitarkādipūrvadharmatrayavarjitatvakhyāpanārthaḥ||

[47,11] vitarkaś cittasyālambante sthūla^ āmogaḥ| sthūla(api)miśrī-bhāvaḥ 
prathamam ālambanākāreṇa pariṇamamānasya cittasya ya^ ālambanavyatikaraḥ sa vitarkaḥ|| sūkṣmo vicāraḥ āmogaḥ sūkṣma^ iti saṃbandhaḥ| ānando hlādaḥ vicāraḥ sūkṣmatara^ ābhogas tṛtīyaḥ| sānandatā viśeṣaḥ| ekarūpātmikā saṃvid asmitā svasyāṃ prakṛtāv asmitāyāṃ samāpannasyāsmitā pratyayamātratā ekarūpātmakatvam| tathā codāhariṣyati tam aṇum ātmānam anvavaityāsmīty etāvat saṃjānīta^ iti||

[47,18] nanu cāsmitā kleśeṣu paṭhiṣyati dṛgdarśanaśaktyor ekātmatevāsmitā iti tat katham ivāsmitārūpeṇa kleśena samādhir anugamyata iti| satyam evaṃ prakṛṣṭasvakāraṇākārāvabhāsakatvād anyaviṣayavinivṛttātmakatvāc cāsmitāyāḥ mamādhirūpatve apy adoṣaḥ|| yadi vā^ asmitārūpasyālambanasyāvisyātve api na yogipratyayasyāvidyātvam| na hi khalu paracittavividiṣāyāṃ parasyāvisyātvam| na hi khalu 
paracittapratyayaṃ gṛhṇan parakīyāvidyāpratyayarūpālambanadoṣeṇa svayam avidyāvān bhavati||

[48,9] yaḥ punaḥ kleśaḥ sa viparyayaviśiṣṭa^ eva| yathā^ ahaṃ gacchāmi kṛśo 'ham iti| yogicittasya tannyagbhūtaṃ rajastamasī^ iti na tathā||

[48,11] tatra pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ svakīyottarottaradharmānugataḥ paraḥ 
paras tu pūrvapūrvadharmavikalaḥ| yasmāś ca^ etad-dharmacatuṣṭayānugata evāyaṃ samprajñātaḥ samādhid tasmād etal-lakṣaṇa^ iti lakṣaṇārthaṃ sūtram| loke api yo yad^ asādhāraṇadharmānuyātaḥ sa tal-lakṣaṇaḥ yathā sāsnādilakṣaṇo gaur iti||

[48,15] asmitāmātragrahaṇān nirālambanatām eṣāṃ samādhīnām mā kaścid āśaṅkiṣṭety āha sarva^ ete sālambanā iti| yaḥ punar manyate nirvikalpatvam asmiteti tat asataḥ| asmitāśabdasyāhaṅkāre prasiddhatvāt| tathā ca^ āha asmibhāvo 'smitā ||1.17||

athāsamprajñātaḥ yogaś cittavṛttinirodha^ ity upapāditalakṣaṇaḥ kim upāyaḥ kiṃ sādhanaḥ kenopāyenopetya^ ity arthaḥ|  kiṃ svabhāvaḥ kiṃ svarūpaḥ kim avastha^ iti svabhāvaviśiṣṭasādhanasaṃbandhapradarśanārtham āha virāmapratyayābhyāsa pūrvaḥ saṃskāraśeṣo 'nyaḥ||

[48,22] virāma pratyayābhyāsapūrva^ ity etāvatā sādhanasambandham ācaṣṭe|  saṃskāraśeṣa^ iti tu svabhāvavyākhyānam| anya^ ity anantaroktalakṣaṇāta samprajñātād anyo^ ayaṃ nirbījaḥ asamprajñātaḥ||

[48,25] anya^ ity ubhābhyāṃ saṃbandhyate virāmapratyayābhyāsapūrvaś ca saṃskāraśeṣaś ca| ko'sāv ubhyaviśiṣṭa^ ity āhānya^ iti||

[49,8] viramaṇaṃ viramaḥ| virāmaś cāsau pratyayaś ca virāmapratyayaḥ|  virāmarūpatvāt sa^ eva pratyayo virāma^ ity ucyate| sarvaviṣayebhyo vinivartamānasya vinivartanakāle prāg apratyayāpatteḥ pratyayarūpatvam|  etad yathā pāvakasya jvalataḥ prakṣīyamāṇedhanasya śanaiś śanair upaśāmyataḥ prāg aṅgāratāpatter jvālātmakatā||
