49.12   tasmād virāmarūpaḥ pratyayo virāmapratyayaḥ tasyābhyāsaḥ pūrvo mukhyaṃ yasya saḥ virāmapratyayābhyāsapūrvaḥ| saṃskāra^ eva śeṣo yasya sa saṃskāraśeṣaḥ| pratyayoparame tu pratyayasaṃskāra^ evāvaśiṣyate|  viṣayapratyayebhya^ uparataṃ cittaṃ saṃskāraśeṣam eva^ ity arthaḥ| tasya paraṃ vairāgyam upāyaḥ tasya samādheḥ param uttaram||

[49,16] nanu ca paraṃ vairāgyaṃ ceti vaktavyam abhyāsavairāgyābhyām vṛttinirodhasyābhipretatvāt| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| virāmapratyayābhyāsasya sūtropāttatvād anāśaṅkyas tadabhyāsaḥ| vairāgyaṃ punar anupāttatvād āśaṅkyeta| tasmāt tad eva prapayatā na canirmokṣaḥ [caḥ] paṭhitaḥ||

[49,19] yady evaṃ kasmān na sūtra^ eva vairāgyagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam pūrvatra 
paṭhitatvāt| tatra kim arthaṃ paṭhitam iti cet vairāgyaprasaṅgāt| 
pūrvavairāgyaviśeṣa^ evottaraṃ vairāgyam iti prasaṅgād uttaram [duktam]| 
pūrvasya ca vairāgyasya samprajñātaviṣaye ( 'pi) viniyogād aviśiṣṭya 
parasya sādhanatvād anyaviṣayatvāc ca nirbījena^ eva sambandha^ iti na sūtre vairāgyagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam| sāmarthyaprāptaṃ tu bhāṣyakāro darśayati||

[49,24] abhyāsavairāgyābhyām tannirodha^ iti hi pratipāditam| sālambano hy abhyāso viruddhatvān nirālambanasya samādheḥ| sādhanatvāya na kalpata^ iti nirvastuko api virāmapratyayo nirālambanasamādhyanurūpatvād ālambanīkriyate| tasmāt tadabhyāsavairāgyopāyapūrvakaṃ cittaṃ nirālambanam abhāvaprāptam iva bhavati| sa^ eṣa nirbījaḥ saṃskāraśeṣasvabhāvaḥ samādhir asamprajñāta^ iti ||1.18||

[50,11] sa khalv ayaṃ dvividhaḥ| saḥ nirbījaḥ samādhiḥ upāyapratyayo bhavapratyayaś ca| upāyena pratīyate prāpyata^ ity upāyapratyayo yogināṃ bhavati| yady api videhā^ api yogina^ eva| tathāpi yamādiyogasādhanānuṣṭhāyino 'bhipretāḥ| teṣāṃ śraddhāvīryādy upāyalabhyaḥ samādhiḥ||

[50,15] videhaprakṛtilayānāṃ tu janmanā^ eva labhyaḥ| te hi tanutarapratibandhāvaśeṣāḥ sa tu pratibandhāvaśeṣas teṣāṃ sakṛdāsāditapunarjanmanāṃ samādhivyāghātāya na paryāpnoti| sa tu janmapratilambhamātranimittam| (tvāt) jñānavairāgyādyanuṣṭhānakāryatvād videhaprakṛtilayatvasya tad anuṣṭhānas ity arthasūtram| īdṛśāṃ hi tadanusṭhānamāhātmyam| yad^ uta sāmarthyādivaikalyād amhīṣsitaphalānavāptāv api mahanīyataraṃ videhaprakṛtilayatvaṃ prāpyate||

[50,21] videhā nāma devāḥ puryaṣṭakaśarīropabandhāḥ| te svasamskāramātropbhogena vairāgyābhyāsāhitaḥ samskāraśeṣas tanmātropabhogena cittena kaivalyam ihānubhavanta^ iti sattvaguṇavinirbhogāt tathājātīyaka svasaṃskāraparipākaṃ kṣpayanti||

[50,24] prakṛtilayāḥ sādhikāre^ aparyavasitā[dhi]kāre| yāvad dhi na kṛtā guṇapuruṣāntarakhyātiḥ tāvad aparisamāptādhikāram eva cittaṃ tasmin 
prakṛtlayāpanne kaivalyam ivānubhavantīti| pūrvavat| yāvan na punar āvartate 'dhikāravaśāt vidyākartavyatāvaśāt tāvad anubhavanti kaivalyam iva||1.19||

[51,14] yeṣām upāyapratyayo yogināṃ teṣāṃ śraddhāvīryasmṛtismādhiprajñāpūrvakaḥ samādhiḥ śraddhā nāmāpavargaprāptau tat sādhanaśravaṇe ca katakaphalasaṃparka^ iva salilasya cetasaḥ [samprasādaḥ] saṃprasattiḥ| sa hi jananīva kalyāṇī yoginaṃ pāti rakṣaty aśubhebhyaḥ| tasya hi vivekārthinaḥ samyagdarśanārthino vīryam utsāha upajāyate yogasādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ prati| sañjātavīryasyāgamajñānādiviṣayā dṛḍhatarā smiṛtir upatiṣṭhate| vivekārthina^ iti sarvatrānuṣajyate||

[51,20] dṛḍhasmṛtyupasthāne ca cittam anākulaṃ samāḍhīyate| samāhitacittasya prajñāyā buddheḥ viviktatā sarvārthaprakāśanasāmarthyam| ta(thā)t viśinaṣṭi yena yathābhūtam ātmādhi vastu jānātīti||

[51,23] tad abhyāsāt etad uktaṃ bhavati^ ātmadarśanavirāmapratyayābhyāsād iti| tad viṣayāc ca vairāgyā(khyād viṣayā)t parasmād vairāgyād iti| asamprajñātaḥ samādhiḥ iti||1.20||

[51,26] te api śraddhādisādhanā nava(nupa)yogino bhavanti| tad darśayati tad yathā mṛdupāyo madhyopāyo 'dhimātropāya^ iti| teṣām ekekas tridhā bhidyate tatra mṛdupāya^ iti sādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ prati mandopakramo madhyopakramaḥ tīvra-/tīvrasaṃvegoakrama^ iti| evam itaratrāpīti| nātra tirobhūtam(t)||

[52,18] tatrādhimātropāyānāṃ tīvrasaṃvegānām āsannaḥ samādhilābhaḥ samādhiphalaṃ ca||1.21||

[52,20] teṣām api mṛdu-madhyatīvropāyakramatayā viśiṣyamāṇatvāt tato [api]viśeṣaḥ sādhanānuṣṭhānasaṃskārasya| tadviśeṣād apy uttarasya samādhilābhaḥ pratyāsīd aitarām||

[52,22] etad asya sūtrasya prayojanam yogasādhanānuṣṭhānaṃ prati yoginaḥ katham upabṛṃhitotsāhā bhaveyur iti| yathā loke drutataraṃ sīmāntaṃ 
pari[prati]dhāvatām etat-phalaṃ bhavatīti tadvat| atha vā mandamandetaropakramāṇām api yoginām abhimataphalāvāptiprakāśanād anavasannacetasaḥ pravarterann iti| itarathā tīvratarāyāsajanitabhītayo 'vasīdeyur iti||1.22||

kim etasmād eva^ āsannataro bhavati^ atha (tasya) asya lābhe bhavaty anyo api kaścid upāyo na vā^ iti| upāyāntaram astīty āha^ īśvarapraṇidhānād vā| īśvaraśabdārthm uttaratra vyākhyāsyati| 
praṇidhānam iha vyācaṣṭe bhaktiviśeṣād (53,15) āvarjita^ iti| anugrahaṃ 
praty abhimukhībhāvam āpāditaḥ taṃ bhaktyatiśayayoginaṃ yoginam abhidhyānamātreṇa parama-īśvaratvād eva nirāyāsatayā satyasaṃkalpatvāt anugṛhṇāti| tadanugrahād api pratyāsannataraḥ samādhilābhaḥ phalaṃ ceti ||1.23||

[53,18] atha pradhānapuruṣavyatiriktaḥ ko 'yam īśvara^ iti sāṃkhyaśāstre 
prasiddhyabhāvād, īśvarasadbhāve ca^ upapattim avaśyam anavagata-īśvaraviśeṣo vā pṛcchati| tatra tāvat pratijānīte kleśakarmavipakāśayair aparāmṛṣṭaḥ puruṣaviśeṣa^ īśvaraḥ|| kleśā iti| avidyā-ādayaḥ kleśayantīti kleśāḥ| tat-pūrvānuṣṭhitāni kuśalākuśalāni karmāṇi| kuśalāni cākuśalāni [ca kuśalakuśalāni] cety ekaśeṣaḥ| kuśalākuśalānīti yāvat| tatphalaṃ vipākaḥ jātyāyurbhogalakṣaṇaḥ| ta eva kleśādaya nirvāṇāc cherata^ iti kleśakarmavipakā^ āśayāḥ| yadi vā kleśādīnāṃ rāśayaḥ kleśakarmavipakāśayāḥ||

[53,26] te manasi vartamānā manovṛttiprabhavatvāt puruṣe vyapadiśyante| kasmāt sa hi tat-phalasya bhokteti| yathā rājani jayaparājayau yuddheṣu vartamānāv apadiśyete tat-phalaṃ rājasaṃbandha^ iti||

[54,10] yo hy anena bhogenāparāmṛṣṭa^ iti^ ucyate| na kālavivakṣā upalakṣaṇarthatvaṃ na parāmṛśyate nāpi parāmṛkṣyate nāpi parāmṛṣṭa iti| kleśakarmavipakāśayāsaṃbandhī(ndhayogī)ty arthaḥ||

[54,13] sūtrārthaviśuddhiṃ kurvann āha kaivalyaṃ prāptas tarhīti| kleśādy aparāmarśasya kādācitkatayā api saṃbhavāt| santi bahavaḥ kevalinaḥ kiṃ tadī[te 'pī]śvarā iti| parāmarśasyāpi saṃbhavān na ta ekāntena kleśādibhir aparāmṛṣṭāḥ| parāmarśāparāmarśau hi staḥ teṣām| īśvaratvaparāmṛṣṭa^ eva| na hy anavadhṛtaḥ śabdārtho lakṣaṇatvenopādeyaḥ aparāmṛṣṭa^ iti| tasmād ya^ ekāntenāparāmṛṣṭa^ ity ucyate||

[54,18] te hi trīṇi bandhanāni prākṛtavaikṛtadākṣiṇāni samyagdarśanena hitvā kaivalyaṃ prāptāḥ| īśvarasya tu tatsaṃbandhaḥ kleśādisambandho na bhūto yathā muktasya pūrvā bandhakoṭer muktvopapatter eva jñāyate| bandhapūrvikā hi kuktiḥ| nāpi bhāvī| yathā prakṛtilīnasya| tasya hy anārabdhasaṃsārasya^ uttarā bandhakoṭiḥ sambhāvyate| pravṛttasaṃsārasya cāprakṛtilīnacittasya sā(samā)adhikāratvāt pūrvottarabandhakoṭī sambhavataḥ| tad idaṃ sarvam atrāparāmṛṣṭa^ iti kālāvivakṣāpradarśanārtham| sa tu sadā^ eva muktaḥ sadā^ eva-īśvaraḥ||

[54,25] tatra puruṣaviśeṣa^ īśvara^ ity uktatvāt īśvarasya 
puruṣasyānaiśvaryadharmatvāt cittasya caiśvaryadharmatvāt niratiśayaiśvaryasya ca prakṛṣṭacittasambandhāpekṣatvāt prāptam evottarārtham anuvadati yo śau prakṛṣṭasattvopādānād īśvarasya śāśvatika^ utkarṣa^ iti| nityaniratiśayasarvajñānāiśvaryaśaktisampattir utkarṣaḥ||

[55,4] tatra pratijñāviśuddhyarthaṃ praśnapūrvam upakramate sa kiṃ sanimitta^ āhosvin nirnimitta^ iti| nimittaśabdaḥ kāraṇaparyāyaḥ| sa kiṃ sakāraṇa^ āhosmin niṣkāraṇa^ iti| kiñcātaḥ yadi tāvat sakāraṇas tadā sadā^ eva-īśvara^ ity ayuktam anityatvāt| atha niṣkāraṇaḥ tathā ca^ utkarṣasya kāryatvād asattvaprasaṅgaḥ| na hi niṣkāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ dṛśyate loke|| 

atrocyate na niṣkāraṇaḥ śāstranimittatvāt| śāstraṃ jñānam| śāstrasya hetutvāt| yataḥ satvaviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ nityaṃ dravyasvabhāvasādhyasādhanavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭāspadam||

[55,12] yady evaṃ jñānasyānyato 'dhigamapūrvatvāt prāg anutkarṣaḥ prāpta^ ity ata^ āha śāstraṃ punaḥ kiṃ-nimittam iti| yadi svābhāvikaṃ jñānaṃ mattonmattajñānavad anutkarṣahetutvam| atha punaḥ sanimittakaṃ nimittāt prāg anutkarṣa^ iti manyate ||

[55,15] na tāvan nirnimittam| yataḥ prakṛṣṭasattvanimittaṃ prakṛṣṭasattvāśrayam iti yāvat| ata^ eva svabhāvajñānadoṣābhāvaḥ||

[55,17] kiñ ca svābhāvikajñānatve api na mattādijñānavad apakarṣahetutvam| nityamuktakleśādisattvāśrayatvāt| tatra yathā^ eva jñānasaṃskārasmṛtiprabandhānām anyonyanimittanaimittikabhāvena bījāṅkurvad anādisaṃbandhaḥ tathā^ eva śāstraprakarṣayor īśvaracetasi nityapravṛttaprabandharūpeṇa pravartamānayor anādyantaḥ saṃbandhaḥ|  tatrotkarṣo jñanasya kāryam eva jjānam api tasya kāraṇam eva||

[55,22] anyeṣāṃ vyākhyānam nimittaśabdaḥ pramāṇavācī| tasya śāstraṃ nimittaṃ pramāṇam tena hi tadutkarṣaḥ pramīyate| śāstraṃ punaḥ kiṃpramāṇakam īśvaranirmalasattvapramāṇam| viśuddhasattvapraṇītatvād dhi śāstrasya prāmāṇyam yathā manvādīnām| tathā ca śrutiḥ yakiṃcinmanur abhyavadat tad bheṣajam iti yathā ca loke guruṇā 'bhihitam iti||

[56,8] etayoḥ śāstrotkarṣayoḥ pramāṇaprameyatayā nirādyantaḥ sambandhaḥ||

[56,9] nanu ca naiva-īśvarasattve [ā]vartamānena śāstreṇacitta tadutkarṣaḥ 
pramīyate| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| tat-prabhavatvāt tatra vartamānam| varta(māna)ta eva tat-prabhavam api śāstraṃ tasmin sarvajñatvād eva| loke api yato yat prabhavati vartate tat tasmin yathā tantvādiṣu paṭādi| tat prabhavatvam anumānāgamābhyām avagamyate| śāstrapramāṇakaḥ utkarṣaḥ īśvarapramāṇakaṃ śāstram iti bhinnāśrayatvāc chāstrotkarṣayor netaretarāśrayatvam||

[56,14] nanu ca śāstrād īśvaraprāmāṇyaṃ tat prāmāṇyāc ca śāstraprāmāṇyam iti syād anyonyāśrayatvam usyate^ īśvaraprāmāṇyasyānumānena siddhatvād adoṣaḥ||

[56,16] etasmād etad bhavati| kasmāt īśvaraprakṛṣṭasattvāśrayayor niratiśayajñānotkarṣayoḥ kāryakāraṇarūpe prabandhanityayayā nityatvād etad bhavati sa tu sadā^ eva muktaḥ sadā^ eva-īśvara^ iti| (ity atrāha) tac ca tasyaiśvaryaṃ sāmyātiśayavimuktam iti vakṣyamāṇapramāṇaphalam| etatsūtrapiṇḍārtho vā||

[56,20] atiśayavimuktatāṃ vyācaṣṭe na tāvad aiśvaryāntareṇā tadatiśayyate| kutaḥ yad^ evaiśvaryāntaraṃ tadatiśayānaṃ manyase tad eva ta aiśvaryaṃ mayā 'bhihitam| yatraivaiśvaryāntarātiśayi bhavaty aiśvaryaṃ sa^ eva^ īśvara^ iti yāvat| na ca tatsamānam aiśvaryam asti| samāndadharmaprakarṣāsaṃbhavāt||

[56,24] na hy ekasmin rājye dvayoḥ sabhavo rājñoḥ| ekasya vā dve rājye| tathā pratipādayati tad-dvayos tulyatvaṃ prasaktam| na hy ekasyānyataratra 
prākāmyam abādhitvā^ anyatarasmin pravṛttir upapadyate| dvayoś ca yugapad ekārthayor ekasya prākāmyeṇa pravṛttir eva nāsti| prakāmite vastuni samānaprakarṣasyārthasya viruddhatvāt| yena ca samānaprakarṣatā tasya tena^ eva sa bādhyate| (te ca) tac ca na vācā bādhanam itarasya rūpātiśayā^ āvṛttyatiśayāś ca viruddhyante| sāmānyāni tv atiśayaiḥ saha vartanta^ iti vakṣyāmaḥ||

[57,13] tasmād yasya sāmyātiśayaviproṣitam aiśvaryaṃ sa^ īśvara^ iti 
pradhānapuruṣavyatiriktaḥ pūruṣaviśeṣa^ īśvaraḥ siddhaḥ||1.24||

[57,15] yathāpratijñātasya-īśvarasya siddhau pramāṇam upanyasyate tatra niratiśayaṃ sarvajñabījam| tatra tasmin yathāpratijñātalakṣaṇa^ īśvare niratiśayaṃ bhavitum arhati| kiṃ tad iti yad^ idam avyabhicāri 
pratyakṣānumānajñānam atītaviṣayaṃ anāgataviṣayaṃ(vartamānakāla)pratyutpanna[viṣaya]m atītādisamuccayaviṣayam atīndriyaviṣayaṃ ca| atīndriyayor atītānāgatayoḥ saṃbhavād atīndriya[grahaṇa]m ity ucyate||

[57,20] tac cātīndriyagrahaṇaṃ trividham sūkṣmaviṣayaṃ vyavahitaviṣayaṃ viprakṛṣṭaviṣayañ ca| alpaṃ bahv iti| yathāprasiddham etad vivardhamānaṃ sātiśayatvena sarvajñabījaṃ yathā dhūmavijñānam agnivijñānasya| katham yatra prakarṣakāṣṭhāṃ pratipadyate| jñānasya jñātrāśrayatvāt yatreti jñātā^ ucyate yasmin jñātarīti| sa sarvajñaḥ siddhaḥ||

[57,24] tathā śaktir api sātiśayatvena vivardhamānā yatra kāṣṭhāṃ prāpnoti sa sarvaśaktiḥ| tena jagannirmāṇasthāpanopasaṃhārādikartṛtvasiddhiḥ| tathaiśvaryasyāpi vardhamānasya yatra kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ sa parama-īśvaraḥ| tataś cāśaktyādiviparyayadoṣābhāvasiddhiḥ||

[58,1] athāpi syāt ajñānasyāpi vivardhamānasya svabhāvasiddhasya kāṣṭhāprāptasya tasminn īśvare siddhir iti na jñānavirodhāt| na hi jñānājñānayor viruddhayor ekasminn abhisaṃyogo 'sti| na hi jñāne 
prakarṣavati tatrājñānaṃ saṃbhavati||

[58,4] yatra hi jyotir vivardhate tatra tamo 'pakṛṣyata^ eva| viparyayo 'pīti cet tac ca na sati jyotiṣi tamaso 'nupalambhāt| vidyamānaṃ hi tamo jyotiṣā tiraskriyate na kadācid vidyamānaṃ jyotis tamasā 'abhibhūyate||

[58,7] yad^ api jaladharoparodhāt prāvṛṣi sveradarśanam tad api dṛṣṭavyavadhānamātram| na punaḥ prakāśa^ eva torodhīyate tamovat| tasmānnityā na yatra jñānavivṛddhis tatrājñānasya bhāvaḥ samitarīva tamasaḥ| nityātyantatirohitarajas-tamo-guṇasattvopādānāt||

[58,10] kiñ ca nājñānasyāvastuviṣayatvād vivṛddhiḥ saṃbhavati| jñānasya jñeyavastuviṣayatvād abhivṛddhyupapattiḥ| ajñānasya hi vastuviṣayatve jñānatvaprāptiḥ||

[58,12] yaś cāpi sthāvarādhiśv ajñānaprakarṣa^ iti nāsau prakarṣaḥ| atyantajñānābhāvāpekṣayā hi sa^ ucyate.ajñānasya prakarṣe hi saty ajñānasahasrasya na jñānena^ekena bādhaḥ syāt| tathā[dā] hi pratyajñānaṃ jñānentpannenānekena bādhanaṃ syāt| tasmānnityā nājñānasya prakarṣaḥ sverūpeṇa||

[58,16] yasya hi sātiśayatvaṃ tasya kāṣṭhāprāptir dṛśyate| yathā 
phalādiparimāṇānām ākāśaparyantatā| tathā sarvajñabījam api parāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ prāptum arhati||

[58,18] samastaṃ vyastaṃ ca sarvaṃ vastu-jātam ekasya pratyakṣaṃ jñeyatvāt ghaṭādivat||

[58,19] jñānaviṣayatvena puruṣārthatvāt ghaṭādivad eva||

[58,20] utpattimatī ca pṛthivī sāvayavatvāt ghaṭādivat||

[58,21] prāṇikarmatatsādhanavibhāgavideka(gadhaika)niṣpāditaṃ jagat tadupabhogyasthānādiracanāviśeṣavattvāt prāsādādivat||

[58,23] anekaprāṇyupabhogavijñānavad ekanirvartitā pṛthivī anekaprāṇavadupabhogyatvāt brīhiyavādivat||

[58,25] anekaprayojanārthinā ekena nirmitā pṛthivī gṛhādivad eva||

[58,26] sarvaprāṇyāśrayo girinadīsamudāyaḥ tannivāsaparibhoga-yogyavijñānavad ekakuśalotpāditaḥ 
prakṛtahetunidarśanābhyām eva||

[59,1] anekaprāṇabhāk-prakāśādisāmarthyajñānavatā ekena nirvartitaḥ savitā prakāśātmakatvāt pradīpādivat||

[59,3] niyatakālam ādityodayāstamayādisañcaraṇam etat-prayojanajñānavad ekaprayuktaṃ pratiniyatakālatvāt kṛṣyādivat||

[59,5] budhimad-eka-īśvaraprayuktā sūryagrahacandranakṣatrādipravṛttiḥ pravṛtter duḥkhātmakatve sati niyatasamayatvāt dharmaśiṣyabhṛtyādipravṛttivat ||

[59,7] candramaso vṛddhikṣayau tithyādikālajñānavataikenaprayuktau kālaparicchedakatvāt ghaṭikā[harā]divat||

[58,9] kalaparicchedajñānavataikena nirvartataś candramāḥ vṛddhikṣayayuktatvāt ghaṭikādivad eva||

[59,11] buddhimad ekasvāmikaṃ jagat anyonyaviruddhasaṃhatānekaprāṇināyakādhiṣṭhitabahu-maṇḍalatvāt itthaṃjātīyakaikarājyavat||

[59,13] sarvam etat sādhanasādhyavyāpārāvasthaṃ pratyakṣaṃ kasyacid ekasyāvāntaravirodhānugrahavattayā^ ekaprayojanatvāt sāṃgrāmavastuvat| 59,15 saṃhatatvāt kulālādivastuvat| sā^ eva pratijñā||

[59,16] yugapac ca sarvaṃ pratyakṣam eka(ta)syānekatve (tva)saty anyonyasaṃbandhāt prasiddhasambandhānekārthavat||

[59,18] agnihotrādisādhanasādhanīye kasyacit pratyakṣe sādhyasādhanarūpatvāt bhujitṛptivat||

[59,29] sarvāḥ śaktayaḥ kasyacit pratyakṣāḥ vastutvāt ghaṭādivat|| a[vā]ntaraprativbandhābhāve sarvaṃ vastu-jātaṃ nairantaryeṇa kasyacit 
pratyakṣaṃ śabdādimattvena sambandhāt drāghīyasīm iva śaṣkulīṃ bhakṣayataḥ śabda^ādayaḥ||

[59,23] svatantratayā kenacid ekena sarvaṃ pratyakṣaya^ upalabhyate jñeyatvenābhimatatvāt nāṭyavastuvat||

[59,25] apratibandhena kasyacit pratyakṣopalabhyaṃ sarvam aneka(sattve)tve saty anyonyasaṃbandhāt nāṭyasaṃbandfinartakādivat||

[60,1] saṃsārarahitaḥ sarvajñaḥ ajñānābhāvāt muktātmavat| kleśādirahita^ īśvaraḥ apratibandhajñānatvāt siddhayogivat||

[60,3] etena kleśādipratibandhājñānarahitatvena cakṣur-ādīndriyagrāmanirapekṣasarvaviṣayajñānatvaṃ siddham| sarvārthagrahaṇasamarthasya parama-īśvaracittasya sarvavyāpino yugaptsarvaviṣyayogitvāt| evaṃ sati samastavastugrahaṇābhāve kāraṇābhā(vānu)vopapatiḥ||

[60,7] nāpi mūrtimatpratibandho apy ākāśavad eva sarvaviṣayayogino api cittasya| viṣayānāvasthānād āvir-bhāvatiro-bhāvasmṛtisaṃkalpānavasthitatvopapattiḥ| viṣayākāratayā vṛttilābhād arkaprabhāvat||

[60,10] na hi kleśādyāvaraṇāvaddhannaṃ cittasattvam tasyādharmādiyogāt /sarvagatasya sarvārthasyāpi sattvasya^adharmādipratibandhakaniruddhasvakīyapravṛttimārgatvād indriyādidvārāpekṣitvam upapadyate| yathā^ apavarakādyāvaraṇāvṛtaḥ 
pradīpas tadāvaraṇagatacchidradvāreṇa bahiḥ prakāśayati| sa^ eva tu 
pradīpaḥ prabhinnāpakarakāvaraṇaḥ sannanapekṣya tat-tacchidramārgaṃ samantataḥ prakāśayati||

[60,15] tathā^ eva-īśvarasattvam kleśādyāvaraṇayogābhāvād asati viṣayaviśeṣanirodhikāraṇe yugapad aśeṣārthagrahaṇam| tatac ca svātilaṅghanānatilaṅghanādidoṣābhāvaḥ||

[60,17] sarvasya viṣayatvāt sarvam eka-īśvaraṃ jagat 
paripālakāpekṣasthānādyādyanekavastu-viśeṣatvāt 
prasiddhaivaṃbhūtaikarājyavat||

[60,19] tathā niyatasthitihetvanekopakaraṇatvāt anyonyābhibhavanīyaprabhūtataradurgayuktānekadeśatvāt abhaṅguravyavasthaśarīrabhedavattvāc ca pratītaivaṃvidhaikarājyavat||

[60,22] anekakartṛbhoktṛkriyāsādhanaphalasaṃbandhaviśeṣajñānavad upadiṣṭāni varṇāśramādyanuṣṭhānāni phalārthibhiḥ pratyavāyabhīubhis taiś cānuṣṭhīyamānatvāt bhaiṣajyarasāyanādyanuṣṭhānavat||

[60,25] tathā tadupadeśāḥ parārthatvāt upadeśatvāt vidvacchradveyatvāt upadeśād ṛte manuṣyānavagamyārthatvāc ca bhaiṣajyādyupadeśacat||

[60,27] śarīrendriyāṇi sarvatatprayojanavid ekanimittakāraṇābhinirvartitāni niyamakriyāsthitihetusādhanatvāt 
prāsādagṛhadāruyantrādivat||

[61,1] niyataprayojanaracanāvattvāc ca tadvad eva||

[61,2] tathendriyāṇi karaṇatvāt vādīvṛkṣādānādivat||

[61,3] tathā sarvaṃ kāryaniyatopādānatvāt bhoktrarthatvāt bhogasādhanatvāc ca ghaṭādivat| utpattimattve apy ete hetavaḥ||

[61,5] evaṃ madhyamaparimāṇatvāt anekavināśahetusaṃbandhayogyatvāt bhokādhiṣṭhānatvāt kriyāphalasādhanatad-rūpādimattvāc ca ādhīyamānāpacīyamānāpūryamāṇāpanīyamānadahyamānābhidyamānānekadeśatvāt vinaśvarī pṛthivī||

[61,8] śarīrādivann aśvarāṇi pṛthivyādibhūtāni anyonyanāśakaśaktimattvāt śastrapuruṣādivat||

[61,10] tathā^ eva virodhāt puruṣaviparītatvāc ca^ utpttivināśadharmakam ākāśaṃ sākṣād upakaraṇatvāt ghaṭādivat bāhyendriyagrāhyaguṇāśrayatvāc ca||

[61,12] tasmānnityā niratiśayaśaktijñānāiśvaryaḥ parama-īśvaraḥ siddhaḥ||

[61,13] atrocyate^ īśvaraśabdārthaḥ sarvajño na bhavati 
prameyatvavastutvaśabdābhidheyatvātmatvasādhanāpekṣopalabdhṛtvādibhyaḥ asmadādivat||

[61,15] īśvaraśabdo api niratiśayajñānayuktābhidhāyī na bhavati śabdatvāt ghaṭādiśabdavat||

[61,17] sarvajñasarva-īśvaraśūnyaṃ jagat anekaprāṇivyapāśrayatvāt anyonyaviruddhanekanāyakarājyavat||

[61,19] bhūtabhaviṣyatkālayor apīdānīm iva sarvajñasyānupalabdhāraḥ sarvaprāṇinaḥ prāṇitvāt asmadādivat||

[61,21] vādyantarapaikalpitaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarva-īśvaraś ca nāsti anupalabhyamānatvāt śaśaviṣāṇavat||

[61,23] tathā^ asarvajño 'nīśvaraś cāśarīratvāt muktātmavat| saśarīraś ced dharmādyapekṣaśarīratvaṃ tathā ca saṃsāritvam asmadādivad eva| aśarīratvam eva^ iti cej jagatkartṛtvānugrāhakatvādyanupapattiḥ muktātmanidarśanena||

[61,26] viśiṣṭaparibhoga-yogyajagad-racanā-viśeṣopapattiḥ svabhāvād eva kaṇṭakatīkṣṇatvakumudakamalasaṅkocavikocādivat| niśākaradivākaratārakādigamanāgamanādy api svābhāvikaṃ lohāyaskāntamaṇivat||

[62,1] atrābhidhīyate svābhāvikī tāvan na bhavati pṛthivyadiracanā| dṛṣṭānto na^ eva tatsādhanasya vidyate| kaṇṭkādīnāṃ ca taikṣṇyaṃ na svābhāvikam abhyupeyate||

[62,3] agnyādīnām iva^ ūṣṇyādi svabhāvo vastu-dharmatvād dhetoḥ 
pṛthivyādiracaneti cet prāsādādiracanāyā vastudharmatvāt syād anaikāntikatvam||

[62,5] atha svābhāvikatvād iti cet asiddhatvam jagadracanāyā dharmādharmaphalopabhogārthaprayuktatvābhyupagamāt| kartṛdharmādharmāstitvam upariṣṭāt sādhayiṣyate||

[62,7] tasmāt pṛthivyādiracanā svābhāvikī na bhavati 
prāṇyupabhogārthatvāt prāsādādiracanāvat| candrādipravṛttir na svābhāvikī pravṛttitvāt asmadādipravṛttivat||

[62,10] asmadādipravṛttir na svābhāvikīṭicet nāgnyuṣṇatvavan nityatvaprasaṅgāt| na hy agnyuṣṇatvaṃ svābhāvikaṃ san niyataṃ nimittam ātmalābhāya kāṅkṣati dharmisattāvyatirekeṇa| kriyāphalaracanā-ādīnāṃ tu viśiṣṭanimittākāṅkṣṇam ātmapratilambhāyeti na svābhāvikatvam||

[62,14] niyatanimittakāṅkṣṇam api prāsādādiracanānāṃ svabhāva^ iti cet astu tathāvishasvabhāvatvam| tathā api pṛthivyādiracanāyā niyatanimittakāṅkṣṇasvabhāvatvaṃ prāsādādivad eva| tataś ca nāmni vipratipattir na doṣāya kārkāntarāpekṣatvād iti pratijñā pratirūpakāriṇī ca||

[62,18] bhavadbhir uktani [anumānāni] lokānumāgamavirodhāt [apramāṇāni] anumānavirodhaṃ tāvat sātiśayatvādibhyo brūmaḥ| āgamavirodhañ ca yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit, eko vaśītyevamādibhyaḥ samācakṣmahe|

[62,20] samastalokavirodhaṃ cāpi| sarva evā nārogopālaṃ saṃvyavaharamāṇāḥ satataṃ śivanārāyaṇādināmnā praṇihitacetasaḥ 
pratiṣedhasv api saṃbhinnabuddhiṣu^ akarṇīkṛtatatpratiṣedhaviṣayapallavāḥ 
parama-īśvaraṃ praṇatam ūrdhānaḥ kusumānjaliprabhṛtibhir arcayanti| phalaṃ cāpi tata^ eva prārthayante||

[63,1] atha ced brūyāt sarvajñābhāvapratipādibhir asmadanumānair itarapratijñānāṃ vridha^ ity atrocyate 63,4   na^ etāny anumānāni| katham 
prathamaṃ tāvat pratijñāiva duṣṭā| yadi tāvad īśvaraśabdārthaḥ sarvajño na bhavatīti yuṣmadabhipreta^ īśvara^ ucyeta tathā ca siddhasādhyatā syāt||

[63,6] athāsmadabhipretaḥ tathāpi yuṣmākam asiddhaviśeṣyatā bhavet||

[63,7] atha-īśvaratvasyāsarvajñatvam ucyate tathāpi siddhaṃ sādhyate||

[63,8] tathā^ eābhyupagamavirodho api| katham aiśvaryasarvajñabījatvayor vivardhamānatvāt kāṣṭhāprāptiḥ parimāṇavad evābhyupagamanīyā| tathā kāṣṭhāprāptaiśvaryasarvajñatvavācināv apīśvarasarvajñaśavdāv eṣitavyau| kāṣṭhāprāptiś ca niravadhikatvam| yathā parimāṇādīnām ātmākāśādyanantaparimāṇāntatvam| tathā cānumānābhyupagamasvavacanavirodhāḥ syuḥ||

[63,12] kiñcānyat kāṣṭhāprāpta-īśvaratvasya śabdārthasya parityāge śabdārthavādaḥ prāpnoti| na hi śabdaḥ parityaktābhidheyaikadeśa uccaritaḥ||

[63,14] atha parimāṇāder api niravadhikaniṣṭhatvaṃ neṣyate siddhāntavirodhaḥ| ātmākāśādīnāṃ hi sarvagatatvābhāve madhyamaparimāṇatvānityatvādidoṣaprasaṅgāś ca| athāpy ātmanaḥ sarvatra kāryadarśanāt sarvagatatvam tatrāpi śakunyādyātmanāṃ manuṣyagocarākāśādigatatvaṃ kena vāryate||

[63,19] athānumānavirodhān nāvadhāryata^ iti cet ātmādīnām api 
prameyatvavastutvadibhyaḥ pṛthivyādivad ity anumānavirodhodbhāvanā śakyate [kartum] vaktum| na ca tāvatā'trānumānavirodhitvam anumānābhāvaprasaṅgāt||

[63,22] tathā^ anumānābhāva^ eva^ iṣyata^ iti cet pratyakṣe apy anāśvāsaḥ syāt| atha niścīyamānabuddhyutpatteḥ pratyakṣapramāṇatvam anumānasyāpi tarhi niścitabuddhisamudhbhavād astu prāmāṇyam| atha dṛṣṭasambandhadhūmadiviṣayam evānumānam iti cet tatrāpy anumānābhāva^ evānagniḥ dhūmavān predeśaḥ 
pratyakṣānupalbhyamānāgnisambandhe sati pradeśatvāt agniśūnyopalabhyamānapradeśavad iti||

[63,27] atha pramāṇāntarasaṃvādadṛṣṭasambandhaviṣaye 'numānasyānumānatvam ucyate pratyakṣam api pramāṇāntarasaṃvādāt pramāṇaṃ bhavet pramāṇatvāt anumānavad eva| (64,1) apramāṇaṃ syāt pramāṇāntarasaṃvādābhāvāt sāmānyatodṛṣṭādivad iti| tasmānnityā na sāmānyatodṛṣṭasya pramāṇatvaṃ niścitabuddhitvād bādhituṃ śakyam||

[64,3] kiṃ ca sāmānyatodṛṣṭasya mānatvam anabhyupagacchataḥ svaśarīramaraṇasambandhalakṣaṇasyānupalabdhapūrvatvāt sarpādibhyo bhayānupapattiḥ| vyādhipratīkārāya cikitsādiprayogo  ghaṭeta| maraṇaśaṅkākāraṇābhāvāt| tathā janmanoapy upalambhābhāvād ātmaśarīrasyājatvaṃ cintyeta| hānopādānaniyogābhāvaś ca syāt||

[64,7] tasmāt sāmānyatodṛṣṭaparimāṇatvādisātiśayatvasāmānyād īśvarajñānaniravadhikatvasiddhiḥ||

[64,9] kiṃ cānyat īśvarasarvajñatvasya pratiṣiddhyamānatvāt sattvābhyupagamaḥ ā(nupa)padārthasya pratiṣedhāsambhabāt| na hi kevalo neti (na) prayujyate| īśvaraḥ sarvajño no bhavatīty ucyate| tatra kvacit sarvajñatvam abhyupagataṃ bhavati||

[64,12] tadyathā śaśasya viṣāṇaṃ nāsti vandhyāyāḥ suto nāsti kusumam ākāśasya nāstīti kvacid vidyamānānām eva viṣāṇasutakusumādīnāṃ śaśādisaṃbandhena pratiṣedhaḥ tadvad ihāpi syāt||

[64,15] kiṃ ca^ īśvaraḥ sarvajño na bhavatīti īśvaraśabdārthasarvajñatvasambandhapratiṣedhādinā īśvarād anyasya sarvajñatvābhyupagamaḥ| sambandhināv anabhyupagamya sambandhapratiṣedho hi na śakyaḥ kartum||

[64,18] sa^ eṣa yuktaḥ sambandhaḥ pratiṣidhyate^ ayuktaś cābhyupagamyate| tat-pariśramamātram| īśvarasarvajñayoḥ prasiddhapadārthayor apratiṣedhāt| na hy anantaparimāṇam ākāśaṃ na bhavatīty ākāśasya prasiddham anantaparimāṇatvaṃ pratiṣidhyāprasiddhānantaparimāṇatveṣu ghaṭādiṣu tat vyavasthāpayituṃ yuktam||

[64,22] atha sambandhināv eva-īśvarasarvajñarve pratiṣidhyate svavacanavirodhinī pratijñā syāt| īśvarasarvajjatvayor īśvarasarvajñaśabdārthatvābhyupagamāt| na hi bhavaty agnir agniśabdārtho na bhavatīti||

[64,25] athāprasiddhapadārthāv īśvarasarvajñaśabdau tathā api tadarthapratiṣedhānupapattiḥ| aprasiddhasyārthasya prāptyabhāvāt| na hi kiṃcid aprasiddham etacchikhare meṣakuṭumbinīṃ pratiseddhum arhati| kiṃ ca śabdopādānānarthakyaṃ ca| aprasiddhapadārthatvāt||

[65,1] atha prasiddhārthayor īśvarasarvajñaśabadayor niravadhika-īśvarasarvajñatvābhyām aprasiddhābhyāṃ gaganasya^ iva kusumaiḥ saṃbandhaḥ kaiś cit kalpitaḥ sa pratiṣidhyeta tathāapy ahyupetārthapratiṣedhāt svavacanavirodhiny eva pratijñā syāt| sātiśayaniratiśayāneka-īśvarasarvajñavyaktimatsāmānyayor īśvarasarvajñaśabdārthatvenābhyupagamāt||

[65,5] prasiddhaṃ hi loke^ īśvarasarvajñaśabdayor utkarṣāpakarṣa-yuktānekavyaktiviṣayabuddhyutpādakatvam| na hi grāmādhipatir īśvara^ iti dvitrigrāmaviṣayādhipataya^ uttarottara-īśvarā na bhavanti| yo vā kuṭumbādhipatir īśvaro na bhavati sarvaiś cāsāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ arthavān īśvaraśabdaḥ||

[65,9] tathā vaiyākaraṇaḥ sarvajña^ ity ukte nādhikavijñānayukto na bhavati| niravadhikau sāvadhikau vā^īśvarasarvajñāv īśvarasarvajñacabdayor arthāv abhyupagamyeyātām| atas tatpratiṣedhād abhyupagamavirodhinī pratijñā syāt ātmākāśādīnāṃ mahattvasya mahacchabdārthatvapratiṣedhavat||

[65,13] atha-īśvarasarvajñau niravadhikau na staḥ anupalabhyamānatvāt anīśvaradvitīyaśirovad ity ucyeta tathāpi śabdārthābhyupagamād abhyupagamavirodhinī pratijñā||

[65,15] sātiśayatvādibhir aiśvaryasarvajñatvabījādiliṅgair nirabadhika-īśvarasarvajñatvayor upalabhyamānatvān niravadhikaparimāṇatvavad ity asiddhir anupalabdheḥ||

[65,17] athāviśeṣya^ eva-īśvaraśabdārthaḥ sarvajño na bhavatīty ucyeta vastutvādihetubhir asmadādivad iti tadā sarvapramāṇavirodhinī 
pratijñā syāt| prasiddha-īśvaraśabdārthasyaprākṛtasarvajñatvasya sarvapramāṇasiddhatvāt| yathā īśvaraśabdārthaḥ [sarvajñena bhavati] 
prameyatvādibhir hetubhir ghaṭādivad ity ukte yat brūyāt tad evāhaṃ vakṣyāmi||

[65,21] iha sarvajñaśūnyaṃ jagat| kālo vā diśa^ ākāśaṃ vā prāṇimad deśo vā 
prāṇino vā sarve sarvajñaśūnyāḥ| pramāṇāni vā sarvajñāviṣayāṇi| pramātāro vā sarvajñasyāpratipattaraḥ ity evaṃjātīyāsu pratijñāsu niravadhika-īśvare sarvajñe vā pratiṣidhyamāne 
prasiddha-īśvarasarvajñaśabdārthābhyupagamavirodhāḥ pratijñādoṣā vacanīyāḥ||

[65,25] nanu ca vandhyāsuto nāstīti tacchūnyaṃ jagat kālo veti 
pratijñāyamāne yathoktapratijñādoṣaprāptir bhavet naiṣa doṣaḥ vandhyāsutasattālakṣaṇasya pratiṣeddhavayasya vākyārthasya-īśvarasarvajñaśabdārthavad aprasiddhatvāt kevalaśabdaprāpitasannidhānabuddhimātrapratipanne padārtha[padārthe pratiṣedha]vyāpāro'stīti vandhyāsutāstitvasya (vā nāstitvasya) nābhyupagamo 'sti| tato nābhyupagatapratiṣedhadoṣaḥ||

[66,1] nanu ceśvaraḥ sarvajño na bhavatīty atrāpi buddhimātraṃ prāpyamāṇaṃ pratiṣidhyate na kvacit api prāpnuvad vastu (na) pratiṣeddhaṃ śakyate pratihastineva hastī| yadi ca nañā vastu nirākariṣyate nañmātreṇaiśvaravādipakṣatvam api [pakṣe api] nirākariṣyate(ataḥ) tasmād ihāpi busshimātrapratiṣedha iti nābhyupagatapratiṣedhaḥ pratiñādoṣaḥ||

[66,5] satyam evam| kiṃ tv asti viśeṣaḥ| pramāṇasiddhavastuviṣayā buddhir vastvantare kālaviśeṣe vā pratiṣidhyate vastusāmānyāt prāpnuvatī yathā ghaṭo nāstīti| kvacid vināpy arthena buddhir eva śabdapratyupanītasannidhānā kalpitavastvantarā pratiṣidhyate| yathā vandhyāsuto nāstīti||

[66,9] yatra tu pramāṇāntarasiddhavastupratiṣedhas tatrāpi[sti]abhyupagatārthapratiṣedhadoṣaḥ| yathā īśvaraśabdārthaḥ sarvajño na bhavatīti| īśvarasarvajñaśabdayor abhivardhamāna-īśvarasarvajñavyaktyanugatasāmānyavacanatvena liṅgena niravadhika-īśvarasarvajñasattvāvadyotitvaṃ siddham eva| mahadbahusarvānantaparimāṇādiśabdavat||

[66,13] yathā^ evaite mahadādiśabdāḥ 
prasiddhasātiśayamahadādyanugatasāmānyārthābhidhāyinaḥ santo niravadhikamahadbahu-sarvānantaparimāṇavyaktīnām astitvāvadyotanaliṅgā bhavanti| mahadādisāmānyānāṃ sātiśayavyaktyanugatānāṃ niravadhikamahadādivyaktivyāpana-(ravyākṣīṇa)-śaktitvāt| na hi santaṃ vyāpyam arthaṃ tadvyāpakaḥ sann artho na vyāpnotīti| niravadhikāt tu 
paro vyāpyo nāstīti vyāpakasya sāmānyasya kṣayaḥ vyāpyaviṣayatvena^ eva 
prasiddhatvāt tatra| tasmāt pramāṇasiddhatvād īśvarasarvajñaśabdārthyor abhāvaṃ sādhayato 'bhyupagatārthapratiṣedha eva^ iti duṣṭā pratijñā||

 66,20  na ca^ īśvarasarvajñaśabdayor atadarthatvam anyārthatvaṃ vā kartuṃ [vaktuṃ] śakyate| tathā śaktijñānāiśvaryāṇāṃ pratipuruṣaṃ samuccitānāṃ sātiśayānāṃ niravadhikaṃ tattvaṃ ataḥ ]nānunmattaḥ] 
pratiṣeddhum utsametā^ ity anumānavirodhitvaṃ pratijñāyāḥ||

 66,23  kiṃ cānyat pari[para]kalpitasyeśvarasya pratiṣedhaṃ kuruvantaḥ 
pratyakṣaparacittāniṣṭāḥ prāpnuvanti| paravākyād iti cen na sātiśayatvādisarvajñasādhakaliṅgapratipādakavad aviśiṣṭatvāt 
paravākyasya| tādṛśavākyārthapratiṣedhe cābhyupgatapratiṣedhaḥ 
pūrvavad evānumānavirodhaś ca ||

[66,27] kiṃ ca prameyatvādihetūnām api^ asiddhatvam| kutaḥ asmābhir niratiśayajñānāiśvaryaviśiṣṭatvasyābhyupagatatvāt| atha svayam api tād_ṛṛśam abhyupagacchet tadā sarvajñasiddhiḥ abhyupgatatvāt||

67,1 kiṃ cānyat---abhyupagatatādṛkpratiṣedhe, yathā ghaṭo ghaṭno na bhavati, vastutvāt, paṭavaditi, evamihāpi syāt||

67,3 kiṃ ca---niravadhikasyānabhyupagame, prameyatvādīnām [a]pakṣadharmatvamāśrayāsiddhatvaṃ ca| tadabyupagame hi kiṃ sādhyaṃ vhavet? yo hi tṛptavantaṃ brūyāt mā .atrātsīriti, kiṃ tena kṛataṃ syāt||

67,6 athāpi syāt---niravadhikamīśvaramabhisandhāya tasya sarvajñatvaṃ niṣedhāmīti| naitadapi yuktam---sarvajñatvasyāpi niravadhikatvāt||

67,8 api ca sātiśayaiśvaryasarvajñatvayorutkarṣānutkarṣaviṣayatvena vyabhicārānneśvarasarvajñaśabdamukhyābhidheyatvam| yathā mahadādayaḥ śabdā vyabhicaritamahattvādiṣu ghaṭādiṣvamukhyāḥ, nākāśādiṣu, tathaivātrāpi niratiśayamaiśvaryaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ cāvyabhicārīti sa eva mukhyaḥ śabdārthaḥ| na ca mukhyanirākaraṇaṃ sādhimānam ṛcchati||

67,12 kiṃ ca---yathā bhavatā anumānāgamaprasiddeśvarasya prameyatvādidharmavikalpatayā sarvajñatvaṃ nirākriyate, tathaiva prameyatvādhibhiracetanatvamapi ghaṭādivadeva prāpnuyāt| avirodhāt| yathā prameyatvaṃ [a]sarvajñatvena vyāptamevamacetanatvādibhirapi||

67,15 pratyakṣavirodha iti cet---ihāpi anumānāgamavirodhaṃ kiṃ kaṭākṣeṇāpi nekṣase||

67,17 nāpi ca prameyatvādhdharmasāmānyena sapakṣe vipakṣe vā kiñcit kvacidadhyāropayituṃ śakyam| vastuviṣaye sarvatra prameyatvādīnāmastitvāt| na matimohanārthaḥ prameyatvādyupanyāsaḥ||

67,20 atha ātmatvāditi cet, muktātmavadajñatvaprasaṅgaḥ syād bhavadīśvarāṇām| pratyakṣādivirodha iti cet---atrāpy anumānādivirodhaḥ pūrvavad eva||

67,22 bhūtabhaviṣyatkālayorapi īśvarasyānupalabdhāra itīdānīntaneśvarāṇām upalabdhṛbhir anaikāntikaṃ prāṇitvād ityādi| kiṃ ca atītanāgatakālayor api sātiśayatvādiliṅair upalabdhavanta upalapsyamānāś ca prāṇinaḥ pramāṇakuśalāḥ, pramāṇakuśalatvāt, asmadādivat||

67,26 atha aśarīratvādasarvajñaḥ, muktākāśādivat iti cet---na---saśarīratvāt| vyaktāvyaktaṃ hi sarvaśarīraṃ saśarīratvādanityeśvaratvaṃ dharmādyapekṣatvādityuktatvāditi cet---na---aśarīratvāt| saśarīrāśarīratvavirodha iti cet---na---muktamucyamānaikātmano .aśarīratvasaśarīratvadarśanāt| tatra bhinnakālatvādavirodha iti cet---na---atrāpi bhinnakālābhyupagamāt||

68,4 kiṃ ca---yugapadīśvaratvādeva dvayasambhavaḥ| sakaraṇatā akaraṇatā.api īśvaratvādeva yuktā| tathā ca śrutayaḥ---``aśarīraṃ śarīreṣu'' (kaṭha 2.21) iti| tathā ``yaḥ sarveṣu bhūteṣu tiṣṭhan'' (BAU 3.7.15) iti| tathā ``yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvavit'' (Muṇḍaka 1.1.9) ityādayaḥ||

68,7 śrutīnāṃ vipratiṣedhārthatvādīśvarāpratyāyakatvam iti cet---na---ananyaśeṣatvāt| na hi karmaśeṣatve śrutiliṅgādīnāmanyatamadapi pramāṇamasti||

68,9 svādhyāyavidhiparigrahāddarśapūrṇamāsādiśeṣa iti cet---na---jyotiṣṭomādivadvidhyantarasya bhāvāt| yathaiva jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ (prathamapra)pṛthakprakaraṇādikāraṇādanyonyaśeṣaśeṣibhāvo nāsti| tathaiva ``mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ'' (BAU 2.4.5) ityādiviṣayatvānnagnihotrādiśeṣatvaṃ yuktam||

68,13 phalābhāva iti cet---na---``sarvāṃś ca lokān āpnoti sarvāṃś ca kāmān yas tam ātmānam anividya vijānāti'' (ChU 8.7.1) ityādi phalam uddiśya vidhānāt||

68,15 athaivam apyupāsanāvidhiśeṣatvāt stutir eveti cet---nātyantam asatā stutir asti| na hi ``vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devatā'' iti vāyorakṣepiṣṭatvam| kvacidasatā.api stutyādisambhava iti cet---na---anumānasiddhasyehopādānāt||

68,18 atha sarvakāryakaraṇayogādīśvarasya sarvaprāṇisukhaduḥkhādisaṃbandha iti cet---na---nimittabhedāt| dharmādinimittaḥ saṃsāriṇāṃ śarīrādisambandhaḥ| dharmādinimi(ttānāṃ)tto duḥkhādiyogaḥ| neśvarasya dharmādayaḥ| niratiśayaiśvaryādihetutvāccharīrādikāryakaraṇasaṃbandhasya| yathā grahatadāviṣṭayoḥ||

68, 22 atha cakṣurādikaraṇatvādasmadādivat sarvānupalabdhiriti cet---na---sarvaviṣayagrahaṇasamarthānāṃ pratibandhakāraṇābhāvāt| sarvagatānāṃ hi karaṇānāṃ [a]dharmādisaṃvaraṇena svaviṣayasaṅgocaḥ kriyate| yasya punarāvaraṇaṃ pratibandhaka[ma]dharmādi na vidyate, tasya tadabhāvādaśeṣasvaviṣayagrahaṇāni karaṇānīti na duṣyati||

69,1 atha vā sarvaprāṇikaraṇaiḥ [īśvaraḥ] pṛthagātmanaḥ karaṇābhāve .apyāveśagraha iva sarvamanubhavati| na ca prāṇiśūnyo viṣayaḥ kaścidvidyate, ``yaḥ pṛthivyāṃ tiṣṭhan'' (BAU 3.7.34) ``yo .apsu tiṣṭhan'' ityevamādiśruterantaryāmināmna īśvarasya sarveṣavasthitatvaśravaṇāt||

69,4 api vā prakṛṣṭameva sa(rvaṃ)ttvaṃ niryānatiśāyitaśaktijñāneśvaratvadharmakam, sarvagatatvādākāśavat sarvayogi, sarvārthatvāt sarvaviṣayaprakāśakam, dharmādharmādiviprayogādapratibandhaśaktikaṃ ca iti akāyānindriyatvapakṣe .apīśvarasarvajñatvasiddhiḥ||

69,7 cakṣurādyapekṣameva cittasya rūpādipratilambhasāmarthyamiti cet---na---aiśvaryadharmādyabhāvābhyāṃ parihṛtatvāt||

69,9 api ca cakṣurgrāhyasyāpi cakṣuṣā agrahaṇaṃ loke dṛśyate| cakṣurgrāhyaṃ sat sarvaṃ tamaḥ sannimīlitalocanairapi svasthāntaḥkaraṇairupalabhyate| tadeva cāndhatamasaṃ viṣphāritanayanairapyanyagatacittairnaiva gṛhyate| yathā gṛhyamāṇa evākāśe prakāśaḥ||

69,13 athāpi prakāśābhāvamātrameva tamo na vastviti brūyāt---tacca na---abhāvasya vastutirodhānasāmarthyābhāvāt||

69,15 atha grahaṇanimittasya prakāśasyābhāvādeva ghaṭādyagrahaṇaṃ, na vastutirodhāyakaṃ tama iti bruvīta---na---cakṣuṣaḥ prakāśakatvāt prakāśasāhāyakāpekṣānupapattiḥ| asati bhinnajātīye vastuni vastūnāṃ tirodhātṛṇi tatra yā ca yāvatī ca mātrā prakāśakatvāccakṣuṣā prakāśyeta| na hi pradīpaḥ pradīpāntaradvaitīyakāpekṣaḥ prakāśayati||

69,20 kiṃ cānyat---cakṣuṣaḥ prakāśāntarasahāyatvāpekṣatāyāṃ ca niśāyāmapi tārādhipatiprakāśadvitīyena cakṣuṣā divasa iva rūpādigrahaṇaprasaṅgastirodhānāsyāsati nimitte||

69,23 kiṃ cānyat---abhāvaścettamaḥ, candrabhāsu niśāyāṃ bhā(bhava)ntīṣu tamasā mandena na pravartitavyam| sarvātmanaiva hi tena vinaśanīyam| bhāsāṃ bhāvāt| bhāsāṃ tu vastutvānmandatvapāṭavādiviśeṣo ghaṭate, na(bhāvasya)abhāvasya nirviśeṣatvāt||

70,1 kiṃ ca sahāyābhāvāccakṣuṣa upalabdhisāmarthyābhāvādagrahaṇamityetadapi vyabhicarati| yathā satyapi viduyllatāprakāśe tīvratare vidyullatāvilāse ca kiṃ taddṛṣṭirnopalabhate| na hi vidyutah shāyake dṛṣṭacakitatvenāgrahaṇaṃ virodhābhāvādyuktam||

70,5 kiñca---cikitsāśāstre chāyā madhuraśītaletyucyate| na hyavastuno madhuraśītalatvam| tathā cākṣpiati pthyatvam| nābhāvasya pathyāpathyatvaṃ brūyuḥ||

70,7 pradīpācchāyopalabdheśca| abhāvaścettamaḥ, pradīpaprabhāmaṇḍale pradīpācchāyā kathaṃ bhavet| vastutve .api virodhādayuktamiti cet---na---viṣasarpayoriva saṃbhavāt| yathā prāṇiviyogakaramapi viṣaṃ pannagaprāṇaviyogāya na paryāpnoti, tathā pradīpatacchāyayorapi||

70,11 tasmādvastu tamaḥ, prakarṣāpakarṣavattvāt, prabhāvat| virodhidravyāpaneyatvācca, ghaṭavat\ upalabdhṛvyavadhānakaratvācca, bhittivaditi||

70,13 itaśc endriyanirapekṣam apy antaḥkaraṇam indriyaviṣayagrahaṇāya samartham| pihitakarṇavivarasya ghoṣopalambhāt| na ca pihitakarṇasaṃpuṭasya śrotrāntaramanumātuṃ śakyam| badhirasyāpi pihitaśrotrapuṭasya tadagrahaṇāt||

70,16 kiñca---pratijñājñānenāpi rūpādigrahaṇamindriyanirapekṣaṃ dṛśyate||

70,17 kiñcānyat---smṛteścānupapattiḥ syāt indriyanirapekṣasya cittasya sātiśayagrahaṇasāmarthye .api| spaṣṭāśca smṛtayaḥ svāpnā rūpādiviṣayāḥ||

70,19 yadi cakṣurādyantaḥakaraṇābhyāṃ samanvitābhyāṃ rūpagrahaṇaṃ pūrvamapi nirvartiṣyate, svapnādāvapi samuccitābhyāmeva paryagrahīṣyata| na cacivamasti| sukhādigrahaṇe svātantryaṃ cittasy sarvaprasiddham||

70,22 tasmādālokasthānīyānīndriyāṇi cittasya viṣayagrahaṇe svatantrasyaiva svataḥ| taduktam ``indriyapranāḍikayā'' (YS 1.7) iti| tathā śrutirapi---```manasā hyeva paśyati manasā śṛṇoti'' (BAU 1.5.3) iti||

71,1 athocyate---indriyāṇāmālokāpekṣāvat antaḥakaraṇamapindriyasāhāyakāpekṣameva grāhakamiti---tanna---vyabhicārāt| tātriñcarāṇāṃ vibhinnnavyālokadvaitīyakena divasa iva rūpagrahaṇam| satyapi prakāśe na rūpasya grahaṇamulūkādibhirdine||

71,5 kiñca---sādhanāpekṣasya vijñānasādhanād ṛte .api tulādau darśanamātreṇaiva suvarṇādiparimāṇajñānaṃ dṛśyate||

71,7 atha cedbrūyādānarthakyaṃ cakṣurādīnāmiti---sa vaktavyaḥ---tulādivadevānyatrārthavattvamindriyāṇāmiti| yathaiva tulādiviṣayavaimalyaprakarṣātu puruṣasya vināpi tulādisādhanena darśanamātrādeva kanakādivastuparicchedakauśalaṃ, tathaivāntaḥkaraṇasyānapekṣitacakṣurādisāhāyakasyāpi vaimalyaprakarṣādrūpādigrahaṇasāmarthyaṃ na vihanyate||

71,11 nāpi hyanekanirvartanīyaṃ kriyājātamekena nābhinirvartyate| dṛśyante ca bahubhiruttolyamānaṃ śilādikamadvitīyā hastipuruṣādayaḥ keciduttolayantaḥ||

71,13 tathaivendriyasahāyenānyeṣāmantaḥkaraṇena gṛhyamāṇā rūpādayaḥ parameśvaracittasattvenānākalita(vakarṇita)cakṣurādiparispandena kevalenaiva gṛhyante| kāṣṭhāgatatvādvaimalyaśakyaiśvaryaprakarṣasya||

71,16 saṃsāriṇāṃ tato nikṛṣṭatvādavidyādimalenaiśvaryaśaktijñānādīnāmindriyasahakāritvāpekṣitvamantaḥakaraṇasya| śilādisamuttolana iva hīnabalānām||

71,18 ye(ṣāṃ) punaḥ saṃsāriṇa eva santa īśvarībhavanti buddharṣabhādayaḥ, teṣāṃ niratiśayaśaktijñānaiśvaryānupapattiḥ| kālaparicchedyatvāt| abhyupagacchnati ca taddaraśanaślādhinaḥ kālaparicchedanīyakaṃ teṣām||

71,21 nāsmadīśvarasya kālaparicchedyatā| tatkālaistadādyakālaiśca buddhādibhiraparijñātaiśvaryādisātiśayatvādliṅgairanumeya evāsau| nāmuṣmin kāla utpanna utpadyata utpatsyamāna iti tridhā kaiścidanumīyate| niratiśayatvavirodho hi tathā[dā]syāt||

71,25 tasmāt buddhādīnāṃ siddhyapekṣatvāt, pūrvabuddhānāṃ kāladhikyaprāpteruttarakālabuddhasyapi na niratiśayaśakyaiśvaryajñānatvam| viruddham| atha tulyaśaktijñānaiśvaryāṇāmeva teṣāṃ samānarājyarājānāmivāsamānakālatvamiti brūyāt, tathāpi buddhāntarakālasya vyāptyabhilāṣavighātānniratiśayaiśvaryādyabhāvaḥ||

72, 9 abhilaṣati hi kaścit buddhantaraṃ vaśīkartum, tadviṣayaṃ ca vyāptum| aniyatatvādicchāyāḥ| buddhantarāvaśikaraṇe ca sarvavaśitvaṃ hī(nī)yeta||

72,11 atha ced ucyeta, buddhantarasyānanugrāhyatvādrāgādyabhāvācca tadviṣayavyāptāvanabhilāṣa iti---atorcyate---yathaiva prāptabuddhatvasya kaṃcitkālamavasthānamabhilāṣapūrvaka, tathā buddhāntarakālāvasthānābhilāṣeṇāvaśyaṃ bhavitavyam||

72,14 anugrāhyaprāṇyantarabhāvācca kālāntarāvasthānābhilāṣeṇa yuktaṃ bhavitum| na ca bhaviṣyatā buddhena sarve prāṇabhṛto .anugrahīpyante||

72,16 buddhatvasya ca duḥkhādyātmakatve .api prāṇyanugrahārthatvādbuddhasthiteḥ kālāntarāvasthānecchayā bhavitavyemeva| siddheśca prāgavidyādiparāmarśāt, buddhasvarūpasya ca duḥkhaśūnyādyātmakatvasya tairabhyupetatvāt, buddharṣabhādyanyatīrthakaraṇāt [karāt] niratiśayaśaktijñānaiśvaryānupapattiḥ||

72,20 kālānavacchedāttu naite doṣā asmadīśvare kramante| śaktijñānaiśvaryāṇāṃ tu niravadhikatvānumānāt sātiśayatvānavasthitatvādidoṣaḥ nātmākāśādīnāmivātiśaṅkanīyaḥ||

72,23 tathā ca darśayati---sāmānyamātropasaṃhāre kṛtopakṣayamanumānam iti| niratiśayaśaktijñānaiśvaryayuktapuruṣaviśeṣasattvasāmānyamātropasaṃhāre kṛtopakṣayaṃ kṛta upakṣayo yasyānumānasya tatkṛtopakṣayaṃ sātiśayatvāditi| taduktamanumāne---yatra prāptistatra gatimātramiti| tasmāneśvarasaṃjñāguṇādiviśeṣāṇāṃ satāmapi pratipādane samartham| sāmānyamātropasaṃhāraparatvāt||

73,1 tadetaduktaṃ bhavati| tadaivaṃ satāmapi guṇānāṃ sāmānyapratipādanakṛtakṣayatvānna pratipādakamanumāna, kimaṅga punaratyantāsambhāvyamānasannidhīnāmanumeyadharmaviruddhānāmaśayajñānadharmādharmānaiśvaryādānāṃ (sic) [na]tatpratyāyakamiti||

73,4 ata eva ca viruddhapratisādhanānupapattiḥ| sarvajñāstitvānumānavat prathamaṃ tadabhāvapratipādakānumānāpravṛtteḥ| na hyanumānasya paroktyudīkṣādharmatvamasti| na hi dhūmadarśanenāgnyanumāne vaktrantaroktirudīkṣyate||

73,7 na ca svayameva sarvajñāstitvaṃ nāstitvaṃ vā buddhi(prati_pūrvamāśaṅkyate| ghaṭādivat sarvajñasadbhāvasya svayamanabhyupagamāt| na hyanirdhāritaśaśasvarūpaḥ śaśe viṣāṇamasti nāstīti kaścidāśaṅkate||

73,10 paravākyāt pratipādyāśaṅkata iti cet---pareṇāpi kathamaprasiddheśvarasadbhāvenāśaṅkitamiti vaktavyam| atha parasya bhrāntiriti cet---na sāmānyānupalambhe bhrāntikāraṇamasti| na hi śukitkāyāṃ pikabhramaḥ kasyacidupajāyate| tasmādāha---saṃjñādiviśeṣapratipattirāgamataḥ paryanveṣyā iti| vedetihāsapurāṇayogadharmaśāstrādyāgamataḥ paryanveṣyā||

73,15 tasyātmānugrahābhāve .api tasyātmārthamavāptavyābhāvāt bhūtānāṃ cāvidyāpaṅkanimagnānāṃ saṃsārasāgarottaraṇopāyaplavopadeśyantarasyābhāvāt tadanugrahaḥ prayojanam| jñānadharmopadeśena ye .ananyaśaraṇāstasmin sarvātmanā niveditātmānastān kalpapralayamahāpralayeṣu īśvarānugṛhītāgamajñānaviśeṣeṣvācāryeṣu pralīneṣu punḥpunarjātakāruṇyāmṛtaḥ san uddhariṣāmīti pravartata iti vākyaśeṣaḥ||

73,20 tathā coktamādividvāniti---ādau vidvān ādividvān rajastamobhyāmanabhibhūtajñāna ityarthaḥ| jñānadharmādi vā tata ādīyate ityādiḥ, ādiścāsau vidvāṃśca ādividvān nirmāṇacittaṃ saṃkalpamātranirmitaṃ yogicittam āveśagrahavadupadeśanārtham adhiṣṭhāya bhagavān paramarṣiḥ paramadarśana ityarthaḥ| paramatvaṃ cāgamataḥ| darśanārthasya vā gatyarthasya vā dhātoḥ ṛṣergrahaṇam| paramarṣirīśvara eva kapilanārāyaṇādisaṃjñaḥ āsuraye provāceti||

73,26 tasmāt pradhānapuruṣavyatiriktaḥ sarvaprāṇibhāvakarmaphalaparisphuraṇajñānāttadanugrahakāraṇam īśvaraḥ siddhaḥ| saprakṛṣṭasattvadhanopādāna īśvaraḥ kathaṃ kartā? kathaṃ cānugṛhṇāti? ityevamadi na codayitavyam| āgamataḥ paryanveṣyā ityuktatvāt| anumānasya ca tadviṣayasyānupapattiḥ viśeṣasaṃbandhāgrahaṇāditi||25||

74,10 saḥ yathoktarūpaḥ parameśvaraḥ pūrveṣāmapi gurūṇām abhyudayaniḥśreyasasarvasādhanasādhyasaṃbandhopadeśināṃ guruḥ teṣāmapi jñānopadeśasya kartetyarthaḥ| tadudbhavatvāt sarvajñānānām| yathā jvalanalavaṇajaladhisamudbhavā viṣphuliṅgalavalṇakaṇāḥ||

74,13 tasya cādividvattāmavocāma kālenānavaccehdāditi| atītānāgatapratyupādīyamānatvaviśiṣṭena kālenānye guravo .avacchidyante| sa tvīśvaraḥ sarvadā mukteśvaratvena tairapy asmābhirivānumeyaḥ||

74,16 kasmāt punartasya vastutve gurutvasāmānye ca sati nāvacchedakaḥ kāla iti cedāha---yatrāvacchedārthaḥ kālo nopāvartata iti| yasminnīśvare viṣaye avacchedārthaḥ prasthādivat paricchedakaraḥ nopāvartate na paricchedakatveneśvaraṃ viṣayīkaroti sarvapariṇāmivastvavacchedakaro .api san||

74,20 nanu ceśvaraprakṛṣṭasattvasya pradhānakāryatvāt, pradhānakāryasya ca kālāvaccedyatvāt, kasmāttatsattvaṃ nāvacchidyate puruṣasvarūpaṃ hi sarveṣāmeva kālānavacchedya(miti nāśaṅkanīya)mapariṇāmitvāt puruṣasya||

74,23 atha manyethāḥ---tantrāntaraprasiddheśvaravat sattvanirapekṣa īśvara iti---tadayuktam---prakṛṣṭasattveopādānābhyupagamāt||

74,25 athāpi manvīthāḥ---īśvarasattvāt pravṛttameva kāryamavacchinatti kālo neśvarasattvamiti---tadapyayuktam---vyaktasya paricchedyatvāt| vyaktaṃ ceśvarasattvam||


75,1   athāpy abhisandadhīthāḥ pradhānāvastham eva-īśvarasattvam iti tat anupapannam jñānānupapatteḥ|| 75,3    athāpi pratipadyethāḥ satkāryābhyupagamād bījaphalaval larīkusumādivaj jñānādīnāṃ 
pradhānāvasthā yat sattvam eva^ iti tac ca na sādhu vyavahārayogyatvāt| sarveṣāṃ ca tathā saty anavacchedyatvaṃ jñānādisattāmātrasambandhāviśeṣāt prāonuyāt||

[75,6] athāpi bruvīthāḥ vyaktatve saty api kāryāṇāṃ kasyacit 
paricchedyatvaṃ kālīnāṃ kasyacin neti tac ca na nyāyavirodhāt||

[75,8] athāpi pratyavatiṣṭheyāḥ īśvarasattvasya kālaparicchedyatvam āgamasiddham iti tac ca nāvadyotakatvād āgamasya||

[75,10] athāpy ācakṣithāḥ vidyamānasya^ eva kālāparicchedyatvasyājñātasyāvadyotaka^ āgama^ iti tac cāpi na nirodhādisamanuṣṭhānanimittatvāt kālā(na)vaddhedyo vaimalyādanavaddhdyaś ceti viruddhaṃ syāt||

[75,13] atrocyate^ īśvarasattvavaimalyaprakarṣāpekṣatvāt kālānavacchedyatvasyādoṣaḥ||

[75,14] yathā^ anyeṣāṃ gurūṇāṃ yogadharmadisamanuṣṭhānanimittatvāt kālānavacchedyo vaimalyaprakarṣaḥ tathā na bhagavataḥ| katham sarvadā 'tyantatirobhūtarajastamastvād anapekṣitadharmādinimittaṃ sakalārthāvadyotakaraṃ kālānavacchedyaṃ sattvasvarūpavad bhagavatsattve jñānam || tathā tatkāryam apy utkarṣaḥ kālena nāvacchedyate| yathā cāgnir uṣṇaprakāśāv agnau kālānavacchdyau tathā bhagavatsattvasya vaimalyajñānaprakarṣās tatsattve kālānavacchdyatām upayāntīty etad apekṣayābhihitaṃ yatrāvacchedārthaḥ kālo na^ upāvartata^ iti| na tv akāryāpekṣayā hy ucyate||

[75,22] gurutvaṃ hi nāvadhimat| tad eva^ āha yathā^ asya sargasyādau jñānavaimalyaprakarṣagatyā siddhaḥ sargādijanmanām anumeyatayā siddhiḥ āgamataś cājñāyi tathā 'tikrāntasargādiṣv api tatkālajanmanāṃ tataḥ 
prāktanānām api siddhiḥ| tathā bhaviṣyatkāle apy anumānāgamābhyām avagamyate||

[75,36] asya ca sūtrasyedaṃ prayojanam yathā dṛṣṭā guravo jñānadharmādyupdeśitvād ananyaśaraṇair anavastam upāsyante tathā bhagavān ayam api sarvagurūṇām api gurutvāt tato api viśeṣeṇa nārāyaṇādināmā(dīnāma)nanyacetobhiḥ svahṛdayenāniśaṃ dhārayitavaya^ iti||

% Cf| BSBh 2.2.42: yadapi tasya% bhagavato'bhigamanādilakṣaṇamārādhanamajasramananyacittatayābhipreyate, % tadapi na pratiṣidhyate, śrutismṛtyorīśvarapraṇidhānasya prasiddhatvāt|

[76,6] yathā dṛṣṭagurava^ upāsanenābhimukhīkṛtās tadupāsanaparānanugṛhṇanti tathā 'yaṃ paramagurur apy abhidhyānamātreṇānugrahaṃ karoti| tathā ca śrutiḥ 76,8 yasya deve parā bhaktir yathā deve tathā gurau| 76,9    tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ 
prakāśante mahā-ātmanaḥ|| iti| 76,10 smṛtir api 76,11        matkarmakṛn matparamo madbhaktaḥ saṅgavarjitaḥ| nivairaḥ sarvabhūteṣu yaḥ sa māmeti 
pāṇḍava|| iti||1.26||

[76,13] īśvarapraṇidhānād ity uktaṃ sa kathaṃ praṇidhātavyaḥ kiṃ ca tat-praṇidhānasādhanam yena praṇidhātā praṇidhatta^ iti tat-praṇidhānasādhanapratipādanārtham āha tasya vācakaḥ praṇava^ iti| tasya^ īśvarasya yathā^ uktalakṣaṇasya vācakaḥ abhidhāyakaḥ praṇavaḥ sāsnādimata^ iva kośabdaḥ||

[76,17] prakarṣeṇa nūyate stūyate^ aneneti praṇavaḥ| yadi vā praṇauti stautīśvaram iti praṇava^ oṅkāraḥ| praṇdhīyate cānena bhagavān 
praṇidhātṛbhir iti praṇamanti vā^ aneneti praṇidhatte vā tena bhagavantaṃ cetasīti dhāño vakāropajanaṃ kṛtvā| śabdena hi parokṣāṇām arthānāṃ manasi praṇidhānaṃ merur indra^ iti yathā||

[76,21] tasya ca^ oṅkārasya vācyaḥ īśvaraḥ| navarthaṃ khalv api nirvacanam| avateḥ rakṣaṇādyarthān maniṣṭilopaś ceti| praṇidhātYn saṃsārād avatīti gamayati vā saṃsāriṇaṃ nirvāṇam iti niratiśayaṃ vā 
prīṇayati samādhyādyanugraheṇāvagamayati vā paramārtham ity evam ādy arthānugatam sarvathā tu nediṣṭhaṃ priyaṃ nāma bhagavataḥ||

[77,8] tena hi manasi satataṃ praṇidhīyamānaḥ prasīdati| yathā cānekaśaḥ śrutayaḥ oṃ khaṃ brahma^ om iti brahma^ ityādayaḥ| smṛtayaś ca^ oṃ tat sat iti| oṃ viśvaṃ viṣṇuḥ ityādayaḥ| oṃ-śabdasya kṛdantaṃ yanmāntam ityavayayatvād vibhaktinivṛttiḥ| 

[77,12] kim asya saṅketakṛtaṃ vācyavācakatvaṃ atha pradīpaprakāśavad asthitam iti| kiṃ cātaḥ| yadi tena bhagavatā^ anyair vā saṃvyavahārārtham asya nāmedam astv iti saṃketitam tato^ asya prāg oṃśabdāvācyatvād oṃśabdena vā praṇidhātāraḥ prāṇyadhiṣatānyena vā nāmāntareṇādhītavantas tathā^ idānīm apy astu kim oṃkāraviśeṣavacanenāthāpi pradīpaprakāśavad avasthitaṃ tathāpi prathamaśravaṇo 'pīśvara^ upalabhyeta yathā prakāśena savitā||

[77,18] kiṃ ca saṅketo apy anarthakaḥ sthitasambandhanatvāt athāpūrvaṃ saṅketyeta pūrvoktadosaprasaṅga^ iti manyate||

[77,20] atrocyate sthito^ asya vācyasya vācakena sambandho yadi nityaḥ śabdo yadi vā^ anityaḥ saṅketas tu tam eva sthitaṃ sambandham avajvalayati||

[77,22] nanu ca^ uktaṃ sthitaś cet prathamaśravaṇe api taṃ pratīyur iti| ucyate---na hi pratyāyyaprattyāyakasambandhaḥ śabdārthayoḥ sthito api sann indriyeṇa gṛhyate| yathendriyārthayoḥ| apratyakṣe hi vācyavācakaśaktī||

[77,26] tataś ca yena^ udyate śabdād artho 'numeya^ iti tad asat| sambandhāgrahaṇāt| paraprayogavyaṅgyatvāc ca sambandhasya| na hy anumānaṃ 
paraprayogam apekṣate ||

[78,5] atha brūyād anumitāt sambandhād arthānumānam iti| sa vaktavyaḥ katham anumitah śabdārthasambandhaḥ iti||

[78,7] sa cet brūyāt kāryadarśanād arthaṃ hi pratītya paścād anuminoti tatsaṃbandham| yathā cakṣuṣā rūpam upalabhya cakṣursambandham iti| sa 
prati vaktavyaḥ siddhā tarhi vinā'numānena śabdād eva pratipattir iti||

[78,10] yo hi grāṣṭrapākena^ eva kṛtārthaḥ kiṃ tasya kilośipākena| athāsakṛt prayogadarśanād gṛhyate sambandhaḥ yathā'agnidhūmayor iti cet na tu brūmaḥ na hi sahasreṣv api prayogeṣu pratyakṣeṇa śabdārthayoḥ sambandho gṛhyate yathā 'agnidhūmayor ādāv api sambandho gṛhyate| vākye apy ayam eva||

[78,14] tasmād avasthitam eva vācyasya-īśvarasya vācakena praṇavena sambandham avadyotayati saṅketo yathā sthitaṃ pitāputrasambandham| saṅketopayāpekṣatvāt prathamaśrutān nārthaḥ pratīyate| yathā cakṣusa ālokābhāve||

[78,17] nanu ca śabdānityatvapakṣe katham anitye sambandhani nityaḥ sambandha^ iti| na hy anityayor ajju-ghaṭayoḥ sambandho nityah syāt| ucyate yathā pramāṇaprameyayor indriyaviṣayayoś ca kriyākārakāṇāṃ sambandhaḥ sthita^ evānityeṣv api sambandhiṣu tadvad atrāpi sthita evāvyabhicāreṇa sambandhaḥ sargādiṣv api vācyavācakaśaktyapekṣaḥ saṅketaḥ||

[78,22] yathā^ eva rūpacakṣuṣor grāhyagrāhakaśaktyapeksayā^ eva sargādau sargas tathehāpi vācyavācakaśaktyapekṣayaiva saṅketaḥ kriyate||

[78,24] sampratipattinityatayā vṛddhaparamparayaiva gamyagamakabhāvena śabdārthayoḥ sampratipattinityā nānyathety āgamino vedavādinaḥ pratijānate| sampratipattiś ca śabdārthasambandhasya hetus tadeṣām||

[78,27] tad etad yuktaṃ bhavati yady āgamipakṣaḥ syād athānyapakṣaḥ sarvathā sthita^ eva sambandhaḥ pitāputrayor iva saṅketenābhivyasyata^ iti||

[79,12] vācyavācakayor asthitasambandhatve tu praṇavarūpeṇābhimukhībhavatīśvara^ iti nāvakalpate| na hi 
pācyapācakasambandhe na ca sthite pācakāgnyupādānaṃ pākārthaṃ kalpate| sthite tu vācyavācakasambandhe bhavavadārādhanasādhanasya praṇavasyopādānabhavakalpata^ iti tatpratipādanārthaṃ sarvaṃ bhāṣyam||1.27||

[79,16] evam avagatavācyavācakasambandhasya yoginaḥ parama-īśvaraprasādanaṃ kathaṃ kriyata^ iti tadartham āha tajjapastadarthabhāvanam tasya-īśvaravācakasya praṇavasyārdhacaturmātrasya trimātrasya vā japaḥ manasā^ upāṃcu vā āvartanaṃ japaḥ tadarthasya ca^ īśvarasya praṇavena vācakena samarpitasya buddho samāropitasya bhāvanam abhidhyānaṃ tadarthabhāvanam| kartavyam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ||

[79,21] tad evam ubhyaṃ kurvato yoginaḥ cittam ekāgraṃ sampadyate| ekāgrasampattiṃ ca tadārādhanaphalaṃ darśayati tathā ca^ uktam svādhyāyād yogamā(tiṣṭhet)sīta svādhyāyāt praṇavajapād īśvaraṃ pratyavanatadittaḥ san yogam āsīta| tadartham īśvaraṃ dhyāyet.(rasyayat) tadarthadhyānāc cāpracalitamanāḥ svādhyāyaṃ praṇavam āmanet manasā 'bhijapet| manaso 'bhijapaḥ praśasyate dhyānasyāsannataratvāt| mā viṣayapravaṇacitto bhūt iti| evaṃ svādhyāyayogasampattyā tadvirodhinā pratyayāntareṇānabhighātaḥ svādhyāyayogasampattiḥ| tayā ca praṇavajapaparama-īśvaradhyānasampattyā 
para^ ātmā parameṣṭhī prakāśate yogina^ iti||1.28||

[80,11] kiñcāsya bhavati pūrvasūtre cittam ekāgraṃ sampadyata^ ity uktam ekaṃ phalaṃ tadapekṣayā kiñceti cakāraḥ| kiñcānyad apy asti phalam āhosvic cittaikāgratvam eva^ iti tatrāha tataḥ pratyakcetanādhigamo apy antarāyābhāvaś ca| tataḥ tasmād īśvarapraṇidhanāt pratyakcetanādhigamaḥ 
pratyak svabuddhiṃ detayata^ iti pratyakcetana^ ātmā tasyādhigamo yathāvat svarūpasaṃvedanam||

[80,16] nanu cādhigata^ eva pratyātmaṃ puruṣo 'haṃ sukhī duḥkhī cāham iti| evaṃ prasiddhaṃ tataḥ kiṃ viśeṣaṇocyate^ iti| satyam evam kiṃ tv aviśiṣṭo 'sau pratyayaś sittasya vṛttyā| kiñ ca sukhī duḥkhī cāham iti sukhaduḥkhisāmānādhikaraṇyād ihāhaṃ pratyayasya cittavṛttiviṣayatvād avidyapratyaya^ eva prasiddhiḥ||

[80,20] katham tadhy adhihama^ ity āha yathāiva-īśvaraḥ puruṣaḥ śuddhiḥ kleśādimalavarjitas tata^ eva prasannaḥ svaśśhas tata^ eva kevalo nistriguṇas tata^ eva nirupasargas tāpatrayavarjitaḥ 
prakṛṣṭasattvapratisaṃvedī ca tathā 'yam api madīyaḥ puruṣaḥ śuddhaḥ 
prasannaḥ kevalo nirupasargo buddheḥ pratisaṃvedīty avagacchati||

[80,24] yathā tathā^ iti ca dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikanirdeśād īśvarakṣetrajñayor bheda^ ukto bhavati buddham uktopapatteś ca| pradhānasya pārārthyopapatteḥ kṣetrajñānāṃ cāta^ eva parasparabhedaḥ||

[81,12] kiñ cāsya bhavati ye tāvad antarāyā vyādhiprabhṛtayo vakṣyamāṇās te api cāsya-īśvarapraṇidhānān na bhavanti||1.29|| tatra ke te 'ntarāyāḥ kiyanto vā^ ity āha vyādhis tyānetyādi| saṃkhyayā tāvan navāntarāyāḥ| svarūpeṇa ca vyādhyādayaḥ| vyādhir dhātur asakaraṇavaiṣamyam| dhātavo vātapittaśleṣmāṇas teṣāṃ viṣamabhāvo vaiṣamyaṃ tac ca vātapitaśleṣmabhūyiṣṭhadravyopayogādibhyo jāyate| svayoyāgame hi dhātūnāṃ vṛddhim ācakṣate|| rasa^ upayuktāharasya 
pariṇāmaviśeṣaḥ sa ca saptadhā rasakāryatvād rasa^ ity ucyate rasalohitamedomāṃsāsthimajjāśuklākhyaḥ| tasya vaiṣamyaṃ vṛddhikṣayau karaṇavaiṣamyamandhabadhiratvādi||

[81,22] styānam akarmaṇyatā cittasya stimitam| saṃśayaḥ sthāṇur vā puruṣo vā^ iti viruddhobhayakoṭismṛk pratyayaḥ||

[81,24] pramādaḥ samādhisādhanānām abhāvanam aśīlam| ālasyaṃ kāyasya cittasya ca gurutvād apravartanam| aviratiś cittasya viṣayasamprayogasvabhāvo viṣayasamprayoganimitto vā garddhaḥ abhikāṅkṣā tṛṣṇā||

[82,12] bhtāntidarśanaṃ viparyayajñānaṃ yogasādhaneṣu tanmārge vā viparītavṛttiḥ| alabdhibhūmikatvaṃ samādhibhūmer alābhaḥ| bhūmayo vitarkādayaś cittasya dharmaviśeṣā uktā vakṣyamāṇāś ca| anavaritāyām api samādhibhūmau tataḥ katham api pracalitasya cittasya punar atrāvasthitatvam eva syāt tadalābhas tv anavasthitatvam ucyate||

[82,16] tad ete navāntarāyāḥ| antaraṃ vivaraṃ vicchedaṃ kurvanta^ āyāntīty antarāyāḥ vikṣepāḥ pratipakṣāḥ yogaprāpter upasargāḥ| ete ca saha cittavṛttibhir bhavanti| cittaṃ vikṣapanti viṣayeṣv iti cittavikṣepāḥ| kṣiptaṃ te [kṣipanti] sahaite vṛttibhiḥ paścatayībhir anyonyaprayojakabhāvena bhavanti| atheṣām abhāve vyādhiprabhṛtīnām abhāve sahāyavalābhāvān na bhavanti pūrvoktāḥ pramāṇādayaś cittavṛttayaḥ| tad bhāvabhāvitvāt tulyapratyanīkā vṛttivikṣepā ity uktaṃ bhavati||1.30||

[82,22] duḥkhaṃ yenābhihatāḥ prāṇinas tadapaghātāya prayatante ceṣṭante| tac ca trividham| ādhyātmikam adhi ātmanīty adhyātmam tatra bhavam ādhyātmikam| tac ca śarīraṃ mānasaṃ ca| śārīraṃ dhātuvaiṣamyādinimittam| mānasam iṣṭavighātādihetukam| ādhibhautikam adhi bhūteṣv ity adhibhūtam tatra bhavam ādhibhautikam| tac ca paśumṛgādyupanipatitam| ādhidaivikam ity adhi deveṣv ity adhidevam tatra bhavam ādhidaivikam| adhyātmadibhyaṣ ṭhak ity upasaṃkhyānāt ṭhak| anuśatikāditvād ubhayapada_Vṛddhiḥ| tac cāpi vātavarṣādinimittam| tad ekam eva sannimittabhedena rajorūpaṃ bhidyate|| 

[83,13] daurmanasyam icchāvighātāc cetasaḥ kṣobhaḥ ākulī-bhāvaḥ saścalanam| yad^ aṅgāny ejayati kampayati tadaṅgam ejayatvam| (na tac ca 
prāṇāyāmādy athābhyāsaprabhavaś cāsāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ api tathā samudbhavaḥ) yad bāhyaṃ vāyum ācāmati bāhyavāyor ākarṣaṇabāhulakaṃ [lyam] niṣkramaṇam andatā ca śvāsaḥ| tadviparītavyāpāraḥ praśvāsaḥ yat kauṣṭhyaṃ vāyuṃ nissārayati| ete vikṣepasahabhuvaḥ vikṣepaiḥ sahaite vikṣiptacittasya^ eva bhavantīti vikṣepasahabhuvaḥ| samāhitacittasyaite na bhavantīti yāvat| yāvad yāvac cetaḥ samādhīiyate tāvat tāvan na bhavantīti| tābhyām evābhyāsavairāgyābhyām upaśamayitavyāḥ| nānyaṃ pratipakṣam apekṣante|| 

[83,21] abhyāsavairāgye muktvā na hi parameśvarapraṇidhāyinyaḥ[bhyaḥ] 
prabhāvo bhavatīti gamyate| antarāyābhāvaś cety ukyā prāktanād apīśvarapraṇidhānād antarāyā nirudhyante ||1.31||

 abhyāsavairāgyābhyām tannirodha^ ity uktam| tatra vairāgyaviṣayaḥ 
pratipāditaḥ| idānīm abhyāsasya viṣayam upasaṃharann āha tatpratisedhārtham ekatattvābhyāsa^ iti| teṣāṃ vikṣepāṇāṃ pratiṣedhāya tat-pratiṣedhārtham| ekaṃ tattvam ekatattvam| tad ca^ ekaṃ tattvaṃ vastutvānn abhyasituṃ śakyam| na hy ātmādivastūnāṃ (84,7) śilanaṃ vaktuṃ śakyate| svamāhābhāgyāvasthitāni hi tāni na cittamātrāṇi| vastūnāṃ tu cittatantratāṃ pratiṣedhayiṣyati 'na ca^ ekacittatantraṃ vastu' ityādinā| tasmād etad āśaṅkamāno bhāṣyakāra^ āha^ ekatattvālambanaṃ cittam abhyased iti| sūtre apy ekatattvābhyāsa^ iti samāsād ayam artha^ upātta eva|| 

[84,1] nanu na^ ekaṃ cittam avasthitam asti yasya^ ekatattvāvalambanaḥ 
pratyaya^ āpadyeta| pratyayā eva cittam te ca pratyayaṃ niyatāḥ kṣaṇapraṇāśinac ca| pratyātmasaṃvedyaṃ ca pratyayānām anavasthitatvam| nāpi pratyayavyatirekeṇāvasthitaṃ pratyayi samānasantatipatitattvāt sād_ṛṛśyanibandhanaḥ| yathā sa^ eva pradīpaḥ ta eva keśā iti||

[84,16] tad apy āha pratyahaṃ [prati]pratyayaṃ pratyayī bhinnaḥ 
pratyayebhyo'yuantaparasparabhinnebhyo 'nanyatvāt| eka-ekapratyayasvarūpasadvartamānāhaṃ-pratyayaviṣayaḥ pratyayī bhūtabhaviṣyatkālāhaṃpratyayaviṣayāt pratyayino bhinnaḥ bhinnapratyayaviṣayatvāt ghaṭat(gha)paṭa^ iva| yadi vā bhinnakālatvāt vinaṣṭabhaviṣyadghaṭā_Bhyāṃ vartamāno ghaṭaḥ[iva]| ahaṃpratyayā sarve bhinnālambanāḥ pratyayatvāt ghaṭapaṭādiviṣayapratyayavad iti||

[84,22] atrābhidhīyate svaśāstravirodho api syād evaṃ bruvata^ ity āha kṣaṇikaṃ ca yasya cittaṃ tasya sarvam eva cittam ekāgram vikṣepābhāvād eva nāsyeva vikṣiptam iti sarvadittaikāgratvād vikṣepaprāptabhāvād vikṣepapratiṣedhāya śāstropadeśo 'narthakaḥ syāt| asti ca cittaparikarmārthaṃ maitryādyupadeśaḥ| sa ca virudhyeta^ itthaṃdharmaṃ cittam abhyupagacchatah||

[84,27] nanu ca tavāpi vrttivṛttimator abhedād anupapanno vikṣepa^ iti tat^pratiṣedhopadeśānarthakyam na^ eṣa doṣaḥ vṛttivṛttimattvābhyupagamāt||

[85,5] yadi vṛttaya^ eva parasparabhinnāḥ vṛttimato 'tyantabhinnā jāyeran 
pratyarthaniyatāś ca tata^ eṣa doṣaḥ syāt| na ca^ evam abhyupeyate||

[85,7] kathaṃ tarhi vṛttimad rūpeṇa^ ekam anekārthaṃ ca tadvṛttnāṃ visayabhedād avasthitaṃ ca sadasator viparyayānabhyupagamād ity evam ātmapakṣe samañjasatāṃ darśayati yadi punar idaṃ cittaṃ vṛttibhedenānekadhā (mmilavayāvad dhi) prasṛtaṃ sāṃkhyayogavādina^ iva tavāpi sarvataḥ sarvebhyaḥ viṣayebhyaY pratyāhṛtya pratyanīyaikasminn ātmādyarthe samādhīyeta sthāpyeta tadā bhavaty ekāgraṃ 
puruṣaprayāsamāvyatvārham ity arthaḥ||

[85,12] tato yuktas tat-parikarmaṇe śāstropadeśaḥ| (ato na pratyakṣam) ato 'smāt kāraṇāt tat-parikarmaviṣayaśāstropadeśāt sāṃkhyayogavādivat kṣaṇikavādino api ekam anekārtham avasthitaṃ ca cittaṃ na 
pratyarthaniyataṃ (vā) paścād abhyupagamanīyam| anyathā śāstravirodhaḥ syāt| yo api sadṛśapratyayapravāhaṃ cittam ekāgraṃ manyate sadṛśaḥ samānaḥ pratyayapravāho yasya^ ekasyāṃ santatau tac citam ekāgram| tadviparītam asamānapratyayapravāhaṃ vikṣiptam| tathā caikāgratā vikṣepapratiṣedhena^ upapadyate tadarthopadeśaś ceti kutaḥ śāstravirodha^ iti||

[85,19] itara^ āha na pravāhacittasya^ ekasya dharmiṇo 'nabhyupagamāt| na hi 
pravāhacitaṃ nāmaikam atītavartamānabhaviṣyat pratyayānāṃ dharmiṇam abhyupagacchati| yadi hy abhyupagacchet parityaktaḥ svapakṣaḥ| kasmāt 
pravāhacittayākṣaṇikatvāt tad evaikaṃ nāsti yasyaikāgratā dharmaḥ| 
pratyayān eva hy anyonyasaṃhatān pipīlikāpaṅktikalpānasāvabhyupagacchati||

[85,24] athaivaṃ manyate yady apy ekaṃ nāsti tathāpi sarveṣām eva 
pratyayānāṃ sadṛśapravāhāṇām ekāgratā dharmaḥ pradīpārciṣām iva prakāśa iti tatrāha nāyugapatkālotpattivināśānāṃ samāhāraḥ sambhavati| dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalatvāt| na hi bahvīnām aiciṣām ekaḥ prakāśaḥ| 
pratyarciṣam asya bhedāt| na cāpi prakāśasyārciṣāṃ ca^ eko dharmo nā[mā]sti||

[86,9] athāpi manyate pravāha^aṃśasya^ eva pratyayasya kasyacid ekāgratā dharmaḥ, yathaikasyāṃ pipīlikāsantatāv ekā^ api pipīlikā lohiteti tac ca na pūrvadoṣānuṣakteḥ| sarvaḥ pratyayaḥ pravāhāṃśaḥ sadṛśavāhī visadṛśavāhī vā pūrvavat pratyarthaniyatatvād ekāgra eveti vikṣiptacittānava_.liptiḥ| tasmād ekam anekārtham avasthitaṃ ca cittam abhyupagantavyam| yadi śāstreṇacittaparikarmopadekṣyate||

[86,14] kiṃ ca yadi cittenaikenānanvitāḥ karaṇena svabhāvabhinnāḥ pratyayā jāyeraṃs tadā brūhi katham anyapratyayadṛṣṭasyānyaḥ pratyayaḥ smartā bhaved devadattadṛṣṭasyeva yajñadattaḥ| anyapratyayopacitasya vā karmāśayasyānyaḥ pratyaya^ upabhoktā||

[86,17] na hi bhaviṣyat-putrā racitakarmāśayaphalaṃ svargādi praty upabhuñjīta| atha brūyāt viśiṣṭabhaviṣyat-putravijñānārthaṃ vinaśyatpravṛttivijñānam ālayavijñānasaṃskāraṃ kañcanādhatte^ iti na^ etad evam ālayapravṛttivijñānayor atyantabhedād avastutvāc ca||

[86,21] yady ālayavijñānān na tat-pravṛttivijñānaṃ tadā tad eva tad iti nāmamātrabhedaḥ tathā ca saṃskārādhānayuktatvāt smṛtyanupak_.lptiḥ| athāpy anyat evam apy anyatpravṛttivijñānam ālayavijñāne saṃskāraṃ nādadhīta, abhinnatvāt| yathā santaty antarālayavijñāne||

[86,24] kiṃ ca yady api saṃskāram ādhattām tathāpi 
pravṛttivijñānāpāditasaṃskāram ālayavijñānam deśakālāntarākṣamam| tasyāpi kṣaṇikatvābhyupagamād vinaśyac ca tāvaty eva nottaravijñānopkārāya paryāpnoti| deśakālāntaravyavahitaṃ ced upakurvītqa tadā bhinnasantānāntarajanmanāpy upakartavyam||

[87,1] atha kālāntarāvasthāyi tadā kṣaṇikatvahāniḥ| kṣaṇāntaraṃ ced avatiṣṭheta dīrghakālāvasthāyitve kasya syād akṣamā||

[87,3] athāpi pravṛttivijñānasaṃskāraviśiṣṭasyāntarālabhāvina ātmasadṛśasya bhavisyata^ ālayabijñānasya saṃskāram ādhāya pūrvam ālayavijñānaṃ vanaṅkṣyatīti 87,5 tac ca na saṃskārāntarābhāvāt| asti ced anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ| atha nāsty ādhāraḥ tatra kiṃ 
pravṛttivijñānasyālayavijñānakalpanayā saṃskārādhānāya| yathā ālayavijñānam utpitsiṣyamāṇasya tatkālam asataḥ ālayavijñānasya saṃskāram ādhatte tathā pravṛttivijñānam api^ utpitsiṣyamāṇasya tatkālam asataḥ 
pravṛttivijñānasya^ eva saṃskāram ādhattām anapekṣyālayavijñānam||

[87,10] na ca vinaśyat anyasya bhāvinas tatkālam avidyamānasya saṃskāram ādhatte^ ity asya dṛṣṭānto dṛśyate| nāpi bhāviny eva saṃskāraṃ kurvīta| 
pūrveṇa bhaviṣyatsaṃskriyate bhaviṣyati ceti viruddham| tatkālaṃ ced asadbhaviṣyatsaṃskriyate kiṃ tulyātulyasamtatikalpanenāsaṃbandhāviśeṣāt sarvaṃ sarveṇa saṃskriyeta 87,14       nāpi bhaviṣyadvinaśyator yugapadutpattivinācāv akṣaṇikatvaprasaṅgāt| atha tulāntayor iva natyunnatī yugapatpūrvavinācottarasamutpattī syātām (na)vastunor iti cet nābhyupagamavirodhāt| na hi vaināśikenotpattivināśakriyā-vytatirekeṇa kārkam anyad iṣyate| abhyupagame ca siddhāntavirodhaḥ||

[87,18] kiṃ ca kriyākārakayor yāvat kriyā^ utpatti kārakeṇāvasthātavyaṃ tataś ca kṣaṇikatvahāniḥ| ā[na]vasthāne ca kriyārthaṃ tataḥ [tasya] upādānam anrthakam ||

[87,20] kiṃ cānyat kriyāyā eva kārakatve kārakasyāpi kriyātve kriyākārakaśabdao paryāyau syātām| tataś cāṅkuro jāyate devadatto jāyata^ ity aṅkuradevadattoyoḥ paryāyatvaṃ syāt| tayor janikriyāmātratvāt|| 


[87,23] athānyajanikriyā devadattaviśiṣṭā^ anyā cāṅkuraviśiṣṭeti cet abhyupetaṃ tarhi viśeṣeṇa kārakaṃ na h svarūpeṇ akiṃcid viśiṣṭaṃ syāt||

[87,25] athocyeta svarūpeṇa^ evotpattikriyā utpattikriyāntarād viśiṣṭā yathā svalakṣaṇāni rūpādīnīti tac ca na bhāvābhāvayor ekatvaprasaṅgāt| ihāṅkuro jāyate naśyaty aṅkura^ iti ca ya^ eva jāyate sa^ eva nācyati tatra^aṅkurajananam (88,6) aṅkuravināśanam iti jananaṃ vināśa^ ity uktaṃ bhavati| aṅkuro hi kriyā^ eva kārakam iti vocyeta| (citaḥ)||

[88,8] athāpi syāt anyā^/anyasya^ evāṅkurajananakriyāyā aṅkuravināśakriyā^ iti  tathā apy aṅkurajananasya nityatvaprasaṅgaḥ| vināśasyānyatvād eva||

[88,10] athāpi nāśajananavad vastu śabalam iti tathā apy abhāvasya śabalatvaṃ prasajyeta| katham kriyāvyatiriktasya kārakasyābhāve api nāśa eva jananavināśavān jananam eva jananavināśavad iti| na hy abhāvasya śabalatvādiviśeṣo nirviśetṣavād bhāvābhāvayoś cāvirodhāt 
pratyakṣānumānānupapattiḥ||

[88,14] yad^ apy ucyate tulāntayor nāmonnāāv iti tatra yuktā tulāntayor ekakālayor vidyamānatvād anyatarasya vastvantaragauravanimittā ānatirantarasya connatiḥ| iha punar bhavadbhaviṣyatkālayor vijñānayor nāśotpattī tulānāmāhetuvat tṛtīyasya nmittāntarasyābhāvād ekakālaṃ na^ upapadyete| vinaśyad eva vijñānam ātmasadṛśaṃ janayati vināśenākṣaṇatvāt||

[88,20] atha mṛtpiṇḍo yathā ghaṭam utpādayan vinaśyati tadvad iti cet tac ca na mṛdavaytavānām eva ghaṭā-kāreṇa pariṇatatvāt| ekā^ eva hi mṛtpiṇḍaghaṭādi bahudharmayominī||

[88,23] yac cāpi bījeṣu dravyāntaravāsiteṣu vāsakadravyānuvidhāyitvaṃ vāsyadravyasya^ iti tad apy akāraṇam vāsyavāsakayor avasthitatvāt| avasthitāny eva hi bījeṣv aṅkura^ādīni vāsyante bījavāsanena| tad anyatvāt vāsakadravyasyāpy avayavānanupraviṣṭāḥ sūkṣmā vidyanta^ eva| tasmād vidyamānāny evāṅkurādīni bījavāsanayā vāsyanta (89,6) iti yuktam asatāṃ śaviṣāṇādīnām utpattyabhāvāt| asatāṃ ced utpattiḥ syād vāsyavāsakayor_Vinaṣṭatvādf avidyamānatvāviśeṣe vāsakadravyānanuvidhāyi na^ utpadyeta  anyadf vā hastādi||

[89,10] yadi vā^ atyantāsad eva śaśaviṣāṇādyutpadyeta| ato viśiṣṭaṃ hi trayāṇām asattvaṃ vāsite bīje| tasmād avasthitaṃ kārakaṃ bhūtabhaviṣyatkālānekavṛttiyogi cābhyupagantavyam| anyathā 
pratyayamātraṃ kṣaṇikaṃ cittam iti samarthayamānasya samādhīyamānam api (samarthyamānam api) gomayapāyasīynanyāyam ākṣipati||

[89,14] yathā^ eva bahūbhir api go-vikāratvādihetubhiḥ samīhamāne kṛtāṭopenāpi gomayaṃ pāyasīkartuṃ na śakyam tathā kṣaṇikena cittena smaraṇādi samarthayitum ||

[89,16] kiṃ ca svātmānubhavāpahnavaḥ cittasyānyatve pratyayamātratve nānyadṛṣṭam anusmaratīti svātmanānubvhūtam apahnuvīta||

[89,18] kiṃ tv abadrākṣīr ity ukto dṛṣṭavān api nāham adrākṣam iti bruvīta| anyena hi tat-pakṣe tat dṛṣṭaṃ dṛśyate| tad apahneve cānubhūtasya pratipattiḥ katham 'yad^ aham adrākṣaṃ tat aham eva spṛśāmi' yad aham asprākṣaṃ tat aham eva smarāmi'iti| saiṣa pratyayasmaraṇayor ekakartṛtve sati ghaṭanām upaḍhaukate pratipattiḥ| anyathā matsadṛśo yad adrākṣīt tat sadṛśo 'haṃ taṃ smṛśāmīti smarāmīti ca syāt| sarvasya 
puruṣasyāham iti pratyayaḥ smṛtipratyayayor kasmin sati teṣāṃ ca 
pratyayānām ekasmin kartari pratyayiny abhnne ca^ upasthitaḥ pratyakṣaś ca||

[89,89],26      nanv ekapratyayaviṣaya^ evānyaḥ smṛter ekatve api kartur ahaṃ pratyemi smarāmy aham iti ca na bhavitavyaṃ pratyatā smarteti śabdabhedād bheda^ eva| tatrāha na pratyakṣatvāt| tad eva darśayati svānubhavagrāhyaś cāyam abhedāt mā^ aham iti pratyayaḥ||

[90,5] atha pratyetā smarteti śabdabhedāt pratyayabhedāc cāhampratyayasya bhinnaviṣayatvam anumāyiṣyata^ iti ced ata^ āha na hi 
pratyakṣāsya māhātmyaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ cakṣuṣo rūpa^ i_Vanumānād abhibhūyate bādhyate||

[90,8] kiṃ ca pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam ayam ahaṃ pratyayo na tat smṛtipratyayānām ekasmin kartari sāmānyena vartamānaḥ| sa cet 
pratyakṣapramāṇabhūto 'numānenābhibhūyeta tadānīm anumānasyāpi 
pramāṇatvaṃ hīyeta| tanmūlatvād anumānasya| tad etad āha pramāṇāntaraṃ ca anumānavirodhena ca prativādi(pādita)pramāṇapratijñā-doṣo vyākhyātaḥ||

[90,14] atītānāgatapratyayasaṃskārādhārād ananyaḥ idānīntanapratyayasaṃskārḍhāraḥ ekasantānasaṃbandhitve sati bodhātmakatvāt ātmasvarūpād yathā||

[90,16] bhūtabhaviṣyatpratyayasaṃskāṛśrayaḥ vartamānapratyayasaṃskārāśrayād anyaḥ ekasantānasaṃbandhen sati bodhātmakatvāt ātmana^ iva svarūpāt|| vartamānāhaṃ pratyayī mhūtyabhaviṣyatpratyayibhyām abhinnaḥ ekasantānayoge sati pratyayitvāt yathā svarūpāt||

[90,18] vartamānāhaṃpratyayī bhūtabhaviṣyatpratyayibhyām abhinnaḥ ekasantānayoge sati pratyayitvāt yathā svarūpāt||

[90,20] bhūtabhaviṣyatpratyayibhyām anyo vartamānaḥ pratyayaḥ ekasantānasaṃbandhe sati bodhātmakatvāt ātmarāgasvarūpādibhya^ iva||

[90,22] bhūtabhaviṣyatpratyayebhyo nānyo vartamānaḥ pratyayī ekasantānasaṃbandhitve sati bodhātmakatvāt ātmano yathā svarūpāt||

[90,24] bhinnasantatiṣu parasparasmṛtipratisandhānādyanupalambhād ekasantāneṣu ca smṛtyādidarśanād eva siddham ekakartṛtvam| tathā sarvapramāṇebhyo garīyasā pratyakṣeṇa pramāṇena tatra na kiṃcidanumānenasādhyam| na hi kaścit karatalalāñchanadidṛkṣayā darpaṇamā datte| tathā api vaināśikoparacitānumānāpaśadam asahamānāḥ kecit 
pratyanumānāni(91,14) peśalāni darśayanti| tathā ca^ āha tasmād ekam anekārtham avasthitaṃ ca cittam| yasyedaṃ śāstreṇacitta parikarma nirdiśyata iti śāstropadeśasambandhitvaṃ cittasyāvasthitatve hetum upadiśati| na hi vaiśīyamāṇasya ghaṭasya prakṣālanaparivartanādisaṃskāraḥ kriyate||1.32||

[91,18] kathaṃ cittaparikarma nirdiśyate tathekatattvābhyāsa^ iti ca^ uktam kiṃ ca tad ekaṃ tattvam yadiviṣayo 'bhyāsa^ iti tata^ āha 91,20        maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāṇāṃ sukhaduḥkhapuṇyāpuṇyaviṣayāṇāṃ bhāvanātac cittaprasādanam iti| maitrī mitraviṣayā bhāvanā| tādṛśīṃ sukhiteṣu sukham anumodayantīmasūyādivaijitām| tathā duḥkhiteṣu karuṇāṃ kṛpām anujānatīṃ duḥkham| puṇyātmakeṣu muditāṃ puṇyaśīlatām anujānatīm| apuṇyaśīleṣu copekṣām apuṇyaśīlavyāpāreṣūdāsīnatāṃ ca bhāvayed ity anuṣajyate||

[91,25] sarvatraivam asya bhāvayataḥ śukla^ iti viśuddhiḥ 
prāṇyupaghātādivarjanāc chkulo dharmaḥ| tataḥ sa dharma^ iva jātaś cittaṃ prasādayati| tena prasannaṃ cittam ekāgraṃ sthitipadam labhate| ekāgratayā samādhīyata^ ity arthaḥ tad uktam 'prasannacetaso hy āśu buddhiḥ paryavatiṣṭhate' iti||

[92,9] tatropekṣāyā^ ākriyārūpatvād dharmāhetutve sati kim artham iha^ upādānam ucyate^ upekṣānupādāne cāpuṇyaśīleṣv api cittaṃ vyāpāraṃ yāyāt| tataś ca tacvyāpāropadhānakāluṣyān maitryādibhāvanāyā na yogyaṃ bhavati| apuṇyaśīlanimittanaimittikākāravyāpārād adharmo vā mā saṃjaniṣṭā^ iti cittasthitir eva^ upekṣā^ iti tasyā upādānam| cittasthitiś ceha samīhitā||

[92,14] tasmād itthaṃ samāhitacittasya maitrīkaruṇādy ekatattvālambanaṃ cittaṃ śīlayataḥ prāg abhihitā^ antarāyā na janiṣyante||1.33||

[92,16] pracchardanavidhāraṇābhyāṃ vā prāṇasya| vāśabdo maitryādibhāvanopāayāntaravikalpārthaḥ| maitryādibhāvanādy upāyānām anyatamena yena kenacit cittasthitiḥ sampādanīyeti prakaraṇārthaḥ anekopāyapradarśanaṃ kasyacit kvacit sukaratābhiprāyeṇa| 
pracchardanavidhāraṇābhyāṃ vyastābhyāṃ samastābhyāṃ vā| pracchardanam kauṣṭhyasya vāyor ātamitor udvamanaṃ nāsikābhyām na sukhena| vidhāraṇaṃ 
prāṇāyāma^ ātamitor eva| yady api pracchardanenāpi prāṇaḥ āyamyate tathāpi bahirvṛttir na nirudhyata^ iti vidhāraṇaṃ prāṇāyāma^ iti viśeṣyate||1.34||

[92,23] viṣayavatī vā pravṛttir utpannā sthitinibandhanī| cittasya^ iti vākyaśeṣaḥ| viṣayā gandhādayo yasyā yogapravṛtter gandhādisaṃvedanalakṣaṇāyā^ ālambanībhūtāḥ sa viṣayavatīty ucyate||

[92,26] pravṛttir nāma yogino yogam abhyasyataḥ abhimukhī-bhāvaṃ 
pratipadyamānasya yogasya yaḥ prathamo 'bhimukhībhāvaḥ pratyayakraṇo yogānuṣṭhānaṃ pratyutsāha(93,14)janano nirmadhyamānasyāgner iva dhūmasamudbhavaḥ sāpravṛttiḥ| sā ca tādṛśī pravṛttir utpannā 
pratyayakaraṇāt praharṣayantī yoginaś cittaṃ sthitau nivadhnāti||

[93,16] nāsikāgre cittaṃ dhārayato gandhasaṃvit 
prahlādanakarasurabhigandhasaṃvedanaṃ nirantaratayā sammatasaṃyogam iva^ ācakṣāṇā jāyate| sā gandhapravṛttiḥ| tathā jihvādiṣu| dhārayata^ iti sambadhyate| tā etāḥ pravṛttayaḥ sthitau cittaṃ nibadhnanti| saṃśayaṃ vidhamanti samādhiprajñāyāś ca dvārībhavanti||

[93,20] etena raśmacandrādityagrahapradīpādiṣu cittaṃ dhārayato hṛdayapuṇḍarīke vā dhārayato vaiṣamyād raśmicandrādyākārā pravṛttir utpannā viṣayavatīty eva veditavyā||

[93,23] yady api śāstrānumānācāryebhyo 'vagatam arthatattvaṃ tathābhutam evāvyabhicārisvarūpam^ eva| teṣām anyathābhūtāpratipādakatvāt| tathā '_Pi yāvad ekadeśo api svakaraṇasaṃvedyo na bhavati pratyakṣo na bhavati śāstrādu-upadiṣṭsyārthasya^ ekadeśo api tāvat sarvaṃ parokṣam ivāpavargādiṣu sūkṣmeṣv artheṣu na dṛḍhāṃ buddhim utpādayati| tasmāc chāstrānumānācāryopoddhalanārtham avaśyaṃ kaścid viśeṣaḥ 
pratyakṣīkartavyaḥ||

[94,11] tatra śāstrādyupadiṣṭārthaikadeśapratyakṣatve sati sarvaṃ sūkṣmaviṣayam api^ ā^ apavargāc chraddhīyate| etad artham evedaṃ citaparikarma yad^ etat maitrīkaruṇā ityādinā paramāṇuparamamahattvānto^ asya vaśīkāraḥ ity evam antena prakaraṇena yamaniyamāsanaviśiṣṭasya cittaparikarmopadiśyate||

[94,15] tad etad uktaṃ bhavati^ etat-prakaraṇopadiṣṭānām upāyānām anyatamānuṣṭhānāt pratyakṣīkṛtaikadeśatayā vidhūta(pidhmāya)saṃśayaṃ tu^ ā apavargāt dṛḍhāvasthitaśraddhānādi praśāntavahir vṛtti upasthitavaśīkārasaṃjñāvairāgyaṃ tasya tasya tṛtīyapādopadiṣṭapariṇāmatrayāder arthasya pratyakṣikaraṇāya samarthaṃ syāt jñānavibhūtyādiprāpyartham iti | tataś ca^ evam arthaṃ cittaparikarmopadiśyata^ iti prakaraṇārthaḥ| tayā ca bhāṣyakāraḥ 
pradarśitavān 'etad artham evedaṃ cittaparikarma' ity evamādinā ||1.35||

[94,21] viśokā vā jyotiṣmatī pravṛttir utpannā manasaḥ sthitinibandhanīti prakṛtam abhisaṃbadhyate| jyotir yasyāṃ vidyate pravṛttau sā jyotiṣmatī| sā ca śokaṃ vyapagamayatīti viśokā nāma||

[94,24] sā ca katham anupajāyata^ ity āha hṛdayapuṇḍarīke dhārayato buddhisaṃvid bhavati svarūpasavedanam | buddhisattvam hi kiṃrūpam ity āha buddhisattvaṃ hi prabhāsvaraṃ prabhāsanaśīlam ākāśakalpaṃ vyāpi| yatra yasmin budddhisattve||

[95,12] sthitivaiṣamyād iti| buddhisattvātmatāsāmyānāpatter iti| hṛdayapuṇḍarīka^ eva dhārayataḥ pravṛttijyotiṣmatī sūryendumaṇiprabhārūpakāreṇa vikalpate| tathā cāsmitāyām ahaṅkāre samāpnnaṃ syāt buddhisattvam svarūpeṇa sāmyāpatter nistaraṅgamahodadhikalpaṃ śāntam anantam asmitāmātraṃ bhavati||

[95,16] yatredam uktaṃ yatrāsmitāsamāpatta taṃ etaṃ prakṛtam asmitātmānam ahaṅkāram aṇuṃ sūkṣmam ātmānam anuvidya prāpyāsmīty evaṃ tāvat saṃjātīta^ iti| yad_ṛṛśamālambanasvarūpam tāvan mātraṃ sañjānīte| ahaṅkārasvarūpreṇa^ eva tadānurūpyād avacchidyate sphuṭikamaṇir ivopadhānarūpreṇa| evaṃ dvayī viśokā pravṛttir viṣayavatī cāsmitāmātrā ca| tara gandhasaṃvidādir āsmitāmātrāntā sarvā viśokā^ eva| jyotṣmatī 
punar gandhasaṃvidādipravṛttipañcatayād anyatra| viṣyavatī tv asmitāmātrāt prāg eva^ ity evaṃ viṣayavibhāgaḥ| yayā viśokayā jyotiṣmatyā yoginaś cittaṃ sthitipadam labhate||1.36||

[95,24] vītarāgaviṣayaṃ vā cittaṃ vīto rāgo yasmāt pratyayāt sa vītarāgaḥ rāgaśūnya^ ity arthaḥ| sa^ eṣa viṣaya^ ālambanaṃ yasya cittasya tadvītarāgaviṣayam| vītarāgaś ca pratyayaḥ prasiddho loke| rāgiṇo api hi 
puruṣasya rāgakāraṇe api viṣaye yaḥ strīāu mātrādiṣu| sa^ eva pratyayaḥ [tam eva pratyaye] vitarāgaḥ [gaṃ] ālambanatvenābhyasyet(96,14)na tu tasya yo viṣayaḥ ālambanam | viṣayāṇāṃ khalatvāt| tat vītarāgapratyayālambanoparaktaṃ cittaṃ tadvad eva vītarāgaṃ 
prasannataraṃ sthitipadam labhate| rāgādgikhalīkṛtaṃ hi kṛtakhalīnam iva turaṅgamānaṃ pracalati||1.37||

[96,18] svapnanidrā-jñānālambanamṃ vā| svapnajñānālambanaṃ vā nidrājñānālambanaṃ vā cittaṃ tadākāram eva bhavati| evaṃ svabhāvaṃ hi cittaṃ yadālambate tadākāraṃ bhavati| svapne ca śabdādiviṣayaśūnyaṃ jñānam| jñānasyāpi svabhāvo 'vabhāsakatā| tatrāpi jñānasvaprūpam eva^ ālambate| na tu smaryamāṇaviṣayasvarūpam| smaryamāṇenāpi viṣayeṇa khalīkāro dṛśyate| nidrā-jñānaṃ tu viśeṣāgrahaṇātmakam abhāvapratyayālambanaṃ śāntam anantam anubhūyamānācalanadharmakam| tataś ca tadālambanaṃ saccittaṃ sthitipadam labhata^ iti yuktam||1.38||

[96,25] yathābhimatadhyānād vā| yad^ evābhimatam iti cittasthityapekṣayā| cittasthiteḥ prakṛtatvāt| na tu sukhādyapekṣayā| pratiṣidhatvāc ca na tv eva viṣayān prāpya dhārayeta kathaṃ cana" iti| tatra labdhasthitikaṃ karmaṇyaṃ sat anyatra śāstrīyeṣu^ api sthitipadam labhate||1.39||

[97,14] paramāṇuparamamahattvānto^ asya vaśīkāraḥ| antaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate| sūkṣme niviśamānasya parimāṇe niviśamānasya yoginā saṃkṣipyamāṇasya| abhuāsakāle tv alpam alpataraṃ ca krameṇālocayat 
paramāṇvantaṃ saṃkṣipyate| sa ca paramāṇuc cittasya saṃkṣepakoṭiḥ| tena tadantaṃ t\sthitipadam||

[97,18] ittham ubhayakoṭim anudhāvataś cittasya saṃkṣepaṃ vistāraṃ cānubhavato yaḥ tadvirodhinā pratyayāntareṇāpratighātaḥ sa paro vaśīkāraḥ| pūrvo api pare^ eva| tathāapy asyāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ| tadvaśīkārāt paripūrṇam anavakhaṇḍitaṃ labdhasthitikaṃ na punar abhyāsakṛtaṃ parikarmāpekṣata^ iti| pūrve tu kiyad api parikarmāntaram apekṣante||

[97,22] tatra saṃkṣiptā viśālā vikaraṇī ceti trayī dhāraṇā| tatrobhayakoṭismarśinī vikaraṇī| paramamahatvāntaspṛgviśālā| paramāṇvantaspṛk saṃkṣiptā| sā ca trayy apīha sūtropāttā||1.40||

[97,25] atha labdhasthitikasya cetasaḥ atha^ uktānām anyatamenopāyena labdhasthitikasya kiṃ-svarūpā kiṃviṣayā vā samāpattir iti tannididarśayedaṃ sūtraṃ pravavṛte kṣīṇavṛtter abhijāyasyeva maṇer grahītṛgrahaṇagrāhyeṣu tatsthatadañjanatā samāpattiḥ||

[98,10] nanu ca samāpatter viṣayaṃ vakṣyati tṛtīyapāde 'parārthāt svārthasaṃyamāt puruṣajñānam' ity ādinā| samāpattisvarūpam ca saṃyamasvarūpam tatkāryanirvartanena^ eva setsyati| tataś cānarthakam idaṃ sūtram na^ eṣa doṣaḥ pūrvoktopāyavaśikāraṇārthavattvapradarśanārthatvāt| evaṃ hy eṣa cittavaśīkāro 'rthāan yena grahītr-ādyarthasamāpannaṃ cittaṃ tadābhāsaṃ bhavatīti||

[98,15] kiñ ca vitarkādyanugamāt saṃprajñātaḥ" ity uktam vitarkādisvarūpaṃ ceha darśayitavyam anuktāyāṃ samāpattau vitarkādisvarūpam āha na śakyaṃ darśayituṃ samāpattir dharmā (di) vitarkādaya^ iti| kṣīṇavṛtter iti pratyastamitabāhyapramāṇādipratyayasya^ ity arthaḥ|| 

[98,18] nanu sarva^ eva pratyayāḥ pratyastamitāḥ| sarvapratyastamaye hi^ avasitādhikāraṃ na sthūlasūkṣmādikam ālambate||

[98,20] abhijātasya^ iva maṇer iti dṛṣṭānt-upādānam| yathā sphaṭika epāśrayabhedāt tat-tad^rūpoparakta^ upāśrayākāreṇa nirbhāsate| tathā grāhyālambanoparaktaṃ cittaṃ grāhyasamāpannaṃ grāhyasvarūpākāreṇa nirbhāsate||

[98,23] tathā bhūtasūkṣmoparaktaṃ tanmātroparaktaṃ bhūtasūkṣmasamāpannaṃ bhūtasūkṣmasvarūpāvabhāsam avabhāsate||

[98,25] [tathā sthūlālambanoparaktaṃ sthūlarūpasamāpannaṃ sthūlarūpābhāsaṃ bhavati| tathā viśvabhedoparaktaṃ viśvabhedasamāpannaṃ viśvarūpābhāsaṃ bhavati ||

[98,27] tathā grhaṇeṣv apīndriyeśv api draṣṭavyam| grahaṇālambanoparaktaṃ grahaṇasamāpannaṃ grahaṇasvarūpākāreṇa nirbhāsate||

[99,8] tathā grahītṛpuruṣasamāpannaṃ buddhibodhakasvarūpam^ ity arthaḥ| gṛhītṛsvarūpākāreṇa nirbhāsate||

[99,10] tathā muktapuruṣāla,banoparaktam iti| sa^ eva buddhr boddhā yadā viṣayavṛttīnāṃ na boddhā bhavati tadā sattvapuruṣānyatākhyātimātrāvasthā||

[99,12] yadā udāsīno muktapuruṣa ity ucyate tadā hi sarvakleśādimuktaṃ cittaṃ sā^ eva hi puruṣasya muktatā yā kleśādivimuktiś cittasya| ata^ eva sūtre grahītṛgrahaṇam eva kṛtam| grahītā puruṣaviśeṣa^ eva muktapuruṣa ihābhipreta^ iti||

[99,15] yadā/yathā hi puruṣagrahaṇam eva kuryāt| parasyāṃ vaśikārasaṃjñāyāṃ jātāyāṃ na punaḥ saṃsārayogo bhavati| tathā ca^ uktam śaty eva cittasattvasambandhe kleśādisambandhābhāvāt sa tu sadaiva muktaḥ sadaiveśvaraḥ" iti||

[99,18] tasmāt muktapuruṣāla,banoparaktaṃ muktapuruṣasamāpannaṃ muktasvarūpākāreṇa nirbhāsata^ iti| yo hi manyate nivṛttasattvasambandhena^ eva muktena puruṣeṇ asamāpannaṃ tadākāram eva nirbhāsata^ iti tad  ayuktam, cittasya svātmapralayaprasaṅgāt||

[99,22] nirariśayasūkṣmaś c apuruṣas tatsamasūkṣmaṃ pradhānaṃ tatkāryakryakāryaṃ(nimittaṃ) sattvaṃ tac cittaṃ pradhānaṃ puruṣaṃ vā samāpadyamānaṃ na sthitena svena rūpeṇa samāpattum utsahate| na hi ghaṭo mṛdavasthām aparityaktasvarūpaḥ samāpadyate||

[99,25] nanu ca kāryeṇāpi yogisattvena mahadahaṅkārau saṃvedyāv ucyete| mahati tāvadvipratipattiḥ saṃvedyate na vā^ iti| yadā hisaṃvedyatā grahītṛmuktasvarūpavat saṃbandhaviśeṣa^ upapadyate mahadahaṅkārayor api| vyaktau hi tau tataḥ saṃbandhaviśeṣo(100,1) yujyate saṃbandhaviśeṣāc ca yathā svapuruṣo grahītṛ-svarūpaś ca muktaś ca cittavṛttiviśeṣasaṃbandhāt gṛhyate tadvat| na tu sarvātmanā^ eva mahadahaḥkārayor api grahaṇaṃ mahattvāt||

[100,4] tayor api [tāv api] yadi sarvātmanā gṛhyeyātām cittasattvamātrātmānau jāyeyātām tathā ca sati tadviṣayagrahaṇam eva na syāt| pradhānaprurṣayos tutatsaṃbandhasya cātyantam avyaktasvarūpatvān na svarūpagrahaṇam||

[100,7] nanu ca^ īśvarasyāsarvajñatā [yataḥ] pradhānapuruṣatatsaṃbandhā na pratyakṣā iti| ucyate pradhānapuruṣasaṃbandhebhyo^ anyat sarvaṃ 
pratyakṣam ity atra tāvat avipratipattiḥ niravadhikapratyakṣajñānābhyupagamāt| (yatra tv anumānagośaraḥ) [yad tu asmākam agocaraḥ tat] sa^ eva^ īśvaro jñātum arhati| jñeyaṃ ced asti tat sarvam asau yathā vā tathā vā jānātīti niścayaḥ| ajñātṛkasya jñeyatvānupapatteḥ||

[100,12] kiñ ca yadi prakṛtipuruṣau svarupeṇa jñeyatvam ā(nā)padyeyātāṃ tadā buddhivadbhogyatāprasaṅgaḥ| tataś ca parārtho api syāt paraścānyaḥ kalpyaḥ syāt||

[100,14] nanu puruṣāṇām anyonyabhogyatā^ iti na paro bhoktā kalpyaḥ na^ etad evam samatvāt puruṣāṇāṃ viṣayaviṣayitvānupapatteḥ| na hi pradīpāv anyonyam uparut\rutaḥ| 100,16 kiñ ca bhogyatve sukhādirūpatāprasaṅgaś ca 
puruṣasya| tathā ca sati pradhānasya nirhetukatvaprasaṅgaś cety evam ādayo bhūyaṃso doṣāḥ ||

[100,18] nanu ca parārthāt svārthasaṃyamāt puruṣajñānam iti 
puruṣapratyaykṣatāṃ vakṣyati satyaṃ yuktam uktam| ata^ evocyate puruṣo na svarūpeṇa viṣayibhavatīti| yena^ evaṃ tatra^ uktam " na ca 
puruṣapratyayena buddhisattvātmanā puruṣo dṛśyate | puruṣa^ eva taṃ
