pratyayaṃ svālaṃbanaṃ paśyatīti| tathā hy uktam "vijñātāramare kena vijānīyāt" itīti||

[100,23] nanu puruṣālambanoparaktaṃ cittaṃ puruṣasya viṣayaḥ| tathā ca sati pratyakṣa^ eva puruṣaḥ| yathā ghaṭālabmbanoparaktte pratyaye 
puruṣeṇopalabhyamāne ghaṭaḥ pratyakṣa^ ucyate na^ eṣa doṣaḥ ghaṭavat 
purusasya cittenāvyāpyatvāt| ghaṭādis tu bāhyaś cittena vyāpyate na 
puruṣaḥ ānantyāt||

[101,6] paricchinnaṃ hi cittaṃ viṣayīkurvan na puruṣaṃ viṣayiṇamanantaṃ vyāptum utsahate| yadi puruṣaṃ vyāpnoti pradhānam api vyāpnuyāt||

[101,8] tasmād yathā darpaṇe mukhaṃ chāyārūpeṇa gṛhyate tathā puruṣacchāyākāreṇa pariṇataḥ pratyayo gṛhyate puruṣeṇa| tathā ca vyāsaḥ "yathā 'darśatalaprakhye paśyaty ātmānam ātmani"|| iti||

[101,11] na tu muktānyapuruṣākāreṇa pariṇatau dvāram asti| yadi tadākāreṇa pariṇameta muktasyāpi tatsvam eva^ iti dṛśyam syāt| na hi cittasya pariṇāmā^ anyatvaṃ nāmāsti| svākāreṇa hi pariṇataṃ puruṣo na tu cittaṃ na paśyati| svapuruṣe svatvāt sati saṃbandhe pariṇatidvāram asti||

[101,15] kathaṃ tarhi svapuruṣād anyasya svatvāsaṃbandhino grahaṇam ucyate yathā paradarpaṇadvāreṇa paramukhadarśanaṃ tathā darpaṇasthānīyaparasatvākārapariṇataṃ svasattvaṃ puruṣaḥ paśyan 
param api puruṣaṃ jānāti||

[101,18] tasyāyam ātmā mamāyam ity anyatvan tv anumānād eva pratipadyate svakīyād anyadīyasattvagataviśeṣaloggena| sattvānāṃ ca triguṇatvād avaśyaṃ viśeṣāḥ santi| puruṣāṇāṃ tu nirviśeṣatvāt svarūpeṇa bhedo duravabodha^ eva||

[101,21] tasmād evam abhijātamaṇikalpasya cetaso grahītṛgrahaṇakrāhyeṣu 
puruṣendriyabhūteṣu tatsthatadañjanatā| teṣu grahītrādiṣu tiṣṭhatīti tatsthaṃ tatsthatāviśiṣṭā yāṃ grahītrādyañjanatā tad eva^ āha teṣu sthitasya tadākārāpattiḥ samāpattir ity ucyate||

[101,25] samyag-āpattiḥ samāpattiḥ sā ca tādṛśī pratyastamitabahirvṛtter eva bhavati| yady api vyutthitacittasyāpi samāpattir asti| tathā api sā na samīcīnatarā rajas-tamobhyāṃ cittasattvasyātyantābhibhūtatvād iti||1.41||

[102,10] saiṣā samāpattiś catuṣṭayī| tatra prathamāṃ samāpattiṃ vyācaṣṭe śabdārthajñānavikalpaiḥ saṅkīrṇā savitarkā samāpattiḥ| śabdaś cārthaś ca jñānaṃ ca śabdārthajñānāni teṣāṃ vikalpāḥ śabdārthajñānavikalpaḥ| vyātavyālambanaviṣayair vikalpaiḥ saṃkīrṇā vimmiśrā itaretarapratyayānubuddhā sā saṃkīrṇasamāpattiḥ||

[102,14] nanu ca śabdārthapratyayā itaretaravyāvṛttasvarūpāḥ yogī ca^ ekaṃ vastu dhyeyatvenālambate śabdam arthaṃ pratyayaṃ vā tatra kaḥ śabdārthajñānavikalpasaṅkaraḥ||

[102,16] evam evaitat| tathā api ayam eva saṃvyavahāro loke yad utāvibhāgena grahaṇam| tadyathā gaur iti śabdo gaur ity artho gaur iti jñānam ity avibhāgena loke grahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭam| evaṃ hi lokaḥ trayam api gaur ity eva vyapadiśyati| tathā^ anyataragrahaṇe^ avaśiṣṭayor api^ smṛtiḥ||

[102,20] nanu ca tathābhutam etadvastu na^ evaṃ śabdārthasaṅketagrahaṇottarakālabhāvitvāt| nanu saṅgetād api prāk sāmānyaśabdaṃ yaṃ kañcid adhyavasyaty eva na viviktānām api grahaṇadarśanāt| vivicyamānāś cānye śabdadharmā gakārādivarṇodāttasvaritatadrutam adhyavilambitaśrotragrahaṇādayaḥ| te nārthajñānayoḥ| tathānye 'rthadharmāḥ sasnālāṅgūlakakudakhuraviṣāṇadarśanādayaḥ| tathā (ca) anye 
pratyayadharmāḥ 
puruṣagrāhyātmakatvāvabhāsātmakatvasaṃskārādhāyitvādayaḥ na te śabdārthayoḥ| na [tena] paramārthato 'nyonyadharmasaṅkaragandho apy asti| tena viviktaḥ pandhās teṣām||

[102,27] tatra yatra kvacana samāpannasya yo hy evārthaḥ samādhiprajñām upārūḍhaḥ sa cec chabdārthajñānavikalpānuviddhaḥ śabdārthasaṅketakṛto ya^ itaretarasmṛtyanuvyādhas tadviśiṣṭaś ced artha^ upāvartate sā saṅkīrṇā samāpattiḥ savitarkety ucyate||

[103,9] anekajanmāntaraśabdārthasaṅketajñānasaṃskāraviśiṣṭatvād yogino api prathamatarā samāpattir lokapratyaya^aviśiṣṭā| eṣa tu viśeṣo yat labdhasthitipadatvād yogicittasya samānapratyayapravāhate||1.42||

[103,12] idānīṃ nirvitarkā samāpattir ucyate| yadā punaḥ śabdasaṅketasmṛtipariśuddhau| śabdasaṅketo 'syāyaṃ vācakaḥ ayam asya vācya^ iti samayapratipattiḥ| tajjanitasmṛtiḥ śabdasaṅketasmṛtiḥ| tasyāḥ 
pariśuddhir mithyātvād vinivṛttiḥ| sā rajas-tamasor nyag-bhāvāt||

[103,16] śrutijñānaṃ āgamikam anumānajñānaṃ laukikaṃ te ca sāmānyaviṣaya evānumānāgamajñāne tayor vikalpo hi viśeṣādhyāropaḥ tenādhyāsena śūnyāyāṃ yoginaḥ samādhiprajñāyāṃ vyāvṛttalaiṅgikasāṅketikajñānādhyāsāyāṃ svarūpamātreṇa mātragrahaṇaṃ digdeśakālānubhāvādibhyo api vyāvṛtta^ ity evam arthaṃ svair eva dharmair mahīyamānaḥ tatsvarūpamātratayā^ eva tasyārthasya svarūpamātratayā^ eva yoginaḥ prajñā^ avicchidyate| na tasmād arthād bahirdeśakālādi kiṃcid ālovayatīty arthaḥ||

[103,22] svam api grahaṇātmakaṃ rūpaṃ tat-prajñā na^ ālocatati nirmalataratvāt| grāhyamātrā^ eva sā vibhajyate| sā ca tādṛśī nirvitarkā samāpattiḥ| nirgato vitarko 'syāḥ nirvitarkaḥ vitarko 'dhyāropaḥ| so 'syāṃ nāsti||

[103,25] tat etat param utkṛṣṭaṃ śuddhaṃ pratyakṣaṃ aparaṃ pratyakṣaṃ sarvasādhāraṇaṃ purastād vṛttimadhye paṭhitam idaṃ yogina^ eva^ iti| tacchutānumānayor bījam| yady api pūrvoktam api śrutānumānayor bījam eva tathā api tatkārye śrutānumāne vyabhivarato api na punar nirvitarkasamādhijapratyakṣaprabhave vyabhivarata^ ity ata^ āha tataḥ śrutānumāne prabhavata^ iti||

[104,9] na ca (nanu) śrutānumānajñānasahabhūtaṃ tad-darśanam śrutānumānajñānābhyām asamāniviṣayam ity etat| viśeṣaviṣayaṃ hi tad-darśanaṃ sāmānyaviṣaye śrutānumānajñāne  tasmād asaṅkīrṇaṃ 
pramāṇāntareṇa yogino nirvitakajaṃ darśanam iti (tannirvitarkāyāḥ tad iti tasmād iti) samāpatter asyāḥ sūtreṇa lakṣaṇaṃ dyotyate samṛtiparuśuddhau svarūpaśūnyevārthamātranirbhāsān nirvitarkā||

[104,14] śabdasaṅketaśrutānumānajñānavikalpasmṛtipariśuddhau śabdasaṅketavikalpaḥ śrutajñānavikalpaḥ anumānajñānavikalpaś ca sa^ eva smṛtiḥ| smaraṇena hy anyasyānyadharmo'adhyāropyate| na hi vastu svarūpeṇa vastvantare 'dhyāropayituṃ śakyam| nanu ca kathaṃ śrutānumite pratyakṣe 'adhyāropyete| nanu ca prasiddho 'dhyāropaḥ tathā cedaṃ mayā śrutam idaṃ mayā 'numitam idam iti ca paśyati||

[104,20] nanu tad eva tat dṛśyamānam api yad^ anumitaṃ śrutaṃ ca na sāmānyaviśeṣabhedāt| sāmānyaṃ śrutam anumitaṃ ca viśeṣas tu pratyakṣasya viṣayaḥ| tasmiṃś ca tatsāmānyādhyāsaḥ prasiddha^ eva||

[104,23] grāhyoparaktā yoginaḥ prajñā evaṃ prajñārūpam grhaṇātmakaṃ tyaktvā padārthamātrasvabhāvā grāhyasvarūpāpanneva bhavati| ivaśabdena grahaṇātmakatāṃ na jahatīti| na hi sphaṭika^ upādhyāśrayatāyāṃ svāṃ svaccharūpatāṃ hāsyati| kiñ ca grahaṇavyatiriktaṃ ca grāhyaṃ vastvantaram astīti ca^ uktaṃ bhavati||

[104,27] sā nirvitarkā samāpattiḥ| tathā ca vyākhgyātam| tasyā hy ekabuddhyupakramo'rthātmā| upakramyate^ ekayā buddhyā yaḥ sa ekaśbuddhyupakramaḥ ekabuddhiparicchedya^ iti| arthātmā bodhyātmety etat||

[105,5] gavādir ghaṭādir vā viṣayaḥ na tu bodha^ eva gavādyākāro viṣayaḥ aṇupracayaviśeṣātmā^ aṇūnāṃ tanmātrayavānāṃ pracayaviśeṣa^ ātmā yasya sa gavādir ghaṭādir vā^ ālambanaṃ buddheḥ sa ca viṣayaḥ saṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ tathā saṃsthitāni hi tāni bhūtasūkṣmāṇi||

[105,9] etad uktaṃ bhavati bhūtasūkṣmāṇāṃ sādhāraṇo dharma^ iti^ ātmabhūto na vyatiriktaḥ| yathā sarpasya^ ābhogakuṇḍalanādi||

[105,11] yeṣāṃ punar atyantavyatiriktam anyad eva kāraṇāt kāryaṃ teṣāṃ kāraṇatantratvaṃ kāryasya na prāpnoti| aṃśu-tantro hi tantuḥ tantutantraś ca paṭaḥ| na cātyantāvyatve'nyatantratvaṃ dṛṣṭam ansyasya| yathā mṛtpiṇḍatantratā na paṭasya tantūnāṃ vā||

[105,14] kiñ ca tadvināśānuvināśitvaṃ ca na śakyaṃ na hi kapālavināśam anuvinaśyati paṭaḥ||

[105,16] athocyeta sambandhaḥ kāraṇam iti| paṭasya hi tantavas tantūnāñ cāṃśava^ iti sambandhas tatra hetuḥ kāraṇaṃ tat-tantratvatadanuvināśayor iti||

[105,18] atrocyate caitrakṣetrādisambandhe api prasaṅgaḥ syāt| caitrasyāpi kṣetrāditantratā tadanuvināśitvam ca sambandhaviśeṣāt 
prasajyeta||

[105,20] athāpi syāt samavāyalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ kāraṇam iti na tasyāpi saṃbandhatvāviśeṣād ahetutvaṃ yathānyasaṃbandhaḥ saṃbandhinor anyataratantratvādy ahetuḥ evaṃ samavāyalakṣaṇasyāpi saṃbandhatvād anyataratantratvādāv ahetutvam eva|| kiñcānyat saṃbandhasya ca saṃbandhinor evānyataratantratvādihetutve samavāyasyāpi samavāyāntaraṃ tasyāpy anya^ ity anavasthā prasajyeta| yathā guṇādīnāṃ samavāyalakṣaṇāt saṃbandhāt dravyatantratvaṃ tathā samavāyasyāpi dravyavyatiriktatve dravyatantratvārthaṃ samavāyāntaraṃ kalpyeta tasyāpy anyat tasyāpy anyad ity anavasthā syāt||

[106,1] prāg-utpatter abhāvāt kāryasya kāraṇena saṃbandhānupapattiḥ aviśiṣṭaṃ cāsattvaṃ śaśaviṣāṇasya ghaṭasya ca| tatra ghaṭena^ eva kāraṇasya saṃbnandho na śaśaviṣāṇādibhir iti hetur vāditavyaḥ||

[106,4] prāk asannotpadyeta śaśaviṣāṇavat| yas tu tantubhyo 'nyaḥ sa tantubhyo na^ utpadyate tantubhyo 'nyatvāt| yathā tantubhyo ghaṭaḥ||

[106,6] itara^ āha prāk sannapi na^ utpadyate satvāt visyamānaghaṭavad iti| yo yasmād ananyaḥ so api tasmānnityā na^ utpadyate vidyamānatvāt svarūpavad iti||

[106,8] apara^ āha yadi sannotpadyata^ iti sata ytpattyabhāva^ ucyeta siddhasādhyatā syāt| athābhivyaktyabhāvaḥ tadā dṛṣṭāntābhāvaḥ||

[106,10] yad^ apy ucyate yo yasmād ananya^ iti tatrāpi siddhasādhyatā dṛṣṭāntābhāvaś ca| svarūpād eva hy bhivyajyate nāsaty abhivyaṅgyasvarūpe 'abhivyaktir abhivyañjakasahasreṇāpi śakyate kartum||

[106,13] kiñcānyat sarvavyavahāralopaś ca| katham yadā devadattasya gaur aśvo vā prātar vidyamānakarṇalāṅgūlaḥ punar aḍavīṃ gataḥ kenacil lūnakarṇo lūnalāṅgūlo vā syāt tadā pūrvāvayavavibhāgād vinaṣṭaḥ anyas tu navaḥ 
pralūnakarṇalāṅgūlo 'svāmikaḥ sañjāta^ iti yena kenacid upādīyeta tathā cāvayavavibhāgaḥ sarvatra sambhavatīti svasvāmisambandhādiniyamābhāvād aśeṣavyavahāralopaḥ syāt|| atha brūyāt 
pūrvopārjitāvayavair evāvayavī navaḥ pralūnakarṇalāṅgūla^ ārabdha^ iti na^ evam na hi mātāmitrādivyatirekeṇa^ idānīntana lūnakarṇalāṅgūlāvayavyantarapratyārambhiṇo dṛśyante||

[106,21] kiṃ ca yasya ca kṣetre lūnakarṇalāṅgūlo jātas tasya^ evāsau syāt na pūrvāvayavikasya tatkṣetrajātatṛṇapalāśādivat||

[106,23] yathā cānyakṣetragatāni bījāni salilaughenāne^ akṣetrem upanītāni cet tat-tadbījaniṣpannasasyaṃ kṣetravān eva gṛhṇāti na tu 
pūrvabījavān (taḥ)| na cāsyāsau pratibadhnāti vidvān api madīyabījapariniṣpannaṃ sasyam aham upādīyeti||

[106,26] tathā lūnakarṇakāṅgūlam api kṣetravān eva gṛhṇīta| na tatra 
pratibandha(rūpa)nīyam madīyāvayavair ārabdho 'yam avayavīti||

[107,7] yeṣām apy ekasmin gṛhīte tasminn aśve lūnakarṇalaṅgūlake jāte svakṣetre ca dṛṣṭe sa^ evāśvadātā anyoyaṃ (vitisanyoyaṃ mahattamādaśvānmayā datto mahattama^ iti hareta| haratām iti cet satvavyavahāralopaprasaṅgaḥ śāstrakopaś ca| tasmād ā[nyo ']vayavebhyaḥ kāraṇebhya^ eko 'vayavī kāryam iti siddham||

[107,11] yad^ apy ucyate śabdapratyayadharmādibhedād anya^ iti tad apy uktam| dṛśyate hi śabdādibhede apy ananyatvam| yathā hastaḥ karaḥ 
pāṇir iti śabdabheda^ ekasyapitṛtvaṃ putratvaṃ ca pratyayabheda^ ekasya^ eva baddhaḥ punar muktaḥ sthito gataś ceti kālabhedāt| dāhakatvaṃ 
pācakatvam ity ādayo dharmabhedā iti| dig-deśādibhedās tv asiddhā eva| tasmād āha^ ātmabhūta^ iti||

[107,16] sa ca svaphalena vyaktenānumitaḥ| phalena udakāharaṇādhāraṇasambhavanādinā| yasya hi phalaṃ kāryaṃ vyaktam upalabhyate sa astīti niścayaḥ| anumita iti vipratipannāpekṣayā sādhāraṇaśabdena śaktirūpeṇa vocyate| sa tu pratyakṣa evāvyaktena daṇḍasūtrakūlālādikārakavyāpārābhivyaktena phalena kāryeṇa ghaṭākhyena prāg api mṛdādyavasthāyām astitvenānumitaḥ saiva daṇḍādikārakaiḥ svavyañjakair abhivyajyamānaḥ prādur bhavati dharmāntarodaye ca tirobhavati saiva bhūtasūkṣmāṇāṃ dharmo 'vayavīty ucyate||

[107,23] etad uktaṃ bhavati^ aṇūnām apratyakṣatvān nāvirbhāvatirobhāvau yuktau| (tata) s tāś ca tau| tasmād ya^ āvirbhavati dharmāntarodaye virodhāttirobhavati ca^ āvir-bhāve ca yo vyañjakādyapekṣate so 'vayavīty ucyate| yaś ca^ evaṃ dharmaḥ so 'ṇubhyo 'nya^ iti||

[107,26] kiñ cāmī^ apy anye hetavo 'vayavisadbhāve yo 'sāv ekaś ca mahāṃś ca^ āṇīyāṃś ca kriyādharmakaś cānityaś ca| pratyekam ete hetavaḥ||

[108,6] api ca^ eko ghaṭa^ iti ghaṭaśabdavācyasya^ ekatvena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt| asti cedaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ yo 'sāv eka^ iti| na hi bahūnām aṇūnām ekatvena sāmānādhikaraṇyam upakalpete||

[108,9] tathā mahatvena| na cāṇūnāṃ amahatāṃ mahatvena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ yujyate tathā^ aṇīyastvena| na hy aṇūnām aṇvantarāpekṣayā anīyastvaṃ ghaṭate| sarva^ eva hi te'ṇīyāṃsaḥ| nāpi cāṇūnām aṇutvaṃ gṛhyate 'ṇubhyaḥ| aṇutve vastvantarasyāṇīyastvam upapatsyate||

[108,13] kiñ ca kriyādharmakaś ca| kriyā dharmo yasya sa kriyādharmakaḥ| 
phalavad vyāpāranirvṛttikaratvād ity uktaṃ bhavati| na hy aṇūnāṃ 
phalavadvyāpāranirvṛttikaratvaṃ yuktam| aśakyaprayojyatvād asmadādibhiḥ| kiñ cānityatvāt| na hy aṇūnām anityatvaṃ gṛhyate||

[108,17] kiṃ ca dṛśyatvāt| na hy aṇavo dṛśyante| tathā spṛśyatvāc ca hānāc copādānāc ca dhāraṇāc cety evam ādayaḥ| tenāvayavinā vyavahārāḥ kriyante nānubhir iti||

[108,20] yasya punaḥ itthaṃ pramāṇasuprasiddhasattāko api avastu[kaḥ] avayavī sa pracayaviśeṣaḥ sūkṣmaṃ ca kāraṇam anupalabhyaṃ aṇvādi tasyāvayavyabhāvād atad-rūpapratiṣṭhaṃ mithyājñānam iti prāyeṇa saevam eva visayajñānaṃ prāptaṃ mithyājñānam||

[108,24] tataḥ sarvam eva jñānaṃ śabdādyākāreṇa sukhādyākāreṇa tadjñānākāreṇa ca^ upajāyate| śabda^ādayaś ca jñānākārakarāvayavitvābhimatā nābhyupagamyante| sukhādayo api viṣayatvād avayavipakṣā eva| tathā tajjānam eva jñānākārakaratvāt svarūpam api mithyeti prāptaṃ sarvam eva mithyājñānam||

[108,28] tataś ca pratyakṣānumānayor apy ābhāsatvam eva tenābhupetaṃ tayor api śabdāddiviṣayatvāt||

[109,7] yad^ api sarvajñajñānam anityaduḥkhaśūnyānātmakatvādiviṣayaṃ tasyāpi viṣayābhāvāt tad api mithyājñānaṃ prāptam iti svatantrasya tīrthakarasya cāprāmāṇyaṃ tatra tīrthakaras sahadharmābhāvaś cābhyupagato bhavati| śabdādivat pramāṇaviṣayatvāviśeṣāt||

[109,10] tad etat pratipādayati samyagjñānam api kiṃ syād viṣayābhāvāt| yad yad^ upalabhyate tat tad avayavitvenāghnātam iti jñānasya svākārasamarpakatvāviśeṣāt| tac ca svākārasamarpakaṃ kiñcana nābhyupeyata iti sarvasyātmīkasya jñānasya tena mithyātvam āpāditam||

[109,14] tathā ca samyagjñānam api kiṃ syāt yadapekṣayā mithyājñānam abhisandhīyate||

[109,16] yadā ca^avayavinaṃ sarvapramāṇasamyak-sarvajñānasiddham apahṇute tadā na samyagjñānasya viṣayaḥ sampādayituṃ pāryate||

[109,18] yadā ca na pārasyati tadā katham abhisandadhīta sarvaṃ mithyeti| samyagjñānam apekṣya mithyā-jñānaṃ mithyā-bhāvam āsīdati^ āpekṣikatvāt samyaṅ-mithyā-jñānayoḥ||

[109,20] yaś cāpi manate nirviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ samyag iti tasyāpi svarūpāgrahaṇāt tad astitvānupapattiḥ| gṛhyate ced viṣayatvād avayavimithyātvaṃ tanmithyātvāc ca taj-jñānamithyātvaṃ tathā taj-jānam apīti satvamithyātvam uktaṃ pūrvam eva||

[109,22] api ca nirviṣayajñānasamyaktve sarvajñānasya viṣayānabhyupagamān nirviṣayatvam iti samyaktvam eva prāpnuyāt| tathāpi tu sarvajñānasamyaktvān mithyā-jñānaṃ kiṃ syād iti vaktavyam||

[109,26] athāpi brūyāt yat saviṣayam ivābhāti tan mithyājñānaṃ yad atyantanirviṣayaṃ tatsamyag iti| tathāpi tasyāstitve pramāṇābhāva^ ity ukta^ eva doṣaḥ| tasmād avayavī samyagjñānasya viṣayo 'vaśyāhyupagantavyaḥ ity āha tasmād asty avayavī yo mahatvād vyavahārāpannaḥ samāpatter nirvitarkāyā viṣayībhvatīti||1.43||

[110,9] etayā^ eva savicārā nirvcārā ca sūkṣmaviṣayā vyākhyātā| tatra bhūtasūkṣmeṣv abhivyaktadharmakeṣu tan-mātreṣv eva deśakālanimittānubhavāv acchineṣu deśāvacchinnaśabdaḥ| sarvaṃ hi vastu deśādibhir avacchidyamānaṃ viṣayiṇā jñātrā saṃbandhyamānaṃ vyavahārāya kalpate| teṣv evaṃ bhūteṣv evaṃbhūtā^ eva deśādivikalpābhisaṃhitā^ eva yā samāpattiḥ sā savicārā^ ity ucyate| tataś ca savicārāyāḥ savitarkāviśeṣatvaṃ siddham||


[110,16] katham ihāpy adhyāsāviśeṣāt| ayan tu viśeṣaḥ śabdasaṅketārthajñānadeśādisarvādhyāsāḥ savitarkāyām||

[110,18] iha punas tanmātrasūkṣmadharmeṣu śabdasaṅketasyāprasiddhatvād viśiṣṭanāmasaṅketajñānānadhyāsaḥ deśādyadhyāsaś ca sūkṣmaviṣayatvañ vā^ iti savitarkāviśeṣatvasiddhiḥ||

[110,20] tatrāpy ekabuddhinirgrāhyam evoditadharmaci\viśiṣṭaṃ uktadeśādidharmaviśiṣṭam udbhūtadharmaviśiṣṭaṃ vā bhūtasūkṣmam ālambanībhūtaṃ na tu nirviṣayaṃ jñānam eva samādhiprajñāyām upatiṣṭhate||

[110,23] yadā punaḥ sarvathā sarvaprakāreṇa sarvataḥ sarvatra śāntāś coditāś cāvyapadeśyāś ca ye dharmās te śāntoditāvyapadeśyadharmāḥ taiḥ anavacchinneṣu| śānta^ iti yo vyāpāraṃ kṛtvā^ uparato dharmaḥ| udita iti yo vyāpāram ārūḍhaḥ| avyapadeśya^ iti na śānto nāpy uditaḥ nāpy udbhāvayad dṛśyamānanimittaḥ| tadyathā suvarṇasyādṛśṭānekāśrutākāropajanaśaktayas tā^ avyapadeśyā dharmāḥ| tair anavacchinneṣu (111,12) bhūtasūkṣmeṣu sarvadharmānupātiṣu sarvan dharmān anupatanti sūkṣmāṇi sarvaviśeṣārambhakatvāt| sarvātmakeṣu sarvebhyo viṣayebhyo 'nantatvāt tanmātrāṇām| tatra yā samāpattiḥ sā nirvicāreti| nirvitarkāyā yal lakṣaṇaṃ tat sarvam iha nivicārāyāṃ draṣṭavyam||

taddarśayati-- evaṃsvarūpaṃ hi tadbhūtasūkṣmamanenaiva svarūpeṇa deśakāla - nimittānubhavādiviśeṣavirahitena sarvātmakena sarvadharmānupātinā ālambanībhūtameva samādhiprajñāmuparañjayati ||

[110,18] prajñā ca svarūpaśūnyevārthamātrā tadā bhavati tadā nirvicārā| tasmin nirvitarkāviśeṣā nirvicārā| tat svayam eva darśayati mahadvastuviṣayā| mahataḥ vastu mahadvastv iti samāsaḥ rāhuśira^ iti yathā tadviṣayo yasyāḥ sā mahadvastuviṣayā savitarkā nirvitarkā ca sūkṣmavastu-viṣayā savicārā nirvicārā ceti||

[111,22] etayā^ eva savitarkanirvitarkākhyayā dvayyā samāpattyā savicārā nirvicārā ca samāpattir dvayī vyākhyātā ubhayor iti savitarkanirvitarkayor badhye^ etayā^ eva nirvitarkayā vitarkahānir vyākhyātā nirvicārā vyākhyāteti| savicārā cārthāt savitarkayā vyākhyātā lakṣaṇasyobhayatra samānatvāt| uktaś ca viśeṣaḥ ||1.44||

[111,26] savicārā nirviārā ca samāpattiḥ sūkṣmaviṣayety uktam| tat-prasaṅgenesaṃ cintyate ki(m asamānaṃ )m avasānaṃ sūkṣmaviṣayatvam iti| sūkṣmaviṣayatvañ cāliṅgaparyavasānam| pārthivasyāṇor gandhamātrasvarūpamātrateti pṛthivīsamīkṣayā sūkṣmo viṣayaḥ||

[112,11] gandhamātrasvarūpamātrasyāpi liṅgamātraṃ mahattattvaṃ sūkṣmo viṣayaḥ| tato liṅgamātrān mahato nirvṛttāni sahāhaṅkāreṇa tan-mātrāṇi layaṃ gacchatīti liṅgaṃ layād vā pradhānāl liṅgāt 
pratyāgacchantīti liṅgaṃ tadviparītam aliṅgaṃ pradhānaṃ na layaṃ gacchati nāpy anyataḥ lutaś cit layād āgacchati| liṅgamātrasyāliṅga sūkṣmo viṣayaḥ||

[112,16] na cāliṅgāt paraṃ sūkṣmam asti| sūkṣmaṃ nāma sthūlakāryāpekṣayā kāraṇaṃ tac cāliṅgāvasānam| na cāliṅgāt 
pradhānā(l liṅgā)d anyat sūkṣmaṃ kāraṇam asti ||

[112,18] nanu puruṣa^ iti| nanu tatra puruṣasyānāśaṅkā^ eva kāryakāraṇasūkṣmatayā prakramāt na^ eṣa doṣaḥ puruṣasyāpi kaiścit kāraṇatvasyābhyupagamāt| tadā śaṅkayā^ eva hy etad ucyate nanu puruṣa iti| satyam aliṅgātmaka[tva]m aliṅgasya saukṣmyam| kutaḥ yataḥ kāraṇam alliṅgaṃ liṅgasya| yady asyānvayikāraṇaṃ tattadapekṣayā sūkṣmam iti prakṛtam| nanu puruṣo .apy aliṅgātmaka eva| bādhaṃ, na tu liṅgāpekṣayā puruṣaḥ sūkṣmaḥ| kutaḥ yat astu liṅgasyānvayikāraṇaṃ puruṣo na bhavati saty apy aliṅgātmakatve| kāryasya caitanyenānanvitatvāt|| 112,25     kiñ ca puruṣasyāpi bhogyatvaprasaṅgaḥ| tathā ca pārārthyaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohātmakatvaṃ ca nirhetukatvaṃ ca pradhānasya (puruṣasya)| tac ca sarvapramāṇa(prāṇa)viprakupitam ity ata^ āha hetas tu bhavatīti| 
pradhānapravṛttau bhoktṛtvena nimittaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ| ataḥ 
pradhānasya saukṣmyaṃ niratiśayaṃ vyākhgyātam||1.45||

[113,14] tā eva sabījaḥ samādhiḥ | catasraḥ samāpattayo bahirvastuviṣayā iti bahirvastuviṣayatvaṃ hetuḥ samījatve| samādhir api sabījaḥ samprajñāto vitarkavicārānandāsmitārūpānugamāt samprajñātaḥ ity upavyākhyātaḥ| sthūle 'rthe savitarko nirvitarkaḥ sūkṣme 'rthe savicāro nirvicāra^ iti copavyākhyātaḥ vitarkādilakṣaṇasūtreṇa||1.46||

[113,19] nirvicāravaiśāradye 'dhyātmāprasādaḥ| aśuddhyāvaraṇamalāpetasya aśuddhir eva^ āvaraṇaṃ tad eva malaṃ kleśādikaṃ tasmād apetasya 
prakāśātmano buddhisattvasya rajastamobhyām anabhibhūtaḥ svacchaḥ sthitipravāho vaiśāradyam| yadā nirvicārasya samādher vaiśāradyam idaṃ jāyate tadā yogino bhavaty adhyātmaprasādaḥ ātmādijñānavivekaḥ||

asyaivavyākhyānam bhūtārthaviṣayaḥ kramānurodhī paripāṭikayā yāvatkleśakṣayam anuruddhyata iti kramānurodhī sphuṭaṃ viviktataraṃ śuddhatarātmakaḥ 
prajñālokaḥ prajñāivālokaḥ tayā yathāvastu jānāti ||

[114,13] tathā coktam prajñāprāsādam ārudhya na śocyaś śocato janān| bhūmiṣṭhān iva śaulasthaḥ sarvān prājño 'nupaśyati|| iti|| 1.47||



[114,16] ṛtaṃbharā tatra prajñā| tasmin prajñāloke 'dhyātmaprasāde samāhitasya yā prajñā jāayate vivekajanmā tasyā vivekajanmanaḥ ṛtambharā^ iti saṃjñā bhavati| anvarthā^ eva sā satyam eva vibharti na tatra viparyāsajñānagandho apy asti| prakṣīṇasakalaviparyāsakalmaṣasya hi jāyate| jāyamānā ca viṣayakatthaṅkam amanudati||

[114,21] tathā coktam

[114,22] āgamenānumānena dhyānābhyāsarasena ca| tridhā prakalpayan prajñāṃ labhate yogam uttamam|| iti.

[114,24] yogāṅgaprajñāṃ tridhā vibhajate| tatra^ eko bhāgaḥ śāstrācāropadiṣṭārthānusārī dvitīyas tasya^ eva yuktyā 'numānena vicāryāgamārthavirodhinirākaraṇapūrvakaṃ tat samyag upapādanaparaḥ| tṛtīyas tu sadāgama^anumānasamyag-upapannārthālambanasya 
pratyayasyānuśīlanaṃ rasopayogī| tad itthaṃ tridhā prakalpayan yogī yagaṃ labhate||1.48||

[115,13] sā punaḥ ṛtambharā prajñā sāmānyapuruṣapratyayaviṣayād anyaviṣayety āha śrutānumānaprajñābhyām anyaviṣayā| viśeṣaḥ viśeṣāṇām anantyān na saṅketayituṃ śakyate| sāmānyena hi kṛtasaṅketaḥ śabdaḥ| tataś cāgṛhītaviśeṣasaṅketo na viśeṣam abhidhātuṃ jñāpayituṃ vā śaknoti||

[115,17] tathānumānaṃ sāmānyaviṣayam eva| tad apy uktam eva sāmānyāvadhāraṇapradhānā vṛttih iti tathodāharaṇena ca pratipāditam yatra 
prāptis tatra gatiḥ| yatrāprāptis tatra na bhavati gatiḥ ity anumānena sāmānyopasaṃhāraḥ| sā mānyamātram anumānena^ upasaṃhriyate  tasmāc chrutānumānaviññānaviṣayo na viśeṣaḥ kaścid asti||

[115,22] yady evaṃ na lokapratyakṣeṇāpareṇa bhūtasūkṣmagataḥ puruṣagato vā viśeṣaḥ prajñāyeta| nānyac ca pramāṇatrayavyatiriktaṃ pramāṇam asti yena tadviśeṣaṃ sañjānīmahe^ iti ced āha na cāsya sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭasya vastuno lokapratyakṣeṇa grahaṇam| na cāsya viśeṣasyāpramāṇakasyābhāo 'stīti| pramāṇāvagamyamānarūpo hi bhāvo 'sti rājavīthīm adhirohati| tasmāt samādhiprajñānirgrāhya^ eva viśeṣo bhavati bhūtasūkṣmagataḥ puruṣagato vā||

[116,13] nanu ca te sūkṣmagatā viśeṣāḥ pratyakṣeṇāvagamyante^ iti kim īśvaraññāpitam vastutvād avagamyanta^ iti cet āgamānumānābhyāṃ santīty avagamyanta^ eva| na ca niyāmakaṃ kiṃcid asti pratyakṣeṇa^ eva nirgrāhyā iti| na hi viśeṣā hastatalagatā^ api sarve gṛhyante na anyavastugataviśeṣāṇāṃ pratyakṣagrāhyatvasyānumeyatvāt||

[116,17] katham evaṃ manyate bhūtasūkṣmādgatā viśeṣāḥ pratyakṣagrāhyāḥ kasya cit kāryavastuviśeṣatvāt gṛhyamāṇasvapāṇigataviśeṣavad iti| tasmāc chrutānumānaprajñābhyām anyaviṣayā sā prajñā viśeṣārthatvād iti| viśeṣārthā hi sā||1.49||

[116,20] samādhiprajñāpratilambhe yoginaḥ prajñākṛtaḥ saṃskāro navo jāyate| prajñā hi saṃskārārambhiṇīti siddham| prajñābhinavbatvāt tatsaṃskāraḥ pratyagrataro jñyate^ iti viśeṣaḥ| prajñānavatvaṃ tu tasyā (sāmānya)anyaviṣayatvāt| taj-jassaṃskāro'nyasaṃskārapratibandhī ṛtumbharaprajñāprabhavasaṃskāro vyutthānasaṃskārāśayaṃ anyaṃ bādhate viśiṣṭyarthārthaviṣayaprabhavatvāt pratibandhnāti| ā mokṣāc cheta iti saṃskārāśayaḥ||

[116,26] vyutthānasaṃskārābhibhavāt vyutthānasaṃskārāśayasya samadhiprajñāsaṃskāreṇābhibhavāt tat-prabhavāḥ vyutthānasaṃskāraprabhavāḥ 
pratyayā na bhavanti| pratyayanirodhe vyutthānapratyayanirodhe samādhiḥ samprajñāta^ iva^ upatiṣṭhate| tataḥ samādhijā prajñā tataḥ prajñākṛtaḥ saṃskārā iti^ evaṃ navaḥ saṃskārāśayo vardhate| tataś ca prajñā tataś ca saṃskārā iti||

[117,12] atrāha samādhiprajñābhavaḥ saṃskārāśayaś cittasyādhikārapravṛttihetuḥ pratyayaprabhavatāt vyutthānaprabhavasaṃskārāśayavad iti tathā cittasaṃskāratvāc ceti| tad etad darśayati katham asau saṃskārāśayaḥ cittaṃ sādhikāraṃ saṃskāropetaṃ na kariṣyato^ iti||

[117,16] na^ eṣa doṣaḥ tadviroshitvāt| kathaṃ te saṃskārāḥ kleśakṣayahetutvāc cittam adhikāraviśiṣṭaṃ na kurvanti| vyutthānapratyayaprabhavānāṃ hi saṃskārāṇām avidyādikleśahetutvāc cittasaṃskārābhimukhabhāvakāraṇatā| tadvirodhipratyayaprabhavatvāt tu na samādhijapratyayajanmanāṃ saṃskārāṇām adhikārābhimukhyanimittatā||

[117,20] kiñ ca cittañ ca te svakāryāt saṃskārābhimukhavṛttibhyo 'vasādayanti viramayanti| khyātiparyavasānaṃ hi cittavicoṣtitam| 
puruṣakhyātiś ca jātā saṃskāraś ca bhavatīti viruddham||

[117,23] yo hi vigatatṛṣṇāsamuāyo nāsau pānīyaṃ pipāsati| etāvad eva cittena puruṣasya karaṇīyam| na hi kaścit kṛtaṃ karaṇīyaṃ cikīrṣati||1.50||

[118,8] kiñ ca tasyāpi nirodhe sarvanirodhān nirbījas samādhir iti| iti śabdaḥ parisamāptyarthaḥ| tasyāpi samādhiprajñāprabhavasya saṃskārasyābhinavasya nirodhe^ apiśabdāt saṃskārakāraṇasya samādhiprajñāyā api nirodhe sarvanirodho bhavati| uktam evopāyadvayaṃ 
paravairāgyavirāmapratyayābhyāsākhyaṃ tannirodhasya||

[118,12] kiṃ punas tat sarvaṃ ṛtambharaprajñā-janitasaṃskāraḥ samādhiprajñānirodhī yathā^ eva tāṃ samādhiprajñām ṛtambharāṃ niruṇāddhi tathaivātmanā sahātmasajātīyānāṃ svakāraṇaprajñkṛtānāṃ saṃskārāṇām api 
pratibandhī nirodhād bhavati||

[118,17] kasmāt nirodhajaḥ saṃskāraḥ yasmāt samādhiprajñāyās tajjanmanaś ca saṃskārasya nirodhe nirodhakaḥ samādhiprajñājanitasaṃskārād anyo jāyate sa cāpi yataḥ samādhiprajñāṃ tajjanitasaṃskārāṃścātiraskṛtasya na janma 
pratipadyate tasmāt sa nirodhajaḥ saṃskāraḥ samādhijān saṃskāran bādhata iti||

[118,21] kathaṃ punar asau nirodhajaḥ saṃskāro 'stīti gamyate vidyamane api sa bādhako bhavatīti^ āha nirodhasthitikālakramānubhaveneti| nirodhasya sthitis tasyāḥ kālaḥ sa ca kālaḥ krameṇānubhūyate yataḥ 
pratabhyāsam abhivardhate tasmāt tena nirodhasthitikālakremānubhavena nirodhacittakṛtaṃ nirodhāvasthacittakṛtaṃ saṃskārāstitvam anumeyam||

[119,10] vyutthānanirodhasamādhiprabhavaiḥ vyutthānasamādhiprabhavaiḥ nirodhasamādhiprabhavaś ca saha kaivalyabhāgīyaiḥ saṃskāraiś cittaṃ avasitādhikāratvāt svasyāṃ prakṛtau svakāraṇe 'smitāyām ahaṅkāre pravilīyate pratyastameti| tasmāt te khyātinirodhajnmānaḥ saṃskārā yasmāt cittasyādhikāravirodhinaḥ na sthitihetavo bhavantīti||

[119,16] yasmād avasitādhikāraṃ saha kaivalyabhāgīyaiḥ saṃskāraiś cittaṃ yaṃ puruṣaṃ prati caritaprayojanaṃ tasmāt nirvartate| tasminvinivṛtte puruṣaḥ svarūpamātrapratiṣṭhaḥ kevalo mukta^ ity ucyate cittavṛttinivṛttir eva muktiḥ iti ||1.51||

[119,19]iti śrīgovindabhavgavatpūjyapādaśiṣyasya paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryasya śrī-śaṅkarabhagavataḥ kṛtau śrīpātañjalayoga(śāstra)sūtrabhāṣyavivaraṇe ||prathamaḥ samādhipādaḥ|| 



[121,10] athedānīṃ dvitīyaḥ sādhanapāda ārabhyate| kaivalyasya sādhanaṃ samyagdarśanam| yogasādhanāni ca yogadvāreṇa samyagdarśanasādhanāny eva| tāni ca prādhānyenāsmin pāde pratipādyanta iti sādhanapāda ity ucyate||

[121,13] tathā yogasādhanānuṣṭhānapravṛttasyānuṣaṅgikavibhūteḥ prādhānyena pratipādanād vibhūtipāda^ ity ucyate tṛtīyaḥ 121,15   tathā sakalayogaiśvaryavibhūtibhyo viraktasya sarvopasaṃhāradvāreṇa kaivalyasya prādhānyena pradarśanāt kaivalyapāda^ ity ucyate caturthaḥ||

[121,15] tathā samādhiḥ prādhānyenādāv upālbhyāta iti prathamaḥ samādhipāda ity ucyate||

[121,19] tad etad āha^ uddiṣṭaḥ samāhitacittasya yoga^ iti karmaṇy acittasya^ ity arthaḥ| maitrīkaruṇādibhāvanādyupā(khyā)yalabdhisthitipadasya yoginaḥ 
paravairāgyavirāmapratyayyasādhano nirbījaḥ samādhir uddiṣṭaḥ| kathaṃ vyutthitacitto api yoga-yuktaḥ syād iti| vikṣiptacitto api kathaṃ nāma samādhiyogyo bhaved ity ata^ idam ārabhyate| 

[121,23] tapādisādhanam anukramyate tapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānāni kriyā-yogaḥ| nanu ca tapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānāni niyameṣu vadiṣyamāṇāni kim artham ihocyante|| 

[122,1] nanu ca^ etad eva prayojanaṃ yad^ uktaṃ vyutthitacitto api yoga-yuktaḥ syād iti||

[122,2] na^ etad asti yatra niyamāḥ paṭhyante tatra^ evedaṃ prayojanaṃ sidhyati||

[122,3] atha kleśatanūkaraṇārtham iti cet tasyāpi tatra^ evābhidhānāt| yathā "yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād aśuddhikṣaye" iti| aśuddhiś ca kleśādiḥ| samdhibhāvanārthañ ca yogāṅgānuṣṭhānam āmnāyiṣyata^ eva| yathā śamādhisiddhr īśvarapraṇidhānāt" iti| tasmād iha tapādisamāmnānam anarthakam||

[122,7] na yogāṅgānuṣṭhānasya samyagdarśanopāyasya samyagdarśanapūrvatvapratipādanārthatvāt| sarvāṇi ca yogāṅgāni samyagdarśanopāyatvāt samyagdarśanaprāk kālāni| teṣāṃ ca niyamarkadeśa^ iha paṭhitaḥ sarvopāyaprākk-ālatve nidarśanāya| yady apy upāyatvād eva prāk-kālatvam arthāt siddham tathā api spaṣṭatvam evaṃ sati syād iti||

[122,11] nanu ca^ evam api prāk samyagdarśanād anantaraṃ paṭhitavyam ucyate na samyagdarśanasya^ eva sākṣātkleśādipratipakṣatvāt| avidyā hi samastānarthamūlam | sā ca sākṣāt pratyanīkabhūtasamyagdarśanena nirvartyate| tapāades tadanantraṃ pāṭhe saty avidyāsamyagdarśanayor vyavadhānaṃ bhavet| tato 'abhiśaṃkena kiṃ avidyāyās tapāadiḥ sākṣāt 
pratipakṣaḥ kiṃ vā samyagdarśanam eva^ iti||

[122,16] kiñ ca viṣayaś ca prathamaṃ darśanīyaḥ samyagdarśanaśāstrasya| sa ca kleśakarmavipākāśayābhibhūtaḥ puruṣaḥ| yathā rogārtaḥ cikitsāśāstrasya viṣayaḥ| tapādes tadanantaraṃ samāmnāne viṣayaviṣayiṇau vyavadhīyeyātāṃ tataś cāyaṃ viṣayo 'yañ ca viṣayīti na vivicyeta||

[122,20] pādādau ca pāthe samyagdarśanopāyatvaṃ ca pratipāditaṃ bhavati 
prathamapādasambandhaś ca vyākhyātaḥ syāt samāhitacittasya yoga^ uddiṣṭaḥ vyutthitacitto api tapādisādhanaḥ kathaṃ yoga-yuktaḥ syād iti||

[122,23] kiñ ca samādhibhāvanakleśatanūkaraṇārthatvañ ca tapādīnāṃ darśayitavyam||

[123,4] nanu parastād evaitad vakṣyati śamādhisiddhir iśvarapraṇidhānāt(_Y_ṣ 2.45)" "kāyendriyasiddhir aśuddhikṣayāt tapasaḥ(_Y_ṣ 2.43)" ity evam| pratyekasādhanānāṃ siddhiṃ ca vakṣyati||

[123,7] na^ etad asti siddhānām iha tatra sādhanastutyartham anuvādāt| atra hi tādarthyam ucyate| tathā ca^ āha śamādhibhāvanārthaḥ kleśatanūkaraṇārthaś ca(_Y_ṣ 2.2)" iti| tatra hy anūdyante sādhanastutaye||

[123,10] samprati sūtrākṣarāṇi vyākhyāyante tapaś ca svādhyāyaś ca^ īśvarapraṇidhānañ ca tapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānāni| tāny eva kriyārūpo yogaḥ kriyāyogah| tapādikriyā ca yogārthatvād yoga^ ity ucyate| cittadharmo hi samādhir yogaḥ| tadarthaś cāyaṃ kriyā-yogaḥ| tasmād anena kriyā-yogena yogīti||

[123,14] tapa^ iti kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi śītoṣṇādidvandvasahatvam| svādhyāyaḥ praṇavasya mokṣaśāstrāṇāṃ copanisatprabhṛtīnāṃ pavitrāṇāṃ japaḥ| īśvarapraṇidhānaṃ kriyāṇāṃ paramagurāv īśvare samarpaṇam| tāsāṃ vā phalasaṃnyāsaḥ parameśvare saṃnyasanam||

[123,17] kathaṃ punaś cittasamādhānaṃ prati viprakṛṣṭasya kāyakleśādirūpasya tapaso yogatvam svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānayos tu yuktam antaraṅgatvād yogatvam iti tadartham āha nātapasvino yogaḥ sidhyatīti||

[123,20] śarīrādipoṣaṇābhiratacittasya kāyendriyamanaḥkhed aparihārāyaṇasyātyantaśarīrādy ātmatvadarśinaḥ sukumārataraṃmanyasya na yogaḥ sidhyatīti tapasa^ upādānam||

[123,22] kiñ cānādivāsanācitrā viṣayagrahaṇavāsanābhir anādikālabhir uparacitavicitrabhāvā tathā pratypasthitaviṣayajālā vartamānakālā viṣayā eva jālaṃ tena ca vartamānaviṣayākhyena jālena^ ānāyena jhaṣan iva cittaṃ acuddhiḥ pāśayati yā sā pratyupasthitaviṣayajalā satī yasmāt (124,13) ṇaṇṭāṛeṇa tapasā vinā sambhedaṃ vināśam āpadyate^ asambhinnāśuddheś cittasya kutaḥ samādhiḥ iti tatsambhedanāya ca tapasa^ upādānam| tac ca tapah cittaprasādanaṃ samādhyantaraṅgaṃ abādhamānam anenāsevyam iti yogā manyante| cittaprasādārthatvāt tapasas tam eva ced bādheta tādarthyam eva hīyate||2.1||

[124,17] sa hi kriyā-yogaḥ kim artha^ iti tasya pārārthyapratipādanārtham āha samādhibhāvanārthaḥ kleśatanūkaraṇāyaś ca||

[124,19] kathaṃ punar ubhayārthatvam ity āha sa hi^ āsevyamānaḥ samādhiṃ bhāvayati| itara-yogāṅgasahitaḥ samādhiṃ bhāvayati kleśāś ca tanū-karoti| vakṣyati ca "yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād aśuddhikṣaye jñānadīptir ā vivekakhyāteḥ(_Y_ṣ 2.48)"  iti| tad etad āha tanūkṛtān kleśān 
prasaṃkhyanena kleśatanūkaraṇāt sañjātasamyagdarśanaśīlanena dagdhabījakalpān bījānīva dagdhaprasavaśaktīn ata^ evāprasavadharmiṇaḥ avidyamānaḥ prasavo yeṣāṃ te^ aprasavāḥ aprasavāś ca te dharmiṇaś cety aprasavadharmiṇaḥ(sarvabījādiṣu vā draṣṭavyam) tānaprasavadharmiṇaḥ kariṣyati prasavaśaktirahitān kariṣyati||

[125,7] teṣāṃ tanūkaraṇānujanmā punaḥ kleśair aparāmṛṣṭā satvapuruṣānyatāmātrakhyātiḥ samāptādhikārā prajñā pratiprasavāya [kalpiṣyate] saṃsārābhimukhatāyai punar na kalpiṣyate||

[125,10] yady api śāstrācāryopadeśena yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād ṛte api satvapuruṣānyatāmātrakhyātir upajāyate tathā api na kleśādharmādīnatīvātiraskṛtya sā jāyateti punaḥ punaḥ kleśādibhiḥ 
parāmṛśyata^ ity etad darśayati punaḥ kleśair aparāmṛṣṭey anena||2.2||

[125,14] ke te kleśāḥ svarūpeṇa kiyanto vā saṃkhyayā iti saṃkhyāsvarūpapratipādanārthaṃ sūtraṃ praṇīyate avidyā-asmitā-rāgadveśābhiniveśāḥ kleśāḥ eṣām ekaikasya lakṣaṇaṃ sūtrair eva vyākhyāyiṣyata^ iti [na] vyākhyeyatve yatnaḥ kriyate vyākhyeyaṃ tv etāvad eva||

[125,18] yathā pramāṇaviparyayavikalpādayaḥ parasparabhinnāḥ na tathā kleśānāṃ viparyayād bhedaḥ| kintarhi viparyayasya^ evaite bhedā iti| kasmāt viparyayabhāvabhāvitvād ity etad āha pañca viparyayā ity arthaḥ||

[125,21] nanu ca yathā vṛttibhedavyākhyāne pramāṇavṛtteḥ "pratykṣānumānāgamāḥ pramāṇāni" iti tridhā bhedas tatra^ eva vyākhyātaḥ tathā kasmāt tatra^ evāsya viparyayasyāpi bhedaḥ pañcadhā nopavyākhyātaḥ cittavṛtter eva ca viparyaya^ ity uktam| tathā codāhataṃśadviṣayeṇaikacandradarśanena dvicandradarśanaṃ bādhyate" iti||

[126,6] na^eṣa doṣaḥ kleśānām avṛttikatvāt| na hi kleśāḥ pratyayamātrāḥ 
pratyayamātrā hi vṛttayaḥ| kleśās tu cittasya malaṃ yathā cakṣuṣas timiram| kleśamalaviyoge cittasya viparyayapratyayābhāvāt||

[126,9] yadi hi pratyaya^ eva viparyayyaḥ tadā yathā kleśanirodhe api yoginaḥ smṛtipramāṇapratyayā bhavanti tathā viparyayo api bhavet| na tu viparyayaḥ kṣīṇakleśānām| tasmāc cakṣuṣa^ ivāyathārthagrahaṇanimittaṃ 
pratyayasya malaṃ viparyaya^ ity ucyate||

[126,13] tat punaḥ pratyayavyatirekeṇa pratipattum ākhyātuṃ cāśakyam iti pratyayena^ evākhyāyate "viparyayo vṛttiḥ" iti| tathā ca vṛttayaḥ kliṣṭākliṣṭaś ceti kleśena viśeṣitāḥ| na ca vṛttibhr eva vṛttīnāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ yuktam| tathā "kleśahetukā" iti ca vyākhgyātam| na ca vṛttaya eva vṛttihetukā iti yuktam||

[126,17] tasmāc cāvṛttitvād vṛttibhedatvena na tatra kleśā vyākhyātāḥ| tathā coktam"cittamalaprasaṅgenabhidhāyiṣyante" iti kleśānāṃ na vṛttitvaṃ malatvam eva^ ity abhiprāyeṇa| abhedopacāreṇa tu pratyayena sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ yathā dhūmo malair agniṃ garbhādaśāntavṛttās tathā jñānam eteneti, (?) tathaivātrāpi||

[126,21] pratyayaviparītatvam āha te spandamānā ityādinā| spandamānāḥ 
pratyayadvāreṇodbhāsamānāḥ na hi svatas teṣāś calanam asti vastrādirāgavat| guṇādhikāraṃ draḍhayanti guṇānām adhikāraṃ pravṛttiṃ (saṃ)kartavyatvaṃ sthirīkurvānti| na hi teṣu parispandamāneṣu kartavyatā-śūnyaṃ cittaṃ bhavati||

[126,25] parīṇāmam avasthāpayanti punah punaḥ pariṇamayantīti yāvat| kāryakaraṇasrota^ unnamayanti śarīrasya karaṇādīnāṃ cakṣur-ādīnāṃ 
pravṛttisrota^ ucchrā(va)yayanti| katham ity āha 
parasparānugrahatantrībhūtvā^ avidyā-ādaya itaretaranimittanaimittikabhāveneti||

[127,9] kiñ ca karmavipākāṃś cābhinirvartayanti| karma trividhaṃ tadvipākāṃś ca jātyāyur-bhogān iti||2.3||

[127,11] avidyā kṣetram uttareṣāṃ prasupatatanuvicchinnodārāṇām| katham asmitā-ādayo viparyayā iti tat-pradarśayate sūtreṇa^ eva| avidyā kṣetraṃ 
prasavabhūmiḥ ātmalābhakṣetraṃ yathā pṛthivī tṛṇalatāgulm avīrudhāṃ svātmano 'natiriktānāṃ evam avidyāto viparyayād avyatiriktānāṃ asmitā-ādīnāṃ caturṇām ekaikaśaḥ prasuptatanuvicchinnodāratayā caturvikalpānāṃ prasavabhūmir avidyā||

[127,16] viparyayapratyayā hi tām āśritya^ ātmānaṃ pratilabhante| tasmād acidyaivāsmitādicatuṣṭayarūpeṇa vipariṇamate||

[127,18] tatra kā prasuptir iti| asmitā-ādīnāṃ prasuptir nāma dharma ucyate nāvidyāyāḥ| kāraṇatvāt| sarvāsmitādivikalpeṣv anugamāt sarvadā 
prabuddhā^ eva sā na prasuptā| prasvapne vyavahārābhāvāt| asmitā-ādīnām anyatamasya prasuptāv anyatamasyāvasthānam avaśyam eva| anyathā kleśāntarasya^ udbhava^ eva na syāt| tasmād asmitā-ādīnām eva caturṇāṃ 
prasuptādicaturvikalpatvam||

[127,23] cetasi śaktimātrapratiṣṭhānam ity asya vyākhyānaṃ karoti bījabhāvopagama^ iti| yathā bījaṃ sad evānaṅkurībhavad api bhūmigataṃ tiṣṭhati tathā cetasyāsmitādayaḥ śaktirūpeṇānabhivyaktasvarūpāṅkurā vyavatiṣṭhante||

[127,26] tasya prabodha^ iti tasya kleśasyāsmitādeḥ prabodha^ udbhavaḥ ālambane viṣaye sammukhibhāvaḥ svavyañjakāñjanasya||

[128,10] tatra^ etat syāt sarvadā^ eva kleśasya bījabhāvopagamaḥ prabodhaś ca niyamena syād iti| tata^ āha prasaṃkhyānavataḥ samyagdarśanābhyāsavataḥ adagdhakleśabījasya yoginah saṃmukhībhūte apy ālambane nāsau bījabhāvopagamaḥ| dagdhasya dagdhatvād eva kutaḥ punaḥ prabodhaḥ udbhavaḥ| vidyamānasya hi bījasya kṣitijalādisaṃyoganimittaprāptāv aṅkurī-bhāvo na dagdhabījaśakteḥ tathā kleśasyāpi||

[128,15] yady evam eṣā pañcamī kasmād avasthā noktā dagdhaprasuptatanuvicchinnodārāṇam iti nāsādhāraṇatvāt| na hi sarvadehināṃ sādhāraṇī pañcamī kleśāvasthā| yogina^ eva hi sā tato nāmnāyate||

[128,18] kathaṃ vā^ asādhāraṇīti tata^ āha tatra^ eva sā samyagdarśanābhyāsavaty eva dagdhā nānyatra| kṣīṇakleśaḥ kuśalaś caram adeha^ iti samyagdarśana^ ucyate yogī| tatra^ eva sā dagdhabījabhāvā 
pañcamī kleśāvasthā| satāṃ kleśānāṃ tadānīṃ tagdhaṃ bījasāmarthyam| yathā vidyamānānāṃ yavādibījānāṃ bharjitānāṃ bījasāmarthyaṃ dagdham| viṣayasya sammukhī-bhāve api na bhavaty eṣāṃ prabodhaḥ samyagdarśinaḥ saṃskāraśeṣāvasthatvadiṣor iva lakṣyavimuktasya||

[128,24] uktā prasuptiḥ| kiñ ca dagdhabījānāṃ ca kleśānām aprabodho yogina^ ukta^ iti| tanutvaṃ idānīṃ ucyate pratipakṣabhāvanopahatāḥ 
pratipakṣasya śarīrādyavadyadarśanādeḥ bhāvanayopahatāh abalīkṛtā mandaprabhāvāḥ kleśās tanava^ ucyante||

[128,27] tathā vicchidya vicchidyārthāntaropajanakālāntarāleṣu svātmānam apradarśayantaḥ tena tenātmanā yena rūpeṇa pūrvam upalabdhās tena^ eva^ ātmanā samudācaranti punar udbhavantīti vicchinnāḥ||

[129,7] yadi vā vicchidya vicchidya vicchedakāle te vyaktenātmanā svarūpeṇa pūrvottarakālavat na samudācaranti||

[129,9] tatrodāharaṇam kathaṃ rāgakāle krodhasyādarśanāt| na hi rāgakāle krodhaḥ samudācarati| kutaḥ rūpāriśayānāṃ vṛttyariśayānāṃ ca virodhāt| sa hi rāgodayāttirobhūto'nyenāvyaktenātmanā vartata^ iti vicchinnaḥ||

[129,12] tathā rāgaś ca kvacid dṛśyamāno na viṣayāntare nāsti| kutaḥ viṣayavirodhād atra| pūrvatra rāgeṇa virodhād asamudācaraṇaṃ krodhasya| atra tu viṣayāṇām anyonyavirodhāt| na hi viṣaya^ ekasminn upajanayati rāgaṃ viṣayāntaram api tasya^ eva rāgasyotpādakam||

[129,16] tad yathā na^ekasyāṃ striyāṃ vcaitro rakta^ ity anyāsu viraktaḥ| kin tu tatra rāgo labdhavṛttiḥ yasyāṃ raktaḥ| anyatra bhaviṣyadvṛttiḥ anyāsu strīṣu sa hi bhaviṣyad rāgavyaktiviśeṣaḥ tadā prasuptas tanur vā^ iti||

[129,19] nanu ca rāgaś kacit dṛśyamāna^ ityāddi bhāṣyaṃ kimarthaṃ nanu rāgasvabhāvākhyānaṃ ktthaṃ viṣayabhedāl labdhabhaviṣyadvṛttisvabhāvo rāga iti satyam tathāpi prakrāntānupayogaḥ| prakrāntaṃ hi vicchedavyākhyānaṃ| na ca^ etad vicchedavyākhyānam| nāpi vakṣyamāṇodāravyākhyānnena sambandhaḥ| tasya nirākāṅkṣatvāt||

[129,23] evaṃ tarhi pūrvaśeṣa^ evāyaṃ| katham evaṃ manyate 
prasuptatanūdāratvavtyatirekeṇa vicchittir nāma dharma^ eva nāsti| katham yāvan na labdhavṛttis tāvat prasupta^ ity uktam| pratipakṣabhāvanopahato mandapravṛttis tanur ity ucyate||

[130,1] yaś cāpi labdhapravṛttiḥ sa^ udāra^ ity ākhyāyate| vicchinno nāma sa bhavati yaḥ kvacid upalabdhaḥ punar upalabhyate| yathā sarasvatīsrotaḥ| na hi rāgodaye krodhaḥ kvacid api dṛśyate| tasmānnityā na vicchittir nāma dharmo 'sti| adarśanāt||

[130,4] atrocyate yady adarśanād vicchedo nāsti tadā rāgasya kvacid dṛśyamānasyānyatrādarśanād abhāvo 'stu| nanv astv evābhāvaḥ anyatrādarśanāt| na hi| kasmāt ? kvacit dṛśyamānatvād eva| na hi viṣayāntarābhimukho na bhavatīti dharmiṇy api cetasi nāsti| na hi cakṣuṣaḥ kadācid gajābhimukhaṃ na bhavatīti ghaṭābhimukhapravṛtaṃ sannāsti| ghaṭābhimukhadarśanād eva| tad etad āha kvacit dṛśyamāno na viṣayāntare nāstīty ādi||

[130,10] tathā krodho api rāgeṇodbhūtenābhibhūtatvād vicchinnadarśano bhaviṣyati sarasvatīsrotovad eva| sa hi viṣayāntare prasuptaḥ tanur vā| tadābhimukhyenādṛśyamānaviśeṣatvāt||

[130,13] kaḥ punaḥ prasuptivicchedayor viśeṣaḥ ? yāvatobhayatrādarśanam aviśiṣṭam| ucyate pravṛttidarśanaprabandhasyāntarāvirodhinā 'anubaddhatvād adarśanaṃ vicchittir adagdhasya||

[130,18] nanu ca^ evaṃ sati śa(na)hi tadā prasuptas tanur vā^ iti viruddhaṃ pravṛter adarśanaprabandho hi viṣayāntareṇa dṛśyate na viṣayāntābhimukhyasya vivakṣitatvāt| viṣayāntarābhimukhyaṃ nāma yo rāgasya dharmaḥ sa hi tadā prasuptaḥ tanur vā| na rāgaḥ sarvadā^ ātmanā^ eva rāgakāle sarvaviṣayeṣu na dṛśyate||

[130,23] athavā vicchinodāharaṇāntarapratipādanārtham eva rāgaś ca kvacid ityādi bhāṣyam| kathaṃ dviprakāro vicchedaḥ pradarśayuṣya(ti) te| ekas tāvat pravṛttadarśanaprabandhasya virodhinā 'bhibhavāt sarvatrādarśanam| yathā darśitaṃ rāgakāle lrodhasyādarśanād iti||

[130,27] tathā 'yam anyo viṣayaviśeṣe dṛśyamānasya^ eva viṣayāntare 
prāptinimittasyādarśanam| yathā caitrasya^ ekasyāṃ striyāṃ raktasyānyāsu satīṣv api rāgasyādarśanaṃ (131,10) tatra labdhavṛttir anyatra na dṛśyata^ ity āha sa hi tadā prasuptas tanur vā| na hi prasuptis tānavaṃ vā vivakṣyate| yathā tathā vā bhavatu| tatrādarśanaṃ vicchedatvena vivakṣitam||

[131,13] viṣaye yo labdhavṛttiḥ sa^ udāraḥ| nanu ca 
prasuptatanuvicchinnānāṃ kāryāsamarthānāṃ na kleśatā| na hi prasuptas tanur vicchinno vā prasuptivicchittitanniyamāvasthāyāṃ kāryaṃ kartuṃ kṣamate| na hy ajātaśśatrur upaghātāyālam| vicchhinne vā pradeśe srotasi srātuṃ śakyam| tasmād avidyā kṣetram uttareṣām ity etāvad evāstu| kin tair akāryakṣamaiḥ prasuptatanuvicchinnair upadiṣṭaiḥ| etad āśaṃkyāha sarve ca^ ete kleśaviṣayatvn nātikrāmantīti| na hi śatruḥ prasupto janiṣyamāṇo vā nāpakariṣyati||

[131,19] kas tarhi vicchinnaḥ prasuptas tanur udāro vā kleśaḥ| na yuktam evaṃ bhedena vacanam| ekam evodāharaṇam astu| na codārāvasthāyā^ anyatra kāryaṃ kariṣyati||

[131,21] satyam evam etat| tathāpi parihāra^ ucyate kiṃ tu viśiṣṭānām evaiteṣāṃ apariktyuktakleśatvadhamakāraṇām eva vicchinnāditvam| vicchinnādyavasthāyām api nāpagamadapakrtṛtvaśaktir ity adoṣaḥ||

[131,24] tatkāle kāryākṣamatvam iti cet na pratipakṣavidhānārthatvād vacanasya| na hi śatror adṛśyamānasya prasuptasya janiṣyamāṇasya vā na 
prativighātavyam||

[131,26] śrūyate cendraḥ pratipakṣamayena diter garbham bhinad iti| tatra kāraṇaṃ svayam eva prakaṭayati yathā^ eva pratipakṣabhāvanāto nivṛttis tathā^ eva svavyañjakāñjanena viṣayādinā^ abhivyakta^ iti||

[132,9] yasmāt te punar abhivyaktāḥ punaś ca tirohitāḥ punar apy abhivyañjiṣyante ca tasmāt prasutādyavasthā^ api pratipakṣavidhānāya vaktavyā eva kleśatvānatikrāntatvāt||

[132,12] idānīm upasaṃharati sarva^ evāmī kleśā^ avidyābhedāḥ| kasmāt yasmāt sarveṣu^ asmitā-ādiṣu avidyā^ eābhiplavate pratarati^ udbhūtā dṛśyate||

[132,14] kiñca yad^ avidyayā vastvākāryate prakalpate tad anuśerate tadviṣayā upajāyante kleśāḥ rāgādayaḥ| yathā stryādipiṇḍe heye api bhogyo 'yam ity avidyayā parikalpayātmābhimānaṃ kṛtvā rajyate| dharmajñāne viparītaṃ prarikalpya dveṣṭi||

[132,18] kathaṃ punas tad evānuśerate^ iti yasmāt viparyāsapratyayakāla evopalabhyante| itaś cāvidyābhedāḥ yasmāt kṣīyamāṇām avidyām anu kṣīyanta^ iti| avisyāpraṇāśānupraṇāśino hi kleśā^ asmitā-ādayaḥ| na hy asatyām avidyāyāṃ kaścid api kleśaḥ sambhavati||2.4|| 

[132,19] tatrāvidyāsvarūpam āha^ ucyate| kiṃ punar avidyāsvarūpākhyāne prayojanaṃ tat svayam eva bhāṣyakṛd ākhyāsyate^ avidyā mūlam asya kleśasantānasya ityādinā| ajñāte ca kleśasantānamūlasvarūpe tan-mūlonmūlanaṃ na śakyaṃ kartum iti tat svarūpam^ ākhyāyate||

[133,7] anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhyātmakhyātir avidyā| katham anitye kārye nityakhyātiḥ nityam  iti viparyayabuddhiḥ yathā dhruvā 
pṛthivī dhruvā sa candratārakā dyaiḥ amṛtā divaikasa^ iti||

[133,12] nanu ca^ etasmān mantrāt pṛthivyādīnāṃ dhrutvaṃ pratīyate| tatra katham eṣāṃ kāryatvam ucyate^ āpekṣikatvād adoṣaḥ| manuṣyādyapekṣayā 
pṛthivyādayo dhruvāḥ| kāryatvam anityatvaṃ casarvapramāṇasiddhaṃ śrutiṣu vacanāt dyair āsīn na pṛthivī nāntarikṣam ity ādiṣu siddham||

[133,16] tathā^ aśucau paramabījabhatse paramodvegakare 'tyantaghṛṇāspade kārātmasadganīvāśucau kāye| kathama(pi)sya bīmatsakatvam ity āha rathānādaśuciḥ garbhāśayasthānatvād aśuciḥ| bījād aśuciḥ śklaśoṇitabījo hi kāyaḥ| upaṣṭambhāt vātapittaśleṣmopaṣṭambhāt| teṣāṃ cāśucitvaṃ 
prasiddham| niṣyandāt prasvedamūtrpurīpādimalājasnaniṣyandāt| nidhānād api maraṇāt| mṛtasaṃbandhināṃ daśāhādyāśaucasmaraṇam||

[133,22] nanu ca svabhāvato yady āśuciḥ kāyas tasya śaucavidhānama anarthakaṃ prāapnotīty ata^ āha kāyamodheyaśaucatvād iti| śāstrata ādhātavyam eva śaucam asmin na svato 'stīti yāvat||

[133,25] yad vā karaṇāni buddhisattvādīni sattvātmakatayā śucīnyādheyānyasminn iti tacchucitvād asyāpi śaucam| buddhisattvādyādheyaviprayoge cātyantāśucitvadarśanāt||

[133,27] tasmāt piṇḍatā hy aśuciṃ viduḥ| piṇḍitagrahaṇaṃ viparyastadhiyāṃ śucitvagrahaṇakhyāpanārtham| aśucau śucikhyātir dṛśyate| viparītacetasām||

[134,12] nanv eva śaśāākalekhā kamanīyā^ iyaṃ kanyā^ amṛtāvayavanirmitā^ iva candraṃ bhitvā^ iva nismṛtā jñāyate nīlotpalapatrākṣī bhāvagarbhābhyāṃ locanābhyāṃ jīvalokaṃ samāśvāsayantīti kasya kenābhisaṃbandho bhavatīti| kva śarīrādi yāthātmyaṃ kva bhavaty evam aśucau (kvacid vi)śuciviparyāsa^ iti||

[134,16] etenāpuṇye puṇyapratyayaḥ yathā saṃsāramocakānāṃ tathā^ evānarthe cārthapratyayo vyākhyātaḥ yathā taskarāṇām||

[134,18] tathā duḥkhe sukhyakhyātiṃ vakṣyai pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair guṇavṛttivirodhāc ca duḥkham eva sarvaṃ vivekinaḥ iti| tatra sarvasmin saṃsāre duḥkhe sukhakhyātir avidyā||

[134,21] tathā^ anātmany ātmakhyātir bāhyopakaraṇeu cetanācetateṣu gavādughaṭādiṣu bhogādhiṣṭhāne vā śarīre puruṣopakaraṇe vā manasy anātmany ātmakhyātir iti||

[134,23] atrocyate kasya punar manasy anātmanu^ ātmakhyātir iti nanu manasa^ eva khyātiḥ kiṃ codyate yāvatā^ avidyā viparyayaś cittavṛttir iti hy uktam| cittam eva prāptacaitanyopagrahaṇarūpatvād ātmānaṃ cetanam abhimanyate^ ahaṃ draṣṭṛ śrotṛ vā^ aham idaṃ mameti cāviśeṣeṇa||

[135,9] tathaitad atra^ etasminn arthe^ uktaṃ vyaktam avyaktaṃ vā 
pradhānādikaṃ śarīrendriyādi viṣayaviśeṣaparyantaṃ kiṃcit vyaktaṃ ānubhāvikaṃ kiṃcid ānumānikam āgamikaṃ avyaktaṃ vā sattvam ātmatvenābhipratītaḥ pratipannaḥ tasya sampadam anunandati tasya vyaktāvyaktātmakasyātmatvenāvagatasya sampadaṃ samṛddhiṃ vibhūtim anu nandati^ anu hṛṣyati^ ātmasampadaṃ manvānaḥ sampanno 'ham anayā sampadā mamaiṣā sampad iti manyamānaḥ| tasya vyāpadaṃ tasya kṣayaṃ anu śocati mamaiṣā vyāpadāham eva^ iti| sa sarva^ evābhimānā^ apratibuddhiṃ ajña iti||

[135,16] eṣā catuṣpadī bhavaty avidyā| nanu cāpuṇye puṇyakhyātiḥ puṇye apuṇyakhyātiḥ ity ābhyāṃ saha ṣaṭpadītvam na catuṣv eva pādeṣu yathāsaṃbhavam antar-bhāvāt| avidyā mūlaṃ prabhavabījam asya kleśasantānasya abhiniveśāntakleśānām anavaratapravartanasya| kiṃ ca karmāśayasya ca trividhasya jāñtyāyur-bhogalakṣaṇavipākatrayasahitasya sakalasya saṃsārasya^ ity arthaḥ||

[135,21] tasyāś cāvidyāyāḥ amitrāgoṣpadavad vastu-satatvaṃ vastvantaratvaṃ vijñeyam| kim artham idam ucyate| yāvatā viparītakhyātir iti vastu-satatvam uktam eva||

[135,23] ayam abhiprāyaḥ avidyā iti nañ-samāso 'yaṃ tatpuruṣaḥ sa ca^ uttarapadārthapradhāno vā syāt yathā rājapuruṣa^ iti| yad vā pūrvapadārthapradhānaḥ ardhapiṣpalīti||

[135,26] tatra yadi pūrvapadārthapradhānas tadānīṃ vidyāyāḥ pramāṇasyābhāva (vādi)iti yatraiva vidyāprasaṅgo vṛttisāmānyāt tatraiva (smṛtya)vṛttyantarādau pratītir ity anirdhārita-(136,5)viśeṣatvo 'rthaḥ prajñayeta| yā tu viparītakhyātiḥ pūrvoktāvidyeti sā pāribhāṣikī syāt||

[136,7] tataḥ ko doṣaḥ syāt avidyāśabdaśravaṇe sandehaḥ syāt| kiṃ prasiddha eva śabdārthaḥ paribhāṣitaḥ śāstrasaṃvyavahārārthaṃ yathā loke yeṣv artheṣu prasiddhani padāni tāni sati sambhave tadarthāny eva sūtreṣv ity avagantavyaṃ (ŚBh 1.1.1) iti| athāśabdārtha eva yathā iko guṇavṛddhī (AA 1.1.3) iti| tataś cāniścite padārthe nāvidyāśabdena vyavahāraḥ kriyeta||

[136,12] athottarapadārthaprādhānyaṃ samānādhikaraṇatvān nīlotpalavat tatrāpi nīlotpalaśabdārthāyor avirodhād viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvena saṃbandho yuktaḥ||

[16,14] iha nañā nivṛttir ucyate| vidyāśabdenāpi vidyā| tatra yady uttarapadasyārthaṃ pradhānaṃ prati guṇabhūto nañ tasya^ evottarapadārthasyābhāvaṃ kuryāt tadā tad-guṇatvam eva hīyate| nāpītarasya prādhānyam||

[136,17] yo hi kiñcin nirvartayituṃ anuprasthito nirvartyam eva vyāpādayet na tasya nirvartyaṃ prati guṇatvam| nāpi nirvartyasya pradhānatā tadvat| tasmāt pradhānavaśavartinā guṇena bhavitavyam iti vidyāyām ansyasyābhāva^ eva^ iti kevalo nañ nityānuvādīti vidyāmātraṃ 
pratīyeta||

[136,21] tad etad evaṃ kṛtvā^ ucyate| tasyāś cāmitrāgoṣpadavad vastusatatvaṃ vijñyetam iti| yathā^ amitro na mitrābhāvaḥ yathā 
pūrvapadārthaprādhānye^ ardhapippalīti| na mitrāmātram| yathā^ uktaṃ uttarapadārthaprādhānye pradhānavaśavartinā guṇena bhavitavyaṃ na 
pradhānavināśeneti| tathā ca^ uktam abrāhmaṇamānayety ukte brāhmaṇamātrasyānayanaṃ prāpnoti^iti nañ-vārtike| uttarapadārthaprādhānyapakṣe naño nityānuvādatvāt| evam amitra^ iti| evam avidyādi||

[136,27] yathā vā^ agoṣpadaṃ na goṣpadābhāvo na goṣpadamātraṃ kin tu deśa eva tābhyāṃ goṣpadābhāvagoṣpadamātrābhyām anyad vastv antaram| [katham evam?] (137,9) yāvatā goṣpadamātraṃ tadabhāvaś ca śrūyate^ ucyate^ ata^ eva 
prayogasāmarthyād atyantāgoṣpadārthapratītiḥ||

[137,11] katham nañs tāvat bhāvaviruddho 'rthaḥ yena ca saha prayujyate tad abhāvam āha||

[137,12] tataś cāgoṣpadam iti goṣpadaprasakto 'rthaḥ goṣpadamātraṃ goṣpadaviruddhaś ceti trayo 'rthāḥ pratibhānti| tatra yadi goṣpadamātraṃ bhavet tadā nañaḥ prayogo 'narthaka^ eva| anyābhāvānuvādī ced goṣpade nañ syād evam apy anarthaka^ eva||

[137,15] atha goṣpadaprasaṅgayukto 'rthe tathāpi nañmukhye 'rthe pravṛttas syāt gobhir atyantāsevitatvasyānuktatvāt| yataḥ sevitatve bhaviṣyaty api triṣv api kāleṣu śabdaprayogo yuktaḥ syāt| mukhyaś cārtho nāma sa bhavati yatra gobhiḥ sevitaṃ na bhūtaṃ na vartate nāpi bhaviṣyati| tasmāt sati sambhave nañarthaḥ sa^ eva yukta^ iti na sevitaprasaṅgo nāpi sevitamātra^ iṣyate| gosevitaviruddha^ eva deśo'goṣpadaśabdenābhidhīyate||

[137,21] kathaṃ punar nīlotpalavadviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāva^ ekasya bhāvābhāvayor virodhāt| tad abhāvapratipādanamātreṇa saṃvyavahārānupapatteḥ yasmin vastuni tadabhāva^ ucyate tad eva vastvantaraṃ pratīyate| tad uktam goṣpadaṃ sevitāsevitapramāṇeṣu^ iti||

[137,24] atrāsevitagrahaṇe punaḥ kriyāmāṇe ca bahuvrīhir ayaṃ vijñāyate avidyāmānasevito'sevita^ iti| (tasmāt) evam avidyā na pramāṇaṃ na ca 
pramāṇābhāvaḥ| kin tu viparītaṃ jñānāntaram iti||2.5||

[137,27] dṛgdarśanaśaktyor ekātmtevāsmitā| adhunā^ asmitā-ākhyaṃ viparyayam ācaṣṭe dṛk darśanaṃ upalabdhiḥ puruṣaḥ sa^ eva śaktiḥ| dṛśyate 'neneti darśanaṃ sā^ eva śaktiḥ antaḥkaraṇādhyavasāyasvarūpā| tayoḥ dṛgdarśanaśaktyoḥ upalabdhy(138,8)adyavasāyasvarūpayoḥ ekātmatā^ iva ekaś cāsāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ ātmāca tasya bhāva^ ekātmatā| ivaśabdenātyantavivaktatām ācaṣṭe| ekasvarūpāpattir ivāsmitā kleśa^ ucyate||

[138,10] yasminn ahaṃ-pratyaye cittapuruṣayor vivekā na^ upalabhyate so 'yam ahaṃ pratyayo'asmitākhyaḥ kleśa^ ucyate||

[138,12] pūrvatra tu puruṣopakaraṇe 'antaḥkaraṇe 'nātmanyātmakhyātir iti viparītadarśanam evāvidyā^ ity uktam| iha tu punar buddhipuruṣayor aviśeṣadarśanam ekasvarūpāpattir iva darśanam asmiteti viśeṣaḥ||

[138,15] tad etad uktaṃ bhavati bhoktṛbhogyaśaktyor atyantaviviktayoḥ atyantāsaṅkīrṇayoḥ avibhāgaprāptāv iva satyāṃ bhogaḥ kalpate| bhogahetur asmitābhidhānakleśa^ ity arthaḥ| sati hy ahaṃ paratyaye sukhito 'haṃ duḥkhio 'ham iti sukhaduḥkhādisambandho bhavati| tasmād etad evam iti||

[138,19] svarūpapratilambhe tu tayoḥ viveke nāhaṃ mameti svarūpopalabdhau satyāṃ buddhipuruṣayoḥ kaivalyam evāvimiśratā^ eva bhavati| kuto bhoga iti kāraṇe hy aviśeṣāhaṃ-pratyayātmani sati kāryaṃ bhogaḥ tasminn asati kāraṇe na bhoga^ iti || 138,22       na hi manasi cetanāvattvam anadhyāropya sukhitvaṃ duḥkhitvaṃ vā śakyaṃ pratyetum| ahaṃ pratyayaviṣayo hi sukhaduḥkhapratyayādhikaraṇam| ahaṃ sukhī duḥkhī cāham iti sāmānādhikaraṇyāt| tasmād asmitā-ākhyo 'haṅkāro bhogahetuḥ||

[138,25] tathā ca^ uktaṃ buddhitaḥ paraṃ puruṣaṃ buddhitaḥ sakāśāt vibhat\ktaṃ vilakṣaṇam anyam| kair guṇair vibhāga^ ity āha ākāraśīlavidyābhiḥ ākāreṇa vṛttiārūpyeṇa śīlena buddhipratisaṃveditathā| vidyayā caitanyasvarūpatayā| athavā^ ākāreṇa śuddhānantasvarūpatvena śīlenāpariṇāmitvād apratisaṃkramatvena vidyayā citiśaktisvarūpatayā| tad etair dharmair buddhito vibhāgaḥ||

[139,7] tathā buddher api^ ākāreṇā sukhaduḥkhamohātmakatvena śīlena 
prakhyāpravṛttisthityātmanā vidyāyā sarvārthādhyavasāyātmakatvena puruṣāṭ vibhāgaḥ| ittham anyonyam ākāra_Vīlavidyābhir vibhaktaṃ puruṣaṃ apaśyan kuryāt tatra tasyāṃ buddhāv ātmabuddhiṃ ātmapratyayaṃ mohena loka^ iti| vidyāśīlākārair atyantavibhaktayor buddhipuruṣayor avibhāga^ iva tad-darśanam ity anenāṃśenodāharaṇam||

[139,12] yat tu vihaktam apaśyan kuryāt atrātmabuddhim iti| sā 
pūrvoktaivāvidyā| yad vā^ amitāyā^ avidyābhedatvāt sarvam udāharaṇam iti| apaśyam kuryād iti puruṣasvarūpādarśanam avidyāyāḥ asmitāyāś ca hetuḥ kāraṇaṃ cittasya daṣṭṛdṛśyoparaktatā| puruṣasya tv adarśanaṃ draṣṭṛtvād eva||2.6||

[139,16] sukhānujanmā sukhānuśayī rāgaḥ| anu janmānu utpattir yasya saḥ| sukhānuśayī tathā duḥkhānuśayīti^ anyeṣāṃ pāṭhaḥ| sukhānuśayī rāgaḥ duḥkhānuśayī dveṣa^ iti te vyācakṣate sukham anubhavituṃ śīlam asya sa sukhānuśayī tathā duḥkhānuśayīti| ubhayadhāapy anujanmatā^ eva| tad eva tācchīlyam| tathā ca vakṣyati dharmāt sukhaṃ sukhād rāgaḥ iti||

[139,21] kathaṃ sukhānuśayivam iti tad āha sukhābhijñasya sukhe^ ābhijñānam abhijñā yasya sa sukhābhijñaḥ sukhaṃ ya^ upalabdhavāṃs tasya sukhyābhijñasya sukhānusmṛtir pūrvaḥ sukhaṃ pūrvānubhūtam anusmaran sukhe hlāde tatsādhane vā^ agarucandanādāv anurajyate| tatra yo 'sau gardhaḥ sa ucyate tṛṣṇā lobho rāga^ iti||2.7||

[140,12] duḥkhānujanmā duḥkhānuśayī dveṣaḥ| duḥkham uktam yenābhihatāḥ 
prāṇinas tad upaghātāya prayatante^ iti| duḥkhābhijñasya^ iti pūrvavad hy ākhyānam| duḥkhānusmṛtir pūrvo duḥkhe tatsādhane vā sataskara\ādau yo manyurujjihāsā uddhātum icchā krodhaḥ sa dveṣaḥ| rāgeṇa tulyaṃ vyākhyānaṃ sarvam||2.8||

[140,16] svarasavāhī viduṣo api tathā rūḍho 'abhiniveśaḥ svarasena svabhāvena cittasyāvidyāmātreṇa boddhuṃ pravartituṃ śīlam asya| bahater akarmakasyāpi darśanāt yathā nadī vahatīti| atha vā svarasaṃ svabhāvaṃ boddhuṃ śīlam asya svarasavāhī visuṣoapi samyagdarśanasyāpi| apicabdād aviduṣa^ eva yukto maraṇātrāsa^ ātmocchedamānina^ ity abhiprāyaḥ| samyagdarśanasyātmānucchedamānino 'yukta^ iti| tathā vimṛḍhyatarabuddhiṣv ātmocchedadarśiṣv ayam abhiprāyo maraṇatrāso rūḍho 'bhiniviṣṭaḥ sthiro yathā tathāiva viduṣo apy ātmānucchedadarśitvād ayukto api rūḍhaḥ||

[140,23] kasmāt punar ayaṃ maraṇatrāsaḥ svarasena^ eva rūḍha^ iti yataḥ sarvasya prāṇinaḥ iyam ātmāśīr nityā bhavati yad^ asau maraṇaṃ mānvabhūvam ucchedaṃ mā prāpam| iyam āśir vā bhūyāsaṃ guṇaiḥ śubhātmabhiḥ sampatsīyeti na tair viyogo bhūyād iti| mūḍheṣu bhogasampadām ātmanāś cānucchedaḥ 
prārthanīyaḥ||

[141,10] kim etayā ''śiṣā^ avagamyata^ ity āha na cānanubhūtamaraṇadharmakasya bhavty eṣā^ ātmāśīr iti| etayā ca^ ātmāśiṣā 
pūrvajanmānubhavaḥ pratīyate| tad etad uktaṃ bhavati na hy ananubhūte sukhe bhavati prārthanā nāpy ananubhūte duḥkhe tat parihārvāñchā bhavatīti| yenāpi pratyakṣānumānābhyām ananubhūtaṃ maraṇaduḥkhaṃ tasyāpy anayātmāśiṣā pūrvo maraṇānubhavaṅḥ pratīyate so api nāntareṇa janmeti janmānubhavaḥ| tayā pūrvaḥ pūrvo janmamaraṇānubhavo 'nādir iti mataḥ| sa cāyam abhiniveśaḥ kleśaḥ kṛmer api jātamātrasyeti||

[141,17] maraṇānubhavakāraṇam ācaṣṭe pratyajṣānumānāgamair asambhavito maraṇatrāsa^ ucchedadṛṣṭyātmaka^ ātmabhogocchedapratipattihetuḥ 
pūrvajanmānubhūtaṃ maraṇaduḥkham anumāpayati| yathā cāyam atyantamūḍheṣu dṛśyate kṛmiprabhṛtiṣu kleśas tathā viduṣo api vijñātapūrvāparāntikasyāpi pūrvāntikaṃ bandhaḥ aparāntikaṃ muktiḥ| jñātajanmamaraṇāntikasya vā rūḍhaḥ| kasmāt punar mūḍhasya^ iva viduṣo rūḍha^ iti tata^ āha samānā hi kuśalāluśalayor vidviduṣor maraṇānubhavād iyaṃ vāsanā maraṇabhayavāsanā||2.9||

[141,24] kleśamūlaḥ karamāśayaḥ sati mūle tadvipākaḥ iti vakṣyate| tasmāt te kleśā hātavyāḥ| hānārthañ ca te pradarśitāḥ| tadavahānau copāyaṃ vakṣyati dhyāna-(142,13)heyās tadvṛttayaḥ iti| tatra na jñāyate kiṃviṣayo dhyānaprayogaḥ kiṃ dagdhabījakarmakleśaviṣayan kiṃ sarvaviṣayaḥ iti| tādviṣayavibhajanārtham idaṃ sūtrem ārabhyate te pratiprasavaheyāḥ sūkṣmā iti||

[142,16] te pañca kleśāḥ dagdhabījakalpāḥ yoginaś caritādhikāre cetasi 
pralīne saha tenāṣtaṃ gacchanti| etad uktaṃ bhavati samyagdarcanābhyāsānaladagdhabījasārarthyānāṃ kleśānāṃ kṛtāśeṣapurusaprayojanasya cetasaḥ pratiprasavena^ eva pralayena^ eva 
pralayotpatter na dhyānasādhanāpekṣā| na hi dagdhaṃ dāham apekṣate  piṣṭaṃ vā peṣaṇam iti| cetas tu sādhitapruruṣārthatvāt| sthitiprayonanābhāvāc ca svayam eva nirvartate||2.10||

[142,21] kīdṛśānāṃ punar dhyānādisādhanāprekety ata^ ucyate sthitānāṃ tu bījabhāvopagatānām iti||

[142,23] dhyānaheyās tadvṛttayaḥ| kleśānāṃ yā vṛttayaḥ sthūlās tāḥ kriyā-yogena tapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānākhyena sthūlapratipakṣeṇa tanūkṛtāḥ pūrvam| tata^ idānīṃ tanūkaraṇād bījaśaktyavasthāḥ prasaṃkhyānena samyagdarśanaśīlanato naipuṇaviśeṣeṇa dhyānena mahatā balīyasā 
pratipakṣeṇa hātavyāḥ tato api yāvat sūkṣmīkṛtāḥ| kim uktam bhavati yāvad dagdhabījakalpāḥ yāvan nirbījīkṛtā iti||

[143,10] tat kim iva^ iti yathā vastrāṇāṃ sthūlo malaḥ pūrvaṃ nirchūyate 
pañcāt sūkṣmo malaḥ snehanādinimitto yatnena copāyena kṣārādiviśiṣṭopāyenāpanīyate tathā svalpapratipakṣāḥ tapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhāna-yamaniyamādiyogāṅgānuṣṭhānādipratipakṣāḥ sthūlā vṛttayaḥ kleśānām| sūkṣmās tu mahāpratipakṣā iti| tapassvādhyāyāduanuṣṭhānapratilabdhadīptiprakarṣāviplavasamyadgarśānamahāpratipakṣāḥ sūkṣmā bījaśaktyapekṣā iti||2.11||

[143,16] kim arthaṃ punaḥ kleśahānāya prayatata^ ity āha yataḥ kleśamūlaḥ karmāśayo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ| kleśā^ avidyā-ādayo mūlaṃ janmakāraṇaṃ yasya karmāśayasya sa kleśamūlaḥ karmāśaya^ iti| ākarmaphalapradānād antaḥkaraṇe śeta iti karamāśayaḥ śuklakṛṣṇātmakaḥ| dṛṣṭaṃ cādṛṣṭaṃ ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭe janmanī tayor vedanīyam anubhavanīyaṃ 
phalaṃ yasya sa dṛṣṭādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ karamāśayaḥ| yo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭakarmānubhavitavyaphalaḥ karamāśayaḥ sa kleśamūla^ iti sūtrārthaḥ||

[143,22] yasya puṇyāuṇyarūpasya karmāśayasya yasmin janmani niṣpattis tasya tasmnn eva janmani vedanīyaṃ phalaṃ sa dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ yasya tu janmāntare vedanīyaṃ phalaṃ so'dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ karmāśaya^ iti||

[143,25] bhāṣyam idānīṃ vyākhyāyate tatra puṇyāpuṇyakarmāśayo lobhakrodhamohaprabhava^ iti kleśamūlatvaṃ tasya^ ācaṣṭe| puṇyakarmāśayo lobhaprabhavo yathā svarge vā^ atra vā śubhaṃ phalam anubhaiṣyāmīti svargakāpaputrakāmayādādiḥ krodhaprabhavo yathā viśvāmitrasyāmbāyāś ca| mohaprabhavo yathā draupadyāḥ kumbhakarṇasya ca||

[144,6] apuṇyakarmāśayo api lobhavrabhavo yathā carmaṇī dvīpaniṃ hanti kuñjaraṃ hanti dantayoḥ| karṇasya śāstrajñānalobhād aniṛtavacane| krodhaprabhavo yathā brahmahatyā| bhārate(bhāve) apy aśvatthāmnaś ca| mohaprabhavo yathā saṃsāramocakādīnāṃ nahuṣasya ca||

[144,9] asmitāvidyāyor lobhakrodhamohaiḥ pratyekaṃ sdambandhaḥ| katham aham adhikṣipto'smīti krudhyati| ahaṃ sukham anubhaviṣyāmīti libhyati| ahaṃ muhyāmīti muhyati||

[144,12] avidyā api sarvatraiva viparītadarśanalakṣaṇā sambadhyate| na hy asatyām avidyāyām asmitā-ādīnāṃ sambhavo 'sti| rāgas tu lobha^ eva| dveṣas tu krodhaḥ| abhiniveśo vimoha^ evāntarbhavati||

[144,15] yat tu mohaḥ punar avidyeti tad avidyekasāmānyād uktam| lobhakrodhamohās tu parasparanirapekṣā dharmādharmotpttidvāram| tathā ca vakṣyati lobhakrodhamohapūrvikā iti||

[144,18] sa dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś ca sa lobhakrodhamohaprabhavaḥ karmāśayo d_ṛṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś cādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś ca||

[144,20] kathaṃ punaḥ karmaṇāṃ svarūpanimittatulyatve kasyaciddṛṣṭajanmvedanayitvaṃ kasyacid adṛṣṭajanavedanīyatvañ cety āha yathā tīvrasaṃyogena tīvrodyamena mantratapassamādhibhir yathāprasiddharūpais tīvrasaṃvegānuṣthitair nirvartitaḥ īśvaradevatāmaharṣimahānubhāvānām ārādhanād vā| īśvara^ uktaḥ devatā ādityādayaḥ maharṣayo bhṛgvādayaḥ mahā-anubhāvā dharmajñānaniratamatayaḥ eta eva vā (tad eṣāṃ vā) tīvratarārādhanapariniṣpanaḥ sadyaḥ 
paripacyate sa tasminn eva janmani| yathā nandīśvarakumārasya||

[145,10] tathā tīvrakleśena lobhādiprakarṣeṇa bhītavyādhitakṛpaṇeṣu visrambhopagateṣu mahānubhāveṣu vā tapasmiṣu jñānadharmaniratamatiṣu tapasviṣu punaḥ punaḥ ābhīkṣṇyena kṛto 'pakāraḥ sa cāpi pāpakarmāśayaḥ sadya paripacyate phalati| yathā nahuṣasya||

[145,14] loke api kṣetrabījasaṃskāraviśeṣeṇa vṛkṣādiphalasannikarṣaḥ prasiddhaḥ vṛkṣāyurvedaprasiddhyā| tathā kriyāsu phalasannikarṣaviprakarṣadarśanaṃ gamipacyādiṣu tīvramandānuṣṭhānaviśeṣataḥ||

[145,17] tatra puṇyakarmāśayasyodāharaṇam āha yathā nandīśvaraḥ kumāro manuṣyapariṇāmaṃ svakaṃ hitvā maheśvarārādhanapariniṣpannena tīvratarakleśānuṣṭhānena janmanā devatātvena pariṇataḥ||

[145,20] tathā nahuṣo 'puṇyakarmāśayasyodāharaṇam| sa devānām indraḥ| sasvakaṃ pariṇāmaṃ hitvā tīvrataramohānuṣṭhānajane karmāśayena tiryaktvena pariṇata^ iti ||

[145,22] krodhādīnāṃ tu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyatvenodāharaṇāni vyākhyātāni pūrvam eva| etena ca mandamandataralobhādikleśānuṣṭhānajanmānau 
puṇyapāpakarmāśayāvadrṣṭajanmavedanīyau vyākhyātau lokavad eva| tatrapi tāvad ucyate||

[145,25] tatra nārakāṇāṃ nāsti yair narakaprāptyartham eva karmānuṣṭhitaṃ brahmahatyāguru-dāragamanādhilakṣaṇaṃ teṣāṃ nārakāṇāṃ nārakatvasyāśubhāvitvam āśritya teṣāṃ nārakāṇāṃ nāsti dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaphalaḥ karamāśayaḥ [yena phalaṃ] ārabhyate||

[146,9] kathaṃ punar nārakāṇāṃ karmārambhas tiryag-ādīāṃ devatānāṃ ca manuṣyāṇāṃ hi karmādhikāraḥ eṣā ca karmabhūmir na narakādir ity ucyate na nahuṣādīnāṃ karmadarśanāt| śrūyate ca śakarato api vṛtrādivadhena brahmahatyāprāptir yogādyanuṣṭhānaśravaṇaṃ ca||

[146,13] yan manuṣyāṇāṃ karmādhikāraḥ iyaṃ karmabhūmir iti tatkṣipraphalasiddhyapekṣam| tad uktam kṣipraṃ hi mānuṣe loke siddhir bhavati karmajā (BhG 4.12) iti| vyāsena kīṭopākhyāna(vara)smaraṇāc ca tiraścām api karmopapattiḥ||

[146,16] kiñ ca dehendriyādimataḥ kriyopapatteḥ| kriyāṇāṃ ca kāyavāṅmanaḥprabhavānāṃ phalāra,bhakatvaṃ kleśapūrvakatvāt||

[146,18] kṣīṇakleśānām api samyagdarśinām api nāsty adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ karamāśayaḥ kleśābhāvād eva| na hi vītarāgo jāto dṛśyate| kleśānāṃ karmārambha^ iva karmaphalārambheapi hetutvāt| kṣīṇakleśasyāpi jñānaprāpteḥ prāg ārabdho dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ sambhavati karamāśayaḥ 
pravṛttiphalatvān muktaśaravad iti ||2.12||

[146,22] kiñ ca sati mūle tadvipāko jātyāyurbhogāḥ| mūlaṃ hi karmaṇāṃ kleśaḥ| asmin sati mūle kleśesu satsv ity arthaḥ| tasya lobhakrodhaprabhavasya puṇyāpuṇyakarmāśayasya vipākaḥ phalaniṣpattiḥ atadvipākaḥ| kiṃ punas tat-phalam ity āha jātyāyurbhogāḥ||

[146,26] bhāṣyaṃ satsu kleśeṣu karmāśayo vipākārambhī bhavati nocchinnakleśamūla^ iti mūlasyavyākhyānam| tatra satsu kleśeṣu karmāśayo vipākārambhīty asya dṛṣṭāntam āha yathā tuṣāvanaddhāḥ śālitaṇḍulā adagdhabījabhā_Vāḥ||

[147,7] tuṣāvanaddhā^ api kadācid dagdhabījabhāvā na prarohasamarthā bhavanti dagdhabījabhāvā^ api vyapanītatuṣā na prarohāyālaṃ bhhavanti| ubhayathā ca dārṣṭāntikapratipādanāṛthaṃ dṛṣṭāntavikalpaḥ||

[147,10] katham tathā kleśāvvanaddhaḥ karṃāśayo vipākaprarohī bhavati kleśopabandhaḥ tuṣāvanaddhaśālitaṇḍulavat| nāpanītakleśo [na prasaṃkhyāna] dagdhakleśabījabhāvo vā^ iti| apanītatuṣālitaṇḍulavad apnītakleśo na vipākaprarohī||

[147,13] yogāṅgānnuṣṭhānakleśakṣayapūrvasamyagdarśanānāṃ yoginām[na]apnītakleśo apy ā[na]panītatuṣadagdhabījaśaktiśālivad yogāṅgānuṣṭhānapraṇālikevalasamyagdarśanānaladagdhabījasāmarthyo na vipākaprarohī bhavati||

[147,16] tathā ca vyāsaḥ sāṃkhyānāṃ yogināṃ ca prthaṅmokṣaṃ darśayati||

[147,17] "bījānyagnyupadagdhāni na rohanti yathā punaḥ| 147,16   jñānadagdhais tathā kleśair nātmā sampadyate punaḥ||" iti|| (Mbh 12,204.016)

[147,19] tathā yogaiścaryam atikrānto yogī mucyeta " ity ādi| vakṣyati cātrāpi prāptāprāptaiśvaryayogayoḥ kaivalyam||

[147,21] katiprakāro 'sau vipāka^ ity āha sa ca vipakas trividhaḥjātir āyur bhoga^ iti||

[147,21] tatredaṃ vicāryate| kiṃ punar vicāraphalam ucyate^ ekaekena 
prāṇinā viruddhaphalāni bhūyiṣṭhāny ekasminn eva janmani karmāṇi kriyante| tad yathā 147,23  śarīrajaiḥ karmadoṣair yāti sthāvaratāṃ naraḥ| 147,24  vācakaḥ pakṣimṛgatāṃ mānasair antyajātitām||

[148,7] svargārthāni ca tāvad anutiṣṭhanti| narakaprāpīṇi ca karmāṇi| tatra^ ekasya brahmahtyādikarmaṇo 'nekajanmakārakatvaṃ śrūyate śvasūkarakhroddrāṇām ityādismṛteḥ||

[148,9] atha viruddhadeśakālanmimittavipākānāṃ karmaṇām ekajanmārambhe viruddhavipākatādarthyaṃ hīyeta| tataś ca tādarthyacodanāḥ karmaṇāṃ bādhyeran ||

[148,11] atha prayāyeṇa janmārambhitvaṃ karmaṇām asaṃkhyeyatvāt kenacid eva 
phalam ārabdhaṃ nānyair iti tatra niyamānupapattiḥ kenārabdhaḥ kena vā neti| tathā ca kartṛṇāṃ karmaphalaṃ pratya(pya)bismnambhād utsāhavibhaṅgaḥ syāt abhyudayaniśreyasapravṛttiṣu||

[148,15] athānyatamenārmbhe vināśa^ evānyeṣām iti tathā api sa^ eva doṣaḥ kiṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kim ārambhakam ity aniyamāt||

[148,17] athāvināśa^ eva tathāpy avaśiṣṭasyārambhakālābhāvo mokṣābhāvaś ceti| tasmāt pravṛtti nivṛttiśāstrārthavatvapratipādanārtham idaṃ vicāryate||

[148,19] kim ekaṃ karma^ ekasya janmanaḥ kāraṇaṃ svargādiprāptinimitteṣu bahuṣu karmasu satsu| atjaikaṃ karmānekaṃ janmākṣiptīti prathamā vicāraṇā| viruddhapakṣadvayālambinī hi vicārāṇā ekā^ eva syāt||

[148,22] dvitīyā vicāraṇā kim anekaṃ karmānekaṃ janma nirvartayati viruddhaphalam aviruddhaphalaṃ vā^ athānekaṃ karmaikaṃ janma nirvartayati viruddhaphalam aviruddhaphalaṃ vā||

[149,8] kiñ cātaḥ na tāvad ekaṃ karmaikasya janmanḥ kāraṇam | kasmāt bahuṣu karmasu satsv ekam eva karma janmaikaṃ nārabhata^ iti||

[149,10] yady ekasya^ eva janmārabhitvaṃ tadā^ anādikālapracitasya saṃsārānāditvād ekaikena puruṣeṇāsaṃkhyeyam ekaikasmin janmani karma 
pracīyate | viruddhaphalam aviruddhaphalaṃ ca||

[149,13] tatrāvaśiṣṭasya janmārambhikarmavyatiriktasyāsaṅkhyeyasya anekajanmāntarapracitasya karmaṇaḥ karamkalāpasya sāmpratasya ca vartamānajanmapracitasyānārabdhakāryasya phalakramāniyamāt kiṃ 
pūrvajanmopacitakarmarācigatasya kasyacid eva bhaviṣyajjanmārmbhitvaṃ āhosvid idānīntanajanmakarmarāśigatasya kasyacid iti phalakramo na niyamyetteti^ anāśvāso lokasya||

[149,18] kiṃ pūrvajanmakarmaṇā duṣṭenāduṣṭena vā phalam ārabdhavyam athedānīntanakarmaṇā duṣṭenāduṣṭena vā ity avisrambhāt na kaścid agnihotrādi kurvīta| nāpi mokṣāya samīheta^ ity anāśvāsaḥ prasaktaḥ sa cāniṣṭaḥ||

[149,21] idānīṃ prathamavicāraṇāyāṃ dvitīyaṃ pakṣaṃ nirākaroti na ca^ ekaṃ karmanekasya janmanaḥ kāraṇam| kasmāt ekaikasmiṃs tāvaj janmani^ anekaṃ karmānuṣṭhīyate tatra^ ekam eva cet karmānekasya janmavipākasya janmapariṇāmasya nimittaṃ bhavet tato^ avaśiṣṭasya ārabdhakāryād anyasya vipākakālābhāvaḥ prasaktaḥ sa cāpy aniṣṭaḥ| phalakramāniyamś ceti 
pūrvadoṣo api| na^ evaṃbhūtam aniṣṭam abhyupagantavyam| yat tu brahmahatyādikarmaṇām ekaikasyānekajanmārambhitvaṃ tasya śāstragocaratvāt brahmahatyādi viṣayatvam evābhyupagamyate||

[150,9] dvitīyavicāraṇāyām idānīṃ prathamaḥ pakṣo nirākriyate na cānekaṃ karmānekasya janmanaḥ kāraṇam| kasmād evaṃ nābhyupagamyate tad ucyate tad evānekaṃ janma yugamn na sambhavati^ ekasyānīśvarasya| tataś ca krameṇa cācyamāne kim ekam anekaṃ janmārabhata^ iti| tathā ca 
pūrvadoṣānuṣaṅgaḥ sarvatra prathamadoṣānuṣaṅgaḥ phalakramāniyamād anāśvāsa iti||

[150,14] tasmāt evaṃ pakṣatrayānupapattāv idānīṃ siddhāntam upasaṃharati janma prāyaṇānrtare janmamaraṇayor antare madhye kṛtaḥ 
puṇyapāpakarmāśayaḥ aviruddhajanmavedanīyaḥ arpratiniyatavipākaḥ| tasya tridhā viniyogaṃ vakṣyati tasmād aviruddhaphalasya^ eva 
puṇyāpuṇyakarmāśayasyāyaṃ niyam ucyate||

[150,19] pracayavicitraḥ tulyātulyānekakarmapracitatvāt pravcayena vicitraḥ| pradhānopasarjanabhāvenāvasthiyaḥ kiñcit karma kasyacit karmaṇaḥ pradhānam api kasya cid anyasya^ upasarhanam upasarhanam api 
pradhānam ity evam aneksakarmaśabalimnā 'vasthitaḥ||

[150,22] prāyaṇābhivyaktaḥ yathā bījakṣetrasaṃskārasaṃyogo 'ṅkurabhāvābhivyaktikāraṇaṃ tathā prāyaṇam apy aviruddhānāṃ karmaṇām abhivyaktinimittam| ekapradhadṛkena^ ekalolībhāvena samuccitaḥ ekībhūtaḥ ekam eva janma karoti||

[150,25] tac ca janma tena^ eva prāyāṇbhivyaktātmanā karmaṇā labdhāyuṣkaṃ bhavati| tasminn āyuṣi tyena^ eva karmaṇā bhogaḥ sampadyate yena janmāyuṣī kṛte| yathā yena bījenāṅkuro nirvṛttas tena^ eva phalapuṣpādayo api||

[151,7] tena ca karmaṇā bhogāyurnirvṛttir bhavaty eva^ ity avadhāryate| na 
punar anyasyāyurbhogādinimittasya putrapaśukāmādiyāgādeḥ 
putrāyurādiprāpter hetutvaṃ nirākriyate| itarathā hi sarvaśrutismiṛtiviprakopaḥ syāt| tasmāt tena^ eva karmaṇeti janmamātram ārahya kṛtakāryatvād vinivṛttyāśaṅkāniṣedhamātraṃ kriyate ||

[151,11] asau karmāśayo janmāyurbhogahetutvāt trayo vipākā janmādayo yasya sa trivipāko'abhidhīyate| ayam aikabhāvikaḥ karmāśaya^ uktaḥ eka(eva)janmaprayojana^ ekabhavikaḥ||

[151,14] dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyas tv ekavipākārambhī yathā 
putragrāmādikāmayāgaḥ| dvivipākārambhī vā bhogāyur hetutvāt| yathā āyurādikāmayāgaḥ| trivipākārambhī vā nandīśvaranahuṣavat||

[151,17] nanu ca nandīśvaranahuṣayor dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyatvaṃ trivipākārambhitvaṃ ca viruddham| dṛṣṭaṃ hi janma nindīśvarasya manuṣyatvam| yat punar nandīśvarasya devatvaṃ nahuṣasya cājagaratvaṃ tat dvayam apy adṛṣṭaṃ na hi manuṣyatva^ eva devatvaṃ saṃvedyaṃ nandīśvarasya nāpi devatva^ eva nahuṣasyājagaratvaṃ saṃvedyam iti kathaṃ trivipākārambhitvaṃ dṛṣṭajanmavedanītyatvaṃ ceti||

[151,22] na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| dṛśyamānajātiviśiṣṭam eva śarīraṃ tayor jātyantaratvena pariṇatam| na ca yathā 'nyeṣāṃ dṛṣṭajātiviśiṣṭaśarīropamardena jātyantaropādānaniṣekādinimittāpekṣayā jātyantaraprāptiḥ tathā nandīśvaranahuṣayoḥ| tasmāt trivipākārambhitvaṃ dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyatvaṃ cety udāhṛtam||

[151,26] tatra trividhaḥ karmarāśir dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś cādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś ca| dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyas tv aniyatavipākaḥ| kadācidbhogahetur ity ekavipākaḥ| kadācid bhokāyurhetutvāt dvivipākaḥ| kadācit jātyāturbhogahetutvāt trivipāko nandīśvaranahuṣayor iva||

[152,7] adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyas tu dvividho niyatavipākaś cāniyatavipākaś ca| niyatavipākas tu pradarśitaḥ tasmāj janmaprāyaṇāntare kṛta^ ity ādinā| yas tv aniyatavipākaḥ sa trividho vakṣyate||

[152,10] idānīṃ tu karmavipākajanito vāsanārāśir apara^ ākhyāyate kleśakarmavipakānubhavanirvartitābhiḥ kleśānubhavaś ca karmānubhavaś ca vipākānubhavaś ca kleśakarmavipakānubhavāḥ tair nirvartitābhiḥ vāsanābhiḥ saṃskāraiḥ anādikālasaṃmūrcchitam idaṃ cittaṃ citrīkṛtam iva 
paṭakuḍyādivat sarvato matsyajālaṃ granthibhir ivātataṃ vyāptam||

[152,15] athaikaikasmin janmani^ anekabhavapūrvikā vāsanāḥ karmaṇi vipāke ca tad aṅgī-bhāvena vyāpriyante| na hi vāsanāyāḥ karmavipākānaṅgabhāve sati jātamātrasya pṛṣadaṃśdārakasya vṛṣayuvānaṃ prati sphuritaparikopasyāvaskandanaṃ jāghaṭīti| na ca sakṛd anubhavavāsanāmātratayā tena śakyaṃ bhavitum||

[152,19] yas tv ayaṃ karamāśayaḥ yas tv ayaṃ janmaprāyaṇāntare kṛto'bhiruddhajanmadeanimittaḥ pradhānopsarjanībhāvena trivipākārambhī^ eṣa evaikabhavika^ uktaḥ| na dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ nāpy aniyatavipaktir adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ nāpi ca vāsanāḥ||

[152,22] tāsāṃ tāvat svarūpam ye saṃskārāḥ smṛtihetavas tā vāsanās tāś cānādikālīnāḥ saṃsārasya kleśakarmavipakānubhavarūpasyānāditvāt||

[152,24] yas tv asāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ ekabhavikaḥ karamāśayaḥ pūrvoktas tasyāpavāda ucyate^ ukedeśasya| kathaṃ sa niyatavipākaś cāniyatavipākaś ca| ko 'sau niyatavipākaḥ yaḥ prāyaṇābhivyaktaḥ kāryam ārabhate| sa^ eva ca^ ekabhavikaḥ||

[153,1] yas tv viruddhajnamadeśakālanimittaḥ so 'dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyatvasāmānye saty apy aniyatavipākaḥ| kasmāt niyatavipākena virodhāt| na cāyam ekabhavikaḥ virodhād eva| yat tv asya^ ekabhavikatvam uktaṃ yas tv asāv ekabhavikaḥ karmāśaya^ iti tad adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyatvasāmānyena||

[153,5] nanu ca yady evaṃ janmaprāyaṇāntare kṛtaḥ puṇyapāpakarmāśayaḥ 
pracayavicitraḥ pradhānopasarjanabhāvenāvasthitaḥ prāyaṇābhivyakta ekapraghattena sammūrcchita^ ekajanma karotīty evamādiniyamo virudhyeta| kutaḥ prāyaṇasya sarvapūrvakarmābhivyañjakatvāt āśayādhāropamardena cāśayāntarotpatteḥ mānuṣyakāśayādhāre pūrvaṃ daivatairyagyonāśayānāṃ samavasthānaṃ tadādhāropamardanakāle ca prāyaṇe sādhāraṇe sarvakarmābhivyaktikāraṇe daivatairyadyonāśayānāṃ kasyacid evābhivyaktir netareṣām iti nāsti hetuḥ| na hi svagocarāpannaṃ vyaṅgyam ekadeśaparityāgenābhibyanakti pradīpaḥ||

[153,13] atrocyate na^ etad yuktaṃ karmānarthakyaprasaṅgāt| bahuprakārāṇi hi karmāṇi parasparaviruddhāni kriyante| yo hi svargārthaṃ karma kṛtavān sa^ eva paśukrāmādyarthaṃ karmānuṣṭhāya punar brahmahatyāṃ vā sthāvarādiprāptyarthaṃ vā balavatkarmānutiṣṭhetṭatra narakasthāvarādiprāptasya na hi paśugrāmādilābhasaṃbhavaḥ| tataś ca tatkarmaṇām ānarthakyaṃ prasajyeta||

[153,18] atha tatrāpi grāmādilābhasukhaṃ bhavatīti cet na hi gramakāma^ ity ukte grāmasukhatvam abhipretam| na hi grāmasukhaṃ prāpto grāmaṃ prāptavān||

[153,20] kiñ ca sarvaśāstraviprakopaś ca syāt| adṛṣṭānāṃ ca svarganarakādīnāṃ sukhaduḥkhamātraprasaṅgaḥ| tataḥ śeṣeṇa^ iti śṣasadbhāvajñāpanaśrutismiṛtayo bādhyeran| tasmānnityā na sarvaṃ karma prāyaṇe 'bhivyajyate| atyantaviruddhānāṃ karmaṇām ānarthakyaprāpteḥ| karmaśāstrānarthakye ca mokṣaśāstre apy anāśvāsaprasaṅgaḥ||

[153,24] abhivyañjakānām api kasyacid abhivyañjakatvaṃ kasyacinneti dṛśyate vyabhicāraḥ| yathā cakṣuṣo rūpābhivyañjakatve api caitrolūkacakṣuṣor ahorātrayor itaretaravyabhicāraḥ| ālokasya ca samāne sarvacakṣus sahakāritve, na^ ulūkacakṣus-sahakāritvam| ekasmiṃś ca stryādipiṇḍe sukhaduḥkhamohānāṃ sarveṣām abhivyaktinimitte api kasyacid evābhivyaktiḥ||

[154,1] tatra karmādyapekṣayā virodhād adoṣa^ iti cet ihāpi virodhād anabhivyaktiḥ sarvakarmaṇām| na cāpi bījādhāre kṣetre^ upamardyamāne jalādinimittasaṃyoge saty api tad-gatānāṃ bījānāṃ biruddhajātīyānām aṅkurādikāryārambhitvam| evaṃ mānuṣyakālīnāśayādhāre apy upamardyamāne na viruddhajātīyadevatairyagyonāśayānāṃ sarveṣāṃ yugapadvṛttilābhaḥ||

[154,6] na ca prāyaṇam eva karmaṇām abhivyañjakam| dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyānāṃ bhogārthānāṃ darśanāt| yeṣāṃ prāyaṇam evābhivyañjakaṃ teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavatv abhivyaktir iti cet na virodhād daivatairyagyonamānuṣyakāśayānām||

[154,9] prāyaṇānāṃ ca bhūtabhaviṣyatkālaviśeṣair vi(ṣāvi)śeṣopapatter na sarvakarmābhivyañjakatvam| prāyaṇaviśeṣaśravaṇāc ca prāyaṇabhedaḥ yathā yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaran bhāvaṃ tyajaty ante kalevaram iti (BhG 8.6) smaraṇaviśeṣabhedāt prāyaṇabhedaḥ| tasmān nityā na sarvakarmābhivyañjakatvaṃ prāyaṇasya| anyathā viśeṣaṇānarthakyaprasaṅgāt||

[154,13] na ca smaraṇādiviśeṣaṇena prakṛtasyānyathātvaṃ nāśo vā syāt| tannāśānyathātvakaraṇe hi smaraṇasya prakṛtakrmasahakāritvaṃ na syāt| svatantraṃ hi smaraṇaṃ syāt| tathā cānuṣṭhitakarmanairarthakyaṃ bhavet||

[154,16] kiṃ ca kalalādyavasthāsu bhṛtānāṃ mokṣaprasaṅgaḥ| karmānārambhāt| tāsāṃ phalatvavatt karmatvam apīti cet na karaṇāvyūhāt| karaṇeṣu hi vyūhiteṣu karmaṇāṃ vṛttilābhaḥ phalatvaṃ ca na tadā tadvyūha upapadyate||

[154,19] svapnavad iti cen na| tatrāpi karmānabhyupagamāt| śrutiś ca sa yat tatra kiṃcit paśyaty ananvāgatas tena bhavatīti (BAUBh) karmaṇā svapnadarśanajenāsambandhaṃ darśayati| na kaścit svapnair aparādhavān asti| brahmahatyādikaraṇadarśanāt| atha skannaprāyaścittādidarśanān neti cen na retas-skandananimittatvāt prāyaścittasya||

[154,23] tasmād yady api svapnādyavasthāyām iva kalalādyavasthāsu 
phalam abhyupagamyate tathā^ api karaṇavyāpārābhāvāt svapnāvasthāvad eva 
phalamātram tat na karmakartṛtvam iti mokṣaprasaṅga eva garbhaprasṛtānām āpatita^ iti||

[155,8] tasmānnityā na sarvakarmābhivyañjakatvaṃ prāyaṇānāṃ teṣāṃ ca cviśeṣopapatter ity ata^ idam ucyate tatrādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya nityatavipākasya^ evāyaṃ niyamo yo 'sau janmaprāyaṇāntare kṛta^ ity evamādibhāṣyeṇa trivipākārambhitvaniyama^ uktaḥ na tu adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasyāniyatavipākasya| kasmāt yas tv adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaḥ aniyatavipākas tasya trayī gatiḥ||

[155,13] katham kṛtasyāvipākasya vināśaḥ nāśakālam aprāptasya^ eva nāśaḥ| yathā prāyaścittādinā pratiṣiddhasya kṛtasya nāśaḥ||

[155,15] pradhānakarmaṇy āvāpagamanaṃ pradhānakarmaṇi niyatavedanīye āvāpāpattiḥ pradhānakarmaphalātiraskāreṇa svayam api mandamandaṃ phalaṃ madhyamānupraviśya prayuddhati|  yathā sakaleṣu taṇḍuleṣu vipaśyamāneṣu asakalā^ api taṇḍulakaṇā uptā alpīyāṃsamarthyaṃ sādhayanty eva| niyatavipākapradhānakarmābhibhūtasya vā apy avasthānaṃ niyatavipākaṃ yat 
pradhānakarma prārabdhaphalaṃ tenābhibhūtasya nyak-kṛtasya viruddhadeśakālanimittavipākatvād avasthānam|  yathā vijigīṣamāṇayor anyatareṇa pravṛttarājyena abhibhūtasya^ itarasya avasthānaṃ deśakālanimittāpekṣayā||

[155,22] tatra kṛtasyāvipakkasya nāśo yathā śuklakarmodayāad iha^ eva tapas-svādhyāya-yogādilakṣaṇasya śuddhasya^ udayāt 
prāyaścittādilakṣaṇasya ca kṛṣṇasyāśuddhirūpasya hiṃṣā-ādilakṣaṇasya prāg eva vṛttilābhāt nāśaḥ| iha^ eva^ iti tv avivakṣitam| janmāntare api prāgvṛttilābhān nāśopapatteḥ| yathā kaunakhyādinimittapāpaviśeṣāṇāṃ kaunakhyādicihnair avagamya prāyaścittena nāśaḥ kriyate||

[156,9] tad etat tantrāntaraparasiddhena^ udāharaṇena vyācaṣṭe yatra^ idam uktaṃ dve dve ha vai karmaṇī śuklakṛṣṇe janmani janmani sādhakasya 
puruṣasyādhikṛtasya|  eko rāciḥ svādhyāyādipuṇyena kṛtaḥ puṇyakṛtaḥ apahantītaram apuṇyena kṛtaṃ rāśim| atha vā puṇyakṛtaḥ puruṣasya śuklakarmarāśiḥ kṛṣṇakarmarāśim apahanti| tad iddhasva tasmāt svaccchakarmāṇi svakīyāni karmaṇi śuklāni sakṛtāni kartum| kutaḥ yataḥ ihaivātraiva tava śarma^ ukhaṃ śāntiṃ kavayo vedayante karmavipākavibhāgajñāḥ jñāpayanti||

[156,16] pradhānakarmaṇyāvāpagamanam udāharaṇena pradarśyate  svalpasaṅkaraḥ alpīyān doṣāvakāraḥ| kutaḥ saparihāraḥ parihārasahitaḥ| 
parihāram apekṣate na tu parihṛtaḥ| parihāraśaktyupāyavijñānasamānāśraya ity arthaḥ| sapratyavamarśaḥ sapratyavekṣaṇaḥ| katham akāryaṃ mayā kriyate kadā nu nistariṣyābhyetat na punaḥ kariṣyāmīty āpadi 
pravṛttasyākāryaṃ kuvataḥ prāṇasthityādinimittam|| kuśalasya karmaphaladoṣaparihārādivibhāgaviśārad asya viduṣaḥ| nāyam apakarṣāya nāyaṃ svalpaḥ saṃkaraḥ puṇyaphalāt pravṛttād bahutarād apakraṣṭuṃ apakṛṣya ca svakārye niyoktuṃ paryāptaḥ| atha vā kuśalasya karmaṇa^ eva nāyam apakarṣāya paryāptaḥ| kasmāt kuśalaṃ hi me| sādhaka^ evaṃ manyate| śubhaṃ yasmān mama bahv anyad asti yatrāyaṃ yasmin bahuni śubhe^ ayaṃ saṅkara āvāpagataḥ svarge apy apakarṣam alpaṃ kariṣyato^ iti||

[157,10] niyatavipākapradhānakarmābhibhūtasya vā apy avasthārnam| katham iti duṣṭābhiprāyaḥ praśnaḥ| kathaṃ pravṛttarājyenāparasyāpravṛttarājyasyābhibhavo yukto nimittabhedena rājyapravṛtteḥ| iha tu nimittam abhivyañḥjakaṃ maraṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ  tulyaṃ tasmins tu nimitte sati sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ vṛttilābho yukta^ iti pradhānakarmābhabhavo na yukta^ itarasya^ iti manyate| tataś cāvāpagamanam evāstv iti||

[157,15] atrocyate tatrādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya^ eva aviruddhadeśakālavipākādinimittasya niyatavipākasya 
puṇyāpuṇyarūpasyānekasya karmaṇaḥ samānaṃ maraṇam abhivyaktikāraṇam uktam| na tu dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya putragrāmādiviṣayasya aniyatavipākasya vā^ adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya yathākṛtavyākhyānasya||

[157,19] yat tv adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyam aniyatavipākaṃ tan naśy_ṛṛdāpāṃ vā gasshed abhibhūtaṃ vā ciram apy upāsīta^ udādīta yāvat samānam aviruddhaṃ tulyajātīyam abhivyañjakaṃ nimittaṃ karma| samānajātīyaṃ hi karma vipacyamānam etad abhimukhaṃ karoti||

[157,22] tadvipākasya^ eva tasyāniyatavipākasya karmaṇo vipākasya^ eva| deśakālanimittānavadhāraṇāt kasmin vā deśe kasmin vā nimitte kasmin kāle  vā tatkarma(158,8) vipākābhimukhaṃ bhavīyatīty anavvadhāraṇāt| iyaṃ gatiś citrā durjñānā ca bhavati| citrā nāśāvāpābhibhavādivaicitryāt| durjñānā deśakālanimittānavadhāraṇāt||

[158,10] nanu cādṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya prāyaṇe 'bhivyaktasya^ ekam eva janma karotīty ekajanmāraṃbhitvam uktam tad idānīṃ nāśā_Vāpābhibhāvasthānādivacanād bādhyeta^ iti cet na^ upapāditottaratvāt adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyasya niyatavipākasya^ evāyaṃ niyama^ iti| na tasyotsargasyāsmād aniyamāpavādān nivṛttir iti| kathaṃ [na] nivṛttiḥ ekabhavikaḥ karmāśayo durjñāna^ ity evaṃ na nivṛttih aniyatavipākasyāpy ekajanmārambhitvaṃ na pratiṣiddham ity arthaḥ||

[158,16] atha vā utsargasyāpavādān nivṛttir iti matvā naimabhavikaḥ karmāśayo durjñāna^ ity anena nañ saṃbadhyate| yasmād utsargasya^ ekadeśasyāpavādo nirvartako na sarvasya tasmānnityā niyatavipāka^ ekabhavikaḥ karamāśayaḥ sujñāna^ eva ||

[158,19] aparaḥ kalpaḥ iha karmagatir jñātaveti prakṛtaṃ tasyāyam apavāda^ ekabhavikaḥ karmāśayo durjñāna^ iti| tatretiśabdo hetvarthe| yasmād dhetor autsargikasya karmagatis tathā jñātavyā^ eva| durjñānā iti duḥkhena jñeyatvam ucyate kleśena||2,13||

[158,23] karmāśaya^ upavyākhyāto dṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś ca adṛṣṭajanmavedanīyaś ca| tasyāpi trividhā vipākā jātyāyurbhogāḥ hlādaparitāpaphalāḥ puṇyāpuṇyagetutvāt| hlādaḥ sukhaṃ paritāpo duḥkhaṃ hlādaś ca paritāpaś ca hlādaparitāpau tau phalaṃ yeṣāṃ te hlādaparitāpaphalāḥ| tathā puṇyaṃ cāpuṇyañ ca puṇyāpuṇye te^ eva hetū yeṣāṃ te puṇyāpuṇyahetavaḥ| teṣāṃ bhāvaḥ puṇyāpuiṇyahetutvaṃ tasmāt 
puṇyapuṇyahetutvāt| te jātyāyurbhogāḥ puṇyahetukāḥ sukhaphalāḥ| apuṇyahetukā duḥkhaphalāḥ| yathedaṃ duḥkhaṃ pratikūlātmakam evaṃ viṣayasukhakāle api duḥkham asty eva pratikūlātmakaṃ yoginaḥ||2.14||
