[159,8] kathaṃ viṣayasukhakāle yoginaḥ suḥkham asti| tathā pūrvatra ca^ uktaṃ duḥkhe sukhakhyātiṃ vakṣyatīti| tad uppādayate pratipādyate| tatra ca^ etan na pratipāditam| duḥkhasya karmakāryatvāt kramāntaraṃ 
pratipādana yuktam iti bahuvaktavyatvāc ca| pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair guṇavṛttivirodhāc ca duḥkham eva sarvaṃ vivekinaḥ||

[159,12] pariṇatiḥ pariṇāmaḥ taptis tāpaḥ saṃskrtiḥ saṃskāraḥ tāpaś ca saṃskāraś ca tāpasaṃskārau pariṇāmaś ca tāpasaṃskārau ca 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskārāḥ ta eva duḥkhā duḥkhanimittāni^ etaiḥ 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair hgetubhiḥ sarvam eva duḥkham| kin tat sarvaṃ kleśā duḥkhakāraṇatvād eva hetoḥ| karmāṇi hlādaparitāpaphalatvāt| jātyāyurbhogāś ca kleśādīnām āśrayaniomittaviṣayāś ca duḥkhahetutvād ity etat sarvam eva duḥkham||

[159,18] kiṃ sarvasya^ eva netyāha vivekina^ iti kleśādīnāṃ vibhāgaviveko yasya vidyate sa vivekī| tasya kleśakarmavipakābhijñasya 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraguṇavṛttivirodhātmaka(dhāc ca) sarvaduḥkhatvopapādaka(pratipādaka)hetvabhidarśina^ ity arthaḥ| netarasyānitthaṃ daiśinaḥ||

[159,22] tasya tu taptimātraṃ duḥkham| tad api^ ahaṃ tapya^ ity ātmatvena 
pratipdyamānaḥ tatrāpi saṃśayāno 'sau mamedaṃ duḥkham ity etāvan mātram api viviṅkte| yadi hy etāvad apy antaraṃ viviñcīta tato duḥkhasādhanam apy etad duḥkha(sādhana)m iti manvīta| (160,6) tathā ca sarvam idaṃ duḥkham iti titikṣed iti so^ api vivekī syāt| tasmād yady api tāpakāle duḥkhamātraṃ paśyanti tathāpi na tatsādhanādi sarvaṃ duḥkham iti manyante nirvivekinaḥ| yasya punaḥ pariṇāmaduḥkhahetudarśanaṃ tasya vivekinaḥ sarvam eva duḥkham||

[160,11] tatra tāvat pariṇāmaduḥkhatāṃ vyāṣaṣṭe sarvasyāyaṃ rāgānuviddhaḥ/ sarvasya^ ity anena sarvaprāṇiprasiddhatvād avyabhicāripratyakṣāvagamyatvaṃ darśayati| cetanācetanādhīnaḥ 
putrapuśuhiraṇyādisādhanāyattaḥ sukhānubhava^ iti sukhānubhavakāle rāgo manovyāpāraḥ tadviśiṣṭo hi sukham anubhavati| sa sarvo vyāpāraḥ kleśopasaṃhitatvād dharmādharmahetuḥ| tat-pūrvaś ca karamāśayaḥ 
prārabdhaphala^ upabhujyate bhogakāle cāpara^ upacīyate| tataś ca kleśamūlaḥ karamāśayaḥ sati mūle tadvipāka^ ity upapannaṃ bhavati||

[160,18] tathā sukhavelāyāṃ sukhapratyanīkabhūtāni duḥkhasādhanāni upalabhmāno dveṣti tathā sukhopabhogaparipālanavyagrataramānaso muhyati ceti dveṣamohapūrvavyāpārajanitoapy asti karamāśayaḥ| athavā dveṣamohāv eva vyāpārarūpāv iti tatkṛta^ eva||

[160,21] tathā yaś cāsau sukhopabhohgaḥ sa nānupahatya nānupahiṃsya bhūtāny upabhogaḥ sambhavatīti| spabhoga^ evopaghātahetutvād upahantṛtvena vivakṣita^ iti samānakartṛkatvam| anyo api madīyamevopabhokṣyata^ iti manyate tatrāpi sarvopbhogasāmarthyayuktaṃ dhanam iti tasminn upādīyamāne dhruvaṃ parapīḍā syāt| kim uta pīḍayitvā^ eva^ yad upādīyate| tataś ca hiṃsā kṛto apy asti karamāśayaḥ śārīraḥ| tadupādānavyāpārasya śarīranirvartyatvāt||

[160,27] evam ekasminn upabhogakāle bahudhā karmāśayo lobhakrodhamohanimitta^ upacīyate| tathā ca rāgadveṣamohaviśiṣṭatvād anekamukhakarmāśayaviśiṣṭaḥ sukhānubhavaḥ pariṇamānaḥ sannanubandhe duḥkhībhavatīti pariṇāmaduḥkhatā^ ity ucyate| vivekinas tv evaṃ 
paśyataḥ suklhānubhavakāle api duḥkham asty eva| tad yathā viṣadighatāṃ jñātvā dadhyodanaṃ bhuñjānasya||

[161,7] taraś ca sarvaṃ suḥkham evāvidyātvād viṣayasukhasya| avivekapratyayātmako hi viṣayasukhānubhavaḥ| tad etad āha viṣayasukhaṃ cāvidyate^ uktam iti| kva^ uktam ihaiva paryāyaśabdena "atyantavivi[bha]ktayor atyantāsaṅkīrṇayor avivekaprāptāv iva satyāṃ bhogaḥ kalpate| svarūpapratilambhe tu tayoḥ kaivalyam eva bhavati kuto bhoga^ iti " ity anena bhāṣyeṇa||

[161,12] yad vā sūtrakāreṇa^ uktom  upariṣṭāt "pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ"iti| avidyā ca pariṇamamānā sarvam anarthaṃ karoti| tataś ca duḥkhabījatvād duḥkham eva viṣayasukham| ytathā vyādhinimittatvāt svādutaram api bhakṣyamāṇam||

[161,15] viṣayasukhaṃ cāvidyety asya^ evārthasya prakācanāṛthaṃ tantrasiddham udāharaṇaṃ darś_Vayati yā bhokoṣv indriyānāṃ bhujyanta^ iti bhogāḥ śabda^ādayaḥ teṣu nimitteṣu yā tṛptiḥ tata^ indriyāṇāṃ upaśāntiḥ upaśamaḥ kin anarthāyāsahetukayā vṛttayeti nirākulatayāvasthānaṃ tat sukham||

[161,19] yā tu laulyādīpsayā tṛṣṇātaḥ sakāśād anupaśānti^ anuparitaḥ tad duḥkham| tad uktamṅāsti tṛṣṇāsamaṃ dsuḥkhaṃ nāṣty atṛṣṇāsamaṃ sukham" iti||

[161,22] nanu ca^ upabhogena^ eva vaitṛṣṇyaṃ bhavati^ upabhogānte| upabhogaś ca sukhaṃ bhaviṣyati| tata^ āha na cendriyāṇāṃ bhogābhyāsena śakyaṃ vaitṛṣṇyaṃ kartum| kasmāt ata^ āha yato bhogābhyāsam anuvuvardhante indriyāṇāṃ rāgāḥ| tathā ca^ uktam 161,26   ṅa jātu kāmaḥ kāmānām upabhogena śābhyati| 161,27      baviṣā kṛṣṇavatseva bhūya evābhibardhate||" iti||

[162,9] kauśalāni naipuṇāni ca bhogābhyāseṣv indriyāṇām anu vivardhante| yady api manaḥ kauśalapūrvakam indriyāṇāṃ kauśalaṃ tathā api dṛśyata^ eva bhikṣā[kṣvā]dīnām indriyasyāpi kauśalam| tasmād anupāyaḥ sukhasya bhogābhyāsa^ iti bhogābhyāsān nivṛttir eva sukham||

[162,13] sa khalv ayaṃ vṛścikabhīta^ ivāśīvipepeṇa daṣṭo yaḥ sukhārthī viṣayānanvavasitaḥ anvavabaddhaḥ anupravṛtto 'nuvyavasito vā yathā mandaviṣādvṛścikād upajātabhītiḥ paṭureṇāśīviṣeṇa grasto mahattaraṃ duḥkham abhiprapadyate 

[162,16] tathā 'yam agraduḥkhābhāsāyāḥ pariṇāmamahāsukhāyā nivṛtto duḥkhabuddhyā bhītaḥ sann apāre mahati duḥkhapaṅke magna ity eṣā 
pariṇāmaduḥkhatā sukhasya pratikūlā sukhāvasthāyām api yoginaḥ| sa hi yogī 
pravṛttaveparhunā^ eva hladayena pratiniyatavedanīyasya karmaṇo balīyastvād anubhuṅkte sukham| tastmāt sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ vivekinaḥ||

[162,21] atha tāpaduḥkhatā vyākhyāyate sukhopabhogas tu pariṇamamānaḥ sannanubandhe vijñāt viṣadigdhasvādudadhyodanopabhogavad duḥkhī bhavati svarūpataḥ punar avidyārūpo'nukūlavedanāgandho api| iha punaḥ svarūpata^ eva duḥkhatā tāpasya ratikulātmakatvāt||

[162,26] katham sarvasya dveṣānuviddhaḥ dveṣānugataḥ cetanācetanasādhanādhīnaḥ śatrukaṇṭakādisādhanāyattaḥ tāpānubhavaḥ 
pūrvavad evādharmaphalam anu bhavati^ apūrvaś copacīyamānaḥ tatrāsti dveṣajaḥ karamāśayaḥ||

[163,10] tena c duḥkhena paritapyamānaḥ sukhasādhanani candanādīni 
prārthayamānaḥ kāyena vācā manasā ca parispandate ceṣṭate| tac ca 
parispandanaṃ parānugrahāya parapīḍanāya vā bhavati| tataḥ 
parānugrahapīḍābhyāṃ dharmādharmāv upacinoti| sa ca pparānugrahapīḍākṛto dharmādharmākhyaḥ karmāśayo lobhān mohāc ca prabhavati^ity eṣā tāvat tāpaduḥkhatā drāghīyasī viprasṛtā| evaṃ paśyato vivekinaḥ sarvam evāśrayaviṣayanimittair duḥkham iti||

[163,16] adhunā hlādaparitāpānubhavajanitasaṃskāraduḥkham ucyate kā 
punaḥ saṃskāraduḥkhatā sukhānubhavāt sukhasaṃskārācayaḥ duḥkhānubhavād api duḥkhasaṃskārācśayaḥ| sukhaduḥkhayo rāgadvsṣānuviddhatvāt tatsaṃskārāśayāv api rāgadveṣānuviddhāv evaṃ karmaṇo vipāke jātyāyurbhohākhye 'nubhūyamāne sukhaduḥkhen bhavataḥ||

[163,20] tato api sukhaduḥkhānubhavasaṃskārāśayaḥ yathā nairantaryeṇa sukhaduḥkhānubhavas tathā^ eva tadanubhavasaṃskārāv api rāgadveṣānuviddhau nirantaratayā bhavataḥ| tau ca saṃskārahetukaṃ saṃskāraduḥkhaṃ tathā ca tatsaṃskāraduḥkhatā| na hy asmarann upabhogāya kriyāyā^ eivā sādhanam upādatte| smṛteś ca saṃskāranimittatvam upapāditam| 163,25    yady api saṃskārasyasvarūpato duḥkhatā nāsti tathāpi saṃskārasya duḥkhahetutāṃ jānato viveinaḥ sattāmātreṇa duḥkhatvaṃ bhakṣitamātraviṣavat| ity ametaiḥ pariṇāmatāpasaṃskārahetubhiḥ sarvam eva duḥkham ity anādiduḥkharoto yoginam eva pratikūlātmakatvād uddhejayati||

[164,9] nanu cetarasyāpi pratikūlātmakam eva kasmān na tam udvejayati| nātyantakleśādikāluṣyā-vivekakiṇīkṛtacetastvād itaram uddhejayati| na pratikūlātmaktā prabhāvyate| yo hi dayitām uṣṭighātaṃ mudgarābhighātadeśīyam api mastakena patīcchati| putrasya vā mūtrapurīṣamurasā dhārayati| asāvānandamahodanvattaraṅgāyamāṇaḥ [ṇaṃ] svātmany api na vātitarām [svātmānam api na vetti tarām]| na hi tasya tat-pratikūlātmakam| rāgam eva hi tad itarasya pratipattṛtvād abhivardhayati tarām||

[164,15] nanu ca yogino api tatsamānaṃ nimittaṃ kasmāt tam uddhejayatīti| uddhejayaty eva tam| kasmāt akṣipātrakalpo hi saḥ| 
prasannatvād viraktatvād ity arthaḥ| tasmānnityā na samānaṃ nimittaṃ tasya yato 'sau vidvān iti| na hi viduṣaḥ kleśādikāluṣyā-vivekakiṇīkṛtacetastvam| na ca tatkiṇīkṛtacetastvatiraskārād ṛte vidvattā| tasmād akṣpātrakalpatvād vidvāṃsam evoddhejayati netaram||

[164,20] tatra dṛṣṭāntābhyāṃ vidvadaviduṣor antaram ācaṣṭe yathorṇātanturatimṛdusparśo apy akṣipātre nyastaḥ sparśena dūkhyati svaddhataratvād akṣipātrasya tathā yoginaṃ viviktataraprajñānamakaluṣahṛdayam udvejayati duḥkham| sa evorṇātanturanyeṣu gātreṣu kṣiptaḥ kharatarasaṃsparśenāpi na duḥkhayati tathā netaraṃ pratipattāraṃduḥkhaṃ pīḍayati kleśādikiṇīkṛtacetastvāt kaṭhinataretaragātrāvayavakalpo hi saḥ||

[164,25] tad idam āha^ evam etāni duḥkhāni^ akṣipātrakalpaṃ yoginam eva kliśnanti netaraṃ pratipattāram| itarasya^ tu pratipattur itaragātrāvayavasvabhāvatāṃ darśayann āha^ itaraṃ tv iti| tāpā anuplavanta ity evam antena vākyasamaptiḥ||

[165,5] svakāraṇopahltaṃ duḥkhaprāptinimittaiś cetanācetanādibhir upahṛtam upanītaṃ buddhivṛttidvāreṇa puruṣasya karmatām āpāditaṃ duḥkham upāttaṃ tyajantaṃ śarīratyāgenānyatra ca tyaktaṃ punar anyaṃ śarīrādilaksaṇam eva duḥkhasamudāyam upādadānamanādivāsanācitrayā^ avidyayā vittavṛttyā srvato 'nuviddham iva||

[165,9] itaraṃ pratipattāraṃ kiṃ-viśiṣṭam hātavyāhaṅkāramamakārānupātinam| kāryakaraṇeṣu tad-dharmeṣu ca kriyāguṇaphaleṣu viparyayeṇa pratipattir ahagkārah mamakāras tu teṣu bāhyeṣu ca cetanācetaneṣu mameti pratyayaḥ| tau ca viparyayatvāt samastakleśakarmavipākabījatvāc ca hātavyau| na hi tācantareṇa rāgādayaḥ karmavipākā vā samudācaranti| tasmād dhātavyau tau tāv anupatituṃ śīlam asya^ iti hātavyāhaṅkāramamakārānupātī tam||

[165,15] jātaṃ jātaṃ anyagātrāvayavakalpaṃ bāhyādhyātmikobhatanimittāḥ bāhyaṃ tevatā bhūtāni ca^ ādhyātmikaṃ kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ ca bāhyādhyātmikāni cobhyani ca nimittaṃ yeṣāṃ te bāhyādhyātmikobhayanimittāḥ||

[165,18] triparvāṇaḥ bāhyādinimittam eva triparvatvam| bhūtabhaviṣyad vartamānatvaṃ vā triparvatvam| vakṣyati ca heyaṃ duḥkham anāgatam iti yad vā kleśakarmavipakātmakatvam| yad vā pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhatvam| atas te triparvāṇas tāpā anuplavante^ anuṣajyanti||

[165,22] ta(me)d evaṃ avivekaṃ pratipattāram anādiduḥkhasrotasohyamānam ātmānaṃ ca duḥkhasrotasi magnaṃ tathā bhūtagrāmaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā yogī sarvaduḥkhakṣayakaraṃ samyagdarśanaṃ śaraṇaṃ prapadyata^ iti||

[165,25] nanu ca yogitvād eva samyagdarśanaṃ pratipannaḥ| ucyate yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād eva yogitvaṃ ṛte api samyagdarśanāt| tasmāt samyagdarśanasādhanatvaṃ yogasya (166,7) darśayati| yogaṃ yogaphalaṃ ca samvam eva duḥkhaṃ paśyan samyagdarśanaikaśaraṇaṃ pratipadyate| tathā ca vakṣyati tadvairāgyād api doṣabījakṣaye kaivalyam iti||

[166,9] itaś ca sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ vivekinaḥ kutaś ca guṇvṛttivirodhāc cāpi| nanu ca kimarthaṃ sūtre pṛthag bibhaktikriyā yāvatā 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraguṇavṛttivirodhair ity eva kartavyam| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| sukhaduḥkhānubhavatadvāsanāḥ sarvajanaprasiddhā iti 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair iti nirdiṣṭāḥ| tatkāraṇavibhāgajñānaviśāradasya^ eva vivekaprakarṣavato guṇavṛttivirodhā vijñeyā iti pṛthakkaraṇam| sarvaduḥkhahetutvasāmānyāt tv ekasūtravinyāsaḥ||

[166,15] guṇāḥ sattvādayaḥ teṣāṃ vṛttayo guṇavṛttayaḥ tāsāṃ virodhād guṇavṛtivirodhāt| te ca guṇā^ antaḥkaraṇādyākārasanniviṣṭāḥ| teṣāṃ guṇapradhānabhāvenābhinnapariṇāmatvāc ca viruddhavṛttitvaṃ bhavati| anyatamavṛttirudbhavantyā 'nyatamavṛttyābhibhūyate||

[166,19] evaṃ trayāṇām api pratikṣaṇa(pakṣa)m anyonyavṛttitiraskāra itaretaravṛttyudbhāvitvaṃ ca| na sukhaduḥkhamohātmakānāṃ guṇānām anytamavṛttilābho'nyatamānanusaṃhitaḥ| tata^ etat siddhaṃ bhavati yadā duḥkhātmakaṃ raja^ udbhavati tadā duḥkhatvād eva tadvṛtter na duḥkham iti vaktavyam| sattvatamasor api rajo 'nugatir asty eva^ iti tayor api duḥkhatvam eva| sattvatamaḥpravṛttinivṛttyor api rajovyāpārānujatvād duḥkhatvam eva sarvasya^ iti||

[166,24] etad upavyācaṣṭe prakhyāpravṛttisthitiśīlābhir vṛttibhir guṇāḥ 
parasparānugrahatantrāḥ parasparairysshā^ api 
parasparopakāryopakārakabhāvena tailavartyagnivat śāntaṃ sāttvikam ghoraṃ rājasaṃ mūḍhaṃ tāmasaṃ pratyayaṃ triguṇam eva^ ārabhante| i(tare)tarayor aṅgabhāvenānugatatvād yadā sāttvikaḥ śāntaḥ pratyaya^ udbhūyaḥ so 'nantaram evodbubhūṣatā tamo 'nusaṃhitena ghoreṇa rājasena pratyayena hanyate||

[167,5] yadā ca rāsaso ghoraḥ pratyāya^ itaraguṇānugata^ udbhūto bhavati so 'nanntaram evodbubhūṣatā tāmasena pratyayena mūḍhena sattvānugatena sāttvikena vā tamo 'nugatena hanyate| tathā tāmas_O 'pītarapratyāyābhyāṃ 
paryāyeṇa| tathā sāttvikena^ itarāv ity anoyonyodayābhibhavapravṛttayā guṇāḥ||

[167,11] kasmāt yataḥ phalaṃ guṇavṛttaṃ guṇasvabhāvatvaṃ cittākāreṇa guṇāḥ sanniviṣṭā iti kṣiprapariṇāmaṃ cittam uktam| kva tantre||

[167,13] kasmāt punar guṇā virudhyanta^ iti| anekātiśayānām ekatrāsambhavāt| yathā^ apavarake tamaḥprakāśayor atiśayavatoḥ evam antaḥkaraṇākārasanniveśināṃ sukhaduḥkhamoharūpāṇāṃ 
prakāśapravṛttiniyamavṛttīnāṃ guṇānāṃ rūpeṇa  ā sukhādinā vṛttyā vā jñānādilakṣaṇayā yugapad atiśayo na sambhavati||

[167,17] sāmānyānāṃ tv avirodhād avyaktarūpāṇām ati_Vayair vyaktaiḥ saha vṛtti yuktā| tena rūpātiśayā vṛttyatiśayāś ca virudhyante| sāmānyāni tv atiśayaiḥ saha vartante^ ity ucyate| yadā sukharūpātiśayas tadā na duḥkharupātiśayo māṃharūpātiśayo vā| tathā itarātiśaye netarātiśayaḥ| tathā jñānavṛttyatiśaye na lobhapratyayavṛttyatiśayaḥ| tathā anyatamavṛttyaticaye nānyatamavṛttyatiśayaḥ||

[167,22] evam ete guṇā itaretaropāśrayopārjitasukhaduḥkhamohapratyayāḥ| sattvena rajastamaupāśrayeṇopārjitaḥ sukhapratyayaḥ| rajasāpi sattvatamaupāśrayeṇopārjito duḥkhapratyayaḥ| tamasāpi sattvarajaupāśrayeṇopārjito mohapratyayaḥ| tataḥ sarve sarvakāryā bhavanti||

[167,26] yady evam ayaṃ pratyayaḥ sāttvikaḥ tāmaso 'yaṃ rājasaś cāyam iti viśeṣaḥ ka^ ity āha guṇapradhānabhāvakṛtas tv eṣāṃ viśeṣa^ iti| 
pradhānopsarjanatayā saṃjñālābhālābhau| yathā pāñcabhautikatve api 
pārthivaṃ taijasamāpyam iti||

[167,29] yata^ evaṃ guṇavṛttivirodhaḥ tasmāt sarvam eva duḥkhaṃ vivekina iti| guṇavṛttivirodhād ity etat paravairāgyārtham| kathaṃ 
pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhair iti dṛṣṭānuśravika(168,9)viṣayavitṛṣṇasya^ eva sarvasya duḥkhapratipādanārthaṃ pṛthagvibhaktikaṃ kṛtam| na hi tatra duḥkhatvam apaśyato vairāgyam upakalpyata^ iti||

[168,11] etat tu guṇavṛttivirodhāc ceti guṇānāṃ 
parasparavirodhivṛttitvena duḥkhapratipādanāt tebhyo vyaktāvyaktadharmakebhyaḥ paraṃ vairāgyam upakalpyata^ ity evam artham| tathā ca^atrāpi pradhānopasarjanabhāvena sarve sarvarūpā bhavantīti vyaktāvyaktadharmatvaṃ pratipāditam| evaṃ ca kṛtvā sūtre api guṇavṛttivirodhāc ceti pṛthak paṭhitam||

[168,16] pariṇāmatāpasaṃskāraduḥkhadharmatvāt tasyaitasya mahato guṇagauṇalakṣaṇasya duḥkhasamudāyasya prabhavabījam avidyā| tasyāś ca samyagdarśanam abhāvahetuḥ pratipakṣatvāt| bhūtārthāśrayatvād vidyāyā abhūtārthāśrayatvāc cāvidyāyā vidaivābhāvahetuḥ| yathā sadviṣayeṇa^ekacandradarśanenāsadviṣayasyānekacandradarśanasya bādhanam||

[168,19] saha duḥkhanidānena kleśakarmavipakalakṣaṇena sarvaṃ duḥkham ity upapāditam| anena duḥkhena yo duḥkhī vivekī sa^ etasya samyagdarśanaśāstrasya viṣayaḥ adhikṛto netaro duḥkhasya^ eva 
pratipattṛtvād ity etad dṛṣṭāntena pratipipādayiṣyann āha yathā cikitsāśāstraṃ caturvyūham rogo rogahetur ārogyaṃ bhaiṣajyam iti roherogahetumāṃs tannivivartayiṣui bhaiṣajyasya śāstrasyārogyasya 
prayojanasya viṣayaḥ||

[168,24] evam idam api śāstraṃ caturvyūham eva yathā saṃsāraḥ saṃsarahetur mokṣo mokṣahetuḥ| tatra duḥkhabahulaḥ saṃsāro heyaḥ| 
pradyānapuruṣasaṃyogo heyahetuḥ| saṃyogasyātyantikī nivṛttir hānam| hānopāyaḥ samyagdarśanam ity ete catvāro vyuhāḥ||

[169,5] yathā cikitsāśāstraṃ roge rogahetau ca^ ārogye ca tatprayojanake bhaiṣasye ca catuṣtaye svarūpapratipādanāya vyūhitaṃ tata^ eva ca rogādayo^ asya catvāro vyūhā iti caturvyūham tathā^ asya śāstrasya| duḥkhabahulaḥ saṃsāraḥ [heyaḥ] avidyāhetukaś ca pradhānapuruṣasaṃyogo hetuḥ sanimittasya saṃsārasya hānaṃ samyagdarśanaśāstrasya prayojanaṃ tathā samyagdarśanaṃ ceti catvāra vyūhā^ asyāpīti caturvyūham śāstram| eṣu vā 'rtheṣu caturdhā vyūho 'sya^ iti caturvyūham||

[169,12] tatra hātuḥ svarūpam^ iti| nanu draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ iti hātṛ-lakṣaṇasūtra^ etad viditavyam| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ śāstrārambhaprayojanapratipipādayiṣuṇā kleśādikaḥ samasto duḥkhāvasānaḥ so^ asya śāstrasya viṣaya^ upapāditaḥ| tasya vivekinaḥ śāstramataḥparam ārabhyante| etac ca prathamasūtre pradarśitam| ārambhyamāṇasya śāstrasya samyagdarśanam eva prayojanam| tac ca saṃsāraduḥkhanirvarṇanānantaram avasaraprāptaṃ pratipādayitavyam ity etad ucyate| tatra hātuḥ svarūpam iti||

[169,19] hātā puruṣaḥ tasya svarūpam^ upādeyaṃ vā heyaṃ vā na bhavati| kuta etad evam heyopādeyavyatikekeṇa vastvadarśanāt| yathā loke sukhaṃ tatkāraṇaṃ copādeyaṃ duḥkhaṃ tatkāraṇaṃ ca heyam evam ayam api puruṣo vastutvād anyataradharmā bhavitum arhatīty abhiprāyaḥ| kim ato yady evam ata^ etad bhavati hāne tv asyocchedavād aprasaṅga^ iti||

[169,24] yady asau heyatvadharmā tata^ eṣa hātavyaḥ| hāne tv asya kiṃ syāt ātmocchedavād aprasaṅgaḥ syāt| ataḥ ko doṣaḥ syāt doṣaṃ kaivalyapāde 
pratipādayiṣyāmaḥ||

[169,27] kiñ cānyat asya hātrantarasya (kiñcānyatarasya) abhāvād dhānānupapattitvam| svayam eva^ ātmana^ ātmā hāteti cet na svātmani kriyāvirodhād bhedābhāvāc ca| heyād ātmana^ ātmana hātā ced anyas tadā 'nanvasthāsaṅgaḥ| yo yo hātā tasyāpi heyatvāt| na ca yugapaddvyor hātroḥ saṃbhavo 'ati| bhinnakālabhāvinos tu heyahātṛtvānupapattiḥ||

[170,5] hātuś ca heyatvābhyupagame tasyānavasthitatvād dheyasambandhānupapattiḥ| hātur anavasthitatve ca svayam eva heyatvapratipatter hātur hānāyodyamānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ| hānaphalānupapattiś ca na hi baddhasya svarūpanāśa^ eva phalam| mokṣo hi tasya phalam| nāpi mriyamāṇo api jñātakṣaṇāntaramaraṇaḥ svarūpavināśam icchati| vināśasyāthaprāptatvād evam anavasthitaś ced dhātā na svarūpahānam icchet arthaprāpter hānasya||

[170,10] yathā ca hātur nityatvaṃ tathā draṣṭṛ-sūtre kaivalyapāde ca vyākhyāsyāmahe| tasmānnityā na heyatvadharmā puruṣaḥ| saṃsāras tu mahadādivyaktiḥ duḥkhabahulo heyaḥ| tat-tulyadharmakaḥ puruṣo na bhavatity arthaḥ| tattulyadharmakatve tasyocchedavād aprasaṅga^ iti||

[170,13] astu tarhy upādeyaḥ yathā sukhārthaṃ sukhakāraṇaṃdravyam| na^ etad evam| kasmāt upādāne tv asya hetuvādaprasaṅgāt /tasya dravyādyupādānaphalena sambandhaḥ sa cedupādīyeta, tannirhetukamasya^ upādānaṃ syāt|  anyasyopādātur abhāvāt| na hi paṭaḥ paṭena^eva^upādīyate| anyasya hy anyad upādeyam| upādātrantarasambhave hi tadanavasthāprasaṅhaḥ so apy anyenopādīyate so api cānyeneti||

[170,18] kiṃ ca pārārthyaprasaṅgaś ca| anyasya hi hetor anyad upādīyate sattvādilakṣaṇasya pradhānasyopādāne puruṣo hetur abhyupeyate yathā ghaṭāya mṛtpiṇḍopādāne kilālo hatuḥ| sa cet puruṣaḥ pradhānopādānahetuḥ 
pradhānavad upādīyeta tasyāpi sattvādidharmatvaṃ syāt upādeyatvān mṛt-piṇḍavat| tato^ asya pradhānasya nirhetukaṃ kāryārtham upādānaṃ 
prāpnoti||

[170,23] atha-īśvarārtham upādeyaḥ puruṣāṇāṃ vā parasparārtham iti cet bhogyatvācetanatvapariṇāmitvādidoṣasāmagrī prasajayeta tasmānnityā nāpi 
pradhānadharmakaḥ puruṣa^ ity upādāne ca hetuvādaprasaṅga^ ity ucyate||

[170,26] tad uktam hetus tv apuruṣo bhavatīti| tasya copādāne^ ahetuḥ 
pradhānavṛttirnirhetukī syāt| na ca svayaṃ svārthaṃ śakyaṃ vijñātum| 
prakāśādiṣu svārthatvād adarśanāt||

[171,11] ubhayapratyākhyāne heyatvopādeyatvapratyākhyāne| śāśvatavādaprasaṅgaḥ ātmanityatvavādaprasaṅgaḥ| yady api 
pradhānasyopādeyasyāpi śāśvatatvaṃ tathāapi vikārātmanā tad aśāśvatam eva| anekātmakatvam aśucitvam avidyātvaṃ parārthatvam ity evamādayo doṣāḥ 
pradhāne| puruṣāṇām api tathātve saty anirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ| bandhomokṣayos taddhetvoś ca vidyāvidyayoḥ svarūpāvyatirekād bandhamuktayoś ca nirviśeṣatvaprasaṅgāt| tasmād dheyopādeyatvapratyākhyānena śāśvatavāda ity etad eva samyagdarśanam||2.15||

[171,18] yathā cikitsyavyatiriktarogādicatuṣṭayam antareṇa cikitsāśāstram ārogyaprayojanaṃ caturvyūham na saṃbhavati tathedam api hātṛ-svarūpavyatiriktaheyādicatuṣṭayam antareṇa samyagdarśanaśāstraṃ hānaphalaṃ caturvyūham na sambhavatīti heyādicatuṣṭayapratipādanena tad etacchāstraṃ caturvyūham abhidhīyate||

[171,22] katham heyaṃ duḥkham anāgatam duḥkham atītam atikrāntaṃ vartamānāj janmano'tikrāntajanmalakṣaṇaṃ duḥkham tadupabhogenātivāhitam atinītaṃ na heyapakṣe vartate| svayam eva hīnatvāt||

[171,25] vartamānaṃ ca yajjanmalakṣaṇaṃ duḥkhan svakṣaṇopabhogārūḍham svaṃ vartamānaṃ bhogaviśiṣṭaṃ kṣaṇamadhvānam upabhujyam ānātmakatvenārūḍhaṃ tat duḥkhaṃ na kṣṇāntare heyatām āpadyate| svayam eva bhogena hīnaṃ na hānāya kṣaṇāntaram apekṣate||

[171,28] tasmād yad^ evānāgataṃ prāyaṇānantarabhāvijanmaduḥkhalakṣaṇaṃ duḥkhaṃ tad evākṣipātrakalpaṃ yoginaṃ kliśnātīti tad eva heyaṃ samyagdarśanena hānīyam ucyate| bhaviṣyajjanmābhāvāyaiva yatitavyam| na vartamānaduḥkhanirodhāya||/

[172,8] vartamānajanmayiyatiṣāyāṃ hi samyagdarśanam aśaktyaviniyogatvād anarthakaṃ syāt| muktabāṇavat pravṛttaphalatvād vartamānaduḥkhasya| bhaviṣyati punar apravṛttatvād bījanirodha upakalpata iti samyagdarśanārthavattvam| svaviṣayo hi samyagdarśanasya sa^ iti||2.16||

[172,12] tad etac-śāstraṃ caturvyūham abhidhīyata^ ity uktam ekaś caturṣuvyūhaḥ pratipāditaḥ heyaṃ duḥkham anāgatam iti| idānīṃ dvitīyovyūhaḥ tasya duḥkhasya kāraṇaṃ hetuḥ pratinirdiśyate||

[172,15] nanu nirdiṣṭam eva duḥkhakāraṇam iti tathaivopasaṃhṛtam ante tasyaitasya mahato suḥkhasamudāyasya prabhavabījam avidyeti| satyam evam| kin tu duḥkhasvarūpamātraṃ tatkāraṇasvarūpamātraṃ ca pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭam| na ca tatsvarūpamātre^ iti tatkāraṇaṃ nirdiśyate| draṣṭṛdṛśyayoḥ saṃyogo heyahetuḥ||

[172,19] draṣṭā buddheḥ pratisaṃvedī puruṣo vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaḥ| 
pratisaṃvedituṃ śīlam asya sa pratisaṃvedī| kasya buddher iti| dṛśyā buddhiḥ antaḥkaraṇaṃ pratyayarūpeṇa||

[172,21] nanu ca cabdādayo api viṣayā dṛśyā eva^ ity ata^ āha sattvopārūḍhāḥ sarve dharmāḥ śabdādayo buddhisattvam upārūḍhā buddhipratyayākārā eva dṛśyā na svataḥ| svataś ca dṛśyatve jñātājñātaviṣayatvaṃ puruṣasya syāt| sadā jñātaviṣayatvaṃ ca puruṣasya nyānena sthāpyate| tasmād buddhyupārūḍhā eva sarve dharmā dṛśyāḥ ||

[173,7] tad etad dṛśyam ayaskāntamaṇikalpam ayaso api kriyā-arthena^ eva^ upakartā 'yaskāntamaṇir yathā tathā puruṣasya puruṣārthena dvividhena bhogāpavargalakṣaṇena^ upakartṛtvād ayakāntamaṇir iva svaṃ dṛśirūpasya cittatvarūpamātrasya svṃminaḥ||

[173,10] kathaṃ svam ity ata^ āha karmaviṣayatvam āpannaṃ karmaiva viṣayaḥ karmaviṣayaḥ karmatvāpattir eva| viṣayo dṛśirūpasya dṛśyatām āpanna^ ity etat| katham bhūtaṃ satkarmaviṣayatām āpadyata^ iti tadviśeṣaṇārtham āha anyasvarūpeṇa pratilabdhātmakam iti||

[173,13] anyasvarūpeṇ asvaparapuruṣarūpeṇādirūpeṇa ca 
pratilabdhātmakaṃ pratilabdhaviśeṣarūpaṃ 
pratilabdhapuruṣaśabdādiviśeṣasvabhāvaṃ puruṣasvarūpeṇa labdhātmakaṃ saddṛśyatvena karmatām āpannam apavargārthena svaṃ bhavati| śabdādyākāreṇa labdhātmakaṃ saddṛśyatvena karmatām āpannaṃ bhogārthena svam bhavati||

[173,17] nanu kathaṃ svatantrasya svatvam| na hi kiñcit svatantraṃ svayaṃ vyāpriyamāṇam anyad apekṣate| yad^ api svavyāpārānuniṣpādipuruṣārtharūpaṃ tad api svabhāvād eva| na 
puruṣaniyogakārī| tasmāt svatantrasya sataḥ svatvaṃ na yuktam ity ata^ āha svatantram api puruṣāṛthdvayakartṛtvena parārthatvāt paratantram| tataś ca svam eva||

[173,21] tayor drgdarśanaśaktyor iti| draṣṭṛdṛśyayor ity upkramya dṛgfarśanaśaktyr ity upasaṃhāraḥ buddhipuruṣasaṃyogasya buddhyutpattyanantarabhāvitvād ādimattvam āśaṅkya tadanādimattvapradarśanārthaṃ kriyate| dṛgdarśanaśaktyor anādiḥ saṃyogaḥ| darmiṇāṃ nityatvāt nityasambandhopapattes tacchaktyor api draṣṭṛdṛśyatvalakṣaṇo 'nādiḥ saṃyogo'rthakṛtaḥ puruṣārthaprayojavavaśa ity arthaḥ||

[173,26] tathā ca saty anāditve api prayojananivṛttau saṃyoganivṛttir upapadyate| karmiṇāṃ tu svarūpasaṃyogaḥ sattāmātreṇa na prayohjanakṛta iti nitya eva| dharmamātrābhis tv anityābhir anityaḥ saṃyogaḥ| sa tu buddhipuruṣasaṃyogo heyahetuḥ duḥkhasya kāraṇam ity arthaḥ||

[174,8] tathā caoktaṃ tantre tatsaṃyogahetuvivarjanāt tayor buddhipuruṣayoḥ saṃyogas tasya hetur avidyā yadvivarjanāṭ| kathaṃ vivarjanam vidyayā samyagdarśanena tatpratipakṣeṇa nirvartanāt| tasmāt tatsaṃyogahetuvivarjanāt ātyantiko 'tyantāya prabhavati duḥkhapratīkāraḥ| ko 'sau yatsamyagdarśitvam||

[174,12] katham etad iti prasiddhaṃ dṛṣṭāntam āha tad yathā pādatalasya bhedyatā sukhamārataratvāt| kaṇṭakasya bhoktṛtvaṃ karkaśasvabhāvatvāt| tatsaṃyogo viśiṣṭo duḥkhahetur ity arthasiddham| tatra kaṇṭakavyathāduḥkhasya parihāraḥ kaṇṭake padā pādenānadhiṣṭhānam| etad-dvayaṃ yo veda bhedyabhettṛdvayaṃ parihāradvayaṃ vā yo veda loke sa tatrānyataraṃ parihāram ārabhate| sa tatra pratī_Kāramāramamāṇo bhedajaṃ kaṇṭakabhedajanitaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāpnoti||

[174,19] tathehāpi puruṣaḥ sattvadvāreṇa kaṇṭakasthānīyaṃ pradhānam tayor viśiṣṭaḥ saṃyogo'vidyamātraviśeṣebhyo dṛśyebhyo nirapekṣatā samyagdarśanena prayojanābhāvaṃ buddhvā puruṣasyāvikriyatvāt kaṇṭakādhiṣṭhānavaccharīrārambhiṇo vā karmaṇo niyatavedanīyatvād avvaśyaṃ 
phaladāyitvaṃ buddhvā^ aprārthitopanatānāṃ śabda^ādīnāṃ samyagdarśanavyavahitena sattvena bhogaḥ| evaṃ parihāraṃ sañjānānaḥ kaṇṭakabhedaduḥkhaparihāram iva saṃsāraduḥkhaṃ nāpnoti||

[175,7] kasmād etad evam iti tritvopalabdhisāmarthyāt| 
pradhānapuruṣasaṃyogatritvopalabdhisāmarthyāt| evaṃ hi vijñānasya sāmarthyaṃ dṛśyate pādatalakaṇṭakatatsaṃyogeṣv ajñānasya ca tritvānupalabdher duḥkhahetutvaṃ dṛśyata^ eva||

[175,10] etam arthaṃ pracikhyāpayiṣann āhātrāpi tāpakasya rajasaḥ| yady api pradhānaṃ kaṇṭakasthānīyaṃ tāpakaṃ tathāpi rajaso duḥkhātmakatvād rajodvāreṇa^ eva pradhānasya tāpakatvam ity ucyate tāpakasya rajasa^ iti| tasya rahjasaḥ karmabhūtaṃ sattvam eva tapyam| kasmāt cchidikriyāvattapikriyāyāḥ karmasthatvāt| karmaṇi hi tapikriyā vartate na rajasastāpakasya viṣayiṇi puruṣe| tapteś ca phalasya puruṣaviṣayatvāt||

[175,16] na hi paraśuśdhettari svātmani vā vartate cchidir vā phalaṃ viṣaya^ eva chedye kāṣṭhādau| evaṃ puruṣasya^ eva taptiḥ karmaṇi sattve vartamānā phalaṃ rajaś ca kāṣṭhādāv iva paraśuḥ sattva eva puruṣasya dṛśye vartate||

[175,19] itaś ca puruṣe tapikriyā na vartate| apariṇāmitvāt draṣṭu| tapir api hi karmavikriyā| yathā chedanasya phalaṃ chedyasya karmaṇo dvaidhī-bhāvo vikriyā tadvat| nāpariṇāmini niṣkriye kṣetrajñe| apariṇāmitvaṃ ca sadā jñātaviṣayatvena sādhayiṣyati||

[175,23] yady evaṃ puruṣaś cen na tapyate puruṣārthaṃ pradhānaṃ 
pravartata^ ity etad anupapannam| vṛttisārūpyeṇa^ uktottaratvāt| tad evvedam ucyate darśitaviṣayatvāt| apariṇāmino 'vikriyatvād darśitaviṣayatvam| tasmād dṛśikarmaṇi sattve tapye tadākārānurodhī tadākārānuvartī| tadākārānurodhitvaṃ tad-dṛśisannidhau bauddhasya 
pratyayasya dṛśirūpāvabhāsatā| yathā lākṣārasasannidhau sphaṭihkasya lākṣākārāvabhāsatā tathā puruṣo api tapyata^ ity upacaryate||2.17||

[176,7] dṛśyasvarūpam^ ucyate| nanu ca draṣṭṛdṛśyayor ity uktaṃ 
pūrvasūtre| tatra kramaprāptasannidhānaṃ dṛṣṭṛ-svarūpam^ eva pūrvaṃ vaktavyam| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| dṛśyasvarūpāvagamapūrvakatvād draṣṭṛsvarūpāvabodhasya| dṛśyasya hi svātmavyatiriktadraṣṭrarthatvād vyatiriktadraṣṭṛsadbhāvasiddhiḥ| ata^ eva prādhānyāt pūrvasūtre draṣṭuḥ 
pūrvanipātaḥ kṛtaḥ| tal-lakṣaṇe tv arthakramo vivakṣyate||

[176,12] prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlaṃ bhūtendriyātmakaṃ bhogāpavargārthaṃ dṛśyam| prakāśaś ca kriyā ca sthitiś ca prakāśakriyāsthitayaḥ| 
prakāśasyābhyarhitatvāt pūrvanipātaḥ| bahuṣu cāniyamāt sthitikriyāśabdayor aniyama^ iti kriyāśabdasya pūrvanipātaḥ| tā eva śīlaṃ yasya tat-prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlam| prakāśanaṃ kriyā kathaṃ tacchīlaṃ sattvaṃ syāt rajo hi tacchīlaṃ sthitiśīlaṃ vā tamaḥ katham iti ||

[176,18] nanu ca svarūpam^ eva tatprakāśanaṃ sthitiś ca| na| svarūpamātre śīlaśabdāyogāt| na hi mṛḍ-aṅgaśīlo mārdaṅgikaḥ| mṛḍaṅgavācanaśīlo hi sa ucyate| na hi vyāpāraśūnye kvacid api śīlaśabdaḥ prayujyate||

[176,21] nanu ca sthitiprakāśāv api sattvatamasoḥ svarūpam| tad etena^ eva gamyate svarūpe śīlaśabdaḥ prasaṃkṣyata^ iti| ucyate na svarūpe sarvatra śīlaśabdaprayoṅaḥ| ihāpi rajasi duḥkhasvarūpe kriyā-śīlam iti kriyayā samprayujya śīlaśabdavyapadeśaḥ| sthitiprakāśāv api vyāpārarūpāvabhipretau||

[176,25] athamataṃ kriyāpi rajasaḥ svarūpam^ eva^ iti na pradhānāvasthāyāṃ sāmyābhyupagamāt guṇānām| yadi kriyā rajasaḥ svarūpam^ abhaviṣyat| 
pradhānasyāpi kriyāvattvaṃ guṇasāmyāvasthāyām abhaviṣyat| kāryāvasthāyāṃ hi kriyāvattvam abhyupagamyate na pradhānavelāyām| tasmānnityā na svarūpābhiprāyeṇa śīlaśabdaprayogaḥ||

[177,5] tatrākriyāśīlayor api sattvatamasor nityaṃ kriyā-śīlena rajasā saṃyogād rajaḥ kriyayā^ eva kriyāvattvaṃ sthitiprakāśayor apīti tatkriyopacāreṇa prakāśaśīlaṃ sattvaṃ sthitiśīlaṃ tama^ iti vyapadiśyate| sthitiprakāśayor ātmalābhasya rajaḥkriyāpūrvatvāc calator evābhivyaktes tācchīlyaṃ sattvatamasor ucyate||

[177,9] tatra prakāśaśīlaṃ yat tat sattvam| kriyā-śīlaṃ pravṛttiśīlaṃ rajaḥ| sthitiśīlaṃ varaṇaśīlaṃ niyamaśīlaṃ tama^ ity ete guṇāḥ 
parasparoparaktapravibhāgāḥ paraspareṇoparaktaḥ pravibhāgo bhedo yeṣāṃ guṇānāṃ te parasparoparaktapravibhāgāḥ||

[177,12] yathā prakāśaśīlam iti sattvam ucyate 'traiva| rajaḥkriyoparañjanāt sarvāvabhāsasamarthasyāpi sattvasya niyataprakāśatvaṃ tapauparaktatvena| tathā rajasoapi pravṛttyabhivyaktiḥ sattvoparāgeṇa| niyatapravṛttitvaṃ tu tamauparāgeṇa| tathā tamaso api sthityabhivyaktiḥ sattvoparāgeṇa| sthitiśīlatvaṃ rajauparāgeṇa| sthitis tu svata^ eva| rajaso api pravṛttiḥ svataḥ| sattvasyāpi prakāśaḥ svata^ eva ittham evānyeṣv api kāryeṣu sattvādīnām itaretaroparāgo draṣṭavaḥ||

[177,18] saṃyogaviyogadharmāṇaḥ| saṃyogaś ca viyogaś ca saṃyogaviyogau tau dharmau yeṣāṃ te saṃyogaviyogadharmāṇa| kasmiṃś cit kārya^ ārabhyamāṇe guṇapradhānabhāvenānyonyaṃ saṃyujyante| tathā^ eva^ ārabdhakāryavirodhidharmāntarodaye parasparaṃ viyujyante||

[177,21] itaretaropāśrayeṇopārjitamūrtayaḥ mahadādistambaparyantā mūrtaya itaretaropāśrayeṇopārjitā dharmarharmilakṣaṇā yais te itaretaropāśrayeṇopārjitamūrtayaḥ||

[177,23] parasparato bhinnā^ api paramārthato 'nyonyato 'nye api| asambhinnaśaktipravibhāgāḥ atyante vyāvṛttakāryārambhābhimukhyasāmarthyaprabhedāḥ||

[178,7] etad uktaṃ bhavati^ ekasmin kārya^ ārabdhavye 'tyantavyatiṣaktaparasparaśaktayaḥ kāryārambhakāle 
pṛthaktvenānupalakṣyamāṇaśaktipravibhāgā iti||

[178,9] tulyajātīyātulyajātīyaśaktibhedānupātinaḥ| sāttvikānāṃ sattvaṃ tulyajātiñ rājasānāṃ rahastulyajātiḥ tāmasānāṃ tama^ iti ca| 
parasparāpekṣayā tu bhinnajātīyatvam| tulyajātīyātulyajātīyānāṃ śaktiprabhedam anupatituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te tulyajātīyātulyajātīyaśaktiprabhedānupātinaḥ| na hi sāttvike vā rājase tāmase vā prārabdhavyasya trayāṇāṃ śaktiprabhedānupātam antareṇa kāryārambhaḥ sambhavati||

[178,15] yady evaṃ sarvaśaktiprabhedānupātitvād ekaikasya kāryārambhe tulyaṃ prādhānyam ity ata^ āha pradhānavelāyām upadarśitasannidhānāḥ sarve| ekaikasmin prādhānyena svakāryārambhiṇi tatkāla^ itaraypr api sannidhānam upasarjanabhāvena^ upadarśitam iti pradhānavelāyām upadarśitasannidhānāḥ| na hi sarveṣāṃ yugapat prādhānyam| samapradhānatve hi pradhānam eva bhaveyuḥ na kāryārambhitvam||

[178,20] athocyate kathaṃ punar ekasmin prādhānena kāryārambhiṇītarāstitvam avagamyata iti| tad ucyate guṇatve copasarjanabhāve ca vyāpāramātreṇa pradhānopalakṣyamāṇena vyāpāramātreṇa| pradhānāntarnītena na hi pradhānasyāntarnīto vyāpāraḥ itarayor upasarjanabhāvenāsannidhāne saṃbhaviṣyatīti pradhānavyāpāreṇānumitāstitvāḥ||

[179,9] puruṣārthetikartavyatāprayuktasāmarthyāḥ puruṣārtho bhogāpavargau tasyetikartavyatā yathā yathā^ arthaniṣpattistathā tathā prayuktaṃ kāryārambhiṃ prati sāmarthyaṃ yeṣāṃ te puruṣārthetikartavyatāpratyuktasāmarthyāḥ||

[179,12] sannidhimātropakārāṇiḥ vikārapratyayarūpasannidhimātreṇa puruṣāsyopakuruvantītyi sannidhimātropakāriṇaḥ| ayaskāntamaṇikalpāḥ||

[179,14] pratyayaṃ vṛttiṃ jñānam antareṇaikatamasya vṛttiṃ sthitilakṣaṇām samastām anuvarmānāḥ| pradhānaśabdavācyā bhavanti| naiva triguṇīvyatirekeṇānyat pradhānaṃ nāmāsti| ta eva guṇāḥ sāmyāvasthāḥ pradhānaikaśabdābhidhānīyakaṃ pratipadyante| vaiṣamyaṃ bhajanto vikāraśabdābhidheyā vṛttimanto bhavanti||

[179,18] etad dṛśyam ity ucyate| tad etad dṛśyaṃ bhūtenriyātmakaṃ bhūtātmakam indriyāmakaṃ ca| kathaṃ tadātmakatvam ity āha bhūtabhāvena 
pṛṭhivyādinā sūkṣmasthūlena sūkṣmeṇa tanmātrarūpeṇa sthūlena viśeṣārūpreṇa| dvividho hi bhūtabhāvas tanmātrarūpo viśeṣarūpaś ca| vipariṇamate sthlasukṣmabhāvam āpadyate| indriyabhāvenendriyarūpeṇa śrotrādinā sūkṣmasthūlena vipariṇamate| sūkṣmeṇāhaṅkārarūpeṇa sthcūlena śrotrādirūpeṇa||

[179,24] tat tu dṛśyaṃ nāprayojanam| sarvatraiva pravṛtteḥ prayojanadarśanāt| api tu prayojanamūrikṛtasyāṅgīkṛtya pravartate| kiṃ punar dṛśyapravṛttiprayojanam ity āha bhāgāpavargārthaṃ hi dṛśyaṃ puruṣasyāgnyādipratipattivat||

[180,9] kau oubas tau bhogāpavargāv ity āha tatreṣṭāniṣṭaguṇasvarūpāvadhāraṇam avibhāgāpannam iṣṭānāṃ agarucandanasparśanadilakṣaṇānāṃ aniṣṭānāṃ pratikūlātmakānāṃ kṛpāṇakaṇṭakādismarśanarūpāṇāṃ guṇānāṃ svarūpāvadhāraṇaṃ svarūpāvagamaḥ svarūpādhyavasānam| tasya viśeṣaṇaṃ avibhāgāpannaṃ puruṣapratyaye nāyaṃ puruṣa idaṃ sattvam ity avibhāgatāṃ gatam| prāptacaitanyopagraharūpatvād bauddhasya pratyaysya| sa itthaṃ bhūtaḥ puruṣasya bhoga ity ākhyāyate||

[180,15] tasmād bhogasvarūpād baduddhāt pratyayād vivicya tatsāmṣiṇo dṛśirūpasya svarūpāvadhāraṇaṃ bhoktṛsvarūpād avadhāryamāṇād uttarakālam apy apavṛttasya viśeṣābhāvād apvarga^ ity ucyate| sarvadā svarūpapratiṣṭYho hi (tadā) sa^ iti| vṛttisārūpyam itaratra viśeṣa^ iti||

[180,19] dvayor bhogāpavargayor atiriktam anyat tṛtīyaṃ darśanaṃ nāsti| yāvad avidyā tāvad iṣṭāniṣṭe sta^ iti bhoga^ eva| puruṣasya tv aheyānupādeyasvarūpatvāt tadavadhāraṇe kuto bhoga^ iti bhogarūpasyāvidyālakṣaṇasya pratyayasya pratipakṣaḥ pratyayaḥ 
puruṣāvadhāraṇam/ yasmād etad ata^ etad darśanadvayavyatirekeṇa tṛtīyaṃ arśanāntaraṃ na kalpayituṃ śakyate||

[180,24] tathā ca^ uktam ayaṃ tu khalv evaṃ samyagdarśī triṣu guṇeṣūktalakṣaṇeṣu kartuṣu sarvavyāpāranirvṛttikareṣv akartāri ca guṇadharmavilakṣaṇe puruṣe caturthe tatkriyāsākṣiṇi guṇakriyāsūpadraṣṭari sandhikriyāsākṣibhūtopadraṣṭṛvad upānīyamānān antaḥklaraṇalakṣaṇair guṇaiḥ prāpyamāṇān darśitaviṣayatvāt sarvabhāvān (181,9) upapannān gauṇān eva bauddhapratyayākāreṇa pariniṣpnnān paśyan upalabhamāno nāhaṃ na mamety evaṃ na darśanam ito 'nyat sambhavati samyagdarśanam iti na śaṅkate| nāsty evānyat samyagdarśanam iti manyate||

[181,12] tāvatau bhogāpavargau buddhikṛtau buddhinirvartikau buddhāv eva vartamānau| anyakṛtasyāpy anyatra vṛttir dṛṣṭā yathā rajakakṛto vastrarāga iti kathaṃ puruṣe^ apadiśyete^ iti tau buddhāv eva vartamānau 
puruṣe 'padiśyete^ iti kuta^ etat sa hi tat-tat-phalasya bhokteti smyaṅmithyāpratyayor upalabdheti||

[181,16] dṛṣṭāntena vyākhgyātam artham nigamayati yathā vijayaḥ parājayo vā^ iti| buddher evārthāparisamāptir bandha^ iti| yāvat samāptau kartavyau bhogā apavargāv iti buddhir manyate tāvad bandhaḥ| tadarthāvasāyo mokṣaḥ| tasyā eva buddher arthāvasāyaḥ kartavyasamāptiḥ| yadā^ ātmavilakṣaṇaṃ bandhamokṣāsambandhināṃ puruṣam īkṣite nāsya kartavyam astīti sa kartavyāvasāyo buddher mokṣa iti||

[181,21] etena grahaṇadhāraṇavijñāna-ūhāpohakriyā-vacana-yathānyāyāvadhāraṇābhiniveśāḥ 
puruṣe'dhyāropitasadbhāvā iti jñeyāḥ| pratyayatvāviśeṣāt buddhidharmāḥ buddhāv eva vartamānāḥ buddhikṛtāḥ puruṣe 'dhyāropitasadbhāvāḥ puruṣo gṛhṇāti puruṣo dhārayatīty evameva puruṣe^ adhyāropyante||

[182,11] grahaṇaṃ śabda^ādīnāṃ śrotrādibhir upādānam| gṛhītānām avismaraṇaṃ dhāraṇam| dhāritasya sāmānyaviśeṣāvadhāraṇaṃ vijñānam| vijñātasya^ eva punas tad-gataviśeṣāvabubhutsayohanmūhaḥ ūhitasya viruddhānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ vyudasanam apohaḥ| viṣayesu pravṛttiḥ kriyā| vākyaviṣayo vṛttiviśeṣaś cittasya vacanam| yo yo nyāyo yathānyāyaṃ yasya ca vastuno yathiavopapattis tathaivāvadhāraṇaṃ yathānyāyāvadhāraṇām| tatra 
punaḥ punaḥ samyakpratyayāvicalanāya yaś cittasyābhiyogaḥ so 'abhiniveśaḥ| ta ete grahaṇādayaḥ puruṣe 'dhyāropitasadbhāvāḥ| sa hi tat-phalasya bhokteti grahaṇādiphalasya^ upalabdheti ||2.18||

[182,19] dṛśyānāṃ guṇānāṃ yathāvyākhyātātmanāṃ svarūpabhedāvadhāraṇārthaṃ tattvāvasthābhedāvadhāraṇārtham idaṃ sūtram ārabhyate viśeṣāviśeṣaliṅgamātrāliṅgā guṇaparvāṇaḥ| viśeṣāś cāviśeṣāś ca liṅgamātraṃ cāliṅgaś ca na liṅgati kutaś cinnāgacchati pradhānākhyo^ artha iti pacād yac sarvadhātubhya^ iti yaugikas sannabhidheyaliṅgaṃ bhajate| yo vā liṅgaṃ na vidyate liṅgam avagamanaṃ paricchedaḥ pratyakṣeṇa 
pradhānākhyasyārthasyāsāv aliṅga^ iti bahuvrīhiḥ||

[182,26] te ca viśeṣādayaḥ puruṣābhiprāyaṃ pālayanti bhogāpavargapradānena 
pūrayanti vā vikārān svān iti piparteḥ anyebhyo api dṛśyante^ iti kartari vanipratyayaḥ| tathā ca sati yannāntam akartari tannapuṃsakam| yat tu nāntuṃ kartari tatsarvaliṅgaṃ (183,1) bhavati yathā berudṛśvā merudṛśvanīti| tataś ca viśeṣādayaḥ parvāṇaḥ| keṣāṃ te sambandhina^ iti guṇānāṃ parvāṇo guṇaparvāṇaḥ| keṣāṃcid ayam eva pāṭho viśeṣāviśeṣaliṅgamātrāliṅkāni guṇaparvāṇīti||

[183,4] tatrākāśavāyvagnijalabhūmayo bhūtāni| śabdatanmātrārabdhamākāśam /sparśatanmātreṇa śabdasparśalakṣaṇenārabdho vāyuḥ| rūpatanmātreṇaśabdasparśarūpalakṣaṇenārabdho'gniḥ| rasatanmātreṇaśabdasparśarūparasacatuṣṭayalakṣaṇenāpāarabdhāḥ| gandhatanmātreṇaśabdasparśarūparasagandhapañcatayalakṣaṇena bhūmirārabdhā ||

[183,8] nanu ca nityamākāśam, prāgutpattervināśakālaṃ ca viśeṣābhāvāt| yo yautpattimāṃstasya tasya pṛthivyāādeḥ prāgutpattervināśottarakālaṃ caviśeṣo dṛṣṭaḥ| tathā ākāśaṃ yadyutpattimat syāt
prāgutpattervināśottarakālaṃ ca bhvedviśeṣaḥ| na ca so 'sti /tasmādākācamanutpattikam ||

[183,12] athāvakāśadānābhāavo viśeṣa iti cenna, mūrtimato 'nyasyābhāvāt tulyaṃnityatve 'api/ anityalakṣaṇābhāvācca nityamākāśam, saragatatvādātmādivat||

[183,14] na---āgamavirodhāt| tasmād vā etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ sambhūtaḥityāgamavirodhaḥ| smṛtivirodhaś ca| avakāśaṃ kurvati tadupacāra iticenna, vāyvādiṣv apyupacāraprasaṅgāt| ākāśād vāyuḥ sambhūtaityekavākyatvāt ||

[183,17] prāgutpatter viśeṣābhāvād anityo na bhavatīti ca bruvatā viśeṣābhāvānnityatvam ityuktaṃ bhavati| tathā ca saty ātmādīnām apibandhamokṣopalabdhyādiviśeṣābhāvāt sato vaiśeṣikasya anityatvaprasaṅgaḥ/ tathā paramāṇvādīnāṃ cānityatvaṃ prasajyeta ||

[183,21] athāpyutpattimata eva prāgūrdhvaṃ ca viśeṣabhāvābhāvau vivakṣitāviticet---na---ākāśasyotpattyabhyupagamaprasaṅgāt| athākāśotpattivādināviśeṣo vaktuṃ na śakyata iticet---na---puruṣārthasādhanāsādhanatvaviśeṣāt| sarvagatatvāditicāsiddho hetuḥ ||

[184,1] asarvagatāni kākacigākāśamanāṃsy anityāni ca svārthābhāve 
parārthatvātghaṭādivat| utpattimanti ca pradhānupuruṣābhyām anyatvāt ghaṭādivat| evam ākāśaṃ [utpattimad anityaṃ ca] bāhyendriyaviṣayatvāt ghaṭādivat| bhūtatvāc ca pṛthivīvat| anityadravyasaṃyogotādyaguṇatvāt| 
pṛthivyādivat| vcikitsānivartyaprakopakaratvāt vāyuvat| nabhaś ca 
prakopaś cikitsānirvartanīyaś cikitsakaprasiddhim upārūḍhaḥ| etenānityasādharmyaṃ vyākhyātam||

[184,7] etāni ca bhūtāni śabdasparśarūparasagandhatanmātrāṇām aviśeṣāṇāṃ viśeṣāḥ| śāntaghoramūḍhādy anekaviśeṣavattvād viśeṣāḥ| śāntādyavhāvāc cāviśeṣāḥ tan-mātrāṇi||

[184,10] tathā śrotratvakcakṣur jihvāghrāṇāni śabdādiviṣayabuddhyarthatvād buddhīndiryāṇi svaviṣayabuddhibhir evānumitāni| vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthāni karmendriyāṇi vacanādikriyānirvartakāni tadanimitāny eva| na vādādīni karmendriyāṇi siddhānīti cen na śrutiprasiddhatvāt| indriyāṇi prakṛtya sarveṣāṃ vedānāṃ vāgekāyanam ityādiśruteḥ||

[184,16] smṛtiṣu ca sarvatraiva vidhipratiṣedhaviṣayapravṛttinivṛttimatvāc ca śrotrādivad indryatvam| na vākyacapalo na pāṇipādacapalaḥ ityāder vidhipratiṣedhaviṣayapravṛttinivṛttimattvasiddhiḥ||

[184,19] tathā vāgāḍīnīndriyāṇi kauśalavattvāt cakṣurādivat| sarvagātreṣu cendriyaśaktiḥ karaṇavṛttyāḥ sandarśanād iṣyata^ eva| vāggudaprabhṛtīnāṃ sarpādīnāṃ mūkaṣaṇḍānāṃ ca mukham evopasthendriyam| ekādaśaṃ manaḥ sarvārtham atītānāgatavartamānaviṣayārthaṃ buddhīndriyakarmendriyaviṣayārthaṃ ca||

[184,23] etāny ekādaśendriyāṇy asmitā-lakṣaṇasyāsmibhāvo 'smitā^ asmīti pratyayaḥ sa ca lakṣaṇaṃ yasya so 'smitalakṣaṇo 'smitāpratyayena hi lakṣyate liṅgyate ṣaṣṭham aviśeṣākhyaṃ tattvam ahaṅkāraḥ| tasyāviśeṣasya viśeṣā etāni(184,26) guṇānām aviśeṣākārasaṃsthitānāṃ sattvādīnām eṣa ṣodaśako viśeṣapariṇāmaḥ sattvabahulā pṛthivī grāhyatvāt| tām asattve apy eṣāṃ guṇapradhānakṛto viśeṣaḥ| (185,12) karaṇāni prakāśatvāt sattvabahulāni manaḥṣaṣṭhāni tu buddhīndriyāṇi prakāśaprādhānyāt sattvabahulāni| karnebdruyāṇi kriyāprādhānyād rajo-bahulāni ||

[185,14] ṣaḍ-aviśeṣāḥ| tadyathā śabdamātraṃ sparśamātram iti dvilakṣaṇasyāpi pradhānena vyapadeśaḥ| tathā rūpamātram iti trilakṣaṇasyāpi pradhānena vyapadeśaḥ| tathottarayor api mātraśabdaḥ śāntaghorādiviśeṣavyāvartanārthaḥ| ekadvitricatuḥpañcalakṣaṇabhedāḥ śabdatattvaikalakṣaṇaḥ śabdatanmātrabhedaḥ| śabdasparśalakṣaṇaḥ sparśatanmātrabhedaḥ ity evam ekaikādhikyenottaratrāpi draṣṭavyam| ṣaṣṭhaś cāviśeṣo 'smitamātraḥ śabdāditanmātrāpekṣayā ṣaṣṭha indriyāṇām ekādaśānām aviśeṣaḥ||

[185,20] ete sattvamātrasyāstitvamātrasya liṅgamātrasya mūrtimātrasya 
pradhānasyāmūrtasya prathamā mūrtir iyaṃ yathā bījasyāṅkurībhāvaḥ| ātmano vasturūpasya mahataḥ sarvaviśeṣāviśeṣebhyo mahattvāt ṣaḍ-aviśeṣapariṇāmāḥ ṣaḍ-aviśeṣā eva pariṇāmāḥ sattvādīnāṃ mahadrūpāvasthitānām amī aviśeṣapariṇāmāḥ ||

[185,24] yat tat paraṃ sūkṣmam aviśeṣebhyo liṅgamātraṃ mahattattvam tasminn ete sattāmātre mahattvānmahaty avasthāya kāraṇasvarūpe mahaty avasthāya vivṛddhikāṣṭhāṃ pṛthivyādiviśeṣāntatām anubhavanti||

[186,14] pratisaṃsṛjyamānāś ca pratilīyamānāś cāviśeṣāḥ tasminn eva sattāmātre liṅgamātre mahaty ātmany avasthāyāvyaktaṃ prati yānti| kiṃ-viśiṣṭam nissattāsattvaṃ viśeṣāviśeṣadharmarahitam| nissad asad iti avyaktādīṣadvikṛtam iti sat aviśeṣebhyaḥ sūkṣmataram iti cāsat sac cāsac ca sad asad iti mahattattvam ucyate| tad-dharmarahitam avyaktaṃ nissad asad iti||

[186,19] eṣa teṣāṃ guṇānāṃ sattvādīnāṃ liṅgamātraḥ pariṇāmaḥ yasminn avasthāya vivṛddhikāṣṭhāṃ pratilayaṃ ca pratiyānty aviśeṣāḥ| nissattasattaṃ cāvyaktaṃ ca yat sa teṣām eva guṇānām aliṅgapariṇāmaḥ| 
paraś ca na sūkṣmo 'vasthāntarapariṇāmo 'sti guṇānām| sūkṣmaviṣayatvaṃ cāliṅgaparyavasānam iti hi vyākhgyātam||

[186,23] aliṅgatvād evāliṅgāvasthāyāṃ na puruṣārtho hetuḥ| aliṅgāvasthānimittaṃ na puruṣārtho hetuḥ nāliṅgāvasthā puruṣārthaprayuktetyarthaḥ| tad etat vyācaṣṭe nāliṅgāvasthāyām ādau puruṣārthatā kāraṇam| kasmāt punas tadavasthā puruṣārthaprayuktā na bhavatīti tata^ āha yasmān nāsyāṃ puruṣārthatā kāraṇaṃ vidyate svayam api nasau puruṣārthatā mahadādivad asaṃvedyatvād īśvarāṇām api ||

[187,4] yataś caitad evaṃ tasmān nityā vyākhyāyate| yo hi puruṣārthaprayuktaḥ so 'nityo dṛṣṭo yathā pṛthivyādiḥ| na caiṣā pradhānāvasthā puruṣārthaprayukteti nityā vyākhyāyate||

[187,7] trayāṇāṃ tv avasthāviśeṣāṇāṃ viśeṣāviśeṣaliṅgamātrāṇām ādau puruṣārthatā mahadādyavasthā puruṣārthaprayuktā| sa cārthaḥ sa ca puruṣārtho hetur nimittaṃ kāraṇam ity anityā vyākhyāyante mahadādyastisno 'vasthāḥ||

[187,10] guṇās tu sarvadharmānupātinaḥ sarvavikārānupātinaḥ| yathā sarpaḥ 
prāṃśutvasarpaṇakuṇḍalatvādiviśeṣānupātī tathā viśeṣāditattvānupātino guṇāḥ piṇḍapratyayānupātinaś ca na pralīyante na^ upajāyante| yathā sarpaḥ kuṇḍalāditve pralīyamāne na pralīyate tad upajane ca na^ upajāyate tathā^ eva guṇā^ api| yathā ca ghaṭapiṭharādiṣu 
pralīyamāneṣu jāyamāneṣu ca mṛnna pralīyate nāpi jāyate||

[187,15] guṇā vyaktibhir eva dharmair evātītānāgatavyayāgam avayībhistrilakṣaṇābhir guṇānvayībhir guṇopādānakāraṇābhir upajanāpāyadharmakā ivotpattipralayavanta^ iva pratyavabhāsante na svarūpeṇa vinaśyanti jāyante vā ||

[187,18] etasya^ evārthasya dṛṣṭāntam āha devadatto daridrāti| kasmāt yenāsya mriyante gāva iti| gavām eva maraṇāt tasya daridrāṇaṃ na svarūpahānād iti samaḥ samādhiḥ tulyaś caroco dṛṣṭāntena dārṣṭāntikasya devadattadaridrāṇavad eva guṇānugatābhir vyaktibhir mahadādibhir guṇavirmardabavaucitryakṛtābhir vivardhamānābhir guṇānām āḍhyatā tat-pralaye ca teṣāṃ daridrāṇaṃ na svarūpeṇāpacaya^ upacayo vā^ iti tulyaḥ samādhir iti||

[187,24] sarvasya pradhānakāryatve sati viśeṣā^ aviśeṣā vā kasmād anantaraṃ na^ upajāyanta^ iti tad āha liṅgasyāliṅgaṃ pratyāsannaṃ yathā mūlaṃ vṛkṣasya pratyāsannaṃ bīje saṃsṛṣṭaṃ mūlam eva prathamataraṃ vivicyte na vṛkṣasyottarāvasthā| evaṃ tatra (188,11) pradhāne tat liṅgaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ saṃlīnaṃ prathamaṃ vīdyate^ āvirbhavati nottare viśeṣā viprakṛṣṭatvād eva| tad eva^ āha kramānatipatteḥ kramānatikramāt| na hi loke kasyacid utpadyamānasya kramātilaṅghanam asti||

[188,14] tathā ṣaḍ-aviśeṣāḥ pūrvoktā liṅgamātre pratyāsannatvāt saṃsṛṣṭatvād vivicyante| kasmād ity āha pariṇāmakramaniyamāt| tathā teṣv aviśeṣeṣu bhūtendriyāṇi| tan-mātreṣu bhūtāni saṃsṛṣṭāni vivicyante| asmitāmātre^ indriyāṇi manassahitāni||

[188,18] tathā ca^ ukta purastāt| na viśeṣebhyaḥ prāg uktebhya ākāśādibhyaḥ paraṃ tattvāntaram asti| na viśeṣāṇām anyas tattvāntarapariṇāma^ ity arthaḥ| etad eva^ āha viśeṣāṇāṃ nāsti tattvāntaram iti tattvaṃ hi nāma sādhāraṇaṃ sarvaprāṇadhāriṇām ā mahāpralayasthāyi| na^ evaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pṛthivyādibhya^ utpadyamānam anyad asti| dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmās tu bhavanty eva^ iti| teṣāṃ tu viśeṣāṇāṃ dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāṃāvyākhyāyiṣyante tṛtīyapāde ||2.19||

[188,24] vyākhgyātam dṛśyam avadhṛtaṃ dṛśyam| yad^ arthaṃ ca dṛśyate tasya^ idānīṃ draṣṭuḥ svarūpāvadhāraṇārtham idam ārabhyate draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ śuddho api pratyayānupaśyaḥ draṣṭā dṛśyasya yathāvyākhyātarūpasya^ upalabdhā puruṣaḥ| tasyedaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ dṛśimātraḥ (189,5) śuddhe api 
pratyayānupaśyaś ceti| punastād ākhyātam tadā draṣṭuḥ svarūpe'vasthānam vṛttisārūpyam itaratra^ iti ca tad eva dvayam iha pratipādyate||

[189,7] pratisaṃvedī buddheḥ puruṣa^ ity upapādayiṣyāma^ iti taccātropapādyate| dṛśir darśanam upalabdhis tāvan-mātro dṛśimātraḥ| yata evaṃ tasmāc chuddhaḥ| śuddho api sann asau bauddhasya 
pratyayasyānupaśyaḥ dṛśimātratvād eva| tasmāt tam anupaśyan tatsvarūpo bhavati| dṛśisannidhau vṛtter dṛśisvarūpāvabhāsatā draṣṭur vṛttisārūpyam||

[189,11] dṛśimātrasya^ evāvikriyātmakasya sato^ asya bauddhapratyayānudarśanaṃ yasmāt tasmāc ca svarūpapratiṣṭhatā ca siddhā| 
pratyayānupaśya^ iti| etat tu tad astitvānumānam| katham ghaṭādīnāṃ dṛśyānāṃ svarūpavyatiriktenānyena dṛśyatvadarśanāt tat-prakāśakānāṃ ca ālokādīnāṃ vyatiriktadṛśyatvāt sarvārthāvabhāsakānām api pratyayānāṃ svarūpavyatiriktadṛśyatvaṃ dṛśyatvād avagamyate ghaṭāditadālokādivad iti||

[189,16] yasmāc ca bauddham eva pratyayam anupaśyati taṃ ca paśyaty eva na kadācit svavisayaṃ pratyayaṃ na paśyati tasmād dṛśimātraḥ| mātragrahaṇaṃ dharmāntaranirvartanārtham| etena draṣṭur dṛśir ity etan nirvartitam iddhādisamānāśrayatvaṃ ca nirvartitaṃ dṛśer eva draṣṭṛtvāt| tad etad ucyate dṛśimātra^ iti dṛkchaktir eva dṛg-darśanaṃ tad eva śaktir dṛkśhaktiḥ| nāpy anyo dṛkchaktimān asti| sā^ eva śaktir dṛg iti| tatra mātraśabdārthaṃ vyācaṣṭe viśeṣeṇāparāmṛṣṭety arthaḥ| viśeṣeṇa kenacid icchādinā guṇair guṇadharmaiś cāparāmṛṣṭaḥ||

[189,22] sa puruṣo buddheḥ pratiṣaṃvedituṃ śīlam asya^ iti pratisaṃvedī| bauddhapratyayopalabdhisvabhāva^ ity arthaḥ| puruṣo buddheḥ pratisaṃvedī dṛśimātraḥ śuddhaś ceti kathaṃ gamyata^ ity āha sa buddher na sarūpo nātyantaṃ virūpa^ iti| na samānalakṣaṇo nāpy atyantavidharvety etat| asarūpatayā dṛśimātratvaṃ śuddhatvaṃ ca^ āha| nātyantavirūpa^ iti vṛttisārūpyatām āha||

[190,5] kathaṃ punaḥ na sarūpatā^ iti^ ucyate na tāvat sarūpaḥ| kasmāt ko hetuḥ| buddhes tāvat svarūpam vaidharmyadarśanārtham āha jñātājñātaviṣayatvāt pariṇāminī buddhir iti| kaḥ punar asau buddher iṣayo yo jñātaś cājñātaś ca| yena bāhyākāreṇa pariṇatā buddhiḥ sa jñātaḥ| yena^ ākāreṇa na pariṇatā so 'jñātaḥ ||

[190,10] yadi na pariṇametāpariṇamamānā ced viṣayaṃ vikhyāpayet sadā^ eva viṣayitvaṃ buddheḥ syāt| tasmāt jñātājñātaviṣayatā pariṇāmitvaṃ khyāpayati| pariṇāminī buddhiḥ jñātājñātaviṣayatvāt| dīpacakṣurādi(viṣaya)vat||

[190,13] tadvilakṣaṇaḥ puruṣaḥ sadā jñātaviṣayaḥ| sarvadā hi jñāta^ eva 
puruṣasya svo viṣayaḥ| tasmāt sadā jñātaviṣayatvaṃ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvaṃ 
paridīpayati| sadā jñāṣtaviṣayatvam asiddhaṃ puruṣasya^ ity āśaṅkyāha kasmāt ko hetuḥ na hīti hiśabdaḥ prasiddhāvadyotanārthaḥ| buddhiś ca nāma buddhir iti bauddhapratyayaḥ puruṣasya viṣayaḥ syād agṛhītā gṛhītā ceti| sarvadā ca gṛhīta^ eva bauddhaḥ pratyayaḥ| tad avyabhicāreṇa^ eva 
puruṣasya vyatiriktatvasiddhiḥ| tasmāt puruṣasya sadā jñātaviṣayatvam| tataś cāpariṇāmitvaṃ iddham| apariṇāmī puruṣa^ iti pratijñā| sadā jñātaviṣayatvād iti hetuḥ| vaidharmyadṛṣṭānto buddhyādiḥ ||

[190,21] nanu ca buddhyā vyavasitam arthaṃ puruṣaś cetayati buddheś ca sarvo viṣayo jñātaś cājñātaś ca puruṣasyāpi samānaḥ| jñātājñātaviṣayatvaṃ buddhyā vyavasitārthopalambhitvāt| tasmāt 
puruṣasyāpi pariṇāmitvaṃ jñātājñātaviṣayatvād buddhivad eva||

[190,24] na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| buddher eva puruṣaviṣayatvāt| buddhir eva hi bāhyārthākārā puruṣasya viṣayo nārthaḥ kevalaḥ svarūpeṇa| tasmānnityā na jñātājñātaviṣayaḥ puruṣaḥ| buddhis tu sadā^ eva jñātā^ eva^ iti na 
pariṇāmitvaṃ puruṣasya||

[190,27] nanu ca buddhyā vyavasitam arthaṃ puruṣaś cetayatīti kaścid āha| tat katham ucyate na tair apy apariṇāmitvasyābhyupagamāt 
puruṣasya| buddhyā vyavasitam artham ity etad(191,6) uktaṃ bhavati buddhyākāraṃ cetayati buddhivyavasāyākārāpannam arthaṃ cetayatīti| anyathā hi teṣāṃ pariṇāmitvaparārthatvasaṃhatyakāritvādayo buddhidharmāḥ sarva^ eva prasajeyuḥ||

[191,9] athāpariṇāmitve darśanānupapattir iti cet na darśanasya^ eva 
puruṣasvarūpatāt| yo hi puruṣapariṇāmitvam abhimanyate sa^ idam (pra)draṣṭavyaḥ kiṃ pariṇāmena kriyata^ iti||

[191,12] sa ced abhidadhīta darśanam iti| sa pratyabhidhānīyaḥ puruṣasya darśanam eva rūpaṃ ataḥ pariṇāmānarthakyam iti||

[191,14] sa cet pratibrūyāt na puruṣasya^ upalabdhir bhogaḥ kasmāt tarhi vikriyā^ eva bhoga^ iti| sa punar vaktavyaḥ tathāpi vikriyāvattvād anityatvam upanataṃ śarīrādivad eva||

[191,16] athāpi pratibhāṣeta svātmani vikriyā nānityatvakāraṇaṃ syāt kiṃ tv avasthāntarabhāvikriyā hi kāraṇam anityatvasyeti| sa punaḥ 
pratibhāṣitavyaḥ vikriyāvasthāyā evāvasthāntaratvāt| pūrvam avikṛtasya vastunaḥ punar vikriyā^ avasthāntaram||

[191,20] kiṃ ca viṣayābhyupagame ca vikriyā^ eva tāvatā niyamaḥ pāryate na kartum| na cāpi svātmani vikriyamāṇānāṃ vajravaidūryādīnām anityatvaṃ nāsti| na ca vikriyājanitasya^ eva darśanasya dṛśyaviṣayatvaṃ na nityasya darśanasya^ iti niyamahetur asti| vikriyā^ eva^ upalabdhir na vikriyājaniteti cet na śarīrādīnāṃ vikriyāvatām upalabdhiprasaṅgāt||

[191,25] tasmād dṛśimātra^ evāpariṇāmī ca sadā jñātaviṣayatvāt puruṣa iti siddhaṃ| tasya ca ntyopalabdhisvarūpatve api dṛśyabauddhapratyayabhāvābhāvakṛto 'yaṃ vyapadeśaḥ paśyati darśayiṣyati dadarśeti ca| savitrādipakāśanavyapadeśavat| (192,11) yathā prakāśayai savitā prakāśayiṣyati prācīkaśād iti ca prakāśyabhāvābhāvakṛto vyapadeśaḥ| śuddhatvādayo hetavo vaidharmyeṇ abuddhyādayo dṛṣṭāntāḥ||

[192,13] itaś cāsarūpaḥ| sarvārthādhyavasāyitvād dhoraśāntādisamastārthāvasāyitvāt triguṇā buddhiḥ śrotrādivat| guṇānāṃ tūpadraṣṭety upadraṣṭṛtvaṃ hetuḥ| atriguṇaḥ purusa^ iti pratijñā| vaidharmyeṇ abuddhyādir eva dṛṣṭānto 'triguṇatvāc cāsarūpaḥ||

[192,16] astu tarhi virūpa^ eva| tataś cātyantavirūpatvād bhogānupapattir iti| atrocyate nātyantaṃ virūpaḥ| kasmāt ko 'tra hetuḥ na hi śuddhasya buddhisvarūpavirūpasyāśuddhabauddhapratyayād atyantavairūpyasiddhaye hetur ity abhiprāyaḥ||

[192,19] na buddhipratyayasākṣitvasya siddhatvāt| yataḥ śuddho^ apy asau bauddhaṃ pratyayam anupaśyati tac ca bauddhapratyayānudarśanaṃ sādhitam| anupaśyatīti viṣayakārapariṇāme sati bauddhaṃ pratyayaṃ paśyatīty arthaḥ| tam anupaśyann api na tadātmā na triguṇaḥ| tadātmaka^ iva śāntaghoramūḍhavyavasāyarūpa^ iva pratyavabhāsate| tasmānnityā nātyantavirūpaḥ pravṛttisārūpyāt| tatra yad uktaṃ buddheḥ pratisaṃvedī puruṣa ity upapādayiṣyāmaḥ iti tad upapāditam| tathā ca tatsārūpyam ādarśitam| tathā^ uktaṃ tantre (193,7) apariṇāminī bhoktṛ-śaktiḥ dṛkśhaktir yathāvyākhyātarūpā^ apratisaṅkramā ca pariṇāminy arthe bauddhe pratyaye 
pratisaṅkrānteva pratyayaviṣayatvāt tadvṛttim anupatantīvāsyā buddher vṛttim anupatantīva draṣṭṛtvād upalabhmānā| bauddhaḥ pratyayo jāyamāna^ eva dṛkśhakteḥ karmatām āpadyate| tam anupatanty upalabhamānā bhoktṛ-śaktiḥ tasyāś ca prāptacaitanyopagraharūpāyāḥ prāptaṃ caitanyopagraharūpaṃ 
puruṣopagraharūpaṃ yayā buddhivṛttyā^ āsāditaṃ 
pāvakopagraharūpataptalohapiṇḍavad dṛśikarmatvād evānukāramātratayā buddhivṛttisārūpyamātratayā buddhivṛttyaviśiṣṭā jñavṛttir iti jña evopalabdhir eva vṛttir ity ākhyāyate||2.20||

[193,15] tadartha^ eva dṛśyasyātmā tacchabdena draṣṭā dṛśimātraḥ śuddho yathādhigatātmā pratyāmnāyate| tadarthaḥ asmai tadarthas tatprayojanaḥ dṛśikarmatvārtha^ ity arthaḥ| bhogarūpeṇāpavargarūpeṇa ca| ko 'sāv ity āha dṛśyasyātmā pradhānasyātmā svarūpam svarūpalābha^ iti| 

[193,19] nanu caturvyūhatvaṃ vyācikhyāsitaṃ kimartham etad aprakṛtam ucyate| na^eṣa doṣaḥ| ātyantikaduḥkhanivṛttiprayojanasya prakṛtatvāt| kathaṃ dṛśyatādarthyalābhāvadhāraṇenātyantikī duḥkhaviratir uktā bhaved iti| ucyate yadi dṛśyasvarūpapratilṃbhas tadartha^ eva tadā aśeṣatadarthaviratau punaḥ kṛtārthatvāt kāryābhāvād ātyantikadṛśyaviraktiḥ syāt| tasya punaḥ pravṛttau prayojanābhāvāt| na hi kṛtam eva punaḥ karaṇīyakam upāśnute| atha tu svārtho api dṛśyasya^ ātmalāmho 'bhaviṣyad ataḥ svārthasya bhāvāt punaḥ punaḥ pravartiṣyata| tataś ca na^ ātyantikī nivṛttiḥ| tasmāt tādarthyam evovadhāryate ||

[193,27] dṛśyātmalābhasya svārthābhāvaś ca dṛśyasya bhogyatvāt odanādivat siddhaḥ acetanatvāt saṃhatyakāritvāc ca| tadāha dṛśirūpasya puruṣasya karmaviṣayatām āpannaṃ dṛśyam iti| itiśabdo hetvarthe| tadartha^ eva dṛśyasyātmābhavati svarūpam bhavatītyarthaḥ| puruṣārtha^ eva 
pradhānasyātmalābho dṛśikarmatāpatter odanādivat||

[194,12] sūtraprayojanam ācaṣṭa^ idānīm tataś ca tādarthyasya niyatatvāt kāraṇāt puruṣarūpeṇa pratilabdhātmakaṃ prāptacaitanyopagraharūpaṃ sadbhogāpavargārthatāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ puruṣeṇa na dṛśyate| bhogāpavargavyatirekeṇa kartavyābhāvāt||2.21||

[194,15] sūtrasambandhaṃ karoti yaṃ puruṣaṃ prati caritārthaṃ dṛśyaṃ taṃ 
prati dṛśikarmatāṃ na^ āpatsyata^ iti svarūpahānam ataś ca svarūpahānād asya vināśaḥ prāptaḥ| na tu vinaśyati sarvātmanā| kasmāt ko hetuḥ 
pradhānānāṃ pratipuruṣaṃ bahutvāt kṛtārthaṃ prati naṣṭam eva^ iti manyate||

[194,19] kṛtārthaṃ prati naṣṭam apy anaṣṭaṃ tad anyasādhāraṇatvāt| kṛtārthaṃ yaṃ puruṣaṃ prati bhogāpavarhanirvartanena kṛtaprayojanaṃ tam ekaṃ puruṣaṃ prati punaḥ kāryakaraṇarūpeṇāpravartanād bhāvābhāvayor aviśeṣān naṣṭam api| asya vyākhyānaṃ nāśaprāptam apīti| anaṣṭaṃ tadanyapuruṣasādhāraṇatvāt| yān praty akṛtārthaṃ tān praty anaṣṭam||

[194,23] katham anaṣṭam ity āha kuśalapuruṣān prati nāśaprāptaṃ bhogāpavargapradānena kuśalapuruṣān prati kṛtaprayonajam| akuśalapuruṣān 
prati^ akṛtārthaṃ bhogāpavargayoḥ kartavyayor akṛtatvāt teṣām akuśalānāṃ dṛśeḥ ta eva dṛśis tasyāḥ karmatāṃ dṛśyatām āpannaṃ labhata^ eva 
puruṣarūpeṇa puruṣanirvhāsatayā^ ātmarūpam iti| ataś ca sādāraṇatvād ekam eva pradhānaṃ pṛthivyādivat| kāryakaraṇānāṃ cābhinnatvāt puruṣāṇāṃ nānātvaṃ siddham| sukhaduḥkhanānātvāc ca puruṣabhedasiddhiḥ||

[195,12] ataś ca dṛgdarśanaśaktyor nityatvād anādiḥ saṃyogo vyākhyātaḥ| tathā ca^ uktaṃ tantre dharmiṇām anādisaṃyogāt guṇānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ cānādisaṃyogād dharmamātrāṇāṃ mahadādikāryakāraṇāntānām api puruṣair anādiḥ saṃyogo dharmibhir dharmāṇām abhinnatvāt||2.22||

[195,16] dṛśyam avadhṛtam| draṣṭā cāvadhṛtaḥ| draṣṭṛdṛśyayoḥ saṃyogo heyahetur iti ca^ uktam| sa ca saṃyogo 'vadhārayitavyaḥ kīdṛśa^ iti| tataś ca saṃyuogasvarūpāvadhāraṇārtham idaṃ sūtraṃ pravavṛte svasvāmiśaktyoḥ svarūpopalabdhihetuḥ saṃyogaḥ| svasvāmiśaktyoḥ buddhipuruṣayoḥ svarūpopalbadher hetuḥ kāraṇam| yasmin sati tayoḥ svarūpam^ upalabhyate vadanadarpaṇayor iva sa tayoḥ svarūpopalabdhihetuḥ| saṃyogaḥ dṛṣṭṛdṛśyasvarūpakāryānumeya^ ity arthaḥ||

[195,22] pramāṇaṃ cātra svasaṃyogapūrvikā buddhipuruṣayoḥ svarūpoplabdhiḥ viṣayaviṣayisvarūpopalabdhitvāt īkṣaṇadarpaṇasvarūpoplabdhivat| yā kācit sukhaduḥkhopalabdhir vivādagocarāpannā sā sukhasuḥkhahetusaṃyogaoūrvikā sukhaduḥkhopalabdhitvāt candanakānṭakasukhaduḥkhopalabdhivat||

[195,26] tad āha puruṣaḥ svāmī dṛśyena svena svayā buddhyā darśānaṛthaṃ bhogāpavargadarśanārthaṃ saṃyuktaḥ| darśanaprayukta^ eva saṃyoga^ ity arthaḥ||

[195,28] tasmāt saṃyogād darśanapratyuktā dṛśyasya^ upalabshiḥ aviśiṣṭā yā sa bhogaḥ| yā tu draṣṭuḥ svarūpopalabdhir viviktā so 'pavargaḥ| yasmād darśanakāryadvayaprayuktaḥ (196,8) saṃyogas tasmād darśanakāryāvasānaḥ saṃyogaḥ tena puruṣasaṃyogavad darśanaṃ viyogasya kāraṇam ity uktam| guṇapuruṣāntaradarśanaṃ pradhānapuruṣaviyogasya kāraṇam ity uktaṃ kāryasya darśanena paryavasitatvāt||

[196,11] saṃsārisaṃsārahetuyāthātmyadarśanaṃ duḥkhaduḥkhisaṃyogahetunivartakaṃ duḥkhiduḥkhahetuyāthātmyaviṣayatvāt rogirogahetuyāthātmyadarśanavat||

[196,13] avidyā draṣṭṛdṛśyayoḥ saṃyogakāraṇaṃ duḥkhanimittasaṃyogahetutvāt roggahetusaṃyoganimittamithyājñānavat| draṣṭṛdṛśyasaṃyogo mithyājñānapūrvakaḥ duḥkhahetutvāt rogirogahetusaṃyogavat| yasmād duḥkhiduḥkhahetuyāthātmyaviṣayaṃ darśanaṃ tasmāt tadanyathāviṣayasyādarśanasya pratidvandvam ity adarśanaṃ saṃyoganimittam ity uktaṃ tasya hetur avidyeti| duḥkhanimittatvāc ca saṃyogasya||

[196,19] yasmād adarśanam eva nivartayati darśanaṃ nānyatkāryaṃ karoti tasmān nātra darśanaṃ mokṣakāraṇam adarśanābhāvād evārthādarśanakāryasya duḥkhahetor bandhasyābhāvaḥ sa mokṣaḥ| na punar bandhābhāvavyatirekeṇabhāvayitavyo mokṣaḥ| tataś ca mokṣasya nityatvasiddhiḥ||

[196,23] darśanasya bhāve draṣṭṛdṛśyaviṣayasya darśanasya bhāve tadviṣayasyādarśanasya nāśaḥ| darśanādarśanayor ekatra virodhāt timioraravikiraṇajālayor iva| ato darśanaṃ kaivalyakāraṇam uktam vivekakhyātir aviplavā hānopāyaḥ iti||

[196,26] kiṃ cedam adarśanaṃ nāma  nanu ca^ uktam avidyā^ eva| sā cāmitrāgoṣpadavad iti vidyāviparyayeṇa vyākhyātā| satyam evaṃ kin tu śāstragatā vikalpā darśayiṣyante (197,7) svābhimatapakṣaniścīṣayā| 
paraparikalpitavastukhaṇḍimānam āpādayatā hi nijābhimatanirūpitavas tu draḍhimānam atitarām ānīyate||

[197,9] kiṃ cedam iti sarvair eva vikalpair vakṣyamāṇair abhisaṃbandhanīyam | kiṃ guṇānām ahikāra^ iti sāmānyena paripṛṣṭasya viśeṣaparyanuyogaḥ| adhikṛtir adhikāraḥ guṇānāṃ pravṛttiḥ||

[197,12] nanu guṇānām adhikārasya katham adarśanāśaṅkā ucyate^ arthadvāreṇa bhavaty evādarśanam adhikāraḥ| yāvad dhi guṇānāṃ pravṛttis tāvad bandhaḥ| bandhakāraṇam avidyeti sthitisthairyam ānīyate| śavdadvāreṇāpi darśanād anyad adarśanaṃ yadā bhavaty eva tadānīm āśaṅkā| guṇādhikāro hi darśanād anya^ eva^ iti ||

[197,16] āhosvid dṛśisvarūpasya svābino darśitaviṣayasya sittasyānutpādaḥ| iha tu pūrvapadārthapradhānatvam adarśanasya gṛgītvā śaṅkyate| cittasyānutpādaḥ prāg utpatter darśanasyābhāvaḥ| kim uktaṃ bhavaty anutpāda^ ity ata^ āha svasmin dṛśye tv arthadvāreṇa^ evāśaṅkā| aprayojanavato hi prayojanam avaśyakṛtyam iti||

[197,20] kim arthavattā guṇānāṃ bhogāpavargābhyāṃ yā guṇānām arthavattā tad adarśanam iti| atra tv arthadvāreṇa^ evāśaṅkā| prayojanavato hi 
prayojanam avaśyakṛtyam iti||

[197,22] kaḥ punar eṣu pakṣeṣu doṣaḥ ucyate yady adhikāro guṇānām adarśanaṃ vidyānarthakyaṃ prāptam| na hy asmin pakṣe vidyā pratyanīkabhūtā syād adarśanasya| yasmād vidyākārayor avirodhaḥ ataś canirmokṣaḥ 
prasaktaḥ| punaḥ punaḥ prayojanavattayā pravṛttisaṃbhavāt||

[197,26] guṇārthavattā^ adarśanam iti pakṣe apy eṣa^ eva doṣaḥ| tathā cittasyānutpādapakṣeapy eṣa^ eva| anutpādo hi darśanābhāvaḥ tasya cāvastutvād vidyayā nivṛttir anupapanneti| abhāve hi sati 
pratyanīkavidhānam anarthakam eva||

[198,8] athāvidyā svacittena saha niruddhā^ iti| atra hy avidyāyā adarśanasya ca kenacid aṃśena bhedaṃ manyamāno vikalpayati | katham aniruddhā hy avidyā dṛśyamānatvād darśanam eva| yā tu niruddhā cittena saha pralīnā svakāraṇe| svacittasyotpattibījam utpattikāraṇaṃ sā na dṛśyata^ ity adarśānam ity abhimānaḥ| tataś cānirodhāvasthāyā darśanatvaprasaṅga^ iti duṣṭa^ evāyaṃ pakṣaḥ syāt| atha tu nirodhānirodhāvasthāyor apy adarśanam eva tathā saty abhimatasiddhir ity adoṣa^ eva| kiṃ sthitisaṃskārakṣaye gatisaṃskārābhibyaktiḥ| kim etad adarśanam iti na saṃbadhyate| kathaṃ tarhi saṃbandhaḥ ucyate tatrāvidyā svacittasahaniruddhā cittasyotpattibījam ity uktaṃ tata^ idam arthā siddham cittaṃ svakāraṇe nirudhyate punaś ca^ utpadyata^ iti| tataś cedam api siddham arthāc cittasya kāraṇam api sthityā gatyā ca vartata^ iti| tataś ca^ etadvispaṣṭīkaraṇārthaṃ pṛcchati||

[198,19] sthityartho guṇānāṃ sāmyāvasthāhetuḥ saṃskāraḥ sthitisaṃskāraḥ| tasya kṣaye sthitisaṃskārakṣaye gatir mahadādytākāreṇa pravṛttis tadarthaḥ saṃskāro gatisaṃskāraḥ tasyābhibyaktir iti||

[198,22] yadi sthityā gatyā ca vartate tataḥ ko doṣaḥ syāt tadudāharaṇena darśayati yatredam uktaṃ pradhānaṃ sthityā^ eva vartamānaṃ vikārākaraṇāt mahadādyākāreṇāpariṇāṃād apradhānaṃ syāt| yataḥ pradhatte vikārān iti 
pradhānam| tena vikārāṇāṃ apradhātṛtvād apradhānaṃ syāt||

[198,26] tathā gatyā^ eva vartamānaṃ nityaṃ vikārarūpeṇa^ eva vartamānaṃ vikāranityatvān na vikārān pradhatta^ iti kāraṇatvahāner apradhānaṃ syāt| ubhayathā vṛttiḥ gatyā sthityā ca vartanaṃ vṛttir yasya pradhānasya tadubhayathāvṛtti vyavahāraṃ pradhānatvavyavahāraṃ kāraṇatvavyavahāraṃ labhatet| nānyathā pradhānatvaṃ pradhānasya| kāraṇāntareṣv api 
puruṣa-īśvaraparamāṇvādiṣu parakalpiṣu samānaś carcaḥ tulyaviṣayaḥ (199,10) nyāyaḥ prakṛta^ evādunā nirūpyate| darśanaśaktir evādarśanam ity eke| darśanasya śaktir anabhivyaktāvasthā bīja ivānabhivyaktāṅkurāvasthā tad etad adarśanam ity eke manyate| kutaḥ 
pradhānasyātmaprakhyāpanārthā prakhyāpanaṃ darśanam ākhyāyamānarūpatā tasyai tad adarśanam iti śruteḥ||

[199,14] asmin pakṣe tad-evānabhivyaktātmakaṃ śaktirūpam adarśanaṃ tad eva cābhivyaktaṃ darśanam iti svātmani virodhānupapatter darśanād adarśanasya nivṛttyanavak_.lptiḥ||

[199,16] tathā darśanāntaraṃ sarvabodhasamarthaḥ bodhātmakatvāt prāk 
pravṛtteḥ pradhānapravṛtteḥ prāṅ na paśyati darśanātmako api san puruṣa iti| sarvakāryakaraṇakriyāsamarthaṃ dṛśyaṃ pradhānaṃ na tadā prāk 
pravṛtteḥ paramārtham api sat dṛśyata^ ity ubhayasya pradhānapuruṣayor adarśanaṃ dharma^ ity eke manyante||

[199,20] atrāpy ubhayor api prāg avasthaivādarśanam uttarakālāvasthā ca darśanam iti pūrvadoṣaḥ sthita^ eva| dṛśyasya^ ātmabhūtam avyatirkitam api puruṣapratyayāpakṣāṃ puruṣākāreṇa puruṣasya na dṛśyate| 
puruṣākāraviśiṣṭatayā vā puruṣasya dṛśyata^ iti puruṣapratyayāpekṣam adarśanaṃ puruṣasya dharmatvena bhavati| tad etat spaṣṭayati 
puruṣasyānātmabhūtam api dṛśyapratyayāpakṣaṃ dṛśyapratyayo 'jñānaṃ tad apekṣaṃ kathaṃ puruṣākāreṇādarśanāt saṃkareṇa vā darśanād antaḥkaraṇapratyayāpekṣaṃ puruṣānātmabhūtyam api puruṣādharvatvena^ evādarśanam ābhāsate| tathā ca sati dṛśyadharma^ evāyam ity adoṣaḥ||

[200,10] sarvavikalpapakṣakhyāpanārtha^ upanyāsaḥ| darśanaṃ jñānaṃ tad eva jñānam adarśanam iti kecinmanyante| samāsasyottarapadārthaprādhānyam ūrīkrtyāyaṃ vikalpaḥ| etasya doṣo^ avidyāsūtra eva vyākhyātaḥ| 

[200,13] ete śāstragatā vikalpāḥ darśanāntaragatā vikalpāḥ| tatra vikalpabahutvamāram etat na bahutvamātreṇārthasiddhir ity abhiprāyaḥ| yasya tu buddhipuruṣasaṃyogahetutvaṃ tasyādarśanaṃ [natvaṃ] yuktaṃ darśanavirodhāt| tathā ca vyākhgyātam iti nātra punaḥ prayatyate||2.23||

[200,17] sarvapuruṣāṇāṃ guṇasaṃyogāviśeṣaḥ| guṇaiḥ saṃyogaḥ sarveṣām aviśiṣṭaḥ sādhāraṇaḥ tathāpy asādhāraṇaviṣayas tu pratyātmaṃ viśiṣṭas tu 
pratyak cetanasya bauddhapratyayānudarśinaḥ svabuddhisaṃyogaḥ| sa cāsādhāraṇaḥ svabuddhisaṃyogo yena bhavati tad evādarśanam| tasya hetur avidyā  | tasya svasvāmiśaktisvarūpopalabdhihetoḥ saṃyogasya hetuḥ kāraṇaṃ avidyā viparyayajñānavāsanety arthaḥ||

[200,22] nanu ca viparyayo mithyājñānam atad-rūpapratiṣṭhaṃ ity uktam| tathā^ anityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nityaśucisukhātmakhyātir avidyā ity uktam| atra kathaṃ viparyayajñānavāsanety ucyate| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ avidyākāryatvād vāsanāyāḥ (201,11) saivāvidyety ucyate| pralīneṣv api kāraṇeṣu sāmyāvasthāyām api satyām avidyāvasanā punaḥ punaḥ pravṛttau pradhānasya hetur bhavati| na punar viparyayaḥ prāk buddhyutpatter abhāvāt 
pravṛttikāraṇam iti tadvāsanaiva pradhānapuruṣāsaṃyogasya kāraṇam ity ākhyāyate||

[201,15] tad mevāha viparyayajñānavāsanāvasitā na kāryaniṣṭhāṃ kāryasmāptim| kāryaniṣṭhām āha puruṣakhyātim iti| tāṃ buddhir na 
prāpnoti| puruṣakhyātiparyavasānā hi buddheḥ karaṇīyavattā| sādhikārā karaṇiyavatī punaḥ punar āvartate viparyayajñānavāsanāvaśāt||

[201,19] sā tu buddhiḥ puruṣakhyātiparyavasānā kāryaniṣṭhāṃ prāpnoti| 
punar api tad eva spaṣṭayati caritādhikārā nivṛttādarśanabandhanā karmavipāpayor adarśanamūlatvād adarśanam eva bandhanam| adarśananivṛttau ca bandhanivṛtter bandhakāraṇābhāvād bandhakāraṇasyādarśanasyābhāvān na 
punar āvartate||

[201,23] atra kaścit paṇḍakopākhyānenoddhaṭṭayati| paṇḍakaḥ ṣaṇḍaḥ| sa kila mugdhayā bhāryayā^ abhidhīyate| katham āryaputra me bhaknyapratyavatī kim arthaṃ nāhaṃ apratyavatyasmīti| tām āha mṛtas te 'ham apratyam utpādayiṣyāmīti| tadhedaṃ vidyamānaṃ jñānāṃ cittanivṛttiṃ na karoti naṣṭaṃ kariṣyato^ iti kā pratyāśā||

[201,28] ayam uddhaṭṭayitur abhiprāyaḥ avidyā ced buddhipuruṣasaṃyogasya kāraṇaṃ tasyāś ca vidyā nivṛttihetuḥ tataś ca vidyāmānāyāṃ vidyāyāṃ tadvirodhin avidyā nivartate| (202,8) na tu vidyotpattyanantaram api cittanivṛttiḥ| tatra yathā^ eva satyāṃ vidyāyāṃ na cittanivṛttiḥ tathā naṣṭyām api vidyāyāṃ na nivartsyati tarām idānīṃ ṣaṇḍād iva mṛtād apatyopattiḥ| ata^ eva kāraṇād avidyā api na saṃyogakāraṇam||

[202,11] atrācāryadeśīyaḥ parihāraṃ vakti| ācāryadeśīyagrahaṇaṃ 
pūrvapakṣasyāsāratvaprakhyāpanārtham| uddhaṭṭanena^ eva pratyuttaraṃ vadāamīti ||

[202,13] nanu buddhinivṛttir eva mokṣaḥ| katham adarśanakāraṇabhāvād buddhinivṛttiḥ| tac cādarśanaṃ bandhakāraṇam darśanān nivartate tannivṛttir eva mokṣaḥ| kimarthāsthāne vibhramaḥ| 
pradhānapuruṣasaṃyogasya duḥkhahetor avidyā kāraṇam iti vistareṇa 
pratipāditam| tataś ca darśanād adarśananivṛttyupapatteḥ pūrvapakṣam asāraṃ manyate||

[202,17] nanu ca^ uktaṃ vidyānantaram eva buddhir na nivartyata^ iti| ucyate| vidyayā tāvcad avidyā nivartata^ eva| nātra vibhramaḥ kāryaḥ tasyāṃ tu nivṛttāyāṃ buddhinivṛttiś ca bhavaty eva punar buddhisaṃyogasyāvidyāvyatirekeṇa kāraṇāntarābhāvāt| tataś ca buddhinivṛttir eva mokṣaḥ||

[202,21] yat tu vidyānantaram eva buddhir na nivartata^ iti tad akāraṇam| kasmāt nimittanaimittikayor anekaprakāradarśanaṭ| yathā takṣādinimittāḥ 
prāsādādayas takṣādiṣūparateṣv api na nimittaviratyā viramanti| bhagnasya ca drumasya ca drumasya bhaṅgānantaram eva viśeṣād adarśanāt| tathā cittasyāpi vimuktasāyakavadārabdhasaṃskārāparikṣayāt tatsaṃskārāpekṣayā kiṃcitkālaṃ darśanena^ evāvastyhānaṃ iti| tathā ca śrutiḥ tasya tāvad eva ciram iti||2.24||

[203,12] catvāro vyūhāḥ prakṛtāḥ| tatra heyaṃ duḥkhaṃ heyaṃ duḥkham anāgatam ity uktam| rogasthānīyaeko vyūho vyākhyāta^ ity arthaḥ| heyakāraṇaṃ ca sanimittaṃ saṃyogākhyam uktam| dvitīyo vyūho rogahetusthānīyo heyahetus tv avidyānimittasahitaḥ pradhānapuruṣasaṃyoga upavarṇitaḥ| uktaparikīrtanaṃ ca sopapattikoktes tadviṣayāṅkṣānivṛttyartham||

[203,17] ataḥ paraṃ hānam ārogyasthānīyaṃ mokṣaśāstraprayojanaṃ vaktavyaṃ| vakṣyamāṇasaṃkīrtanaṃ ca śrotṛbuddhisamādhānārtham| tadabhāvāt saṃyogābhāvo hānaṃ tat dṛśeḥ kaivalyam||

[203,20] tad iti pūrvaprakṛtāyā^ avidyāyāḥ prtyavamarśaḥ| tasyādarśanasya avidyāyā^ abhāvāt buddhiouruṣasaṃyogasyaduḥkhahetoḥ abhāvaḥ ātyantiko bandhanoparama^ ity arthaḥ| etat hānam ārogyasthānīyam| tat dṛśeḥ kaivalyaṃ tad eva hānaṃ ātyantiko bandhanoparamaḥ dṛśeḥ puruṣasya kaivalyam| asya vyākhyānaṃ puruṣasyāmiśrībhāaḥ punar asaṃyogo guṇair ity arthaḥ||

[203,25] padārthaṃ vyākhyāya vākyārtham idānīṃ darśayati duḥkhakāraṇanivṛttau duḥkhoparamo hānam| tadā svarūpapratiṣṭhāh puruṣa ity uktam svarūpapratiṣṭhā vā citiśaktiḥ iti||

[204,10] nanu cāniyatavipākasya karmaṇaḥ sadbhāve kathaṃ hānaṃ syāt 
punar aniyatavipākakarmaṇā śarīrasyārambha^ iti| na abhihitapratividhānatvāt| samyagdarśanena kleśabījasya dāhāt karmaṇām anārambhakatvam uktaṃ dagdhabījabhāvāpanītatuṣaśalitapḍulanidarśanena| tasmāt na viduṣaḥ kāraṇābhāvāc charīrārambhaḥ||2.25||

[204,15] uktas tṛtīyo vyūhaḥ ārogyasthānīyo hānaṃ nāma| adhunā caturthaṃ vyūhaṃ bhaiṣasyasthānīyam upācikhyāsann āha hānasya kaḥ prāptyupāyaḥ iti||

[204,17] nanu c kathaṃ hānasya prāptyupāya^ iti yāvatā na^ eṣa hānam avastutvāt prāpyam| bandhanoparama^ eva hi hānam| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| yatnasādhyatvād avidyānivṛtteḥ yathā nigalabhedaḥ| samyakhyātau satyām avidyānivṛttir iti hānam avastv api prāpyam ity upacaryate||

[204,21] vivekakhyātir aviplavā hānopāyaḥ| kā punar vivekakhyātir ity āha sattvapuruṣānyatāpratyayaḥ sattvapuruṣayoḥ sādharmyavaidharmyāthāt,yāvabodha^ ity arthaḥ sā tu vivekakhyātiḥ anivṛttammithyājñānā viplavatte na sthirībhavati na kāryakṣameti||

[204,24] tathā ca^ uktam yathā hemāavipakaṃ na virājate| tathā 'pajhakaṣāyasya vijñānaṃ prakāśate||

[204,26] tathā kāmagrāhagṛhītasya jñānam apy asya na plavaḥ| iti||

[204,27] yadā tu mithyājñānaṃ dagdhabījabhāvaṃ dagdhabījatvād eva bandhaprasavaṃ kāryārabhākṣamaṃ sampadyate tadā vidhūtakleśarajasaḥ kleśā eva rajaḥ vidhūtaṃ yasya tadvidhūtakleśarajaḥ tasya sattvasya rāgadimalākalaṅkitasya parasyāṃ vaśīkārasaṃjñāyāṃ vartamānasya jñānaprasādamātre vartamānasya| tad evānuvadati vivekapratyayapravāhaḥ vivekajñānanirantarabhāvaiḥ nirmalo bhavati||

[205,13] sāvivekakhyātiḥ itthaṃbhūtā satī^ aviplavā bhavati| yadā^ evam aviplavā vivekakhyātiḥ tato mithyājñānasya dagdhabījabhāvopagamo virudhatvāt| punaś cāpravaḥ mithyājñānasya dagdhabījabhāvopagamād eva| tathā ca^ uktam (205,16) bījāny agnyupadagdhāni na rohanti yathā punaḥ| jñānadagdhais tathā kleśair nātmā sampadyate punaḥ (MBh 12,204.016; this is cited twice!)||

[205,18] sā ca tādṛśī vivekakhyātir mokṣasya mārgo hānasyopāya^ ity arthaḥ ||2.26||

[205,20] utpannasamyagdarśanasya samyagdarśanotpattau svānubhavapratyayaliṅgaprakaṭīkaraṇārtham āha tasya saptadhā prāntabhūmiḥ 
prajñā| tasya^ iti pratyuditakhyāteḥ pratyutpannasamyagdarśanasya 
pratyāmnāyaḥ pratyavamarśaḥ| saptadhā^ ity aśuddhyāvaraṇamalāpagamāt| aśuddhir evāvadharaṇaṃ kleśāḥ karmāṇi ca tad eva malaṃ tasyāpagamāt vittasya pratyayāntarānutpāde mamāham iti ca viruddhapratyayantarāṇām anutpattau| saptadhā^ ity asya vivaraṇaṃ saptaprakārā^ eva prajñā buddheḥ vivekino bhavati||

[205,26] tān sapta prakārān krameṇācaṣṭe yathā parijñātaṃ heyaṃ sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ heyaṃ jñātam upapattibhiḥ| nāsya parijñātavyo duḥkhaprakāraḥ||

[205,28] kṣīṇā heyahetava^ iti bahuvacanaprayogāt kleśāḥ karmāṇica abhidhīyante| na punar eṣāṃ kleśakarmaṇām alpam api samyagdarśanopāvakādagdhabījā(206,9)bhāvāt kṣe(dutprekṣi)tavyam asti| etenāpi prāntabhūmitvam eva^ uktam| itthaṃ ca 
pratyupasthitasamīcīnapratyayasya kṣīṇakleśatvāt na kṣetavyam asti| kṛtakarṇīyo^ aham iti ca samyagdarśanaparipākaliṅgapratyaya^ upāvartate| yathā rogātur asya rogahetukṣaye nīrogo 'haṃ na punar bhaiṣajyam astīti 
pratyayaḥ| evam eva[ṣa] dvitīyaḥ prajñāprakāra^ uktaḥ||

[206,14] sākṣāt kṛtaṃ nirodhasamāpattir hānam | hānapratyāsannatvāt hānatulyarūpatvāc ca niodhasamādhir eva hānam ity ākhyāyate| tac ca sākṣāt kṛtam anubhavaviṣayatām ānītam| tad idaṃ kaivalyaṃ paryavasitam kaivalyaṃ kartavyam iti ca prajñāyate| eṣo api prāntabhūmiprajñāprakāraḥ tṛtīya ākhyātaḥ ||

[206,18] bhāvitaḥ samyagabhyastataraḥ vivekakhyātirūpo hānopāyaḥ| samyagdarśanaṃ yathāvad upasthitam iti niścitā cet prajñā vivekino jāyate sa^ eṣa caturthaḥ prajñāprakāraḥ| ity eṣā catuṣṭayī kāryavimuktiḥ kāryāt kartavyāt vimuktir yasyāḥ prajñāyāḥ sā kāryavimuktiḥ caturūpā prajñā 
puruṣārthakartavyatety arthaḥ ||

[206,22] atha vā kāryasya vimuktiḥ nivṛttiḥ kāryavimuktiḥ| sā ca catuṣṭayī kāryavimuktir iti samāptaṃ vākyam| prajñā iti pareṇa saṃbadhyate| katham prajñā(pra)yuktam vivekakhyātiyuktaṃ vittaṃ prajñācittaṃ tasya vimuktiḥ pralayaḥ prajñācittavimuktiḥ| sā trayī| tathā ca vakṣyati 
puruṣārthaśūnyānāṃ guṇānāṃ pratiprasavaḥ kaivalyam iti||

[206,27] tatra kāryavimuktiś catuṣṭayī| puruṣārthaśūnyatvaṃ pūrvam uktam| idānīṃ buddhiguṇān nivṛttiḥ| puruṣārthakartavyānāṃ puruṣāṛthaśūnyānāṃ pratiprasavaḥ prajñācittavimuktis trayī (207,10) vyākhyāyate| yathā caritādhikārāḥ nivṛttakartavyāḥ buddhiguṇāḥ sattvādayo buddhyākārāvasthitāḥ pralayābhimukhāḥ| kim iva giriśikharakūṭacyutā iva grāvāṇo niravasthānāḥ kartavyābhāvāt svakāraṇe ahaṃkāre pralayābhimukhāḥ saha tena buddhisaṃsthānena kāryeṇātha vā svakāraṇenāhaṅkāreṇa kuśalaṃ puruṣaṃ prati svakāraṇam apy avyaktabhāvaṃ 
pratipadyata^ iti saha tenāstaṃ gaddhantīty ucyate| eṣā prathamāprajñācittavimuktiḥ||

[207,16] na ca^ eṣāṃ pratipralīnānāṃ puruṣaṃ prati punar asty utpādaḥ prayojanābhāvāt| vumuktaṃ hi caturvidhaṃ kāryam| eṣā dvitīyā prajñācittavimuktiḥ||

[207,18] etasyām avasthāyāṃ caritārthaguṇapralayāvasthāyāṃ guṇasambandhātītaḥ guṇasaṃbandho draṣṭṛ-dṛśyalakṣaṇaḥ tam atītaḥ| svarūpamātrajyotiḥ dṛśisvarūpamātram evaṃ jyotiḥ| puruṣaḥ kevalī śuddha iti tṛtīyā prajñācittavimuktir iti||

[207,21] etāṃ samavidhāṃ prāntabhūmiṃ prāntāḥ pragatāvaṣanāḥ bhūmayaḥ avasthāḥ yasyās tāṃ prāntabhūmiṃ prajñām anupaśyan samyagdarśanaliṅgaparipākabhūtāmīkṣamāṇaḥ puruṣaḥ kuśala^ ity ākhyāyate kuśala^ iti saṅketaḥ| pratiprasave api pralaye api cittasya muktaḥ kuśala ity eva bhūtapūrvagatyā kuśala^ ity eva bhavati| kutaḥ kauśala tasya^ ity āha guṇātītatvāt iti| guṇān atīto hi kuśalaḥ||2.27||

[207,26] siddhā vivekakhyātiḥ samadhigatā vivekakhyātiḥ saptavidhaprāntabhūmiprajñāphalāvasānā hānasyopāya^ iti| na ca siddhiḥ antareṇa sādhanam ity ata^ idaṃ vivekakhyātisiddhisādhanārtham ārabhyate yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād aśuddhikṣaye jñānadīptir āvivekakhyāteḥ||

[208,9] yogāṅgānuṣṭhānād eva vivekakhyātir iti nāvadidhārayiṣitam guru-upāsanadharmaprabhṛtikebhyo api vivekakhyāteḥ siddhir ity ata^ eva| kiṃ tarhi sādhanamātram antareṇa na bhavaty eva^ ity avadhārayiṣyate| na punar yogāṅgānuṣṭhānam eva sādhanaṃ yogāṅgānuṣṭhānāt tu bhavaty eva vivekakhyātisiddhir iti| tathā cācāryair uktam yogas tattvajñānārhaḥ iti||

[208,14] yogāṅgāny abhidhāyiṣyamāṇāni teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ yogāṅgānuṣṭhānam tasmāt aśuddheḥ kleśākhyāyāḥ kṣayaḥ tatkṣaye dīptiḥ 
prakarṣaḥ| kriyadavasānam ity āha^ ā vivekakhyāteḥ bhavatīty upaskārah||

[208,17] svayam eva bhāṣyakāraḥ spaṣṭataraṃ vivṛṇoti yogāṅgān anuṣṭhāv abhidhāyiṣyamāṇāni| teṣām anuṣṭhānāt pañcaparvaṇo viparyayasya aśuddhirūpasya kṣayaḥ nāśaḥ tatkṣaye samyagdarśanasyābhivyaktiḥ dīptiḥ| katham etad bhavatīty āha yathā yathā ca sādhanāny anuṣṭhīyante tathā tathā tanutvam aśuddhir āpadyate| tathā ca manur abiṣiṣṭa (208,22) 
prāṇāyāmair dahed doṣān dhāraṇābhiś ca kilbiṣān| (208,23) pratyāhāreṇa saṃsargān dhyānenānaiścarān guṇān|| iti| (208,24) yathā yathā cāśuddhiḥ kṣīyate tathā tathā tatkṣayakramānurodhinī divasakarasyeva śiśirāvasāne jñānasya dīptir vivardhate| sā khalv iyaṃ vivṛddhiḥ prakarṣam anubhavati| kim avasānam tad āha^ ā vivekakhyāteḥ| tasya vyākhyānam ā guṇapuruṣasvarūpavivekajñānād ity arthaḥ| sattvapuruṣānyatākhyātiparyavasānā^ eva śuddhiḥ||

[209,11] yogāṅgānuṣhānam aśuddheḥ kṣayāaraṇaṃvivekakhyāteś ca 
prāptikāraṇam iti dvidhā^ eva kāraṇam dharmaparaśuddhyasthānīyam ekam ubhayakāraṇaṃ nānyathā bhavatīti| tatra kāraṇaprasaṅgena śāstraprasiddhāni kāraṇāni vyācaṣṭe kati ca^ etāni kāraṇāni śāstre bhavanti navaivety āha| tadyathā tāni ślokena^ eva kathayati utpattisthityabhivyaktivikārapratyayāptayaḥ| viyogānyatvadhṛtayaḥ kāraṇaṃ navadhā smṛtam|| iti| tat-pravivekārtham udāharaṇāni 
prastāviṣyante| tatrotpattikāraṇaṃ mano bhavati [vijñājasya] manaso hi vṛttiḥ jñānaṃ yathā jalamūrmāṇām||

[209,19] sthitikāraṇaṃ sthiteḥ kāraṇam manasaḥ puruṣāṛthatā| bhogāpavargārthatvena hi mānasaḥ sthitiḥ| śarīrasya yathā sthitikāraṇam āhāraḥ||

[209,21] abhivyakteḥ kāraṇaṃ rūpasyālokaḥ| tadā rūpajñānam| na hy ālokenānabhivyakte rūpe'vabodhaḥ||

[209,23] vikārasya kāraṇaṃ manaso viṣayāntaram| viṣayāntarasannidhānena hi mano vikriyām āsīdati| yathā^ agniḥ pākasya| yaḥ pākākhyo vikāras tasya 
pākasya vikārarūpasyāgniḥ kāraṇaṃ yathā||

[209,26] pratyayakāraṇaṃ dhūmajñānam agnijñānasya| pratyayasya phalavataḥ 
pramāṇasya kāraṇam ity arthaḥ| pūrvatra tu bhavati vijñānasya^ iti jñānasyopādānakāraṇaṃ vivakṣitam||

[210,9] prāptikāraṇaṃ yogāṅgānuṣṭhānaṃ vivekakhyateḥ dharma^ iva sukhasya| tad evāśuddher viyogakāraṇaṃ paraśur iva chedyasya||

[210,11] anyatvakāraṇaṃ yathā suvarṇakāraḥ suvarṇasya| suvarṇaṃ hi bhittvā vardhamānakam anyaṃ karoti| tathā vardhamānakaṃ bhittvā^ anyad ābharaṇāntaraṃ karoti| tathā 'nyad iti||

[210,14] evam ekasya svī iti pratyayasyāpi^ avidyā kāraṇaṃ mūḍhatve| dveṣas tasya^ eva strīpratyayasya duḥkhatve kāraṇam| tathā rāgaḥ sukhatve| tathā tasya^ eva mādhyasthye heyopādeyaśūnyatāyāṃ vivekayuktaṃ jñānaṃ kāraṇam| evam ekaḥ strīpratyayaḥ suvarṇāvad anekadhā mūḍhatvaduḥkhatvasukhatvamādhyasthyādibhedair bhidyate||

[210,18] dhṛteḥ kāraṇam| dhāraṇaṃ dhṛtiḥ tasyāḥ kāraṇaṃ śarīram indriyāṇām| na hi śarīram aprāpyendriyāṇi dhṛtaṃ labhante| tānicedriyāṇi tasya śarīrasya dhṛtikāraṇaṃ bhavanti| indriyavṛttidvāreṇa hi śarīraṃ dhriyate||

[210,21] tathā mahābhūtāny ākāśādīni śarīrāṇāṃ dhṛtikāraṇam| mahābhūtārabdhāni hi śarīrāṇi brahmādistambāvasānāni| [tāni ca] 
parasparam upakāryopakārakatvadvāreṇa dhṛtikāraṇam||

[210,24] tathā sarveṣāṃ padārthānāṃ tairyagyaunamānuṣadaivatāni dhṛtikāraṇam| kutaḥ parasparārthatvāt/ tairyagyaunaṃ mānuṣadaivatayor vāhanadohanahavirādibhr upakurvat dhṛtikāraṇam||

[210,27] tathā mānuṣaṃ ijyā-rakṣāṇādibhir daivatairyagyaunayoḥ dhṛtikāraṇam| tathā daivamitarayoḥ śītoṣṇapravarṣāṇādinā dhṛtikāraṇam||

[211,8] evaṃ varṇāśramāṇām apy anyonyopakāreṇa dhṛtikāraṇatvam| 
parasparopāśrayeṇa hi jagadakhilam api dhriyate| evaṃ navaiva kāraṇānīti kāraṇāntaraṃ pratiṣedhati| tāni ca yathā 'rthasaṃbhavaṃ yo yo 'rthasaṃbhavaḥ padārthāntareṣv apy anudāhṛteṣu tathā yojyāni| yogāṅgānuṣṭhānaṃ tu dvidhā^ eva kāraṇatvaṃ labhate| tathā ca vyākhyātam||2.28||

[211,13] kāni punas tāni yogāṅgāni yeṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ dvidhā kāraṇatvaṃ labhata^ iti| tatra yogāṅgāny avadhāryante yamaniyamāsanaprāṇāyāmapratyāhāradhāraṇā-dhyānasamādhayo 'ṣṭa aṅgāni||

[211,16] nanu ca yogaśāstrāntareṣv āsanādīny eva ṣaḍaṅgāni| tad yathā ṣaḍaṅgo yoga^ ucyate^ ityādi| kiñ ca sākṣād āsanādīny eva samādher upakurvanti na yamaniyamau||

[211,19] na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| yamaniyamavator yogādhikāropapatteḥ na hi yathā kāmacāriṇāṃ yogādhikāraḥ| nāvirato duścaritān nāśāntaḥ iti śruteḥ| tathā yeṣāṃ tapo brahmacaryaṃ yeṣu satyaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ityādi ca- atharvaṇe| tathā gītāyām brahmacārivrate sthitaḥ (BhG 6.14) iti| tasmād yamaniyamayor apy aṅgatvam||

[211,23] yamaniyamayoś ca yamasya prathapopādānaṃ 
prādhānyakhyāpanārtham| yamasya hi prādhānyaṃ sarvatra siddham| yamaniyamayuktasyādhikṛtasya yogina^ āsanādyaṅgānāṃ pūrvapūrve sthirapadaprāptasyottarottarānuṣṭhānaṃ prāptam| na hi prathamaṃ sopānamanāruhyottaram āroḍhuṃ śakyam||

[212,13] yad^ anyatrocyate 212,14 sthānāsanavidhānāni yogasya vidhayo api vā| vyākṣepajanakāḥ sarve na te yogasya hetavaḥ||

[212,16] sarvadoṣaparityāgaḥ samādhiś ceti taddvayam|| nirṇaya[yāt] yogahetuḥ syād bhavati vānyan na vā [bhavatv anyan na vā bhavet] || iti.[212,19]        tatsamādhisarvadoṣaparityāgayoḥ prādhānyāt tatstutyartham nāsanādyaṅgapratiṣedārtham| yathākramam eṣāṃ yamādīnām anusṭhānaṃ svarūpam ca vakṣyāmaḥ svarūpe hi^ anavagate nānuṣṭhātuṃ śakyam iti||2.29||

[212,22] tatra teṣu yamādiṣu prathamaṃ yamā vyākhyāyante ahiṃsāsatyāsteyabrahmacaryāparigrahā yamāḥ| tatrāhiṃsā sarvathā sarvātmanā sarvaprakāreṇa sarvabhūtānām anabhidrohaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ sthāvarajaṅgmānāṃ anabhidroho'pīḍanam||

[212,25] yamaniyamānām ahiṃsā pradhānabhūtā kāyavāṅmanasaiḥ sarvātmanā karaṇīyā^ iti darśayati^ uttare ca yamāḥ satyādayaḥ tanmūlāḥ ahiṃsāprayojanāḥ ahiṃsārthāḥ tatsiddhiparatayā^ eva ahiṃsādsiddhitat-paratvena pratipādyante| kim uktaṃ bhavatīty āha tadavadāta[rūpa]karaṇāyopādīyante^ ahiṃsāśuddhipratipādanāyopādīyante||

[212,29] tathā hy uktam sa khalv ayaṃ brāhmaṇo yathā yathā bahūni vratāni samāditsate tathā tathā pramādakṛtebhyo hiṃsānidānebhyaḥ hiṃsāmūlebhyaḥ hiṃsākāraṇebhyaḥ nivartamānas tām evāvadātarūpām ahiṃsāṃ karotīti||

[213,9] satyādyanekavratopādānāt anyeṣām aviruddhavratānāṃ copādānāt hiṃsākāraṇān nivṛ(yyiṃ)tasya hiṃsākāraṇād uparataparisphuraṇasyāhiṃsā^ eāvadātatarā kāraṇābhāvād upajāyate| yathā bhikṣādivratānuṣṭhāne kṛṣyādihiṃsānidānanivṛttiḥ ||

[213,12] satyaṃ yathā dṛṣṭaṇ yathānumitaṃ yathā śrutaṃ tathā vāṅmanaś ceti| tat satyam| etad uktaṃ bhavati^ abhrāntaiḥ pramāṇaiḥ yathārthāvagamaḥ sañjātaḥ tam avagamaṃ tathā^ eva paratra saṃkramayituṃ vāṅganasābhyāṃ tathā^ eva pravartanaṃ satyam iti||

[213,15] tad eva^ āha paratra svabodhasaṃkrāntaye vāg uktā| sā yathā vagatārthā api yadi na vañcitā| yathā satyam eva paravipralambhanārthaṃ 
prayuktaṃ deśakālādyapekṣayā kvacid anṛtāyate yathā yidhiṣṭhirasya aśvatthāmā hataḥ sa kuñjara iti||

[213,18] bhrāntā vā yathā satyam iti manyamāno 'nṛtam eva vadati| 
pratipattivandhyā vā rotuḥ pratipādanāyāparyāptā yathā sandigdhaṃ vākyam agṛhyamāṇaprakaraṇādi viśeṣam| kaṣṭaśabdo vā^ ajāvaya^ ityādiḥ||

[213,21] tasmād evamādidoṣarahitā eṣā vāk ṛjumanassahacāriṇī sarvabhūtopakārārthaṃ pravṛttā satyatām āskandati| na bhūtopaghātāya 
pravṛttā yathārthabalādhānā api satyatām āpadyate||
