[213,24] yadi caicam api^ uktena prakāreṇa nirdoṣāpi abhidhīyamānā bhūtopaghātaparaiva syāt na satyaṃ bhavet na hi prāṇighātālambinī vañcanādidoṣatrayavirpoṣitā api satyatvaṃ pratipadyate| pāpam eva bhavet||

[213,27] tad eva^ āha tena puṇyābhāsena puṇyapratibimbakena satyakauśikavacanadeśīyena kaṣṭaṃ tamaḥ prāpnuyāt| tasmāt parīkṣya sarvahitaṃ satyaṃ brūyāt||

[214,12] uktaṃ ca satyaṃ brūyāt priyaṃ brūyān na crūyāt satyam apriyam| 
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ|| iti 214,15      steyaṃ aśāstrapūrvaṃ dravyāṇāṃ parataḥ svīkaraṇaṃ spṛhānimittam| yat steyaṃ tatpratiṣedhas tu na spṛhālūnāṃ sambhavatīti tasya^ eva steyasya 
pratiṣedhas tu| saḥ aspṛhārūpam asteyam ucyate||

[214,18] brahmacaryaṃ guptendriyasya guptanyendriyasya puruṣasya abrahmacaryārthavāṅmanasādivṛttiśūnyasya^ upasthndriyasaṃyama^ iti||

[214,20] viṣayāṇām ārjanarakṣaṇakṣayasaṅgahiṃsā-doṣadarśanād asvīkaraṇam anupādanaṃ aparigrahaḥ| na punar asāmarthyenāsvīkaraṇam| ity ete yamāḥ ||2.30||

[214,22] anyadharmāṇāṃ jātideśakālādyavacchedenānuṣṭhānād ihāpi tathā 
prasaṅga^ iti tat-pratiṣedhāyāha^ ete tv iti| ete tv ahiṃsā-ādayo mahāvratasaṃjñāḥ saṃnyāsavatām anuṣṭhānīyāḥ kīdṛśās te mahāvratanāmānīti jātideśakālasamayānavaddhināḥ sārvabhaumā mahā-vratam| mahac ca tat vrataṃ ca mahā-vratam| mahattvaṃ ca sārvabhaumatvam||

[214,26] kathaṃ punar jātyādyanavaddhinnatvam eṣām iti anavaddhedapratipādanāya vyatirekeṇāhiṃsām udāharati tatrāhiṃsā jātyavacchinnā matsyabandhakasya matsyeṣv eva nānyatra matsyajāter hiṃsā| sā ca matsyajātyavācchinnā matsyabhūmimanaśnuvānā vartata^ iti asārvabhaumyeva||

[215,14] sā^ evāhiṃsā deśāvacchinnā katham na tīrthe prayāgādau haniṣyāmīti| tīrtheṣv ahiṃsā ca tīrthād anyaṃ deśaṃ na vyaśnuta^ iti asārvabhaumī||

[215,16] sā^ eva ca kālāvacchinnā yathā na caturdaśyāṃ na puṇye 'hanīti| caturdaśyādi kālād anyaṃ kālaṃ na vayaśnuta^ iti sā^ apy asārvabhaumī||

[215,18] tribhiḥ api jātideśakālaiḥ uparatasya tasya^ eva matsyabandhakasya samayāvacchinnā samayaḥ kartavyatāniyamaḥ katham devabrāhmaṇārthaṃ haniṣyāmi nānyathā^ iti śrāddhādisamayabhūmimanaśnānā vartata^ iti sā api na sārvabhaumī yathā ca kṣatriyasya yuddha^ eva hiṃsā nānyatra^ iti samayāvacchinnasya^ evodāharaṇam ||

[215,22] ye puner ete yamāḥ jātideśakālasamayair anavaddhinnā^ ahiṃsādayaḥ te sarvathā^ eva paripālanīyāḥ sarvabhūmiṣu jātyādiṣu sarvaviṣayeṣu sarvaprāṇiṣu sarvathā^ eāviditavyabhicārāḥ vinā vyabhicāreṇa te cetthaṃ viśiṣṭāḥ sārvabhaumā mahā-vratam ity ucyante||2.31||

[215,26] śaucasantoṣatapas-svādhyāya-īśvarapraṇidhānāni niyamāḥ| tatra śaucaṃ mṛdādijanitam ādiśabdād udakaṃ ca| medhyābhyvaharaṇāni ca ghṛtapayāadīni bhakṣaṇāni| caśabdāt medhyadarśanaśreavaṇādīni ca| bāhyam etac-śaucam||

[215,29] ābhyantaram ahunā^ ucyate cittamalānāṃ kāmakrodhādīnāṃ tat-pratipakṣabhāvanāsalilaiḥ ākṣālanam||

[216,10] santoṣaḥ sannihitasādhanāśvāsāt sannihitād āśvāsanimittāt adhiksādhanānupāditsā sannihitamātrād atṛptau satyām apy alaṃpratyayaḥ||

[216,12] tapo dvandvasahanam| dvandvaś ca jighatsāpipāse| jighatsā^ attum icchā| jighatsāpipāse^ alpīyaḥpānāśananimitte tapaḥ| śītoṣṇe apratikriyamāṇe^ alpapratikriye vā tapaḥ| sthānāsane tiṣṭhed ahani rātrāvāsīta^ iti smṛtiprasthānam| kāṣṭhamaunākāṣṭhamaune| kāṣṭhamaunaṃ hastaprayogādirahitam| akāṣṭhamaunaṃ hastādiprayogayuktaṃ sambhāṣaṇādirahitam||

[216,18] vratānicaiṣāṃ yogināṃ yathā-yogaṃ yathāsambhavam| homādiviruddhadharmaviruddhapratiṣedhārthaṃ yathā-yogam ity āha| kāni tāni kṛcchrādīni| kṛcchraṃ prājāpatyādi| tryahaṃ prātaḥ tryahaṃ sāyaṃ tryaham adyādayācitam| upoṣaṇaṃ tryaham iti| cāndrāyaṇaṃ yavamadhyapipīikāmadhyādi| ekaikaṃ vadhayet grāsam ityādismṛtiḥ| sāntapanam gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam| ekarātropavāsaś ca kṛcchraḥ sāntapanaḥ amṛtaḥ||

[216,24] etāny eva dravyāṇi trirabhyastāni mahāsāntapanam| ādigrahaṇaṃ 
parākādipratyupādānārtham| svādhyāyo mokṣaśāstrādhyayanam upniṣadādyadhyayanaṃ praṇavajapo vā| pratyakcetanādhigamo apy antarāyābhāvaś ceti||2.32||

[217,14] tatra yamaniyamānāṃ viruddhās tarkāḥ vitarkāḥ taiḥ bādhane sati 
pratipakṣān bhāvayet abhidhāsyamānena prakāreṇa| apratibhāvyamānā hi vitarkā yoginaṃ hiṃsādiṣu praṇidhāpayeyuḥ||

[217,17] yadā^ asya brāhmāṇasya vitarkā hiṃsādayo jāyeran| tatrāhiṃsādivitarkā ye vā hiṃsā-ādayas te vitarkāḥ kīdṛśā iti tatrāhiṃsāyā vitarkaḥ hiṃsā haniṣyābhyapakāriṇam iti| tathā satyasyānṛtaṃ vadiṣyāmīti viruddhaḥ pratyayo vitarkaḥ| tathaivāsteyasya vitarkaḥ dravyam asya svīkariṣyāmīti| tathā brahmacaryasya vitarkaḥ dāreṣu cāsya gamiṣyāmīti| tathā^ eāparigrahasya vitarkaḥ parigraheṣu cāsya svāmī bhaviṣyāmīti||

[217,23] asya^ ity etat sarvatra hiṃsādiṣu^ anugamapratipādanārtham| dravyam asya svīkariṣyāmīti paradravyasyātmasaṃbandhakaraṇam| parigraheṣu cāsya svāmīti parasaṃbandhiṣv eva dreavyeṣu svayaṃ svāmīti| yathā kumāranarapatisacivādīnām ||

[217,26] ittham-unmārgahāriṇā vitarkajvareṇa dīptena bādhyamānas tatpratipakṣān bhāvayet||

[217,28] kathaṃ bhāvayed iti ghoreṣu saṃsārāṅgāreṣu pacyamānena mayā cśaraṇam abhyupagataḥ sarvabhūtābhaytapradānena yogadharmaḥ| sa khalv ahaṃ tyaktvā vitarkān hiṃsādīn punas tānādadānas tulyaḥ śvavṛtteneti| śuna iva yasya saḥ śvavṛttaḥ| śvavad vā vṛttaḥ śvavṛttaḥ| tena tulya^ iti bhāvayet| yathā (218,12) śvā vāntāvalehī tathā tyaktasya punar ādānaṃ kurvan ity upaskāraḥ| tena puruṣeṇa tulyo 'ham iti| tathā katham ahaṃ bhaveyaṃ na yuktam etad iti bhāvayet||

[218,14] ity evam ādi sūtrāntare api niyamasūtre api yojyam| śauca^ādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ vitarkā draṣṭavyāḥ śaucaṃ na kariṣyāmīty ādayaḥ| bhāṣye tu yamānām eva pradarśanam teṣām avaśyakartavyatvāt akaraṇe ca doṣagauravāt atyādarārtham ||2.33||

[218,17] vitarkāṇāṃ svarūpam bhedaḥ prabhavahetuḥ phalaṃ ca sūtreṇa^ eva 
pradarśyate vitarkā hiṃsādaya^ iti svarūpam| kṛtakāritānumoditā iti bhedaḥ| lobhakrodhamohapūrvakā iti prabhavahetuḥ| mṛdumadhyāhimātrā iti mandamadhyottamadoṣatvam| duḥkhājñānānantaphalā iti pratipakṣabhāvanam| 
phalaṃ vā tena^ eva vyākhyāyate||

[218,21] tatra hiṃsā^ eva^ ekā udāhriyate sarveṣāṃ pradarśanāya| sā kṛtā kāritā^ anumoditeti trividhā| kṛtā svavyāpāreṇa| kāritā prayojyavyāpāreṇa| anumoditā pareṣāṃ vyāpāreṇa manasā naujñātā||

[218,24] ekaikā punas tridhā| katham lobhena māsacarmā(sthi)rthī mṛgaṃ hanasti| krodhenāpakṛtam aneneti| mohena dharmo me bhaviṣyatīti| evam ete nava bhedāḥ| ekaieka(tvena)sya punas tridhā bhedaḥ| katham lobhakrodhamohāḥ punas tridhā (219,12) mṛdumadhyādhimātrā iti| ekaikaṃ 
punaḥ tridhā bhavati mṛdumṛdur madhyamṛdus tīvramṛdur iti| tathā mṛdumadhyo madhyamdhyas tīvramadhya^ iti| tathā mṛdutīvro madhyatīvras tīvratīvra^ iti| evaṃ ekāśītibhedā hiṃsā bhavati ||

[218,15] sā ca punaḥ ekāśītibhedā api satī niyogavikalpasamuddayabhedāt asaṅkhyeyā| yathā niyogabhedaḥ kaścinn ity uktaḥ sannanyaṃ niyuṅkte yathā rājñaḥ| sa cāpi niyuktaḥ param sa cāpi param iti| te ca sarve niyuktāḥ samuddayena vikalpena vā hiṃsanti| tathā mūlaniyoktāpi niyojyaiṅḥ saṃhatya vikalpya ca hiṃsām anutiṣṭhatīti|  vadhyaghātakādiprāṇibhedasyāsaṅkhyeyatvād iti| evam anṛtādiṣv api yojyam| kṛtakāritānumoditādi yojyam||

[219,21] te khalv amī vitarkā duḥkhājñānānantaphalāḥ| duḥkhaṃ cājñānaṃ ca duḥkhājñāne te^ evānantaṃ phalaṃ yeṣāṃ te duḥkhājñānānantaphalāḥ|  duḥkham eṣām anantaṃ phalam| ajñānaṃ ca^ eṣām anantaṃ phalam| tasmād etān vitarkān manasā api na^ upekṣayed iti pratipakṣaṃ bhāvayet||

[219,25] tatra hiṃsyasya triprakārā pīḍā pratipādyate tad bhedadvāreṇa phalabhedapradar_Vanārtham| tathā ca hiṃsako vadhyasya vīryam ākṣipati|  ghātakarūpāvalokanabhartsanādinā trastasya vīryam ākṣipya śastrādinipātanena duḥkhayati| tato jīvitād api mocayati| prāṇebhyo viyojayati||

[220,10] tasya vīryākṣepād asya hiṃsituḥ cetanācetanaṃ paśu-kṣetrādikaṃ upakaraṇaṃ kṣīṇavīryaṃ bhavati||

[220,12] śastrādinipātanaduḥkhotpādāt hiṃsāviśeṣāt narakatiryakpretādiṣu duḥkham anubhavati||

[220,14] tathā jīvitavyaparopaṇāt pratikṣaṇaṃ ca jīvitātyaye vartamāno janmamaraṇāntare vartmni pratikṣaṇaṃ maraṇam icchann api duḥkhavipākasya niyatavedanīyatvāt avaśyānubhavitavyatvāt kathaṃ cid evocchvas iti||

[220,17] yadi ca kathaṃcit puṇyāvagamāt prabhūtapuṇyāvagatā^ alpīyasī hiṃsā bhavet tatra puṇyabhūmnā sukhaprāptaḥ sann api bhaved alpāyuḥ āvāpagatahiṃsā-doṣeṇa||

[220,19] ittham anṛtādiṣv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ hiṃsāyām uktaṃ yojyam| evaṃ vitarkāṇāṃ hiṃsā-ādīnāṃ ddhāyām ivānugataṃ vipākam aniṣṭaṃ bhāvayet na vitarkeṣu manaḥ praṇidadhīta||2.34||

[220,22] pratipakṣabhāvanāheyā vitarkāḥ yadā ca sytut aprasavadharmiṇaḥ aprasavāś ca te dharmiṇaś cety aprasavadharmiṇaḥ| yadā yogino manasi notpadyante nikṛttamūlāḥ santaḥ tadā tatkṛtaṃ yamaniyamasthairyakṛtam aiśvaryaṃ yoginaḥ siddhisūcakam ahiṃsādiphalasiddhijñāpakaṃ jāyata^ iti yogasiddhiphalam avasthitam eva| anyad api yogāṅgastutaye^ aiśvaryam anuṣaṅgajātam ucyate| katham ekaikasyāpy ahiṃsā-ādīnāṃ sthiratvaliṅgam īdṛśaṃ yajñādidharmaphalair apy asamānaṃ pratyakṣata^ upalabhyate kim aṅga saṃhatānāṃ yogasiddhikaratvam (221,15) iti| tad etat pratidarśayati tad yathā^ ahiṃsāpratiṣṭhāyām ahiṃsāsthiratve^ avitarkaprādur bhāve|  tatsannidhau vairatyāgaḥ| śāśvatikavicveṣiṇām api sarpanakulādīnāṃ tasyāhiṃsakasya sannidhau vairatyāgo bhavati||2.35||

[221,18] satyapratiṣṭhāyāṃ satyasthair ye kriyāphalāśrayatvaṃ yajñādikriyāphalānām āgamanaṃ prāptir bhavati| satyavādivākyād iti śeṣaḥ| katham adhārmiko api dhārmito bhūyā ity ukte tadvacanād dhārmiko bhavati| yathā dharme^ asya ramatāṃ mana^ iti devair uktaḥ kuṇḍadhārārādhī brāhmaṇo dhārmiko babhūva| tathā svargaṃ prāpnuhīty uktaḥ svargaṃ prāpnoti| yathā viśvāmitreṇa^ uktaḥ svargaṃ prāpnuhīti triśaṅkuḥ svargaṃ prāpa | kiṃ bahunā| amodhā^ asya vāk bhavati||2.36||

[221,24] asteyapratiṣṭhāyāṃ asmṛhāyām| tataḥ kim asya bhavati stnopasthānaṃ bhavati sarvadiksthāny asya^ upatiṣṭhante ratnāni^ ātmānaṃ darśayanti||2.37||

[222,16] brahmacaryapratiṣṭhāyāṃ vīryalābhaḥ| brahmacaryalābhād apratighāt guṇānutkarṣayati| apratibandhānātmana^ upapādayati|  sarvaśubhārthapravṛttiṣv aprativaddhavīryo bhavati| na kenacit pratighātena vivadhyata^ ity arthaḥ| 222,19       kiñca vinayeṣu śiṣyeṣu yogyeṣu vā sādhuṣu jñānam ādhātuṃ samartho bhavati yathā bhagavān vyāsaḥ saṃ(jñeye)jajñe||2.38||

[222,21] aparigrahasthair ye janmakathantāsambodhaḥ| janmanaḥ kathaṃbhāvabodho^ asya bhavati| 222,23     kiṃsviditaṃ (sama)mama janmanaḥ kiṃ satattvam kathaṃ sviditaṃ kena prakāreṇa svit kiṃ bhaviṣyāmaḥ prāṇo [prāyaṇo]uttarakālam kiṃ vā na bhaviṣyāmaḥ bhaviṣyanto vā ke bhaviṣyāmaḥ kathaṃ bhaviṣyāmaḥ ity evam asya pūrvāparāntamadhyeṣu bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamāneṣv ātmajijñāsā samyagdarśanasya pūrvarūpaṃ svarūpeṇopāvartate| bāheaparigrahāsaṅgābhāvāt svātmaviṣayālocanam ayatnena pravartate| nānyeṣāṃ parigrahasantāpavitānitatṛṣāṃ yatnenāpi jāyate ||2.39||

[223,16] yameṣu siddhaya^ uktāḥ| idānīṃ niyameṣu vakṣyāmaḥ śaucāt svāṅgajugupsā parair asaṃsargaḥ| svāṅgajugupsayā śaucam ārabhamāṇaḥ kāyāvadyadarśī śarīragarhyatvadarśī kāyān abhiṣvaṅgī yatir bhavati| kiñ ca parair asaṃsargo bhavati| kāyasvabhāvāvalokī svam api kāyaṃ jihāsur mṛjjalādibhir ākṣālayann api kāyaśuddhim apaśyan kathaṃ parakāyair atyantam evāprayataiḥ saṃsṛjyata^ iti ||2.40||

[223,21] kiṃ cānyad bhavati| sattvaśuddhisaumanasyāikāgryendriyajayātmadarśana-yogyatvāni cāsya bhavanti| pūrvasya pūrvasya sthairyād uttarasyāvirbhāvaḥ| katham śuceḥ sattvaśuddhiḥ | tataḥ saumanasyam tata^ ekāgryam tataś cendriyajayaḥ tataś ca^ ātmadarśanayogyatvaṃ buddhisattvasya bhavatīty ataḥ śaucasthairyād adhigamyata^ etat sarvam| tataś ceyaṃ śaucasiddhir eva||2.41||

[223,26] santoṣād anuttamaḥ sukhalābhaḥ| santoṣavitarkānutpattāv anuttamaḥ na vidyate'smād anya^ uttamaḥ||

[224,17] tathā coktam — 
yac ca kāmasukhaṃ loke yaccaihikam yac ca divyaṃ mahat sukham| — devādisambandhi| 
tṛṣṇākṣayasukhasyaite nārhataḥ ṣoḍaśīṃ kalām (MBh 12.168.36/12.171.051/12.268.6/13,134.057d@015_4008/) ||2.42||

[224,20] kāyendriyasiddhir aśuddhikṣayāt tapasaḥ| nirvartyamānam eva tapo hinasty aśuddhyāvaraṇam kim aṅga nirvartitam ity abhi(mānaḥ)prāyaḥ||

[224,22] tadāvaraṇam alopagamāt kāyasiddhir aṇimādyā| mātāpitrādisaṃbandhajaṃ kāyam alaṃ baijaṃ cārādijaṃ ca sarvaṃ tapasā nighātayiṣyate| tataś cāṇimādikāyasiddhiḥ||

[224,25] tathā viṣayādisamparkajam indriyāṇām adharmādyāvaraṇaṃ malaṃ tapassrotasvinīsrotasā kṣālyate| tathā indriyasiddhir dūrāc chravaṇād yā ||2.43||

[224,27] svādhyāyād iṣṭadevatāsamprayogaḥ| devāḥ ṛṣayaḥ siddhāś ca ya iṣṭās te svādhyāyaśīlasya darśanaṃ gacchanti kāryeṣu cāsya upadeśādiṣu vartante||2.44||

[225,12] samādhisiddhr īśvarapraṇidhānāt| īśvarārpitasarvabhāvasya samādhiḥ siddhyati| yayā samādhisiddhyā sarvam īpsitam avitathaṃ jānāti| tathā deśāntar kālāntare cāsya prajñā yathābhūtam jānāti||2.45||

[225,15] uktāḥ saha siddhibhir yamaniyamāḥ| idānīṃ āsanādīni vakṣyāmaḥ tatra sthirasukham āsanam| sthiraṃ sukhaṃ ca^ āsanam| yasminn āsane sthirasya manogātrāṇām upajāyate sthiratvam duḥkhaṃ ca yena na bhavati tad abhasyet| tadyathā śāstrāntaraprasiddhāni nāmāni padmāsanādīni pradarśyante||

[225,19] tatra śucau devanilayagiriguhānadīpulinādau jvalanasalilāsamīpe jantuvivarjite nira(ṅga)śmake śuciḥ samyagācamya parama-īśvaram akhilabhu1vanaikanārthaṃ abhivandyāṃś ca yoga-īśvarān ātmagurūṃś ca 
praṇipatya caulajinakuśottaram aduḥkhakaraṃ prāṅmukha^ udaṅmukho vā viṣṭaram adhiṣṭhāyānyatamadeśām āsanaṃ nirbadhnīyāt||

[225,23] tatra padmāsanaṃ nāma savyaṃ pādam upasaṃhṛtya dakṣiṇopari ndadhīta| tathā^ eva dakṣiṇaṃ savyasya^ upariṣṭāt| kaḍyurogrīvaṃ ca viṣṭabhya mṛ(ga)tasuptavann āsikāgranihitadṛṣṭiḥ samudrakavad api hitoṣṭhasampuṭaḥ dantair dantāgramaparāmṛśan muṣṭimātrāntaraviprakṛṣṭacibukoras sthalaḥ rājaddantāntaranihitarasanāgraḥ hastau pāṇyor upari kacchapakaṃ brahmāñjaliṃ vā kṛtvā sakṛd āsthāpitaitthaṃsaṃsthānaḥ punaḥ punaḥ śarīrāvayavaśarīravinyāsaviśeṣaparityaktaprayatnaḥ san yenāsīt tat 
padmāsanaṃ ||

[225,29] etac ca sarvam anyeṣām āsanānām api tulyam| kaścid eva viśeṣaḥ||

[225,30] tathā dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ savyasya^ upari kṛtvā hastaṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ savyahastasya^ upari nidhāya yenāste tat bhadrāsanam| anyat samānam||

[226,11] tathā kuñcitānyatarapādam avanivinyastāparajānukaṃ vīrāsanam| ucyamāna^ eva viśeṣaḥ sarvatra||

[226,13] dakṣiṇaṃ pādāṅguṣṭhaṃ savyenor ujaṅghena parigṛhyād dṛśyaṃ kṛtvā tathā savyaṃ pādāṅguṣṭhuṃ dakṣiṇenorujaṅkgenād dṛśyaṃ parihṛhya yathā ca pārṣṇibhyāṃ vṛṣaṇayor apīḍanaṃ tathā yenāste tat svastikam āsanam||

[226,16] samagulphau samāṅguṣṭhau prasārayan samajānū pādau daṇḍavadyena^ upaviśet tat daṇḍāsanam||

[226,18] (na) yogapaṭṭasaṃ[sthaṃ}stambhāduāśrayaṃ vā sopāśrayam||

[226,19] ājānuprasāritabāhuśayanaṃ paryaṅkāsanam||

[226,20] krauñcaniṣadanaṃ hastiniṣadanaṃ uṣṭraniṣadanaṃ ca krauñcādiniṣadanasaṃsthānasādṛśyād eva draṣṭavyam||

[226,22] bhūmau nyastor ujaṅghanaṃ samasaṃsthitam||

[226,23] sthitaprasrabdhiḥ| anyenāpi prakāreṇa svayam utprekṣya sthitaprasrabdhiḥ| anāyāso yena bhavati tad apy āsanaṃ sthitaprasrabdhī nāma ||

[226,25] yathāsukham ca yena rūpeṇāsīn asya sukhaṃ bhavati tat yathāsukham| ādiśabdād anyad api yathācāryopadiṣṭam āsanaṃ draṣṭavyam||2.46||

[226,27] tad āsanajayābhyupāya^ idānīm upapādyate prayatnaśauthilyānantyasamāpattibhyām| bhavatīti vākyaśeṣaḥ| āsanaṃ dṛḍhaniṣpannaṃ bhavatīty upaskāraḥ| prayatnoparamād āsanabandhottarakālaṃ prayatnākaraṇād vā siddhyati| yena nāṅgamejayo bhavati| yena prayatnoparameṇa| prayatnena hy aṅgaṃ kampayati| yena acalitāsano (227,15) bhavatīty arthaḥ| ānantye vā samāpannam| anantaṃ viśvam anantabhāva^ ānantyam tasmin samāpannaṃ vyāpya viśvabhāvaṃ sthitaṃ cittam āsanaṃ nirvartayati draḍhayati||2.47||

[227,18] tato dvandvānabhighātaḥ| tataḥ āsanasthirībhāvāt| idaṃ dṛṣṭam ānuṣaṅgikam| yat śitoṣṇādibhir dvandvair nābhibhūyate||2.48||

[227,20] kiñ ca prāṇāyāmādiyogyatā ca bhavati| katham tasmin sati śvāsapaśvāsayor gativicchedaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ| saty āsane dṛḍhe bāhyasya vāyor ācamanaṃ cvāsaḥ| yathā santatatvād udakaṃ nālenākṛṣyate tathā santatatvāt nāsikāpuṭanālābhyām apānavāyusaṃbandho bahirvāyur ākṛṣyate tadākarṣaṇaṃ śvāsaḥ| tathā kauṣṭhyavāyor niścāraṇam| prāṇavṛttisambandho hi kauṣṭhyo vāyuḥ| tasya bahirnissāraṇaṃ praśvāsaḥ| tayor gativicchedaḥ| kim uktaṃ bhavaty ata^ āha^ ubhayābhāvaḥ prāṇāyāma^ iti||2.49||

[227,27] sa tu trividhaḥ bāhyābhyantaras tambhavṛttir deśakālasaṃkhyābhiḥ 
paridṛṣṭo dīrghasūkṣmaḥ| sa bāhyavṛttir abhyantaravṛttiḥ stambhavṛttiś ca| yatra śvāsapūrvako gatyabhāvaḥ sa bāhyavṛttiḥ| bāhyasya vāyor antaḥ 
praveśanaṃ prati vṛttiḥ yasya sa bāhyavṛttiḥ| tasmāt sa bāhyaḥ| bāhyam anye pūraka^ ity ācakṣate||

[228,11] yatra praśvāsapūrvako ratyabhāvaḥ sa^ ābhyantaravṛttiḥ| ābhyantarasya vāyor bahirnissāraṇaṃ prati vṛttir yasya sa ābhyantaravṛttiḥ| tasmāt sa^ ābhyantaraḥ| taṃ recaka^ ity ācakṣate||

[228,14] tṛtīyas tu stambhavṛttiḥ| tatrobhayābhāvaḥ ācamananiścāraṇayor abhāvaḥ| sa tu sakṛtprayatnāt prabhavati| yathā tapte nyastaṃ upale jalaṃ sarvataḥ saṃkocam āpadyate tathā sakṛtprayatnāt prāṇādivṛttayaḥ saṃkocam upayāntīty āha dvayor yugapat gatyabhāva iti||

[228,18] trayo apy ete deśena paridṛṣṭāḥ iyān asya deśa iti| yathā bahirvṛtter antarācamyamānasya nāsikāgraprabhṛtyāpādāṅguṣṭhaṃ deśavyāptiḥ| evam antarvṛtter bahirniścāryamāṇasya vāyoḥ pādāṅguṣṭhaprabhṛtyānāsikāgraṃ deśavyāptiḥ| stambhavṛtter āmastakāpādatalavyāptiḥ| atha vā bahir api vitastyādikrameṇa deśavyāptir asty eva||

[228,22] kālena paridṛṣṭāḥ kṣaṇānām iyattāvadhāraṇena^ iyantaḥ kṣaṇāḥ 
prāṇāyāmenātivāhitā iti||

[228,24] tathā saṅlhyābhiḥ paridṛṣṭāḥ etāvadbhiḥ śvṣāsapraśvāsaiḥ atītaiḥ 
paridṛṣṭaḥ prathama^ uddhātaḥ| yathā nirodhakṣubhitānāṃ vāyūnāṃ sakṛt mūrdhānam uddhasya nivṛttiḥ prathama^ uddhātaḥ| sa^ eva mṛduḥ||

[228,27] punaś ca sakrd uddhatanigṛhītasya vāyoḥ tāvadbhiḥ praśvāsair atītaiḥ paridṛṣṭo dvitīya^ iddhātaḥ prāṇāyāmo madhyama^ ity ucyate||

[229,11] tathā tṛtīyaḥ etāvadbhiḥ śvāsapraśvāsaiḥ paridṛṣṭaḥ tṛtīya uddhātaḥ| sa^ eva ca trir uddhātaḥ tīvra^ iti||

[229,13] tathā vāyūnāṃ dīrghatvam andatvāpatteḥ sūkṣmaṃ ca bhavati||2.50||

[229,14] bāhyābhyantaraviṣayākṣepī caturthaḥ bāhyasya viṣayāḥ 
pādāṅguṣṭhādayaḥ| bāhyo hi vāyur ācabhyamāno 'ntardeśān vyāpnoti ākṣipati| ābhyantarasya viṣayā bhūmyādayaḥ| sa ca bahirniścāryamāṇo bhūmyādīn deśakālasaṅkhyāparidṛṣṭān vyāpnoti^ ākṣipati| sa bāhyābhyantaraviṣayāk_ṛṛpī caturthaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ||

[229,18] deśakālasaṅkhyābhir bāhyavastuviṣayaḥ paridṛṣṭa^ ākṣipto vyāptaḥ| tathā^ ābhyantaraviṣayaḥ paridṛṣṭo deśakālasaṅkhyāparidṛṣṭān vyāpnoti ākṣipati| sa bāhyābhyantaraviṣayākṣepī caturthaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ||

[229,23] dseśakālasaṅkhyābhir bāhyavastuviṣayaḥ paridṛṣṭa^ ākṣipto vyāptaḥ| tathā^ ābhyantaraviṣayaḥ paridṛṣṭo deśakālasaṅkhyābhiḥ ākṣipto vyāptaḥ| ubhayathā dīrghasūkṣmaḥ| bāhyābhyantaraviṣayākṣepeṇobhayathā dīrghasūkṣmatvāpannaḥ||

[229,26] kim uktaṃ bhavatīty āha tat-pūrvakaḥ ubhatyaviṣayākṣepapūrvakaḥ| bhūmijayāt ubhayavṛttivāyubhūmijayakrameṇobhayoḥ prāṇāpānavṛttyor bāhyābhyantarayoḥ gatyabhāvaś caturthaḥ||

[229,29] tṛtīyacaturthayor ubhayabṛttisāmānyād aviśeṣatvam āśaṅkyāha tṛtīyas tadviṣayānālocitaḥ sakṛd ārabdha^ eva gatyabhāvo deśakālasaṅkhyābhiḥ paridṛṣṭo dīrghasūkṣmaḥ| caturthas tu śvāsapraśvāsayoḥ ātamitoḥ viṣayāvadhāraṇāt krameṇa vā yad gatyabhāvaś caturthaḥ prāṇāyāṃ ity ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ tṛtīyacaturthayoḥ||2.51||

[230,17] tataḥ kṣīyate prakāśāvaraṇam| prakāśa^ āvriyate yena tat 
prakśāvaraṇaṃ karma| prāṇāyāmānabhyasyato yogino vivekajñānam āvriyate yena tat vivekajñānāvaraṇīyaṃ karma| yat tu^ ācakṣate śāstrāntare mahāmohamayenendrajālena prakāśaśīlaṃ sattvam āvṛtya tad evākārye viniyuṅkte^ iti||

[230,21] yad^ āvriyate viparyayajñānamahendrajālena tad eva sattvam āvaraṇakarmaṇā sarvākārye viniyujyate| tat prāṇāyāmābhyāsād asya 
prakāśāvaraṇaṃ karma saṃsāranibandhanaṃ durbalaṃ bhavati| na kevalaṃ durbalabhāva^ eva pratikṣaṇaṃ ca kṣīyate||

[230,24] tathā ca^ uktam bhavaty anyad api prāṇāyāmābhyāsāt| dhāraṇāsu vakṣyamāṇāsu yogyatā manasaḥ prāṇāyāmād eva pracchardanavidhāraṇābhyāṃ vā 
prāṇasya^ iti vacanāt tābhyāṃ hi manasaḥ sthitisampattir uktā||2.53||

[231,15] atha kaḥ pratyāhāraḥ pratyāhāraviśeṣam abhidhitsamānaḥ praśnena^ upakramate| svaviṣayād indriyāṇāṃ pratyāharaṇamātraṃ pareṣāṃ 
pratyāhāraḥ| atra punar viśeṣavān ucyate svaviṣayāsamprayoge cittasya svarūpānukāra^ ivendriyāṇāṃ pratyāhāra^ iti||

[231,18] svaiḥ svaiś śabdādibhir viṣayaiḥ indriyāṇāṃ śrotrādīnāṃ saṃyogābhāve viṣayadoṣadarśanāt dhyāyināṃ svaviṣayāt nirvartitānāṃ cittasvarūpānukārateva| yoginaś cittam yadakāratāṃ bhajate tat cittasvarūpānukārateveti| 231,21      etad uktaṃ bhavati cittanirodhe cittavannruddhāni nopāyāntaram apekṣ_ṛṛnte jayārtham| cittanirodhād eva tāni nirudhyante| yathā madhukararājānam śrutyakṣaram etat| madhukararājam ity arthaḥ| makṣikā utpatantam anūtpatanti niviśamānam anu niviśante| tathaivendriyāṇi cittanirodhe niruddhānīty eṣa pratyāhāraḥ||2.54||

[231,26] tataḥ pratyāhārasthairyāt paramā vaśyatendriyāṇāṃ bhavati| itiśabdaḥ samāptyarthaḥ| yāsāṃ ca vaśyatānāṃ madhye pratyāhārasthairyāt 
paramā vaśyatā jāyate tā ucyante 232,11      śabdādiṣv indriyajayaḥ avyasanaṃ punaḥ punar apra_Vrttir anāsaktir vā^ iti kecid āhuḥ| vyasanam iti nirbravīti saktir vyasanam| katham vyasyati kṣipati^ enaṃ 
pratipattāraṃ śreyasaḥ sakāśād iti vyasanam||

[232,14] aviruddhā pratipattir vā| viruddhānāṃ vā śabda^ādīnām apratipattir vaśyateti| tasya^ eva vyākhyānaṃ nyāyyaḥ śāstrānujñātaḥ śabdādiṣu saṃyogaḥ svecchyā| teṣv aviruddheṣu yadīcchati pravartate na vā ity evam anye manvate ||

[232,17] rāgadveṣābhāve sukhaduḥkhaśūnyaṃ heyopādeyatvavarjitaṃ śabdādijñānāṃ vaśyatety eke| madhymāyāṃ tu rāgadveśṣasadbhāvamātram api na vāryata^ iti viśeṣaḥ||

[232,21] paramā tv iyaṃ sarvāsāmuktānām iyaṃ paramā vaśyatendriyāṇāṃ yat yasmin cittaniruddhānīndriyāṇi netarendriyajayavat pūrvoktāsv indriyavaśyatāsūpāyāntaram apekṣante jayārthaṃ asyāṃ punar na 
prayatnakṛtam upāyāntaram apekṣante yoginaḥ||2.55||

[232,25] iti śrīgovindabhgavatpūjyapādaśiṣyasya 
paramahāṃsaparvrājakācāryasya śrīśaṅkarabhagavataḥ kṛtau śrīpātāñjalayoga(śāstra)sūtrabhāṣyavivaraṇe || dvitīyaḥ sādhanapādaḥ||





[233,13] uktāni pañca bahiraṅgāni sādhanāni samprajñātasya yogasya| adhunā^ antaraṅgaṃ dhāraṇādhyānasamādhisamākhyaṃ sādhanatrayaṃ tasya^ evābhidhīyate| bahiraṅgāntaraṅgatvabhedād eva hi bahiraṅgasādhanavyākhyānena^ eva pādaḥ parisamāpitaḥ| saṃhatyakāritvāc ca sādhanatrayasya pṛthagvacanam| bahiraṅgasādhanābhidhānānantaraparyupasthāpitāntaraṅgasādhanapratipādanena 
pādasambandho vyākhyātaḥ| asmiṃś ca pāde vibhūtayaḥ prādhānyena 
pradarśyanta^ iti ca^ ṛthak(tvād ā)pādārāmbhaḥ| etad eva hi samākhyālābhe kāraṇam avādiṣma||

[233,20] dhāraṇā vaktavyā| pratyāhārānantaraṃ kramopanītasannidhānā dhāraṇā^ abhidhānīyā| deśabandhaś cittasya dhāraṇā| deśabandhanaṃ deśabandhaḥ| kasya cittasya||

[233,23] svayam eva ca sarvaṃ vyācaṣṭe nābhicakre nābhideśaś cakram iva| sarve(vāyava)avayavās tatra cakrībhūtā iti nābhicakram| hṛdayaṃ 
puṇḍarīkākāram| mūrdhajyotiṣi| mūrdhanāḍīdvāraṃ prabhāsvaratvāj jyotir ity ākhyāyate| nāsikāgre jihvāgre^ ity evam ādiṣu deśeṣu bāhyeṣu ca viṣayeṣu candrādityādiṣu cittaṃ badhyate||

[234,12] tasya cittasya^ ity evam ādiṣv apracalitarūpeṇa yā vṛttiḥ sā dhāraṇety ucyate| vṛttimātreṇeti| tad-deśapratyayamātreṇa vikṣepaśūnyatayā vartata^ iti ||3.1||

[234,14] tatra pratyayaikatānatā dhyānam| tasmin deśe dhāraṇāvṛttiviṣaye nābhicakrādau| dhyeyālambanasya dhyeyo deśādi tadālambanasya| 
pratyayasya [ekatānatā] sadṛśapravāhaḥ tulyapratyayānāṃ pravāha^ ekākāraḥ 
pratyayasantānaḥ pratyayāntareṇa vijātīyena aparāmṛṣṭaḥ anākīrṇaḥ| tat dhyānam||

[234,19] dhāraṇā tu tasminn eva dhyeye tadavasthasya^ eva cittasya tadviṣayavikalptaiḥ pratyayāntaraiḥ parāmṛṣṭā api| yathā sūrye dhāryamāṇasya tad-gataparimaṇḍalatīvrataradīdhititvādipratyayeṣv api dhāraṇā^ eva| vṛttimātreṇa tatra^ eva vartamānatvāc cittasya| dhyānaṃ 
punaḥ na tu tathā| bhinnajātīyapratyayāntarāparāmṛṣṭa-ekapratyayapravāha^ eva hi dhyānam||3.2||

[234,23] tad evārthamātranirbhāsaṃ svarūpaśūnyam iva samādhiḥ| tad eva dhyānaṃ tulyapratyayapravāharūpaṃ svām ekapratyayasantānatām iva hitvā dhyeyākāranirbhāsaṃ dhyeyākāravad avabhāsate| pratyayātmakena svena grahaṇātmanā svarūpeṇa śūnyam iva| yathā sphaṭikadravyam upadhānāvabhāsaṃ svarūpaśūnyam iva| yadā bhavati dhyeyasvabhāvāveśāt kāraṇāt cittasya tadā tad eva dheānaṃ samādhir ity ucyate||

[234,28] nanu ca yogaḥ samādhir ity aṅgī pūrvam uktaḥ| ihāṅgo yogasya samādhir ity ākhyāyate| kaḥ punar atrāṅgāṅginor bheda^ iti^ ucyate yogaḥ samādhir iti cittasthitiviśeṣo vivakṣitaḥ| iha tu pratyayasantānasya^ eva dhyeyasvabhāvāveśāt dhyeyākāratvam aṅgam iti viśeṣaḥ||3.3||

[235,15] tad etad-dhāraṇā-dhyānasamādhitrayaṃ yad vyākhyānaṃ tat ekatra ekasmin deśe pariniṣpannaṃ sat sāṃyama^ ity ucyate| tad āha^ ekaviṣayāṇi trīṇi sādhanāni saṃyama^ ity ucyate| tad asya trayasya^ itthaṃ krameṇābhiniṣpannasya yatra yatra saṃyamaḥ śrūyate jetavyajayāya vā tatra tatra trayam etat praveditavyam ity evam artham etasya trayasyāsmin śāstre paribhāṣeyaṃ saṃyama^ iti||

[235,21] vakṣyati ca pariṇāmatrayasaṃyamād atītānāgatajñānam sthūlasvarūpasūkṣmānvayārthavattvasaṃyamād bhūtajayaḥ iti ca||3.4||

[235,23] tajjayāt prajñā 'lokaḥ| tasya saṃyamasya jayāt sthiratvāpādanāt abhīpsitārthābhivyañjanasamarthaḥ pradīpālokadeśīyaḥ samādhiprajñāyā bhavaty ālokaḥ.[235,25]        yathā yathā saṃyamaḥ sthirapado bhavati tathā tathā samāhiprajñā sthirapadī bhavati| yena samādhiprajñā 'lokena vyavahitaviprakṛṣṭādivastunirbhāsanasamarthena yoginaḥ karatalakalitam ivābhimatam artham avalikayanti||3.5||

[235,28] tasya bhūmiṣu viniyogaḥ| tasya saṃyamasya bhūmiṣu bāhyādhyātmikeṣu dhyānālambanabhūtāsu vakṣyamāṇāsu pariṇāmatrayādiṣu viniyogaḥ prayogaḥ kartavyaḥ||

[236,7] tadbhūmijayasākṣāt prayojanārthinā tasya saṃyamasya jitabhūmeḥ jitāyā bhūmeḥ yatra bhūmau yoginaḥ saṃyatāyāṃ saṃyamo labdhaḥ tasyā jitāyā bhūmeḥ anantarā yā bhūmis tatra saṃyamasya pūrvabhūmijayānantaraṃ viniyogaḥ karaṇīyaḥ ||

[236,10] tad yathā pṛthivyādīnāṃ sthūlasvarūpasūkṣmānvayārthavattvabhūmiṣu krameṇa jetavyāsu sthūāyāṃ bhūmau saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā tajjayānantaraṃ svarūpa^ evānantare saṃyamo viniyojanīyah| nānantaraṃ svarūpam vyatilaṅghya sūkṣmādibhūmiṣu saṃvidadhīta||

[236,13] kasmād evam na hy ajitādharabhūmir anantarāṃ bhūmiṃ vilaṅghya 
prāntabhūmiṣv uparitanīṣu bhūmiṣu saṃyamaṃ labhate||

[236,15] yadi cānantarāṃ bhūmim ajitvā prāntabhūmiṣu saṃyamaṃ viniyuṅkṣeta tam eva saṃyamaṃ na labhate| tataś ca tad alābha^ eva doṣaḥ| tadalābhāc ca saṃyamābhāvāc ca kutas tatra saṃyamaviṣaye prāntabhūmāv ity arthaḥ| prajñā 'lokaḥ| nimittābhāvāt| na hy asati tailavartijvalanābhisannipāte pradīpālokaḥ ||

[236,19] jitottarabhūmikasya jitottarātmādibhūmikasya yoginaḥ nādharabhūmiṣu paracittajñānādiṣu saṃyamo yuktaḥ| kasmāt tadarthasya ātmādeḥ uttarabhūmer apakṛṣṭaparacittajñānādibhūmivaiparītyena anyathā^ evādhigatatvāt| paracittajñānādibhiḥ saṃkīrṇaṃ asamādhigatavivekātmakatvāt| tatra hi jitottarabhūmisaṃyamasya parakīyacittālambanatve saṃkīrṇacittākāratā yogicittasyāpi syād iti na yuktaḥ| tatrātmāder arthasyānyathā^ eva viviktatayā 'adhigatatvāt||

[237,11] anyeṣāṃ vyākhyānam arthasya paracittajñājādeḥ| anyathā^ evādhigatatvāt ātmādyuttarabhūmisamādhigamanena^ eva adharaparacittajñānādibhūmer arthasyādhigatatvāt iti||

[237,13] asmin pakṣe^ adharabhūmiṣu saṃyamo 'narthaka^ iti vaditavyam abhaviṣyat na na yukta^ iti| na yukta^ ity etad asamīcīnam iva dṛśyate||

[237,15] bhūmer asyā iyam anantarābhūmiḥ iyam anantaraṃ jayanīyā^ iti katham avabhotsyata^ ity āha tatra yoga^ evopādhyāyaḥ| yogaḥ pūrvadhūmau saṃyamalābhaḥ| tena^ eva^ ānantaryam avagamyate||

[237,18] katham evaṃ hy uktam yogena yogo jñātavyaḥ| pūrvasaṃyamalābhena yogenānantaro togaviṣayaḥ prajñānīyaḥ| sa ca yogaḥ saṃyamaḥ tyogāt eva 
pūṛvasaṃyamalābhād eva pravartate vivicyate||

[237,21] yathā^ eva januṣā^ andhakasya sopānapaṅktimārurukṣataḥ 
prathamasopānapratilabdhapadasparśapratyayena^anantarasopānajñānam upaploṣyate tathā yogo yogāt pravartate||

[237,23] yata^ evaṃ tasmāt sa^ itthaṃ jānan apramattas tu yogī yogena yuktaḥ san yogeṣu yogaiśvaryaphaleṣu ramate ciram iti||2.6||

[237,25] trayam antaraṅgaṃ pūrvebhyaḥ| dhāraṇādhyānasamādhitrayam antaraṅgaṃ pūrvebhyaḥ pañcabhyaḥ sādhanebhyaḥ yamādibhyaḥ samprajñātasya yogasya| tatrāntaraṅgagrahaṇaṃ pūrvasādhanāsampattāv apy atra trayo^ ādaraḥ karaṇīya^ ity evam artham||

[238,9] vināpi hi yamādisādhanapañcatayasampadanena janmāntaravihitasaṃskārāveśavaśād eva videhaprakṛtilayānam iva dhāraṇāditrayasampattyā yoga^ upakalpyate| dhāraṇāditrayeṇa vinā na yogaḥ saṃbhavati kasyacit| tadvyāpāranairantaryasaṃvādātmakatvād dhāraṇādhitrayasya| yagasya cittasampattirūpatvāt||

[238,13] yadā tu jñānavairāgyasampattiḥ tadā na kiṃcid anyaddhāraṇādy apy apekṣyate| tathā ca maṅkipiṅgalāprabhṛtīnām anuśrūyate vairāgyād eva samastasiddhiḥ| tathā ca^ uktam sthānāsanavidhānānīti pūrvatra ślokadvayam ||3.7||

[238,16] tad api vbahiraṅgaṃ nirbījasya tad api^ etat antaraṅgam api sabījasya yogasya bahiraṅgaṃ bhavati nirbījasya yogasya| kasmāt tad abhāve bhāvāt| tasya sabījāntaraṅgasādhanatrayasyābhāve api puruṣasarvaviśeṣāpekṣayā nirbījayogasya bhāvāt bahiraṅgatvam||

[238,20] kecid atisamīcīnadarśanā janmaniva saṃskārādparaktāḥ| teṣāṃ 
paravairāgyavirāmapratyayasaṃśīlanādisamīkṣayā nirbījaḥ samādhir upajaniṣyata^ eva| na hi teṣāṃ dhāraṇādisamīkṣaṇam| tathā ca^ uktam uktaḥ samāhitacittasya yogaḥ vyutthitacitto api kathaṃ yoga-yuktaḥ syād ity ata idam ārabhyate^ iti| tathā bhavapratyayo videhaprakṛtilayānām iti||3.8||

[238,25] atha nirodhacittakṣaṇeṣu nirodhayuktaṃ niarudhyamānaṃ cittaṃ yeṣu kṣaṇeṣu nirudhyate te nirodhacittakṣaṇāḥ teṣu nirodhakāle^ ity arthaḥ| kṣaṇeṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ atītānāgatavartamānavṛttitvapradarśanārtham||

[239,10] nirodhasya (trikṣaṇaṃ)kṣaṇo hi nirodhadharmam anvīyamānaṃ cittaṃ nirudhyate| calaṃ guṇavṛttaṃ gauṇaṃ cittaṃ guṇavṛttaṃ calam ity avaśyaṃ bhāvī tasya bāhyavṛttibhyo nirudhyamānacittasya pariṇāmḥ| na hy apariṇamamānasya cittasya puruṣavat kauṭasthye nirodha^ upakalpate| tataś ca^ āha kīdṛśas tadā nirodhapariṇāma^ iti||

[239,14] vyutthānanirodhasaṃskārayor abhibhavaprādur bhāvau nirodhakṣaṇacittānvayo nirodhapariṇāmaḥ| vyutthānaṃ vividham utthānaṃ calanaṃ pratyayākāreṇa cittasya| tadupajanitaḥ saṃskāraś cittasya dharmiṇo dharmaḥ| na sa pratyayātmakaḥ||

[239,17] tathā nirodhajanmā saṃskārah apratyayātmakaḥ cittasya^ eva dharmaḥ| sa ca vyutthānasaṃskāro 'pratyayātmaka^ iti sa pratyayanirodhe na nirudhyate| vyutthānapratyayanirodhe nābhibhūtaḥ||

[239,20] pratyayanirodhāc ca nirodhajaḥ saṃskāraḥ sañjāyate| tayoḥ vyutthānanirodhasaṃskārayoḥ dharmiṇi citte vartamānayoḥ abhibhavaprādurbhāvau bhavataḥ||

[239,22] kiṃ tācāvirbhāvatirobhāvau aniyamena bhavataḥ na| kathaṃ tarhi| vyutthānasaṃskārā hīyante svakāryākṣamatvam upāśnuvate| nirodhasaṃskārā ādhīyante| tau cābhibhavaprādurbhāvau nirodhakṣaṇaṃ cittaṃ dharmitvena naveti sa tayor abhibhavaprādurbhāvayoś cittenānugamo nirodhapariṇāmaś cittasya||

[239,26] tat etad abhihitaṃ bhavati^ ekasya cittasya pratikṣaṇam idaṃ saṃskārānyathātvaṃ vyutthānasaṃskārābhibhavena nirodhasaṃskāraprādurbhāvena saṃskārānyathātvam| vyutthānasaṃskārāṇām api hīyamānatayā saty api sambandhe prādurbhavatā nirodhena^ eva balavattvāt nirodhapariṇāma^ iti samākhyātam||

[240,12] [tadā] nirodhyamānaṃ bāhyebhyaḥ pratyayebhyaḥ saṃskāraśeṣaṃ cittam iti vyākhgyātam virāmapratyayābhyāsapūrvaḥ saṃskāraśeṣo 'nyaḥ iti||

[240,14] kim etena^ āha nirodhāvasānatvāt pratyayābhāvāvasānatayā 
pariṇāmasya nirodhapariṇāma^ iti||

[240,16] samprajñātāvasthāyāṃ santo .api pratyayā na vivakṣitāḥ
 kiṃ tu pratikṣaṇasaṃskārānyathātvam eva vivakṣyate| yathā niruddhabhūmini cetasi vidyamāno .api samādhiḥ na vivakṣyate| nirodhamātrasya vivakṣitatvāt| tathā sabījāvasthāyām api saṃskārānyathātvamātram eva vivakṣitam| na pratyayāḥ samādhir ekāgratā ceti||3.9||

[240,20] tasya cittasya nirodhapariṇāmam anugacchataḥ praśāntavāhitā saṃskārāt| nirodhasaṃskārapāṭavāpekṣā nirodhasaṃskārasya pāṭavaṃ draḍhimā draḍhimā tadapekṣā praśāntavāhitā prasannata¯cittasya bhavati||

[240,23] sā praśāntavāhitā kiyantaṃ kālam ity āha---taṃ saṃskāraṃ nirodhasaṃskāraṃ ārabhya yāvat vyutthānadharmiṇā vyutthaānaṃ dharmo byutthānajanitatvād yasya tena saṃskāreṇa nirodhadharmasaṃskāro nābhbhūyate, tāvantaṃ kālam asya praśāntavāhitā| nirodhasaṃskārasamudayād iti||3.10||

[241,12] sarvārthataikāgratayoḥ kṣayodayau cittasya samādhipariṇāmaḥ| sarvārthatā bhogāpavargārthayogyatāvicitratā cittadharmaḥ| vakṣyati---"draṣṭṛdṛśyoparaktaṃ cittaṃ sarvārtham" iti| ekāgratāpi cittasya dharmaḥ śāntoditapratyayoḥ sāmyaṃ samādhīyamānasya cittasya sarvārthatāyāḥ kṣayaḥ bhavati| tirobhāva ity arthaḥ| na hi kiṃcidvidyamānaṃ vinaṅkṣyati| ekāgratāyā udayaḥ bhavati| āvirbhāva ity arthaḥ| nāvidyamānam utpadyate||

[241,17] tayoh kṣayodayayoḥ dharmitvenānugataṃ cittam| pūrvasūtrād anvayagrahaṇam ihāpi sannidhīyate| sarvārthatāyāḥ kṣayam ekāgratāyāś codayaṃ niyamenānvīyamānaṃ cittaṃ samādhiprādhānyena pariṇamate| yo .asau cittena sarvārthataikāgratākṣayodayayor anugamah sa samādhipariṇāmaḥ| samādheś ca prādhānayam ekāgratābhūmau vyākhyātam "yas tv ekāgre cetasi sa bhūtam arthaṃ pradyotayati" iti||

[241,22] tad evāha---tad idaṃ cittam, apāyopajanau svātmabhūtau avyatiriktau tayor dharmayoḥ sarvārthataikāgratayoḥ anu(bhavā)gamāt samādhīyate| sa cittasya samādhiprādhānyāt samādhipariṇāmaḥ||3.11||

[241,26] tatra punaḥ samādhikālae śāntoditau tulyapratyayau cittasyaikāgratāpariṇāmaḥ| samāhitacittasya niruddhabāhyavṛtteḥ pūrvaḥ 
pratyayaḥ śāntaḥ tirobhūtaḥ| uttaras tatsadṛśa^ uditaḥ prādurbhūtaḥ| samādhicittaṃ samāhānaviśiṣṭaṃ samādhyavastham| samādhicittamubhayoḥ śāntoditayoḥ pratyayoḥ anugatam||

[242,10] punas tathā^ eva yathā pratyaya^ ādyaḥ śānta^ uttaras tatsadṛśa upajātaḥ sa ca śāntaḥ punar anya uditaḥ sa śāntaḥ punaś cānya^ uditaḥ ity evaṃ ā samādhibhreṣāt vyutthānasaṃskāreṇa yāvat samādhiḥ vibhraṃśate||

[242,13] sa khalv ayaṃ dharmiṇaḥ cittasya pratikṣaṇaṃ sad_ṛṛśaśāntoditapratyayayor anugamaḥ ekāgratāpariṇāmaḥ||

[242,15] tatra yāvat bāhyavṛttinirodhād ārabhya cittasya saṃskārapariśeṣatā tāvat nirodhapariṇāmaḥ||

[242,17] tathā bāhyapratyayanirodhāt prabhṛti^ ā prāk aśeṣapratyayanirodhāt aśeṣapratyayanirodhottarakālaṃ ca^ ekāgratodayābhāvāt tāvat samādhipariṇāmaḥ||

[242,19] tathā samāhipariṇāmakāla^ eva śāntoditayos tulyayoḥ saṃbhavād ekāgratāpariṇāmaḥ| pūrvapariṇā(māna)manāntarīyakatvam uttarottarapariṇāmasya^ iti krameṇa trayo api pariṇāmā vyākhyātāḥ||

[242,22] kim anena pariṇāmānvākhyānena prayojanam ucyate calaṃ guṇavṛttaṃ gauṇaṃ ca samastaṃ pariṇāmadharmakam anavasthitam iti vairāgyabhāvanārtham| tathā pariṇāmatrayasaṃyamād atītānāgatajñānārthaṃ ca^ etad anvākhyānam||3.12||

[242,25] etena bhūtendriyeṣu dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmā vyākhyātāḥ| etena pūrvoktena cittapariṇāmena| kiṃciśiṣṭena dharmalakṣaṇāvasthārūpeṇa dharmarūpeṇa lakṣaṇarūpeṇāvasthā-rūpeṇa ca| bhūtendriyeṣu bhūteṣu pṛthivī-prabhṛtiṣu sthūleṣu^ indriyeṣu śrotrādiṣu yeṣāṃ tattvāntarapariṇāmo nāsti| dharmapariṇāmo lakṣaṇapariṇāmo'vasthāpariṇāmaś ca^ ukto veditavyaḥ||

[243,7] cittasyānavasthitatvena gamyamānena pariṇāmena bhūtendriyāṇāṃ 
pariṇāmasya pariṇāmasya prakāratrayayoginaḥ cittapariṇativat asuvijñānatvāt atideśaḥ kriyate||

[243,9] nanu ca nirodhasamādhy-ekāgratāp-ariṇāmāś cittasyābhihitāḥ| na tu dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmāḥ| kutas tatrātideśa^ ity abhihitatvam eṣāṃ darśayati, tatra vyutthānanirodhayoḥ dharmayoḥ abhibhavaprādur-bhāvau dharmiṇi citte dharmapariṇāmaḥ||

[243,12] etasmād dhi dharmabhedavacanāt dharmabhedam avādiṣma| tayoś ca dharmapariṇāmayoh vyutthānanirodhayor abhibhavaprādurbhāvavacanāt lakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmāv api cāvādiṣma^ eva||

[243,15] katham vartamānalakṣaṇād atītalakṣaṇam abhisampadyamāno dharmo 'abhibhūyate| yaś cānāgatalakṣaṇāt vartamānalakṣaṇam abhisampadyamānaḥ sa prādur asti| tad abhibhavaprādur-bhāvavacanāc ca pratikṣaṇaṃ durbalarūpo 'vasthāpariṇāma^ ukto bhavatīty āha lakṣaṇapariṇāmī kūṭastha^ ity ucyate||

[243,19] sa khalu nirodhaḥ anāgatalakṣaṇam adhvanaṃ hitvā dharmatvaṃ nirodhadharmatvaṃ anatikrāntaḥ asatkāryārambhāvāt| asatkāryārambhe hi trikālatvam ekasya vastuno na^ upapadyate (nāpi) kālatrayaparicchedyasya trilakṣaṇasyābhāvāt| ata^ ucyate dharmatvam anatikrāmann eva vartamānaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pratipannaḥ yatra yasmin vartamāne lakṣaṇe^ asya svarūpeṇābhivyaktiḥ eṣo^ asya nirodhasyānāgatādhvasamīkṣayā dvitīyo 'dhvā||

[244,10] yadā tu vyutthānaṃ vartamānaṃ lakṣaṇam ārokṣyati tadā nirodho 'tītalakṣaṇam adhvānaṃ tṛtīyaṃ prāpsyatīti sa tṛtīyo 'dhvā bhaviṣyaty eva| 244,12      syād etat vartamānalakṣaṇapratipattau nirodhasya tryadhvaṃ nāstīty ata^ āha na cātītānāgatābhyāṃ lakṣaṇābhyāṃ viyuktaḥ| kutaḥ yena sa^ evānāgato vartamāno 'tītaś ceti||

[244,15] tathā vyutthānaṃ trilakṣaṇaṃ tribhir adhvabhiḥ atītānāgatavartamānaiḥ yuktaṃ vartamānalakṣaṇaṃ hitvā dharmatvam anatikrāntaṃ yathā vyākhgyātam nirodhe nirodhini dharme vartamānam adhvānam ahisampadyamāne^ atītalakṣaṇaṃ adhvvānaṃ pratipannam||

[244,18] eṣo^ asya vyutthānasya vartamānaṃ prathamam adhvānam abhisamīkṣya dvitīyo 'dhvā| dvitīye tu paryāye tṛtīyua^ evādhvā dvitīyo bhaviṣyati| na cānāgatavartamānābhyāṃ lakṣaṇābhyāṃ viyuktam||

[244,21] punar vyutthānaṃ nirodhasaṃskāreṇa^ upasampadyamānam anāgatalakṣaṇaṃ adhvānaṃ hitvā dharmatvam anatikrāntaṃ vartamānalakṣaṇaṃ 
pratipannam| yatrāsya svarūpeṇābhivyaktau satyāṃ vyāpāraḥ svakāryāpādanasāmarthyam| eṣo^ asya svarūpeṇābhivyaktaū satyāṃ vyāpāraḥ svakāryāpādanasāmarthyam| eṣo^ asya anāgatābhisamīkṣyā dvitīyo 'dhvā| na cātītānāgatābhyāṃ lakṣaṇābhyāṃ viyuktam ||

[244,25] yathā cittaṃ vyutthānanirodhadharmābhyāṃ na yujyate| evaṃ punar nirodhaḥ evaṃ punaḥ vyutthānam iti^ eṣa lakṣaṇapariṇāmaś cittadharmayor vyutthānanirodhayoḥ||

[245,10] tathā^ avasthāpariṇāmaḥ paridṛśyate| katham nirodhakṣaṇeṣu nirodhe vartamāne nirodhasaṃskārā balavanto bhavanti| saṃskārabalavattvena nirodhabalavattvam api| durbalā vyutthānasaṃskārāḥ tata^ eva vyutthānadurbalatvam api| tathā vyutthānalakṣaṇe vyutthānasaṃskārabalīyastvaṃ nirodhasaṃskāradurbalatvam ity evaṃ 
pratikṣaṇaṃ dṛḍhadurvalāvatthābhyāṃ yujyamānānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ avasthāpariṇāmaḥ||

[245,15] tatra kena rūpeṇa kasya pariṇāma^ iti darśayati dharmiṇo dharmaiḥ 
pariṇāmaḥ| yathā cittasya dharmiṇo nirodhavyutthānādidharmaiḥ| dharmāṇāṃ tryadhvanāṃ lakṣaṇaiḥ pariṇāmaḥ| yathā nirodhādicittadharmāṇāṃ anāgatādilakṣaṇaiḥ| lakṣaṇānām apy avasthābhiḥ pariṇāmaḥ|  yathā vartamānādilakṣaṇānāṃ dṛḍhadurbalatvādyavasthābhiḥ| ity evaṃ dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmaiḥ aśūnyalakṣaṇam aśūnyarūpaṃ guṇāvṛttam avatiṣṭhete ||

[245,23] calaṃ guṇavṛttam| etad eva tu guṇasvabhāvyaṃ pravṛttikāraṇaṃ sakalavyāpārakāraṇaṃ uktaṃ guṇānāṃ sattvādīnām iti| etena cetasi 
prasiddhena dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmatriyena bhūtyendriyeṣu dharmadharmibhedāt trividhaḥ pariṇāmo veditavyaḥ||

[246,10] paramārthatas tv eka^ eva pariṇāmaḥ| katham dharmisvarūpamātro hi dharmaḥ| na hi dharmiparihāreṇa dharmo nāma pṛthag asti| nāpi dharmaparityāgena lakṣaṇaṃ nāma pṛthak bhāvyate| nāpi lakṣaṇavyatirekeṇāvasthānāṃ pṛthaktvaṃ bhavati| tena dharmivikriyā^ evaiṣā dharmadvārā dharmabhedadvāreṇa tridhā prapañcyate||

[246,14] kathaṃ punar dharmivikriyā^ evaiṣā| na punar atyāntāpūrvotpattivināśāv iti tata^ āha tatra dharmasya ghaṭādeḥ dharmiṇi mṛdākhye vartamānasyādhvasvatītānāgatavartamāneṣu bhāvānyathātvam eva 
pratyayānyathātvam atīto ghaṭaḥ vartate^ anāgataḥ iti ca dharmiṇivartamānasya^ eva| sa^ eva dharmasyāvirbhāvatirobhāvānyathātvam| na dravyānyathātvaṃ tad eva mṛd-dravyaṃ dharmi rūpā-antaraṃ ca||

[246,19] udāharaṇaṃ paridarśayati yathā suvarṇabhājanasya bhitvā^ anyathā kuṇḍalādibhāvena kriyāmāṇasya bhāvānyathātvaṃ svastikakuṇḍalādisaṃsthānānyatvam| na suvarṇadravyānyathātvam iti||

[246,22] apara^ āhāsatkāryasamārambhābhimānī dharmābhyadhiko dharmavyatirikto dharmī dharmaś cāvidyamāno^ anyaś cānyaś ca samudāyiṣyate| na punar eko dharmī dharmeṣu bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamāneṣu dharmāvyatirikto 'nvayīti||

[246,25] yady anvayī dharmānabhyadhiko dharmāvyatiriktaḥ pūrvatattvāt 
pūrvapūrvadravyasvarūpāt anatikrāman pūrvāparāvasthābhedam atītādyavasthābhedam anugatitaḥ anugataḥ syāt tataś ca^ eṣa doṣaḥ prasajati sa dharmī tavābhimataḥ kauṭasthyenāpi parivartetāpariṇāmmitve varteta||

[247,5] atrāhāyam adoṣaḥ| kasmāt ekāntānabhyupagamāt ātyantakauṭasthyavināśayoḥ dharmiṇo'nabhyupagamāt| yady atyantaṃ kūṭastho dharmī syāt ātmavat tataś cāsmād dharmiṇo dharmāṇām abhedāt kauṭasthaṃ 
prasajyateti doṣaḥ syāt| na tu dharmiṇāṃ guṇānām atyantakauṭastham abhyupagamyate| pariṇāminityatvaṃ hi guṇānāṃ vadiṣyati||

[246,10] atha vā dharmadharmiṇoḥ ekāntabhedānabhyupagamāt| ekāntena hi dharmibhyo'nvayibhyo dharmiṇāṃ [ā]bhedaṃ kauṭasthyaṃ prāpnuyāt| atyantabhede vā dharmāṇāṃ atyantavināśaḥ syāt| na tv atyantabhedābhedāv abhyupayiṣyete| tasmāt na kauṭastgyadoṣa^ iti||

[247,14] kathaṃ kauṭasthyābhāva^ ity āha tad etat vailokyaṃ vyakter apaiti na kūṭastham| kasmāt nityatvapratiṣedhāt| kāryatvād eva hi nityatvaṃ 
pratiṣiddham| athātyntavinaṣṭam iti ced āhāpetam apy asti vināśapratiṣedhāt| kāraṇasya nityatvāt tad0abhedāc ca kāryasya atyantavināśaḥ pratiṣiddha^ eva||

[247,18] tataś ca yad^ ucyate kaiś cit viruddhahetvābhāsodāharaṇam etad iti kathaṃ yathā^ asau vikāro vyakter apaiti nityatvapratiṣedhāt apetam apy asti vināśapratipadhot ity atra nityatvapratiṣedhāt ity ayaṃ hetuḥ apeto api vikāro 'stīty anena siddhāntena virudhyate||

[247,22] katham vyaktir ātmalābhaḥ apāyaḥ pracyutiḥ yady ātmalābhāt 
pracyuto vikāro 'sti nityatvapratiṣedho na^ upapadyate| yat tu vyakter apetasyāpi vikārasyāstitvaṃ tat khalu nityatvam iti||

[247,25] nityatvapratiṣedho nāma vikārasyātmalābhāt pracyutiḥ| yadātmalābhāt pracyuvate tad anityam| yad^ asti na tāatmalābhāt 
pracyuvate | astitvaṃ ca^ ātmalābhāt pracyutiś ceti viruddhāv etau dharmau saha na sambhavataḥ| so 'yaṃ hetuḥ yaṃ siddhāntam āśritya 
pravartate tam eva vyāhantīti viruddho hatur iti ||

[248,1] nāyaṃ doṣaḥ| vastūnāṃ vyaktāvyaktadharmakatvābhyupagamāt| yathā hi ghaṭādividyamāno api san agāre na^ upalabhyate| na ca tāvatā^ asau nāstīti śakyaṃ pratijñātum| ālokopāyena hi vyajyate| tathā idaṃ trailokyaṃ kāraṇe saṃsṛṣṭaṃ vidyamānam eva anabhivyaktātmakaguṇasvabhāvatayā puruṣārthanimittenāviṣkriyate ||

[248,5] tathā^ eva ca gatisaṃskārakṣaye sthitisaṃskārābhivyaktau kāraṇaṃ 
prati saṃsṛjyamānaṃ vyakter apaiti| yathā dīpālokatirodhāne vidyamāno api ghaṭādih santam asāvaṣṭambhāt vyakter apasarati tathā^ atrāpi mahadādayo dharmāḥ tryadhvānaḥ saṃsargavisargadharmāṇaḥ na tu sattvādayo dharmiṇaḥ||

[248,9] tatas trailokyaṃ mahadādistambaparyavasānaṃ sattvādīnāṃ dharmabhūtam[na]bhivyaktidharmakaṃ sat atyantaniruddhasūkṣmadharmasamudāyāt vyaktisthūladharmarūpeṇa na gṛhyata ity ucyate tad etat trailokyaṃ vyakter apaitīti| na punaḥ darmisvarūpeṇāpāyaḥ| tathā ca dharmirūpeṇa nityatvād ucyate tadapetam apy astīti||

[248,13] tad evaṃ vyaktirūpaś ca śaktirūpaś ca^ eko dharmī vyaktirūpeṇa ca tasyānityatvaṃ śaktirūpeṇa ca nityatvam iti katham eṣa virodhaḥ syāt| na hi devadattaś calanādivyāpāreṇa sarvadā gṛhyamāṇaś calanādivirāme viraṃsyati| calanam eva hi tirodhīyate| tasmād avyaktāvasthitaghaṭādivat||

[248,18] vyaktir api svakīyadharmāntarasamīkṣayā dharmiṇī| sā ca dharmirūpeṇanityā dharmarūpeṇānityā^ abhyupeyate| evaṃ vikāreṣu dharmadharmibhedo 'anavasthita^ ity anavasthāapi iṣyata^ eva||

[248,21] tatra vyakter apaitīty anena na virudhyate nityatvapratiṣedhāt ity ayaṃ hetuḥ| nāpi^ apatem apy astīty anena virodhaḥ| ubhayor bhinnaviṣayatvāt| nāpi vināśapratiṣedhāt ity ayaṃ apetam apy astīty anena virudhyate| yathā bāhyād apeto apy asti devadattam||

[249,1] tad etad anirjñāya parābhiprāyaṃ virodhodāharaṇatvāpādānam andhadarpaṇīyavṛttāntam udbhāvayati| yathā kila kaścid andhaḥ svāṃ adarśanaśaktiṃ anavadhārya svārtham ādarśam upājayati tadvad etad ābhāti||

[249,4] api ca yady apy ekatraivavyaktau śaktau vā nityatvam anityatvaṃ ca adhyāropya tvaṃ pakṣasiṣādhayiṣayā bravīthāḥ evam api viruddhodāharaṇatvaṃ na sambhavati viparītā(ta)sādhanāt| katham apeto apy astīti nityatvapratiṣedhāt ity ucyate| nityatvapratiṣedhāt ity anityatvād ity uktaṃ bhavati| apetasyāpy astitvaṃ nityatvam ucyate| nityatvasya viparītam anityatvam| na ca pakṣasyānityatvasyānityatvaṃ hetur bhavati yo viparītaṃ sādhayan nityatvaṃ virundhīta| na hy aniṣṭam asādhayan viruddhatām iyāt||

[249,11] athāpi nityatvapratiṣedho nāma^ ātmalābhāt pracyutir iti vika(lpasya)lpya vāvadyothāḥ evam api śabdabhdamātraṃ(trāt) nārthabhedaḥ| ātmalābhāt pracyutiḥ anityatvaṃ ity eko 'rthaḥ||

[249,14] athāpi nityatvopapattyabhāvād ity avalambethāḥ evam api nityatvapratiṣedhāt ity ayaṃ śabdaḥ (ekasyāṃ śuktikāyāṃ) nityatvopapattyabhāvād ity asyārthasya [ā]vācakaḥ||

[249,16] athāpi vācaka^ ity āropya pratibhāṣeta^ evam api śaktivyaktidharmadharmibheda(rūpeṇa)de na nityatvam anityatvaṃ'tyrūpaṃ) cety ukyam eva samādhānam||

[249,18] etena vyakteḥ apeto apy asti vināśapratiṣedhāt iti virodhodbhāvanaṃ praty ākhyātam||

[249,20] evam avasthite vyakter apetasya pratyakṣeṇānupalbhyamānasya katham astitvam yāvatā nāsti snupalabhyamānatvāt nabhaḥkusumavat iti||

[249,22] atrocyate^ anupalabdhir asiddho hetuḥ| anumānāgamābhyām upalabdheḥ| yad^ etat vyaktaṃ etat prāg utpatter apy asti yata^ utpadyate| asaccennotpadyeta caśaviṣāṇavad eva||

[249,25] kiṃ cāsataḥ sattve sataś cāsattve sarvatra na^ āśvāsyuḥ| kiṃ cānupalabdheś ced asattvaṃ muktātmakāraṇādīnāṃ ca prāg upalambhād ūrdhvaṃ cāstitvaṃ kena lahyate tathā[250,9] nasato vidyate bhāvo nābhāvo vidyate sataḥ (BhG 2.16)| iti^ āgamaḥ| pratyakṣānupalabdhiś ca nāsattvasya kāraṇam| atha vyakter eva vikārp vidyamānaś cet kuto na^ upalabhyata^ iti^ āha sākṣāt kāraṇe saṃsargād asya saukṣmyam ato 'nupalabdhiḥ| nāsatvāt||

[250,13] idānīṃ bhūtendriyāṇāṃ lakṣaṇapariṇāma^ idam ucyate lakṣaṇapariṇāmo dharmo yathā mṛdo ghaṭādiḥ adhvasu^ atītādiṣu vartamāno 'tīto dharmaḥ| kim uktaṃ bhavati^ atītalakṣaṇayuktaḥ na punar asattvam āpannaḥ| itarābhyāṃ cānāgatavartamānābhyām aviyuktaḥ| kāraṇasya mṛdo vartamānatvāt tatra saṃsṛṣṭasya ghaṭasya [ā]vidyamānatve (pi(nasyāpi) yukto vartamānena lakṣaṇena ghaṭaḥ| tathā bhaviṣyann api ghaṭo mṛda evānāgataḥ san bhaviṣyatīty anāgatalakṣaṇenāpy aviyuktaḥ tathā vartamānah vartamānalakṣaṇayuktaḥ atītānāgatābhyāṃ lakṣaṇābhyām aviyuktaḥ||

[250,21] tathā^ anāgataḥ anāgatalakṣaṇayuktaḥ vartamānātītābhyāṃ lakṣāṇābhyāṃ aviyuktaḥ atītānāgatalakṣaṇayoḥ mṛdātmakatvāt ghaṭasya vyavahitenāpy atītalakṣaṇenāviyogaḥ||

[250,24] katham ekalakṣaṇayukto dharmo lakṣaṇābhyām aparābhyām aviyukta ity aviyogasya dṛṣṭāntam āha yathā puruṣa^ ekasyāṃ striyāṃ rakto na śeṣasu virakto bhavati| śeṣāsu tu bhūtabhaviṣyallakṣaṇayukto rāgaḥ| sataś cāvināśād asataś cānutpatter iti hi pratipāditam||

[251,8] atra lakṣaṇapariṇāme sarvasya dharmasya sarvalakṣaṇayogād adhvasaṃkaraḥ paurdoṣaś codyate| katham yo hi bhaviṣyati sa vartate^ atītaś ca| tenātīte vartamānabhaviṣyattvam vartamāne^ atītabhaviṣyattvam bhaviṣyati cātītavartamānatvam ity evam anyonyādhvasaṃkaraḥ||

[251,12] tasya parihāraḥ ayam ucyate dharmiṇo mṛdākhyasya dharmāṇāṃ miṇḍaghaṭakapālānāṃ dharmatvaṃ dharmāntarāc cābhinnatvam aprasādhyam| sati ca dharmatve tad bhede ca lakṣaṇabhedo api siddho vācyaḥ||

[251,15] katham yathā piṇḍāvasthāyāṃ anāgatalakṣaṇayukto ghaṭo na vartamāno nāpi atītaḥ| yady api mṛttvabhaviṣyattvasamīkṣayā atītavartamānābhyāṃ api [avi]yuktaḥ||

[251,18] tathā vartamānalakṣaṇayukto nātīto nāpy anāgataḥ| yady api dharmirūpeṇāpe[ve]kṣyamāṇaḥ atītānāgatalakṣaṇābhyāṃ hīyamānajāyamānābhyāṃ lakṣaṇābhyām api[vi]yuktaḥ||

[251,20] tathā piṇḍakapālādiṣu yojanīyam| tasmād āha na vartamānasamaya evāsya^ ekaṃ lakṣaṇo dharmasya| kiṃ tarhi prakāreṇoktena trilakṣaṇatvam| ato nādhvānaḥ saṃkīryante||

[251,23] evaṃ hi na cittaṃ rāgadharmakam iti na citte vi[rāga]dharmiṇi rāgādīnāṃ dharmatve tadbhede ca siddhe^ advasaṃkaraḥ| kasmāt krodhakāle rāgasyāsamudācārāt| (252,9) na hi krodhakāle rāgaḥ svakāla^ iva samudācarati| krodhakāle hi rāgaḥ atīto 'nāgato vā| tathā krodho api rāgakāle| yadi hi vartamānasamaya^ evaikaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ abhaviṣyat kodhakāle nāsti||

[252,13] kiṃ ca trayāṇāṃ lakṣaṇānāṃ yugapad ekasyāṃ lakṣaṇasya vṛttau virodhāt nāsti sambhavaḥ| krameṇa lakṣaṇānāṃ krameṇa svavyañjakānjanasya ghaṭādisvavyañjakāñjanasyānyatam asya kaṣaṇasya bhavet sambhavaḥ||

[252,16] uktaṃ ca rūpātiśayāḥ vṛttyatiśayāś ca virudhyante| vartamānarūpavṛttyatiśayair yukte ghaṭe ca piṇḍādivyaktirūpātiśayā virodhāt na saṃbhavanti| ata^ evātītānāgatarūpā dharmisāmānyānugatatvenāvirodhāt atiśayaiḥ saha vartante||

[252,20] tasmāt adhvanām asaṃkaraḥ| yathā rāgasya^ eva kvacit samudācāra iti na tadānīm anyatra ctriṣu^ abhāvo rāgasya| kin tu kevalaṃ sāmānyena cittarūpeṇa samanvāgataḥ atīto'nāgato api san ity asti tadā tasya rāgasya bhāvaḥ| tathā lakṣaṇasya^ iti||

[252,24] nanu ca piṇḍakapālayor atītānāgatatvāt ghaṭāvasthāyām abhāvāc ca tābhyāṃ mṛdākhyasya dharmiṇo api atītānāgatatvam iti dharmiṇi 
piṇḍakapālayor abhāva^ iti kṛtvā dharmāṇāṃ piṇḍakapālādīnām adhvatrayānupapattir iti pariśaṅkamānaḥ (253,10) kathayati na dharmī tryadhvā| mṛdākhyo hi dharmī piṇḍakpālātītānāgatatvena nātīto 'nāgato vā| sarvadharmeṣu mṛdabhijñānānu(pra)vṛtteḥ| kiṃ tarhi dharmāḥ 
piṇḍādayaḥ tryadhvānaḥ ghaṭakāle piṇḍakapālayor adarśanāt| tasmād adhvatrayāyogo na dharmāṇāṃ pariśaṅkanīyaḥ||

[253,14] te dharmā lakṣitāḥ piṇḍādayaḥ tryadhvatvenābhilakṣitāḥ tāṃ tām avasthāṃ dṛḍhadurbalatvādikāṃ prāpnuvanto 'nyatvena navo ghaṭaḥ purāṇaś cety anena pratinirdiśyante 'vasthāntarataḥ| na dravyāntarataḥ mṛdatiriktāt||

[253,17] yathā ekā rekhā śatasthāne śūnyadvayena tṛtīyasthāne śataṃ bhavati| sā^ eva ca daśasthāne^ ekena śūnyena dvitīyasthāne daśa| sā^ eva 
prathamasthāne^ ekā ||

[253,19] yathā vā ekatve api strī mātā cotyate duhitā ca sā^ eva anyāpekṣayā svasā ca| tad evam avasthāntarād eva śabdapratyayādibhedāḥ| na dharmadharmibhedena| tasmāt yad^ ucyate paraiḥ śabdapratyayalakṣaṇadharmakālādibhedo hetuḥ kāryakāraṇabhede^ iti so'nenānaikāntikīkṛtaḥ||

[253,23] avasthāpariṇāme tṛtīye 'smin kauṭasthyaprasaṅgadoṣaḥ [kaiścit] uktaḥ| kathaṃ kauṭasthyaprasaṅgaḥ adhvānaḥ vartamānādayaḥ svavyāpāreṇa vyavahi(sthi)tāḥ| katham yadā dharmo ghaṭaḥ piṇḍāvasthāyāṃ svavyāpāraṃ na karoti tadā^ anāgataḥ| yadā karoti tadā vartamānaḥ ayam| yadā kṛtvā nivṛttaḥ (254,12)tadā^ atītaḥ| tatra sa^ eva dharmaḥ kṛtaḥ kriyamāṇaḥ kariṣyamāṇaḥ nijavyāpāropārūḍhaḥ dharmitvātmabhūta^ evāvasthāpitaḥ| na vyatiriktarūpaḥ| tena dharmiṇāṃ guṇānāṃ nityatvāt nityatvāvyatirekāt dharmalakṣaṇāvasthanāṃ kauṭasthyaṃ prāpnoti^iti doṣa^ ucyate||

[254,16] nāsau doṣaḥ kasmāt guṇinityatve api guṇānāṃ vimardavaicitryāt udbhavābhibhavatvavicitratayā puruṣavad ekāntakūṭasthatā neṣyate guṇānām| 
pariṇāmābhyupagamāt| na cānavaratapariṇatīnām api 
parasparavimardātrirekeṇa dravyāntarabhāvāpattir iti na kauṭasthyam||

[254,20] kathaṃ vimardavaicitryam ity āha yathā saṃsthānaṃ śarīrādikaṃ [ādi]mat kāraṇavat dharmamātraṃ kāraṇasyātmabhūtam avyatiriktaṃ vināśi tirobhāvadharmakaṃ dharmāntarasamudaye| keṣāṃ saṃsthānam ity ā(mā)ha śabda^ādīnām avināśinām| evaṃ liṅgaṃ mahadākhyaṃ ādimat kāraṇavat dharmamātraṃ vināśi guṇānām avināśinām ātmabhūtam| tasmin mahadādidharmamātre vikārasaṃjñā na guṇānāṃ vimardavicitratayā vināśitvaṃ guṇāsvarūpeṇa nityatvaṃ iti||

[254,26] tatrdāharaṇam mṛta dharmī piṇḍākārā piṇḍākārāt ākārāntaraṃ ghaṭaṃ upasampadyamāno dharmataḥ pariṇamate||

[255,12] [ghaṭākāro 'nāgataṃ lakṣaṇaṃ hitvā vartamānalakṣaṇaṃ 
pratipadyata^ iti lakṣaṇataḥ pariṇamate||] 255,14  tathā ghaṭo navapurāṇatāṃ pratikṣaṇam anubhavan avasthāpariṇāmaṃ pratipadyata^ iti dharmiṇo api mṛdādeḥ dharmāntaraṃ piṇḍādikaṃ avasthā| dharmī 
piṇḍakapālādirūpāvasthām āsīd iti||

[255,17] tata^ eka^ eva pariṇāmo bhedena^ upadarśita^ iti| evaṃ adārthānareṣv api mṛdaḥ anyeṣv api śarīrendriyādiṣu yojyam| ta ete dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmā dharmisvarūpam anatikrāntā ity eka^ eva dharmiṇaḥ pariṇāmaḥ sarvān dharmalakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmān viśeṣān abhimpavate| kutaḥ yena avasthitasya dravyasya pūrvadharmanivṛttau dharmāntarotpattiḥ pariṇām iti dharmī pariṇām eva^ iti śakyaṃ 
pratipattum||3.13||

[255,23] kaḥ punar asau dharmī yasmin avasthite dharmāntaravirāme dharmāntarodayaḥ pariṇāma^ iti| tatra śāntoditāvyapadeśyadharmānupātī dharmī| śāntoditān avyapadeśyāṃś ca dharmānanupatituṃ śīlaṃ yasya so 'nvayī bhinneṣv abhinnātmā dharmīti vyapadiśyate| dharmaḥ nāma yogyatāvacchinnā śaknoti raśaṇākuṇḍalādibhāvam āpattum iti yā suvarṇasya dharmiṇaḥ kuḍalatvādikā śāktiḥ sa^ eva dharmaḥ||

[256,12] sa cāvyakto api san dharmiṇi 
phalaprasavabhedānumitasadbhāvaḥ| yathā deśāntare kālāntare ca tāḍaṅkādikāṃ phalaṃ kankapiṇḍaḥ prasūyate| tatphalaprasavabhedadarśānenānyānanyān api prakārānyaṃ prasavituṃ vibhavatīti tasmin dharmiṇi phalaprasavabhedānumitasadbhāvaḥ| ekasya dharmiṇaḥ anyaś cānyaś ca dharma^ ity anena^ekasya dharmiṇo dharmāsāṃkhyatvam āha||

[256,17] tatra yo dṛṣṭaḥ sa vartamānaḥ svaṃ vyāpāram udakāharaṇādikaṃ anubhavan dharmāntarebhyaḥ śāntebhyaś cāvyapadeśyebhyaś ca bhidyate||

[256,19] yadā tu sāmānyena dharmirūpeṇa samanvāgato bhavati^ atha tadā dharmisvarūpamātratvāt dharmisvarūpātmabhūtatvāt ko 'sau viśeṣataḥ kena vā bhidyate| vyāpārādhirūḍharūpāṇām eva bhedo na kāraṇasamuanugamanirīkṣitātmanām ity arthaḥ||

[256,22] ke punaḥ śāntā uditā^ avyapadeśyā vā te khalu dharmiṇo dharmāḥ śāntāḥ ye kṛtvā vyāpāram paratāḥ| savyāpārā uditāḥṭe cānāgatalakṣaṇasya samanantarāḥ| tathā vartamānasyānantarā^ atītāḥ| kimartham atītasyānantarā na bhavanti | sa santīty arthaḥ pūrvapaścibhābhāvāt| yo yasya paścād bhavati sa tasya (257,10) paścimo 'nantaraḥ| itaraś ca tadapekṣayā pūrvaḥ| tad iha paścibhābhāvāt eṣa svayam api pūrvo na bhavatīti pūrvapaścibhābhāvād atītasyānantarā na santi| yathā^ anāgatavartamānayoḥ pūrvapaścimatā na^ evam atītasya| tasmāt na[nātī]tasyāsti samanantaraḥ||

[257,13] yas tv ayaṃ pāṭhaḥ tadanāgata^ eva samanantarībhavati vartamānasya^ iti na tasya prakṛtena sambandhaḥ| tasmāt tadanāgata^ eva samanantaro bhavaty atītasya^ ity ayam eva pāṭhaḥ| arthopapatteḥ| kathaṃ yadā vyutthānasaṃskāro nirodhasaṃskāreṇa^ ādyo 'abhibhūyate tadā vartamānam adhvanaṃ hitvā vyutthānasaṃskāro 'tītam adhvānam upasampadya punar anāgataḥ san vartamānībhavatīty asamanantaro apy anāgata^ eva samanantarībhavaty atītasya^ ity ucyate| atītānāgatayor lakṣaṇayor dharmāṇāṃ dharmisvarūpamātrāviśeṣāt||

[257,19] athāvyapadeśyāḥ ke tadavyapadeśyābhipradarśanārtham āha sarvaṃ sarvātmakaṃ iti| katham tatra^ uktam jalabhūmyoḥ pāriṇāmikaṃ rasādivaiśvarūpyaṃ sthāvareṣu dṛṣṭam| yathā rasa^ eka^ eva ikṣuśṛṅkagerakādiṣu madhurakaṭukatvādi vaiśvarūpyaṃ pratipadyate| tathā rūpasya śuklakṛṣṇatvādivicitrarūpatvam| evaṃ gandhādiṣv api| tathā sthāvarāṇāṃ jaṅgameṣu rasādivaiśvarūpyam| sthāvarāṇām upayuktāṇāṃ jaṅgameṣu rasalohitādiviśvabhedaḥ| tathā jaṅgamānāṃ sthāvareṣu upayuktānām vṛkṣāyurvedaprasiddhaṃ rasādivaiśvarūpyam||

[257,26] evaṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ sthāvareṣu jaṅgamānāṃ jaṅgameṣu^ ity evaṃ jātyanucchedena sattvādyavināśena sarvaṃ sarvātmakam| aṅguliśikhare lokatrayam apy asti| tathā cāṅguṣṭhād asṛjat brahmā romakūpebhyaḥ ity ādiśrutismṛtiśataprasthānam||

[258,10] yadi sarvaṃ sarvātmakam tadā sarvaṃ sarvatropalabhyeta sammativiprayogajanmā ca śoko mā pra(ti)vartiṣṭetyāder uttaraṃ pathati deśakālākāranimittāpabandhāt deśādīnām apabandhaḥ avabandhaḥ tasmāt na samānakālaṃātmanāṃ sarvavastūnāṃ abhivyaktiḥ| kasya cit kvacid eva deśe kāle ca kena cidevākāreṇa tanubhṛtāṃ ca dharmādinimittābhisamīkṣayā kvacid evābhivyaktiḥ yathā rāhuprabhṛtīnām | etena^ eva sammativiprayoganimittaśokādyupapattiḥ| ata^ ekaikasmin atilaṅghitasaṃkhyāgocaratvāt dharmā na śakyā vyapadeṣṭum||

[238,18] ya teṣu śāntoditāvyapadeśyeṣu^ abhivyaktānabhibyakteṣu dharmesu anupātī sāmānyaviśeṣātmā sattvādiśabdādirūpeṇa so 'nvayī dharmī| yasya tu _Vadinaḥ dharmamātram evedaṃ niranvayaṃ bāhyamādhyātmikaṃ ca yasya ca jñānamātram eva tasya dharmāṇāṃ kṣaṇapraṇāśitvād atītānāgatavartamānaparhārohiṇāṃ samānakālānāṃ ca bhoktṛbhogyatvādisaṃbandhānupapatteḥ bhogābhāvaḥ||

[238,23] sambandhābhāvaṃ darśayati kasmāt anyena vijjānena kṛtasya karmaṇo^ anyat vijñānam akartṛ bhaviṣyat kathaṃ bhoktṛtvenādhikriyeta| kartuś ca 
phalābhipravṛtteḥ phalena saṃbandho yujyate| anyaḥ kartā phalena cānyaḥ sambadhyata^ iti virudhyate||

[238,26] smṛtyabhāvaś ca| kṛtakaraṇīyādiviṣayasmṛtyabhāvaś ca| anyadṛṣṭasya smaraṇam anyasya nāstīti| na hi caitradṛṣṭasya vasumitraḥ smarati| [vastu]pratyabhijñānāc ca yasmāt tad evedam iti vastu 
pratyabhijānīmaḥ tasmāt sthito 'nvayī dharmī dharmānyathātvaṃ dharmair anyathātvaṃ abhyupagataḥ pratipannaḥ| na tu svarūpeṇānyathātvam| yathā rucakādibhedānugatam api kanakam eva^ iti pratyabhijānīmaḥ| tasmāt nedaṃ dharmamātraṃ niranvayam iti ||3.14||

[260,10] idānīṃ pariṇāmabhede hetur ucyate siddhe hi bhede 
pariṇāmatrayasaṃyamaḥ prayoktur upakalpeta| yataḥ kayācil liṅgadvārā atītādiṣu matir upajanayituṃ śakyate nānyathā iti kramānyatvaṃ 
pariṇāmānyatve hetuḥ| ekasya dharmiṇaḥ cittādeḥ eka^ eva pariṇāma^ iti 
prasakteḥ dharmādīnāṃ dharmtabhedād iti pariṇāmabhedasiddhaye hetur eṣa ucyate kramānyatvam| kramaḥ anatarabhāvaḥ tasyānyatvaṃ bhedaḥ||

[259,15] trayo hi kramā^ anyonyavidharmāṇa^ upalakṣyante lakṣaṇāvasthākramābhyām anyo dharmakramaḥ| dharmāvasthākramābhyām anyo lakṣaṇakramaḥ| dharmalakṣaṇakramābhyām anyo'vasthākramaḥ| yac ca teṣām anyatvaṃ prasiddhamārgam adhirūḍhaṃ tat kramakāryāṇāṃ pariṇāmānām anumāpakaṃ anyatve hetuḥ||

[259,19] tadyathā mṛt dharmī cūrṇamṛt iti cūrṇādibhir dharmair ayugapatkālajanmabhiḥ kramavartibhiḥ paiṇamate| sarvatra mṛdgrahaṇeṇadharmiṇo mṛdākhyasya 
parasparalakṣyamāṇabhedacūrṇapiṇḍaghaṭakapālakaṇādidharmānugamaṃ 
pratipādayati||

[259,22] cūrṇasyānantaraḥ piṇḍaḥ piṇḍasyānantaro ghaṭaḥ ghaṭasyānantarāṇi kapālāni kapālānāṃ kaṇā anantarā iti dharma^ eva cūrṇamṛt piṇḍamṛt ghaṭamṛt kapālabṛt kaṇamṛt iti kramaḥ dharmāntarasya kramaḥ| na lakṣaṇaṃ nāpy avasthā| yathā yo yasya dharmasya samanantaro dharmaḥ sa tasya kramaḥ| katham piṇḍo ghaṭa^ upajāyata^ iti piṇḍarūpā mṛt ghaṭībhavatīty arthaḥ| abhedopāaerṇa piṇḍo ghaṭa^ upajāyata^ ity ucyate| na 
punaḥ piṇḍo dharmo dharmāntarātmatāṃ (260,9) pratipadyate| 
piṇḍadharmavimardanena tadvirodhī tadanantaraṃ ghaṭākhyo dharma upajāyate||

[260,10] dharmapariṇāmaś ca dvividhaḥ tulyapariṇāmaś cātulyapariṇāmaś ca| yathā piṇḍo ghaṭa upajāyata^ ity atulyapiṇḍasya ghaṭādhidharmair mṛdākhyo dharmī pariṇamate saḥ atulyadharmapariṇāmaḥ| yāvac ca ghaṭasya janmanaḥ prabhṛti^ ā kapālāpatteḥ tāvat ghaṭarūpeṇa^ eva 
pariṇamate mṛd iti sa tulyadharmapariṇāmaḥ||

[260,14] idānīṃ lakṣaṇapariṇāmakrama^ ucyate lakṣaṇapariṇāmakramaḥ ghaṭasyānāgatāt lakṣaṇāt vartamānabhāvaḥ| tathā piṇḍasya virodhidharmodaye vartamānād atīta[bhāva]ḥ kramaḥ| nātītasyāsti kramaḥ| dharme hi vartamānalakṣaṇasya samanantaraṃ yal-lakṣaṇaṃ sa lakṣaṇakramaḥ| na cātītalakṣaṇasya samanantaraṃ lakṣaṇam asti||

[260,18] kasmāt pūrvāparatāyāṃ hi satyāṃ samanantaratvam| sā cātītasya nāsti| tasmāt dvayor evānāgatavartamānayoḥ lakṣaṇayoḥ kramaḥ| atītasya kramābhāvapratipādane prayojanam kṣaṇatatkramayoḥ saṃyamāt ity atra bhaviṣyati ||

[260,21] tathā^ avasthāpariṇāmakramaḥ ghaṭasyābhinavasya prānte avasānāvasthāyāṃ purāṇātā dṛśyate| sā ca kṣaṇaparamparā 'nupātinā krameṇābhyūhyamānā (261,10)pratikṣaṇam abhinavabhāvaparimardanena  
purāṇatā sañjaniṣyate| sā ca prānteṣu parāṃ vyaktim āpāditā| madhye tu sūkṣmatayā na vibhāvyate| tena madhyāvasthāyāṃ sann api kramo na lakṣyate| prāntakālābhivyaktiliṅgenānumāyiṣyate yena^ iyaṃ purāṇatā parāṃ vyaktim upaprāpitā so'yaṃ krama^ iti| na hi ghaṭe pariniṣpannmātre 
pratikṣanājanmā api purāṇatā gṛhyate||

[261,15] tathā ca vadiṣyati kṣaṇapratiyāgī pariṇāmāparāntanirgrāhyaḥ kramaḥ iti| tasmāt dharmalakṣaṇakramābhyām avasthākramasyānyatvāt dharmalakṣaṇapariṇāmābhyāṃ viśiṣṭo 'yaṃ tṛtīyaḥ pariṇāmaḥ||

[261,18] ta ete kramā dharmadharmibhede sati pratilabdhasvarūpāḥ|  vivakṣite tu dharmadharmiṇor abhede tatra kramabhedo na labhyate| dharmo api dharmī bhavaty anyadharmāpekṣayā| guṇabhyo 'nyatra dharmadharmitvaṃ nāma na vyavasthitam| mahadādīnām apy aviśeṣāpekṣayā dharmitvam|  aviśeṣāṇāṃ ca viśeṣāpekṣayā viśeṣāṇāṃ ca śāntoditāpekṣayeti| yadā tu paramārthato dharmiṇy abhedopacāradvāreṇābhidhīyate dharmaḥ tadā 'yam ekatvena^ eva kramaḥ pratyavabhāsate||

[261,24] tatra cittasya dvaye dvīdhāḥ dharmāḥ paridṛṣṭāś cāpariḍṛṣṭāś ca upalabdhāś cānupalabdhāś ca| ke te^ ity āha tatra pratyayātmakāḥ rāgadveṣamohādayaḥ paridṛṣṭāḥ| vastumātrāḥ svarūpeṇa vidyamānāḥ kāryayoginaḥ aparidṛṣṭāḥ| (262,10) [te ca] svaviṣayavṛttisadbhāvaiḥ saptā^ eva bhavanti| te katham avagamyanta^ ity anumānena prāpitavastumātrasadbhāvāḥ kāryāliṅgenāvagatasadbhāvāh| svarūpeṇa tu nānīśvarair upalabhyante| tataś ca^ ekaikatvenocyante||

[262,13] nirodhadharmasaṃskārāḥ| nirodhaḥ uktaḥ dharmaḥ iti dharmādharmau|  saṃskārāḥ vāsanārūpāḥ| pariṇāma uktalakṣaṇaḥ| atha jīvanam iti dharmādyapekṣayā sakalakaraṇavṛttiḥ prāṇalakṣaṇā yena cittadharmeṇa bhavati sa jīvanam ity ucyate/ seṣṭā vyāpāraḥ śaktiḥ sāmarthyaṃ cittasya dharmāḥ| darśanavarjitāḥ apratyakṣāḥ| iti^ ete sapta nirodhādayaś cittadharmā^ aparidṛṣṭā ucyante||3.15||

[262,18] ete trayaḥ pariṇāmā dharmalakṣaṇāvasthārūpāḥ yoginaḥ upāttasarvasādhanasya samāsasamādita-yamādisakalasādhanasya sthiratarasaṃyamasya atītānāgatapratupasthitabubhutsitārthapratipattaye saṃyamasya viṣayaḥ upakṣipyate| trayoapy eko viṣayaḥ saṃyamasya||

[262,22] pariṇāmatrayasaṃyamād atītānāgatajñānam| pariṇāmatraye tāvat saṃyamaḥ karaṇīyaḥ yāvat samādhiprajñā 'lokaḥ| sa ca yadviṣaya^ upasthitaḥ taṃ viṣayaṃ yathābhūtam pratyakṣatām āpādayati| sūkṣmṃ vyavahitam atītam anāgataṃ viprakṛṣṭaṃ vā yam uddiśya prāpaṇābhiprāyeṇa sūkṣmādiṣu saṃyamaḥ kriyate tadā so 'rtho (263,11)vaśyatāṃ yogina^ upayāti|  dharmakṣaṇāvasthāpariṇāmeṣu saṃyamāt yogino bhavaty atītānāgatajñānam|  dhāraṇādhyānasamādhitrayam ekatra saṃyama^ uktaḥ| tena saṃyamena pariṇāmatrayaṃ sākṣātikriyamāṇam atītānāgatajñānam teṣu pariṇāmeṣu sampādayati| sakalapadārthānāṃ pariṇāmitvāt teṣāṃ cātītādiyogāt kālatraya-yuktasamastapadārthaviṣayapariṇāmatrayasaṃyamena sarvaṃ pratyakṣībhavati| tathā pratikṣaṇapariṇāmadharmakam anavasthitaṃ viśvam īkṣamāṇasya vairāgyaṃ jāyati||3.16||

[263,17] ayam anyaḥ saṃyamaviṣayaḥ upakṣipyate sarvaśabdārthapratipattyarthaḥ śabdārthapratyayānām itaretarādhyāsāt saṃkaraḥ tat-pravibhāgasaṃyamāt sarvabhūtarutajñānaṃ sampadyate| tatra kaḥ śabdaḥ ko vā arthaḥ pratyayo vā saṃkaraś ca kinnīmittaḥ kathaṃ ca teṣāṃ pravibhāgaḥ iti||

[263,21] tatra kiṃ vāguccāryaḥ śrotragrāhyaḥ sa śabdaḥ utārthāmtaram kiṃ vāg eva tadvyatirikto vā iti tācad vicāryate vāk tāvan na śabdaḥ| kasmāt vāg varṇeṣv evārthavatī| vā iti varṇābhivyañjakam aṣṭasu sthāneṣu viṣaktam indriyaṃ varṇābhivyaktimātraprayojanopakṣayaṃ na tāvad arrhābhibyaktau śabdābhivyaktau vā vyāpāraṃ karoti||

[263,26] tathā śrotraṃ ca na svayaṃ śabdaḥ nāpi śabdaviṣayam| kasmāt dhvanipariṇāmamātraviṣayaṃ hi tat| dhvanir iti sāmānyaṃ varṇāvarṇarūpam|  tasya viśeṣaḥ pariṇāmaḥ akārādivarṇātmakaḥ kākādivāśitātmakaś ca mūḍhakaraṇavyaṅgyaḥ| tat dhvanipariṇāmamātraprakāśāvasānaṃ śrotraṃ nārthaśabdayor vyāpriyate||

[264,6] nanu ca śrotragrāhya eva śabdaḥ| na, arthapratyāyakatvāt| arthaṃ yaḥ sampratyāyayati sa śabdaḥ| kaḥ punar asau padam| tat punar nādānusaṃhārabuddhinirgrāhyaṃ nādānāṃ dhbanīnāṃ śrotraviṣayabhūtānāṃ pratyekaṃ pratyayini saṃskāram ādhāya nirvartamānānām antyena dhvaninā yā samupasthāpitā buddhiḥ sā nādānusaṃhārabuddhiḥ prathamadhvanikramānusaṃhārāsāditasaṃskārasya pratyayinaś cittasya jāyate tayaikayā buddhyāyaṃ nirgṛhyate| tat padam tac ca pratyakṣatvāt rūpādivan nānyapramāṇāpekṣam||

[264,13] nanu ca gakāraukāravisarjanīyāḥ iti bhagavān upavarṣaḥ| tan na avācakatvāt| katham samudāyāsambhavāt| samudāyāsambhavaś ca ekasamayāsambhavitvāt| na hi gakāroccāraṇakāle^ aukāravisarjanīyau| nāpy aukāroccāraṇakāle gakāravisarjanīyau| nāpi visarjanīyapravṛttivelāyām itarayoḥ samavasthānam iti varṇasamudāyāsambhvād avācakatvam||

[264,18] yathā^ eva varṇānāṃ yugapatsamayānavasthānam  evaṃ saṃskārāṇām smṛtīnāṃ ca tajjanitānām ayugapatkālatvād avācakatvam|  smṛtyupayogārthatvāc ca saṃskārasyānubhūtaviṣaya^ eva^ upayogāt pratyāyasya cāpūrvatvāt||

[264,21] api ca saṃskārāṇāṃ vācakatvābhyupagame śabdād arthapratipattiḥ pūrvāparībhūtaṃ bhāvam ākhyātenācaṣṭe dharmasya śabdamūlatvād aśabdama anapekṣyaṃ syāt iti prasiddhir apahnūyate||

[264,24] athāpi varṇabhāvabhāvitvena smṛtisaṃskārayor api śabdatvam upacaryeta| na^ etad api tadbhāvabhāvitvaṃ nāma tasminn eva sati bhavaty asty abhāva^ eva yatra tatra syāt| (na ca) tac ca varṇānāṃ śabdatve siddhe bhavet| oloks tu śrīrādāv ātmābhimānād avivekād atasminn api tadbuddhiṃ karotī^ iti na sarvatra vastunirṇaye hetur bhavati||

[265,1] api ca vākśrotravyāpāra^ eva saty arthapratipattir iti tasyāvyabhicārāt tadbhāvabhāvitve śabdatvaṃ prāpnoti| tasmād antyo varṇo na vācakaḥ varṇatvāt ādyavarṇavat| tathā tatsmṛtisaṃskārā na vācakāḥ smṛtisaṃskāratvāt ādyavarṇasmṛtisaṃskāravat||

[265,5] yadi cāntyo varṇaḥ pūrvavarṇajanitasaṃskārasahitaḥ vācaka ity ucyate naitad evam| kasmāt antyavarṇoccaraṇamātrād evārthapratyayaprasaṅgāt| katham atītadivasoccaritagakārādivarṇāhitasaṃskāro hi samastaḥ śrotā| tatrāntyovrṇoccāraṇamātrād arthaḥ pratīyeta| athāpi niyamārtham ādyavarṇoccāraṇam iti cet na pūrvavarṇānāṃ paścimabarṇenātulyakālatvāt sāhāyakābhāve niyamānupapattiḥ||

[265,10] tavāpi varṇānāṃ padavyañjakatve niyamārthaṃ pūrvavarṇā uccāraitavyā iti doṣa^ eva^ iti padavyañjakatvakalpanāto varṇānām eva vācakatvaṃ yuktam iti cet na varṇānāṃ buddhau svakīyasaṃskārādhānamātrābhyupagamāt|  uccaritapradhvaṃsamānavarṇasaṃskāraparaṃparopacitāyāṃ hi buddhau padaṃ nirbhāsate| etad eva hi varṇānāṃ padavyañjakatvam na padaṃ prati dīpavat sākṣāt pravartate||

[265,15] na buddhau dharmidvāreṇā saṃskārānām ekāśrayāvasthānād anyonyopakāryopakārakabhāvaḥ samīcīnataraḥ|  iṣṭāniṣṭaviṣayopalabdhismṛtīcchādveṣādivat| na kadācid antyena varṇena pūrvavarṇānāṃ tadīyasmṛtisaṃsārāṇāṃ vā sambandha^ upakalpate||

[265,18] atha mamāpi niyatakramavarṇasampādit-asaṃskāraparamparopacitabuddhyutpādakatvena^ eva varṇānāṃ vācakatvam iti cet nābhyupetaparivarjanāt| yadi tāvad varṇānekasaṃskāraviśiṣṭā buddhiḥ arthaviṣayā vā padaviṣayā vā tathāpi padārthapratipattir iti vādhyeta||

[265,22] tasmād ekabuddhiviayo varṇavyatiriktaḥ śabdo 'bhyupagantavyaḥ|  tena pūrvavarṇajanitasaṃskārasahito 'ntyo varṇaḥ tatsmṛtiḥ saṃskāro vā na vācakaḥ| saṃskārasāhāyakāpekṣatvāt buddhivat||

[265,25] evaṃ gate katham varṇānām arthaviṣayaḥ padaviṣayo vā vyāpāro nāstīty āha varṇāḥ ekasamayāsambhavitvāt| ekakālasambhavitvaṃ ca vācaḥ kramavartitvāt| ataḥ parasparaniranugrahātmānaḥ kasyacid abhisambandhasyābhāvād itaretaranirupakārāḥ| te padam anupasaṃspṛśyānupasthāpya kañcana pade vyāpāram avhidhāya^ āvir-bhūtāś ca tirobhūtāś ca vyastā vā samastā vā na padātmānam apāṅgavikṣepeṇāpi parāmṛśanti| pratyekam apadasvarūpāḥ yugapad anavasthānāt samudāyātmanā apy apadasvarūpā eva| kimaṅga punaḥ pratyekam anarthakānām avasthitānām iti||

[266,14] varṇaḥ punar ekaikaḥ sarvābhidhānaśaktipracitaḥ| abhidhīyate yaiḥ tāny abhidhānāni padāni teṣāṃ sarveṣām abhidhānānāṃ padānām abhivyañjikābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ pracītaḥ arāṇām ekaika^ iva nābhau| tadyathā gakāraḥ kovargāgnigaganādisarvapadābhidyotanasāmarthyapracitaḥ||

[266,18] sahakārivarṇāntarapratiyogitvāt| yathā gaur iti gakārāu-kāravisarjanīyasahakārisavyapekṣaḥ agnir itīkārapratiyogī|  tasmād anekavarṇāntaropādhisambandhāt pratipadaṃ vaiśvarūpyam ivāpannaḥ| uttaraś ca varṇaḥ pūrveṇa viśeṣe padaviśeṣapradyotane avasthāpitaḥ| pūrvaś cottareṇa madhyamo api pūrvottarābhyām||

[266,22] evaṃ bahavo varṇāḥ kramānurodhinaḥ viśiṣṭakramaṃ ānupūrvīm anurudhyamānāḥ||

[266,23] arthasaṅketenāmī^ asminn arthe^ etatkramāvalmbinaḥ ity anta^ iti saṅketaḥ samayaḥ tenārthasaṅketenāvacchinnāḥ||

[266,25] varṇasaṅketadvāreṇa padaṃ saṅketyate| varṇāvyabhicaritacāritram asādhāraṇavarṇakramāpekṣitasaṅketasamayaṃ hi padam| varṇasaṅketa^ eva tasya saṅketaḥ| iyanta^ ete varṇāḥ sarvābhidhānaśaktiparicyutāḥ viśiṣṭasāsnādimmadarthapratipādakāsādhāraṇa(267,1)padāvabhāsanavyāpāraparatvād evāparapadāntarapratipādakaśaktyantarebhyaḥ paricyutāḥ nivṛttāḥ itaretaraviśeṣaviniyogaparavaśāveśād udāsīnapadāntaraprakāśanaśaktayaḥ gakāraukāravisarjanīyāḥ sāsnādimantam artham avadyotayanti padam abhivyañjayantaḥ||

[267,4] tadeṣām arthasaṅketenāvacchinānām abhihitena nyāyamārgeṇa anugṛhītadv\hvanikramāṇāṃ viśiṣṭānuparipāṭisamāyoginām itthaṃ kramaviśeṣakramalabdhajanmanāṃ varṇānāṃ sambandhaḥ ya^ eko buddhinirbhāsaḥ buddher nirbhāsaḥ tat padaṃ bhūmiḥ (bhūmiṣum eva) bhūmikeva vācakaṃ vācyasya na varṇāḥ| varṇadvāreṇa tu saṅketyate||

[267,8] tat padam ekam ekam api yadvastu sāvayavaṃ ghaṭādi tadanekabuddhiviṣayaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ na tu padaṃ tathā| tat ekabuddhiviṣayaṃ sarvalokapratyakṣam| varṇāḥ punar anekatvād ayugapatkālajanmānaḥ ekabuddhiviṣayatāṃ na samupāśnuvate| yas tv evam ekabuddhinirgrāhyam anumānena nirācikīrṣati tasya pratyakṣavirodhinī pratijñā syāt||

[267,12] evam ekaprayatnākṣiptaṃ varṇakramajanmapratyupanītasaṃskāranimittabuddhiparipākābhisamīkṣayā paścim abuddhau jhaṭiti parisphuritaṃ avibhāgaṃ avayavaśūnyaṃ ata^ eva akramam avrṇātmakam antyavarapratyayavyāpāropasthāpitam ādyavarṇavyāpāraparamparānugṛhītapaścimavarṇagrahaṇamātrapratyudbhāsitam||


[267,16] paratra parabuddhipratipipādayiṣayā varṇair evābhidhīyamānaiḥ abhidhātṛbhiḥ śrūyamāṇaiś ca śrotṛbhiḥ anādivāgvyavahāravāsanānuviruddhyā lokabuddhyā  siddhavat saṃpratipattyā pratīyate| (nāpi) anāadisaṃsāracakramaṇḍale parivartate| śabdād evārthapratipattir pūrvako vyavahāraḥ tadvāsanāvāsitayā lokadhiyā siddhavat pratīyata^ iti laukikānāṃ sammugdhākārapravartitavyavahāramātracaritaprayojanānāṃ ātmanīva pade vipratipattiḥ||

[267,22] tasmāt [padaṃ] varṇavyatiriktam ekabuddhinirgrāhyatve sati pratyāyakatvāt pradīpavat| arthapratipattau ca kāraṇatvāt pradīpavat buddhivac ca| na varṇāḥ padam ekena kartā krameṇa prayujyamānatvāt karaṇatvāc ca vāsīvṛkṣādānādivat||

[267,25] atra kaścit āha (_ś_V sphoṭa kk.131-136) 238,1 varṇā vā dhvanato vā^ api sphoṭaṃ na padavākyayoḥ| vyañjanti vyañjakatvena yathā dīpaprabhādayaḥ||

[268,1] sattvāt ghaṭādivac ceti sādhanāni yathāruci|  laukikavyatirekeṇakalpito 'rthe bhavanti hi|| % laukikavyavahāreṇa [all the mss]

[268,5] nārthasya vācakaḥ sphoṭo varṇebhyo vyatirekataḥ| ghṭādivan na dṛṣṭena virodho dharmyasiddhitaḥ||

[268,7] pratiṣedhāt tu yo varṇāṃs tajjānānantarodbhavāt| dṛṣṭabādho bhavet tasya śaśicandraniṣedhavat||

[268,9] varṇotthā vā 'rthadhīr eṣā tajjñānānantarodbhavā| yādṛśī sā tadutthā hi dhūpāder iva bahnidhīḥ||

[268,11] dīpavad vā gakārādir gavādeḥ pratipādakaḥ| dhuvaṃ pratīyamānatvāt tat-pūrvaṃ pratipādanāt|| iti| 

268,13       atrocyate varṇā vā dhvanayo vā api sphoṭaṃ na padavākyayoḥ iti padaṃ vākyaṃ vā svatas tāvat na prasiddham| tatrāsati varṇavyatirikte pade vākye vā padavākyayoḥ sphoṭaṃ na vyañjayantīti kathaṃ cetasvināṃ vacanāvakāśaḥ syāt| evaṃ ca sati^ adṛśya padavākyayoḥ sphoṭaṃ pratiṣedhatā padaṃ vākyaṃ cābhyupagataṃ bhavet||

[268,18] athāpi parābhiprāyeṇocyeta tathā api siddhasādhyatā| na parasya padavākyayor anyaḥ sphoṭo vidyate yaḥ pratiṣidhyeta| padavākye eva hi tasya sphoṭaḥ||

[268,21] athāpi brūyāt arthāvacchedakā viśiṣṭakramopaniveśino varṇāḥ padaṃ vākyaṃ ca pratiṣidhyate^ iti| tac ca na yuktam svato virodhāt||

[268,25] athāpi brūyāt varṇavyatiriktaṃ padaṃ vākyaṃ vā pratiṣidhyate iti| tathāpi vyatiriktam uddiśya tasya vyañjanapratiṣedhāt vyatiriktapadavākyābhyupagamaḥ prasaktaḥ||

[269,1] athāpi parakalpitapadavākyapratiṣedhaḥ evam api vyañjakatvād ity asiddho hetuḥ| katham svātmavyatiriktasya vā padasya vyañjakatvaṃ avyatiriktasya vā vyañjakatvaṃ arthasya vā vyañjakatvaṃ varṇānām iti trayī vikalpanā| yadi vyatiriktasya vyañjakatvaṃ tadā svato 'siddhaḥ syāt||

[269,5] athāvyatiriktasya tathāpi parasyāsiddhaḥ| kiṃ ca vyañjakatvāc ca vyatiriktasya^ eva vyañjakatvaṃ nāvyatiriktasya pradīpāder iva|  anyavyañjanārthaṃ hi vyañjakaḥ pradīpādir upādīyate na svātmavyañjanāya| svātmavyaktyarthaṃ ced upādāsyate januṣā 'ndhasyāpi vyajyeta||

[269,9] atha vyatiriktavyañjakatve siddhe pādasya vyañjakā bhaveyuḥ arthasya vā| tatrānekasādhyaṃ yathā śibikodvahanādi tat anyatamena na śakyaṃ anuṣṭhātuṃ evam anyatamena varṇānām arthasya pratipādanam anekasādhyatvād aśakyam iti pratipāditam||

[269,13] na ca kūpakhānakanyāyenāpi kiṃcit kiṃcit vyaṅgye saṃskārab ādhāya varṇā nirvartante^ iti śakyaṃ vaktum| abhidheyasya^ ekabuddhiviṣayatvāt||

[269,15] yathā ca sugatakṛtānte pradhvaṃsamānaṃ vijñānam utpatsyamānasya vijñānasya saṃskāram ādhātum aparyāptaṃ vthitasya dharmiṇaḥ kasyacid abhāvāt| evam nyonyasaṃbandhābhāvād ayugapatsamayajanmanāṃ varṇānāṃ pūrvasya pūrvasya vinaśyata^ uttarasyottarasya copajaniṣyamāṇasya balādhānānupapattir iti nārthasya vyañjakā varṇāḥ||

[269,19] pariśeṣāt padasya^ eva vyatiriktasyābhivyañjitāraḥ iti sthitadharmaḥ| asmākaṃ tu sthite dharmiṇi cetasi pūrvapūrvavarṇapratyupasthitasaṃskāraparamparāparipākaviśārade paścimavarṇajanmavelāyāṃ niravayavam ekabuddhinirgrāhyabuddhinirbhāsātmakaṃ jhatiti samunmiṣatīti na saṃskārādhānāsāmarthyadoṣaprasaṅgaḥ||

[269,23] kiṃ ca yady api varṇāḥ svabuddhiṃ vyañjayanti| sā buddhir arthapratītiṃ prati karaṇabhūtā evābhivyajyate na tu phalaṃ prati varṇāḥ karaṇatvena vyāpriyante| akaraṇbhūtabuddhyutpādanopakṣayatvāt| buddhir eva hi phalaṃ prati vyāpāraḥ| yasya hi phalaṃ prati vyāpāraḥ tat pramāṇaṃ sa śabdaḥ| tataś cārthapratītiṃ prati kāraṇabhūtabuddhivyavahitatvāt na varṇānāṃ vācakatvam| parasparayā tu padaṃ pratypāyatvaṃ na varṇānāṃ pratiṣidhyate||

[270,1] tasmād evam ayaṃ aślokaḥ paṭhitavyaḥ varṇā vā dhvanayo vā api bhinnam evārtham ātmanaḥ|| vyañjanti vyañjakatvena yathā dīpaprabhādayaḥ||

[270,4] tathā ghaṭādīnām apy arthāntarapratyupasthānamātratvam abhyupagamyata^ eva| ghaṭādayo hi darśanamātreṇa 
pacanāharaṇadhāraṇādiyogyatābuddhim anyad vā kiṃcit upasthāpayanty eva| tathā varṇānāṃ pāramparyeṇa^ ātmabyatiriktapadapratypasthāpakatvaṃ sattvāt ghaṭādivad iṣyata^ eva^ iti tulyam etad api||

[270,8] sattvād ghaṭādivac ceti sādhanāni yathā-ruci| laukikavyatirekeṇa kalpite 'rthe bhavanti hi|| % laukikavyavahāreṇa (all the mss)

[270,10] sarvathā jātir vā buddhir vā bhavatu yato vā kutaścit uccāritapradhvastvarṇānasaṃbhavino varṇavyatiriktāt arthapratipattiḥ sa sphoṭaḥ| sa cāvaśyaṃ varṇāsaṃbhavitvāt kāryasyābhyupagamanīyaḥ| tathā ca 270,13     naṛthasya vācakaḥ sphoṭo varṇebhyo vyatirekataḥ iti bruvato 'bhyupagamavirodhaḥ syāt| api ca varṇebhyo vyatirekataḥ ity anyatarāsiddhaḥ| svato vyatirekasyāsiddhatvāt||

[270,15] kiṃ ca nāmākhyatopasarganipātapadajāticatuṣṭayam abhyupagaddhatā jātivyañjakaṃ varṇavyatiriktam anyad abhyupagantavyam| na varṇāḥ padajātim abhivyañjitum utsahante| varṇā hi varṇajātim abhivyañjayanti| na cāvayaveṣu bhinnakālajanmavināśeṣv avayavijātivyañjanam upapadyate| vyavasthiteṣv api nāma tāvad avayaveṣu na 
pāṇipādādayaḥ śarīrād ṛte śarīratvam abhivyañjayanti| kiṃ punar avasthiteṣu||

[270,20] kiṃ cāśvaḥ ayātam ayātam akṣaḥ bhavati tena mama vāyur vāyuḥ ityādiṣu tulyasvarūpeṣu nāmatvākhyātatve parasparaviruddhajātīye na^ eka eva varṇo 'bhivyanakti||

[270,22] yadi vā^ aśvaḥ iti nāmajātyā^ abhivyañjakāḥ akāraśakāravakārākāravisarjanīyāḥ ākhyātajātyabhivyañjakebhyo bhinnā bhaveyuḥ tadā upapadyate bhinnānāṃ bhinnajātyabhivyañjakatvam| tad ekatve na^ upapadyate ||

[270,25] yady api^ arthaprakaraṇaśabdāntarasannidhānādibhiḥ idaṃ nāma idam ākhyātaṃ iti parikalpyeta tathā api nāmākhyātajātyabhivyaktim arthaprakaraṇādibhir eka^ eva varṇo parikalpyeta tathāpi nāmākhyātajātyabhivyaktim arthaprakaraṇādibhir eka varṇo na śaknoti kartum| na hi vrāhmaṇa^ eva brāhmaṇatvaṃ kṣatriyatvaṃ ca^ bhivyanakti||

[271,1] tasmāt bhinnā^ akārāḥ śakārā vakārādayaś ca bahava^ eṣitavyāḥ| evaṃ go-śabdādayo api bhinnā eva| tathā go-śabdatvam aśvaśabdatvaṃ ca siddham| evaṃ matvam ātvam amītvaṃ ca siddham| evaṃ ca saty akārādayo varṇā atvādīny eva sāmānyāny abhivyañjayanti| nāśvādīn nāmākhyātajātibhedān iti||

[271,5] tasmāt varṇavyatiriktaṃ padatvasāmānyābhivyañjakaṃ padam abhyupagantavyam| evaṃ ca saty eṣa śloka^ evaṃ paṭhitavyaḥ pratiṣedhet tu yaḥ sphoṭaṃ varṇadhīsamanantaram| dṛṣābādho bhavet tasya śaśicandraniṣedhavat|| varṇotthā 'varṇadhīr eṣā tajjñānānantarodbhavā| iti||

[271,12] karaṇabuddhivyavahitatvād anantarodbhavatvam asiddham| 
pāramparyeṇa cet siddhasādhyatā| dhūmasyāpi na vahnidhiyaṃ prati kāraṇatvam | dhūmadhiyaḥ kāraṇatvāt| dhūmādīnāṃ pāramparyavat varṇādīnām api pāramparyeṇopakārakatvam iṣyata^ ity uktam||

[271,15] tasmād ayam api śloka^ evaṃ paṭhitavyaḥ varṇotthā nārthadhīr eṣā tajjñānānantarodbhavā| yedṛśī sā tadutthā na dhūmotar iva vahnidhīḥ||

[271,18] na ca dīpavat sannipatya gakāraukārau sāsnādimadarthavyañjakau arthavyaktikālānavasthānād anayor dṛṣṭo vyabhicāraḥ visarjanīyaviṣaye api samunnīyate niravasthānādi| varṇā nārthavyaktisamānakālāvasthāyinaḥ pradīpas tu vyaṅgyakālena tulyakālāvasthānaḥ kāraṇatvaṃ pratilabhate||

[271,22] tasmād ayam apy evaṃ ślokaḥ paṭhitavyaḥ dīpavad vā gakārādir gavādīnām avācakaḥ| dhruvaṃ pratīyamānatvāt tat-pūrvaṃ pratipādanāt||

[271,25] pramāṇaṃ cātra bhavati^ arthasya na vācakāḥ nāpy arthaṃ 
prati pramāṇaṃ varṇāḥ sākṣāt saṅketāpekṣatvāt aṅkapratimādivat| yathā śatādyaṅkā viṣṇupratimāś ca saṅketāpekṣā^ āgamam evāvadyotayanti| na viṣṇuśatādyarthaṃ prati pramāṇam upayānti||

[271,28] yat tu nāpekṣate saṅketaṃ tat pramāṇam yathā vākyaṃ dīpādijātytantaraṃ ca| na tathā varṇā iti pratimāvad eṣāṃ aprāmāṇyam āpatitam| tathā varṇā^ arthapratipādakam ātmavyatiriktam abhivyañjayanti sākṣāṭ saṅketāpekṣatvāt aṅkaviṣṇupratimādivat sphoṭaḥ tatsaṅketaṃ na sākṣād apekṣate^ ity avādiṣma||

[272,10] tasmād evāneka-yuktiparivārapratyupānītabalādhānaṃ kaḥ sphoṭam apākartuṃ śaknoti||

[272,12] tad evaṃ gate bhāṣyam idānīm ucyate tasya padasya saṅketabuddhitaḥ pravibhāgaḥ| śabdārthajñānavyatibhinnarūpā hi saṅketabuddhiḥ| tasyāḥ sakāśāt padasya pravibhāgaḥ karaṇīyaḥ||

[272,15] saṅketarūpaṃ darśayati^ etāvatāṃ varṇānām evaṃjātīyako 'nusaṃhāra etasyārthasya vācakaḥ iti| yathā^ ayam aṅkaḥ śataṃ sahasraṃ viṣṇureṣā 
pratimā iti ca||

[272,18] saṃketas tu padapadārthayor itaretarādhyāsarūpāḥ smṛtyātmā na mramāṇabhūtaḥ| yathā viṣṇureṣā pratimā eṣa eva caturbhuja iti tathā pratyaya ubhayam idaṃ ubhayaṃ nu pratyaya ity anyonyādhyastarūpā viṣṇupratimāpratyayāḥ ||

[272,21] tathā yo 'yaṃ śabdaḥ so 'yam arthaḥ yo 'yam arthaḥ sa śabdaḥ yaś ca pratyayaḥ so'rthaḥ śabdaś ceti^ evam itaretarādhyāsarūpaḥ saṃketo bhavati| evam ete śabdārthapratyayā itaretarādhyāsāt saṃkīrṇāḥ| tadyathā gaur iti śabdo gaur ity arthā gaur iti jñānam iti||

[272,25] ya^ eṣāṃ śabdārthapratyayānāṃ pravibhāgajñaḥ sa sarvajñaḥ| kasmāt aśakyatvāt pravibhāgajñānasya| na hy asarvajñāḥ śaknoti 
pravibhāgena tānavagantum||

[273,5] sarvapadeṣu cāsti vākyaśaktiḥ| vṛkṣa^ ity ukte^ astīti gamyate| na sattāṃ padārtho vyabhicaratīti||

[273,7] kim artham etad ucyate^ iha sarvabhūtarutajñānopāyaḥ 
pratipipādayiṣitaḥ| sarvabhūtarutaṃ ca vākyārthaviṣayam eva na 
padārthaviṣayam| padānāṃ kevalānāṃ varṇānām ivānarthakatvāt saṃvyavahārābhāvāc ca| yathā^ eva varṇānāṃ varṇāntarābhisamīkṣayā 
padāvadyotanaprayojanavattvam evaṃ padānām api padāntarāpekṣayā vākyopasthāpanopāyatvam| evaṃ sati vākyasya^ eva prāmāṇyaṃ kevalapadaprayoge 'arthāpratīteḥ||

[273,12] kiṃ ca kevalaṃ padam apramāṇaṃ tāvatā nirṇayābhāvāt ekaikavarṇavat| nāpi padaṃ vācakam saṅketārthāpekṣatvāt śatādyaṅkavat| nāmākhyāte kevale na vācake kevalāprayogāt upasargavat| yatrāpi kevalaṃ padaṃ prayuyukṣitaṃ tatrāpy avaśyam asti tiṣṭhati devadatta ityādipadaṃ buddhau viparivartata iti na kevalāprayogasiddhiḥ|| L104v1: "devadatta..."

[273,17] pralambate paryāgacchati^ abhyagacchatītyādiṣu upasargakarmapravacanīyānāṃ na kaścid arthaḥ| tathā^ abhimānāyate sumanāyate durmanāyate^ ityādiṣu ca^ upasargāṇāṃ dyotakatvaṃ siddham| tathā 
pratiṣṭhate^ adhīta^ ityādiṣv apy upasargāṇāṃ dyotakatvam eva na vācakatvam iti| tathā na hy asādhanā kriyā^ asti| kevalakarmaprayoge kim api sādhanaṃ dravyaṃ guṇo vāvagamyate||

[273,22] kiṃ ca prakṛtipratyayānām anavasthitāv adhikatvāt vyabhicārād avācakatvam| tadyathā daridrātiḥ keṣāñcit dravyamātra^ eva paṭhyate| daridraśabdas tu nipātopasargasthānīyaḥ punar āgacchati drāti nidrāti daridrātīti| tatra kā prakṛtir iti vaktavyam| kiṃ (drākitvaṃ) drā iti kiṃ vā daridreti| keṣāñcit tu daridrety etāvat paṭhyate tathā drāśabdo api pṛthagāmnāyate||

[273,27] evam asti sakāramātraṃ paṭhanti| vīti ca pratyaye kāram āgamaṃ kurvanti| kecit tu saha kāreṇa paṭhitvā (aviti) vīti ca pratyaye lopaṃ kārasya kurvanti| tatra kā prakṛtir iti vaktavyam| na nirjñāyate| 
prakṛtyavadhau pratyayāvadhau vā prakṛtipratyayārtho śakyau vivektum||
