[274,1] kvacid upasargo .api asaṃgrāmayata śūra ityādiṣu prakṛtyekadeśatām āpannaḥ| tathātater nāsyāpatyamiḥ iyān adhunā ityādau kaḥ prakṛtyarthabhāgam asatyāṃ prakṛtau brūyāt| agnicit sopasut bhidā chidā atho kati^ amā paca paṭha^ ityādiṣu pratyayārtham asati pratyaye ko brūyāt||

[274,5] evam ādiṣu lakṣitāpacārāṇāṃ prakṛtipratyayanāṃ kaḥ śaknuyād arthavattvaṃ kalpayitum| atra bhavatu nānyatra^ iti nārdhajaratīyaṃ labhyam| uddālakapuṣpabhañjikāpāraṃ na puṣpapracāyikā ity atra kaḥ pūrvapaottarapadārthaḥ prajña^ ityādiṣu pratyanvayo api nāstīti kasya ko 'rthaḥ iti||

[274,9] ṛṣabho vṛṣabho vṛṣaḥ udakaṃ udakubhaḥ kṣīrodaḥ yāvo yāvakaḥ kūpaḥ sūpaḥ yūpaḥ ityādiṣu kasya ko 'rtha iti vaktavyam| dadhyaśāna madhv atra ityādiṣu kim avadhi kiṃ padaṃ vācakam||

[274,12] tasmāt sarvatra dṛṣṭāpacārāṇāṃ varṇaprakṛtipratyayapadānāṃ anarthakānām adṛṣṭāpacāraniravayavavākyopāyatvam eva| tāni niḥsandhibandhanāt niravayavāt mithyā-bhūtavarṇapadadhvanikṛtacitrī-bhāvād vākyād apoddhṛtya vyākaraṇīyāni||

[274,15] tāni ca vākyasyānavayavabhūtāni^ upāyatvāt vākyāvayavavad avabhāsamānāni yāvat phalam asattvāt śatādyaṅkavad eva mithyābhūtāni anuccāryamāṇavākyāvadyotanārthaṃ karaṇasannipāte viśeṣeṇopādāya parihīyamāṇāni svakīyakramaniṣpattivinivṛttikālādibhedavicitratayā vākyātmānaṃ kramavantam iva niṣpadyamānam iva nirvartamānam iva drāghīyāṃsam iva kālavantam iva bhedavantam iva vicitrākāratām āpādayanti||

[274,21] evaṃ ca sati yad^ ucyate śaśaviṣāṇād apoddhṛtya kharaviṣāṇasya kiṃ tad iti^ etad alīkaṃ yathā^ eva mṛgatṛṣṇikātoyamalīkam api satyādūṣarād apoddhriyate yathā ca sathāṇoḥ puruṣaḥ puruṣāc ca sathāṇuḥ śuktikātaś ca rajatamapoddhrtiyate yathā^ eva ca mṛgatoyādayo yathābhūtoṣarādivastupratipattihetavaḥ tathā varṇapadaprakṛtipratyayā yathā bhūtavākyārthapratipattikāraṇe tatpratipattau cāpoddhriyante||

[274,26] kvacid arthaprāptam apoddhriyate yathā mṛgatoyādikam| kvacit tu 
prayojanatvena vivakṣitaṃ yathā śatādisaṅkhyātaḥ śatādyaṅkāpoddhāraḥ kriyate yathā ca lipyakṣarāṇi saṅketāpekṣatvāt kṛtasamayarūpāṇi akārādivarṇād apoddhriyante| atha ca sarvāṇy etāni yathā bhūtārthapratipādanopāyatvaṃ pratipadyante| na ca tāvatā mithyātvam eṣāṃ nāsti| tathā padādīny api||

[275,1] ye hi prakṛtipratyayapadavarṇānāṃ saṃketadvāreṇānyopāyānāṃ mithyābhūtalipyakṣarakalpānāṃ svayam api svasaṃketena vākyārthapratipattyupāyam anekadhā nirmāya vyavaharamāṇaḥ satyatvam eṣāṃ manyate nūnam asāvabhre candramasaṃ didṛkṣamāṇaḥ candradarśanopāyabhūtam abhramapi candratvena pratipadyata^ iti||

[275,5] yathā ca vākyam eva satyaṃ tathā vākyārthasya^ eva satyatvaṃ na 
padāni padārthā vā kevalāḥ satyatvam upayānti| sarvam eva sāmānyaviśeṣātmakaṃ vastu| na hi sāmānyavyāvṛtto viśeṣaḥ viśeṣavyāvṛttaṃ vā sāmānyam asti| gaur ity ukte^ astīti gamyate| tena kevalapadaprayoge apy antarnītapadāntarārtho vākyārtha^ eva pratīyate na kevalapadārthaḥ||

[275,10] evaṃ ca sati padārthā gamayanty etaṃ pratyekaṃ saṃśaye sati|| sāmastye nirṇayotpadāt sthāṇumūrdhastha kākavat|| (_ś_V vākya k.363) 275,13    ity asiddham asmākam| na hi sthāṇur nāma vākyārthaḥ 
pṛthakpadārthatvena prasiddhaḥ| yasmāt vṛkṣaḥ san sthāṇuḥ san mūrdhā san kākaḥ san mūrdhani sthāṇoḥ ity antarnītapratīyamānapadāntarāsthān apekṣāyāṃ sthāṇumūrdhasthakākabuddhir eva na syāt| satsv api teṣu sadbuddhisamutpatteḥ satpadārthāntarabuddhir avaśyaṃ bhāvinī||

[275,19] tasmāt vākyārtha^ eva pratyakam api saṃśayātmaka^ iti kutaḥ 
padārthāḥ pratyekam asanto vākyārthaṃ gamayeyuḥ| tasmād evam ayaṃ ślokaḥ 
paṭhitavyaḥ vākyaṃ tu gamayaty enaṃ pratyekaṃ saṃśaye sati|| samastye nirṇayotpādāt sthāṇumūrdhasthakākavat||

[275,23] evam vākyena^ evocyate tasmāt vākyārtho 'nyair arūpaṇāt| ante cāstīti boddhāc ca vṛkṣeṇa^ eva ca vṛkṣatā||

[275,26] yat tu palāśaśabdena vṛkṣatā nocyate^ iti satyam evam etat| na hi 
palāśavākyena vṛkṣavākyārthasyābhidhānam| bhinnaviṣayatvāt| anyo hi 
palāśavākyārthaḥ palāśo 'sti tiṣṭhatīti vā| anyo vṛkṣavākyārthaḥ| yadā ca pācarṇānāṃ niravayavānāṃ pṛthag astitvam abhyupagamyate tadā niravayavavarṇapadopādānaṃ vākyaṃ sāvayavaṃ katham avakalpeta| na hi kriyākāśādibhiḥ sāvayavaṃ śakyam ārabdhum| atha sāvayavatvam abhyupāyiṣyate tatrāpi doṣa^ eva prasajyeta||

[276,8] sadbhāve padavarṇānāṃ bhedo yaḥ paramāṇuvat| sarvābhāvas tataś ceti seyaṃ bālavibhīṣikā||(_ś_V vākya k.150) 276,10 paramāṇunām sattvādidharmamātratvābhyupagamāt vivṛtya suṣṭhu bhāṣāmahe seyaṃ vṛddhavibhīṣiketi||

[276,12] atha kevalapadaprayoge padārthāntarasyāpi saṃgrahāt kim anekapadaprayogeṇei cet niyamārtho 'nuvādaḥ kartṛkaraṇakarmaṇāṃ caitrāgnitaṇḍulānāṃ kriyāvavacanāt sādhanāni vidyanta^ ity arthaprāptānāṃ niyamaḥ caitra^ eva kartā^ agninā^ eva karaṇena taṇḍulāneva karma^ iti||

[276,16] api ca niyamārthamātre(mantare)ṇānuvādaḥ| dṛṣṭaṃ ca vākyārthe kevalaṃ padam| yathā śrotriyaḥ chando 'dhīte^ iti| sādhanavacanam eva kevanaśrotriyapadaṃ chando 'dhīte^ ity asmin kriyāsādhanasamudāye vartate| tathā jīvatīti kriyāpadam ākṣiptakriyāsādhanaviśeṣam| (cchatraṃ) chando 'dhīte prāṇān dhārayatīti vākye śritriyo jīvatīti ca padārthābhivyaktiḥ| sarvathā sarvapadeṣu vākyaśaktir ity aṅgīkṛtapadāntarārthāny eva sarvāṇi padānīty etad ucyate||

[276,22] tasmāt kvacit kriyāpadena^ eva sādhanam pakṣiptam| kvacic ca nāmapadena^ eva kriyā^ abhihitā| tasmāt teṣāṃ vibhāgo duravabodha^ iti| tataḥ vākyāt pravibhajya [padaṃ] vyākaraṇīyaṃ kriyāvācakaṃ vā jīvatītyādi kārakavācakaṃ vā śrotriyaḥ kṣatriyaḥ ityādi||

[276,26] anyathā yadi vākyāt pravibhajya na vyākriyeta tadā tato bhavaty aśvo 'jāyaḥ ity evamādiṣu nāmākhyātasārūpyād anirjñātaṃ kathaṃ kriyāyāṃ kākake vā vyā[kri]priyeta bhavati nidhehīti sarvanāmnaḥ śatrantasya vā saptabhyāṃ rūpam| bhavati bījād aṅkura^ ity ākhyātam||

[277,9] aśvaś caratīti jātivācakam| aśvastvaṃgrāmam iti śvayater luṅ-antasya madhyamapuruṣaikavacanāntasyaprayogaḥ| ajāpayaḥ pīyatām iti nāmapadam| ajāpayastvaṃ rājanam iti japater ṇij-antasya laṅmadhyamapuruṣīkavacanāntasya rūpam||

[277,12] teṣāṃ śabdārthapratyayānāṃ saṅkaraṃ pradarśya vibhāgaḥ 
pratiādyate śvetate prāsāda^ iti kriyārthaḥ| śvetaḥ prāsāda^ iti kārakārthaḥ| kriyākārakatvād vastunaḥ śvetate śveta^ iti ca kriyā-arthena ca karakārthena cābhidhānam||

[277,16] yathā śabdaḥ kriyākārakātmā yaḥ śvetaḥ sa śvetate yaḥ śvetate sa śvetaḥ ity evaṃ kriyākārakāṣytiriktaḥ ekaḥ śabdaḥ tathā^ arthaḥ tadvācyaḥ kriyākārakātmā| tathā tadviṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ kriyākārakātmā| tathā^ arthaḥ śabdapratyayātmā| pratyayo api śabdārthātmā| kasmāt so 'yam ity abhisambandhāt| yo 'rthaḥ sa śabdaḥ yaḥ śabdaḥ so'rthaḥ pratyayaś ca yaḥ 
pratyayaḥ so 'rthaḥ śabdaś cety evaṃ abhedākārapratyayaḥ smṛtyātmā saṅketaḥ iti| evaṃ saṅkararūpataḥ pravibhāgaḥ kāryah||

[277,12] katham yas tu śveto 'rthaḥ śvetaguṇaḥ prāsādādir arthaḥ sa śabdapratyayayoḥ ālambanībhūtaḥ vācyatvena viṣayatvena ca| sa hi svābhir avasthābhiḥ navapurāṇatvādibhiḥ vikriyamāṇaḥ na śabdasahagato nāpi buddhisahagataḥ| śabda(278,12)pratyayābhāve api bhāvāt| evaṃ pratyayaḥ na śabdasahagato nāpy arthasahagataḥ| itarābhāve api bhāvāt| tathā śabdaḥ api^ arthapratyayavyabhivārāt iti^ evaṃ anyathā^ arthaḥ anyathā śabdaḥ anyathā pratyayaḥ iti pravibhāgaḥ| evaṃ ca [tat]pravibhāgasaṃyamāt idaṃ varṇaiḥ saṅketitaiḥ vākyātmakam abhivyajyamānam ayam arthaḥ pratyayaś cāyaṃ iti pravibhāgena saṃyamāt pravibhaktapadapadārthapratyayānāṃ svena rūpeṇa sākṣāt karaṇāt yoginaṅ sarvabhūtarutajñānaṃ sampadyate||3.17||

[278,18] saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇāt pūrvajātijñānam dvaye khalv amī saṃskārāḥ vāsanā 'bhidhānāḥ paridṛṣṭāḥ aparidṛṣṭāś ca smṛtikleśahetavaḥ| paridṛṣṭāḥ (ubhaye api) kleśakarmavipākānubhavanirvartitāḥ vittadharmāḥ dharmādharmarūpāḥ dharmādharmanimittāḥ pūrvabhavābhisaṃskṛtāḥ (tā)atikrāntānantajanmaniṣpāditāḥ 
pariṇāmadeṣṭānirodhaśaktijīvanadharmāḥ pūṛvavyākhyātāḥ aparidṛṣṭāḥ| teṣu dvayeṣu saṃskāreṣu saṃyamaḥ kriyamāṇaḥ saṃskārasākṣātkriyāyai samarthaḥ bhavati ||

[279,12] te ca deśakālanimittānubhavaviśiṣṭayā nirvartitatvāt sākṣātkaraṇakāle api deśādiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭā eva pratyakṣībhavanti| tasmāt na ca (te) deśakālanimittānubhavair vinā teṣām asti sākṣātkaraṇam||

[279,15] tad itthaṃ saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇāt pūrvajātijñānam utpadyate yoginaḥ| paratrāpi prāṇiṣu^ evam eva yeṣāṃ jātiṃ bubhutsate tadīyasaṃskāreṣu saṃyamāt[saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇāt] tatupūrvajātijñānam utpadyate||

[279,18] tatra saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇaphalavyañjanārthaṃ idam ākhyānam upakṣipyate bhagavato jaigīṣavyasya saṃskārasākṣātkaraṇāt daśasu mahāsargeṣu janmapariṇāmaṃ anupaśyato vivekajaṃ vairāgyanimittaṃ jñānaṃ 
prādur abhūt||

[279,21] atha bhagavān āvaṭayaḥ tantradharaḥ tam uvāca daśasu mahāsargeṣu [bhavyatvāt] anabhibhūtabuddhisattvena tvayā narakatiryaggamanaduḥkham anupaśyatā devam anuṣyeṣu punaḥ punaḥ utpadyamānena sukhaduḥkhayoḥ kim upalabdham adhikam iti||

[279,24] bhagavantam āvaṭyaṃ jaigīṣavya^ uvāca daśasu mahāsargeṣu bhavyatvāt anabhibhūtabuddhisattvena mayā narakatiryaggamanam anupaśyatā devam anuṣyeṣu punaḥ punaḥ utpadyamānena yat kiñcit anubhūtaṃ tat sarvaṃ duḥkham iti [duḥkham eva] pratyavaimi||

[280,17] bhagavān avaṭya^ uvāca yad^ idam āyuṣmataḥ pradhānavaśitvam anuttamaṃ ca sanyoṣasukhaṃ [kim idam api duḥkhapakṣe nikṣiptam iti| bhagavān jaigīṣavya^ uvāca viṣayasukhāpekṣayaivedam anuttamaṃ santoṣasukhaṃ [prdhānavaśitvasukhaṃ ca] uktam| kaivalyasukhāpekṣayā duḥkham eva| buddhisattvasyāyaṃ dharmaḥ triguṇaḥ.] triguṇaś ca pratyayo heyapakṣe nyasta^ iti| kim apekṣayā punar etat dvayaṃ sukhaṃ bhavatīty āha duḥkharūpas tṛṣṇātantuḥ| tṛṣṇāsantānaduḥkhopaśamarūpaṃ tu prasannam anābādhaṃ sarvānukūlaṃ sukham idam uktaṃ sanyoṣasukhaṃ pradhānajayasukhaṃ ca||3.18||

[280,25] pratyayasya paracittajñānam| parapratyaye saṃyamāt 
parapratyayasya sākṣātkaraṇam| tatsākṣātkaraṇe tatsaṃbandhinaḥ 
paracittasya jñānaṃ bhavati ||3.19||

[281,13] tatra parapratyayasākṣātkaraṇāt tadduḥkhe duḥkhitvaṃ tatsukhe skhitvaṃ ca prāpnotīty āśaṅkyāha na ca sālambanaṃ tasya aviṣayībhūtatvāt| raktaṃ pratyayaṃ jānāti yogī| parasyāmuṣmmin ālambane raktaḥ pratyaya^ iti na jānāti| parapratyayasya yadālambanaṃ sukharūpaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ vā yena sukhī duḥkhī vā bhavet||

[281,18] nanu ca^ ālambanam asañjānānaḥ katham amusmin rakta^ iti sañjānīta iti^ ucyate raktam amuṣminn iti sāmānyaṃ vijānāti| na punaḥ sukhaviśeṣaṃ sukhinaḥ pratyayasyālambanībhūtam ālambate| parapratyayamātraṃ tu yogicittasyālambanībhūtam||3.20||

[281,22] kāyarūpasaṃyamāt tadgrāhyaśaktis tambhe cakṣuḥprakāśāsamprayoge antardhānam| yogina^ ātmīyasya kāyasya rūpaṃ kāyarūpam| tasmin rūpe saṃyamāt tasya rūpasya yā paracakṣurgrāhyā śaktiḥ tāṃ pratibadhnāti  (282,12) niruṇāddhi| tataḥ pareṣāṃ cakuṣuḥprakāśo yogikāyarūpeṇa na samprayujyate| tataś ca^ āntardhānam| etena śabdādyantardhānam uktam| yogiśabdasya yā grāhyaśaktiḥ tasyāṃ saṃyame sati yā śabdasya grāhyaśaktiḥ sā pratiṣṭamyate| tena yogiśandaṃ na paraḥ śṛṇoti| tathā sparśādiṣv api draṣṭavyam||3.21||

[282,16] so 'pagamaṃ nirupakramaṃ ca karma tatsaṃyamād aparāntajñānam ariṣṭebhyo vā| āyurvipākaṃ karma dvividhā| katham tv aritaṃ nimittam ādāya phalapadaṃ sopakramam ucyate| yat tu mandaprasaraṃ cireṇa phalaṃ dāsyati tat nirupakramam ||

[282,19] yathā^ ārdraṃ vastraṃ vitānitaṃ vistāritaṃ kṣepīyasā kṣipratareṇa kālena śuṣyet tathā sopakramaṃ karma kṣipramāyurvipākaṃ kṛtvā kṣīyate||

[282,21] tad eva vastraṃ sampiṇḍitaṃ avitānitam vireṇa śuṣyed evaṃ nirupakramaṃ cireṇa^ āyuḥ karoti||

[282,23] yathā vā^ agniḥ śuṣke kakṣe yuktavāte^ anukūlamātariśvani samantato niyuktaḥ kṣepīyasā kālena dahet tathā sopakramaṃ karma kṣepīyasā kālena āyurdhakṣyati||

[283,14] sa^ evāgniḥ tṛṇarāśau krameṇa nyastaścireṇa ddaheta tathā nirupakramaṃ karma||

[283,15] tad etat dvividham ekabhavikam āyuṣkaraṃ karma| tatsaṃyamād aparāntajñānam| aparāntasya prāyaṇasya^ ātmīyasya jñānam| tathā 
parakīyadvividhakarmasaṃyamāt paraprāyāṇajñānam api bhavati| prāyaṇajñāne ca prayojanaṃ puruṣakartavyatāṃ pratisamīhanam||

[283,19] ariṣṭebhyo vā prāyaṇajñānam| trividham ariṣṭam ādhyātmikam ādhibhautikam ādhidaivikaṃ ca| tatra^ ādhyātmikasyodāharaṇam ghoṣaṃ svadehepihitakarṇo na śṛṇoti| jyotir vā netre 'vaṣṭbdhe na paśyati||

[283,22] tathā^ ādhibhautikaṃ yamapuruṣān paśyati| pitṛnatītān āgatān aksamād vetti| ādhidaivikam akasmād eva siddhādīn paśyati viparītaṃ vā sarvaṃ paśyati karoti ca| tatrāriṣṭam iti laukikī vācoyuktiḥ| evaṇ hy āha viparītadarśanaṃmamāsīd ariṣṭam iti||3.22||

[284,16] maitryādiṣu balāni| maitrī karuṇā muditeti tisro bhāvanāḥ| tatra bhūteṣu sukhiteṣu maitīṃ bhāvayitvā maitrī-balaṃ labhate| duḥkhiteṣu karuṇāṃ bhāvayitvā karuṇābalaṃ labhate| puṇyaśīlesu muditāṃ bhāvayitvā muditā-balaṃ labhate||

[284,20] bhāvanātaḥ maitryādibhāvanātaḥ samādhir yaḥ saṃjāyate sa saṃyamaḥ maitryādibhāvanāsrthair yannimittaḥ| tataḥ saṃyamāt maitryādigatāt yogino balānyavandhyavīryāṇi jāyante||

[284,23] pāpaśīleṣu tu^ asya^ upekṣā na tu bhāvanā| tataś ca bhāvanāyā abhāvāt tasyām upekṣāyāṃ nāsti samādhir ity ato na balam upekṣātaḥ| tatra saṃyamābhāvād iti||3.23||

[284,26] balesu hastibalādīni| etair maitryādinimittair balair balavān yogī hastyādīna baleṣu saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā hastyādibalo bhavati||3.24||

[284,28] pravṛttyālokanyāsāt sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭajñānam| yā^ asau jyotiṣmatī pravṛttir uktā tasyāṃ prakāśaḥ ālokaḥ taṃ prakāśaṃ yogī (285,29) sūkṣme vā vyavahite vā viprakṛṣṭe vā^ arthe vinyasya sthāpayitvā tam adhigaddhati||3.25||

[287,21] bhuvanajñānaṃ sūrye saṃyamāt| sūrye saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā samastaṃ bhuvanaprastāraṃ pratyakṣīkurvīta| bhāṣyaṃ tu gatārthaṃ sarvapurāṇaprasiddhatvāt||3.26||

[287,23] candre tārāvyūhajñānam| sūrye saṃyamanād bhuvanaprastāraṃ viditvā tadanantaraṃ candre saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā tārāṇāṃ vyūhaṃ vijānīyāt| sūryasaṃyamād bhuvanaprastāramātraṃ prajñātam evaṃ prastīrṇāḥ lokā nadyaḥ samudrā girayaś ceti| na tu tārā itthaṃ vyūhitā^ anena sambaddhā iti| candrasaṃyamāt tu tārāvyūhaprakārāvagatir iti||3.27||

[287,28] dhruve tad-gatijñānam| tataś ca samanantaraṃ dhruve saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā tārāṇāṃ gatiṃ vijānīyāt anena prakāreṇa sañcakaramāṇā^ anena viśiṣṭā ity asmin kāle^ anena graheṇāyaṃ graho virudhyate^ ittahastamayaḥ| ittham udayaḥ tad-dvāreṇa prāṇabhṛtāṃ śubham aśubhaṃ cetyādi vijānīyāt||

[288,19] evam anyeṣu cordhvaṃ vimāneṣu saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā tāni vimānānītthaṃ 
prakārāṇyevaṃ gatānīty evam ādi sarvaṃ vijānīyāt||3.28||

[288,21] nābhicakre kāyavyūhajñānam| nābhicakre saṃyamaṃ kṛtvā kāyavyūhaṃ vijānīyāt| vātapitaśloṣmāṇas trayo doṣāḥ etāvatpramāṇā etat-pratiṣṭhā ityādi jñeyam||

[288,24] tathā dhātavaḥ sapta bāhyābhyantarabhāvenāvasthitāḥ| (rasaḥ)tvak bāhyā sarveṣām| tato 'hyantaraṃ lohitaṃ tato māṃsaṃ tato 'sthi tato medaḥ tato majjā tataḥ śuklaṃ sarvābhyantaram ity evaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvam eṣāṃ bāhyam ity eṣa vinyāsaḥ| tathā nāḍīnāṃ ca vyūhaṃ vijānīyāt||3.29||

[288,27] kaṇṭhakrūpe kṣutpipāsānivṛttiḥ| jihvāyā^ adhastāt tantuḥ unnāmikāyāṃ jihvāyāṃ dṛśyate| tantor adhattāt kanṭhaḥ| tato 'dhastāt kūpaḥ| tatra saṃyamāt kṣutpipāse na pratibādhete||3.30||

[289,16] karmanḍyāṃ sthairyam kūpasyādhastād urasi kurmānāma nāḍī| tasyāṃ kṛtasaṃyamaḥ sthirapadaṃ labhate cittasthairyaṃ labhate| yathā sarpo godhā vā dhāraṇād urasā^ atyantaṃ sthirībhavati||3.31||

[289,19] mūrdhajyotiṣi siddhadarśanam| śiraḥ-kapāle 'nyaś cchidraṃ 
prabhāsvaratvāt jyotiḥ ity ucyate| tatra saṃyamāt siddhānāṃ dyāvāpṛthivyor antarālacāriṇāṃ darśanaṃ bhavati||3.32||

[289,22] prātibhād vā sarvam| ātmani saṃyamaṃ kurvataḥ īśvaraṃ vā atyarthaṃ praṇīdadhānasya yoginaḥ prātibhaṃ tārakaṃ svayam utthitaṃ mānasaṃ jñānam tat vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇasya vivekajasya jñānasya _pūrvarūpam| yathā udaye prabhā^ auṣasī kiṃcid unmīlantī lohitāyamānā bhāsakarasya ujjigamiṣataḥ pūrvarūpam| tasmād vā sarvaṃ vijānanti yoginaḥ prātibhasya jñānasyotpādād iti||3.33||

[290,12] <bh>hṛdaye cittasaṃvit| yad idam asmin brahmapure śarīre daharam</bh> alpaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ puṇḍarīkākāro māṃsapiṇḍo 'dhomukhaḥ veśmavadane kanāḍīsuṣirayogād veśma| tatra vijñānaṃ cittaṃ tasmin urassarovilagnāle prāṇaveśmani puṇḍarūke saṃyamāc citte sattve saṃvit bhavati||3.34||

[290,16] sattvapuruṣayor atyantāsaṃkīrṇayoḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ parārthāt svārthasaṃyamāt puruṣajñānam| sattvaṃ cittaṃ puruṣo bhoktā tayoḥ atyantāsaṅkīrnayoḥ atyantavilakṣaṇayor atīva pṛthagbhūtayoḥ vṛttisārūpyaṃ pratyayāviśeṣaḥ sa bhogaḥ puruṣasya| parārthāt sattvāt puruṣapratyayena saṅkīrṇābhāsāt vivicya svārthe citimātrasvarūpe saṃyamāt puruṣajñānaṃ bhavait ||

[290,21] kīdṛśaṃ tat parārthaṃ cittasattvam yato vivicya svārthe puruṣe saṃyamaḥ kriyata^ iti| buddhisattvam prakhyāśīlaṃ samānaṃ sattvaṃ vastukāryaṃ puruṣāṛthakartavyatayā samavashānam upanibandhanaṃ yoyos te samānasattvopanibandhane rajastamasī vaśīkṛtya nyag-bhāvam āpādya sattvapuruṣānyatāpratyayena yoginaḥ pariṇatam||

[290,25] tasmāc ca sattvāt pariṇāminaḥ triguṇād anityād aśuddhāt parārthād acetanāt atyantavidharmā viśuddho 'nyaḥ apariṇāmī svārtho nityaḥ cinmātrarūpaḥ puruṣaḥ||

[291,9] tayor atyantāsaṅkīrṇayoḥ pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ puruṣasya darśitaviṣayatvāt|  sa bhogapratyayaḥ| nāppariṇāmitve sati darśitaviṣayatvād anyo bhogaḥ sambhavati| sattvasya parāthatvāt abiśiṣṭo bhogapratyayo dṛśyaḥ puruṣeṇ na||

[291,12] yas tu tasmāt gauṇapratyayāt pratyayinaś ca viśiṣṭaś citamātrarūpo 'nyaḥ pauruṣeyaḥ pratyayaḥ svārthaḥ puruṣa^ eva 
pariṇāmāṇupapatteḥ pratyayaḥ| tatra sayamāt puruṣaviṣayā prajñā jāyate||

[291,15] nanu ca yadi puruṣaḥ pratyayasya viṣayīkriyate tena cet puruṣo gṛhyate parārthaḥ puruṣaḥ prāpnoti | na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| na ca 
puruṣapratyayena puruṣaviṣayeṇa pratyayena buddhisattvātmanā buddhisattvadharmeṇa puruṣo dṛśyate buddher acetanatvāt||

[291,18] puruṣa eva taṃ pratyayaṃ svālambanaṃ puruṣābhāsaṃ paśyati puruṣābhāsatvem eva pratyayasya puruṣālambanatvam| na pratyayasya puruṣālambanatvam| yathā mukhasannidhau darpiṇo mukhākāreṇa pariṇamate tathā cittaṃ puruṣākāreṇa pariṇataṃ puruṣābhāsaṃ puruṣeṇa gṛhyate||

[291,22] tathā coktam vijñātāram are kena vijānīyāt iti| na kasyacit puruṣaḥ karmatāṃ pratipadyata^ ity arthaḥ||

[291,24] nanu cakṣurādarśatalapratihataṃ pratinivṛtya yathā mukhasya grāhakaṃ padaṃ puruṣoapi cittasattvapratihataḥ pratinivṛtya svātmānaṃ gṛhṇīyāt na pariṇāmānupapatteḥ puruṣasya| na hy apariṇāminaś cittaṃ prati pravṛttiḥ pratinivṛttir vā sādhimānam upāśnuvīta||

[291,28] na cāpi cakṣuḥ pratinivṛtya mukhaṃ grāhayati| mukhasya darpaṇamaṇḍalādhikaraṇatvenānubhūyamānatvāt| na hi svamukhaṃ darpaṇādhikaraṇastham| kiṃ ca (292,17) ādarśānuvidhānāc ca| yathā khaḍge dīrghaṃ mukhaṃ dṛśyate| na svamukhaṃ dīrgham| pratihatena ced upalabhyate cakṣuṣā nijāmukhaṃ tatra^ ādarśānuvidhānam ayuktam||3.35||

[292,19] tataḥ prātibhaśrāvāṇavedanādarśāsvādavārtā jāyante| tataḥ svārthe samādhānāt pratibhāt ātmasaṃyamajanitāt mānasāt jñānāt sūkṣmavyavahitaviprakṛṣṭātītānāgatajñānam sampadyate| śrāvaṇāt divytaśabdānāṃ śravaṇam| vedanāt sparśajñānnāt siddhādīnāṃ sparśaṃ jānāti| ādarśāt cākṣuṣāt jñānāt divyaṃ rūpam avagacchati| āsvādāt rasanendriyajñānāt divyarasasaṃvedanaṃ bhavati| vṛttau bhavaṃ vārtaṃ lokasaṃvyavahārajñānaṃ tasmād eṣa saṃvyavahāratattvarūpaṃ yathāvad adhigacchati||3.36||

[292,26] te samādhāv upasargā vyutthāne siddhayaḥ| te prātibhādayaḥ puruṣe samāhitacittasya^ u(sya samu)utpadyamānā upasargāḥ 
puruṣadarśanapratyanīkatvāt| vyutthitacittasyotpadyamānāḥ siddhayaḥ| yady api puruṣe saṃyamād utpadyante tathāpi na viraktasya samāhitascittasya jāyate||3.37||

[293,13] bandhakāraṇaśaithilyāt pracārasaṃvedanāc ca cittasya paraśarīrāveśaḥ| lolībhūtasya ghaṇṭābhighātulyasya dedīpyamānāṅgārarāśidyuticañcalasyamanas_O'pratiṣṭhasya śarīre karmāśayād bandhaḥ pratiṣṭhety arthaḥ| śarīramātrasaṃkocāvasthānam||

[293,16] tasya bandhasya karma kāraṇam| kāraṇaśaithilyaṃ karmāśayaśithilatvaṃ samādhibalād bhavati| pracārasaṃvedanaṃ ca cittasya samādhijanmeiva| karmabandhakṣayāt svacittapracārasaṃvedanāc ca asminnimitte hṛṣyati muhyati lubhyati cānena kāraṇena^ ity evamādisvacittasañcaraṇasattavavedanāc ca yogī cittaṃ svaśarīrān niṣkṛṣya śarīrāntareṣu nikṣipati||

[293,21] cittam asya^ utpatat indriyāṇi^ anūtpatanti| tasmād indriyavṛttilābhāt paraśarīreṣu prāṇādivṛttaya^ utpadyante| yathā madhukararājaṃ makṣikā utpatantam anūtpatanti niviśamānam anu niviśante tathendriyāṇī paraśarīrāveśe cittam anu vidhīyante||3.38||

[293,25] udānajayāt jalapaṅkakaṇṭakādiṣv asaṅga^ utkrāntiś ca| samastendriyavṛttiḥ jīvanaṃ sarveṣām ekādaśānām indriyāṇām ekena saṃmūrcchtena vyāpareṇa yena śarīraṃ dhriyate tajjīvanam| tasya jīvanasya 
puruṣārthakartavyatāniyamāt pañcatayī kriyā 
prāṇāpānavyānodānasamānabhedena prastīryate||

[294,15] tatra prāṇo mukhanāsikāgatir āhṛdayavṛttiḥ| samaṃ nayanāt samānaḥ ānābhivṛttiḥ ānārbhadeśaṃ vartate| mūtrapurīṣādīnām apanayanādpānaḥ sa ca nābhiprabhṛti^ āpādat alaṃ vartate| ūrdhvaṃ śarīrasya nayanād udānaḥ sa ca pādatalād ārabhya^ āmastakavṛttiḥ| samastaśarīravyāpī vyāna^ iti||

[294,19] eteṣāṃ pradhānaḥ pradhānaḥ prāṇaḥ| tajjayāc ca sarveṣām apānādīnāṃ tu jayo bhavati| teṣāṃ tu jayopāyo vistareṇa hairaṇyagarbhe vyākhyātaḥ| iha tu manojayānujitatvāt teṣāṃ jayopāyo na pṛthag abhihitaḥ prāṇāyamajayāt tu jīyanta eva na pṛthag eṣāṃ jayopāyapratipādanam ādṛtyam iti phalamātram udānajiter ucyate^ udānajayāt jalapaṅkakaṇṭakādiṣu^ ādinā kṛpāṇadhārādiṣv asaṅgaḥ| kiṃ cotkrāntiś ca prāyaṇakāle bhavati| tāṃ prāyaṇakāle bhavantīm utkrāntim udānajayāt vaśīkṛtya svatantratayā pratipadyate||3.39||

[294,26] samānajayāt jvalanam| jitasamānaḥ tejasa^ upadhyamānaṃ kṛtvā samānavāybāśrayatvāt udānatejasoḥ tadupadhmānakarmakārakas tāvad yogī jāṭharaṃ jvalayati||3.40||

[294,28] śrotrākāśayoḥ saṃbandhasaṃyamād divyaṃ śrotram| kaḥ punar asau saṃbandho yatra saṃyama^ ityāha sarvaśrotrāṇām ākācaṃ pratiṣṭhā| śabdānāṃ ca^ ākāśakuṇatvād ākāśam eva pratiṣṭhā| grāhyaṃ śabdaṃ prati śratrasya vyavadhānābhāvaḥ śrotrākāśayoḥ sambandhaḥ||

[295,17] tathā ca^ uktam tulyadeśaśravaṇānām| śrūyate 'neneti śravaṇam| tulyadeśāni śravaṇāni yeṣāṃ te tulyadeśāśravaṇāḥ| teṣām ekaśrutitvaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhavati| tad-ekasminn avakāśadāyini nirāvaraṇātmake vastuni sati yujyate| taccaitad ākāśasya sattve liṅgam||

[295,21] tathā^ anāvaraṇātmakaṃ ca^ uktam| (kutaḥ) tathā mūrtasyānyatrākāśāt ākāśavilakṣaṇāsya ghaṭādeḥ āvaraṇadarśanāt| ato yasya maṇivajrādimadhyeṣv apy āvaraṇaṃ nāsti tadākāśam| ata^ eva vibhutvam api 
prakhyātam ākāśasya||

[295,24] śrotrasya liṅgam āha śabdagrahaṇānumitaṃ tu śrotram| yasmāt badhirābadhirayor ekaḥ śabdaṃ gṛhṇāti^ aparo na gṛhṇāti| yannimittam abadhirasya śabdagrahaṇam itarasya cāgrahaṇaṃ tacchrotram| tasmācchrotram eva śabdaviṣayam| badhireṇāśravaṇāt| śrotrākāśayoḥ saṃbandhe kṛtasaṃyamasya toginaḥ civyaṃ śrotraṃ pravartate||3.41||

[295,28] kāyākāśayoḥ sambandhasaṃyamasya laghutūlasamāpatteś ca ākāśagamanam| yatra kāyāaḥ tatra^ ākāśam| tasyāvakāśadānāt kāyasya avinābhāvalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ tenākāśena| tatra kāyākāśayoḥ itaretarāvinābhāvaprāpti(296,13)lakṣaṇasambandhe kṛtasaṃyamo yogī jitvā tatsambandhaṃ tato laghuṣu tūlādiṣu ā paramāṇubhyaḥ samāpatteḥ jitasambandhaḥ tūlādivat lāghavaṃ pratipadyate||

[296,15] jitakāyākāśasambandhaḥ samāpannaḥ tūlāditulyalāghavaśīanārthaṃ jale pādābhyāṃ viharati| tataḥ ūrdhvam ūrṇānābhitantumatre viharati| tatra sañjātakokilatantusamānalāghavaḥ punaḥ raśmiṣu viharati| tato mātariśvani| tato yathā^ iṣṭam ākāśe gatir asya bhavati||3.42||

[296,19] bahir akalpitā vṛttir mahā-videhā tataḥ prakāśāvaraṇakṣayaḥ| śarīrāt bahiḥ deśāntare viṣayaviśeṣe svayam eva samāhibalāt upajāyate yā manaso vṛttiḥ sā mahā-videhā nāma dhāraṇā| sā yadi śarīrapratiṣṭhasya^ eva manasp vṛttimātreṇa bahiḥ viṣaye bhavati tadānīṃ kalpitā ucyate| śarīrasthamnassaṅkalpapūrvakatvāt| yā tu samādhibalanimittāt śarīre bandhakāraṇaśaithilyāt| śarīranirapekṣā bahir bhūtasya^ eva manaso bahir vṛttiḥ sā khalu^ akalpitā ity ucyate| yathā^ eva kalpitāyāṃ śarīrāvadhika eva bahirvṛttilābhaḥ evaṃ nākālpetāyāṃ śarīrādhiṣṭhānāvadhisamīkṣā||

[296,26] sā cobhayy api videhā^ eva bahir upakalpitatvāt| tatra yā iyam akalpitā mahāvideheti samākhyāyate| tatra kalpitayā sādhayanty akalpitāṃ mahāvidehām| kalpanāsaṃśīlanasāvyatvād akalpitāyāḥ kalpitayā ca saṃsādhayanti| (297,11) yayā mahāvidehayā paraśarīrāṇy āviśanti yoginaḥ| tataś ca dhāraṇātaḥ mahāvidehātaḥ prakāśātmano buddhisattvasya yadāvaraṇaṃ kleśakarmavipakatrayaṃ rajastamomūlaṃ tasya ca kṣayo bhavati||3.43||

[297,14] sthūlasvarūpasūkṣmānvayārthavattvasaṃyamāt bhūtajayaḥ| sthūlaṃ ca svarūpam ca sūkṣmaṃ cānvayaś cārthavattvaṃ cety evam ekaikaṃ bhūtaṃ pañcātmakam| tatra pārtivāḥ śabda^ādayaḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ viśeṣāḥ pañcalakṣaṇatanmātrārabdhatvāt 
pṛthivyāḥ| saha^ ākārādibhiḥ| pṛthivyās tāvad ākārādayaḥ ākāro gauravaṃ raukṣyaṃ ceraṇaṃ sthairyam eva ca| sthitir bhedaḥ kṣamā kāṣṇryaṃ kāṭhinyaṃ sarvabhogyatā|| iti|| tathā snehaprabhāsaukṣmyamārdavagauravarakṣāpavitratvasandhānādaya audakā guṇāḥ || tathā taijasāḥ ūrdhvabhāktvapāvanatā-dagdhṛtvapācakatvalāghavabhāsvaratvādayaḥ || tathā vāyavīyāḥ tiryag-yānapavitratvākṣepabalanodanatva^ ādayaḥ|| tathā nābhasāḥ sarvagatyavyūhaviṣṭambhāḥ||

[297,24] tad etaiḥ ākārādibhiḥ saha śabdādayo viśeṣāḥ sthūlaśabdena saṃvyavahārārthṃ śāstre paribhāṣitāḥ evamāpyāścatvāro rasādayo viśeṣāḥ saukṣmyādibhiḥ saha^ evaṃ taijasā rūpādayas trayo^ ūrdhvabhāktvādibhiḥ saha sthūlā ucyante| tathā vāyavīyāḥ śabdasparśatiryakpātāayaḥ sthūlā viśeṣāḥ| evam ākāśasya sarvagatatvādayaḥ sthūlā viśeṣāḥ| tat etat bhūtānāṃ 
prathamaṃ sthūlaṃ rūpaṃ uktam||

[298,11] dbitīyam adhunā svarūpam^ ucyate| tac ca sāmānyaṃ prthiveṣu bhinneṣv abhinnam anugataṃ mūrtir eva bhūmīti| tathā rasādiṣv āpyeṣv anugataṃ sāmānyaṃ sneha^ eva jalam iti| tathaivāgnayeṣu rūpādiṣv anugataṃ sāmānyaṃ bahnir uṣṇatvam eva| tathā sparśatiryakpātādiṣv anugataṃ sāmānyaṃ praṇāmitvam eva vāyuḥ| tathā śabdādi_ṛṛv anugatam sāmānyaṃ sarvatogatir eva^ ākāśaḥ ity etat svarūpaśabdena^ uktaṃ dvitīyaṃ rūpam||

[297,16] tasya sāmānyasya rūpasya śabdādayo viśeṣāḥ| tathā ca^ uktam ekajātisamanvitānāṃ sattvādiguṇasamanugatānām eṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ pṛthivyādīn sāmānyānāṃ śabdādidharmamātraṃ vyāvartate||

[297,19] sāmānyaviśeṣasamudāyo dravyam| kasmāt dviṣṭho hi samūhaḥ dvābhyāṃ 
prakārābhyāṃ sthito dviṣṭhaḥ pratyastamitabhedāvayavaḥ vyāvṛttapratyayaśūnya^ ity arthaḥ| yathā śarīraṃ vṛkṣo yūṭhaṃ vanam iti||

[297,22] upāttabhedāvayavānugataḥ āgṛhītabhedāvayavānugataḥ samūhaḥ uhayaśabdenopādīyate| ubhaye devam anuṣyā iti| tadyathā samūhasya devā eko bhāgaḥ manuṣyā eko bhāgaḥ| tābhyām eva bhāgābhyām āgṛhīta-yathāsvabhedānugatābhyām abhidhīyate samūhaḥ ubhaye^ iti||

[299,12] tayoḥ bhedābhedo vivakṣitaḥ āmnāṇāṃ vanam iti| āmnāṇām iti bhedena vivakṣā| vanam ity abhedena| tābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ bhedābhedābhyām āmnāṇāṃ vanam iti ṣaṣṭhī prathamā| taddvayor vivakṣita^ ekaḥ samūha^ iti||

[299,15] sa punar dvividhaḥ samūhaḥ yutasiddhāvayavo 'yutasiddhāvayavaś ca| yutasiddhāvayavaḥ pṛthaksiddhāvayavaḥ samūho vanaṃ saṅgha^ iti| ayutasiddhāvayavaḥ apṛhaksiddhāvayavaḥ saṅghātaḥ śarīraṃ vṛkṣaḥ paramāṇur iti ||

[299,18] paramāṇūnām api sāvayavatvaṃ sattvādikāryatvāt mūrtatvāc ca ghaṭādivad eva| ayutasiddhāvayavabhedānugataḥ samūho dravyam iti patañjaliḥ| etatsvarūpam^ ity uktaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣātmakam||

[299,21] atha kim eṣāṃ sūkṣmaṃ rūpam iti| tanmātraṃ bhūtakāraṇam| anekaparamāṇūpārabdhatvāt tasya tanmātrasya^ eko 'vayavaḥ paramāṇuḥ| so api sāmānyaviśeṣātmā^ ayutasiddhāvayavabhedānugataḥ samudāya^ iti| evaṃ sarvayanmātrāṇi| tataś ca tanmātrāṇiviśeṣavinirmuktāni tadavayavāś ca 
paramāṇava^ iti^ etat bhūtānāṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūkṣmākhyaṃ rūpam saṃyamasya viṣaya iti| tanmātrārabdhatvāc ca sāvayavam ākāśam anityaṃ ca| na ca śabdavyatirekeṇa^ ākāśaṃ nāma dravyam upaladhuṃ śakyam| dharmadharmiṇor abhedāt||

[300,5] nanu ca keśādīnāṃ kṛṣṇmā apy apaiti śuklimā samupajāyate| tathā cāpāyopajanadharmāṇaḥ kṛṣṇimādayaḥ te guṇāḥ| yat tu na kṛṣṇimānam anu vinaśyati śuklimānamanveti tat dravyam| evam ākāśādīnām api guṇāvyatirekeṇa sadbhāvaḥ śabdapratyayādibhedāt iti||

[300,9] na dṛṣṭāntābhāvāt| te^ eva kṛṣṇāḥ śuklāś ca kleśāḥ kṛṣṇaśuklatvādidharmaiḥ pariṇamante| sarvaṃ hi sarvātmakam ity uktam| na khalu kvacid api kūṭasthe vyapanayanena dravyasvarūpam| yad^ api darśanasparśanābhyāṃ yad^ upalabhyate tat deavyam iti tad apy ayutasiddharūpasparśasamudāyatvād ubhābhyāṃ daranasparśanābhyāṃ gṛhyata ity adoṣaḥ||

[300,15] nanu ca na rūpaṃ sparśanena gṛhyate nāpi darśanena saprśaḥ yad ubhYbhyāṃ gṛhyate yat paśyāmi tad eva sparśāmīti tad dravyam| yat tu cakṣuṣā^ eva gṛhyate sparśanena^ eva ca gṛhyate sa guṇa^ iti||

[300,18] na māṣatilayavādibhinnajātivimiśrarāśāv anaikantikatvāt| gṛhyate ca darśanasparśanābhyāṃ rāśiḥ| kiṃ ca darśanasparśane guṇasya^ eva grāhake bāhyendriyatvāt śrotravat| na hi jātir api guṇavyatiriktā vidyate bhedenāgrāhaṇāt||

[300,21] kiṃ ca ghaṭādāv api parasparādhyāsena darśanasparśanābhyāṃ rūpasparśayor eva grahaṇam| yathedaṃ rūpaṃ durgandhi surabhirandhi ceti| tasmād ākāśam api tanmātrārabdhaṃ śabdavyatirekeṇāśakyaṃ pratipattum iti sāvayavam anityaṃ ca||

[300,24] atha bhūtānāṃ caturthaṃ rūpam| khyātiriyāsthitiśīlā guṇāḥ kāryasvarūpānupātinaḥ kāryadharmānuyāyinaḥ anvayaśabdena^ uktāḥ| anugamāt| tad-etadbhūtānāṃ caturthaṃ rūpaṃ saṃyamasya guṇānvayāhkyo viṣayaḥ||

[301,12] atha^ eṣāṃ pañcamaṃ rūpam arthavattvam| asya vyākyānaṃ bhogāpavarkārthatā| sā ca guṇeṣv anvayinī| tatra yā guṇeṣv anuvṛttā bhogāpavargāyatā tad idaṃ bhūtānāṃ pañcamaṃ rūpaṃ saṃyamasyārthavattvākhyo viṣayaḥ||

[301,15] guṇās tan-mātreṣu bhūteṣu bhūtavikāreṣu cānvayinaḥ| evaṃ tanmātraṃ sūkṣmarūpaṃ bhūtabhūtavikāreṣv anugatam| bhūtasvarūpam ca sthūleṣi bhūtavikāreṣv anugatam iti sarvam arthavat| guṇānāṃ bhogāpavargārthatvāt teṣāṃ ca sarvānvayitvāt tadarthavattvena sarvaṃ bhūtavikārādikam arthavat||

[301,19] teṣv idānīṃ bhūteṣu mañcasu pañcarūpeṣu sthūlāddippañcarūpeṣu yathākramaṃ saṃyamāt pūrvaṃ sthūlena saṃyamaṃ kṛtvāatajjayasālṣātkaraṇānantaraṃ svarūpasaṃyamaṃ kurvīta| punaḥ sūkṣme 
punaś cānvaye punaś cārthavattve^ ity evaṃ yathākramam saṃyamāt tasya tasya sthūlādikasya darśanaṃ jayaśś ca yathāvat prādurbhavati| tatra bhūtasvarūpāṇijitvā yogī bhūtajayī bhavati| tajjayāt vatsānusāriṇya^ iva gāvaḥ (yathā prakṛtibhūtā gāvaḥ)asya saṃkalpānuvidhāyinyo bhūtaprakṛtayo bhavanti||3.44||

[301,25] (svaṃ) saṃyamaphalam idānīm āha tato 'ṇiprādurbhāvaḥ kāyasampat tad-dharmānabhighātaś ca| tatrāṇimā ko bhavatīty āhāṇuḥ iti| sūkṣmād api sūkṣmataro bahvatīddhātaḥ| tenāṇimnā sarvam anupraviśati vajram api| tathā sarvasyādṛśyo bhavati||

[301,29] laghimā laghuḥ laghubhyaḥ tūlādibhyo api laghutaraḥ bhavati tena nirālambanaḥ sarvato gantuṃ paryāpnoti| mahimā mahān bhavati| ākāśam api (302,12)vyāpnoti prāptiḥ ihastha^ evāṅgulyaguṇāpi spṛśati candramasam| 
prākāmyaṃ pracurakāmo yatheṣṭakāmo bhavatīddhānābhighātaḥ| bhūmāv unmajjati ca yathākāmatvāt yathā^ udake||

[302,15] vaśitvam sarvalokavaśitvam| asya vyākhyānaṃ bhūtabhautikavaśī bhavati^ avaśyaś cānyeṣām| īśitvam teṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prabhavavyūhāpy ayānām utpattisthitipralayānām īṣṭe| yatrakāmāvasāyitvam yasmin kāmas tatreva tadavasānaṃ tadantaṃ gacchati| satyasaṃkalpād hetoḥ yathā saṃkalpaḥ tathā bhūtaprakṛtīnām avasthānaṃ bhavati||

[302,20] śakto api sa yogī na padārthaviparyāsaṃ karoti| nāgniṃ śītīkaroti| kasmāt anyasya yatrakāmāvasāyinaḥ pūrvasiddhasya 
parama-īśvarasya tathā teṣu padārtheṣu viparyāsena saṃkalpāt| na hi 
padārthaviparyāsaṃ cikīrṣamāṇasya pūrva shiddhaṃ prati vinā dveṣeṇa tadviparyāso bhavati| tad aśuddhyabhāvād eva na padārthaviparyāsaṃ karoti| kaliāṇatarācaraṇo hi sa^ iti| etāny aṣṭāv aṇimādyaiśvaryāṇi bhūtajayād bhavanti||

[302,26] kāyasampadvakṣyamāṇā| tddharmānabhighātaḥ teṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ dharmaiḥ na yogino'bhighāto bhavati| pṛthivī svena dharmeṇa mūrtyā na viruṇaddhi yoginaḥ śarīrādikriyām| katham śilām apy anupraviśati| nāpaḥ snigdhā yoginaṃ kledayanti varṣasahasram apy udake tiṣṭhantam| nāgnir uṣṇe dahati| na vāyuḥ praṇāmī (303,15) vahati| anāvaraṇātmake api prakaṭarūpe api bhavaty āvṛtaḥ prakaṭo na bhavati| siddhānām apy adṛśyo bhavati||3.45||

[303,17] rūpalāvaṇyabalavajrasaṃhananatvāni kāyasampat| rūpavān darśanīyaḥ| lāvaṇyavān kāntimān atiśayabalo vajrasaṃhananaś ca bhavati||3.46||

[303,19] grahaṇasvarūpāsmitānvayārthavattvasaṃyamād indriyajayaḥ| sarvam etad-gatasūtreṇa tuyavyākhyānam| asmitāmātraṃ tu viśeṣo bhūtānām iva tan-mātrāṇi| sāmānyaviśeṣātmā śabdādir viṣayaḥ| teṣv indriyāṇāṃ yā vṛttiḥ viṣayaviśeṣāvalokanātmikā tat grahaṇam| na ca tatsāmānyamātre śabdādisāmānyābhāsanamātre manasi grahaṇākāraḥ upapadyate| katham anālocitaḥ svaviṣayaviśeṣa^ indriyavasīyeta| na kathaṃ cid anuvyavasīyeta taimirikātaimirikayor anuvyavasāyānanuvyavasāyāhyām| tasmānnityā nendriyeṇānālocite viṣaye manasā^ anuvyavasāyaḥ| tatrāsyāṃ viśeṣātmikāyāṃ vṛttau prathamaṃ saṃyamaḥ kartavyaḥ||

[304,10] atha svarūpam punaḥ indriyāṇāṃ dvitīyaṃ rūpam| prakāśātmanoḥ sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ viṣayaviśeṣālocanam indriyāṇāṃ viśeṣaḥ| yathā pradīpasya ghaṭādiviśeṣākārāloko viśeṣaḥ| prakāśamātraṃ sāmānyam| ataḥ sāmānyaviśeṣātmakam ayutasiddhāvayavabhedānugataṃ dravyam indriyaṃ 
pradīpavat| yathā sāmānyamūrtidharmaḥ sthūrādiṣv anupravartate tathendriyaṃ prakāśātmakaṃ dravyaviśeṣātmikāyāṃ grahaṇavṛttāv anupravartate| tad etasmin dvitīye^ indriyāṇāṃ svarūpe saṃyamaḥ kriyeta||

[304,17] teṣāṃ indriyāṇāṃ tṛtīyaṃ rūpam asmitā-lakṣaṇo 'haṅkāraḥ| tasya ahaṅkārasya sāmānyasyendriyāṇi^ ayutasiddhāvayavabhedānugatāni grahaṇarūpānvayīni viśeṣāḥ| tatrāsmitāyāṃ saṃyamaḥ karaṇīyaḥ||

[304,20] caturthaṃ rūpaṃ vyavasāyātmakāḥ prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlā guṇāḥ| yeṣām incriyāṇi sāhaṅkārāṇi pariṇāmaḥ| tatra guṇānugame saṃyamaḥ kartavyaḥ||

[304,22] pañcamaṃ rūpaṃ guṇeṣu yad^ anugataṃ puruṣārthavattvam| ataḥ sarveṣām arthavattvam anugamaś ca pūrvavat||

[304,24] evaṃ eṣu pañcasv indriyarūpeṣu yathākramam kramānatikrameṇasaṃyamaḥ| tatra pratyekaṃ pañcasu jayaṃ ṛtvā 
pañcasvarūpajayaṃ labhate| tasmāt pañcasvarūpajayād indriy(ādi)jayo vakṣyamāṇaphalāya prādurbhavati yoginaḥ| yat tu tataḥ 
paramāvaśyatendriyāṇām iti tadviṣayagrahaṇavṛtter vaśitvamātraṃ viṣayāpratipattirūpam||3.47||

[305,16] tato manojavatvaṃ vikaraṇabhāvaḥ pradhānajayaś ca tata itndriyajayāt kāyasyānuttamaḥ na vidyate 'smād uttama^ ity anuttamaḥ gatilābho manojavatvam| videhānāṃ śarīranirapekṣāṇāṃ indriyāṇāṃ abhipretadeśāpekṣo 'bhipretakālāpekṣo'bhipretaśabdādiviṣayāpekṣaśa ca vṛttilābho vikaraṇabhāvaḥ| sarvaprakṛtivikāravaśitvaṃ aṣṭānāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ tadvikārāṇāṃ ca vaśitvaṃ pradhānajaya^ ity etās tisraḥ siddhayo madhupratīkāḥ iti^ ucyante| tāś ca pañcasvarūpajayād adhigamyante||3.48||

[305,23] sattvapuruṣānyatākhyātimātrasya sarvabhāvādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sarvajñātṛtvaṃ ca sarvaprakāreṇa sarvasādhanena nirdhūtarajastamomalasya sattvasya pare vaiśāradye svaddhe sthitipravāhe pare vartamānasya parasyāṃ vaśīkārasaṃjñāyāṃ jñānaprasādamātre niratiśaye vartamānasya sattvapuruṣānyathā-khyātimātrarūpapratiṣṭhasya sarvabhāvādhiṣṭhātṛtvam| sarvasya vastujātasyeṣṭe||

[306,16] sarvātmanā sakalaprakṛtivikārātmanā guṇādhyavasāyātmakāḥ buddhyādikāraṇabhāvapariṇatādhyavasāyātmakāḥ śāntoditāvayapadeśyadharmānuprasarmṇaḥ svāminaṃ k_ṛṛstrajñaṃ dṛśimātraṃ aśeṣaguṇāḥ dṛśyatvena bhogyatvenāvasthitāḥ labhyante^ ity arthaḥ||

[306,19] sarvajñātṛtvaṃ sarvātmakānāṃ śāntoditāvyapadeśyadharmatvena- avasthitānāṃ guṇānāṃ akramopārūḍham ekakālabhāvi vivekajaṃ jñānam ity arthaḥ| ity eṣā sarvavaśitvasarvajñātṛtvalakṣaṇā viśokā nāma siddhiḥ| yāṃ 
prāpya yogī sarvajñaḥ kṣīṇakleśabandhano viharati vaśī||3.49||

[306,23] tadvairāgyād api doṣabījakṣaye kaivalyam| yadā^ asya^ evaṃ bhavati sarvajñasya sarvabhāvānadhitiṣṭhataḥ kleśakarmakṣaye vivekī sattvasya pratyayaḥ| kīdṛśa puruṣaś ca sattvavidharmā^ apariṇāmī śuddho 'nyaḥ sattvāt ity anena prakāreṇa||

[306,27] tataḥ api sarvajñasarvabhāvādhyakṣatvāt virajyamānasya yāni kleśabījāni^ avidyāvāsanārūpāṇi dagdhaśālibījakalpāni prasavāsamarthāni tān caritārthena pralīyamānena saha^ āśrayeṇa manasā pratyastaṃ gaddhanti| teṣu pralīneṣu puruṣaḥ punar idaṃ tāpatrayaṃ ādhyātmikādilakṣaṇaṃ na bhuṅkte||

[307,13] tad eṣāṃ guṇānāṃ manasi kleśakarmavipakasvarūpeṇābhivyaktānāṃ caritārthānāṃ pratiprasave pralaye puruṣasyātyantiko guṇaviyogaḥ kaivalyam| tadā svarūpapratiṣṭhā citiśaktir eva puruṣaḥ| tatra tadvartāgyād apītyapiśabdād aprāptayogaiśvaryasyāpi samyagdarśanāt kaivalyam iti||3.50||

[307,17] sthānyupamantraṇe saṅgasmayākaraṇaṃ punar aniṣṭaprasaṅgāt| sthānaṃ svargādikaṃ yeṣān te sthānino devā indrādayaḥ| taiḥ upamantraṇaṃ bho bho^ ihoṣyatām ityādi| tasmin satyātmano hīnatvaṃ smarataḥ prāpnutaḥ saṅgasmayau (arthād iti) tau ca na kartavyau| saṅgasmayakaraṇe 'niṣṭaṃ prasajyeta||

[307,21] catvāraḥ khalv amī yoginaḥ prāthamakalpiko madhubhūmikaḥ 
prajñājyotiḥ atikrāntabhāvanīyaś ca| tān krameṇa vyācaṣṭe tatrābhyāsī abhyāsanaśīlaḥ pravṛttajyotiḥ jyotiṣmatyādīnām anyatamā pravṛttiḥ 
pravṛttā yasya sa pravṛttajyotiḥ prathamaḥ||

[307,25] ṛtambharaprajño dvitīyaḥ| bhūtendriyajayī tṛtīyaḥ| sa tu sarveṣu bhāviteṣu bhāvanīyeṣu sākṣāt kṛteṣu jiteṣu ca kṛtarakṣābandhaḥ upāttasya hi rakṣā vidhānīyā bhāvanīyeṣu sākṣātkaraṇīyeṣu kartavyatāsādhanavāṃś ca| kartavyatāyāḥ sādhanāni kartavyatāsādhanāni^ abhyāsavairāgyādīni yasya saḥ kartavyatāsādhanavān||

[308,24] yas tv atikrāntabhāvanīyaś caturthaḥ tasya cittapratisarga^ eva cittapralaya^ eva^ eko 'rthaḥ pariśiṣṭaḥ| saptavidhā tasya prāntabhūmim 
prajñā| tatra madhumatīṃ bhūmiṃ dvitīyām ṛtaṃbharaprajñābhūmiṃ sākṣātkurvato brāhmāṇasya sthānino devāḥ sattvaviśuddhimanupaśyantaḥ sthānair upamantrayante| bhoḥ ityādi prasiddhārthaṃ bhāṣyam||3.51||

[309,9] kṣaṇatatkramayoḥ saṃyamād vivekajaṃ jñānaṃ| kṣaṇaṃ kramaṃ ca svayam eva vistareṇācaṣṭe tatrāpakarṣaparyantaṃ yataḥ param apakarṣo na śakyate kartuṃ tad-dravyaṃ paramāṇuḥ| evaṃ lokabudhivikalpitād ahorātralakṣaṇād apakṛṣyamāṇāt kālād alpīyaḥ parijighṛkṣayā yataḥ param apakraṣṭuṃ na śakyate saḥ apakarṣaparyantaḥ kālaḥ kṣaṇaḥ vastubhūtaḥ||

[309,14] yad vā yāvatā samayena vicalitaḥ paramāṇuḥ pūrvaṃ deśaṃ jahyād uttaram abhisaṃpadyeta sa kālaḥ kṣaṇaḥ| sarvathā 'yu^ asti (sūkṣma)kṣaṇa ity arthaḥ||

[309,16] tasya pravāhavicchedabhāvinaḥ tasya kṣaṇasya pravāhaḥ prabandhaḥ avidhedo nairantaryaṃ tathā bhavituṃ śīlaṃ yasya kṣaṇasya 
pravāhāvicchedabhāvinaḥ kṣaṇasya yat ānantaryaṃ saḥ kṣaṇasya kramaḥ| tayoś ca kṣaṇatatkramayor nāsti vastusamāhāraḥ kṣaṇakramasyāvastutvāt kṣaṇasya ca^ ekatvāt nāsti tayoḥ samāhāraḥ samūhaḥ||

[309,20] nanu cāsati kṣaṇatatkramasamāhāre kathaṃ māsasaṃvatsarādāḥ 
pravarterann iti tadartham āha buddhisamāhārāt buddhiparikalpitāt kṣaṇatatkramayoḥ samāhārādād ityādideśāntarasaṃbandhāvabandhabuddhisamudbhāvitāt muhūrtāhorātrādayaḥ| tataś ca buddhisthita^ eṣa bhedaḥ||

[309,24] yo hi prasannasamānapratyayavāhinasaḥ ekāgrabhūmipratiṣṭhicittasattvaḥ tasya saty api kālānubhave kṣaṇayugasahasrayos tulyatā| na^ evaṃ vyutthitacittasya| tathā svapne api ghaṭikāmātreṇa buddhiparikalpitaṃ yojanasahasram anekaṃ saṃvatsareṇa^ eva [gantavyaṃ] gaddhati| tasmād buddhivaicitryanimrmita^ eva kālasamāhāraḥ||

[310,6] yasya tu kriyāvyatiriktaḥ kālaḥ nityo vibhuḥ tasya cirakṣipratvādy api kriyāmātre paryavasitatvāt pṛthakkālāstitvena liṅgaṃ ghaṭate| niyataparimāṇā hi kriyānavagatapariṇāmāyāḥ kriyāyā lakṣaṇam| yathāgodohanaṃ svapiti| odanapākam adhīte^ iti| sa^ eva ca kālaḥ| na hi nityasya vibhoḥ tasya kūṭasthasyāsambandhatvāt vastvantaraparicchedakatvaṃ prasthādivad upapadyate ||

[310,11] kriyāvān kālaḥ paricchedakaḥ prasthādivad iti cet tasyāpi kriyāvattvāt kriyāvataś ca pariddhedanīyatvād anyena kriyāvatā 
paricchedakena kālena bhavitavyam| tasyāpy anyenety anavathāprasaṅgaḥ| kriyāvattvāc cānityatvam api||

[310,14] atha svavyāpāreṇa^ eva kālaḥ paricchedanīyakaṃ yāyād iti cet anye api svavyāpāreṇa^ eva paricchedyā iti kālapṛthaktvakalpāa nirarthikā| tathā ca kriyā^ evāstu kālaḥ||

[310,17] athāpi sadbhāvamātreṇa kālaḥ paricchedaka^ iti sarveṣām api sadbhāvāviśeṣād evaṃ prasaktam||

[310,19] kriyākālapakṣe apy eṣa vikalpaḥ prāpta^ iti cet na kriyāyāḥ sarvaprasiddhatvāt| paricchedakatvasya ca^ ā godohamās te^ ityādiṣu siddhatvāt kriyā^ eva pricchedikā labhyate nāparaḥ kālaḥ pariddhedaka^ iti| ciraṃ kṣipram iti ca prayatnamandimapāṭavakṛtaḥ pratyayo na vyatiriktakālanibandhana^ ity āha sa khalv ayaṃ kālaḥ saṃvatsarādirūpo vastuśūnyo buddhinirmāṇaḥ buddhyā nirmāpiṣyate||

[310,24] śabdadharmānupātī truṭinimeṣāhorātrādiśabdavikalpānupravartī|  laukikānāṃ vyutthitadarśanānāṃ vastusvarūpa^ ivābhāsate| kṣaṇas tu vasturūpaḥ vāstavikaḥ taddravyānyathātveenānumīyamānaḥ kramāvalambī|  kramaś ca kṣaṇānantaryātmā kṣaṇapravāhāviddhedabuddhinimittaḥ||

[311,10] taṃ kṣaṇaṃ kālavidaḥ kāla^ ity ācakṣate yoginaḥ| na ca dvau kṣaṇau saha bhavataḥ yayoḥ samāhāraḥ kalpyeta| ekasmin dharmiṇi sahajanmanoḥ kṣaṇayor asaṃbhavāt| jāyamāno hi caitra^ ekasmin kṣaṇe jāyate| na tatra dvau kaṇau saha bhavataḥ| kramaś ca na dvayoḥ| kasmāt dvayoḥ kṣaṇayoḥ sahabhuvorāsambhavāt ||

[311,14] pūrvasmād uttarasya bhāvino yad^ ānantaryaṃ kṣaṇasya sa kramaḥ|  yathā piṇḍād uttarabhāvino ghaṭasya yad^ ānantaryaṃ sa kramaḥ| na hi cakrāntarasthasya mṛt-piṇḍasya cakrāntarasthamṛtpiṇḍajanito ghaṭaḥ krame vartate| tasmād vartamāna^ ekaikaḥ kṣaṇaḥ| na pūrvottarāv atītānāgatau staḥ pūrvottarayor dharmisvarūpamātratvāt| dharmī ca vartamānakṣaṇārūḍha^ eva| tasmānnityā nāsti tatsamāhāraḥ kṣaṇatatkramasamāhāraḥ||

[311,19] ye tu bhūtabhāvinaḥ atītabhaviṣyantaḥ kṣaṇāḥ te dharmiṇaḥ pariṇāmāstitayā dharmipariṇāmāpekṣayā vyākhyeyāḥ| tena^ekena kṣaṇena kṛtsno lokaḥ pariṇāmam anubhavati| tatkṣaṇopārūḍhāḥ vartamānakṣaṇopārūḍhāḥ khalv amī sarve dharmāḥ sarvapadārthāḥ||

[311,23] yad^ uktam atītakṣaṇasya nāsti krama^ iti tasyatat prayojanaṃ tatkramakramapratiṣedhaḥ| kṣaṇakramakrame hi sati kṣaṇakramasya vastutvaṃ prasajyeta| vastuno hi^ etayoḥ kṣaṇa[tat]kramayoḥ saṃyamāt tayoḥ sākṣātkaraṇam| tataś ca vivekajaṃ jñānam| kṣaṇatatkramāṅkitā eva hi sarve padārthā iti tān aśeṣān vivicya vijānāti||3.52||

[312,15] tasya vivekajasya jñānasya kīdṛśaṃ jñetam iti tasya viṣayaviśeṣaḥ upakṣipyate jātilakṣaṇadeśair anyatānavaddhedāt tuyayos tataḥ pratipattiḥ| [tulyayor deśalakṣaṇasārūpye ] jātibhedaḥ padārthānām anyatāyāḥ anyatvāvaddhedasya hetuḥ| yathā gaur iyaṃ baḍabeyam iti||

[312,19] yatra jātibhedo nāsty ekā^ eva jātiḥ tatra tulyajātitve lakṣaṇaṃ liṅgam anyatvāvaddhedakaram kālākṣī gauḥ svastimatī gaur iti||

[312,23] yatra na jātibhedo nāpi lakṣaṇabhedas tatra deśabhedo 'nyatvāvacchedahetuḥ| yathā dvayor āmalakayor jātilakṣaṇasārūpye deśabhedo 'nyatvakaraḥ idaṃ pūrvam āmalakam idam uttaram iti||

[312,26] yadā tu pūrvam āmalakam anyavyagrasya samākulitacittasya jñātūr uttaradeśa^ upāvartate pūrvadeśe cottaraṃ tadā tulyadeśatve pūrvam etad uttaram etad iti tulyayoḥ pravibhāgānupapattiḥ| asandigdhena tattvajñānena bhavitavyaṃ jñeyasya vidyamānatvād ekarūpasya^ ity ata^ idam uktaṃ tataḥ 
pratipattir vivekajñānād iti| kṣaṇatatkramasaṃyamāpāditavivekajanmano jñānāt pratipattir iti||

[313,11] tatkatham pūrvāmalakasaha(la)kṣaṇo deśaḥ pūrvāmalakasahacaro deśaḥ uttarāmalakasaha(la)kṣaṇāt uttarāmalakasahacarāt deśāt bhinnaḥ| te ca^ āmalake pūrvottara(la)kṣaṇasahacarite svadeśa(la)kṣaṇānubhavabhinne svadeśacihnabhūte yaḥ kṣaṇaḥ tad anubhavabhinne svadeśa(la)kṣaṇānubhācihnite^ anyadeśa(la)kṣaṇānubhavas tayor anyatve hetur iti||

[313,16] anena dṛṣṭāntena paramāṇor atulyajātilakṣaṇabheda(deśa)sya 
pūrvaparamāṇudeśasaha(la)kṣaṇasākṣātkaraṇāt pūrvaparamāṇudaśena sahabhūtasya(la)kṣaṇasya sākṣātkaraṇāt uttaraparamāṇos tad-deśāpavṛttau 
pūrvadeśagatāv api^ uttarasya tad-deśānubhavabhinnaḥ kṣaṇaḥ| kṣaṇabhedāt tayor īśvarasya yogino 'nya(thā)tvapratyayaḥ bhedāvacchedo bhavati||

[313,21] atrāpare varṇayanti ye^ antyā viśeṣāḥ paramāṇu-gatā nityāḥ te^ eva anyatāpratyayaṃ kurvantīti kiṃ tatkṣaṇatatkramasaṃyamena| yathā ghaṭādigatā viśeṣā^ anyatāpratyayaṃ kurvanti tathā paramāṇu-gatā ye viśeṣāḥ ta eva tadbhāvapratyayaṃ svasadbhāvapratyayaṃ ca kurvīran| āśrayabhedapratyayam api kurvanti||

[313,25] atrocyate deśalakṣaṇabhedo mūrtivyavadhijātibhedaśa cānyatve hetuḥ ukta^ eva sthūleṣu (na tu ) nanu paramāṇuṣu pratikṣaṇapariṇāmitvāt| avasthiteṣu hi viśeṣeṣv antyeṣu syād anyathā 'vadhāraṇam| na cānavaratapariṇāmeṣu ghaṭate||

[314,11] yadā pūrvaḥ paramāṇur uttaraparamāṇurūpeṇa pariṇamate pūrvaparamāṇurūpeṇa cottaraḥ tatrāvasthitāntyaviśeṣābhāvād vivekapratipattir anupapannā| tatrāvaśyaṃ kṣaṇatatkramasaṃyamajanitavivekañānād eva saṃpratipattiḥ| anityāś ca paramāṇava^ iti hy uktam| tasmāt kṣaṇabhedas tu yogibuddhigamya^ eva||

[314,15] tathā cānityatvasya paramāṇunām ācāryāntaramatapratipādanadvāreṇa prasiddhiṃ darśayati^ ata^ uktam mūrtivyavadhijātibhedābhāvāt nāsti mūla(jāti)pṛthaktvam iti vārṣagaṇyaḥ| yathā pratikṣetrajñaṃ pradhānabahutvaṃ nyāyaviruddhaṃ mūrtivyavadhijātibhedābhāvāt pradhānānāṃ pṛthatvam ekapuruṣārthadṛśyavat anupapannam evaṃ paramāṇunām api mūlatve pṛthaktvaṃ na^ upakalpate| tataś ca^ ekaṃ mūlam iti prāptam| na paramāṇubhedo hi nityo^ uktaḥ| paramāṇunām ca mūrtatvāt rūpādimattvāc cānityatvaṃ ghaṭādivad eva^ iti||3.53||

[314,22] tāra_Kaṃ sarvaviṣayaṃ sarvathāviṣayam akramaṃ ceti vivekajaṃ jñānam| kāṣṭhāprāptaṃ jñānaṃ devarṣigandharvādīnām| tārakam iti vivekajam idam ucyate svapratibhottham anaupadeśikam ity arthaḥ| nānyena^ upadeṣṭaṃ śakyaṃ nāpy upadeśena yathāvat grahītuṃ śakyate| yasmāt tat sarvavastuviṣayam| nāsya kiṃcid aiṣayībhūtam||

[315,16] sarvathāviṣayaṃ ca sarvam atītānāgatapratyutpannaṃ sarvaprayāyaiḥ sarvaviśeṣaṇaiḥ sarvathā sarveṇa prakāreṇa jānāti| akramam ekakṣaṇopārūḍham| ekasmin kṣaṇe sarvaṃ sarvathā gṛhṇāti| nāsadādivat krameṇa||

[315,19] tat etad vivekajaṃ jñānaṃ paripūrṇam asya^ evāṃśo yogapradīpaḥ| sa^ eva jñānāṃśo yogaś ca pradīpaś ca| yogasya vā pradīpo yogapradīpaḥ| madhumatīṃ bhūmim upādāya^ ārabhya yāvad asya vivekajasya jñānasya 
parisamāptiḥ tāvat| vivekajajñānaprisamāptiparyantāni jñānāni monojavattvādiyuktāni^ asya^ evāṃśaḥ ||3.54||

[315,23] yagaje jñānāaiśvaryasyā^ eva tadvairāgyānantaraṃ kaivalyam iti 
prāptamata^ āha prāptavivekajñānasya [aprāptavivekajñānasya vā] bāhyadarśananivṛttau kaivalyam| katham iti sattvapuruṣayoḥ śuddhisāmye kaivalyam iti||

[315,27] yadā nirdhūmatarajas tamomalaṃ puruṣānyatāpratyayamātram anyatāmātrapratyayavaibhavayuktaṃ dagdhakleśabījaṃ vinivṛttāvidyāpratyayavāsanārūpaṃ buddhisattvam bhavati tadā puruṣasya śuddhisārūpyam ivāpannam||

[316,11] yady api triguṇaṃ puruṣavilakṣaṇaṃ puruṣaś ca tadvilakṣaṇaḥ tathāpi puruṣānyatāpratyayapariṇāmitvāt śuddhisārūpyam ivāpannam ity ucyate| etasyām avasyāyāṃ kaivalyaṃ bhavatīśvarasyānīśvarasya vā vivekajñānabhāginaḥ itarasya vā| yasmād ubhayor avidyānivṛttir eva kāraṇaṃ tasmāt| na hi dagdhakleśabījasya jñāne kācid apekṣā| yataḥ samyagdarśī sarvaṃ triguṇam iti manyate triguṇaś capratyayo heya^ iti madhumatyādijñāne tasya na kadācid apy apekṣā||

[316,17] yat tu yogajñānam aiśvaryaṃ ca^ uktaṃ tat samyagdarśanārthānukrāntasattvaśuddhipadamārgeṇa prakrāntam ānuṣaṅgikam| paramārthatas tu jñānād adarśanaṃ vinivartate| tasmin vinivṛtte na santy uktakleśāḥ asmitā-ādayaḥ| teṣām avidyākṣetratvāt| kleśābhāvāc ca karmavipākābhāavaḥ kleśamūlaḥ karamāśayaḥ sati mūle tadvipākaḥ iti hi pratipāditam||

[316,22] tasmāt samyagdarśanārtham evedaṃ śāstram na jñānāiśvaryavibhūtiprayojanam | caritādhikārāś ca^ etasyām avasthāyāṃ guṇāḥ| na puruṣasya punar dṛśyatvenātiṣṭhante| tat puruṣasya kaivalyam| tadā puruṣaḥ svarūpamātrajyotiḥ ity amalaḥ kevalībhavatīti| itiśabdaḥ samāptyarthaḥ ||3.55|| iti śrīgovandabhagavatpūjyapādaśiṣyasya 
paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryasya śrī-śaṅkarabhagavataḥ kṛtau 
pātañjalayogasūtravbhāṣyavivaraṇe||vibhūtipādas tṛtīyaḥ||

[317,9] prathame pāde samādhiḥ prādhānyena nirdiṣṭaḥ| dvitīye tatsādhanāni vyākhyātāni| tṛtīye tu samyagdarśanārtha-yogāṅgānuṣṭhānānuṣaṅgajñānāiśvaryānukramaṇaṃ kṛtam| tac ca sarvaṃ duḥkhasya^ iti jaigīṣavyakhyānena pratipāditam| duḥkhahānaṃ ca^ ātyantikaguṇoparama^ ity uktam tathā^ eva tadvairāgyād api doṣabījakṣaye kaivalyam prāptavivekajñānasya- aprāptavivekajñānasya vā sattvapuruṣayoḥ śuddhisāmye kaivalyam ity anantaram uktam| tac ca kaivalyaṃ pratipakṣanirvartanena pratipādanīyam itīdaṃ kaivalyapādānukramaṇaṃ kriyate||

[317,16] tathā^ anekajanmāntaroparjitavāsanoddhedaprakārāś ca kaivalyārtham eva pradarśanīyāḥ| tathā kaivalyopayogisamādhisiddhistutyarthaṃ pūrvatrānuktaṃ siddhyantaraṃ 
prakriyate| talyajātīyānām anyatamanindanenānyatamastutir bhavati| tatra dhyānajamanāśayam karmaśuklākṛṣṇaṃ yoginas trividham itareṣām iti vakṣyamāṇena stutyarthatā^ āviṣkariṣyate||

[317,21] janmauṣadhimantratapassamādhijāḥ siddhayaḥ| dehāntaritā yathā yogādinā dehāntareṣu svargādiṣu janmasiddhiḥ| oṣadhibhir asurabhavaneṣu rasāyanena (318,14) somāmalakādibhakṣaṇena 
pūrvadehānapanayena^ eva| mantraiḥ upajpyamānaiḥ ākāśagamanādi| tapasā saṃkalpasiddhiḥ kāmarūpī yatra kāmagamana^ ity evamādiḥ| samādhijāḥ siddhayaḥ pūrvam eva vyākhyātāḥ||4.1||

[318,17] tatra siddhiḥ kāyakaraṇānām utkarṣaḥ| janmasiddhau tu 
patitapūrvopārjitakāryakaraṇasya śarīrāntaram upādāyiṣyata^ iti nāsti tatra saṃśayaḥ| apatitaśarīrasya tu jātyantarapariṇāme kiṃ tad eva kāryakaraṇam upādānam utānyad apīti tatra kāyendriyāṇām anyajātipariṇatānāṃ jātyantarapariṇāmaḥ prakṛtyāpūrāt||

[318,21] pūrvapariṇāmāpāye samavasthitānām eva pūrvakāryakaraṇānām uttarapariṇāmopajanaḥ apūrvāvayavānupraveśena bhavati| kasmāt alpaparimāṇasyopādānaya mahāparimāṇakāryānupapatteḥ| na hi daśapalāt tapanīyapiṇḍādaśītipalā patrī janiṣyate[318,25]        kāyendriyaprakṛtayaḥ svaṃ svaṃ vikāraṃ kāyam indriyaṃ vā^ anugṛhnanty āpūrṇadharmādinimittam ādigrahaṇād adharmam api nimittam apekṣamāṇāḥ||

[318,17] dharmādinimittam apekṣamāṇāḥ svaṃ svaṃ vikāram anugṛhṇāntīty uktam| tatrakioṃ dharmādinimittaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ prayojanaṃ kim vā neti tad āha nimittam aprayojakaṃ (319,12) prakṛtīnāṃ varaṇabhedas tu tataḥ kṣetrikavat| na hi dharmādinimittaṃ prayojakaṃ prakṛtīnām| kasmāt na hi kāryeṇa dharmādinā nimittena kāraṇaṃ kāryakaraṇapratirūpaṃ pravartyate||

[319,20] kathaṃ tarhi varaṇabhedas tu tataḥ dharmādinimittāt bhavati| kṣetrikavat| yathā kṣetrikaḥ kedārād apāṃ pūrṇāt kedārāntaraṃ 
piplāvayiṣuḥ nāpaḥ pāṇinā^ apakarṣati| āvaraṇaṃ tu tāsāṃ bhinatti| tasmit bhinne svayam evāpaḥ kedārāntaram āplāvayanti||

[319,24] tathā dharmaḥ prakṛtīnām āvaraṇam adharmaṃ bhinatti| dharmam adharmo api bhinatty āvaraṇam| tasmin bhinne svayam eva prakṛtayaḥ svaṃ svaṃ vikāram āplāvayanti pūrayanti||

[319,27] yathā vā sa^ eva kṣetrikas tasmin eva kedāre na prabhavaty audakān bhaumān vā rasān dhānyamūlāny anupraveśāyitum| kiṃ tu mudgatindukagavī śrukaśyāmākādīn tasmāt dhānyamūlāt apakarṣati| apakṛṣṭeṣu teṣu svayam eva rasā dhānyamūlāny anuviśanti||

[320,14] tathā dharmo nivṛttimātre kāraṇam adharmasya| adharmo api dharmasya| śuddhyaśuddhyor atyantavirodhāt| na tu prakṛtipravṛttau dharmo hetur bhavatīti| 320,16        yadā tu yogī bahūn kāyān nirmīte tadā kim ekamanaskās te bhavanti^ athānekamanaskā iti samādhisiddhau kaścid anukto viśeṣa^ ucyate nirmāṇacittānyasmitāmātrāt| so 'haṅkāraṃ cittakāraṇam upādāya nirmāṇacittāni nirmitāni cittāni aprakṛtānīśvaracittāni navāni karoti yogī| cittagrahaṇam udāharaṇamātram| indriyāṇy apy asmitāmātrād eva| tataḥ te sacittāḥ sakaraṇāś ca bhavantiu||

[320,22] cittendriyābhāve ca mṛtadeśīyaḥ kāyo nirarthakaḥ syāt| ekacittatve tu guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ pravṛttibhedaś ca na^ upakalpate| tatra vibhutvād ekakṣetrajñatve api bahukāyārambhitvam upapadyate| guṇāpradhānānekapravṛttibhedārthaṃ tu karaṇabheda^ eṣitavyaḥ||

[320,25] kecit tu kṣetrajñā^ apy anye pratikāyabhedaṃ bhavantīti manvate| kecid atra codaytanti^ akṛtābhyāgamam| anye tu tadanurūpopārjitakarmāṇaḥ kṣetrajñā bhavantīti parihāram āhuḥ||4.4||

[321,14] yady evaṃ bahūnāṃ cittānām ekacittābhiprāyapurassarāpravṛttir iti| tatra^ ucyate pravṛttibhede prayojakaṃ cittam ekam ankeṣām| pravṛttibhede saty api savacittānāṃ prayojakaṃ cittam ekaṃ phalādhikṛtadeśīyaṃ śarīrāntare nirmimīte||

[321,17] tac ca yogibuddhim anuvartate| anyāni ca nirmāṇacittāni bhinnānekapravṛttīni tannāyakacittānuvartīni bhavanti| tataś ca teṣāṃ pravṛttiḥ nivṛttiḥ apekṣā ity evamādipravṛttibhedaḥ upapadyate| rāhaprakṛtipravṛttibhedavat| evam ekacittābhiprāyapuraḥsarā ca pravṛttir avakalpate||4.5||

[321,21] tatra dhyānajamanāśayam| pañcavidhaṃ nirmāṇacittaṃ janmaiṣadhimantratapas-samādhisāmarthyanirmitam| tatra teṣu yad^ eva dhyānajaṃ cittaṃ tad evānāśayaṃ kleśakarmāśayāpavarjitam| tasya^ eva nāsty āśayaḥ rāgādipravṛttiḥ ato na puṇyapāpābhisaṃbandhaḥ kṣīṇakleśatvād yoginaḥ| kleśamūlo hi karamāśayaḥ ity uktam| tadabhāvāc ca karmāśayānupapattiḥ| apareṣāṃ tu janmādisiddhānāṃ karamāśayaḥ akṣīṇakleśatvād vidyate^ eva||4.6||

[322,8] kuta^ etad yogina^ eva na karamāśayaḥ itareṣāṃ tv astīti| yataḥ karmāśuklākṛṣṇaṃ yoginas trividham itareṣām| siddhāsiddhasāmānyaviṣayam idaṃ sūtram||

[322,10] nanu ca pūrvasūtram api sāmānyaviṣayam eva| na| samādhisiddhistutyarthatvāt| sāmānyaviṣayatve tu samādhisiddhistutir anupapannā syāt| siddhiparisaṃkhyānena tu viśeṣaviṣayatve tulyajātīyajanmādisiddhicatuṣṭayasāvadyatvasaṃkīrtanāt samādhisiddheḥ stutir upapadyate||

[322,14] kiṃ ca janmādisiddhisādhanānāṃ kaivalyārthatāpratiṣedhaś cārthād iti viśiṣṭaviṣayam eva pūrvasūtram| idaṃ tu sāmānyaviṣayam eva 
pūrvasūtrahetutvenopādānāt||

[322,16] catuṣpadī khalu^ ayaṃ karmajātiḥ| kṛṣṇā durātmanāṃ śāstramārgātivartiduṣṭacetasām aśuddhatarā| yā tu bahissādhanasādhyā jāyāputrapaśuyājakādiniṣpādyā sā śuklakṛṣṇā| kā 'sau yatra [para]pīḍā 'nugrahadvāreṇa karmāśayapracayaḥ sā kleśabāhulakaniṣpādanyatvāt kṛṣṇā ca śuklā ca||

[322,20] nanu jyotiṣṭomādikarmaśāstrasamadhigatatvān na kṛṣṇaṃ śuklam eva tu tat| atrocyate na kāmyair anaikāntikatvāt| tāny eva cārthānarthobhayakāraṇāni| atha tat-phalasya svargāder abhimatatvād arthatvam eva^ iti cen na paradārābhimarśanasyāpi vāñchitatvād arthatvaprasaṅgāt||

[322,24] atha paradārābhisaraṇādāv abhyugradoṣasampātāt pratiṣiddhatvāc ca rāgāginiyogapravṛtteś cāsamānam iti cen na tatrāpi samānatvāt| kāmātmatāniṣedhād aniṣṭayogaś ca| janmamaraṇayor abhyugradoṣabhūtayor avaśyaṃ bhāvitvād iṣṭāniṣṭadvayahetutvam eva| sa cāniṣṭayogaḥ 
parapīḍāder ity avasīyate| tat-pratiṣedhopapatteḥ||

[322,28] avaśyaṃ hi janmamaraṇadvayena vinā svargādiphalam upabhoktuṃ na śakyam| tac ced asti tasya duḥkharūpatvādāyātaṃ tarhi jyotiṣṭomādīnām ubhayātmakatvam| atha karmāntaram eva pratiṣiddhaṃ janmamaraṇārambhakāraṇam iti cen na tadbhāvabhāvitvāt| tasmin hi sati janmamaraṇabhāvāt||

[323,1] atha tu bhavatpakṣe nityam eva kurvīta na janmamaraṇe 
pravarteyātām| kurvata^ eva te bhavato nākurvataḥ| tasmānnityā nimittatvam avagamyate| kiṃ ca yena janmamaraṇam ārapsyate svargādau tat karma yadi janmāntaragataṃ tatrānirmokṣaprasaṅgaḥ| kāmyapratiṣiddhākriyāyām api 
pūrvajanmakṛtaphalasya- avaśyaprāpaṇīyatvāt||

[323,5] atha prāktanajanmakarmakṣayārthāni nityānīti cet tan na niyamānupapatteḥ| aniṣṭaphalānyevārabdhakāryāṇi pūrvakarmāṇi na tv iṣṭaphalānīti [na] nityantuṃ śakyate| adṛṣṭaphaleṣu hi satsu nityāny eva kurvato api mokṣānupapattir janmārambhitvaṃ ca||

[323,9] tata^ iṣṭānām apy ubhayahetutvaṃ siddham| bāhyasādhanānāṃ karmaṇāṃ 
pīḍānugraharūpatvāt| na vai khalv api nityena pūrvakṛtakarmaṇām iṣṭānāṃ jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ nivṛttir upakalpate| virodhābhāvāt| avirodhaś cobhaeṣāṃ śuddhirūpatvāt| tantrasiddheś ca||

[323,13] athāpi brūyāde[dane]kajanmārabdhāni karmāṇi janmai[kai]kamārabhante nānārabdhakāryam avaśiṣyata^ iti| tathā api 
pūrvajnmakarmaṇaidānīntanajanmaphaladānena kṣayaṅgatatvād adhunātanajanmaniṣpāditānām abhinavānām eva kāmyānāṃ svargaphalajanmamaraṇaduḥkhārambhitvam iti śuklakṛṣṇarūpāpattiḥ||

[323,17] tataś ca janmamaraṇaduḥkhasya svargābhidhānāyāḥ prīteś ca darśanāt tatkāraṇasya dvirūpatvam anumāsyāmahe| tathā ca siddhe 
pratiṣiddhabhūtahiṃsācaraṇaṃ kratvartham apy agnīṣomīyādau pravartmānaṃ ṛṣṇarūpam ity anumāsyāmahe kratvarthāpi satī hiṃsā^ aniṣṭabhūtā^ eva tārthatāṃ pratipadyate||

[323,21] yad^ uktam abiśeṣeṇa yacchāstraṃ śirovad iti cottaram iti tatra śirovad ity evaṃ jātīyakadṛṣṭāntāsiddhatvam| aviśeṣeṇa yac-chāstram iti tu homasvarūpasya virodhād bādhyabādhakatvam upapadyate| hiṃsāyās tu virodhābhāvād ubhayor hetutvaṃ bhojanasyeva sukhaśarīrasthitidvayahetutvam| yathā ca tīrthasnānasya hlādādṛṣṭārthatvaṃ tathā hiṃsāapy aniṣṭaphalatvam aparityajantī mahāphalaṃ kratuṃ na^ eva nābhinirvartayati||

[323,26] atha bāhyasādhanasādhyam api nityaṃ kevalaṃ kāmasaṃyogābhāvāc chuklam eva^ iti ced evam ucyate na doṣavattvena nityakāmyānām aviśeṣāt| yathā kāminaḥ kāmyānīti kāmasaṃyogena kāmyānāṃ doṣavattvam evaṃ kāmādihetu bhūtāvidyādoṣavattvam aviśiṣṭam iti nityānām apy ubhayātmakatvam eva| avidyāvattvaṃ ca nityeṣu pravṛttasya 
prāyaścittanimittotpatter avaśyaṃ bhāvitvāt||

[324,11] api cāviduṣa^ eva sarvaṃ karma| viduṣaḥ karmānutpatteḥ| vidvān hi puruṣaḥ svarūpāvasthānavyatirekeṇa phalaṃ nābhimanyate| svarūpamātrāvasthānaṃ ca samastavyāpāravirāmeṇa pravartate| na ca^ evaṃ viduṣaḥ kriyā 'uṣṭhānaṃ ghaṭate| na hi sthalapathenābhipratiṣṭhāsmāno jalamārgābhiprasthānasādhanaṃ nāvādikam anutiṣṭhati||

[324,15] nanu (na ca) nityakāmyayor evaṃ satyaviśeṣaḥ syāt| atrocyate kāmitaphala(tanmātraṃ ni)karmamātranivṛttiprayojanatvān nityānām adoṣaḥ| yo hi kāmyādvinivṛttas tasya bahulaṃ karmaṇāṃ kāmarahitena śuddhena kartrā kṛtatvād atiśuddhaphalatvaṃ śuklatvabahutvāc ca saṃskārārthatvam| api ca kāmanivṛttir eva mahat phalam||

[324,19] kiṃ ca nityaṃ kurvataḥ kim arthaṃ karomi kiṃ vā^ asya phalaṃ ke vāsyānuṣṭhāne doṣāḥ iti samyagdarśanarūpaḥ parāmarśo jāyata^ iti nityasyāsti viśeṣaḥ| śuklajṛṣṇātmakatvaṃ tv aviśiṣṭam ubhayeṣām api karmaṇām| bāhyasādhanasādhanīyatvena parapīḍopapatteḥ| [iti] tasmād ucyate||

[324,23] śuklā karmajātiḥ tapas-svādhyāyānavatām| sā hi kevale manasyāyattatvād abahissādhanā bāhyasādhānānapekṣā na parān pīḍayitvā bhavati||

[324,26] aśuklākṛṣṇā saṃnyāsināṃ kṣīṇakleśānāṃ caram adehānām| eṣām ārabdhaikaśarīrāṇām aśuklā saṃnyāsāt akṛṣṇā cānupāānāt akaraṇāt| nāpi śuklakṛṣṇavimiśrā bāhyasaṃnyāsānupādānābhyāṃ hetubhyām| tathā ca^ uktam 325,8     aniṣṭam iṣṭaṃ miśraṃ ca trividhaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalam|| bhavaty[tyatyāgināṃ]yogināṃ pretya na tu saṃnyāsināṃ kvacit||iti||

[325,10] itareṣāṃ tu bhūtānāṃ janmauṣadhimantratapassiddhānām anyeṣāṃ ca trividhaṃ śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ miśraṃ ca pūrvam iva yathā dvitīye pāde kleśamūlaḥ ityādau||4.7||

[325,12] kaivalyasya prastutatvāt tasya ca prativandhahetavaḥ kleśāḥ karmāṇi vāsānāś ca[vyākhyātavyāḥ]  kleśānāṃ tu vyāpāraḥ pratipakṣaḥ nivṛttiś ca teṣāṃ vistareṇa vyākhyātāḥ| evaṃ karmaṇām api vāsanānāṃ tulyavyāpārāvasthānapratipakṣanivṛttayo nirvarṇanīyā ity ata^ uttarāṇi sūtrāṇy ārabhyante yatastannivṛtau kaivalyam upapadyate nānyathā| tatas tadvipākānuguṇānām evābhivyaktir vāsanānām||

[325,17] tataḥ tasmāt trividhāt uktalakṣaṇāt kṛṣṇaśuklavimiśrarūpāt karmaṇaḥ pravipacyamānād hetu-bhūtāt tadvipakānuguṇānāṃ tasya^ eva karmaṇo vipākaḥ phalaṃ tadanuguṇānāṃ tadanurūpāṇāṃ tādṛśīnām eva vāsanānām abhivyaktiḥ||

[325,20] etad uktaṃ bhavati devatiryaṅmanuṣyavipākahetuṣu karmasu madhye yajjātīyasya karmaṇ_O yādṛśo vipākaḥ tasya vipākasyānuguṇā vāsanāḥ tat karmavipākam anuśarete^ anūtpadyante tāsām evābhivyaktiḥ| tatsadṛśena karmavipākena saṃskṛtatvāt| yathā mātṛ-sadṛśīṃ pravilokya vatso 'nudhāvati/ nānyāsām atadanuguṇānām abhivyaktiḥ||

[325,25] na hi daivaṃ karma vipacyamānaṃ phalābhimukhībhāvam āpadyamānaṃ nārakatiryaṅ-manuṣavāsanā-abhivyaktinimittaṃ [saṃ]bhavati| nimittanaimittakayoḥ pratiniyatānuguṇatvāt| kiṃ tu daivānuguṇā evāsyābhivyajyante| nārakatiryaṅmānuṣeṣv evaṃ samānaścarcaḥ samādhiḥ||

[326,8] nārake karmaṇi vipacyamāne tatkarmānuguṇā vāsanāḥ prādurbhavanti| tathā tairyagyonakarmaṇi vipacyamāne tadvipākānurūpā vāsanā vyajyante| tathā mānuṣe api samāno nyāyaḥ| na hi svavipākasadṛśīr anupādāya vāsanās tasya tasya karmaṇo vipākaḥ saṃbhavati| nāpi vāsanānām ātmaniṣpādakavipākārambhikarmatulyarūpāṇi karmāntarāṇi vipākābhimukhāny antareṇābhivyaktiḥ| evaṃ karmaṇāṃ vāsanānāṃ ca 
parasparahetuhetamadbhāvo 'nācibhūtaḥ||4.8||

[326,14] tatra vāsanānām anekajātikāladeśavyavadhānena davīyasīnām ānantaryaṃ vipacyamānena karmaṇā saha nāsti| yathā davīyasāṃ mitrasvajanādinām| yathā^ eva ca nedīyasī vāsanā paṭīyasī na tathā daviyasī paṭīyamānam āsīdati| tasmānnityā nāsti davīyasīnām ānantaryaṃ vāsanānām||

[326,18] tadabhāve cāyaṃ doṣaḥ prāpnoti yathākalpaśātāntaritaḥ vṛṣadaṃśavipākaḥ bhaviṣyataḥ vṛṣadaṃśavipākābhisyānantarātīto daivavipākaḥ| tatra nedīyasī daivavāsanā viruddhatvāt vṛṣadaṃśavipākābhivyaktihetutāṃ na pratipadyate| kalpaśatavyavahitānām api vṛṣadaṃśavāasnānāṃ viprakarṣād anupādāne vṛṣadaṃśavipākahetoḥ karmaṇo vipāko nāvakalpeta| tataś ca karmaṇāṃ vāsanānāṃ ca^ ānarthakyaṃ 
prasajyeteti||

[326,23] atrocyate jātideśakālavyavahitānām apy ānantaryaṃ smṛtisaṃskārayor ekarūpatvāt| vṛṣadaṃśavipākādayaḥ svakarmavyañjakāñjanāḥ vyañjakena svena karmaṇā vyajyamānāḥ yadi jātiśatena vā duradeśatayā vā kalpasamasreṇa vā vyavahitāḥ||

[327,9] nanu ca jātivyavadhānena^ eva kālaviprakarṣa^ uktaḥ kiṃ kālagrahaṇena^ ucyate bhūyaṃsi janmāni divase ekasminn api kṣudrajantuprabhṛtīnāṃ keṣāṃcid bhavanti| na tatrātītakālaviprakarṣa iti kālagrahaṇam||

[327,12] teṣu vṛṣadaṃśavipākādayaḥ pūrvam api svavyañjakanirvartitāḥ santaḥ punaś ca svavyañjakāñjanodayāḥ drāg ity eva kṣipram eva vṛṣadaṃśavipākābhisaṃskṛtā vāsanā upādāyābhibyajyante| kasmāt evam yato vyavahitānām apyāsāṃ sadṛśaṃ karmābhivyañjakaṃ nimittībhūtam iti||

[327,16] na hi balīvardavipākābhivyaktikāraṇaṃ karma vṛṣadaṃśavāsanābhivyaktinimittaṃ bhavati| visadṛśatvāt| anurūpaṃ hi nimittam abhivyañjakam| āmlādikṣaṇadarśane jihvā sravati| tasmāt ānantaryam eva vāsanānām| yatas tāsāṃ vyañjakaṃ tulyam| tathā ca sati na ca^ āsanānāṃ nedīyas tā davīyas tā vā samānacittāśrayatvāt| kutaś cāyam apy aparo hetur ānantaryasya smṛtisaṃskārayor ekarūpatvād iti||

[327,23] pūrvaṃ tulyajātīyaṃ karmābhivyañjakaṃ vāsanānantaryahetur uktaḥ| idānīm ayam anya^ ucyate yathā^ anubhavās tathā saṃskārāḥ| yādṛśāḥ pratyayāḥ tathā vāsanāḥ| te ca saṃskārā vāsanārūpāḥ| yathāvasanāḥ smṛtaya^ iti| (328,12) tathā ca^ uktam grāhyoparaktaḥ pratyayo grāhyagrahaṇobhayākāranirbhāsaḥ ityādi| jātideśakālavyavahitebhyaḥ saṃskārebhyaḥ smṛtayaḥ| smṛtibhyaś ca punaḥ saṃskāro^ ity evam ete smṛtisaṃskārāḥ karmāśayavṛttilābhavaśābeśād abhivyajyante| karmāśayasyāpi vṛttilābho deśādinimittāpekṣayeti pratipāditam||

[328,16] ataś ca vyavahitānām api nimittanaimittikabhāvānucchedād ānantaryam eva| yādṛśaḥ saṃskāras tādṛśī smṛtiḥ| pratiniyatavyañjakatvāc ca tayor na viprakarṣaḥ kāraṇam| yataḥ kalpaśatavyavahitād api saṃskārād eva tulyajātīyā smṛtir iti^ ānantaryam eva siddham||4.9||

[328,20] odānīṃ vāsanānām anāditvena vipākanirvartakatvam abhidarśayati saṃskārasya cānāditvam ekaikasya cāsaṅkhyayonigamanaṃ vairāgyapratipattyarthaṃ pratipādayati tāsām anāditvaṃ cāśiṣo nityatvāt| tāsāṃ vāsanānām āśiṣo nityatvād anāditvam| yeyamātmāśīḥ maraṇaṃ mā nu bhūt iti^ evam āśīr vā bhūyasam iti sarvasya dṛśyate| sāna svābhāvikī svarasapravṛttā api nirūḍhā api||

[329,8] kasmāt jātamātrasyānannubhūtajananamaraṇadharmasya dveṣānusmṛtinimitto duḥkhānusmṛtihetuko vā maraṇatrāsaḥ kathaṃ bhavvet na hi kathañcana duḥkhasmaraṇādinimittopādānaṃ svābhāvikasya^ upapadyate| na ca svābhāvikaṃ vastu nimittam upādatte| na hi jvalanasyoṣṇatvam svābhāvikaṃ sadātmīyakārye nimittam īkṣeta||

[329,15] na cānanubhūte viṣaye smṛtir iṣṭe 'niṣṭe vā kasyacid upaploṣyate| anubhūtapriyānanusmaraṇe na ca tadviṣyavāñdhā| tathā anubhūtāpriyānanusmaraṇe na tadviṣayo dveṣastrāsaś ca loke paridṛśyate| tathā ca yo yo jātas tasya tasyeha janmany ananubhūtajanmamaraṇasya janmānantaram evātmāśīr abhidṛśyate| tasmāt tadāśīr ityatvāt 
pūrvajanmamaraṇānubhavo 'numīyate| tataḥ pūrvas tato api pūrva^ iti smṛtisaṃskārāṇāṃ tatkāraṇānāṃ ca janmamaraṇānubhavānām anāditvam anumimīmahe||

[329,21] tathā^ eva^ ekaikasya prāṇabhṛto 'nekayonigamanasiddhiḥ| evam etā vāsanā duravahānā^ avihānīyāḥ| kuta^ evam yasmād anādivāsanānuviddhaṃ cittam idaṃ nimittavaśāt karmanimittavaśāt kāścid eva ca^ āsanāḥ 
pravipacyamānakarmatulyajātīyakriyā-vipākābhi(nna)niṣpāditāḥ 
pratilabhya puruṣasya bhogāyopāvartate yāvad avasitādhikāram iti||

[329,26] tad etac cittaṃ ghaṭaprāsādapradīpakalpaṃ saṃkocavikāsi hastimaśakādiśarīraparimāṇākaraṃ yathā dīpo ghaṭaprāsādādiṣu| tad evam apare pratipannaḥ| tatra ca yuktam āha tathā ceti| tathā ca saṃkocavikāsitve cāntrābhāvaḥ (330,10) maraṇād uttarakālam janmanaś ca 
prāg etasminantarāle bhavatīty antarābhāvaḥ saṃsaraṇaṃ sañcaraṇṃ yena sañcarati tad api śarīram ātivāhikaṃ cittasyāvibhutve yuktam iti bruvanti| vṛttir evāsya vibhunaś cittasya saṅkocavikāsinīty ācāryāḥ| ācāryagrahaṇam upapattimattvāt pūjārtham| cittasya sthitir gatiś ca 
pariṇāmitvād vibhutve 'pīndriyāṇāṃ ca^ upapadyate yogināṃ ca vikaranabhāvaḥ sarvajñātvaṃ vibhutve ghaṭate||

[330,15] na copabhogaśūnyasyātivāhikaśarīrasya kalpanāyām asti pramāṇam| caśaviṣāṇavad anupalabhyamānatāt| na ha vai saśarīrasya sataḥ 
priyāpriyayor apahatir astīti śruteḥ| tathā^ atha yo haitānanantānupāste iti śrutiḥ karaṇavyāpitvaṃ khyāpayati||

[330,19] tac ca saṃkocavikāsitvaṃ vṛtteḥ dharmādinimittāpekṣam| nimittaṃ ca dvidhā bāhyam ādhyātmikam ca śarīrādisādhanāpekṣaṃ bāhyaṃ stutyabhivādanādi karma| kiṃ punar ādhyātmikam āha kevalacittamātrādhīnaṃ śraddhādi||

[330,22] tathā ca^ uktam ye vaite maitryādayo dhyāyināṃ vihārāḥ vittavyāpārāḥ veṣṭāni te bāhyasādhananiranugrahāḥ bāhyasādhananirapekṣāḥ kevalacittamātranirvṛttāḥ prakṛṣṭaṃ dharmam abhinirvartayanti||

[330,25] tayoḥ bāhyādhyātmikayor nimittayoḥ mānasam ādhyātmikaṃ maitryādicittābhiniṣpannaṃ nimittaṃ balīyaḥ| katham iti jñānavairāgye kenātiśyyete na kenāpīty arthaḥ| yatas tṛtīyapādānukrāntavibhūtijātaṃ jñānavairāgyasādhyam| daṇḍakāraṇyaṃ (331,11) cittabalavyatirekeṇa kaḥ kartuṃ śaknoti| kaś ca pibet samudram agastyavat iti| ato mānasabalam eva balam| na śārīram| tasmāt mānasaṃ 
paramaṃ vadantīti śruteḥ tathā ca bhagavān ābhāṣe śreyān dravyamayādyajñāj jñāna-yajñaḥ parantapa| sarvaṃ karmākhilaṃ pārtha jñāne 
parisamāpyate|| (_Bh_ṅ 4.33) iti||4.10||

[331,16] kathaṃ punas tāsāṃ vāsanānāṃ nivṛttiḥ| anādirūḍhatvāt na nivṛttir āsāṃ ghaṭām āḍhaikate| na ca tannivṛttim antareṇa kaivalyopapattir ity ata ucyate hetuphalakāśrayālambanaih saṃgṛhītatvād eṣām abhāve tadabhāvaḥ| hetuś ca phalaṃ ca^ āśrayaś ca^ ālambanaṃ ca hetuphalāśrayālambanāni| taiḥ saṃgṛhītatvād upagrathitatvād eṣāṃ hetvādīnām abhāve tad abhāve vāsanānām abhāvaḥ| tasmād upapadyate vāsanānāṃ nivṛttiḥ||

[331,21] ko 'sau hetuḥ ity āha dharmas tāvad avidhāvinamitacetasām| tato dharmāt sukham| sukhānuśayī rāgaḥ| evam avidyāta^ eva niṣdiddhācaranat adharmaḥ| tato duḥkham| duḥkhānuśayī dveṣaḥ| tataḥ sukhopārjane duḥkhaparihāre ca pūrvavad eva prayatnaḥ| tena prayatnena vācā kāyena parispandamānaḥ param anugṛhṇāty upahanti ca| punaś ca tataḥ parānugrahaparapīḍanābhyāṃ dharmādharmau| tābhyāṃ sukhaduḥkhe| tābhyāṃ ca rāgadveṣau pūrvavad eva| tataḥ prayatnaḥ||

[332,9] ity evam anavarataprārabdhaparibhramaṇaṃ pravṛttim idaṃ ṣaḍaraṃ saṃsāracakram| asya ca pratikṣaṇam āvartamānasyāvidyā netrī rathacakasya turaṅgayuvatir iva||

[332,11] sā mūlaṃ sarvakleśānām iti yad^ etad avidhānetṛkaṃ ṣaḍaraṃ cakram eṣa hetuḥ vāsanānām| etac cakrāvṛttikṛtā hi tāḥ| avidyāyās tv abhāve viśīrṇanābhibandhanam iva cakraṃ dharmādihetu-cakram api viśīryate| tad viśareṇa ca cakrāvṛttikṛtajanmanāṃ vāsanānām akāryakṣamatvam||

[332,15] phalaṃ tu yam artham āśritya yasya pratytpannatā tat tasya 
phalam| tad api vāsanānāṃ saṃgrāhakam| tadabhāve vāsanābhivyaktyabhāvāt| sukhādiphalasya vāsanādhāyitvāt| na hy apūrvopajanaḥ| nātyantam asad utpadyate||

[332,18] manaḥ sādhikāraṃ kartavyatāvat na puruṣārthaśūnyam| kṛtapuruṣārthasvarūpajñānaṃ vinivṛttāvidyākam iti sādhikāram āśrayo vāsanānām| tad āśritya hi hetucakraparibhramaṇam| na hy avasitādhikāre karaṇīyapuruṣārthaśūnye manasi nirāśrāyā vāsanāḥ sthātum utsahante||

[332,22] yad^ abhimukhībhūtaṃ vastu yāṃ vāsanāṃ vyankti tasyās tadālambanam| evaṃ hetuphalāśrayālambanir etaiḥ saṃgṛhītāḥ sarvā vāsāaḥ| teṣāṃ hetvādīnām abhāve tatsaṃśrāyāṇām api vāsanānām abhāvaḥ chatrābhāve chāyānivṛttivat||

[332,25] eṣāṃ cābhāvopāyo dvitīyapāde vyākhyātaḥ| tasmāt na ca^ āsanā samyagdarśane sati kaivalyaprativandhinī| bhavaty evāvidyānvṛttau kaivalyam iti||4.11||

[333,10] nanu ca^ eṣām abhāve tad abhāva^ ity uktam| tathā ca asiddhāntahānniḥ| katham evaṃ hi siddhāntaḥ nāsty asataḥ sambhavaḥ utpattiḥ| na cāsti sato vināśa^ iti| atha punaḥ santi vāsanās tadā dravyatvena (vastusatyaḥ) [sambhavantyaḥ] kathaṃ nivartiṣyante| na^ eva nirvartiṣyanta^ ity arthaḥ| tatra katham uktam eṣām abhāve tadabhāva^ iti| kathaṃ vā kaivalyam anuvṛttāsu hetukṛtajamasu vāsanāsv ity ata^ uttaraṃ 
paṭhati^ atītānāgataṃ svarūpato 'sty adhvabhrdād dharmāṇām||

[333,16] evaṃ vaitat| atha vā siddhāntapakṣeṇa^ eva neyaṃ bhāṣyam| katham eṣām abhāve tadabhāvaḥ iti hetvādīnāṃ vāsanānāṃ ca nātyantābhāva^ ucyate| kasmāt nāsty asataḥ sambhavo na cāsti sato vināceti dravyatvena vastutayā sambhavantyaḥ kathaṃ nirvartiṣyante vāsanāḥ| śabdavikalpitatve vāsanā nātyantaṃ nivariṣyante| santy evātītasvarūpeṇety arthaḥ||

[333,21] bhaviṣyadvyaktikaṃ bhaviṣyati vyaktiḥ yasya tat bhaviṣyadvyaktikam| yathā piṇḍāvasthāyāṃ ghaṭarūpam| 
pravṛttetikartavyatāsādhanaṃ tat anāgatam| bhūtavyaktikam atītaṃ bhūtā atikrāntā vyaktir upalabdhiviṣayatvāpattir yasya| yathā prabhagno ghaṭaḥ tad atītam ity ucyate| avyaṅgyam abhaviṣyat yasya vyañjane sādhanam upādeyam| anatītavyaktikam abhūtam| kiṃ tarhi vyāpāripārūḍhaṃ tat vartamānam| yathā ghaṭaḥ svakriyāyāḥ||

[334,5] trayaṃ ca^ etat atītānāgatavartamānalakṣaṇakaṃ vartamāne dharmiṇivartamānaṃ dharmarūpaṃ vastu samastalokaviniścitarūpasyāvyabhicāriṇo jñānasya pramāṇabhūtasya jñeyam iti saṅgirāmahe| kasmāt niścitajñānotpattiviṣayatvāt||

[334,8] yadi ca^ etad vastu svarūpato nābhaviṣyat nedaṃ nirviṣayaṃ jñānam udapatsyata| yathā śaśaviṣāṇādi niścitajñānotpattikāraṇaṃ na bhavati| na ca tatheha niścitavastujñānotpattiviṣayatvāt||

[334,13] atha vā nāvastuviṣayam atīte bhaviṣyati ca ghaṭādau jñānam| śabdavikalpitatve sati niścitarūpeṇa^ utpadyamānatvāt vartamānaghaṭādiviṣayajñānavat| avyabhicārāc ca| liṅgānumeyatvāc ca dhūmenāgnyādijñānavat| liṅgadarśanānantarotpatter vā||

[334,17] yad vā^ atītam anāgataṃ ca vastu sat svarūpeṇa liṅgadarśanānantarabuddhiviṣayatvāt prasiddhānumeyavat| tasmād atītānāgataṃ svarūpato 'sti||

[334,19] kiṃ ca bhogabhāgīyasya bhogahitasya yāgādeḥ apavargabhāgīyasya vā apavargahitasya samādhyādeḥ karmaṇaḥ phalam utpatsu yasya nāsti tasya vādinaḥ tat nirupākhyaṃ prāpnoti śaśaviṣāṇavat| na hi śaśaviṣāṇaṃ bhaviṣyatīty upākhyāyate| na tatheha| kiṃ tarhi yāhena svargo bhaviṣyati samādhyādinā mokṣo bhaviṣyatīti ca kuśalaiḥ atikṭhor matibhir upākhyāyate||

[334,24] tasmāt nirupākhyatvāt tad uddeśena phaloddeśena| nirupākhyatvāt tena phalena nimittena kuśalānujñānam idaṃ kuru bhaviṣyati cānenādaḥ iti svayam apratipadyamānāḥ kuśalāḥ katham anujāni\īyuḥ upadiśeyur vā| kiṃ ca dṛṣṭamātrapratipattau kuśalākucalāviśeṣaś ca prāpnoti| kuśalānāṃ vā mithyāpratipattiḥ akuśalāḥ samīcīnadarśanāḥ iti||

[335,8] kiṃ cābhāvajñānāt kuśalā^ api phalāya nābhisamīheran| abhāvāviśeṣāc ca kāraṇaniyamo nākalpiṣyata| (na) sarvato vā sarvam udapatsyata| nāpi kāraṇam upādāsyata| upādāne vā tailārthī saikatam apy upādāsyata kiṃ kilair anekāyāsaniṣpādyaiḥ| atyantāsad apūrvaṃ cedam ajaniṣyata| na ca^ etad dṛṣṭam||

[335,12] tasmāt tadbhokabhāgīyam apavargabhāgīyaṃ vā karma nimittam kāraṇaṃ vidyamānasya^ eva tasya phalasya vartamānīkaraṇe samarthaṃ nāpūrvopajane| siddhaṃ hi svena^ ātmanā nimittaṃ sthālī kṣīradharmātañjanādi dadhyādikasya naimittakasya karkaśatarabhāvādyabhivyaktidvāreṇa viśeṣānugrahaṃ kurute nāpūrvam eva dadhi^ utpādayati tatheha bhogabhāgīyādikarma||

[335,17] kiṃ ca dharmī cānekadharmasvabhāvo 'nekadharmātmā| yathā kanakapiṇḍaḥ śāntoditāvyapadeśyarucakakuṇḍalādidharmātmakaḥ| tasya dharmiṇaḥ adhvabhedenātītādilakṣaṇabhedena dharmāḥ pratyavasthitāḥ||

[335,20] yathā vartamānaṃ vastu vyaktiviśeṣāpannaṃ kuṇḍalādivyaktiprāptaṃ dravyataḥ kāryarūpataḥ asti vyāpārādhirūḍhatayā na^ evam atītam anāgataṃ ca| kiṃ tarhi svena^ eva vyaṅyena svarūpeṇabhaviṣyatā^ anāatam asti| svena cānubhūtavyaktikena atikrāntopalabdhikenātītam asti||

[336,6] vartamānasya^ eva tv adhvanaḥ svarūpato vyaktir iti na sā vyaktiḥ bhavaty atītānāgatayor adhvanoḥ| ekasya cādhvanaḥ samaye kāle dvāv adhvānau dharmisamanvāgātau virodhāt na bhavata^ eva^ iti| tathā ca^ ukto 'dhvasaṃkaraparihāraḥ||

[336,9] yasya punar atītam anāgataṃ ca lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpeṇa nāsti tasya dharmāṇām adhvabhedo na prāpnoti| katham na hi tasya ghaṭo bhaviṣyann atīto vā vidyate| na hi kapālāni piṇḍo vāghaṭaḥ| ataś ca vartamāna^ eva ghaṭo 'tīto 'nāgata^ iti cocyeta piṇḍakapālayor aghaṭatvāt mṛdi ca ghaṭasyābhāvād bhaviṣyati ghaṭo 'tīto ghaṭa iti ca piṇḍakapāle nirucyeyātām| tathā ca vivakṣato gaur apy aśva syād aśvo api gaur iti||

[336,16] athocyeta ghaṭasya pragabhāvapradhvaṃsābhāvāv atītānāgatatvenābhidhīyete^ iti| tato apy eṣa vastuvācinā śabdena ghaṭo 'tīto 'nāgata^ iti cābhāvasyābhidhānaṃ yujyate sa[tya]tvavādinām||

[336,20] nāpi niyama^ upapadyate| ghaṭena^ eva vyapadiśyate ghaṭo 'tīto ghaṭo bhaviṣyatīti ca| na punaḥ kulālādibhir apadiśyate kulālādayo 'tītā bhaviṣyanti vā^ iti||

[336,23] atha ghaṭasya^ eva tatrānpalabhyamānatvād iti cet na hastiśārtulādīnām api tatrānupalambhe vyapadeśaprasaṅgāt| athopalabdhapūrvasya^ eva vyapadeśa^ iti cet na ghaṭakriyādiṣv api 
prasaṅgāt||

[336,26] atha ghaṭasya tena kriyādyabhāva^ iti cet nābhāvasya sambandhaviśeṣānupapatteḥ| na hi samavāyalakṣaṇaḥ prāptilakṣaṇo vā sambandho bhāvābhāvayor avakalpate| yenāsau ghaṭasyābhāvo na punaḥ kriyā-ādīnām| anyatarasambandhe cābhāvasya vastutvaprasaṅgaḥ| atha vastv eva^ iti cet siddhas tarhi bhaviṣyann atītaś ca padārthaḥ||

[337,7] atha^ upacārāt siddhakapālāvasthā^ avayavā bhaviṣyati ghaṭo bhūto ghaṭa^ iti ca^ āpadiśyeran sambandhāt tadyathā^ annaṃ prāṇā ity atrāpi yathā kulālacakradaṇḍasūtrādiṣu ghaṭi nāsti tathā piṇḍakapāleṣv apīti tadabhāvāviśeṣe ca kapāleṣu ghaṭo 'tīta^ iti vyapadeśo na kulālādiṣv iti niyamānupapattiḥ||

[337,11] na cāpi vidyamānānāṃ sādhanānāṃ ghaṭādiniṣpādinām apy avidyamānair utpādyair ghaṭādibhiḥ sambandha^ upapadyate| na cāsati sambandhe ghaṭotpādanābhiprāyeṇa kārakapravṛttiḥ||

[337,14] athāyutasiddhaḥ samavāyalakṣaṇaḥ sambandho janyānāṃ kāraṇānāṃ ca na pratiṣidhyata^ iti cet na yutasiddhāyutasiddhatvasya samānakālabhāvināṃ vastūnāṃ darśanāt| nābhāvābhāvayoḥ kvacid api yutasiddhatvam ayutasiddhatvaṃ vā dṛśyate ||

[337,17] tasmāj janyajanakānām api sambandhābhyupagamād vidyamānatvam eva^ iti^ utpatteprāg ūrdhvaṃ ca vidyate kāryam vastvantarasambandhāt 
prasiddhaṅhaṭādivat| upalapsyamānatvād upalabdhatvāc ca apavarakasthaghaṭādivat| uddiśya kartrā prāpyatvāt deśāntaravat| tathā kartṛvyāpāraprāpyatvāc ceti||

[337,21] kiṃ ca na^ oaukikā^ atītānāgataśabdābhyāṃ gaganakamalinīvad avidyamānam arthaṃ pratiyanti pratipādayanti canirmokṣaḥ| tasmād adītam anāgataṃ ca svarūpato vidyata^ eva| nābhūtvā bhāvas trayāṇām adhvanām| kiṃ tarhi vidyamānānām evābhivyaktiḥ||

[337,24] tasmād vyaktirūpeṇa vāsanānām atyantavinivṛttiḥ| guṇarūpeṇa tu satīnām api kāryāsamarthatvān na kaivalyābhāvadoṣaḥ| nāpi siddhāntavirodhaḥ||4.12||

[337,26] atītānāgatavartamānadharmāḥ kena punā rūpeṇa santīti tatpratipādanārtham āha te vyaktasūkṣmā guṇātmānaḥ| te khalv amī tryadhvānaḥ atītānāgatavartamānādhvatrayopārūḍhāḥ dharmāḥ vartamānāḥ vartamānalakṣaṇopārūḍhāḥ vyaktātmānaḥ (338,10) likapratyayaviṣayībhūtasvabhāvāḥ| atītānāgatāḥ sūkṣmātmānaḥ lokapratyayaviṣayabhāvam aprāptāḥ ṣaḍaviśeṣasvarūpāḥ asmitādisvabhāvāḥ| tataś ca vyaktātmānaḥ ṣoḍaśavikārāḥ kāryakāraṇalakṣaṇāḥ||

[337,13] sarvam idam ādhyātmikaṃ kāryakāraṇarūpaṃ vyaktātmakaṃ yac ca sūkṣmam aviśeṣalakṣaṇam asmitādi yac canirmokṣaḥ liṅgamātraṃ sūkṣmatvāviśeṣāt sarvam idaṃ jagat guṇānāṃ sattvarajastamasāṃ prakhyāpravṛttisthitiśīlānāṃ sanniveśaviśeṣamātram iti paramārthato guṇātmānaḥ guṇasvabhāvāḥ||

[338,17] tathā ca śāstrānuśāsanam guṇānāṃ paramaṃ rūpaṃ svaṃ 
paramārtharūpam anpāyopajanātmakam ekaikasya| tac ca na dṛṣṭipatham ṛcchati| na dṛṣṭigocarībhavati| yat tu dṛṣṭapathaṃ prāptaṃ guṇāparimardena viṣam abhāvakṛtasanniveśalakṣaṇaṃ tanmāyeva na tu māyā^ evāsāratvāt tu māyāvad iti| sutucchakam atyalpam ekadeśamātrapariṇāmatvāt sāmyāvasthānāṃ guṇānām||4.13||

[338,22] yadā sarve guṇāḥ pṛthivyādayaḥ tadā katham ekaḥ śabdaḥ śaṅkhādijanitaḥ| ekam indriyaṃ śrotrādikam ity ekabuddhyālambanatvaṃ bhavet| na khalu bhavitavyam | triguṇatvena hi kāraṇena traya^ iti pratyaya syāt| evam indriyṇāṃ ca pratyekaṃ tritvam| tathā ca niyamo na prāpnoti| na hi grāhyatvam ekasya^ eva śabdasya grāhakatvaṃ ca^ ekasya śrotrasya^ iti niyam upakalpate| tathā sparśādiṣu tvagindriyādiṣu ca yojyam| niyamaś ca dṛśyate| kāraṇaṃ tatra vacanīyam ity āha pariṇāmaikatvād vastutattvam||

[339,9] prakhyāpravṛttisthitiśīlānāṃ guṇānāṃ grahaṇātmakānāṃ sattvaprādhānyāt boddhātmanām asmitāsaṃsthānānāṃ karaṇabhāvenaikaḥ 
pariṇāmaḥ śrotrendriyam| yathā viruddhānāṃ tailavartitejasāṃ pradīpaḥ| tathā tvagādīndriyeṣu pruṣāṛthetikaraṇīye prayuktānām ekaikam indriyaṃ 
pariṇāma^ iti yojyam||

[339,13] bhūtāntareṣv api yojyam| śabdasparśarūparasānāṃ snehasmānajātīyānām [ekaḥ pariṇāmaḥ rasatanmātrāvayavaḥ jalaparamāṇuḥ| teṣāṃ paramāṇunām ekaḥ pariṇāmo jalam]||

[339,16] śabdasparśarūpāṇām uṣṇatvasamānajātīyānām ekaḥ pariṇāmo rūpatnamātrāvayavaḥ tejaḥ paramāṇuḥ| teṣāṃ paramāṇunām ekaḥ pariṇāmo bahniḥ salilendhano dāruśakaladīpanaś ca||

[339,19] tathā śabdasparśayoḥ praṇāmitvasamānajātīyayoḥ sparśatanmātrāvayavaḥ vāyuparamāṇur ekaḥ pariṇāmaḥ| teṣāṃ paramāṇunām ekaḥ 
pariṇamāḥ vāyuḥ sapta marud-gaṇāś ca||

[339,21] tathā śabdatanmātrāvayavānām anāvṛttatvasamānajātīyānām ekaḥ 
pariṇāma^ ākāśaḥ| ity evam uktena mārgeṇa snehauṣṇyapraṇāmitvādyupādāya sāmānyam ekavikārārambhaḥ samāḍheyaḥ yojyaḥ| tasmād bahūnām apy ekapariṇāmitvād ekaviṣayatvaṃ grāhyagrahaṇaniyamaś ca sarvam upapadyate||

[340,6] nanu ca nāsty evāsāv ekapratyayaviṣayaḥ śabdādiḥ| etat tu sarvaṃ vijñānaparijṛmbhitam eva vijñānabhāvabhāvitvāt| kathaṃ tadbhāvabhāvitvam ity āha nāsti artho vijñānavisahacaraḥ pṛthag-vijñānād upalabdhaḥ pṛthag-bhāvī vā| vijñānam arthavisahacaraṃ pṛthaktvena^ upalabdhaṃ pṛthag-bhāvi vā^ asti| tasmād artho vijñānavyatirekeṇa nāstīti 
pratijānīmahe||

[340,11] pramāṇam apy atra bhavati vijñānavyatirekeṇa nāsti grāhyaṃ vastu vijñānavyatirekeṇānupalabhyamānatvāt vijñānasvarūpavat| bāhyārthaśūnyāni jāgradvijñānāni vijñānatvāt svapnasamayavijñānavat| vijñānaparikalpitā vā jāgradviṣayāḥ viṣayatvāṭ svapnaviṣayavat| vijñānaṃ vā viṣayā-vyatireki vijñānatvāt svapnavijñānavad iti||

[340,16] yady evaṃ syāt kiṃ naś-chinnaṃ syāt puruṣasya dṛśyaviyogāt kaivalyaṃ prastutaṃ na siddhaṃ syāt||

[340,18] yathā upalabhyamānasya stambhāder abhāvaḥ evam upalabhyamānatvād vijñānasyāpy abhāvaḥ| tad abhāve ca liṅgābhāvād upalabdhur apy abhāvaḥ prāpnuyād iti kasya kaivalyam kena dṛśyena viyogaḥ syāt kena samprayoge vā bandhaḥ pratijñāyeta bandhābhāve cābhyudayaniḥśreyasārthapravṛttiphalatā na prasajyeta||

[340,22] kiṃ cānyat vijñānavyatirekeṇārtho nāstīti bruvatā vijñānam artha^ iti ca^ ekaḥ padārtho 'abhyupagato bhavati yathā^ agnir anala iti| evaṃ ca sati vijñānaṃ grāhyaṃ grāhakam arthaḥ grāhyo grāhakaś ceti nāmamātre vipratipattiḥ na punar ekasya grāhyagrāhakabhāvaḥ 
pratyākhyāyate| tathā ca sati puruṣasyāpi grāhyagrāhakabhāvaḥ prāptaḥ| tathā api vipramokṣānavak.lptiḥ ity evamādayo bhūyāṃso doṣāḥ 
prāpnuvanti||

[340,27] tasmāt amuyā diśā^ anena nyāyābhāsabalena ye vastu-svarūpam apahṇuvate jñānaparikalpanāmātraṃ vastu svapnaviṣayopamam na tu 
paramārthato 'astīti (341,8) te svayaṃ tathā^ ity abhyupagamya svamāhātmyena pratupasthitaṃ vastu katham apramāṇātmakena vikalpajñānabalena vastu svayam upagṛhyābhyupagamya tad evābhyupagataṃ apalapantaḥ idam ity abhyupetya^ idaṃ nāstīty apahnuvānāḥ śeaddheyavacanāḥ syuḥ| na hi svānubhavapratyākhyāyinaḥ śraddhānīyavacanavistārā bhavitum arhanti||

[341,12] tatra vijñānavyatirekeṇārtho nāstīti pratyakṣavirodhinī 
pratijñā| saṃvedanīkārakaṃ vijñānaṃ pratīyāmaḥ| pṛthubudhnādyākāram arthaṃ 
pratipadyemahi| grāhakaṃ ca vijñānam avabhāsakaṃ avabhāsanīyaś cārthaḥ| svalakṣaṇabhedenārthajñānayor upalabhyamānatvān nāvyatirekagandho api| tataś ca vyatirekeṇānupalabhyamānatvād ity asiddho hetus tava||

[341,17] na ca jñānasya^ eva pṛthubudhnādyākāratā| jñānasya^ ekasyāvabhāsyāvabhāsakatvānupapatteḥ pradīpasyeva kevalasya|| bhinnayoḥ 
pradīpayoḥ prakāśyaprakāśakatvānupalmbhād ekasya ca svātmani 
prāptyasambhavāt evaṃ jñānasya||

[341,20] svapnajñānam api^ upalabdhārthaviṣayatvāt na nirālaṃbanam| 
pratijñāyāḥ pratyakṣavirodho lokavirodhaś ca| sarvaṃ jñānaṃ sālambanaṃ jñānatvāt prativādiduṣṭavākyajñānavat svavākyaguṇajñānavac ca| tataś ca jñānatvādīnāṃ nirālaṃ banatvahetūnām evamādibhir anaikāntikatā||4.14||

[341,24] kutaś ca^ etad anyāyyaṃ vastu nāstīti vastu-sāmye cittabhedāt tayor viviktaḥ panthāḥ| bahucittālambanībhūtaṃ anekacittaviṣayībhūtaṃ ekaṃ vastu (342,12) putrādikaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ samānam| tat khalu naikacittatantraṃ bhavati| ekacittatantratvābhāvād anekacittaparikalpitam api na bhavati||
