[342,14] kin tu svarūpapratiṣṭhaṃ cittanirapekṣaṃ svatantratayā vyavasthitam| katham vastu-sāmye vastunaḥ putrāder ekatve cittabhedāt ekasmin putrādiviṣaye viṣayiṇāṃ jñānānām anekatvāt||

[342,17] kathaṃ cittabheda^ ity āha dharmāpekṣaṃ cittasya vastu-sāmye api yathā putrata^ eva mātāpitroḥ sukhajñānaṃ bhavati| tata^ eva putrādeḥ adharmāpekṣaṃ ahitānāṃ duḥkhajñānaṃ upajaniṣyate||

[342,21] yadi kasyacic cittena parikalpitam abhaviṣyat (na) anyacittaparikalpitenānyeṣāṃ cittam upāraṃkṣyata| na^ etad yuktaṃ atyantāsambaddhānām adarśanāt| yadi cānyaparikalpitenāpy anyacittoparāgo 'bhaviṣyat tadā sarve api sarvaṃ samjñāsyanta| na cedaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ| tasmāt na cittaparikalpitaṃ vastv iti vastujñānayoḥ grāhyagrahaṇabhedād bhinnayor viviktaḥ panthāḥ| nānayoḥ saṅkaragandho apy asti||

[343,8] sukhajñānālambanībhūtaṃ purtādivastu na tajjñānaparikalpitam tadviruddhānekacittoparañjakatvāt vādiprativādipratijñāguṇadoṣatadābhāsavat| ca^ ādiprativādiprabhṛtīnām api vijñānamātratve sādhyasādhanādivyavahāravilopaḥ syāt||

[343,11] atha svavijñānavyatirekeṇa teṣāṃ jñānarūpāṇām eva pṛthaktvam iti cet astu| tad eva vastu jñānāntaraviṣayatvād arthāmtaram siddham| saṃjñāmātre vipratipattiḥ| viṣayiviṣayabhedas tu na sādhyaḥ| tasmānnityā nānayoḥ saṃkaragandho 'pīti||

[343,14] atha vastuni svarūpapratiṣṭhe api sukhādibhinnapratyayālambanatā na samīcīnarūpeti cet atrocyate vastu 
punas triguṇaṃ calaṃ guṇavṛttam iti dharmādinimittaṃ cāpekṣya vittaiḥ sukhaduḥkhādibhedarūpeṇābhisaṃbadhyate pariṇāmitvād anekaśaktitvāc ca||

[343,17] dharmādinimittānurūpasya cotpadyamānasya pratyayasya tena tena nimittena^ ātmanā hetur bhavati| sattvātmanā dharmādinimittāpekṣaṃ 
putrādivastu sukhahetur bhavati| rajas-ātmanā tv adharmanimittāpekṣaṃ duḥkhahetur bhavati| tamas-ātmanā tv avidyāpekṣaṃ tad eva vastu mohahetur bhavati| sattāmātrātmanā samyagdarśanāpekṣaṃ tad eva mādhyasthyajñānahetur bhavati||

[343,22] kecid āhuḥ eṣām eva bauddhānām anyatame bāhyam artham abhyupagacchanta^ āhuḥ jñānasahabhūr evārthaḥ yathā vijñānaṃ pratikṣaṇaṃ dharmiśūnyaṃ utpadyate vinaśyati ca tathā shaiva jñānenārtho vijñāyate vinaśyati ca bhogyatvāt sukhādivat| tadyathā sukhaduḥkhādirūpaṃ bhogyaṃ vijñānasahotpattivināśam asādhāraṇam evaṃ ghaṭādir artho api vijñānasamānasamayajanmavināśa^ eva bhogyatvād iti||

[343,27] te api tathā^ eva vijñānavādivat sādhāraṇatvaṃ anekasukhādicittālambanatvaṃ bādhamānāḥ pūrvottarakṣaṇeṣu svakīyavijñānotpatteḥ paurastyeṣu kṣaṇeṣu pāścātyeṣu ca vastu nāstīti vastusvarūpam evāpahnuvate||

[344,6] tasmāt na hi vastunaḥ svarūpam vijñānasahabhāvi tad evedam iti 
pratyabhijñānāt| nācarajanmapralayaṃ tad evedam iti pratyabhijñāyate yathā vijñānam| na hi tad evādo me vijñānam iti bhavati| vastu punas tad evedam iti pratyabhijñāyate| arthajñānayor evekasamayabhavitve apy eka[pariśeṣaḥ]viśeṣaḥ syāt||

[344,10] nāpi sādṛśyāt pratyabhijñānam| grāhyagrāhakayor yugapadutpattivināśavattvāt| vyavasthite hi vastvantaradṛśye anyatatsadṛśavastudarśanena sādṛśyapratyayaḥ| evaṃ tatsadṛśaṃ vā drakṣyāmīty uttarābhilāṣaḥ| na hi tad aharniścitamaraṇasyāparedyur arthadarśanākāṃkṣā jāyate| nāpi matsacṛśa^ īkṣiṣyata^ ity abhilaṣati| na ca^ evaṃ dṛṣṭam| sarmo hi svārtham īhate| bhinnasantatiṣv adarśanāt||

[344,15] tasmād idānīṃtano 'rthaḥ nedānīntanajñānasahabhūr eva^ iti grāhyatvāt atītārthavat| atīto vā nātītajñānasahanāśajanmā grāhyatvāt idānīntanārthavat| bhūtabhaviṣyajjñānagrāhyā^ arthā^ adhunā api santi grāhyatvāt adhunātanapratyayagrāhyavat| idānīntanam api vastu 
pūrvottarakṣaṇeṣu tathā^ eva ||

[344,19] atha sukhaduḥkhajñānādibhir anekāntikam iti cet na atītānāgataṃ svarūpato 'stīti sādhitvāt| evaṃ ca sati sukhādīnāṃ ca svarūpeṇa sadbhāvāt sukhādivad iti sādhyasamāno dṛṣṭāntaḥ||

[344,22] atha vartamānarūpaviśeṣeṇātītānāgatalakṣaṇayor arthasya sukhādivad abhāva^ iti cet siddhasādhyatā| na hi kumārabhāva^ eva vṛddhatvaṃ vidyate ||4.15||

[344,24] itaś ca cittatantratā vastuno nāvakalpate| ekacittatantraṃ vā vastu bhaved anekacittatantraṃ vā ekacittatantratve brūmaḥ na ca^ ekacittatantraṃ vastu tadapramāṇakaṃ tadā kiṃ syāt na tāvad ekacittatantraṃ ekacittatantraṃ ced vastu syāt yadi cedam ucyate taccitte yaccittatantraṃ vastu tasmiṃś citte vyagre parāsakte (345,6) niruddhe vā vinaṣṭe vā tena pramāṇacittenāparāmṛṣṭaṃ cānyasya cāviṣayībhūtaṃ ekacittatantratayā^ eva tadā tat vastu^ apramāṇakam agṛhītasvabhāvaṃ pramāṇacittanirodhād eva tadānīṃ kiṃ tat syāt| na hi kiṃ cid api syāt| cittapramāṇakaṃ tat tadabhāve na tena bhavitavyam||

[345,10] nanu ca yathā tat-pramāṇacittaṃ nirudhyate tathā^ eva tad api kṣaṇikatvān niruddham eva| kiṃ tatrābhidhānīyaṃ syāt| ucyate na vyavahāravilopān niyamānupapatteś ca| agnir ayaṃ māsprākṣīḥ imaṃ gṛhāṇa adaḥ pradehīti vyavahāro vilupyeta| ekacittasahakṣayatvād vastunaḥ||

[345,14] tatsantatijamatyantasadṛśaṃ vyavahārapratipattaye bhaved iti cet atrāpi tatsantānād eva tatsamānenānyenānubhavanīyaṃ parapramāṇasya ca tadviṣayabhūtam iti ko niyacchet| na hi niyamahetur asti||

[345,17] api ca^ ekacittatantratvād vyavahāravelāyām anyatara(na)redaṃpratyayaviṣayālambano nānyata(na)rpratyayo yujyate| na hi khalv api bahucitatantraṃ vastu| tataś ca kṛhāṇedaṃ prayucchāda^ iti kathaṃ vyavaharemahi||

[345,20] nāpy evaṃ darśanaśālinaḥ śiṣyācāryopadeśapradānādibhir anyonyaṃ vyavahareran| tasmānnityā na ca^ ekacittatantraṃ vastu| 
pramāṇacittavināśe tadvyagrabhāve vā^ anavadhṛtasvabhāvatvād vastunaḥ||

[345,23] saṃbandhyamānaṃ vā vastu punaś cittena kuta^ utpadyeta| na kutaś cid utpattir upapadyate tatsantānād iti hi pratyavocāma||

[345,25] kiṃ cāsya vastunaḥ anupasthitāḥ ekasya pratyayasyāviṣayībhūtā [ye] bhāgās te cāsya na syuḥ| eko hi vastu-bhāgo viṣayībhūtaś cittatantro nānyaḥ iti| nāsti pṛṣṭham iti^ udaram api^ abhimukhībhūtaṃ na syād eva||

[346,11] athāpi kākatālīyanyāyena bhūyāṃsi cittāni yugapad utpadyeran tadviṣayaṃ ca vastu tatsamānakālam unmajjatīti tad etena^ eva 
pṛṣṭhodarābhāvandarśanena pratyākhyāyi| nāpi kākatālīyavṛttāntenānādisamayādhīno vyavahārasāgara^ uttīryate| sarve ca vijñānadarśanaślādhiṣu doṣā ihāpi samānā eva||

[346,15] tasmāt āha svatantro 'rthaḥ sarvapuruṣasādhāraṇaḥ yasminn avasthite samastalokavyavahāra^ upakalpiṣyate| svatantrāṇi ca cittāni 
pratipuruṣaṃ pravartante| tayoś cārthacittayoḥ smbandhād upalabdhiḥ 
puruṣasya bhogaḥ| tasmād bhoktā puruṣaḥ cittaṃ karaṇam artho viṣayaḥ karmabhūta^ ity eteṣāṃ bhoktṛ-karaṇakarmaṇāṃ viviktaḥ panthāḥ||4.16||

[346,20] kathaṃ cittatadviṣayayoḥ sambandhāt puruṣasya bhoga^ ity āha yad vā yasya cittavyatirekeṇa vastu nāsti cittatantram eva vā vastu tasya jñātājñātaviṣayatvam anupapannam ity āha taduparāga^apekṣatvāc cittasya vastu jñātājñātam| tena viṣayeṇa cittasya^ uparāgaḥ taṃ prati^ apekṣā yasya [tat] cittaṃ taduparāgāpekṣam tasya bhāvaḥ taduparāgāpekṣatvaṃ tasmāt taduparāgāpekṣatvāc cittasya vastu jñātājñātam||

[347,7] yena vastunā cittam uparajyate tat jñātaṃ yena ca na^ uparajyate tat ajñātam| sa coparāgaś cittapariṇāmāpekṣaḥ| indriyamārgeṇa cittaṃ bāhyākāreṇa pariṇāmamānaṃ hi sambadhyate||

[347,10] kiṃ punaś cittapariṇāmanimittam ity āhāyaskāntamaṇikalpā ayaskāntasamānaḥ viṣayāḥ ayassadharmakam ekaṃ cittam ayassardhamakatvaṃ ca viṣayasannidhāne vikriyata^ iti| viṣayāṇām apy ayaskkāntamaṇikalpatvaṃ sannidhimātreṇa cittavikriyāpādanasāmarthyāt| ato viṣayā^ ātmākāreṇa 
pariṇitaṃ cittam abhisaṃbadhyoparañjayanti||

[347,15] yena ca viṣayeṇoparaktaṃ cittaṃ sa viṣayo jñātaḥ| tato 'nyaḥ ('ṇye) punaḥ anuparañjako (kā) viṣayaḥ (yā) ajñātaḥ (tāḥ)| tasmāt vastuno jñātājñātasvarūpa(viṣaya)tvāt pariṇāmi cittam| atha punaś cittatantra eva viṣayaś cittam eva vā viṣayaḥ tasya vādinaḥ sarvaviṣayajñatvam aviṣayajñatvaṃ vā bhavet| ajñātasya cittasyābhāvāt| na cedaṃ dṛśyate| kiṃ tarhi jñātājñātaviṣayatvam eva cittasya pratipadyāmahe| tac ca^ etat sthirāvasthānānāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ prāptyabhāvād anyathā ca saṃbandhānavakalpanāc cittapariṇāmāpekṣam eva^ iti pariṇāmitvam||4.17||

[347,22] yasya tu svāmino bhoktuḥ tad eva jñātājñātaviṣayaṃ cittaṃ vṛttirūpeṇa viṣayaḥ na hi niruddhavṛttinaś cetaso viṣayatvam iti tasya 
puruṣasya sadā jñātāś citavṛttayaḥ[tat-prabhoḥ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvāt]| tāsāṃ pratyakṣatvāt sadā jñātaviṣayatvam aprasādhyam||

[347,25] yad vā pratyakṣagamyaś cittavṛttayaḥ upalabdhatvena śabdaliṅgān apekṣaṃ smaryamāṇatvāt pratyakṣopalabdhaghaṭavat| niścitarūpatvāc ca tathā^ eva| saṃśayāviṣayatvāc ca| yadi hi sadaivāpratyakṣāś cittavṛttayaḥ tā bāhyaviṣayavat saṃśayitā^ api kadācit kāścana bhaveyuḥ| na hi kāścana kadācanasaṃśayamārgasaṃpātinyo dṛṣṭāḥ||

[348,6] api ca bhavati hi kadācijjñātaḥ sann artho jñāta^ iti [rpratīyamānaḥ| cittavṛttipratyakṣāgrāhyatve [tat] na^ upapadyate| na ca 
phaladvāreṇānumātuṃ tatra śakyate| phalasyānavadhāraṇāt||

[348,9] tasmāt saṃśayasya cāviṣayatvāt pṛthak ca viṣayāc cittavṛttīnāṃ svarūpamātreṇa^ eva svaryamāṇatvāt sarvadā sarvāḥ pratyakṣāś cittavṛttayaḥ| tathā pratipattur avabhāsakatvāt karaṇatvāt abhivyañjakatvāc ca pradīpādivat| na tu cakṣurādibhir anaikāntikatvam| tatrāpi pratyakṣagrāhyatvāt| yathā pradīpādyālokaviśiṣṭā ghaṭādayo gṛhyante tathā śrotrādisvālokaviśiṣṭā eva śabdādayo gṛhyante||

[348,15] na ca vivicyāgrahaṇād apratyakṣatvam iti śakyaṃ vaktum| yathā 
pradīpayoḥ parasparavyatibhinnā raśmayaḥ na cāsyāyaṃ raśmir asya cāyam iti| vivicyate na ca tāvatā teṣām apratyakṣatvam [evaṃ] ihāpi cakṣur-ādīnāṃ tulyajātīyabāhyālokādivyatiṣaṅgād vivicyāgrahaṇam| na ca tāvan-mātreṇa teṣām apratyakṣatvam| yo hi 
pratyakṣāvasīyamānacittavṛttyapalāpaṃ karoti nūnam asāv ātmānam apy apaloped iti sarvopalabdheś cittavṛttyadhīnatvāt rūpopalabdher ivāloka(pratyāyakaṃ tattvam) pratyayādhīnatvam| tasmād āha sadā jñātāś cittavṛttayaḥ puruṣasya^ iti||

[348,22] yac ca^ etat sadā jñātaviṣayatvam tat-prabhoḥ 
puruṣasyāpariṇamitvāt| nityopalabdhṛ-svarūpaprayuktaṃ hi tad iti| yadi cittavat prabhur api puruṣaḥ pariṇameta tatas tadviṣayāś cittavṛttayaḥ cittasya^ iva śabdādiviṣayāḥ jñātā^ ajñātā^ api syuḥ||

[348,27] yat tu jñātājñātaṃ śabdādiviṣayajātaṃ tat pariṇāminaś cittasya pariṇāmāpekṣaṃ draṣṭavyam| tathaivendriyāṇām api 
pariṇāmāpekṣam eva jñātājñātaviṣayatvaṃ dṛśyate| na ca tathā cittavṛttīnām anyatamāsām api jñātatvam ajñātatvaṃ ca| nityatas tajjñātā eva cittavṛttayaḥ| tasmād yad vṛttirūpena sadā jñātatvaṃ manasaḥ tat 
prabhoḥ puruṣasyāpariṇāmitvam anumāpayati||

[349,10] apariṇāmī puruṣa^ iti pratijñā| sadā jñātaviṣayatvād iti hetuḥ| vaidharmyeṇa cittam indriyāṇi ca dṛṣṭāntaḥ| upalabdhuś ca viṣayiṇaḥ 
prakṛtatvāt sadā jñātaviṣayatvapratiṣedhāya muktātmavad iti dṛṣṭānto na^ upapadyate| muktasyāviṣayitvāt||4.18||

[349,14] syād āśaṅkā cittam eva svābhāsaṃ viṣayābhāsaṃ ca bhaviṣyatīti| grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ ceti| svasaṃviditākārā eva hi vṛttayaḥ| yathā pradīpaḥ 
prakāśayati ghaṭādīnātmānaṃ ca| kiṃ puruṣeṇa cittavṛttyupalambhinā cittavyatiriktena kalpitena^ iti||

[349,18] atrocyate na tat svābhāsaṃ dṛśyatvāt| yathetarāṇīndriyāṇi śrotra^ādīni viṣayāś ca śabda^ādayaḥ| na svābhāsānīndriyāṇi| nāpi svābhāsāḥ śabda^ādayaḥ| dṛśyatvāt| tathā manoapi pratyetavyam| na svābhāsaṃ cittaṃ dṛśyatvād iti pratipattavyam||

[349,22] nanu cāgnivad eva svābhāsatvaṃ viṣayābhāsatvaṃ ca bhavaut yathā^ eva ghaṭādayaḥ prakāśyāḥ svarūpopalambhanārtham āolkāntaram apekṣante na tathā pradīpaḥ| yathā pradīpaḥ svābhāso viṣayābhāsaś ca| tathā mano apy avabhāsakatvād evaṃ bhavatu kim ātmanā na hi 
pradīpapratipattaye pradīpāntaram ādadate laukikāḥ||

[349,26] yadi ca^ upādīyeran anavasthā^ eva syāt| evaṃ vijñānasyāpi vyatiriktena grāhyatve tasya cānyena grahaṇaṃ tasya cānyenety evaṃ 
prasajyeta| kiṃ ca vijñānasyāvabhāsātmakatvāt grāhakasyāpy avabhāsarūparvāt na parasparopakāryopakārakabhāvaḥ| tadyathā pradīpayor dvayoḥ| tasmāt kalpito api vijñānasya grahītānarthaka iti bhavatu jñānaṃ svābhāsaṃ parābhāsaṃ ceti ||

[350,5] atrocyate tad etad dṛṣṭāntāpaśadāvaṣṭambhena sakalalokaprasiddhamārgopārohiṇā^ ātmano nirākaraṇam aśakyam| kasmāt na cāgnisya dṛṣṭāntaḥ| na hy agnir ātmasvarūpam^ aprakāśaṃ prakāśayati yadi cāgnir aprakāśaḥ san ātmanā^ eva ghaṭādivat prakāśayiṣyata tata upāpatsyata dṛṣṭāntaḥ| na tv evam| na hi kadācid agnir aprakāśaḥ||

[350,10] prakāśaś cāyam| sa ca prakāśyaprakāśakasaṃyohe dṛṣṭaḥ| na ca svarūpamātre 'sti saṃyogaḥ| dvayor hi saṃyogaḥ| nāsati saṃyoge svarūpāvabhāsakatvam||

[350,13] yad^ apy ucyate ghaṭādivat prakāśāntaraṃ nāgnir apekṣata^ iti| tat prakāśyarūpābhāvāt| na tu tasya ghaṭādivat prakāśyaṃ rūpam asti| na tāvatā vyatiriktāgrāhyatvam| cakṣuṣā tulyavyatiriktena gṛhyata^ eva||

[350,16] prakāśyasya haṭāder avatamasatir askārāya prakāśa^ upādāyiṣyate| na tv aprakāśyarūpatvāt| agner ālokāntarānākāṅkṣaṇaṃ tu bhavataivābhihitaṃ dvayoḥ pradīpayor nāsti parasparopakāra^ iti| yatra nāma bhede api dvayoḥ 
pradīpayor nāsti aprakāśyarūpatvād anyonyopakāraḥ kim utāsati bhede 'tra 
prakāśāntaram eva nāpekṣate^ iti||

[350,20] na tu vyatiriktagrāhyatvaṃ vyabhicarati pradīpayor dṛśyatvād ghaṭādivat| etena vyatiriktagrāhyatvaṃ jñānasya dṛśyatvāt 
pradīpādivat siddhaṃ bhavati| pradīpādivac ca jñānasya 
prakāśasvarūpatvāt karaṇāntarānapekṣāpi siddhā| tathā ca anavasthādoṣagandho na vidyate||

[350,24] yad^ apy ucyate jñānasyāvabhāsātmakatvāt tad-grāhakasyāpy avabhāsarūpatvāt na parasparopakāryopakārakabhāvaḥ pradīpayor iva dvayor iti| nāsau doṣaḥ| cakṣuḥpradīpavad ātmajñānayor jātyantaratvāt||

[350,26] nanu cāhaṃpratyayena cittaṃ pratyayi gṛhyata^ iti viṣayaviṣayitvam ekasya^ eva| na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| triguṇatvāc cittasya| yat tatra tamaḥpradhānaṃ cittaṃ tad anyayā (351,9) sattvātmikayā vṛttyā samālambyate hasteneva śarīraṃ kaṇḍūyamānam| tatrāpy upalabdhā viṣayī bhinna^ eva puruṣaḥ| etena jātyantaragrāhyagrāhakatvena niravayavatvena 
puruṣaś ca svayam eva grāhyaḥ grāhakaś cety etat pratyavocāma||

[351,12] kiṃ ca svābhāsaṃ cittam iti^ ucyamāne cāgrāhyam eva cittaṃ syād iti| evaṃ śabda^arthaḥ| svābhāsam iti hy ukte^ anyena nāvabāsyate na gṛhyata^ ity arthaḥ| na tu svayam ātmānaṃ gṛhṇātīty ucyate| evamādiśabdaprayogeṣu śabdasya viparītārthatvam||

[351,16] tadyathā svātmapratiṣṭham ākāśam apratiṣṭham iti śabda^arthaḥ| tathā svastho devadatta^ iti ṇīroga^ ityarthaḥ na tu svātmanisthita^ iti| tatra dṛśyatvād iti hetor asiddhim ācakṣāṇasya pratyakṣavirodho lokavirodho vyavahāravilopaś cety āha svabuddhipracārapratisaṃvedanāt sattvānāṃ prāṇināṃ pravṛttir dṛśyate||

[351,20] kruddho 'haṃ bhogī 'ham amutra me rāgo 'mutra me krodha^ iti svāṃ svāṃ buddhim evaṃ carantīṃ pratisaṃvedya dṛṣṭvā krodhalobhādiparihārāya vivekavatāṃ pravṛttur upalabhyate| tathā prasannaṃ me cittaṃ kalupitaṃ mūḍham iti ca| tat etat svabuddher agrahaṇe na yuktam iti| na hi niśamya parispandinīṃ buddhir adṛyeti yuktaṃ vaktum||4.19||

[351,25] abhyupagamya vādo 'yam yady api svābhāsaṃ viṣayābhāsaṃ ca mano bhavatu| tatra dūṣaṇam eyasamaye cobhayānavadhāraṇam| viṣayaparicchedakatve nānākṣanatvād ātmaparicchedo na yujyate| atha punar ātmānam avadhārayati na tadā viṣayābhimukhaṃ svātmapariddhedakatve nānākṣaṇatvāt||

[352,11] athobhayaparicchittir ekaieveti cen na niravayavatvāt 
paricchitteḥ| na hi vinavayavāyāś cittavṛtteḥ pradīpavad anekābvabhāsakatvaṃ yuktam| avayavabhedāt tu pradīpasyānekaprakāśakatvaṃ saṃbhavati| na tu jñānasya sāvayavatvaṃ parair abhyupeyate||

[352,14] kiṃ ca jñānam ekam ekasmin kṣaṇe^ ubhayam avadhārayatīti cābhyupagame kriyākārakabhedaprasaṅgaḥ| yad^ ubhayāvadhāraṇaṃ sā kriyā| yad avadhārayati tat kārakam| na ca^ ekasmin kṣaṇe svaparobhayāvadhāraṇaṃ kṣaṇavādino yuktam| kasmāt uktaṃ hi kṣaṇkatvasiddhye yad bhāvnam utpattiḥ sā^ eva kriyā tad eva ca kārakam ity abhupagamaḥ| kriyākārakabhede ca ksanābhaṅgabhaṅgaḥ syāt| kārakeṇa hi kriyā-kṣaṇam avasthānīyam||4.20||

[352,20] syānmatiḥ kasyāpi svarasanniruddhaṃ cittaṃ cittāntareṇa gṛhyate^ iti bhāvena vinaṣṭaṃ bhaṅgurataraṃ cittaṃ cittāntareṇa samanantareṇa saṃjāyamānena gṛhyata^ ity athāpi nityāvasthitapuruṣaparikalpinā nirarthikā^ eva| atrocyate cittāntaradṛśye buddhibuddher atiprasaṅgaḥ smṛtisaṅkaraś ca||

[352,24] yadi cittaṃ cittāntareṇa svarasaniruddhaṃ paurastyaṃ pāścātyena jāyamānena samanantareṇagṛhyeta buddhibuddhiḥ ghaṭaviṣayā buddhiḥ buddhir ucyate tadviṣayā tu buddhibuddhiḥ sā kena gṛhyate vyavasthitagrāhakābhāvāt| sā apy anyayā grahītavyā sā apy anyayty atiprasaṅgaḥ||

[353,8] na cāgṛhītāsu buddhibuddhiṣu saṃvyavahāro ghaṭate smṛtisaṃskārānupapatteḥ| tatra^ ekajātīyānām eva ghaṭabuddhibuddhīnāṃ dāśatayī mālā syād anavasthitā na ghaṭabuddhiḥ| parisamāptau ca ghaṭabuddhibuddhīnāṃ asatyāṃ kathaṃ ghaṭādibuddhir upatiṣṭheta||

[353,11] ayaṃ cāparo doṣaḥ smṛtisaṃkaraś ceti| buddhibuddhīnāṃ tatsaṃskārāṇāṃ caikarūpatvāt smṛtisaṃkaraḥ prāpnoti| yāvanto buddhibuddhīnām anubhavās tāvatyaḥ smṛtayaḥ saṃkīrṇāḥ prāpnuvanti| tatsaṅkarāt smṛtisaṅkarāt smṛtyanavadhāraṇaṃ iyam asyā buddheḥ smṛtir ity anavadhārayantaḥ kathaṃ vyavaharemahi| tathā ca sarūpatvāt smṛtisaṃkaraḥ 
prāpnoti||

[353,16] yāvanto buddhibuddhīnāṃ vidānāḥ svabuddhismṛtipūrvavyavahārāsambhavāt sarve sammohena saṅgacchemahi| yadi cānavasthāparijihāsayā kvacit buddhyantare samavatiṣṭhemahi tam eva buddhipratisaṃvedinaṃ buddhipratisaṃvedanaliṅgena spaṣṭataram api puruṣam apalapacbhir vaināśikair ākulīkṛtaṃ jagad iti||

[353,20] nanu ca^ ālayavijñānakalpanayā 'navasthādoṣaṃ pariharemahi| tathā ca^ ātmakalpanām apalapemahīty evaṃ te bhoktṛsvarūpaṃ yatra kvacana kalpayanto na nyāyena saṅgaddhante| tasyāpy alayavijñānasya grāhyatvād anavasthitatvāc cabhihitasakaladoṣānuṣaṅgaḥ||

[353,24] kecit vaināśikaviśeṣāḥ sattvamātram api śabdāntareṇa sattvamātraṃ bhoktṛsvarūpaṃ parikalpyāsti sa sattvo ya^ etān pañca skandhān rūpavedanāsaṃjñāsaṃskāravijñānalakṣaṇān nikṣipya parityajyānyāṃś ca skandhān pratisandhāti gṛhṇātīty uktvā tata^ eva bhūtānāgataskandhapratisandhātr-ekasattvaprikalpanātaḥ trasyanti||

[354,13] tathā teṣam anyā viruddhakalpanā skandhānāṃ mahānirvedāya virāgāyānutpādāya praśāntaye guror antike brahmacaryaṃ carāmīty uktvā 
punas tam evābhyāgataṃ sattvasya sattva(sādhyasādhanasambandha)m apahnuvate| vijñānamātraṃ kṣaṇikaṃ śūnyaṃ ceti^ ātmānam apalapanti| (namabhisandadhīmahi| tad evaṃ ) sāṅkhyayogādayas tu pravādāḥ| svaśabdenapuruṣam eva svāminaṃ vittasya bhoktāram abhyupayantīti||4.21||

[354,18] kathaṃ bhoktāraṃ svaśabdenaivābhyupayanti yataḥ citer apratisaṃkramāyās tadākārāpattau satyāṃ svabuddhisaṃvedanaṃ bhavati| citiḥ caitanyaṃ puruṣa^ ity arthaḥ| sā cāpariṇāmitvāt apratisaṃkramā| tasyāḥ apratisaṃkramāyāḥ api satyāś citeḥ tadākārāpattiḥ| tad iti prakṛtaṃ cittaṃ pratyāmnāyate tasyākārāpattis tadākārāpattiḥ| tasyāś cites tadākārāpattau satyāṃ svabuddhisaṃbedanaṃ bhavati| svasya bauddhasya 
pratyayasya^ upalabdhir bhavati| tad artham eva hi sā buddhiḥ śabdādipratyayarūpeṇ apariṇamate||

[354,25] tad etat pratipādayati (pratītya) apariṇāminī hi bhoktṛ-śaktiḥ dṛk-śaktiḥ yathātrayākhyātā| apratisaṃkramā ca satī pariṇāminy arthe bnauddhapratyaye pratisaṃkrānteva pratyayiviṣayatvāt tadvṛttim anupatantīvānupatantī draṣṭṛtvād upalabhamānā| bauddhaḥ pratyayo jāyamāna eva tasyāḥ karmatām āpadyate| taṃ bauddhaṃ pratyayaṃ vṛttim anupatanty upalabhamānā bhoktṛ-śaktiḥ||

[355,12] tasyāś ca prāptacaitanyopagraha[sva]rūpāyāḥ prāptaṃ caitanyopagraharūpaṃ puruṣopagraharūpaṃ yayā buddhivṛttyā samāsāditapāvakopagraharūpataptalohapiṇḍavat dṛśikarmatvād eva anukāramātratayā buddhivṛttisārūpyamātratayā| buddhivṛttyaviśiṣṭā jñavṛttir iti| jña^ evopalabdhir eva vṛttir iti^ ākhyāyate| vṛttisārūpyam itaratra^ iti vyākhgyātam| tathā pratyayāviśeṣo bhogaḥ iti prakhyāpitam||

[355,18] bhoktṛbhogyabhede ca sati cittākārāpattir upapadyate| na cāsati vyatirikte bhoktari cittasyaiva draṣṭṛdṛśyobhayābhāsatvam akasmād upakalpate| tasmāt suṣṭhūcyate sāṅkhyayogādaya eva puruṣaṃ svaśabdenābhyupayantīti| anye tu śabdāntareṇopayanto api tata^ eva bibhyati|| tatho ca^ uktam buddhivṛttyanukāramātratayā buddhāv eva jñavṛttir upalabhyate nānyatra^ iti| na pātālaṃ no vivaraṃ girīṇāṃ naivāndhakāraḥ kukṣayo na^ udadhīnām evam ādīni na guhā| yasyāṃ guhāyāṃ nihitaṃ brahma śāśvataṃ sā guhā na pātāchādīni| kasyāṃ punar guhāyāṃ nihitam ity āha buddhāv eva guhāyāṃ vṛttim ātmākhyāṃ brahmākhyām aviśiṣṭam apṛthagbhūtāṃ buddhivṛttyaviśiṣṭāṃ vedayante vivekinaḥ||4.22||

[356,8] kasmāt punaḥ kāraṇāt puruṣasya cittākārāpattiḥ| tasyāṃ vā satyāṃ svabuddhisaṃvedanam | kuto vaināśikānāṃ cittam eva grāhyaṃ grāhakaṃ ceti bhrāntir ity āha draṣṭṛdṛśyoparaktaṃ cittaṃ sarvārtham/ drasṭā 
puruṣo dṛśyaṃ śabdādi tābhyām uparaktaṃ cittaṃ sarvārthaṃ puruṣasya bhogāpavargārthaṃ bhavati| mano'bhimantavyenārthena ayaskāntamaṇideśīyena viṣayeṇa^ uparaktaṃ svayaṃ ca viṣayatvāt puruṣasya viṣayiṇā puruṣeṇ asannihitena^ ātmīyayā vṛttyā mānasena pratyayena viṣayatayā^ abhisambaddham||

[356,15] tad etac cittam ātmavṛttidvāreṇa puruṣeṇa sambandhād draṣṭuḥ svarūpopraktaṃ śabdādinā vā mantavyena^ uparaktam iti draṣṭṛdṛśyoparaktaṃ cittam eva na puruṣaḥ| draṣṭṛdrśyoparaktam ity asya vyākhyānaṃ karoti viṣayaviṣayinirbhāsaṃ cetanācetanasvarūpāpannam||

[356,19] kathaṃ punaḥ svayam acetanaṃ sac cetanasvarūpāpannm iti na^ eṣa doṣaḥ puruṣeṇa nityasannidhānāc cetanam iva| tad evāvivekibhiś cetanam ity ucyate| na tathā viṣayāḥ| nityam acetanātmakā hi te| svatas tu vittam acetanam eva^ iti cetanācetanasvarūpāpannaṃ ity ucyate||

[356,23] viṣayātmakam iva śabdādisvarūpamātram iva cety arthaḥ| acetanaṃ cetanam iva puruṣasvarūpāpannam iva cety arthaḥ| sphaṭikamaṇikalpaṃ yathā sphaṭikamaṇir alaktakādisannidhau tadākāratāṃ biharti| tathā^ eva tat sarvārthaṃ cittam ucyate||

[356,26] tasmāt anena cittasārūpyeṇa bhrāntāḥ kecit| etat tu cittasārūpyaṃ vaiśeṣikāṇām pramohakāraṇam| vaiśeṣikādayas tu cittasārūpyamāhendrajālavyāmohitacetasaḥ tad eva cittaṃ cetanam ity āhuḥ| katham yasmān manaḥpruṣasaṃyogajaṃ caitanyaṃ guṇaḥ uddhedanīya^ iti manyate| vaināśikebhyas tv ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ sthiram ekaṃ dharmiṇam icchantīti||

[357,10] apare bauddhāḥ cit-mātram eveddaṃ sarvam nāsti gavādir ghaṭādiś ca svākāreṇa viśiṣṭaḥ sarveṣāṃ pratyayasya sādhāraṇo lokaḥ iti svānubhavam apy apalapanti| te^ evaṃ bhrāntāḥ sukumāratarabuddhayo vainācikā^ anukapmanīyāḥ||

[357,13] kasmāt asti hi teṣāṃ bhrāntibījaṃ sarvarupākāranirbhāsaṃ cittam iti| sanimittā hi teṣāṃ bhrāntiḥ na lokopāyatikānāṃ śūnyatvādinām iva nirbījā| kevalajagadvañcanārthapravṛttatvāt tu na^ eteṣv anukampā kartavyā| tasmād yuktam uktaṃ teṣāṃ varākāṇāṃ sabījabhrāntacetasāmanukampanīyam iti||

[357,17] ke punar abhrāntā ity āha ssamādhiprajñāyām iti| samādhiprajñāgrahaṇam abhrāntacittagrahaṇārtham| vikṣiptacetaso hi bhrāntāḥ| samādhiprajñāyām abhrānāyāṃ viśuddhāyāṃ vivekavatyāṃ 
prasannagambhīratarāyāṃ prajñeyo 'rthaḥ pratibimbībhūtaḥ nirmaladarpaṇamaṇḍala^ iva tasyāṃ samādhiprajñāyāṃ karaṇabhūtāyām ālambanībhūtatvād anyaḥ prajñātaḥ sakāśāj jātisvarūpakriyādibhir anyaḥ||

[357,22] sa cet anyaḥ cittamātraṃ syāt kathaṃ prajñayā^ eva prajñārūpam avadhāryeta| svātmani kriyāvirodhāt na hi kathañcid avadhāryeta| tasmāt 
prajñāyāḥ anyo 'sāv arthaḥ saḥ yasyāṃ prajñāyāṃ pratibimbībhūto 'rtho yenāvadhāryate saḥ tasyāḥ prajñāyā^ ālambanībhūtāc cārthād anyaḥ kartṛkārakabhūtaḥ| sa puruṣaḥ||

[357,26] ity evaṃ grahītṛgrahaṇagrāhyasvarūpabhedāt kartṛkaraṇakarmabhedopapatteḥ na hi niravayavam ekam eva^ ekasmin kṣaṇew vastu kartṛ ca karma ca bhavati| tato ye trayam apy etat jātitaḥ 
pravibhajante te samyagdarśanaḥ tair adhigataḥ pouruṣaḥ prāpta^ ity arthaḥ| sāmarthyāt tu jñāta iti gamyate||

[358,12] kutaś cāsti puruṣaś cittavyatiriktaḥ pūrvaṃ cittasya dṛśyatvena vyatiriktapuruṣasiddhir āpāditā| tasya^ eva tu vittasya saṃhatykāritvenādhunā svāmisiddhuartham āha tadasaṅkhyeyavāsanācitram api 
parārthaṃ saṃhatyakāritvāt ||

[358,15] yady api tad eva cittam asaṃkhyeyābhir vāsanābhiḥ anādisamayābhiḥ citrīkṛtaṃ bhrāntikāraṇam uktaṃ tathāpi parā-arthaṃ 
parasya bhogārtham eva^ iti pratijñāyate| saṃhatyakāritvāt kāryakaraṇaviṣayādivyāpārāpekṣam arthasādhakatvāt saṃhatyakāritvam ataḥ saṃhatyakāritvāt gṛhādivat iti pārārthyaṃ cittasya| na hiḥ kiṃcana loke saṃhatyakāri dṛṣṭaṃ svātham||

[358,20] tasmāt saṃhatyakāriṇā cittena na svārthena bhavitavyam| na hi sukhacittaṃ sukhārthaṃ sukhacittārthaṃ vā| nāpi jñānacittaṃ jñānārthaṃ vā jñānacittārthaṃ vā| kiṃ tarhi sukhacittaṃ jñānacittam api^ etad ubhayaṃ parārtham||

[358,23] yaś ca bhogena sukhacittena jñānacittena ca apavarheṇārthenārthavān puruṣaḥ sa^ eva paraḥ| na parasāmānyamātraṃ śarīram indriyāṇi viṣayā vā| na hi vaināśīkaḥ sukhacittaṃ śarīrendriyaviṣayātham icchati| tathā cecchann abhyupagataṃ virundhīta| vijñānasya^ eva hi sukhāpavargābhyām arthitvam abhimanyate| tasmāt ya^ eva śarīrendriyavijñānasya^ eva hi sukhāpavargābhyām arthitvam abhimanyate| atasmāt ya^ eva śarīrendriyaviṣayavyatiriktaḥ puruṣo bhogāpavargābhyām arthavān anyo vijñānād abhyupagamanīyaḥ||

[359,1] yadi yat kiṃcit parasāmānyamātram udāhared vaināśikaḥ yadi tu vaināśikaḥ saṃhatyakāriṇāṃ gṛhādīnāṃ devadattādisaṃhataparārthatvam iti vyāmohād abhimanyamānāaḥ kaṃcana viśiṣṭaṃ na param icchet evaṃ cen manvīta sa^ idaṃ vaktavyam yaḥ paraḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayapratyayānāṃ sāmānyam uta sarva^ eva^ iti kimeṣām anyatamaḥ kiṃ vā kaścid eva^ etebhyo bhinnajātīya^ iti||

[359,6] na tāvat sāmānyam| na hibhogāpavargārthenārthi sāmānyavastu vaināśiko 'bhyupaiti svasamayavirodhāt| nāpi śarīra^ādīni samastāni bhogāpavargābhyām arthīnīti vājddhati| vijñānasantater eva bhogāpavargārthitvābhimanāt||

[359,9] na cāpi jñānasantatir ity ekaṃ vastu (santateḥ kṣaṇikatvam.) na cāpi santānasyāvastutayā śaśaviṣāṇakalpasya bhogāpavargābhyām arthitvam| nāpi śarīrendriyādīnām anyatamatvam icchati| vijñānavyatiriktānām ātmīyasamaye bhogāpavargārthitvānabhyupagamāt||

[359,13] nāpi śarīrendriyāṇāṃ saṅghāto nāma vastuto bhavato vidyate yena cittaṃ saṃhataparārthaṃ prārthayethāḥ| avastuno hi katham arthitvam| nāpi śarīrendriyādibhyo'nyaṃ saṃhatabhinnajātīyaṃ pratijānīṣe| yadi bhinnajātīyaṃ bhogāpavargābhyām arthavantam adṛṣṭaṃ parikalpayethāh sa evāsmābhir uktaḥ para^ iti| tasya ca saṃhatatve paratvaṃ dṛṣṭānātmavan na^ upakalpate| pariśeṣāt tu^ asaṃhato bhogāpavargābhyām arthī para^ iti||

[359,19] kiṃ cānyat saṃhataparārthaṃ cittam iti sādhayato 'rthinaḥ saṃhatasyānyasyānupapatteḥ svārtham eva cittam ity uktaṃ bhavati| tathā ca sati bhavato dṛṣṭāntābhāvo 'bhyupagamavirodhaś ca| tathā manvānasyānyad vāci hṛdaye cānyad aniśamyakāritvaṃ ca^ āpatati| yena cakṣurādiparārthatvasādhanena viruddhatādoṣaḥ pratyākhyāyi| teṣāṃ vijñānaparārthatvam iti ced vijñānasyāpi saṃhatvatvāt parārthatvaṃ 
prasādhitam| atha vijñānasyāpi kṣaṇikatvād asaṃhatatvam iti cet na tasya vyatiriktadṛśyatvāt dharmamātrānabhyupagamāc ca kṣaṇikatvānabhyupagamāc cety etad api pratyavocāma||

[360,12] tasmād ucyate yat kiṃcit parasāmānyamātraṃ svarūpeṇodāhared vaināśikaḥ sa sarvaḥ saṃhatyakāritvāt parārtha^ eva syāt| na devadattādisaṃhatārthatvam| ubhābhyām āvābhyām anabhyupagamāt| tvam api vijñānavyatirekeṇa nānyam abhyupaiṣi| vayam api puruṣavyatirekeṇa nānyārthatvam abhibāñchāmaḥ| cijñānamātratvaṃ ca na^ upapadyata^ ity uktam| ato yas tv asau paro viśeṣaḥ sa na saṃhatyakārī puruṣaḥ svārtha^ iti siddhaḥ| tasmād asti bhoktā tasya svārthasya svena dṛśyena parārthena yannimittaḥ saṃyogaḥ| tadviyogāt kaivalyam upakalpate ||4.24||

[360,19] guṇapuruṣa-yogasyāvidyā nimittm uktam| sā ca viśeṣadarśina ātmānaṃ vijijñāsamānasya vinivartate| tannivṛttau ca liṅgam āha viśeṣadarśina^ ātmabhāvabhāvanānivṛttiḥ| vyaktāvyaktasāmānyād viśiṣṭaḥ 
puruṣaḥ asādhāraṇo viśeṣaḥ| taṃ viśeṣaṃ vyaktāvyaktasāmānyāt pravivicya draṣṭuṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa viśeṣadarśī| tasya^ ātmabhāvabhāvanā nivartate| sā ca samyagdarśanābhimukhā kaivalyaprāpikarmanimittā||

[360,25] kathaṃ punar ātmabhāvabhāvanā^ asyāstīti vijñāyata^ ity āha yathā prāvṛṣi tṛṇasyāṅkurodbhedena liṅgena tadbījasadbhāvo 'numīya(gamya)te tathā mokṣamārgaśravaṇena yasya romaharṣāśrupāto dṛśyate tatrāpy asti viśeṣadarśanabījasamapavargabhāgīyaṃ karmābhinirvartitam iti romaharṣāśrupātādiliṅgenānumīyate||

[361,13] tasya viśeṣadarśinaḥ ātmabhāvabhāvanā kaivalyabhāgīyakarmanimittā svābhāvikī janmāntarakarmanimittā ihajanmāparihasthairyakāraṇā vā| sā hy ācāryo padeśānapekṣā svābhāvikī 
pravartate| tasya karmaṇas tādṛśasyābhāvāt idam uktam svabhāvadoṣāt aśuddhakarmayogāt eṣāṃ pūrvanpakṣe samyagdarśanatadupāyaviparyaye rucir bhavati^ aruciś ca nirṇaye samyagdarśane||

[361,18] tatrātmabhāvabhāvanā kā punar ity āha ko 'ham āsaṃ katham aham āsaṃ kiṃsvid idaṃ kathaṃsvid idaṃ ke bhaviṣyāmaḥ kathaṃ bhaviṣyāmaḥ iti| sā tu viśeṣadarśino nivartate| yadviṣayā hi jijñāsā tasminn adhigate tadviṣayā jijñāsā vinivartate| kutaḥ etad evam yataḥ cittasya^ evaiṣaḥ ke bhaviṣyāma^ ity ādiḥ citraḥ pariṇāmaḥ na puruṣasya| puruṣatvasatyām avidyāyāṃ śuddhaś cittadharmair aprāmṛṣṭa^ iti| yasmād evam ata^ evaṃ viśeṣadarśinaḥ tasyātmabhāvabhāvanā kuśalasya nirvartate||4.25||

[361,24] yadā ca viśeṣadarśanam udapādi tadā vivekanimnaṃ kaivalyaprāgbhāraṃ cittaṃ| yad^ asya cittaṃ viṣayanimnaṃ viṣayapravaṇam ajñānaprāgbhāram ajñānaniṣṭhaṃ saṃsārābhimukham āsīt tad asya viśeṣadarśinaḥ tadānīm anyathā  bhavati kaivalyamrāgbhāraṃ vivekanimnam iti pratisnotovṛttir jñāyate||4.26||

[362,14] tac chidreṣu pratyayāntarāṇi saṃskārebhyaḥ| vivekanimnasya uttarottaravivekapravaṇacetasaḥ puruṣasya jñāninaḥ anyatākhyātipravāhādhirohiṇaḥ sattvapuruṣānyatāsrotaḥplāvinaḥ cittasya tacchidreṣu vivekapratyayapravāhāntareṣu pratyayāntarāṇi viparyayalakṣaṇāni^ asmīti vā mameti vā| kkutaḥ tāni bhavantīty āha kṣīyamāṇakleśādibījasya cittasya pūrvopacitaviparītasaṃskārebhyaḥ 
pratyayāntarāṇi jāyante||4.27||

[362,20] hānam eṣāṃ kleśavad uktam| yathā kleśā dagdhabījabhāvā na parohasamarthā bhavantīti te pratiprasabaheyāḥ sūkṣmāḥ ity atroktam evaṃ jñānāgnidagdhabījabhāvāḥ asmi mametyādipratyayasaṃskārā na parohasamarthā bhavanti| dagdhaśālibījavat pūrvasaṃskāro na pratyayaprasūrbhavatīty arthaḥ||

[362,24] jñānasaṃskārās tu katham apratipakṣāḥ santaś cittam adhikāre na(bīja)yojayiṣyantīti te tu samyagdarśanasaṃskārāś cittādhikāraparisamāptim anuśerate| jsahaivāśrayeṇāvasitaprayojanena cittena pralīyante| na punaś cittam adhikārapravaṇaṃ kkurvanti| adhikārahetor avidyāyāḥ pratipakṣatvāj jñānasya| tyatra niṣphalatvāt na vintyante| na hi svayaṃ patataḥ patanahetur anbeṣaṇīyaḥ||4.28||

[363,15] vivekakhyātipravaṇāntaḥkaraṇasya brāhmāṇasya prasṃkhyāne apy akusīd asya sarvathāvivekakhyāter dharmamedhaḥ samādhiḥ| sa yadā^ ayaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ prasṃkhyāne vivekadarśanaśīlite apy akusīdaṅ avṛddhiko bhavati tato api prasaṃkhyānaprāsādaviśeṣād adhikaṃ na kiṃcit prārthayate| apiśabdāt prasṃkhyāne api na prārthanā| tatra_Pi prasṃkhyāne viraktaḥ| sarvathā sarveṇa prakāreṇa vivekakhyātir yasya sa sarvathāvivekakhyātir eva bhavati||

[363,21] kleśasaṃskārabījakṣayāt nāsya śarīrādau śūnyeveśmanīvāsmi mameti vā jñānaprasādamātraikāyatanasya pratyayāntarāṇy utpadyante| tadā 'sya brāhmāṇasya sarvathāvivekakhyāteḥ dharmamegho nāma samādhir bhavati| kaivalyākhyaṃ paraṃ dharmaṃ varṣatīti dharmameghaḥ iti saṃjñā||4.29||

[363,25] tataḥ kleśakarmanivṛttiḥ| tasya dharmamedhanāmnaḥ samādheḥ samyagdarśanapākābhirūpasya [lābhāt] bhāvāt abidyādayaḥ kleśāḥ samūlakāṣaṃ kaṣitāḥ vāsanābhiḥ saha samastāḥ pralīnāḥ bhavanti| kuśalākuśśalāś ca karmāśayāḥ samūlaghātaṃ hatā bhavanti| mūlaiḥ kleśaiḥ sahocchinnā bhavanti||

[364,15] kleśakarmanivṛttau jīvann eva vidvān mukto bhavati| kasmāt evam yato viparyayaḥ mithyājñānaṃ bhavasya janmanaḥ kāraṇam| na hi kṣīṇaviparyayaḥ kaścit kvacij jāto dṛśyate||3.30||

[364,18] tadā sarvāvaraṇamalāpetasya jñānasyānantyāt jñeyam alpam| tadā dharmamedhasamādhisamaye sarvaiḥ kleśakarmāvaraṇaiḥ rajastamojanmabhiḥ vimuktasya jñānasya sattvapuruṣānyatākhyātimātrasya bhavaty ānantyam| tat katham iti tamasā(na)bhibhūtam anantaṃ jñānasattvaṃ tasya tiraskṛtarajomalasyānantasya nistaraṅgamahodadhideśīyasya nirahitasyāvikriyasya kathaṃ tad-grahāṇasāmarthyam ityāha kvacid rajasā 
pravartitaṃ cittasattvam uddhāṭitaṃ vāyuneva mahāpayodamaṇḍalam āhataṃ cūcitaṃ grahaṇasamarthaṃ bhavvati| svataḥ sattvasyāpravartanāt rajaso hiḥ 
pravṛttisāmarthyam iti||

[364,25] tadāsya cittasya bhavaty ānantyam aśeṣajñeyaviṣayatvam ambaratalamadhyavartino jaladharanirodhanirgatasya sver iva| tasya jñānasyānantyā^ aśeṣaviṣayatvāt jñeyam alpaṃ saṃpadyate| yathā^ ākāśe khadyotaḥ iti| yathā^ ākāśāpekṣayā khadyoto na kiṃcid evam etajjñānābhisamīkṣayā jñeyaṃ na kiṃcid iti||

[365,15] paramārthajñānāvasthāyām aśeṣabiparyayatirodhāne sarvapuruṣārthaśūnyatā cety etatpradarśanārtham udāharaṇam āha yatredam uktam andho maṇim avindat (dhyat) tam anaṅgulirāvayat| agrīvaḥ [taṃ] pratyamuñcat tamajihvo 'bhyapūjayat || iti| yathā^ andha^ādīnāṃ maṇīvedhādikam asambhavad api viparyayeṇāndho maṇim avidhyad iti vyavahriyate tathā jñānajñeyasaṃvyavahāro api viparyayāvasthāyām eva| viparyayābhāve tu sarvapuruṣārthaśūnyā eva guṇāḥ sampadyanta iti||4.31||

[365,21] tataḥ kṛtārthānāṃ pariṇāmakramaparisamāptir guṇānām| tataḥ dharmamedhasamādhisamudayāt kṛtārthāś cittaguṇāḥ sampadyante| cittādhīnatvāc ca kāryakaraṇārambhiṇo api sattvādayaḥ kṛtārthā bhavanti| samastasya puruṣārthasya kṛtatvāt eṣāṃ kṛtārthānāṃ guṇānāṃ janmamaraṇapra(bhāva)vāharūpaḥ pariṇāmakramaḥ parisamāpyate| na hi kṛtabhogāpavargārthāḥ kṣaṇam api prayojanābhāvāt avasthātum utsahante||4.32||

[365,27] pariṇāmakramaparisamāptir ity uktam| atha ko 'yaṃ guṇakramo nāma yasya parisamāptir upavarṇitā| tasyāyam adhigamopāya^ ucyate kṣaṇapratiyogī pariṇāmāparāntanirgrāhyaḥ kramaḥ| kṣaṇaṃ prati yujyata iti kṣaṇapratiyogī| (366,8) kṣaṇāntaryātmā kṣaṇasyānantaratā kṣaṇānantaryaṃ tadātmā tatsvabhāvaḥ| kṣaṇānantaryam eva kṣaṇatatkramayoḥ iti vyākhgyātam||

[366,10] kiṃ punar asya grahaṇe liṅgam ity āha pariṇāmasyāparāntena vināśāvasthārūpeṇāvasānena liṅgena nigṛhyate saḥ kramaḥ| katham aparāntena nirgrahaṇam iti^ ucyate na hy ananubhūtakramā kramam anavalambamānā kṣaṇe purāṇatā vastrasyānte vināśakāle bhavatīti| na hi kadācidbhūtamātrasya^ eva vastrasya purāṇatā dṛśyate| tasmāt 
purānatābhivyaktiliṅgenābhyūhyate yeneyaṃ purāṇatā vastrasyābhivyaktim upasaṃprāpitā sa kramaḥ||

[366,16] tatra yadi labhāvasānatvam alabdhāvasānatvaṃ ca kramasya syāt tato yuktaṃ kṛtārthānāṃ pariṇāmakramaparisamāptir guṇānām iti vaktum| tac ca labdhālabdhāvasānatvaṃ kramasya nityānityatve ghaṭate| tatrānityeṣu vastrādiṣu kramo dṛṣṭaḥ na tu nityeṣu| tataś ca guṇānāṃ nityānāṃ pariṇāmakramaprisamāptir ayuktā syād ity āśaṅkamāna^ āha nityeṣu ca kramo dṛṣṭaḥ kiṃ sarvatra nety āha dvividheyaṃ nityatā pariṇāmanityatā guṇānāṃ kūṭasthanityatā puruṣasya^ iti||

[366,22] nanu pariṇāmināṃ guṇānāṃ kathaṃ nityatā syād iti vravīti yasmin pariṇammāne tattvaṃ na vihanyate| yasya yadrūpaṃ pramāṇena^ upalabdhaṃ tasya tat tattvam| yadyasmin na vihanyate na vyabhicarati tad api nityam| guṇānām api sukhaduḥkhamohātmatā prakāśakriyāsthitiśīlatā ca tattvaṃ na vihanyate| tasmād ete api pariṇāmanityāḥ| ubhayatra ca guṇāpuruṣeṣu tattvasyāvighātān nityatvam| tato guṇeṣv api nityeṣu 
pariṇāmo dṛṣṭa^ iti labdhālabdhaprayavasānatvaṃ sthitam||

[367,10] tatra guṇadharmeṣu mahadādisu pariṇāmāparāntanirgrāhyaḥ kramo labdhaparyavasānaḥ śarīrādiṣv api| nityeṣu tu guṇeṣu dharmiṣv alabdhaparyavasānaḥ nityatvād eṣām| kūṭasthanityeṣu svarūpamātrapratiṣṭheṣu muktapuruṣeṣu svarūpāstitā krameṇa^ evānubhūyate| tena muktapuruṣeṣv asann apy anubhavadāreṇa śabdapṛṣṭhenāstikriyām upādāya vikalpitaḥ ity alabdhaparyavasāna^ eva| 
paramārthataḥ puruṣeṣv apariṇāmitvān nāsti krama^ ity arthaḥ||

[367,16] athāsya saṃsārasya sthityā pradhānarūpeṇa gagtyā vikārātmanā dvayaṃ ca guṇātmakam iti guṇeṣu ca vartamānasyāsti kramaparisamāptiḥ na vā^ ity avacanīyam etat| asty eva^ ity avadhāraṇaṃ na śakyaṃ kartum| nāpi nāsti eva^ iti| kiṃ tu vibhajya kasyacid asti kasyāpi nāstīti vacanīyam eva||

[367,20] katham asti praśna^ ekāntavacanīyaḥ apravibhajyāpi| yathā sarvo jāto mariṣyati^ ajñānī^ iti| nanu jñānavān api mariṣyaty eva| ucyate^ ajñasya hi maraṇm ity abhimānaḥ na jñāninaḥ| tathā ca^ uktam mithyā^ etad āhur mriyatīti mūḍhāḥ iti||

[367,24] atha sarvo mṛtvā janiṣyata^ iti vibhajya vacanīyam etat ekāntānupapatteḥ| katham pratyuditakhyātiḥ kṣīṇatṛṣṇaḥ kuśalo na janiṣyate^ itaraḥ ajñaḥ aniṣyate||

[368,14] tathā manuṣyajātiḥ śreyasī^ eva na^ eva vā^ ity avacanīyaḥ 
praśnaḥ| pravibhajya tu vacanīya^ eva| katham paśūn uddiśya śreyasī bhavati| devān ṛṣīṃś cādhikṛtya na śreyasī bhavati||

[368,17] ayaṃ tv avacanīya^ evāpravibhajya sāmānyena praśnaḥ yathā saṃsāro 'yam antavān eva^ ity ananta^ eva^ iti vā| bandhamokṣānupapatteḥ| 
pravibhajya tu vacanīya^ eva| yathā kuśalasyāsti saṃsāracakraparisamāptir netarasya^ iti||

[368,20] anyatarāvadhāraṇe doṣaḥ | saṃsāro 'ntavān evānanta^ eva^ iti vānyatarāvadhāraṇe doṣaḥ| tasmād avyākaraṇīya^ evāyaṃ sāmānenāpravibhajya praśnaḥ yathā sarvo mṛtvā janiṣyate^ iti^ evaṃ manuṣyajātiḥ śreyasyeveti ca| tasmāt suṣṭhūktaṃ kṛtārthānāṃ pariṇāmakramaparisamāptir guṇānām iti viśeṣaṇam ||4.33||

[368,24] guṇādhikāraparisamāptau kaivalyam uktam| tatsvarūpam avadhāryate| kaivalyaṃ prati vipratipatteḥ| keṣāṃcid vijñānasantānasyātyantopaśamaḥ kaivalyam| keṣāṃ cit tu navānām ātmaguṇānāṃ buddhīcchādveṣaprayatnasukhaduḥkhadharmādharmasaṃskārāṇām ucchedenācetanasya dravyamātrasya puruṣasyāvasthānam| keṣāṃcid īśvarasāyujyam| keṣāṃcit sarvajñatvādīśvaratulyadharmatvaprāptiḥ kaivalyam iti bhinnā matiḥ||

[369,9] ihāpi vipratipattipraṇodāya kaivalyasvarūpam avadhāryate puruṣārthaśūnyānāṃ guṇānāṃ yaḥ pratiprasavaḥ pratisaṃsargaḥ svakāraṇe pratyastamaya ity arthaḥ pāryakaraṇātmakatayāpariṇatānām tat kaivalyaṃ puruṣasya kevalatvaṃ guṇair abhiśrībhāva ity arthaḥ||

[369,13] svarūpapratiṣṭhā vā citiśktir iti| buddhisattvānabhisaṃbandhinī yā puruṣasya citiśaktiḥ amiśrā kevalā tasyāḥ sadā tathā^ evāvasthānaṃ kevalāyāḥ bhāvaḥ kaivalyam||

[369,16] nanu kaivalyasya^ ekatve kathaṃ vikalpaḥ kriyāyāṃ hi vikalpo na vastuni| vastu-svarūpasya bhedānupapatteḥ kaivalyam api vastu kathaṃ tadvikalpyata^ iti na^ eṣa doṣaḥ| nimittanaimittakayoḥ prabhedopapatteḥ nimittanaimittkabhedopacarito api vikalpo rūpatā sā kāryakaraṇātmakānāṃ 
puruṣārthaśūnyānāṃ guṇānāṃ pratiasṃsargān nimittād apagacchatīti nimittam eva pratiprasavākhyaṃ kaivalyaśabdena^ upacaritaṃ pūrvam||

[396,21] dvitīyaṃ tu guṇapratiprasavanimittabhāvi yat svarūpāvasthānaṃ 
puruṣasya guṇair amiśritatayā tat kaivalyam iti viśeṣaḥ| guṇapuruṣāntarasvarūpatattvanirdhāraṇena tantrāntarīyatatvasvarūpanirākaraṇena ca kaivalyāntarāṇiniveditavyāni||

[396,24] tatra^ oṅkāro maṅgalārthaḥ prayuktaḥ parameśvaranāmāṅkitamastakatayā tantrapracayārthaṃ śāntyarthaṃ vā vedāraṇyakavad iti^ om iti||

[370,1] oṅkāro yasya vaktā samacarat phalaiḥ karma yasmād aśeṣaṃ niṣkarmakleśapāko ghaṭayati sakalaṃ yaḥ phalena kriyāṇām| 370,3        īśānām īśvaro yaḥ sthitibhavanidhanaprakriyāṇāṃ vidhātā dhyāya naḥ śuklimānaṃ vyapanudatu tarāṃ kṛṣṇimānaṃ sa kṛṣṇaḥ||

[370,5] yātrākṛtas trijagatāṃ yad^ acintyaśaktileśām daśāhur iha mīnamukhāvatārān| 370,9     kleśudbhavatrividhatāpaparamparārtāḥ taṃ nāganāthaśayanaṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajāmaḥ||

[370,11] phaṇaratnaoghavidyoti [pṛthivī dyunabhodiśe| yogīndrāya 
phaṇīndrāya tatpatañjalaye namaḥ||

[370,13] yogena cittasya padena vācāṃ malaṃ śarīrasya tu vaidyakena| yo 'pākarot taṃ pravaraṃ munīnāṃ patañjaliṃ prāñjalir ānato 'smi||

[370,15] sa jayati patañjalimuniḥ yena śreyo 'rthasārthasasyānām| vihito harṣas tāpatritayahṛtā dharmameghena||

[370,17] yas tatāna bhavamārgalaṅghināṃ kleśakarmamayadharmanuttaye| dharmamedhamukhayogatoyadam [taṃ patañjalim ṛṣiṃ praṇato 'smi]||

[370,19] vadanāhitapūrṇacandrakaṃ gurum īśānam abhūtibhūṣaṇam| praṇamāmy abhujaṅgasaṅgrahaṃ bhagavatpādam apūrvaśaṅkaram||

[370,21] iti śrīgovandabhagavatpūjyapādaśiṣyasya 
paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryasya śrī-śaṅkarabhagavataḥ kṛtau 
pātañjalayogasūtra(śāstra)bhāṣyavivaraṇe caturthaḥ kaivalyapādaḥ||

|| samāptaṃ cedaṃ vivaraṇam||
